SARASVATĪ VILĀSA
Vyavahārakāṇḍaḥ
vandāmahe mahīyāṃsamasalambijaṭādharam |
yatkaṅkaṇakvaṇatkārarūpaṃ śabdānuśāsanam || 1 ||
ācāndrārkasamau tanotu kuśalaṃ vaśśaṅkarasyātmajo
yasyānargaladānavārisurabheḥ kaṇṭhopakaṇṭhaṃ mudā |
khelantaḥ parito dvirephakalabhāḥ śreṇībhavanto muhuḥ
śumbhajjambhabhidaśśarāsalaharīśṛṅgāram ātanvate || 2 ||
************
********* brahmavido vadanti |
vāṇi! tvadāyattam idaṃ svayaṃ cet
kā vā stutiḥ syād ayam añjalis te || 3 ||
hanūmantaṃ śāntaṃ svayam upadiśantaṃ raghuvarād
upādhyāyād ādyād yad iha samudāyāya yaminām |
namasyāmo hemopamam anupamāmodiyaśasaṃ
pratāpāṅke laṅkeśvaravijayinaṃ kesarisutam || 4 ||
[2]        nirbhinnanīlamaṇigarbhanikāyakāyaṃ
nīlādriśekharamaṇiṃ nitarāṃ smarāmi |
jyotirmaye vapuṣi yasya sitetaratvaṃ
kaṃjekṣaṇasya gaganātmatayaiva nūnam || 5 ||
śiśuṃ kapaṭayādavaṃ śritajanasya māyādavaṃ
niṣiddhavṛṣabhāsuraṃ nigamadarpaṇaṃ bhāsuram |
ramākalitamānasaṃ rayavibhagna bhīmānasaṃ
smarāmi nalinekṣaṇaṃ sarasasaṃvidhāne kṣaṇam || 6 ||
padānataśacīpatiṃ paśupatiṃ diśā cīvaraṃ
surālayaśarāsanaṃ sakṛtihṛdvihārāsanam |
vimuktavividhāmayaṃ vimalacittamedhāmayaṃ
purāṃ samadhikadviṣaṃ punar upaimi śākadviṣam || 7 ||
śrīvīrarudranṛpateḥ śriyam ātanotu
nīlādrināthanijapādasarojayugmam |
yad yogibhir hṛdayapaṅkajamadhyabhāga
saṃrodhanād iva samudgataraktabhāvam || 8 ||
pratāpaśrīrudraṃ nṛpam avatu durgāpadayugaṃ
saraktaṃ raktaughaiḥ mahiṣadanujorassthalabhavaiḥ |
śubhāny asmai diśyāc chrutiyuvatisīmantasaraṇi
pratolīsindūraiḥ paricayavaśenāruṇacaṇam || 9 ||
[3]
atha kṛtipraṇetṛvaṃśāvatāro 'varāryate
na hy atra tatrabhavato vaṃśāvatāranibandhanam anupapannam iti mama manīṣāyāḥ samunmeṣaḥ, yataḥ khalu, anuguṇaguṇaracitarucirataratārahārāvaler api nāyakamaṇinigumbhanavaśenaiva nijṛmbhaṇam | yathā vā lokottaracamatkārabhuvo ‘pi nāyikāyāḥ svānuguṇanāyakasaṃbandhenaiva caritārthatā — yathā vā nayanānandakarasyāpi tārāgaṇasya viśākaraparicayād eva saubhāgyātiśayaḥ — yathā vā kūlaṅkaṣāṇāṃ kūlaṅkaśajalānām api nadīnāṃ pāthaḥparisaraprasareṇaiva śobhātiśayitvam — yathā vā tamonivāraṇanipuṇānām api kiraṇānāṃ kiraṇamālinā saṅkalanayaiva sakalajagadānandahetutā — yathā vā bhagavato vāsudevasya guṇagaṇā api līlāparigṛhītavigrahāvatārasaṃbhandhād eva garimāṇam āvahanto manoharantitarām – tathaiva mahānāyakavaṃśāvatāranibandhenaiva kṛtayaḥ sukṛtisamākalpam ākalpayanti – kim u vaktavyaṃ prabandhaprabandhṛvaṃśāvatāravarṇanam – suvarṇakusumasya saurabhāvirbhāvaḥ atha vā candanataroḥ prasavasamudgamaḥ – atha vā aśokataroḥ phalābhisaṃbandhaḥ |
api ca —
maṅgalācārayuktānāṃ vinipāto na vidyate |
iti smṛtigatam api vinipātanivṛttiphalakam avaśyaṃ maṅgalācaraṇam ācaraṇīyam | tad eva maṅgalācaraṇaṃ yat puṇyapuruṣapauruṣānuvarṇanam | [4] puṇyapuruṣaḥ paramapuruṣād anyo nāstīti khalu sa eva varṇanīyo līlārasaparigṛhītavigrahaḥ, tasyānantā evāvatārāḥ | atītā vartamānā vartiṣyamāṇā iti teṣām ānantyam | atītānām avatārāṇām atītatvaṃ na śaknoti manmanaḥ praveṣṭum, evam eva vartiṣyamāṇatvam api, vartamāneṣu purāṇapuruṣāvatāraviśeṣeṣu bhūkāntāsantatasamārjitanikhilasukṛtaparipākaviśeṣe bhūpaviśeṣe vidviṣatkrūrarudre pratāpagajapativīrarudre gāmbhīrya samudre sati mama mano na rocayate tam atikrāntum | atas tam eva mṛgayate vācaś caritārthayitum, ato lakṣmīmahīmahilākatayā vīrarudras sākṣāt purāṇapuruṣāvatāraviseṣa eveti tadanuvarṇanam asmākam anuvarṇanīyam eva tadvaṃśāgraninabhomaṇiragaṇanīybhāsā vikāsatetarām  —
astīśvarasyāṣṭamamūrtir eṣa svastipradas samprati dīptayoṣaḥ |
astodayādrīśvaramastakāgrīvastāritaikāśvaraṇatkhurāgraḥ || 1 ||
pratāpināṃ dvārasamau pratāpī pratāpavastu pratimānabhūmiḥ |
pratāparudrasya kulāgragaṇyaḥ pratāpabhānuḥ prathamo grahāṇām || 2 ||
kule ‘bhavann aṃśumato nṛpālās tale bhuvo dikpatitulyalīlāḥ |
bileśayādhiśabhujāntarālāḥ balena labdhakṣiticakrabālāḥ || 3 ||
tadanvaye rāmakaviprasādāl labdhaprabhāvo raghunāyako ‘bhūt |
tadātmajo bāhujamauliratnamaṇis trilokīvinutapratāpaḥ || 4 ||
tasmād asūta sutam indumatīndukalpaṃ
kalpadrumaprasavakalpitakalpabhājam |
[5]        jambhāriyuddhamakham iddhayaśonidhānaṃ
sambhāvitaṃ sad asi paṅktirathābhidhānam || 5 ||
paraḥ pumān paṅktirathād athāsīt āsīmavāsīkṛtakīrtipūraḥ |
rāmābhidhaḥ somasamānadhāmā dhīmān asau dhīravarāgragaṇyaḥ || 6 ||
śriyaḥ patis svāvataraprayojanaṃ pratāpalaṅkeśvarasūdanātmakam |
gurūkticaryām iṣato ‘nuṣaṅgataḥ cakāra nūnaṃ kṛtinām iyaṃ vidhā || 7 ||
tataḥ kṛśo ‘bhūd arimastakāṅkuśaḥ kuśāvatīśaḥ kuśeśayāsanaḥ |
tato ‘tithir nāma samiddhavikramaḥ saparyayāvarjitanirjitātithiḥ || 8 ||
vaṃśe raghūṇām udapādi rājā bhūjānirādityasamiddhatejāḥ |
rāmākṛtiś śrīkapilendranāmā sīmātiśāyīśvaratulyadhāmā || 9 ||
anastamitabhānumān anṛtaśūnyadharmātmajo
viśokaraghunāyako vigatalāñchanaś candramāḥ |
aśoṣaṇamahārṇavas satatadānarādhāsutaḥ
pratāpakapileśvaro gajapatiḥ kathaṃ varṇyate || 10 ||
saujanyasya khanī vanīpakavanīdainyasya vicchedinī
saubhāgyasya dhunī dhūnī ca yaśasāṃ dharmeṇa tatsādhinī |
saundaryasya jharī sarīsṛpadarī vidveṣiṇāṃ cāturī
māhātmyaikamahāmaheśvaramahī jāgarti jāgeśvarī || 11 ||
[6]        jambhadviṭkumbhikumbhadvayasacivaśacīcāruvakṣojakumbha-
vyaktavyaktānuṣaktaprasṛmaramasṛṇakṣodakarpūragauraḥ |
yat kīrteḥ kārtikīnduprasahasanarasikaprauḍhakarṇāṭakāntā
kāntākāntānanendusphuritaratikalāhāsabhāsāṃ vilāsaḥ || 12 ||
coladrāviḍanepālapāṇḍyamālavakeralāḥ |
kapilendranṛpe kruddhe yuddhe karṇāṭakīrtayaḥ || 13 ||
sā pārvatī nāma yathārthanāmnī yāvāp sāptnyamilāram ābhyām |
karaṃ gṛhītvā kapileśvarasya sā gām asāpatnyavatīm atānīt || 14 ||
sā pārvatī sakalagaṇyaguṇātirekaṃ bhūpālamaulimaṇirañjitapādapīṭham |
tasmād asūta puruṣottamanāmadheyaṃ vismāritāridharaṇīśvarabhāgadheyam || 15 ||
vīraśrīpuruṣottamo gajapatiḥ vidvatsabhābhyantare
śeṣo vikramiṇāṃ kathāsu nitarāṃ śeṣāhibhūṣo vibhuḥ |
śatrūṇāṃ gaṇane ‘hikaṅkaṇasakhaḥ saundaryasīmā svayaṃ
tatputro ‘yam iti pragalbhavacasām utprekṣaṇīyo ‘nvaham || 16 ||
[7]        rūpāmbikā rūpitaramyaśīlā bhūpālamaileḥ puruṣottamasya |
kalatram āsīt kamalāyatākṣī samomayā sā ramayā ca vācā || 17 ||
japais tapobhis svayam ārjitair asāv asūta sāvitrakulādhipāgraṇīm |
surūpayā sāmajayānayānayā nayādhikaś śrīvaravīrabhūpatiḥ || 18 ||
vīraśrīrudradevo gajapatir abhavat tatpratāpāgir uccair
uccair mattebhkarṇavyajanapavanasaṃvījanena pravṛddhaḥ |
haimaṃ brahmāṇḍabhāṇḍaṃ vilayam arivadhūhematāṭaṅkahārī
neṣyaty evaṃ vicintya vyadhita jalamayīṃ prāvṛṣaṃ tatra vedhāḥ || 19 ||
śrīvīrarudragajarājyaśassamudo lokāṃś caturdaśa samaśnuta eva nūnam |
nocet taraṇyuḍupasañcaraṇaṃ kathaṃ vā naktaṃ divaṃ navirataṃ nabhasaḥ pradeśe ||20||
udgacchaty uḍunāyake jalanidheḥ vārāśisampūrṇatāṃ
pratyemi prakaṭaṃ pratāpagajarāḍ vidveṣi vāmabhruvām |
netrāmbusravaṇapravāhataṭinīsaṅgena nūnaṃ na cet
meghair āvṛtapāthaso ‘sya lavaṇāmbhodheḥ kathaṃ pūrṇatā || 21 ||
[8]        karṇo ‘yaṃ yadi vāsarārdham abhavat tattyāgalīlā na cet
sūryo ‘yaṃ yadi vāsaraṃ samabhavat tathyaṃ pratāpākṛteḥ |
candro ‘yaṃ yadi rātrimātram abhavat tatkīrtikelīnuteḥ
kiṃ kurmaḥ puruṣāyuṣaiḥ kavinutaś śrīvarirudro nṛpaḥ || 23 ||
śrīvīrarudranṛpater jayati pratāpavahniḥ parītya jaladhīn baḍabāgnidambhān |
tadvairiṇo jalanidhīn abhicakravālaṃ gantṝn nirddhu ................... || 24 ||
kalpāntānalpakalpajvaladanalaparīhāsasabrahmacārī
vairīśaprauḍhanārīvilapanalapanasthemasīmāsamānaḥ |
garvākharvor varādhīśvaragalagilanaprasphuradbāhudaṇḍaiḥ
rathyāḍillīkalambākaluṣagajapatiṣv aśvapatyuktir ekā || 25 ||
pratāparudraprathitapratāpabhānus triloke ‘pi tulāvihīnaḥ |
citrārdhakasvāti viśākhagatyā tulāṃ samārohati tigmabhānoḥ |
kāntānāṃ kāmadevaḥ kulagiriravaneḥ kāmadhenukavīnāṃ || 26 ||
prahvānāṃ pārijātaḥ prapadakamalayoḥ paṅkajākṣaḥ prajānām |
dīnāndhānāṃ nidhānaṃ dinakarakiraṇodveṣitāmisrarāśeḥ |
soyaṃ śrī vīrarudro gajapatiratirobhūtabhūteśamūrtiḥ || 28 ||
[9]        api ca — śrīvīrarudro gajaparirairāvaṇa iva vṛṣārūḍhaḥ, puṇḍarīka iva kapilāyattadarśanāsaktaḥ, vāmana iva piṅgalānusaraṇacchandassphuraṇaḥ, kumuda iva anupamaiśvaryaviśrāntiḥ, añjana iva tāmraparṇīprabhūtavilāsavihāranipuṇaḥ, puṣapadanta iva sudatīkaraparicayānuraktaḥ,
api ca — sārvabhauma iva sārvabhaumaḥ, sapratīka iva supratīkaḥ, kumāra iva kumāraḥ, māra iva māraḥ, caturānana iva caturānanaḥ, viṣvaksena iva viṣaksenaḥ, rudra iva rudraḥ, kamalākara iva kamalākaro, nṛsiṃha iva nṛsiṃhaḥ, rāma iva rāmaḥ,
api ca — nṛsiṃha iva karajāhita varilakṣmakiḥ, dāśarathir iva jagadvananipuṇakarapallavaḥ, paraśurāma iva ekavīrākṛtidharo, himācala iva kulācalo, vārākara iva satvasamāviṣṭo, himāṃśur iva kumudollāsī, divākara iva samullasitatanuvibhāvo, nikhilaharidantarajegīyamānayaśaścandrikāprasaraṇaharaśiraścandrakarārdrīkaraṇo lokālokopabhogālokāyamānanijapratāpo dikkarisadṛśo dhikkṛtavākpativibhavaḥ |
api ca — puruhūta iva vṛddhaśravāś śrāvyaghanakīrtir api sahasranetrādṛto ‘pi viṣṇur api na duścyavanaḥ kintvati susthitaḥ, vītihotra iva jātavedarasphuraṇo ‘pi jagatprāṇasakhaḥ, śucir api nāśrayāśaḥ kiṃ tu āśritasaṃrakṣakaḥ, dharmarāja iva samavarty api daṇḍadharo ‘pi vaivasvato ‘pi na pitṛpatiḥ api tu pitṛsevārataḥ nikhilabhūmaṇḍalādhīśvaraḥ, varuṇa iva pracetā api bhuvanādhīśvaro ‘pi [10] navyapāśo ‘pi nodakāsaktaḥ kiṃ tu niṣkalmaṣajanānuraktaḥ (?), śvasana iva anilākṛtir api purāṇanetṛvibhavo ‘pi sadāgatir api na bhujaṅgasahitaḥ kiṃ tu duṣṭajanavinetā, kinnareśvara iva rājādhirājo ‘pi dhanado ‘pi padāsaktavarakacchalo ‘pi na kuberaḥ kiṃ tu siṃhasaṃhananaḥ, śumbhur iva mahāgajādhīśvaro ‘pi sarvajño ‘pi na khaṇḍaparaśuḥ kiṃ tu apratihataśastrakalāpaḥ |
yasya khalu ripurājakuñjarā vindhyāṭavīmadhyamadhyāsīnāḥ siṃhaśaraśārdūlagaṇḍakādisamālokane ‘pi na bibhyati kiṃ tu patākinīpatākāṅkāyitaśarabha-śārdūlagaṇḍabheruṇḍādismaraṇajanitaraṇajanitatadbhrāntimanto bhayavihralatayā gahvarāntareṣu nilīyante, sa hi vicitram vīrarudro ‘pi padmāśliṣṭatanuḥ, samāśritajanaharicandano ‘pi dāsīkṛtaśrīcandanaḥ, svayaṃ vidyādharasadṛśo ‘pi kiṅkarīkṛtavidhyādharaḥ, svayaṃ puruṣottamo ‘pi puruṣottamatanayaḥ, svayaṃ puruṣottamatanayo ‘pi puruṣottamajanakaḥ, svayaṃ puruṣottamajanako ‘pi puruṣottamapriyasevakaḥ,
api ca — trinetrakumārasadṛśo ‘pi atrinetrakumārasadṛśaḥ, nalapratimo ‘py anala-pratimaḥ, laṅkārātisubhago ‘py alaṅkārātisubhagaḥ, dharāsaktānurāgo ‘py adharāsaktānurāgaḥ, balāyattacitto ‘py abalāyattavittaḥ, vanīpakarakṣaṇo ‘py avanapikarakṣaṇaḥ |
api ca — vaktā vaktṝṇām, śrotā śrotṝṇām, prabandhā prabandhṝṇām, netā sanmārgasya, vinetāpy asnmāṛgavartinām, praṇetākhyāyikānām, [11] abhinetā nāṭikānām, apanetā duṣkṛtānām, nirṇetā dharmasandehasya, pariṇetā padmāpdmālayelāmahilāmahilānām, adhinetā sakalorvīcakravālasya,
api ca pratirūpakaṃ vivasvataḥ, vīpsā manuvainyayoḥ, adhyāhāra ikṣvākoḥ, samasyāḥ dilīpasya, saṃvādo raghoḥ, paṇabandho raghukumārasya, pratinidhiḥ paṅktirathasya, vigrahāntaraṃ rāmabhadrasya, sakalajanatānandanajanārdano gauḍendramānamardano vijayasurabhisurabhitarasamadhikodāro vīrakedāraḥ sañcaraṇakulaśailabhramajakamadavāraṇadhikkṛtadikkuñjaraḥ, śaraṇāgatahuśanaśāhisuratrāṇavajrapañjaro, rājādhirājaparameśvaraḥ pratāparudro gajapatir nāma |
atra varṇapitṛvarṇyayor abhedaikaniyatayor api avasthābhedena bhedakathanam ekasyaiva kaveḥ kavisahṛdayatvavat na virudhyate | sa cāyaṃ vīrarudro gajapatir ayodhyām ivāyodhyām, madhurām iva madhurām, dvārāvatīm iva dvārāvatīm avantīm ivāvantīm, māyām iva māyām, vāraṇāsikām iva vāraṇāsikām, api ca kāśīm iva viśveśvarādhiṣṭhitām, ayodhyām iva ikṣvākusanmārgām, kāñcīm iva kāmakoṭipuṇyakoṭisametām, madhurām ivograsenāparipālitām, dvāravatīm ivānakadundubhighoṣasametām, mahānadījalamadhyamadhyāsīnām, amaranadīpratibimbam iva bhūkāntākaṭikaṅkaṭakanagarīm, padmāpadmālayelāmahilābhis samaṃ sammānayan pratidivasaniyatarājakaniyatavyavahārarūpadharmācaraṇam ācaran yathāvihitasabhāmaṇḍapāntare sabhyaprāḍvivākāmātyapurohitajyotirvidādi ******* vijñānayogi-bhāruci-aparārka-medhātithi-asahāya-candrikādi[12]bahugranthaikavākyatā-paryālocanavaśāyātatatkleśo mā bhūd iti sakalasmṛtisamuccayamatigambhīraṃ nātivisṛtaṃ prabandhaṃ prastauti —
atretthaṃ gamanikā —
amā nanu munīśvarā manu-vasiṣṭha-yogīśvarā
nibandhanakṛto ‘pi bhāruci-kulārka-yogīśvarāḥ |
gatā vigatamatsarā bhavatu vī bhavānīpatiḥ
sa tuṣyatutarāṃ mama smṛtinibandhavāgvaikharīm ||
hīne garvamaho naiva naiva garvamaho ‘dhike |
same tu garvaṃ śaṅkena na samasti samastu naḥ ||
iti vīraśrī-gajapatigauḍeśvara-navakoṭikarṇāṭakalubarigeśvara
śaraṇāgatayamunāpurādhīśvara-huśanasāhisuratrāṇaśaraṇarakṣaṇa
śrīdurgāvarputrapavitracaritrarājādhirājarājaparameśvara-
śrīpratāparudradeva-mahārājaviracite
smṛtisaṅgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
prabandhṛvaṃśavataraṇaṃ nāma
prathamo vilāsaḥ
[13]
atha dvitīyo vilāsaḥ
manusmṛtes tadaviruddhānām anyāsāṃ smṛtīnām itarāsām upasmṛtīnāṃ purāṇetihāsādīnāṃ vedamūlakatvena prāmāṇyam aṅgīkṛtaṃ nyāyavidbhiḥ | kvacid atra tāsāṃ smṛtīnāṃ nyāyamūlakatve ‘pi nyāyasya vedamūlakatvāt tatsmṛtīnām api vedatulyatvam iti kecit |
anye tv āhuḥ | nyāyānāṃ vedārthetikartavyatāvedakatvāt tat tat smṛtīnāṃ vedamūlakatvam iti | apare tu “vyavahārān nṛpaḥ paśyet” ity atra vidheyasya darśanasya katham aṃśapūrakatvena pramāṇakoṭiniveśāt prāmāṇyam ity āhuḥ | ataś ca tadukto dharmo ‘nuṣṭheyaḥ sarveṣāṃ varṇānāṃ tatpratipādyaś cādharmo nānuṣṭheya ity avivādam |
etac ca manvādisvarūpāparijñāne tu na śakyam iti tan nirūpyate —
dharmaśāstrakartāraḥ
manvaṅgirovyāsagautamātreyayamavasiṣṭhadakṣasaṃvartaśātātapaparāśara-viṣṇvāpastambahārītaśaṅkhakātyāyanagurupracetonāradayogīśvarabodhāyanapitāmaha-sumantukaśyapababhrupaiṭhīnasivyāghrapādasatyavratabharadvājagārgyakārṣṇajinijābāli-jamadagnilaugākṣivatsamarīcidevalapāraskaralikhitachāgaleyātibhiḥ praṇītāḥ smṛtayaḥ | jābānāciketaskandalaugākṣikaśypavyāsasanatkumāraśaṃtanujanakavyāghrakātyāyana-jātūkarṇikapiñjalabodhāyanakaṇādaviśvāmitra (paiṭhīnasigobhila)praṇītā upasmṛtayaḥ | jābālilaugākṣivyāsādayaḥ pūrvoktā na bhavanti |

purāṇāni
purāṇāni tu — brāhmapādmavaiṣṇavaśaivabhāgavatanāradīyamārkaṇḍeyāgneya-bhaviṣyatbrahmavaivartalaiṅgavārāhaskāndavāmanakaurmamātsyagāruḍabrahmāṇḍāni etāny aṣṭādaśapurāṇāni ||
itihāsanāmāni
itihāsāḥ — satyatapa upākhyānasomotpattiprabhṛtayaḥ | ata eva “mānavo dharmaḥ sāṅgo vedaḥ | purāṇaṃ cikitsitam ity etāni catvāry ājñāsiddhāni” ity uktaṃ viṣṇunā | mānavo dharma ity anena manusmṛtyaviruddhāḥ sarvāḥ smṛtayaś ca saṃgṛhītāḥ | purāṇaśabdaś cetihāsādīnām upalakṣakaḥ | evaṃ ca gṛhyakārapraṇītānām api prāmāṇyam aviruddham | teṣām api manvādipraṇītasaṃskārakalāpasyaiva mantraviniyogadvāreṇa prayojakatvāt |
“gṛhyakārāḥ svatantrataḥ tatprayuktaprayoktāraḥ” iti devalasmṛteḥ evaṃ sthite ṭīkākāraiḥ vijñanayogiprabhṛtibhiḥ nibandhanakāraiḥ kulārka-lakṣmīdharaprabhṛtibhiḥ lokānujighṛkṣayā smṛtivyākhyānavyājena sarvāḥ smṛtayo vyākhyātāḥ śiṣṭānugṛhītasarvānuṣṭhāpakapūrvanibandhṛ-nirmitanibandheṣu vidyamāneṣu |
ekena caritārthatvāt itarānarthatānayaḥ |
pūrvaprabandhair viṣayī bhaved iti mamodyamaḥ ||
madīyagranthe jāgrati pūrvanibandhṛnimittanibandheṣu anarthakyanayaviśrāntir iti | yad vā pūrvanibandheṣu niṣayaikye tadviparītalakṣaṇayā vijñānayogi-bhāruci-medhātithy-asahāya-aparārka-candrikākāra-kularka-lakṣmīpati-prabhṛtibhiḥ prabandhṛbhiḥ nirmiteṣu prabandheṣu [15] pratigṛhaṃ vidyamāneṣu pratyekaṃ smārtakarmānuṣṭhāpakeṣu jāgratsu ekena caritārthatvād itarānarthakyam iti nyāyo nātrāvataratīti vaktavyam | tathaivāsmadgranthe na prasaraty evetarānarthakyanyāya iti mamodyama iti kārikārthaḥ ||
vyavahārasya ācāropajīvyatvam
atra vyavahārasya ācāropajīvyatvaṃ vyavahāradarśananibandhanatvād ācārasya | tathā hi vyavahāradarśanād dhi duṣṭanigrahaḥ | duṣṭanigrahe sati varṇāśramadharmāvasthitiḥ | tadavasthitau satyām eva tadvihitadharmānuṣṭhānam ity ācārasya vyavahāropajīvyatā |
prathamaṃ vyavahārakāṇḍaprāthamyam
tataś cācārakāṇḍād vyavahārakāṇḍaḥ prathamam ārabdhaḥ | yady api manvādismṛtiṣu ācārakāṇḍaḥ pūrvaṃ pratipāditaḥ tadanantaraṃ vyavahārakāṇḍaḥ tadanantaraṃ prāyaścittakāṇḍa ity evaṃ krameṇa nirūpitam | asmin smṛtisaṃgraharūpe granthe tattatsmṛtyanusāreṇa taduktakrameṇa ācārakāṇḍanirūpaṇānantaraṃ vyavahārakāṇḍanirūpaṇaṃ yuktam | tathāpi vīrarudragajapatimahārājasyākāṅkṣānusāreṇa prathamaṃ vyavahārakāṇḍaḥ prakramyate | tatra vyavahāradarśane rājña evādhikāraḥ | varṇāśramadharmanirvāhakasya prajāparipālanasya duṣṭanigrahaśīlasya rājaikaniyatadharmatvāt | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
yo daṇḍyān daṇḍayed rājā samyak daṇḍyāṃś ca ghātayet |
iṣṭaṃ syāt kratubhis tena samāptavaradakṣiṇaiḥ ||
[16]        daṇḍaḥ dhanadaṇḍaḥ śārīradaṇḍaś ca vadhas spaṣṭaḥ | tena rājñā bhūridakṣiṇaiḥ kratubhir iṣṭaṃ syāt | bahudakṣiṇakratuphalaṃ sa rājā prāpnotīty arthaḥ | na ca yacchabdasya vidhyupaghātakatve niścite yacchabdo yadāgneyavan neya iti purastāt svatvanirūpaṇāvasare nirūpyate na ca phalaśravaṇād daṇḍapraṇayanaṃ kāmyam iti mantavyam | akaraṇe pratyavāyaśravaṇāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
adaṇḍyān daṇḍayan rājā daṇḍyāṃś caivāpy adaṇḍayan |
ayaśo mahad āpnoti narakaṃ caiva gacchati ||
daṇḍavidhāne nṛpatyādīnāṃ prāyaścittam
vasiṣṭhena — daṇḍotsarge rājā ekarātram upavaset, trirātraṃ purohitaḥ, kṛcchram adaṇḍyadaṇḍane, purohitas trirātraṃ ceti | ataś ca duṣṭe samyag daṇḍaḥ prayoktavya ity uktam |
duṣṭaparijñāne vyavahāradarśanam antareṇa na siddhyati | tatparijñānāya rājñā vyavahāradarśanam ahar ahaḥ kartavyam |
ata eva viṣnuḥ — ‘prajāparipālanam eva rājñāṃ svadharmaḥ anyathā svadharmāt pracyaveta’ iti vyavahāradarśanaṃ prajāpālanārthatvāt saṃdhyāvandanāditulyam ity abhiprāyaḥ |
ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ —
iti saṃcintya nṛpatiḥ kratutulyaphalān pṛthak |
vyavahārān svayaṃ paśyet sabhyaiḥ parivṛto ‘nvaham || iti |
asyārthaḥ: ‘iti’ uktena prakāreṇa ‘iṣṭaṃ syāt kratubhiḥ’ ityādina pṛthag iti varṇādikramety arthaḥ | varṇādikrame tu sa evāha —
[17]
svadharmāc calitānāṃ kṛtyānusāreṇa daṇḍaḥ
kulāni jātīḥ śreṇīś ca gaṇān janapadāni ca |
svadharmāc calitān rājā vinīya sthāpayet pathi ||
kulāni brāhmaṇādīnām, jātayo mūrdhāvasiktādayaḥ, śreṇayaḥ tāmbūlikādīnām, gaṇaḥ pāṣaṇḍinām, janapadā nāpitakārukādayaḥ, vinīya daṇḍayitvā | ata eva bṛhaspatiḥ |
dharmaśāstrānusāreṇa sāmātyaḥ sapurohitaḥ |
vyavahārān nṛpaḥ paśyet prajāsaṃrakṣaṇāya ca ||
krodhalobhavihīnas tu satyavādī jitendriyaḥ || iti |
dharmaśāstrānusāreṇety anena krodhalobhavisarjanasya siddhatvāt krodhalobhavisarjanaṃ rājñaḥ svabhāvaguṇa iti pradarśitam | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ |
mahotsāhaḥ sthūlalakṣaḥ kṛtajño vṛddhasevakaḥ |
vinītaḥ satvasaṃpannaḥ kulīnaḥ satyavāk śuciḥ ||
adīrghasūtraḥ smṛtimān nakṣudro ‘paruṣas tathā |
dhārmiko ‘vyasanaś caiva prājñaḥ śūro rahasyavit ||
svarandhragoptānvīkṣikyāṃ daṇḍanītyāṃ tathaiva ca |
viditas tv atha vārtāyāṃ trayyāṃ caiva narādhipaḥ || iti |
utsāhaḥ pauruṣaṃ mahān utsāho yasya sa mahotsāhaḥ | sthūlalakṣaḥ sthūlam anekadravyaṃ dadātīti sthūlalakṣo bahupradaḥ, kṛtajñaḥ kṛtam upakāram apakāraṃ vā jānāti smaratīti kṛtajñaḥ | vṛddhānāṃ [18] sevako vṛddhasevakaḥ | vinīto garvarahitaḥ | satvasaṃpannaḥ paraiḥ durdharṣaḥ | kulīno mahākule jātaḥ | satyavāk prāṇātyaye ‘pi satyaṃ vadatīti satyavāk | śucir bāhyābhyantaraśaucavān | adīrghasūtraḥ śīghrkārī | smṛtimān adhītavedadhāraṇaśīlaḥ | akṣudro gambhīrāśayaḥ | aparuṣaḥ parodoṣānāviṣkaraṇaśīlaḥ | dhārmikaḥ nityanaimittikakarmānuṣṭhānaparaḥ | varṇāśramadharmānvitaḥ it vijñāneśvaraḥ | avyasanaḥ aṣṭādaśavyasanaśūnyaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
mṛgayākṣo divāsvāpah parivādaḥ striyo madaḥ |
tauryatrikaṃ vṛthā tyāgaḥ kāmajo daśako gaṇaḥ ||
mṛgayādīnāṃ kāmamūlakatvāt kāmajaṃ gaṇadaśakam iti vākyārthaḥ | krodhajaṃ gaṇāṣtakam ity āha sa eva |
paiśunyaṃ sāhasaṃ droham īṣvaryāsūyārthadūṣaṇam |
vāgdaṇḍajaṃ ca pāruṣyaṃ krodhajo ‘pi gaṇāṣṭakaḥ || iti |
aṣṭasaṅkhyāsaṅkhyātaḥ | etasmin vargadvaye kāmamūlakānāṃ daśānāṃ pānākṣamṛgayāḥ kaṣṭatamāḥ ity āha sa eva |
pānam akṣāḥ striyaś caiva mṛgayā ca yathākramam |
etat kaṣṭatamaṃ vidyāc catuṣkaṃ kāmaje gaṇe ||
atra yathākramam iti kāmakrodhāvalambanenaiva kramavivakṣā na tu pānākṣastrīmṛgayāṇāṃ parasparavaiṣamyavivakṣayā kramapadaprayogaḥ | teṣāṃ pāṇākṣastrīmṛgayārūpāṇāṃ kaṣṭatamatvenaiva tulyatayā [19] catuṣkapadena jñāpitatvāt | krodhaje gaṇe trayāṇāṃ kaṣṭataratvam āha sa eva |
daṇḍasya pālanaṃ caiva vākpāruṣyārthadūṣaṇe |
krodhaje gaṇe trayāṇāṃ kaṣṭatamatvam āha sa eva |
krodhaje ‘pi gaṇe vindyāt kaṣṭam etat trikaṃ sadā || iti |
prājñaḥ śāstrārthasūkṣmadarśī gambhīrārthāvadhāraṇakṣama iti vijñāneśvaraḥ, śūraḥ nirbhayaḥ, rahasyavit gopanīyārthavedanaśīlaḥ | pararahasyavedanaśīla iti bhāruciḥ | svarandhragoptā parapraveśadvārarakṣakaḥ | ānvīkṣikyām ātmavidyāyām, daṇḍanītyām arthayogakṣemopayoginyām, vārtāyāṃ kṛṣivāṇijyapaśupālyāyāṃ dhanopacayahetubhūtāyām, trayyāṃ ṛgyajuḥsāmākhyāyāṃ ca viditaḥ prasiddhaḥ | vinīta iti pāṭhaṃ kṛtvā vyācaṣṭe vijñāneśvaraḥ | viśeṣeṇa prāvīṇyam api nīta iti tattadabhijñair iti śeṣaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
traividyebhyas trayīṃ vidyāt daṇḍanītiṃ a tadvidaḥ |
ānvīkṣikīṃ cātmavidaḥ vārtārambhāṃś ca lokataḥ || iti |
tatparijñānaṃ kimartham iti apekṣite hārītaḥ |
ānvīkṣikyāṃ tu vijñānaṃ dharmādharmau trayīsthitau |
arthānarthau tu vārtāyāṃ daṇḍanītyāṃ nayānayau || iti |
ānvīkṣikyādiṣu vijñānādikaṃ jñātavyam ity arthaḥ narādhipaḥ abhiṣiktaḥ syād iti sarvatra saṃbandhaḥ | atra abhiṣekayuktasya nṛpater antaraṅgadharmakathanāvasare prāsaṅgikaṃ tatraiva bahiraṅganirūpaṇaṃ [20] kriyate | tatra kātyāyanaḥ |
rājā purohitaṃ kuryād uditaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ hitam |
kṛtādhyayanasampannam alubdhaṃ satyavādinam || iti |
atraikatvaṃ vivakṣitaṃ yūpaṃ chinattītivad utpādyatvāt | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
purohitaṃ ca kurvīta daivajñam uditoditam |
daṇḍanītyāṃ tu kuśalam atharvāṅgirase tathā ||
uditoditam iti — uditair vidyābhijananānuṣṭhānādibhir uditaṃ samṛddham iti vijñāneśvaraḥ | bhārucis tu – udite rājñaḥ paṭṭabandhasamaye uditaḥ sampādyaḥ | tathā ca hārītaḥ |
paṭṭabandhodaye kārya eka eva purohitaḥ |
tenaiva kāryāḥ sarvās tu rājñas tasyaurdhvadaihikāḥ ||
alpāyuṣṭvāt kaliyuge tathā kuryāt dvikaṃ sadā || iti |
atra aurdhvadaihikagrahaṇaṃ sarvakriyopalakṣaṇārtham | aihikakriyā rājñas tv aśaktyā purohitenaiva kāryāḥ | tadā paṭṭabandhasamaya eva dvikaṃ dvau kuryāt | ekaḥ prādhānyena, aparas tūpasarjanatayā, pradhānasya nāśe upasarjanasya prādhānyam | tasyopasarjanapurohitasya prādhānyasvīkārasamaye upasarjanaṃ purohitāntaraṃ sampādyam | ‘alpāyuṣṭvāt kaliyuge’ iti nyāysyātrāpi samānatvāt | [21] yady apy atra saṅkhyāvivakṣitā | tathāpy upasarjanasya tatraivāntarbhāvād eka eva purohita iti anena dvitvasaṅkhyā na virudhyate | atharvāṅgirase ceti śāntyādikarmasu kuśalam ity arthaḥ śeṣam atirohitārtham | eka eva purohita iti niyamaḥ | purohitāntaranivṛttiparaḥ na tu jyotirvinnivṛttiparaḥ | purohitatvāj jyotirvidaḥ pṛthaksampādyatvāt | ata eva viṣṇuḥ: ‘jyotirvitpurohitau kāryau’ iti |
tathā ca rājalāsake: “jyotirvitpurohitamantriṇaḥ kāryāḥ” iti |
mantriṇa iti mantraṇaṃ rājakāryavicāraḥ | yady api yājñavalkyenoktam |
purohitaṃ ca kurvīta daivajñam uditoditam |
iti, purohita eva daivajñaś cet daivajñāntaraṃ na kartavyam | ityevaṃparam iti kecid vyācakṣate |
“purohitaṃ ca kurvīta daivajñaṃ ca kurvīta” iti tau kadetyākāṅkṣāyām āha “uditoditam” iti | uditam udayaṃ bhāve niṣṭhā udayasamayāt uditam iti pañcamīsamāsaḥ | udayāt paṭṭabandhāt pūrvam eva jyotirvdvaraṇam | paṭṭabandhanānantaraṃ purohitavaraṇam iti | ata evoktaṃ rājalāsake: “vṛtopadiṣṭamuhūrte ‘bhiṣekaṃ kuryāt” iti | vṛtena jyotirvidā upadiṣṭa iti vṛtopadiṣṭaḥ |
[22]        yat tūktaṃ viṣṇunā: “ātharvaṇikaṃ paurāṇikaṃ dharmaśāstrakuśalaṃ purohitaṃ vṛtvā tato jyotirvidaṃ vṛṇuyāt” iti | tat tu jyotirvidvaraṇāvaśyakatvapratipādanaparam | varaṇasya pūrvāparabhāvapratipādanaṃ ktvāpratyayasya samānakartṛkatvamātra eva vihitatvena na pūrvakālanibandhantvāt samānakartṛkatvamātraniyatatvaṃ mukhaṃ vyādāya svapitītyādau dṛṣṭam | etac ca samānakartṛkayoḥ pūrvakāla ity atra hariṇā prapañcitam | ataś ca jyotirvitkartavyatāpratipādanaparam iti yuktam utpaśyāmaḥ | tataś ca rājalāsakoktam: “vṛtopadiṣṭamuhūrte ‘bhiṣekaṃ kuryāt” iti samañjasaṃ syāt | ata eva rājakāryavicāre,
taiḥ sārdhaṃ cintayed rājyaṃ vipreṇātha svayaṃ tataḥ |
iti yājñavalkyavacane viprapadaṃ jyotirvitparam |
mantripurohitādivaraṇam
rājakāryakuśalamantrivicārānantaraṃ daivajñavicāra eva yujyate | na tu purohitavicāraḥ | vijñānayoginā tu viprapadaṃ purohitaparam ity uktam | yadi purohita eva jyotirvit tatparam ity anusaṃdheyam | tathā ca mantriṇo ‘pi kāryā ity āha viṣṇuḥ: “maulān arthavido mantriṇaḥ kurvīta svarāṣṭrasya guptaye” iti maulyān svavaṃśaparamparāyātān, arthavidaḥ dhanārjanopāyavidaś ca eteṣām abhāve rājyasya guptir na vidyata ity avaśyaṃ saṃpādyā ity arthaḥ | [23] maulyān iti bahuvacanaṃ sapta vāṣṭau vā kāryā mantriṇa iti | tathā ca manuḥ |
maulyān śāstravidaḥ śūrān labdharakṣān kulodbhavān |
sacivān sapta vāṣṭau vā kurvīta suparīkṣitān || iti |
atra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
sa mantriṇaḥ prakurvīta maulān prājñān śucīn sthirān |
taiḥ sārdhaṃ cintayed rājyaṃ vipreṇātha tataḥ svayam || iti |
sa rājā mahotsāsādiguṇayuktaḥ prājñaḥ hitāhitavivekakuśalaḥ, maulyā vyākhyātāḥ, sthirāḥ mahaty api harṣaviṣādasthāne vikārarahitā iti vijñāneśvaraḥ | bhārucis tu sthairyaṃ nāma mitrabhede ‘pi svabhāvato ‘vasthānaṃ svāmihitaiṣaṇam ity arthaḥ | śucayo niṣkalaṅkāḥ evaṃ mantribhiḥ sārdhaṃ rājā rājyaṃ saṃdhivigrahādikāryaṃ cintayet | tataḥ sarvaśāstrārthakuśalena jyotirvidā brāhmaṇena saha kāryaṃ saṃcintya tataḥ svayaṃ svabuddhyā cintayet | tatra viśeṣam āha viṣṇuḥ: “catvāra ṛtvijo vṛṇuyāt sadasyaṃ pañcamaṃ śrautasmārtakriyāsiddaye” iti | catvāra ṛtvija iti brahmā hotā adhvaryur iti kecit | kecid brahmādhvaryurhotodgāteti mahartvijaś catvāra ity āhuḥ | śrautasmārtakriyānuṣṭhānasiddhyarthaṃ sadasyapañcamān ṛtvijo vṛṇuyād ity arthaḥ |
gautamas tu viśeṣam āha: “upadraṣṭāpi sampādyaḥ” iti | ata eva aviśeṣeṇoktaṃ yājñavalkyena |
[24]        śrautasmārtakriyāhetor vṛṇuyād eva cartvijaḥ |
yajñāṃś caiva prakurvīta vidhivad bhūridakṣiṇān ||
yajñān agniṣṭomādīn cakārād rājasūyādīn | tadanantaram āha vasiṣṭhaḥ:
yogakṣemalakṣaṇam
“yogakṣemārthayor yatnaḥ kartavyaḥ” iti |
asyārthaḥ – alabdhasya lobho yogaḥ | labdhasya paripālanaṃ paripālanaṃ kṣemaḥ ubhayam api dharmānusāreṇaiva | ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
alabdham īhed dharmeṇa labdhaṃ yatnena pālayet |
pālitaṃ vardhayen nītyā vṛddhaṃ pātreṣu nikṣipet || iti
alabdhalābhāya dharmāvirodhena yatnaḥ kartavyaḥ | labdhaṃ tu svayam apekṣayā rakṣet | nītiḥ vaṇikpathādikā | vṛddhaṃ pātreṣu triṣū dharmārthakāmeṣu niṣipet | atra pātreṣv iti bahuvacanaṃ tritva eva paryavasitam | kapiñjalādhikaraṇanyāyeneti bhāruciḥ | etad bhārucivyākhyānaṃ pātratraividhyapratipādanaparam, na tu pātrasaṅkocapratipādanaparam | ata evoktaṃ hārītena: “āyavyayau prajñāya” iti | “pālitaṃ vardhayen nītyā” iti yājñavalkīyavacanasamānārthaṃ veditavyam | āyavyayaparijñānānantaram āyavyayayoḥ sāmye vyavasthādhikye vā dātum anucitatvāt kośābhāve rājñaḥ svarāṣṭrasya sthāpanaṃ pararāṣṭrasya sādhanaṃ duḥśakam iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt | yat tu viṣṇunoktam: [25] “yogakṣemasādhitaṃ dravyaṃ brāhmaṇebhyo ‘tithibhyaḥ satribhyaḥ iṣṭāpūrtārthaṃ kuśīlavebhyo nartakādibhyo baline rājñe karaṃ saudāyikaṃ pāritoṣikaṃ dadyāt” iti |
yogakṣemau vyākhyātau | brāhmaṇebhyaḥ pātrabhūtebhyaḥ tulāpuruṣādidānarūpeṇa deyam | atithibhyaḥ pañcamahāyajñāvasāne svayam āgatebhyaḥ, satribhyaḥ yajñadakṣiṇārtham āgatebhyaḥ, etat satripadaṃ dharmajanakakriyāsādhanārthaṃ dravyayācakānām upalakṣakam | ataś ca taṭākādinirmātṝṇāṃ yat kiṃcid deyam | iṣṭāpūrtārtham | iṣṭaṃ yāgaḥ, pūrtaṃ taṭākādi | yāgaśabdena yāgadakṣiṇā | pūrtaśabdena pūrtanirmātṛbhṛtir upalakṣyate | etad dvayaṃ svakṛtam ity avagantavyam | anyathā satripadapaunaruktyāt | ayaṃ ca dharmamūlavyayaprakāraḥ | balinṛ rājñe karam iti arthapuruṣārthamūlavyayaḥ | śeṣas tu kāmapuruṣārthamūlavyayaḥ | kṛṣikāṇāṃ bhṛtiḥ trivargasādhāraṇavyayaḥ | saudāyikaṃ strīdhanaṃ pāritoṣikaṃ strīṇāṃ toṣavaśād dattam | tac ca pāritoṣikapadaṃ sāmānyataḥ paritoṣamūlakadāne viśeṣaniṣṭhatāṃ vihāya pravartate kvacit | yathā: “rājā bhaṭānāṃ pāritoṣikaṃ datte” iti | tad api āyavyayapariśīlanānantaraṃ yathāśakti deyam ity evaṃparam | āyavyayapariśīlanānantaraṃ yathāśakti kiṃ kartavyam ity apekṣite yājñavalkyaḥ |
[26]        bhogāṃś ca dadyād viprebhyo vasūni vividhāni ca |
akṣayo ‘yaṃ nidhī rājñāṃ yad vipreṣūpapaditam ||
brāhmaṇebhyaḥ pātrabhūtebhyaḥ bhogān sukhāni tatsādhanadvāreṇa dadyāt | sukhasādhanāni dadyād ity arthaḥ | vasūni suvarṇarūpyaprabhṛtīni nānāprakārāṇi dadyāt | śeṣaṃ vyaktārtham |
sādhāraṇadharmatvena dānavidhau prāpte rājñāṃ dhanam āvaśyakam iti punar vacanaṃ yājñavalkyasya | sa ca tad eva prastauti |
askannam avyayaṃ caiva prāyaścittair adūṣitam |
agneḥ sakāśād viprāgnau hutaṃ śreṣṭham ihocyate ||
asyārthaḥ – agneḥ sakāśād agnisādhyād bhūridakṣiṇāt rājasūyāśvamedhāder api viprāgner hutaṃ śreṣṭam ucyate | yata etad askannaṃ kṣayarahitaṃ avyathaṃ paśuhiṃsādirahitaṃ prāyaścittādirahitaṃ ca yad brāhmaṇebhyo dīyate | tathā ca manuḥ |
nṛpāṇām akṣayo hy eṣa nidhir brāhme nidhīyate |
na taṃ stenā na cāmitrā haranti na ca naśyati ||
tasmād rājñā nidhātavyo brāhmaṇeṣv akṣayo nidhiḥ |
na skandati na vyathate na vinaśyati karhicit |
variṣṭham agnihotrebhyo brāhmaṇasya mukhe hutam || iti |
tatra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
datvā bhūmiṃ nibandhaṃ vā kṛtvā lekhyaṃ tu kārayet |
āgāmibhadranṛpatiparijñānāya pārthivaḥ || iti |
[27] nibandho vāṇijyādhikāribhiḥ prativarṣaṃ pratimāsaṃ vā svavāṇijyāt dravyāt yat kiṃcid dhanam asmai brāhmaṇāya asyai devatāyai vā deyam iti prabhusamaye labhyo ‘rthaḥ |
yady apy atra dhanadātṛtvaṃ vāṇijyādhikāriṇām eva, tathāpi nibandhakartur eva puṇyaṃ taduddeśenaivetarasya pravṛtteḥ | bhūmiṃ datvety atra bhūmiśabdaḥ ārāmādīnām upalakṣakaḥ | ata eva bṛhaspatiḥ |
śāsanaprakaraṇam
datvā bhūmyādikaṃ rājā tāmrapaṭṭe ‘tha vā paṭe |
śāsanaṃ kārayed dharmyaṃ sthānavaṃśādisaṃyutam ||
atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
abhilekhyātmano vaṃśyān ātmānaṃ ca mahīpatiḥ |
pratigrahaparīmāṇaṃ dānacchedopavarṇanam ||
svahastakālasampannaṃ śāsanaṃ kārayet sthiram |
paṭṭe vā tāmrapaṭṭe vā svamudroparicihnitam ||
etac ca lekhyaprakaraṇe pratipādyate |
durgam
idānīṃ rājño nivāsasthānam āha |
jāṅgalaṃ tu prakurvīta durgaṃ kośasya guptaye |
jāṅgalaṃ jāṅgaladeśasthitaṃ na tu durgaṃ jāṅgalam |
yad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
ramyaṃ paśavyam ājīvyaṃ jāṅgalaṃ deśam āviśet |
tatra durgāṇi kurvīta janakośāptaguptaye ||
[28] ramyam upavanādibhiḥ, paśavyaṃ paśubhyo hitam, ājīvyaṃ kandamūlaphalapuṣpādibhiḥ | yady apy alpodakatarupallavadeśo jāṅgalaḥ tathāpy atra samṛddhajalataruparvatadeśo jāṅgala iti vijñāneśvaraḥ |
durgaṃ ṣaḍvidham,
dhanvadurgaṃ mahīdurgam abdurgaṃ vārkṣam eva ca |
nṛdurgaṃ giridurgaṃ ca samāśritya vaset puram ||
iti manusmaraṇāt | atra viśeṣaḥ smṛtyantare |
dharmakṛtyeṣu dharmajñān arthakṛtyeṣu paṇḍitān |
strīṣu klībān niyuñjīta nīcān nīceṣu karmasu || iti |
anenaivābhiprāyeṇoktam yājñavalkyena |
tatra tatra ca niṣṇātān adhyakṣān kuśalān śucīn |
prakuryād āyakarmāntavyayakarmasu coditān || iti |
tatra dharmādisthāneṣv iti śeṣaḥ | asyārthaḥ spaṣṭīkṛto vṛddhaiḥ |
prājñatvam upadhāśuddhir apramādo ‘bhiyuktatā |
kāryeṣu vyasanābhāvaḥ svāmibhiktiś ca yogyatā || iti |
rājñāṃ pratāpārjitasya dāne phalādhikyam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
nātaḥ parataro dharmo nṛpāṇāṃ yat raṇārjitam |
viprebhyo dīyate dravyaṃ prajābhyaś cābhayaṃ sadā || iti |
raṇārjitadravyābhāve ‘pi taddravyādānāt tatra maraṇam eva śreya ity āha sa eva |
[29]        ya āhaveṣu yudhyante bhūmyartham aparāṅmukhāḥ |
akūṭair āyudhair yānti te svargaṃ yogino yathā ||
padāni kratutulyāni bhagneṣv avinivartinām |
rājā sukṛtam ādatte hatānāṃ vipalāyinām ||
tavāhaṃvādinaṃ klībaṃ nirhetiṃ parasaṃgatam |
nihanyād vinivṛttaṃ ca vṛddhaprekṣaṇakārikam ||
tavāham iti yo vadati tvadīyo ‘ham iti vaded ity arthaḥ | śeṣaṃ sugamam | ādigrahaṇāt virathavisārathyādayo gṛhyante | yathāha gautamaḥ: “na doṣo hiṃsāyām āhave ‘nyatra vyaśvasārathyāyudhakṛtāñjaliprakīrṇakeśaparāṅmukhopaviṣṭasthalvṛkṣārūḍhadūtadobrāhmaṇa-vādibhyaḥ” iti | śaṅkho ‘pi viśeṣam āha: “na pānīyaṃ pibantaṃ na bhuñjānaṃ nopānahau muñcantaṃ nāpavarmāṇaṃ nāktareṇuṃ na striyaṃ na vājinaṃ na rājānaṃ hanyāt” iti | viṣṇus tu: “dīkṣitaṃ kāntāparivṛtaṃ kāntāveṣaṃ kṛtāñjaliṃ prapalāyitaṃ tyaktāstraṃ na hanyāt” iti |
manus tu viśeṣm āha |
na kūṭair āyudhair hanyād yudhyamāno raṇe ripūn |
na karṇibhir nāpi digdhaiḥ nāgnijvālitatejanaiḥ ||
na ca hanyāt sthalārūḍhān na klībān na kṛtāñjalīn |
na muktakeśaṃ nāsīnaṃ na tavāsmīti vādinam ||
[30]        na suptaṃ na visannāhaṃ na nagnaṃ na nirāyudham |
nāyudhyamānaṃ paśyantaṃ na pareṇa samāgatam ||
nāyudhavyasanaṃ prāptaṃ nārtaṃ nātiparikṣatam |
na bhītaṃ na parāvṛttaṃ satāṃ dharmam anusmaran ||
vyavahāradarśanam
tatra viśeṣam āha viṣṇuḥ: “pratidinam āyavyayapariśīlanaṃ kartavyaṃ vyavahāradarśanavat” iti | anenābhiprāyeṇāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
kṛtarakṣaḥ samuttāya paśced āyavyayau svayam |
vyavahārān tato dṛṣṭvā snātvā bhuñjīta kāmataḥ || iti |
kṛtarakṣaḥ purasyātmanaś ca rakṣāṃ vidhāya, pratidinaṃ prātaḥkāla uttāya svayam evāyavyayau paśyet | tato vyavahārān dṛṣṭvā madhyāhnakāle kāmato yathāruci bhuñjīteti kramavivakṣām āha vijñāneśvaraḥ | atra kramo na vivakṣita iti bhāruciḥ | ataḥ prātaḥkāla eva snātvā bhuktvā vyavahāradarśanaṃ kartavyam iti | bhojanānantarakṛtyam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “bhāṇḍāgāranirīkṣaṇam, cāradūtapreṣaṇam, upāyanādisvīkāraḥ, mantravicāro mantribhiḥ” iti | upāyanaṃ sāmantabhūpālaiḥ preṣitaḥ karaḥ | cārā bāṇaveṣadhāriṇo gūḍhāḥ | dūtās tu prakāśāḥ yathāha yogīśvaraḥ |
[31]        hiraṇyaṃ vyāvṛttānītaṃ bhāṇḍāgāre vinikṣipet |
paśyec cārān tato dūtān preṣayen mantrisaṃgataḥ || iti |
atra vijñāneśvaraḥ: “cārās tu trividhāḥ – nisṛṣṭārthāḥ, saṃdiṣṭārthāḥ, śāsanahastāś ceti | tatra nisṛṣṭārthāḥ rājakāryāṇi deśakālocitāni svayam eva kathayituṃ kṣamāḥ | saṃdiṣṭārthās tu uktamātraṃ parasmai nivedayanti | śāsanahastās tu rājalekhyahāriṇaḥ” ity āha[1] |
tadanantarakartavyam āha viṣṇuḥ: “aparāhṇe yatheccham antaḥpuravihārī syāt” iti | ata evāha manuḥ |
bhuktavān viharec caiva strībhir antaḥpure sadā |
viharec ca yathākāmaṃ punaḥ kāryāṇi cintayet || iti |
atra kena sahety ākāṅkṣāyām āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
tataḥ svairavihārī syān mantribhir vā samāhitaḥ |
balānāṃ darśanaṃ kṛtvā senānyā saha cintayet || iti |
tataḥ aparāhṇa ity arthaḥ | tataḥ kiṃ kuryād ity uktaṃ hārītena |
saṃdhyām upāsya śṛṇuyād antarveśvani śastrabhṛt |
rahasyasthāyināṃ caiva praṇidhīnāṃ ca ceṣṭitam || iti |
tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “sāyaṃkāle śastrapāṇiḥ kvacit sthāpitacārān āhūya rahasyaṃ śṛṇuyāt” iti | anenaivābhiprāyeṇoktaṃ yogīśvareṇa |
[32]        saṃdhyām upāsya śṛṇuyāc cārāṇāṃ gūḍhabhāṣitam |
nṛttagītaiś ca bhuñjīta paṭhet svādhyāyam eva ca || iti |
“saṃdhyopāsanasya sāmānyataḥ prāptasyāpi punarvacanaṃ kāryānukūltvād avismaraṇārtham” iti vijñāneśvaraḥ | bhārucis tu “kāryavyāsaṅge ‘pi śaktau satyāṃ na purohitakṛtyaṃ saṃdhyopāsanam iti jñāpanārtham” ity āha | tadanantaraṃ kiṃ kartavyam ity apekṣite yogīśvaraḥ |
saṃviśet tūryaghoṣeṇa prabudhyec ca tathaiva ca |
śāstrāṇi cintayed buddhyā sarvakartavyatāṃ tathā || iti |
ata evāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “brāhme muhūrte cotthāya tricaturaiḥ vidvadbhiḥ saha śāstrāṇi cintayet” iti | sarvakartavyatāṃ tu svayam eva cintayet | etat spaṣṭaviṣayam | yathāha hārītaḥ |
etad vṛttaṃ samātiṣṭhet svasthaḥ san pṛthivīpatiḥ |
asvasthaḥ sarvam etat tu mantrimukhye nivedayet || iti |
tadanantarakṛtyam āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau: “viśvāsinaś cārān samānayet tān anyatra preṣayet” iti | tadanantarakṛtyam āha viṣṇuḥ: “saṃdhyopāsanam agnihotrartvikpurohitāśīrvādasvīkāro jyotirviddarśanasambhāṣaṇarahasyakathanapūrvakaṃ svaśarīrasthitiṃ nivedya pratividhānaṃ cādiśya śrotriyebhyaḥ kanakatilādidānāni dadyāt” iti | [33] svaśarīranivedanaṃ vaidyānām | ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
preṣayec ca tataś cārān sveṣv anyeṣu ca sādarān |
ṛtvikpurohitācāryair āśīrbhir abhinanditaḥ ||
dṛṣṭvā jyotirvido vaidyān dadyād gāṃ kāñcanaṃ mahīm |
naiveśikāni ca tathā śrotriyāṇāṃ gṛhāṇi ca ||
brāhmaṇeṣu kṣamo snigdheṣv ajihmaḥ krodhano ‘riṣu |
syād rājā bhṛtyavarge ca prajāsu ca pitā yathā || iti |
brāhmaṇeṣv adhikṣipatsv iti śeṣaḥ |
rājñaḥ prajāparipālane phalaviśeṣam āha viṣṇuḥ:
ṣaḍbhāgahāritvam
“tapasaḥ ṣaḍbhāgaṃ gṛhṇāti prajārañjanāt rājā” iti |
asyārthaḥ – prajārañjanād eva rājaśabdavācyaḥ | na tu puṇyāt | ṣaḍbhāgam ādatte iti | at evāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “nyāyyo rājā ṣaḍbhāgahārī” iti | nyāyyo nyāyād anapetaḥ ṣaṣṭhaṃ bhāgaṃ tapasa iti śeṣaḥ |
rājñaḥ prajāparipālanaphalam
ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
puṇyāt ṣaḍbhāgam ādatte nyāyena paripālayan |
sarvadānādhikaṃ yasmāt prajānāṃ paripālanam || iti |
sarvadānebhyo ‘dhikaṃ tasmād avaśyaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | vijñāneśvaras tu “yasmād adhikaṃ phalaṃ tasmāt prajāsu yathā pitā tathaiva syād iti gatena saṃbandhaḥ” ity āha |
[34] atra viśeṣam āha hārītaḥ: “rāṣṭrādhikṛtavṛttaṃ tu cārair jñātvā prajāvanam” iti | prajāvanaṃ – prajārakṣaṇam | rājñā ye adhikṛtāḥ – adhikāriṇaḥ teṣāṃ vṛttaṃ cārair jñātvā duṣṭān ghātayet, sādhūn saṃmānayed iti | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “kāyasthebhyo viśeṣato rakṣed utkocajīvibhyaḥ” iti | etac ca vyākhyātaṃ saṃgrahakāreṇa “utkocajīvibhyaḥ kāyasthebhya ity anvaye kāyasthā yady utkocajīvinaḥ tadā tebhyaḥ avaśyaṃ saṃrakṣaṇaṃ kartavyam” iti | tatra kāyasthā – gaṇakā utkocajīvina eva | etac cotkocajīvibhyo ‘pi rakṣaṇaṃ pṛthag eva |
ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |}
ye rāṣṭrādhikṛtās teṣāṃ cārair jñātvā viceṣṭitam |
sādhūn saṃmānayed rājā viparītāṃs tu ghātayet ||
utkocajīvino dravyahīnān kṛtvā vivāsayet |
sadānamānasatkārān śrotriyān vāsayet tadā ||
cāṭataskaradurvṛttamahāsāhasikādibhiḥ |
pīḍyamānāḥ prajā rakṣet kāyasthaiś ca viśeṣataḥ || iti |
kāyasthānāṃ rājavallabhatayā atimāyāvitayā durnivāratvād ity arthaḥ | cāṭāḥ pratārakāḥ |
anyāyena kośapūraṇe rājño hāniḥ
anyāyena nṛpo rṣṭrāt svakośaṃ yo vivardhayet |
[35]        so ‘cirad vigataśrīko nāśam eti sabāndhavaḥ ||
prajāpīḍanasaṃtāpāt samudbhūto hutāśanaḥ |
rājñāṃ kulaṃ śriyaṃ prāṇān nādagdhvā vinivartate ||
ya eva nṛpater dharmaḥ svarāṣṭraparipālanāt |
tam eva kṛtsnam āpnoti pararāṣṭraṃ vaśaṃ nayan ||
“yasmin deśe ya ācāraḥ kulasthitivyavahāro vā yathaiva prāg āsīt tathaivāsau paripālanīyaḥ | yadi śāstraviruddho na bhavati” iti vijñāneśvaraḥ (on YDh 1.343) | “yadā vaśam upāgataḥ” ity anena vaśopanayanāt prāk aniyama iti darśitam | tathā ca pitāmahaḥ |
uparuddhyārim āsīta rāṣṭraṃ cāsyopapīḍayet |
dūṣayec cāsya satataṃ yavasānnodakaṃ samam || iti |
atra viśeṣam āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau: “āphalodayaṃ mantraguptiḥ” iti | āphalodayam – phalapṛaptiparyantam | mantro – rājakāryavicāraḥ | yad āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
mantramūlaṃ yato rājyaṃ tan mantraṃ ca surakṣitam |
kuryād yathāsya na viduḥ karmaṇām āphalodayam || iti |
asya rājñaḥ karmaṇāṃ saṃdhivigrahādīnām | kiṃ ca —
kṛttrimasahajaprākṛtaśatrumitrāṇi
arir mitram udāsīno ‘nantaras tatparaḥ paraḥ |
kramaśo maṇḍalaṃ cintyaṃ sāmādibhir upakramaiḥ ||
[36] arir dviṣan | mitraṃ sakhā | etadvilakṣaṇaḥ udāsīnaḥ – maitraśāstrvarahitaḥ | te ca trividhāḥ | sahajāḥ prākṛtāḥ kṛttrimāś ceti | sahajāḥ – sāpatnapitṛvyasutādayaḥ | kṛttrimāḥ – yasyāpakṛtaṃ yena vā apakṛtam | prākṛtas tadanantaradeśādhipatiḥ | sahajamitraṃ bhāgineyapaitṛṣvaseyamātṛṣvaseyādiḥ | kṛttrimam – yena vopakṛtaṃ yasya vā upakṛtam | prākṛtaṃ mitram – ekāntaritadeśādhipatiḥ | sahajakṛttrimamitralakṣaṇarahitau sahajakṛttrimodāsīnau | prākṛtodāsīno – dvyantradeśādhipatiḥ |
ariḥ punaś caturvidhaḥ – yātavyaḥ, chettavyaḥ pīḍanīyaḥ, karśanīyaś ceti | tatra yātavyo ‘nantarabhūmipatiḥ | vyasanī hīnabalo viraktaprakṛtiḥ vidurgo mitrahīno durbalaḥ chettavyaḥ | pīḍanīyo mantrabalahīnaḥ | prabalamantras tu karśanīyaḥ |
yathoktam |
nirmūlanāśa ucchedaḥ pīḍanaṃ balanigrahaḥ |
karśanaṃ tu punaḥ prāhuḥ kośadaṇḍopakarśanāt || iti |
bṛṃhaṇīyakarśanīyabhedena mitraṃ dvividham
mitraṃ ca dvividham | bṛṃhaṇīyaṃ karśanīyaṃ ceti | balakośahīnaṃ bṛṃhaṇīyam | “kośahīnaṃ bṛṃhaṇīyam, kośabalādhikaṃ karśanīyam” ity āha vijñāneśvaraḥ | etad rājamaṇḍalaṃ kramaśaḥ pūrvādikrameṇa cintyam | purataḥ pṛṣṭhataḥ pārśvataḥ trayastraya ātmā caika iti trayodaśarājakaṃ rājamaṇḍalam | pārṣṇigrāhādayas tu arimitrodāsīneṣv evāntarbhāvān na punar uktā dharmaśāstre |
[37]        uśanaādimate tu ekatvādisaṃkhyāsaṃkhyeyaprakṛtibhedena maṇḍalabahutvam uktam | ata eva uśanā |
parasparābhiyogena vijigīṣor ares tathā |
aritvavijigīṣutve ekā prakṛtir ity ataḥ ||
vijigīṣuṇā abhiyuktasya arer api pratyabhiyoktṛtvākāreṇa ekā prakṛtir ity arthaḥ | parāśaramatānusāreṇa dve prakṛtī ty āha uśanā |
dve eva prakṛtī nyāyye ity uvāca parāśaraḥ | iti |
ayam arthaḥ — vijigīṣuḥ abhiyoktṛtvāt pradhānam | abhiyojyatvāt arir api pradhānam | itareṣāṃ tanmaṇḍalāntargatatvāt tayor ekānupraveśaḥ iti dve eva prakṛtī iti |
“tritayam api nyāyyam” iti viṣṇuḥ | madhyamodāsīnaśatrubhir abhiyuñjānasya arim iti śeṣaḥ | siddhiḥ kāryasiddhiḥ atra vijigīṣor api pratyabhiyogasamaye śatrutvāt śatrukoṭiniveśaḥ iti bhāvaḥ |
ata evāha uśanā |
maṇḍalaṃ trikam ity āhuḥ vijigīṣvarimadhyamāḥ | iti |
anye catuṣkaṃ maṇḍalam ity āhuḥ | yathoktaṃ rājalāsake |
mūlaprakṛtayaś caitāḥ catasraḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
āhaitat tantrakuśalaḥ catuṣkaṃ maṇḍalaṃ mayaḥ || iti |
[38] catasra iti arivijigīṣumadhamodāsīnāḥ | kecit pañcakam āhuḥ |
pañcakaṃ maṇḍalaṃ nyāyyaṃ tritayaṃ vā manīṣibhiḥ | iti |
ākrandakaraṇakapārṣṇigrāhanivṛttimūlakapṛṣṭhaśuddhiman mitrotpāditakopapaścātkopa-vijigīṣoḥ śatrum abhiyuñjānasya kāryasaṃsiddhir iti pañckaṃ nyāyyam iti bhāvaḥ |
kecin maṇḍalaṣaṭkam āhuḥ |
vijigīṣur arit mitraṃ pārṣṇigrāho ‘tha madhyamaḥ |
udāsīnaḥ pulomendrau maṇḍalaṃ ṣaṭkam ūcatuḥ ||iti |
madhyamodāsīnānugṛhītāv arivijigīṣū svaṃ svaṃ śatrum abhiyuñjānāv iti tātparyam |
daśakaṃ rājamaṇḍlam āha uśanā |
arir mitram arer mitraṃ mitramitram ataḥ param |
athārimitraṃ mitraṃ ca vijigīṣoḥ puraḥsarāḥ ||
pārṣṇigrāhas tataḥ paścād ākrandas tadanantaram |
āsārāvanayoś caiva vijigīṣos tu maṇḍalam || iti |
asyārthaḥ – tatra vijigīṣoḥ samantato maṇḍalībhūtabhūmyanantaraḥ śatruḥ, tathaiva bhūmyaikāntaritaṃ mitram, tathā tadanantaram arer mitram, tato mitramitram iti | arim abhiyoktuṃ pravṛttasya vijigīṣoḥ purastād etad evaṃvidhasaṃjñayā vyavahriyate | śatruhitāya pārṣṇiṃ gṛhṇātīti pārṣṇigrāhaḥ | jigīṣuṇā ākrandyate āhūyata ity ākrando vijigīṣumitram | āsārāvanayoś ceti – pārṣṇigrāham abhyanujñādānenāsārayatīti amitram āsāraḥ | tathaiva ākrandaḥ āsāraḥ vijigīṣumitramitram | etad vijigīṣor maṇḍalam | [39] tajjeyatvāt kṛtsnā bhūmir uktā cakravartyapekṣayā, rājamātrāpekṣayā tu,
maṇḍalād bahir eteṣām udāsīno balādhikaḥ |
anugrahe saṃhitānāṃ vyastānāṃ ca vadhe prabhuḥ || iti |
udāsīnāntarbhāve ekādaśavidham iti dhyeyam | dvādaśaṃ maṇḍalaṃ trayodaśakaṃ ca pūrvam eva vyākhyātam | caturdaśaṃ maṇḍalam ity apare — tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
saptaprakṛtikaṃ yat tu vijigīṣor areś ca yat |
caturdaśakam evedaṃ maṇḍalaṃ paricakṣate || iti |
vijigīṣor areś ca pratyekaṃ sapteti caturdaśakam | yatpadadvayopādānād ity āhur ācāryāḥ | kecid aṣṭādaśakam āhuḥ |
saṃyuktas tv arimitrābhyāṃ ubhayāris tathā ripuḥ |
maulā dvādaś rājānaḥ ity aṣṭādaśakaṃ guruḥ || iti |
ayam arthaḥ | arivijigīṣvor ubhayoḥ apakāryupakārilakṣaṇābyāṃ bahiḥsthitasya svārimitrasahitābhyām arimitrābhyāṃ śaḍbhyāṃ saha maulā dvādaś rājānaḥ | evam aṣṭādaśakaṃ maṇḍalam iti | apare tv ekaviṃśatikaṃ maṇḍalam āhuḥ, yathāha uśanā |
saptaprakṛtikāḥ sarve vijigīṣvarimadhyamāḥ |
ekaviṃśatikaṃ prāhur apare paravādinaḥ || iti |
triṃśatkam ity apare |
paurastyau dvau jigīṣes tu paścimau ceti pañcakam |
amātyādyāḥ pṛthak teṣāṃ triṃśatkaṃ parikīrtitam ||
[40] amātyādyāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ rājaprakṛtayaḥ pañceti evaṃ triṃśatkam |
ṣaṭtriṃśatkaṃ kecid āhur maṇḍalaṃ maṇḍalīvidaḥ |
ṣaṭtriṃśatkaṃ pracakṣate || iti |
aṣṭacatvāriṃśanmaṇḍalam ity anye | yathokataṃ bṛhaspatye |
catvāraḥ pṛthivīpālāḥ pṛthaṅmitraiḥ sahāṣṭakam |
amātyādibhir ete ca jagatyakṣarasaṃhitāḥ || iti |
jagatyakṣarāṇi – jagatīchando’kṣarāṇi aṣṭācatvāriṃśat | catuḥpañcāśatkam āha viśālākṣaḥ ity uktaṃ rājalāsake |
ete ‘ṣṭādaś caiteṣāṃ mitraśatrū pṛthak pṛthak |
catuḥpañcāśatam iti viśālākṣaḥ prabhāṣate || iti |
ṣaṣṭir maṇḍalam ity apare |
daśānāṃ bhūmipālānām amātyādyāḥ pṛthak pṛthak |
maṇḍalaṃ maṇḍalavidaḥ ṣaṣṭisaṃkhyaṃ pracakṣate ||
atra daśa rājānaḥ arimitrādayaḥ pañcāśad ity arthaḥ | mānavamate dvyadhikaṃ saptatikaṃ maṇḍalam |
dvādaśānāṃ narendrāṇāṃ pañca pañca pṛthak pṛthak |
amātyādyāś ca prakṛtīnām anantīha mānavāḥ ||
maulā dvādaśa evaite amātyādyās tathā ca yāḥ |
saptatir dvyadhikā hy eṣā sarvaprakṛtimaṇḍalam ||
ayam arthaḥ – ṣaṣṭir dravyaprakṛtayaḥ | dvādaś rājaprakṛtayaḥ | iti sarvaprakṛtimaṇḍalaṃ dvyadhikā saptatiḥ | aṣṭottaraśatamaṇḍalam āha uśanā |
aṣṭādaśānām eteṣām amātyādyāḥ pṛthak pṛthak |
aṣṭottaraśataṃ hy etan maṇḍalaṃ kavayo viduḥ || iti |
[41] aṣṭādaśa rājaprakṛtayaḥ, navatiḥ dravyaprakṛtayaḥ ity arthaḥ | keṣāṃcin mate caturviṃśattyuttaratriśatam maṇḍalaṃ | yathoktaṃ rājalāsake |
catuḥpañcāśato rājñām amātyādyāḥ pṛthak pṛthak |
sacaturviṃśatīdaṃ hi maṇḍalaṃ triśataṃ matam || iti |
atra dravyaprakṛtayaḥ saptatyuttaradviśatam | svāmiprakṛtayaḥ catuḥpañcāśad ity arthaḥ | evaṃ bahuprakāram api rājamaṇḍalaṃ dvādaśake ‘ntarbhūtam iti | tad evoktaṃ yājñavalkyādidharmaśāstrakāraiḥ | yady apy ekaprabhṛtyekādaśāntānāṃ maṇḍalānāṃ dvādaśāntarbhāvo nāsti | tathāpi dvādaśarājamaṇḍalasya lokaprasiddhatvād ekādimaṇḍalānāṃ durjñānatvāt dvādaśakam evāṅgīkṛtaṃ yājñavalkyādibhiḥ | auśanasādimate ‘pi maṇḍalavikalpeṣu vidyamāneṣu dvādaśakam evābhipretam | yathāha uśanāḥ |
itiprakārair bahudhā maṇḍalaṃ paricakṣate |
sarvalokapratītaṃ tu sphuṭaṃ dvādaśarājakam || iti |
caturvidhopāyāḥ
atropāyam āha viṣṇuḥ: “upāyāḥ sāmadānabhedadaṇḍāś catvāraḥ” iti | sāma priyabhāṣaṇam | dānaṃ suvarṇādeḥ | bhedo bhedanam tatsāmantādīnāṃ parasparavairotpādanam | daṇḍaḥ upāṃśuprakāśābhyāṃ dhanādivadhaparyantaś cāpakāraḥ | ete sāmādayaḥ paripanthyādisādhanopāyāḥ | teṣām uddeśata eva catuṣṭvasiddhau punaś caturgrahaṇaṃ [42] daṇḍasyāgatikasagatikatvapratipādanārtham | ataḥ upāyāḥ traya eveti samyañcaḥ | ata evāha yogīśvaraḥ |
upāyāḥ sāma dānaṃ ca bhedo daṇḍas tathaiva ca |
samyaktvayuktāḥ siddhyeyuḥ dāṇḍas tv agatikā gatiḥ || iti |
catvāra iti catuḥsaṅkhyayā ayogasya vyavaccheda eva nānyayogavyavacchedaḥ kriyate ity ahuḥ | tathā ca māyākṣendrajālātmakam upāyatrayaṃ saṃgṛhītaṃ bhavati | taiḥ sārdham upāyāḥ saptaiva | māyā nāma apahnavopāyaḥ | mithyājñānotpādanam iti yāvat | akṣāḥ prasiddhāḥ | yathā śakuninākṣaiḥ yudhiṣṭhirarājyagrahaṇam | atra auśanasādinītiśāstreṣu māyākṣendrajālaiḥ saha sāmādayaḥ sapteti | māyā nāma kīcakavadhe vṛkodareṇa strīrūpadhāraṇam | tatraiva kīcakavadhe virāṭasyāsau kīcako mriyatām ity upekṣety uktam | indrajālaṃ tu māyārūpavastukalpanam | yathā — vatsarājagrahaṇaṃ aindrjālikagajanirmāṇena | ataś ca māyendrajālayoḥ kāryakāraṇatayā bhedaḥ | akṣeṣu yady api māyāsti, tathāpi māyā pratāraṇaśabdavācyeti bhedaḥ | ata eva māyāditrikaṃ pratāraṇanibandhanatvāt kaṣṭam | ata eva catvāra upāyā ity uktam gautamasūtre | yājñavalkyādismṛtikārair api catvāra evopāyāḥ pratipāditāḥ | tathā ca gautamasūtram: “caturupāyān avalambya saṃdhivigrahayānāsanadvaidhībhāvasamāśrayākhyān guṇān parikalpayet” iti |
saṃdhivigrahādiguṇāḥ
saṃdhiḥ vyavasthākaraṇam | vigrahaḥ apakāraḥ | yānaṃ [43] paraṃ prati yātrā | āsanam upekṣā | dvaidhībhāvo balasya bhidākaraṇam | āśrayo balavadāśrayaṇam |
yātrākālaḥ
yānakānān āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
yadā sasyaguṇopetaṃ pararāṣṭraṃ tadā vrajet |
paraś ca hīna ātmā ca hṛṣṭavāhanapūruṣaḥ ||
arthaśāstrasya puruṣakārāyattatvāt puruṣakārasya prābalyam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “daivapuruṣakārayoḥ puruṣakāro balavān, ubhayam ekam ity eke” yājñavalkyādaya ity arthaḥ | svamate puruṣakārasaiva prabalatvam | tathā loke darśanāt | anyathā cikitsakādiśāstravaiyarthyāt | anyathā kauśalādicakravidyānāṃ dṛṣṭārthānāṃ vaiyarthyāt | yathā kauśalo ‘smin yuddhe anenāsāv asminn avayave hanyate, anena hananena mriyata iti niścaye tasminn avayave guptākāreṇa lohapaṭṭādike nikṣipte tasmin lohapaṭṭaghātaḥ | na tu mriyata iti daivāt puruṣakāro balavān iti śāstrasya prayojanaṃ siddham iti bhāvaḥ | yājñavalkyādīnāṃ mataṃ tu –
daive puruṣakāre ca karmasiddhir vyavasthitā |
tatra daivam ativyaktaṃ pauruṣaṃ pūrvadaihikam ||
pūrvdaihikam pūrvadehārjitam | pauruṣam eva daivam ity ucyata iti tadekadeśimatam |
matāntarāṇi tu kecid dehabalabhāvāc ca kālāt puruṣakāraḥ | kecid iṣṭāniṣṭaphalaṃ daivād evecchanti | kecit svabhāvāt na [44] kāraṇam apekṣate iti | kecit kālāt | kecit puruṣakāra evety uktvā svamatam āha yogīśvaraḥ |
saṃyoge kecid icchanti phalaṃ kuśalabuddhayaḥ |
ekaikasmāt phalaṃ na bhavatīty arthaḥ | tatra dṛṣṭāntam āha |
yathā hy ekena cakreṇa rathasya na gatir bhavet |
evaṃ puruṣakāreṇa vinā daivaṃ na sidhyati ||
pañcāṅgāni
pañcāṅgāny āha hārītaḥ |
sahāyāḥ sādhanopāyā vibhāgo deśakālayoḥ |
vipatteś ca pratīkāraḥ siddhiḥ pañcāṅga iṣyate ||
kāryasiddhirūpalābhas trividhaḥ | hiraṇyalābho bhūlābho mitralābhaś ceti | teṣu mitralābho jyāyān | ataḥ tatsiddhyupāyo jyāyān | tatas tatprāptyupāye yatno vidheyaḥ |
sa copāyaḥ satyabhāṣaṇam evety āha vasiṣthaḥ: “satyabhāṣaṇan mitralabdhiḥ sā hiraṇyabhūmibhyo ‘dhikā” | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
hiraṇyabhūmilābhebhyo mitralabdhir varā yataḥ |
tato yateta tatprāptau rakṣet satyaṃ samāhitaḥ || iti |
marmāṇiḥ
pañcasv api marmasu na pramaditavyam | tathā ca gautamasūtram: “bhojananidhuvana-kṣaurābhyaṅgasvāpakālāḥ pañca marmāṇi teṣu na pramaditavyam” iti | bhojanakālaḥ abhyavahārakālamātropalakṣakaḥ | tāmbūlādicarvaṇakālo ‘pi [45] marmakālaḥ nidhuvanakālo ‘pi vyaktyantarasaṃsargasyopalakṣakaḥ | tathā ca mitreṇaikaśayyāsanakālo ‘pi marmakālaḥ | kṣaurakālas tu śastrasaṃsargakālasyopalakṣakaḥ | abhyañjanakālas tu śramādikālānām upalakṣakaḥ | svāpakālas tv ekākitvāvasthāyāḥ upalakṣakaḥ iti vyākhyātaṃ vyākhyātṛbhiḥ | ata uktaṃ manunā |
evaṃ sarvam idaṃ rājā saṃmantya saha mantribhiḥ |
vyāyamyāplutya madhyāhne bhoktum antaḥpuraṃ vrajet ||
tatrātmabhūtaiḥ kālajñair ahāryaiḥ paricārakaiḥ |
suparīkṣitam annādym adyān mantrair viṣāpahaiḥ ||
viṣaghnair auṣadhair adbhiḥ sarvadravyāṇi nejayet |
viṣaghnāni ca ratnāni niyato dhārayet sadā ||
parīkṣitāḥ striyaś cainaṃ vyajanodakadhūpanaiḥ |
veṣābharaṇasaṃyuktāḥ saṃspṛśeyuḥ samāhitāḥ ||
evaṃ prayatnaṃ kurvīta yānaśayyāsanāśanaiḥ |
snāne prasādane caive sarvālaṅkārakeṣu ca ||
bhuktavān viharec caiva stribhir antaḥpure saha |
vihṛtya ca yathākālaṃ punaḥ kāryāṇi cintayet || iti |
saptāṅgāni
saptāṅgāni tu svāmyamātyādīni | tathā ca gautamasūtram: “svāmyamātyasuhṛddurgakośa-daṇḍajanāḥ” iti | svāmī prasiddhaḥ | amātyā mantriṇaḥ | suhṛdaḥ sahajakṛttrimaprākṛtāḥ | [46] durgāḥ giridurgāṇi sapta | kośaḥ suvarṇādidravyarāśiḥ | daṇḍaḥ caturaṅgasainyam | janā brāhmaṇādivarṇāḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
svāmyamātyajanā durgaṃ kośo daṇḍas tathaiva ca |
mitrāṇy etāḥ prakṛtayaḥ rājyaṃ saptāṅgam ucyate || iti |
prakṛtayaḥ mūlakāraṇāni | ataḥ saptāṅgāni sarvathā saṃrakṣyāṇīty āha sumantuḥ: “svāmyam ātmanā saṃrakṣet | amātyān saṃmānena | varṇān rañjanena, janān varṇadharmarañjanena | durgaṃ dhanadhānyādisamṛddhyā, kośam ucitavyayena | daṇḍaṃ svadharmeṇa | mitraṃ satyabhāṣaṇena” iti | atra daṇḍaḥ caturaṅgasainyaṃ na bhavati | aparādhānusāreṇa śārīro ‘rthadaṇḍaḥ parikalpanīyaḥ | ayam abhisaṃdhiḥ – sumantumate caturaṅgasainyasya kośa evāntarbhāvaḥ iti | “kośas tu sarvathā abhisaṃrakṣyaḥ” ity āha gautamaḥ: “tanmūlatvāt prakṛtīnām” iti | prakṛtīnāṃ ṣaḍaṅgānām ity arthaḥ | sarvathā sarvaprakāreṇa asadvyayaṃ vihāya corādibhyaḥ kāyasthādibhyaś cāpi saṃrakṣaṇīyaḥ iti |
ata eva gautamasūtram: “prabhumantrotsāhaśaktayas tanmūlāḥ” iti | tanmūlāḥ kośamūlā ity ārthaḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
tad avāpya nṛpo daṇḍaṃ durvṛtteṣu nipātayet |
daṇḍo hi dharmarūpeṇa brahmaṇā nirmitaḥ purā || iti |
[47] tad iti rājyam |
satyasandhena śucinā susahāyena dhīmatā |
yathāśāstraṃ prayuktaḥ san sadevāsuramānavam ||
jagad ānandayet sarvam anyathā tat prakopayet |
api bhrātā suto ‘rghyo vā śvaśuro mātulo ‘pi vā ||
nādaṇḍyo nāma rājño ‘sti dharmād vicalitaḥ svakāt ||
etac ca mātāpitrādivyatiriktaviṣayam,
adaṇḍyau mātāpitarau snātakaś ca purohitaḥ |
parivrājakavānaprasthāś ca ....
iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt |
idānīṃ sāmānyena rājño vyavahāradarśanaṃ kāryam ity uktam | sa ca vyavahāraḥ kīdṛśaḥ | katividhaḥ | kathāṃ ceti pradarśyate |
vyavahāro nāma anyavirodhena svātmasaṃbandhitayā kathanam | satyabhāṣaṇāhiṃsananiṣṭhatvāt | prayatnasādhyadharmākhyapadārthe lobhadveṣādivaśadvicchinne sati ṛṇādānādau svadhanaprāptyarthaṃ samayadharmādau ca paradharmavarjanārthaṃ nyāyavismaraṇaṃ kriyate | tatra sādhyamūlo yo manuṣyāṇāṃ vivādaḥ sa vyavahāra ity ucyata iti | na caivaṃ cauryapāruṣyavivādo na vyavahāra iti śaṅkanīyam | aṣṭādaśavidhaviṣayāṇām anyatamaviṣayako vivādo vyavahāra iti lakṣaṇāṅgīkārāt | tathā hi —
aṣṭādaśavivādapadāni
ṛṇādānopanidhisaṃbhūyotthānadattāpradānikābhyupetyāśuśrūṣāvetanānapākarmāsvāmi-vikrayāsaṃpradānakrītvānuśayasīmā[48]vivādakṣetrajastrīpuṃsasaṃbandhadāyavibhāgasāhasa-vākpāruṣyadaṇḍapāruṣyadyūtaprakīrṇakāny aṣṭādaśa vivādapadāni | na ca vācyaṃ nikṣepādipadāntareṣ vyavahārasmaraṇāt lakṣaṇasyāvyāptir iti | nikṣepādīnām upanidhyādiṣv antarbhāvāt | aṣṭādaśavivādapadānāṃ saṅgatisaṅgrahaḥ (?) kathyate | sarvāsu smṛtiṣu prāmāṇanirūpaṇānantaram aṣṭādaśavivādapadanirūpaṇam | pramāṇanirūpaṇānantaraṃ prameyanirūpaṇaṃ nyāyyam iti tadanantaram eva aṣṭādaśavivādapadākhyaṃ prameyaṃ tāvan nirūpyate —
tatra ṛṇādāna eva mānuṣadivyātmakasakalapramāṇasādhyatvena vyavahārasya nirūpaṇāt prathamād uddeśakrameṇa prathamam ṛṇādānākhyaṃ vivādapadaṃ nirūpyate | ata evoktaṃ manunā: “teṣām ādyam ṛṇādānam” iti |
ṛṇādāne – upacayāpekṣayā parahastadattam ṛṇam | tadanapekṣayā rakṣaṇārtham evānyahaste dattaṃ dravyam upanidhir iti ṛṇādānānantaram upanidher avasaraḥ | ṛṇādāne prayuktadhād utpannasya upacayasya ekaniṣṭhatvam | sambhūyasamuttāne tv anekaniṣṭhatvam iti ṛṇādānānantaraṃ sambhūyasamutthānasyaivāvasaro nyāyyaḥ | tathāpy etatpradānānantaram upanidhipadasyāvakāśābhāvāt uktanyāyena ṛṇādānānantaram eva upanidheḥ praveśa iti paścād asya padasyāvasarasaṅgatiḥ |
dattāpradānikasya saṅgatis tu ādhyupanidhicauryāgatādidravyāṇām adeyatvapratipānanād ṛṇādānopanidhisambhūyasamutthānānāṃ śeṣatayāsya sampratipādanād dāyavibhāgaśeṣatāpy asti | tathāpi bhūyasāṃ nyāyena trayāṇāṃ śeṣatoketi dhyeyam | dattāpradānike pratiśrutārthasya sopādhikatve pratyādeyatvam uktam | [49] abhyupetyāśuśrūṣākhye vivādapade nirupādhikatve ‘pi pratyādeyatvam iti saṃgatiḥ | na cānayor ekaprakaraṇatvam, pūrvaprakaraṇe dattasyādānaṃ dravyaviṣayam | atropetasyākaraṇam upajīvakaviṣayam iti | bhinnaviṣayatvāt prakaraṇāntareṇa vyutpādyam iti naikaprakaraṇatvam | abhyupetyāśuśrūṣākhye pade upagatapaṇajīvyatādīnāṃ dānānāṃ pratiśrutanirvāhārthaṃ pratinidhir vā kārya ity uktam | atra vetanānapākarmākhye tu pade pratiśrutanirvāhārthaṃ vetanam iva saṃgatiḥ | na cāsya dattāpradānike ‘ntarbhāvaḥ | tatra dattasyādattaprāyatoktā, na tu dattasyāpaharaṇam | tataś ca anayor bhedaḥ | tadabhisandhāyāha manuḥ |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi vetasyānapakriyām | iti |
ataḥ param abhyupetyāśuśrūṣākhyasya padasyānantam ity arthaḥ | pūrvaprakaraṇe vetanānapākarmākhye vetanam anapākaraṇīyam ity uktam | atrāsvāmivikrayākhye tu krītam apākaraṇīyam iti saṃgatiḥ | dattāpradāte tu dattasyādattaprāyatoktā | atra tu krītasyāpākaraṇam eva, na tu krītasyākrītaprāyateti tasmād bhedaḥ | asvāmivikraye pade asvāmivkrīte dravye svatvānutpatteḥ tatparāvartanīyam ity uktam | atra tu vikrīyāsaṃpradānākhye vivādapade krayāt svatvotpattāv api tasya sapratibandhatvāt parāvartanīyateti saṃgatiḥ | na ca gopyādhivat sopādhikasvatve ‘pi ṛṇādānāntaḥpātitvam asya | tatra gopyādhau paribhāṣāmūlaṃ svatvasya sopādhikatvam | atra tv anuśayamūlam ity anayoḥ [50] bhedaḥ | na cāsya dattāpradānike ‘ntarbhāvaḥ | dānakrayayoḥ bhedāt | kiṃ ca dattasyādattaprāyatā | atra tu vikrītasya parāvṛttir iti prāg ukta eva nyāyyaḥ, ata eva vetanānapākarmādāv anantarbhāvaḥ | vikrīyāsaṃpradānākhye vivādapade vikrītasya parāvṛttiḥ, atra krītvānuśayākhye vivādapade krītasyeti saṃgatiḥ | na cāsya pūrvatrāntarbhāvaḥ | krītavikrītagatibhinnatvāt parāvṛtteḥ | nanu krayavikrayayoḥ vīvadhakalaśatulyatayā parasparāpekṣatvāt aikādikaraṇyaṃ yuktam iti cet, satyam | “daśāho ‘nuśayaḥ krayaḥ” ity asmin vacane krayaśabdaḥ parivṛttivinimayayor upalakṣakaḥ | “sajātīyayoḥ dravyayoḥ vinimayaḥ, vijātīyayos tu viparivṛttiḥ” iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | na ca parivṛtteḥ kraya evāntarbhāva iti vācyam | kraye tulyaṃ mūlyaṃ trivarṣaphalam | privṛttau tu svatulyam eva | yady api rikthakrayādigautamasūtre viparivṛttivinimayayoḥ parigaṇanābhāvāt svatvahetutā nāstīti bhāti, tathāpi tilakrayasya niṣedhāt pratigrahasyātiduṣṭatvāt tilaṃ datvā tilavrīhyādigrahaṇasthale vinamayaparivṛttyor eva svatvāpādakatvaṃ lokasiddham | yat tu – “ādhiḥ praṇaśyed dviguṇaḥ” ityādau tilavinimayavat dhanadvaiguṇyaṃ svatvāpādakaṃ na bhavati | api tu krayāntaparyavasānāt svatvāpādakam ity uktam, tat tu vinimayasya svatvāpādakatvaṃ nāstīty evaṃparaṃ na bhavati, kiṃ tu tasmin sthale krayāntaparyavasānād eva svatvāpādakatvam | anyatra tu tilavinimayādau vinimayaparivṛttyor api krayādīnām iva svatvāpādakatvaṃ lokasiddham napahnotuṃ śakyam ity āha bhāruciḥ |
[51]        vastutas tu vinimayaparivṛttipāribhāṣikyā paribhāṣayā tayor vācanikadānarūpeṇa svatvahetutvasaiddhir asty evety uktaṃ tatraiva | ataś ca pūrvaprakaraṇe vikrīyāsaṃpradānatā, atra tu parivṛttyanuśaya iti | samākhyā tu vinimayaparivṛttyanuśayor api lakṣyety āha bhāruciḥ |
pūrvasmin prakaraṇe krītvānuśaye parivṛttyanuśaye ca krītaviṣayaḥ parivṛtteś ca atikrama uktaḥ | atra tu samayānapākarmākhye vivādapade samayasya atikrama ucyata iti saṃgatiḥ | pūrvasmin prakaraṇe samayānapākarmākhye vivādapade naigamādiviṣayatayā saṃvidvyatikrama iti saṃgatiḥ | yady apy anayor aikādhikaraṇyam eva yuktam, tathāpi strīpuṃsākhyavivādapadopayogitayā gṛhakṣetrādinirṇayopayogitayā pṛthagadhikaraṇateti dhyeyam | pūrvasmin prakaraṇe kṣetravivādo nirnītaḥ | kṣetraṃ dvividham | sthāvarakṣetraviṣayavivādaviṣayānantaraṃ jaṅgamātmakastrīrūpakṣetrajavivādanirṇaya iti saṃgatiḥ | pūrvasmin prakaraṇe strīpuṃsākhyo dharmaḥ pratipāditaḥ | atra tu dāyavibhāgākhye vivādapade strīpuṃsāv evādhikṛtya vivādaḥ pravartata iti tayor upajīvyopajīvakatayā saṃgatiḥ | atha sāhasākhyasya padasya saṃgatiḥ | pūrvatra trayodaśavivādapadeṣu deyādeyavicāraḥ kṛtaḥ, yathāha viṣṇuḥ: “vyavahāro dvirutthānaḥ” iti | vyavahārakāraṇadvayaṃ kathitam | tathā ca gautamasūtra: “dvirutthānato dvigatiḥ” iti, vyavahāra ity anuṣajyate | tatra nibandhanakāreṇoktam – ṛṇādānādidāyavibhāgāntānāṃ deyanibandhanasāhasādipañcakasya daṇḍanibandhanatvam iti dvirutthānatety artha iti | [52] yady api manvādibhiḥ |
teṣām ādyam ṛṇādānaṃ nikṣepo ‘svāmivikrayaḥ |
saṃbhūya ca samutthānaṃ dattasyānapakarma ca ||
vetanasyaiva cādānaṃ saṃvidaś ca vyatikramaḥ |
krayavikrayānuśayau vivādaḥ svāmipālayoḥ ||
sīmāvibhāgadharmaś ca pāruṣye daṇḍavācike |
steyaṃ ca sāhasaṃ caiva strīsaṃgrahaṇam eva ca ||
strīṣu dharmo vibhāgaś ca dyūtam āhvaya eva ca |
padāny aṣṭādaśaitāni vyavahāre vidur budhāḥ ||
ityevaṃprakāreṇa kramikāni vyavahārapadāny uktāni | tatra vākpāruṣyadaṇḍapāruṣyastrīsaṃgrahaṇānantaraṃ dāyavibhāgaḥ kramikaḥ | nibandhanakāreṇa tu trayodaśavivādapaṃ dāya ity uktam | ubhayor mahān virodhaḥ sa parihīyate | tathoktaṃ nāradena:
aṣṭādaśavyavahāravācakavacanopanyāsaḥ
ṛṇādānaṃ hy upanidhiḥ saṃbhūyotthānam eva ca |
dattasya punar ādānam aśuśrūṣābhyupetya ca ||
vetanasyānapākarma tathaivāsvāmivikrayaḥ |
vikrīyāsaṃpradānaṃ ca krītvānuśaya eva ca ||
samayasyānapākarma vivādaḥ kṣetrajas tathā |
strīpuṃsayoś ca saṃbandho dāyabhāgo ‘tha sāhasam ||
vākpāruṣyaṃ tathā proktaṃ daṇḍapāruṣyam eva ca |
dyūtaṃ samāhvayś caivam aṣṭādaś padāni ca || iti |
[53] nāradavacanānusāri nibandhakāravacanam | atas tadvākyasyāpi gautamasūtrasya nāradavacanānusārtvam eveti dhyeyam | etadanusāreṇaivāsmābhir apy uktā vivādapadānāṃ parasparasaṃgatiḥ | ataś ca ṛṇādānādidāyavibhāgāntānāṃ deyanibandhanatvena pratipādanam | sāhasādipañcakasya daṇḍanibandhanatvam iti prakaraṇabhedaṃ sūcayitum “dāyabhāgo ‘tha sāhasam” ity athaśabdaḥ prayuktaḥ | atatś ca deyapratipādanānantaraṃ daṇḍapratipādanasyāvasara iti saṃgatiḥ | vākpāruṣyadaṇḍapāruṣyayoḥ sāhasasya ca parasparabhedābhāve ‘pi daṇḍālpatvabahutvārthaṃ pṛthaggrahaṇam iti vakṣyate | pūrvaprakaraṇe cauryaṃ nirūpitam | dyūtasamāhvayayos tulyatogakṣematayā cauryānantaraṃ saṃgatiḥ | atha vā saptadaśavivādapadaprakīrṇakapadayoḥ saṃgatyantaraṃ pūrvaṃ parājitasyaiva daṇḍavidhānam uktam, atra tu (vi)jayino ‘pi daṇḍavidhānam ity anayoḥ saṃgatiḥ |
durdṛṣṭāṃs tu punar dṛṣṭvā vyavahārān nṛpeṇa tu |
sabhyāḥ sajayijo daṇḍyā vivādadviguṇam damam ||
iti yājñavalkyena jayino daṇḍavidhānaṃ prakīrṇakākhyavivādāntargatatvena jayino daṇḍavidhānaṃ prakīrṇakākhyavivādāntargatatvena smṛtatvāt | atra tu bahu vaktavyam asti | tat tu tatraiva vakṣyate | atha vā saṃgatyantaram,
bhṛtacchalānurodhena dvigatiḥ samudāhṛtā |
iti vyavahārasya chalānusaraṇaṃ tatvānusaraṇaṃ iti gatidvayam uktam | tatra tatvānusaraṇena saptadaśa vivādapadāny anukrāntāni | chalānusaraṇena prakīrṇakākhyaṃ vivādapadam anukrāntam iti | tathā ca asmin prakaraṇe bṛhaspatiḥ |
[54]         sākṣisabhyārthasannānāṃ dūṣaṇe darśanaṃ punaḥ |
svavācaiva jitānāṃ tu noktaḥ paunarbhavo vidhiḥ || iti |
atra “vyavahārān nṛpaḥ paśyet” iti vidhiḥ svāparādhena vyavahārasya samāptatvāt svavāganusaraṇarūpachalam evāvalambate | na tu pramāṇāntaragaveṣaṇaṃ vilambatvād vidher iti | “teṣām adyam ṛṇādānam” ityādimānavakrameṇāpi saṃgatir vaktuṃ śakyate, granthavistarabhayān nocyate | etac ca sarvasaṃgatikathanaṃ tattadvivādapadavivaraṇāvasare ‘pi samyaṅnirūpitam api śāstrārthaṃ karatalāmalakīcikīrṣuṇā gauravasahiṣṇunā granthakāreṇa saṃkṣepato nirūpitam iti na paunaruktyam | putrapautrādyadhikāriṇā bālyānantaraṃ kāladvaiguṇyādiprakāreṇa ṛṇaṃ deyam |
deyādeya ṛṇe
dyūtādikutsitakuṭumbabharaṇādyāvaśyaketarakāryārthaṃ pitrādikṛtam ṛṇam adeyam | bandhagrahaṇādidhanaprayogadharmāḥ śanaiḥ kīrtyādipṛthudhanādānadharmāś ca yasmin vivādaviṣaye uktāḥ tatparam ṛṇādānam iti |
karaṇḍastham asaṃkhyātam anākhyātam adarśitam |
parahaste dattam upanidhikam iti dvitīyaṃ vivādapadam | etad eva saṃkhyātaṃ cen nikṣepa ity ato na bhinnaṃ vivādapadam | dāyādebhyaḥ bhayāt rājacorabhayāc ca sthāpyate ‘nyagṛhe dravyaṃ nyāsa iti na pṛthagvivādapadam | vaṇijo vāṇijyaṃ ṛtvijo yajñaṃ kṛṣīvalāḥ kṛṣiṃ hemakārādayaḥ śilpinaḥ śilpaṃ nartakādayo nṛtyādikaṃ stenāḥ steyaṃ yatra saṃhatya kurvate tat saṃbhūya kriyamāṇaṃ [55] vāṇijyādikarma samuttiṣṭhate ‘neneti vyutpattyā saṃbhūyasamutthānākhyaṃ vyavahārapadam | yatra svarṇakārādibhyaḥ prītyādinā dattaṃ dravyaṃ vaiguṇyādinā tebhyaḥ punar apahāryaṃ dattasyāpradānam asmin vidyata iti dattāpradānikaṃ nāma vivādapadam | karmakārādyavaiguṇyād dattasyānapākarma — anapanayanam asminn iti dattānapākarmāpi vivādapadam ity ato na pṛthag abhihitam | ataś ca deyam adeyaṃ dattam adattam iti dravyabhedena cāturvidhyam aviruddham | deyam aniṣiddhadānakriyānirvartakam | dattaṃ dāne sati saṃpradānād anapahāryam | dattaṃ cāpahāryam iti yac chiṣyādeḥ śuśrūṣādikam  aṅgīkṛtya gurvādyājñākaratvādyakaraṇaṃ tad abhyupetyāśuśrūṣākhyaṃ vivādapadam | śiṣyaśabdo ‘ntevāsibhṛtakakarmakṛddāsānām upalakṣaka iti tanmūle na pṛthag bhidyata ity ekam eva padam | īdṛśasya deyam īdṛśasyādeyaṃ dattam asya evaṃvidhāt pratyādeyaṃ kvacid dviguṇam ādeyam ityādy uktam | yatra vivāde tad vetanasya bhṛter anapākarma — anapanayanaṃ vidyate tad vetanānapākarmākhyaṃ vivādapadam | svāmino ‘samakṣaṃ yācitānvāhitanyāsādikaṃ paradravyaṃ vikrīyate asāv asvāmivikrayaḥ | so ‘smin vyavahāre nirṇeya ity asvāmivikrayākhyaṃ vivādapadam | mūlyena vikrayapaṇyaṃ kretur yan na pradīyate tad vikrīyāsaṃpradānam |
paṇyaṃ ṣaḍvidham
gaṇitatulitameyakriyārūpakāntibhiḥ paṇyaṃ ṣaḍvidham | gaṇitaṃ nārikelādi | tulitaṃ hiṅgvādi | meyaṃ vrīhyādi | kriyā – vāhanādidohanādikriyā gomahiṣyādeḥ | kāntyā [56] ratnādi | rūpataḥ paṇyāṅganādi | ataś ca kramukādīnāṃ tulitapaṇyaviśeṣatvāt tanmūlena vivādapadasya na pṛthag bhedaḥ | yaḥ kretā mūlyena paṇyaṃ krītvā aparāmarśapūrvakaṃ manyate, tat krītvānuśayākhyaṃ vivādapadam | gaṇaśreṇisamūhanārthaṃ jātānāṃ sthirīkaraṇarūpasya samayasyānapākarma — anullaṅghanaṃ yat tat samayānapākarma | viruddhalakṣaṇayā darśādivat samayānapākarmākhyaṃ vivādapadam | saṃvidvyatikrama ity etasyaiva nāmāntaram | pāṣaṇḍapūgāḥ śreṇinaigamānāṃ saṅghāḥ | samūhas tu brāhmaṇānāṃ saṃhatiḥ | vrātaḥ āyudhadharāṇāṃ vratatiḥ |
yatra kedārasetunikṛṣṭākṛṣṭamaryādāniścayāḥ kṣetraviṣayāḥ tat kṣetravivādākhyaṃ padam | tac cādhikyanyūnatābhedāt ṣaḍvidham | kaṃcid bhūbhāgam adhikṛtya tato ‘dhikā bhūr asti na veti vivāde ādhikyanibandhanaḥ | etāvatī bhūs tava nety ukte mamaitāvaty astīty evaṃvidho nyūnatā hetukaḥ | asyāṃ bhuvi mamāṃśo ‘stīty ukte nety evaṃvido ‘ṃśāstitvakāritaḥ | atra tavāṃśo nāstīty ukte vidyata ity evaṃvidho ‘ṃśo nāstitvanimittakaḥ | madīyā bhūḥ tvayā prāg abhujyamānaiva idānīṃ bhujyata ity ukte prāg api bhuktir astīty evaṃvidhaḥ abhogabhuktibījakaḥ | iyaṃ maryādā madīyāyā bhuva ity ukte nety evaṃvidhaḥ sīmāsaṃbhavaḥ | ataś ca nyūnatādīnāṃ tadanatirakān na tanmūlena bhedaḥ |
[57]        tasya sīmāvivādasya pāñcavidhyam | dhvajanīmātsyānaidānībhayavarjitārājaśāsanānītā-viṣayatvāt | ato dhvajinyādibhedena pṛthag bhidyate vivādapadam | dhvajinī – vṛkṣādinā lakṣitā | mātsyā – jalena | naidānī – dhūtatuṣādinā | bhayavarjitā – ubhayavādisaṃpratipannā | rājaśāsanānītā – rājecchayā nirmitā | yatra strīpuruṣayor mithunībhāvo viṣayīkṛtaḥ sa ca strīpuṃsasaṃbandhākhyaṃ vivādapadam | asyaiva strīsaṃgrahaṇam iti nāmāntaram | pitryāder arthasya putrādibhir vibhāgaḥ prakalpyate yatra sa dāyabhāgo nāma vivādapadam | yat kiṃcit karma māraṇacauryaparadārābhimarśanādikasahasā balena baladarpitaiḥ kṛtaṃ nirṇīyate tatsāhasam iti sāhasākhyaṃ vivādapadam | yad vacanaṃ deśajātikulādīnāṃ bādhakatvenātyantaduḥkhakarārthaṃ tad vākpāruṣyaṃ vivādapadam | yatra pade paragātreṣu hastapādāyudhādibhiḥ kṛtasyābhidrohasya bhasmādibhiś copaghātasya nirṇayaḥ kriyate tad daṇḍapāruṣyākhyaṃ vivādapadam |
nanu pāruṣyadvayasya strīsaṃgrahaṇasya ca sāhasaviseṣatvāt padāntaratvoktir na yuktā, satyam, baladarpāvaṣṭambhopadhinā sāhasaṃ bhidyate iti daṇḍātirekārthaṃ pṛthag abhidhānam | akṣādibhiḥ paṇapūrvakaṃ krīḍanaṃ kauṭilyena kṛtaṃ yatra viṣayīkriyate tad dyūtākhyaṃ vivādapadam | tathā kūṭasākṣibhiḥ paṇapūrvakaṃ devanaṃ kṛtaṃ tadviṣayīkriyate yatra tatsamākhyaṃ vivādapadam | anayoḥ paṇapūrvakakrīḍanamūlakatvāt na bhadaḥ | tathā ca nighaṇṭuḥ |
aprāṇibhir yat kriyate tal loke dyūtam ucyate |
[58]        prāṇibhiḥ kriyamāṇaṃ tu sa vijñeyaḥ samāhvayaḥ || iti |
yatra nṛpeṇa yad yat svājñātikramādau prativāditatvam āsthāya nirṇetavyaṃ yac ca ṛṇādānādipūrvoktapadeṣu noktaṃ tat prakīrṇakākhyaṃ padam | sa caivam aṣṭādaśaprabhedabhinno vyavahāraḥ punar dvividhaḥ | sapaṇaḥ apaṇaś ceti | nāradādayas tu sottaro ‘nuttaraś cety āhuḥ | pratijñāvilekhanāt pūrvaṃ yatra vyavahāre daṇḍābhyadhikaḥ paṇaḥ ubhābhyām upeyate anyatareṇa vā sa sottaro vyavahāraḥ | tadanyo ‘nuttaraḥ | ataś ca yājñavalkyanāradoktayoḥ sapaṇāpaṇasottarānuttararūpayor bhedayoḥ pṛthagbhedo nāsti | tathāpy anye ‘pi bhedāḥ nāradādibhir uktāḥ: “catuṣpādacatuḥsādhanacaturbhūtacaturvyāpicatuṣkāritva-rūpāḥ aṣṭāṅgāṣṭādaśapadaśataśākhātriyonidvyabhiyogadvivāradvigatirūpāś ca” |
dharmaḥ vyavahāraḥ caritraṃ rājaśāsanaṃ catvāraḥ pādāḥ | ataś catuṣpād vyavahāraḥ | atrottaraḥ pūrvabādhakaḥ | tathā ca hārītaḥ |
dharmeṇa vyavahāreṇa caritreṇa nṛpājñayā |
catuṣpād vyavahāro ‘yam uttaraḥ pūrvabādhakaḥ || iti |
nanu pratijñottarapratyākalanapramāṇanirṇayaiś catuṣpāttvaṃ na dharmādibhir iti cet | maivam | nirṇayavādo (-pādo?) dharmāyanusāreṇa caturvidhaḥ | tatra yadanusāreṇa yo nirṇayaḥ kṛtaḥ sa tacchabdenocyate | tena dharmādibhir api catuṣpāttvavarṇanaṃ yuktam eva | dharmo nāma pādaḥ yatra vivāde dharmamūlanirṇayenaiva doṣakāritvaṃ doṣakārī, svakaṃ dhanaṃ dhanasvāmī ca prāpnoti sa dharma ity ucyate | dharmaśāstranirūpitaprtijñottarānusāreṇa nirṇayo vyavahārākhyaḥ [59] pādaḥ | dharmādharavivekarāhityena deśācāram avalambya yadārcyate tac caritraṃ pādaḥ | tadanusārī nirṇayo ‘pi tathaiva | mānāntaraviddho rājabuddhimātraparikalpito nirṇayo rājaśāsanam | atra dharmādīnām ekaikasya dvaividhyam | sa ca dharmākhyanirṇayaḥ tatvānusaraṇena(?) ekaḥ | vinā tatvānusaraṇaṃ tatvottareṇa vā divyapramāṇena vā kṛto dvitīyaḥ | mānuṣapramāṇaniścito vyavahāra ekaḥ | vākchalānantaratvena dvitīyaḥ | ulkāhastādiliṅgena nirṇītaś caritram ekaḥ prakāraḥ | deśasthityā dvitīyaḥ |
nanv evaṃ tarhi “uttaraḥ pūrvabādhakaḥ” ity uktir ayuktā tatvārthānuguṇyātiśayena pūrvasyaivottarabādhakatvāt | “chalaṃ nirasya bhūtena vyavahārān neyan nṛpaḥ” iti yājñavalkyenoktam |
ucyate, satyam, utsargataḥ, na sarvatraivam | kvacid viṣaye tūttaraḥ pūrvabādhako bhavaty eva | tathā hi — yathā yena kenacid rājanyādinā mohāt kathaṃcid rājadārādeḥ sparśanādikaṃ kṛtam, kṛtvā ca prāṇabhayāt mithyākaroti sākṣiṇaś ca santi | karteti mithyāsākṣyaṃ brūyuḥ | tadā doṣakāriṇaḥ tatkartṛtvāprāpteḥ dharmo vyavahāreṇa bādhyate | uktaṃ cātra dharmabādhanam, “varṇināṃ hi vadho yatra” iti sākṣiṇo mithyābhidhānavidhānāt | yadā punaḥ “paradārābhigamanaṃ kṛtam anena asya sākṣiṇo vidyante” iti kenacid abhiyukto brūte | “satyam etat sākṣibhāṣītam | tathāpi nāhaṃ daṇḍyaś caritrabalāt | mayaitat [60] kṛtaṃ niveśitaṃ ca śilātalapustakādau rājñā tat” iti tadā caritreṇa vyavahāro bādhyate | vyavahārataḥ prāptasya daṇḍasya caritrato nivṛttatvāt | caritram api kvacid rājājñayā bādhyate kulagṛhābhyantarena kenacid rājapuruṣeṇa praveṣṭavyam iti caritram īdṛśaṃ kṛtam | tato kaścid aparādho jñātaḥ praviṣṭo ‘ntaḥpuram | tato rājñā kaścid rājapuruṣa ājñaptaḥ “gṛhābhyantaram(pi)bhipraviśya sa duṣṭa ānetavyaḥ” iti | tadā caritram api rājaśāsanena bādhyate | duṣṭanigrahasyāvaśyakartavyatvena caritrato rājaśāsanasya bādhyate | duṣṭanigrahasyāvaśyakartavyatvena caritrato rājaśāsanasya balattvāt | catuḥsthānāni — catvāri sthānāni satyasākṣipustakaraṇarājaśāsanarūpāṇi yasya saḥ catuḥsthānaḥ | etad uktaṃ bhavati — darmasya satyaṃ sthānam | vyavahārasya sākṣiṇaḥ | caritasya pustakaraṇam | lekhyaṃ rājājñeti | sākṣigrahaṇaṃ dharmaśāstradivyetarapramāṇaprāptyupalakṣaṇārtham | catuḥsādhanaḥ — sādhanāni sāmādyupāyā asminn iti sa tathoktaḥ | caturtho varṇāśramebhyo hitaḥ caturhitaḥ | caturvyāpī — caturaḥ kṛtṝn sākṣiṇaś ca sabhyān rājānaṃ vyāpnotīti | catuṣkārī — caturo dharmārthayaśolokānurāgān karotīti | aṣṭāṅgaḥ — aṣṭau rājarājapuruṣasabhyaśāstragaṇalekhaka-hiraṇyodakāny aṅgāni yasya so ‘ṣṭāṅgaḥ | eteṣām upayoga uttaratra vakṣyate | aṣṭādaśa vyavahārapadāni vyākhyātāni |
śataśākhaḥ — śataśabdaḥ sahasrādisaṃkhyopalakṣakaḥ bahuśākha iti yāvat | triyoniḥ — trīṇi kāmakrodhalobhātmakāni tebhyo yathāsaṃbhavaṃ pravartata iti kṛtvā triyonir ucyate | dvyabhiyogaḥ — dvau śaṅkābhiyogatattvābhiyogau yasya sa dvyabhigogaḥ | śaṅkābhiyogaḥ [61] asadbhiḥ kitavastenādibhiḥ saṃsargāt bhavati | tatvābhiyogo heḍābhidarśanam ekadeśopālambhaḥ pratyakṣadarśanaṃ vā | kvacin nikṣepādiśaṅkā satsaṃyogād api bhavatīti asatsaṃyogād ity udāharaṇamātram ity avagantavyam | dvidvāras tu dve dvāre kāryārambhapravṛttipūrvapakṣottarapakṣau | yasmin vāde sa dvidvāraḥ | dvigatis tu dve gatī bhūtacchale yasya saḥ | bhūtam — tatvārthasaṃyuktam, chalaṃ pramādādiyutam |
hārītenāpi kecana bhedā uktāḥ | ekamūlaḥ dvirutthānaḥ dviskandhaḥ dviphalaḥ | kātyāyanas tu tān vyācaṣṭe | ekaṃ sādhyaṃ mūlaṃ yasya sa ekamūlaḥ | dvirutthānaḥ — dviḥ dvisāmkhyābhyāvṛttam utthānaṃ yasminn iti dvirutthānaḥ | supsupeti samāsaḥ | etad uktaṃ bhavati | deyāpradānahiṃsārūpotthānadvayaṃ samād iti dvirutthānaḥ | dvyutthāna iti yāvat | dharmaśāstrārthaśāstrātmakaṃ skandhadvayaṃ yasya saḥ dviskandhaḥ | dve phale jayaparājayarūpe yasya sa dviphalaḥ | kātyāyanenāpy aparo bhedo darśitaḥ | pūrvottarapakṣapratyākalitakriyāpādabhedāt catuṣpād vyavahāra iti | pratyākalitam uktisvīkārānantaraṃ vivadamānayoḥ kasyātra kriyopanyāso nyāyya iti prāḍvivākādīnāṃ vamarśanam | kriyāpādo bhedo nāma likhitādipramāṇanirdeśaḥ | vivādātmakavyavahāraparyavasānabhāgatayā pratyākalitakriyayor api vyavahāratvāt | catuṣpāttvam uktam asmin pakṣe | pratyākalitavādaḥ tṛtīyaḥ pādaḥ | tathā kramasya vivakṣitatvāt | [62] yasmin mate pramāṇopanyāsānantaraṃ pramāṇābhāsaśaṅkāpanodakaṃ jayaparājayaphalakaṃ vimarśanaṃ pratyākalitaṃ tanmate saṃkhyaiva vivakṣitā, na tu kramaḥ | pratyākalitasya turīyapādatvāt | yathoktam –
bhāṣāpādas tu tarādyo dvitīyo marśanaṃ tathā |
kriyāpādas tathā cānyaḥ caturtho nirṇayaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
iti vīraśrī-gajapatigauḍeśvara-navakoṭikarṇāṭakalubarigeśvara
śaraṇāgatayamunāpurādhīśvara-huśanasāhisuratrāṇaśaraṇarakṣaṇa
śrīdurgāvarputrapavitracaritrarājādhirājarājaparameśvara-
śrīpratāparudradeva-mahārājaviracite
smṛtisaṅgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe śāstramukhasvarūpanirūpaṇaṃ nāma
dvitīyo vilāsaḥ
atha tṛtīyollāsaḥ
dharmasthānanirūpaṇam
atha vyavahārasvarūpamātranirūpaṇānantaraṃ saprapañcaṃ aṣṭādaśaprabhedabhinno vyavahāro nirūpayiṣyate | tadupayogitvena ādau dharmasthānāvasthānanirūpaṇaṃ nyāyyam | yady api dharmasthāne avasthitasya rājño vyavahāradarśanavidheḥ dharmasthānam ādau nirūpaṇīyam, tathāpi dharmasthāna eva sarvavyavahāranirṇaya iti niyamābhāvāt vyavahāranirūpaṇānantaraṃ tannirūpaṇam ity adoṣaḥ |
dharmādhikaraṇavyavasthā
yad vā pūrvaṃ vyavahāraḥ saṃkṣipyoktaḥ | idānīṃ dharmasthānapratipādanānantaraṃ vyavahāraḥ prapañcya iti noktadoṣaḥ | yatra sthāne āveditavyatatvaniṣkarṣaḥ dharmaśāstravicāreṇa nirṇetṛbhiḥ kriyata iti dharmasthānam | asyaiva dharmādhikaraṇam iti nāmāntaram | yat sthānaṃ dharmaśāstrair adhikriyata iti dharmādhikaraṇam iti | tac ca dharmādhikaraṇaṃ prāvyāṃ diśi karyam | “dharmasthānaṃ prācyāṃ diśi agnyudakaiḥ samavetaṃ kuryāt” iti śaṅkhasmaraṇāt | prācyāṃ diśi rājamandirād iti śeṣaḥ | atra lakṣaṇyāṃ sabhāṃ prāṅmukhīṃ gandhamālyadhūpāsanabījaratnapratimālekhyadevatāgnyambuyutāṃ kalpayet | evaṃ kalpitāṃ sabhāṃ prātaḥ nṛpaḥ praviśet |
gurūn jyotirvido vaidyān devān viprān purohitān |
yathārham etān saṃpūjya supuṣpābharaṇāmbaraḥ ||
abhivandya ca gurvādīn sumukhaḥ praviśet sabhām ||
iti bṛhaspatinoktatvāt | [64]
atra purohitādīn iti bahuvacanaṃ “pāśān” itivat karmamātrasyābhidhāyakam | na tu bahutvasyāpi | purohitasyaikatvāt | nṛpatir api brāhmaṇasahitaḥ praviśet |
vyvahārān didṛkṣus tu nṛpatir brāhmaṇaiḥ saha |
mantrajñair mantribhiś caiva vinītaḥ praviśet sabhām ||
iti manūktatvāt | caśabdaḥ prāḍvivākapurohitasabhyamantrigaṇakalekhakasādhyapālahiraṇya-dharmaśāstrair api saheti jñāpanārthaḥ | “nṛpaś ca puruṣasabhyagaṇakalekhakahemāgnyambu-prāḍvivākādisādhanasaṃpanno vyavahāraṃ paśyet” iti gautamasmaraṇāt |
vivāde pṛcchati praśnaṃ pratipraśnaṃ tathaiva ca |
priyapūrvaṃ prāḍvadati prāḍvivākas tataḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
pūrvottarapakṣau pṛcchatīti prāṭ, nirṇayaṃ viśeṣeṇa pravaktīti vivāka iti yaugakī tasya saṃjñā | prāḍvivāka iti pṛṣodarāditvāt sādhu | aṣṭādaśavivādapadābhijñaḥ tadbhedāṣṭasahasravit śrutismṛtiparāyaṇaḥ ānvīkṣikyādikuśalaḥ brāhmaṇaḥ prāḍvivākaḥ |
saprāḍvivākaḥ sāmātyaḥ sabrāhmaṇapurohitaḥ |
sasabhyaḥ prekṣako rājā svarge tiṣṭhati dharmataḥ || iti |
prāḍvivākaḥ — pūrvoktalakṣaṇaḥ | tena saha vartate iti saprāḍvivākaḥ | amātyalakṣaṇalakṣitaiḥ yuktaḥ sāmātyaḥ | sarvaśāstrajñānālobhanyāyabhāṣāprajñatvakramāyātatvāni amātyalakṣaṇāni |
śūdrasya vyavahāravicāraṇaniṣedhaḥ
sa ca viprakṣatriyavaiśyānām anyatama eva na śūdraḥ | [65]
dvijān vihāya yaḥ paśyet kāryāṇi vṛṣalaiḥ saha |
tasy prakṣubhyate rāṣṭraṃ balaṃ kośaś ca naśyati ||
iti vyāsenoktatvāt | sabhyān kurvīta vyavahāradarśanārtham |
sabhyajīvanakaraṇāvaśyakatvam
yathā sabhāyāṃ na tyajanti sthitiṃ tathā arthadānasaṃmānaprabhṛtibhir upāyair niyuñjīta | te ca — śrutādhyayanasaṃpannāḥ dharmajñāḥ satyavādinaḥ ripau mitra ca samāḥ sabhāyāṃ niyoktavyāḥ | deśācārānabhijñān nāstikān śāstravarjitān unmattān krūrān lubdhān na yuñjīta | atra sabhāsadaḥ sapta pañca trayo ‘pi vā kāryāḥ | atra prāḍvivākādīnām arthārthitayā ṛtvijām iva sabhāyattatvamātraṃ nādhikāraḥ | adhikāras tu vyvahāradarśane rājña evety uktam prakaraṇādau | ata eva “saprāḍvivākaḥ” ityādinā tasyaiva prādhānyena nirdeśaḥ | atra vyavahāradarśane abhiṣiktasya anabhiṣiktasya vā kṣatriyajātīyasya vā anyajātīyasya vā prajāparipālanādikartuḥ nṛpater adhikāraḥ | ata eva —
vyavahārān nṛpaḥ paśyet vidvadbhiḥ brāhmaṇaiḥ saha |
iti yājñavalkyaḥ |
prātar utthāya nṛpatiḥ śaucaṃ kṛtvā vidhānataḥ |
gurūn jyotirvido vaidyān devān viprapurohitān ||
yatharham etān saṃpūjya supuṣpābharaṇāmbaraḥ |
abhinandya ca gurvādīn sumukhaḥ praviśet sabhām ||
iti bṛhaspatiḥ | yat tv abhiṣekayuktasya rājño vyavahāradarśanaṃ prajāpatinoktam, tan na parisaṃkhyānārtham | anyathā [66] “vyavahārān nṛpaḥ paśyet” ityādiyājñavalkyavacanavyākopaḥ | rājaśabdaprayogo rājyakartari gauṇavṛttyā, aveṣṭyadhikaraṇa evoktatvāt | yadā nṛpatiḥ svayaṃ kāryanirṇayaṃ na karoti vyāsaṃgāt tadā tatra bahuśrutaṃ dakṣaṃ kulīnaṃ madhyasthaṃ śāstrapāragam anudvegakaraṃ sthiraṃ parabhīruṃ krodhavarjitaṃ dharmiṣṭhaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ niyuñjīta | yatra brāhmaṇo na syāt tatra kṣatriyaṃ yojayet |
vaiśyaṃ vā dharmaśāstrajñaṃ śūdraṃ yatnena varjayet |
yasya rājñas tu kurute śūdro dharmavivecanam |
tasya sīdati tad rāṣṭraṃ paṅke gaur iva paśyataḥ ||
rāṣṭrarañjanārthaṃ rājñā katipayair vaṇigbhir api saha saṃsadi sthātavyam |
caturo vaṇijas tatra kartavyā nyāyadarśinaḥ |
gaṇakalekhakāv api |
śabdābhidhānatatvajñau gaṇanākuśalāv api |
nānālipijñau kartavyau
tathā tatra saṃsadi horāgaṇitasaṃhitārūpaskandhatrayābhijñaṃ sphuṭapratyayakārakaṃ śrutādhyayanasaṃpannaṃ jyotirvidaṃ yojayet | sādhyapālas tu śūdra eva kramāyātaḥ dṛḍhaḥ sabhyānumataḥ arthipratyarthisākṣyādīnām āhvānādikaryasya kartā kartavyaḥ | agnyudake divyādyarthaṃ saṃpādye, śapathārthaṃ hiraṇyakośaśakṛddarbhā nidhātavyāḥ, evaṃ manvādibhiḥ śreṣṭhaṃ nirṇayasthānam uktam |
sādhāraṇanirṇayasthānāni
sainikānāṃ sainikā nirṇayasthānam | āraṇyakānām āraṇyakāḥ | [67] sārthikānāṃ sārthikāḥ | ubhayavāsināṃ grāmāḥ | kulikasārthamukhyapuragrāmanivāsinām ubhayānumatatvāt, evaṃ ca pratyekaniyatāni | grāmapauragaṇaśreṇicāturvidyavargakulakulikaniyuktanṛpatibhedena daśavidha sādhāraṇaṃ sthānam | grāmo nāma grāmākāreṇāvasthito janaḥ | pauraḥ puravāsināṃ samūhaḥ | kulānāṃ samūho gaṇaḥ | śreṇayaḥ rajakādyaṣṭādaśahīnajātayaḥ | cāturvidyaḥ ānvīkṣikyādividyācatuṣṭayopetaḥ | caturvidyaś ceti caśabdena vidvadrājapuruṣādisamuccayaḥ svīkṛtaḥ |
ekasya dharmaniyantṛtvaniṣedhaḥ
tasmān na vācyam ekena vidhijñenāpi dharmataḥ |
iti pitāmahena ekasya dharmakathananiṣedhāt | vargiṇo gaṇādayaḥ |
gaṇāḥ pāṣaṇḍapūgāś ca vrātāś ca śreṇayas tathā |
samūhasthāś ca ye cānye vargyākhyās te bṛhaspatiḥ || iti |
bṛhaspatigrahaṇaṃ – gaṇādiṣu iyam ākhyā pūrvam eva prasiddhā iti darśayitum | āyudhadhāriṇāṃ samūho vrātaḥ | kulāny arthipratyarthinoḥ svagotrāṇi | kulikāḥ – kecana arthipratyarthinoḥ gotrajā vṛddhāḥ | niyuktāḥ – prāḍvivākasahitās trayaḥ sabhyāḥ | nṛpatiḥ brāhmaṇādisahitaḥ |
sā sabhā apratiṣṭhitā pratiṣṭhitā mudritā śāstritā ceti caturvidhā | āraṇyakādisthānatraye yā sabhā sā apratiṣthitā, prāyeṇa deśāntaragamanayogitvād ubhayavādipratipanneṣu daśasthāneṣu tadabhāvāt pratiṣṭhitā | niyuktasthāne punaḥ mudrāhastādiprāḍvivākādyadhiṣṭhitā mudritā | nṛpasthāne śāstraniyantṛkatvāt śāstritā | [68]
pratiṣṭhitā pure grāme calā nāmāpratiṣthitā |
mudritādhyakṣasaṃyuktā rājayuktā tu śāstritā ||
iti smṛteḥ | adhyakṣaḥ – prāḍvivākaḥ | eteṣāṃ prābalyam uttarottarasyaiva |
kulāni śreṇayaś caiva gaṇāś cādhikṛto nṛpaḥ |
pratiṣṭhā vyavahārasya pūrvebhyaś cottarottaraḥ ||
iti nāradokteḥ nṛpasya prābalyam | ataś ca,
pūrvasthānagatā vādā dharmato ‘dharmato ‘pi vā |
rājasthānaṃ samāśritya pūrvavādaṃ bhajanti te ||
iti pitāmanenoketḥ, pūrvavādam — pūrvapakṣam ity arthaḥ | atra sabhāyāṃ rājā pūrvābhimukha upaviśet | sabhyās tu udaṅmukhāḥ | gaṇakaḥ paścimābhimukhaḥ | lekhako dakṣiṇābhimukha upaviśed iti niyamārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ | rājavyatirekeṇa prādvivākādibhiḥ nirṇayamātram eva kāryam, sāhasavivāde tu nirṇayamātram api na kāryam | yathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
rājñā ye ‘bhihitāḥ samyak kulaśreṇigaṇādayaḥ |
sāhasanyāyavarjyāni kuryuḥ kāryāṇi te nṛṇām || iti |
vāgdaṇḍo dhigdamaś caiva viprāyattāv ubhau smṛtau |
arthadaṇḍavadhāv uktau rājāyattāv ubhāv api ||
iti smaraṇāt |
dharmasthāpanaphalam
ajñānatimiropetān saṃdehapaṭalānvitān |
nirāmayān yaḥ kurute śāstrāñ janaśalākayā |
iha kīrtim avāpnoti labhate svargatiṃ ca saḥ || iti |
[69]        lobhadveṣādikaṃ tyaktvā yaḥ kuryāt kāryanirṇayam |
śāstroditena vidhinā tasya yajñaphalaṃ bhavet ||
adharmataḥ pravṛttaṃ tu nopekṣeran sabhāsadaḥ |
upekṣamāṇās te bhūpāḥ narakaṃ yānty adhomukhāḥ ||
nyāyamārgād apetaṃ tu jñātvā cittaṃ mahīpateḥ |
vaktavyaṃ tv apriyaṃ tatra na sabhyaḥ kilbiṣī tataḥ || iti |
snehāc cājñānato vāpi mohād vā lobhato ‘pi vā |
yatra sabhyo ‘nyathāvādī daṇḍyo ‘sabhyaḥ smṛto hi saḥ ||
sneho – rāgaḥ, moho – viparītajñānam | atra rāgāl lobhād bhayād vā anyathāvādī vivādaparājayanibandhanadamadviguṇadamaṃ daṇḍya ity āha yogayājñavalkyaḥ |
rāgāl lobhād bhayād vāpi smṛtyapetā(di)rthakāriṇaḥ |
sabhyāḥ pṛthak pṛthag daṇḍyāḥ vivādadviguṇaṃ damam ||
vivāde yo damo bhavati parājaye taddviguṇaṃ dāpya iti bahuvyākhyātṛsaṃmato ‘rtho grāhyaḥ | na punar vivādāspadībhūtadravyadviguṇa iti kasyacid vyākhyātur arthaḥ | prāḍvivākasabhyā vā vyavahāranirṇayāt pūrvam arthinā saha vijane saṃbhāṣaṇamātrād api daṇḍyā ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
anirṇīte tu yady arthe saṃbhāṣeta raho ‘rthinā |
prāḍvivāko ‘tha daṇḍyaḥ syāt sabhyāś caiva na saṃśayaḥ || iti |
nirṇayād ūrdhvam api sabhyadoṣaparijñāne tu daṇḍam āha sa eva |
sabhyadoṣāt tu yan naṣṭaṃ deyaṃ sabhyena tat tadā |
kāryaṃ tu kāriṇām eva niścitaṃ na vicālayet || [70]
