duṣṭasabhyenāpi nirṇītaṃ kāryaṃ na parāvartayet | kiṃ tu dhārṣṭyān naṣṭaṃ dāpayed ity arthaḥ |
na sā sabhā yatra na sati vṛddhāḥ na te vṛddhā ye na vadanti dharmam |
nāsau dharmo yatra na satyam asti na tat satyaṃ yac chalenānuviddham ||
rājā vigatamatsaraḥ sarvabhūteṣu samo vaivasvataṃ vrataṃ bibhrat vyavahārān paśyet | vaivasvatavrataṃ tu yathāha yamaḥ |
priyadveṣyān yathā prāpte kāle dharmo niyacchati |
tathā rājñā niyantavyāḥ prajās tad dhi yamavratam ||
iti smaraṇāt |
nṛpatiḥ dharmaśāstrārthaśāstrābhyām avirodhena samīkṣamāṇo vyavahāragatiṃ nayet | dharmaśāstrāṇi – vedāḥ sāṅgāḥ smṛtayo mīmāṃsā ca purāṇaṃ nyāyaśāstrāṇi | pramāṇāntaradṛṣṭārthaviṣayā smṛtir ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ca ubhayavidhaśāstrānusaraṇaṃ tayor mitho virodhābhāva eva | yadi virodhaḥ syāt tadā yājñavalkīyam upatiṣṭhate | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
smṛtyor virodhe nyāyas tu balavān vyavahārataḥ | iti |
vyavahārākhyanirṇayaviśeṣata ity arthaḥ | śrutimūlasmṛtyor virodhe vṛddhavyavahārāvagatasāvakāśatvāditarkabalena yathā vyavasthā tayor jāyate tathā grahītavyam ity arthaḥ | kevalaṃ śāstram āśrityāyaṃ nirṇayo na kartavyaḥ, kiṃ tu tarkānugṛhītam eva śāstram āśrityeti |
[71]        yas tarkeṇanusaṃdhatte sa dharmaṃ veda netaraḥ |
iti smaraṇāt | ataś cāparīkṣya vyavahāro na kartavyaḥ api tu prīkṣyaiva kartavya ity āha pitāmahaḥ |
asatyāḥ satyasaṅkāśāḥ satyāś cāsatyasannibhāḥ |
dṛśyante bhrāntijanakāḥ tasmād yuktaṃ parīkṣaṇam ||
sūkṣmo hi bhagavān dharmo duṣprekṣyo durvicāraṇaḥ |
ataḥ pratyakṣamārgeṇa vyavahāragatiṃ nayet ||
pratyakṣamārgaḥ – spaṣṭamārgaḥ | vyavahāradarśanopāyo manunā darśitaḥ |
yathā nayaty asṛkpātaiḥ mṛgasya mṛgayuḥ padam |
neyat tathānumānena dharmasya nṛpatiḥ padam ||
bāhyair vibhāvayel liṅgaiḥ bhāvam antargataṃ nṛṇām ||
svaravarṇeṅgitākāraiś cakṣuṣā bhāṣitena ca |
bhūtachalānurodhitvāt dvigatir samudāhṛtāh ||
vyavahārasya kvacid bhūtānusāritvam āśritya nirṇayaḥ, kvacic chalānusāritvam āśrityāpi | yadā tu sarvadā bhūtānusaraṇaṃ na śakyate kartuṃ tadā chalānusāreṇāpi sākṣyādipramāṇādhīno nirṇayaḥ kārya eva | vipratipattau “sākṣinimittā (satyavyavasthā) vyavasthitiḥ” iti gautamoktatvāt |        bhūtachalānusāritvāt dvigatiḥ samudāhṛtaḥ |
vyavahāraḥ ||
iti smṛteḥ chalānusaraṇe ‘pi rājā nādharmeṇa sajyate |
yad yad ācarati śreṣṭho dharmyaṃ vādharmyam eva vā |
kulādideśācaraṇāc caritraṃ tat parikīrtitam ||
[72]        deśasyānumatenaiva vyavasthā yā nirūpitā |
likhitā tu sadā dhāryā mudritā rājamudrayā ||
śāstravad yatnato rājā tāṃ nirīkṣya vinirṇayet || iti |
vyavahāraś caritreṇa nirṇetavya iti bhāvaḥ |
deśapaṭṭaṇagoṣṭheṣu puragrāmeṣu vāsinām |
teṣāṃ svasamayair dharmaśāstrato ‘nyeṣu taiḥ saha || iti |
yatra caite hetavao na vidyante tatra rājājñayā nirṇayam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
lekhyaṃ yatra na vidyeta na sākṣī na ca bhuktayaḥ |
pramāṇāni na santy ekaṃ pramāṇaṃ tatra pārthivaḥ ||
niścetuṃ ye na śakyāḥ syur vādāḥ saṃdigdharūpiṇaḥ |
teṣāṃ nṛpaḥ pramāṇaṃ syāt sa sarvasya prabhur yataḥ || iti |
vyavahārān svayaṃ paśyet sabhyaiḥ parivṛto ‘nvaham || iti |
svayaṃ – rājā | anvaham – etac caturdaśyādivyatiriktaviṣayam |
caturdaśī tv amāvāsyā paurṇamāsī tathāṣṭamī |
tithiṣv āsu na paśyet tu vyavahārapadaṃ nṛpaḥ ||
iti hārītokteḥ | anyāsv api tithiṣv āvartanād arvāg eva vyavahāradarśanam |
ādyād ahno ‘ṣṭabhāgād yad ūrdhvaṃ bhāgatrayaṃ bhavet |
sa kālo vyavahārāṇāṃ śāstradṛṣṭaḥ paraḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
asmin bhāgatrayaparimite kāle vyavahārān paśyed ity arthaḥ |
lekhako gaṇakaḥ śāstraṃ sādhyapālaḥ sabhāsadaḥ |
hiraṇyam agnir udakam aṣṭāṅgaṃ karaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
[73]        tad adhyasyāniśaṃ paśyet pauraiḥ kāryaṃ niveditam |
na tu rājā vaśitvena dhanalobhena vā punaḥ |
utpādayet tu kāryāṇi narāṇām avivādinām ||
kāryāṇi vivādān ity arthaḥ |
na rāgeṇa na lobhena na krodhena gṛhe nṛpaḥ |
parair aprāpitān arthān na cāpi savamanīṣayā || iti |
atra apavādam āha pitāmahaḥ |
padāni cāparādhāṃś ca chalāni nṛpates tathā |
svayam etāni gṛhṇīyāt nṛpas tv āvedakair vinā ||
padāni – dhikkṛtiḥ sasyaghātaḥ, agnidānam, vidhvaṃsaḥ kumāryāḥ, nipānasyopabhogaḥ, setukaṇṭaka kacchedaḥ (?), ārāmacchedaḥ, kṣetrasaṃcāraḥ, viṣapradānam, rājñāṃ drohakaraṇam, tanmudrāmantrayor bhedaḥ, bandhavimocanam, bhogadaṇḍayor grahaṇam, dānam utsekaḥ paṭahādighoṣaś ca, asvāmikadravyasvīkāraḥ, rājāvalīḍhadravyasvīkāraḥ, aṅgavināśanam, etāni dvāviṃśatipadāni nṛpajñeyāni — nṛpeṇa sādkṣād vā sūcakavacanād vā jñātvā draṣṭavyāni |
aparādhās tu — ājñollaṅghanam, strīvadhaḥ, varṇasaṃkaraḥ, parastrīgamanam, cauryam, patiṃ vinā garbhaḥ, vākpāruṣyam, avācyaṃ daṇḍapāruṣyaṃ ca, garbhasya pātanaṃ ceti | etāṃś ca svayam eva vicārayet vedakaṃ vinā | chalāni tu – parabhaṅgaḥ, parākṣepaḥ, prākāropari laṅghanam, nipānāyatanayor vināśaḥ, parighāpūraṇam, [74] rājachidraprakāśanam, antaḥpuravāsagṛhabhāṇḍāgāramahānaseṣu praveśaḥ, aniyogena bhojananirīkṣaṇam, viṇmūtraśleṣmavātānāṃ nṛpāgrataḥ prakṣepakāmanā, rājavidviṣṭasevā, adattavihitāsanam, suvarṇavastrābharaṇaparidhānam, svayaṃgṛhītatāmbūlabhakṣaṇam, aniyuktaprabhāṣaṇam, nṛpākrośakaraṇam, ekavāsodhāraṇam, anyaktatvam, muktakeśatvam, avakuṇṭhitatvam, vicitrāṅgatvam, sragvitvam, paridhānavidhūnanam, śiraḥpracchādanam, chidrānveṣaṇatātparyam, durjanasaṃsargam, dantakarṇākṣidarśanam, dantollekhanam, karṇanāsādiśodhanam ity etāni pañcāśacchalāni |
śāstrāṇi varṇadharmāṃs tu prakṛtīnāṃ ca bhūpatiḥ |
vyavahārasvarūpaṃ ca jñātvā kāryaṃ samācaret ||
prakṛtayas tu pitāmahena darśitāḥ – rajaka-carmakāra-naṭa-buruḍa-kaivartaka-mleccha-bhillādayaḥ |
varṇānām āsramāṇāṃ tu sarvadā ca bahiḥ sthitāḥ |
ete rājaikaniratā yady api prakīrṇakākhyavivādapada eva nirṇetavyāḥ | tathāpi prasaṅgād atroktiḥ | vyavahāras tv aṣṭādaśapadātmakaḥ prāṅnirūpitaḥ |
dharmaśāstraṃ puraskṛtya prāḍvivākamate sthitaḥ |
samāhitamatiḥ paśyed vyavahārān anukramāt ||
arthivacanānukrameṇety arthaḥ |
vyavahāradarśanacāturvidhyam
āgamaḥ prathamaḥ kāryo vyavahārapadaṃ tataḥ |
vicāro nirṇayaś ceti darśanaṃ syāc caturvidham || [75]
āgamo ‘rthivacanaśravaṇam ādau kartavyaḥ | tatas tadvacanam ṛṇādānādyanyatame pade ‘ntarbhāvyam | tataḥ pratijñottarapramāṇānāṃ vicāraḥ | tataḥ pramāṇato jayavadhāraṇam | evam ālocya rājā sabhyā vā vyavahāranirṇayaṃ kuryuḥ |
kena kasmin kadā kasmād evaṃ pṛcchet sabhāṃ gataḥ |
iti manusmṛtiṃ kātyāyano vyācakṣṭe |
kāle kāryārthinaṃ pṛcchet praṇataṃ purataḥ sthitam |
kiṃ kāryaṃ kā ca te pīḍā mā bhaiṣṭīr brūhi mānava || iti |
kiṃ kāryam ity anena deyāpradānasya jñāpanārthaḥ praśnaḥ | kā ca te pīḍeti hiṃsāyāḥ | evaṃ pṛṣṭaḥ kāryārthī tatas tasmai vaktavyajātaṃ vijñāpayet |
āvedayitṛpraśnaprakāraḥ
smṛtyācāravyapetena mārgeṇādharṣitaḥ paraiḥ |
āvedayati ced rājñe vyavahārapadaṃ hi tat ||
anena vākyena parakṛtam upadravaṃ svayam āvedayed ity ārthiko ‘rthaḥ pratīyate | parair iti bahuvacanam upalakṣaṇam, kenetyādimanuvacane ekavacanāntadarśanānurodhāt | cecchabdena āvedakasya svarucyaivāvedanaṃ kāryam | na tu rājājñayeti jñāpitam | ādharṣita ity anena vivāde yeṣām adhikāras te ‘py upalakṣitāḥ | na kartṛmātram uktam | [76]
yathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
arthinā sanniyukto vā pratyarthiprahito ‘pi vā |
yo yasyārthe vivadate tayor jayaparājayau || iti |
pitāmahas tu |
pitā bhrātā suhṛc cāpi bandhuḥ saṃbandhino ‘pi vā |
yadi kuryur upasthānaṃ vādaṃ tatra pravartayet ||
niyuktanirdeśaḥ
yaḥ kaścit kārayet kiṃcit niyogād yena kenacit |
tat tenaiva kṛtaṃ jñeyam anivartyaṃ hi tat smṛtam || iti |
evaṃ ca arthinā vā tadīyena puruṣeṇa vā āvedanaṃ kāryaṃ nānyenety anusaṃdheyam |
pitṛbhrātṛsuhṛtsaṃbandhibhinnānāṃ niyuktakāryakaraṇe daṇḍaḥ
yo na bhrātā na ca pitā na putro na niyogakṛt |
parārthavādī daṇḍyaḥ syāt vyavahāreṣu vibruvan ||
iti nāradokteḥ | kātyāyanas tu viśeṣam āha |
dānakarmakarāḥ śiṣyā niyuktā bāndhavās tathā |
vādino na ca daṇḍyāḥ syuḥ yas tato ‘nyaḥ sa daṇḍabhāk ||
nivedayitṛvinayaḥ anyathā daṇḍaḥ
saśastro ‘nuttarīyaś ca muktakeśaḥ sahāśanaḥ |
vāmahastena vā sragvī vadan daṇḍam avāpnuyāt || iti | [77]
ato naivaṃbhūto vaded ity abhiprāyaḥ |
vyavahāradarśanopakramaḥ
dharmāsanam adhiṣṭhāya saṃvītāṅgaḥ samāhitaḥ |
praṇamya lokapālebhyaḥ kāryadarśanam ārabhet ||
athāsedhavidhiḥ
atrāyam ākrośa āsedhanānantaram | yathāha nāradaḥ |
vaktavye ‘rthe hy atiṣṭhantam utkrāmantaṃ ca tadvacaḥ |
āsedhayed vivādārthī yāvad āhvānadarśanam || iti |
saṃdighe ‘py arthe nirṇayodāsīnaṃ nirṇayāya pravartitavyam ityādikam arthino vacanam avamanyamānaṃ āsedhayet – rājājñayā nirodhayet vyavahāradarśanārthāhvānaparyantaṃ vivādanirṇayārthīty arthaḥ | ayam āsedhaḥ – sthānakālakṛtapravāsakarmabhedāt caturvidhaḥ | sthānāsedhaḥ gṛhadevakulādisthānāt na calitavyam iti nirodhanam | kālakṛtaḥ “pañcamyādau bhavatsvarūpaṃ pradarśanīyam no cet rājājñām ullaṅghitavān” ity evaṃvidhaḥ | pravāsāsedhaḥ – yātrāvāraṇam | karmāsedhaḥ – puṇyaprasaraṇāder vāraṇam | na punar nirīkṣaṇāde indriyanirodhādeḥ saṃkaṭatvāt na tatra rājājñātikramo doṣam āvahati | nadīsaṃtaraṇakāntāradurgadeśopaplavādiṣu āsiddha utkrāman nāparādhnuyāt | anāsedhyam āsedhayan daṇḍabhāk bhavatīty āha nāradaḥ |
āsedhakāla āsiddha āsedhaṃ yo ‘tivartate |
taddine yo ‘nyathā kurvan āseddhā daṇḍabhāg bhavet || iti |
[78] anāsedhyāḥ kātyāyanenoktāḥ |
hastyaśvarathanauvṛkṣaparvatārūḍhāḥ – viṣamasthāḥ – vyādhyārthā vyasaninaś ca – yajamānāś ca – kāryāntarāviṣṭāś ca – tatsamaye nāsedhyāḥ | mattonmattajaḍās tu sarvadā anāsedhyāḥ, karṣako bījakāle ca senākāle ca sainikāḥ | kṛtakālaś ca madhye ‘nāsedhyaḥ samudvāhe bhṛtagavāṃ pracāreṇa pālāḥ śilpinaś ca tatkāle aprāptavyavahāraś ca dānonmukhaś ca | aprāptavyavahāro nāma apūrṇaṣoḍaśavarṣaḥ | viṣamasthaḥ – corādyapahṛtasarvasvaḥ | sainikā ity atra yuddhe āsanna iti śeṣaḥ |
iti vīraśrī-gajapatigauḍeśvara-navakoṭikarṇāṭakalubarigeśvara-
yamunāpurādhīśvara-huśanasāhisuratrāṇaśaraṇarakṣaṇa-
śrīdurgāvarputraparamapavitracaritrarājādhirājarājaparameśvara-
śrīpratāparudramahādeva-mahārājaviracite
smṛtisaṅgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe itikartavyatānirūpaṇaṃ nāma
tṛtīyollāsaḥ
atha turīyollāsaḥ
nirṇetuḥ kṛtyam
evam āsedhakaraṇānantaram ākrośaṃ kurvati vādini, tato nirṇetuḥ kṛtyam āha nāradaḥ |
tatas tadvacanaṃ sarvaṃ phalakādau vilekhayet |
gobhilo ‘pi |
rāgādīnāṃ yad ekena kopitaḥ karaṇe vadet |
tad om iti lekhet sarvam arthinaḥ phalakādiṣu ||
rāgadveṣalobhamohādīnāṃ madhye ekena rāgādinā yad vaded ity anvayaḥ | karaṇe – rājasamakṣam | tadanantaraṃ tadvākyaṃ brāhmaṇaiḥ sabhyaiḥ saha vimṛśya nyāyyaṃ cet sādhyapālena rājapuruṣeṇa prativādinam ākārayet, etad uktaṃ bhavati – phalakādau yal likhitaṃ tat tu janmāntare mayā asmai dhanaṃ dattam, tad asau na prayacchatītivat vicārayogyam yadi na syāt tadā tatpratyarthyāhvānārthaṃ sādhyapālaṃ preṣayed iti | sādhyapālo nāma arthipratyarthyāhvānārthaṃ rājñā niyuktaḥ sabhyānumato rājapuruṣaḥ | sādhyapālagrahaṇaṃ rājamudropalakṣaṇārthaṃ yathāh bṛhaspatiḥ |
yasyābhiyoge kurute tathyenāśaṅkayāpi vā |
tam evākāreyed rājā mudrayā puruṣeṇa vā || iti |
ākāraṇam āhvānam | uttaradāne tasyaivādhikārād ity abhiprāyaḥ | at uktaṃ kātyāyanena |
[80]        adhikāro ‘bhiyuktasya netarasyāsty asaṃgateḥ | iti |
asyārthaḥ – itarasyānabhiyuktasya asmin vivāde saṃbandhābhāvān nāsty uttaradāne ‘dhikāra iti | itarasyottaravāditvaṃ tredhetyāha –
uttarādhikāriṇaḥ
itaro ‘py abhiyuktena pratirodhīkṛto mataḥ |
samarpito ‘rthinā yo ‘nyaḥ paro dharmādhikāriṇi |
prativādī sa vijñeyaḥ pratipannaś ca yas tvayam || iti |
ayam arthaḥ – itaro ‘pi vivādāsaṃbaddho ‘py abhiyuktena pratirodhīkṛtaḥ – prativādīkṛtaḥ mataḥ – manvādīnām iti śeṣaḥ | anyo – dvitīyaḥ prativādi | paro vivādāsaṃbaddhaḥ dharmādhikāriṇi – prāḍvivāke abhiyuktena samarpitaḥ, arthinā vā svayam eva yaḥ pratipannaḥ – prativāditvenāṅgīkṛta iti | etac cānadhikāriṇaḥ prativāditvam akalyādyadhikāriviṣayaṃ veditavyam | atrādhikāriṇaḥ sākṣātkartṛtvasya suduṣkaratvāt | akalyo rogī | ādiśabdena apragalbhajaḍonmattavṛddhastrībālā vivakṣitāḥ |
akalyādīnāṃ pratinidhidānaṃ niyuktatvena |
ata eva hārītena: “akalyabālasthaviraviṣaamasthakriyākulakāryātipātivyasaninṛpakāryotsavākula-mattonmattapramattārtabhṛtyānām āhvānam akāryam” ity uktam | akalyo vyākhyātaḥ | viṣamasthaḥ utpannasaṅkaṭaḥ | kriyākulo nityanaimittikakarmavyagraḥ | yasya tv āgacchato
[81] gurutarakāryahāniḥ sa kāryātipātī | vyasanaṃ vinaṣṭaviyogādijaṃ duḥkhaṃ tadvān vyasanī | matto – madhvādimadanīyadravyanāśitabuddhiḥ | unmatto grahapittādibhiḥ | pramattaḥ sarvāvadhānahīnaḥ | ārto – viṣayādinā duḥkhitaḥ | bhṛtyagrahaṇam asvatantrastrīṇām upalakṣaṇārtham | ataś cākalyādiviṣa eva pratinidhividhānaṃ vyavatiṣṭhate |
mahābhiyoge sarve ‘py āhvānayogyāḥ |
yadā tv akalyādiprahitapratinidhinā na kāryaniṣpattiḥ | tadā akalyādīnām apy āhvānaṃ kāryam | mahābhiyogeṣu abhiyuktasyaivāhvānasyāvaśyakatvāt | mahābhiyogās tu manuṣyamāraṇasteyaparadārābhimarśanāpeyapānāni | asabhyavādeṣv apy akalyādaya āhvātavyā eva | asabhyavādās tu abhakṣyabhakṣaṇakanyādūṣaṇapāruṣyakūṭakaraṇanṛpadrohādīni | eteṣu pratinidhir na kāryaḥ | atra vyāsaḥ |
utpādayati yo hiṃsāṃ deyaṃ vā na prayacchati |
yācamānāya dauḥśīlyād ākṛṣyo ‘sau nṛpājñayā || iti |
āhūtasyānāgamane abhiyogānurūpadaṇḍaḥ
yas tu nṛpājñayā nāgacchati tasya daṇḍam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
āhūto yas tu nāgacchet darpād bandhubalānvitaḥ |
abhiyogānurūpeṇa tasya daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet || iti |
hīnakarmaṇi pañcāśat madhyame tu śatāvaraḥ |
gurukāryeṣu daṇḍaḥ syāt nityaṃ pañcaśatāvaraḥ || [82]
kārṣāpaṇānāṃ saṃkhyeyam |
kalpito yasya yo daṇḍa aparādhasya śaktitaḥ |
paṇānāṃ grahaṇaṃ tu syāt tanmūlyaṃ vātha rājani ||
ity kātyānayoktaḥ | śaktita ity uktyā āpadviṣaye na daṇḍaprakalpanaṃ | kiṃ tu nivṛttau punar āhvānaṃ kāryam | tac ca paramārthaṃ cet, athāparamārtham, tadā daṇḍayitvā paścān nyāyyaṃ pravartayet |
pratiṣṭhāpyas tu yatnena so ‘nyathā daṇḍabhāg bhavet |
daṇḍayitvā punaḥ paścāt rājā nyāyye pravartayet ||
evaṃ yena kenāpy upāyenānītam abhiyuktam abhiyoktā sabhāyāḥ purato ‘nyatra vā sthāne sthāpayet | yatra sthite dṛṣṭalakṣaṇapracchādanam aśakyaṃ tatra sthāpayet | tato rājñā pṛṣṭo ‘bhiyogī svābhiyogavṛttāntam āvedayet | abhiyoktṛkṛtāvedanamātrato bhūtānveṣaṇānupapatteḥ, dvyaoś chalavāditve niścite paścāc chalānusāreṇa darśanavidhiḥ | ato ‘bhiyuktasyāpi vṛttāntavedanam astīty avagantavyam |
vādiprativādinor ubhayoḥ pratibhūgrahaṇam
ubhayoḥ pratibhūr grāhyaḥ samarthaḥ kāryanirṇaye |
iti yājñavalkyasmaraṇād vādinoś chalatvaniścayānantaraṃ sādhitadhanadaṇḍayor anāyāsena prāptirūpakāryanirṇayasamarthaḥ pratibhūr arthipratyarthnor dvayor api grāhyo vyavahāradraṣṭrety arthaḥ |
atra varjanīyāḥ
atra pratibhūkarmaṇi kecin niṣiddhāḥ – śatrusvāmitadadhikṛtaniruddhadaṇḍitasaṃśaya-sthariktamitrātyantavāsirājakāryaniyukta[83]pravrajitadhanadānāśaktāvijñātāḥ saṃśayasthāḥ abhiśastāḥ | atyantavāsinaḥ – naiṣṭhikabrahmacāriṇaḥ | pravrajitāḥ saṃnyāsinaḥ | pratibhūdānāsamarthaṃ praty āha kātyāyanaḥ |
atha cet pratibhūr nāsti sārthayor yasya vādinaḥ |
sa rakṣito dinasyānte dadyād dūtāya vetanam || iti |
dūtaḥ sādhyapālaḥ | vetanaṃ bhṛtiḥ | sārthadvayaṃ koṭidvayam | evaṃ gṛhītvā vādī nyāyye pravartyaḥ |
abhiyoktrādīnāṃ svasvārthanivedanakramaḥ
abhiyoktrādīnām uktiprakāraṃ kātyāyana āha |
abhiyoktā vadet pūrvam abhiyuktas tv anantaram |
tayor ukte sadasyās tu prāḍvikākas tataḥ param || iti ¦
pūrvaṃ vaded iti – pratijñāṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | pūrvavādo ‘bhiyoktur iti niyamasya kvacid vyabhicāram āha |
yasya vāpy adhikā pīḍā akāryaṃ vādhikaṃ bhavet |
tasyārthivādo dātavyo na yaḥ pūrvaṃ nivedayet ||
pūrvanivedanam aprayojakam ity arthaḥ | arthivādaḥ pūrvapakṣaḥ | yatrobhayor api parasparam arthitvaṃ sādhyabhedād yugapad bhavati tatra bārhaspatyam upatiṣṭhate |
ahaṃpūrvikayā tāvad arthipratyarthinau yadā |
vādo varṇānupūrvyeṇa grāhyaḥ pīḍām avekṣya vā ||
grāhyapīḍāvekṣaṇaṃ samānavarṇayor iti dhyeyam | [84]
anekavādiyugmānāṃ yugapadupasthāne tu manuḥ |
arthānarthāv ubhau buddhvā dharmādharmau ca kevalau |
varṇāśramau ca sarvāṇi paśyet kāryāṇi kāryiṇām ||
pratijñārthasthirīkaraṇam
āveditaṃ sādhyam eva lekhyam | na sādhyāntaram | na punar yāvad āveditaṃ tāvad eva lekhyam iti nyāyam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pratyarthino ‘grato lekhyaṃ yathāveditam arthinā | iti |
ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
samāmāsatadardhānarnāmajātyādicihnitam | iti |
“yad āveditam” iti kṛtvā candrikākāreṇa “yat sādhyam” iti vyākhyātam | vijñānayoginā tu “yathāveditam” iti paṭhitvā yathā –yena prakāreṇa pūrvavedanākāla evāveditaṃ tathā na punar anyathā, anyathāvāditve tu bhaṅgaprasaṅgāt |
anyavādī kriyādveṣī nopasthātā niruttaraḥ |
āhūtavyapavāyī ca hīnaḥ pañcavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
āvedanākāle arthivacanasya likhitatvāt punar lekhanam anarthakam ity ata āha – samāmāsetyādi | yatheti thālpratyayāntaṃ prakāravacanaṃ kṛtvā vyācakṣāṇasya vijñāneśvarasya āvedanasamaye yāval likhitaṃ tāval lekhanīyam, kiṃ tu sādhyam eva lekhanīyam ity āśayaḥ | anyavādivacanodāharaṇāt | anyavāditvaṃ nāma svasādhyaṃ vihāya sādhyāntarasvīkāraḥ iti | ato vijñānyogīndracandrikākārayor vyākhyānaprakārabheda eva nārthabhedaḥ | [85] yathāruci svīkāryam | ādiśabdena dravyatatsaṃkhyāsthānavelākṣamā liṅgādīni gṛhyante |
āvedanapatralekhanakramaḥ
arthavad dharmasaṃyuktaṃ paripūrṇam anākulam |
sādhyavad vācakapadaṃ prakṛtārthānubandhi ca ||
prasiddham aviruddhaṃ ca niścitaṃ sādhanakramam |
saṃkṣiptam anvitārthaṃ ca deśakālāvirodhi ca ||
varṣartumāsapakṣāharvelādeśapraveśavat |
sthānāvasathasādhyākhyājātyākāravayoyuktam ||
sādhyapramāṇasaṃkhyāvad ātmapratyarthināmavat |
parātmapūrvajānekarājānamabhir aṅkitam ||
kṣamāliṅgātmapīḍāvat kathitāhartṛdāyakam |
yad āvedayate rājñe tad bhāṣety abhidhīyate || iti |
bhāṣā – pratijñā – pakṣa ity anarthāntaram | yady api sarveṣu vyavahāreṣu saṃvatsarādiviśeṣaṇaṃ nopayujyate, tathāpy ādhipratigrahakrayeṣu nirṇayārtham upayujyata ity adoṣaḥ | arthavat – prayojanavat, dharmasaṃyuktam – alpākṣarapragītatvādiguṇānvitam | paripūrṇam – adhyāhārānapekṣam | anākulam – asaṃdigdhākṣarakam, sādhyavat – siṣādhayiṣitārthāhīnam, vācakapadam – gauṇalakṣaṇādirahitam, prakṛtārthānubandhi – prāgāveditārthāvirodhi, prasiddham – lokaprasiddhavastuviṣayam | aviruddham – purarāṣṭraprāḍvivākanṛpādyaviruddham, tathā pūrvāparāviruddhaṃ pratyakṣādipramāṇāviruddhaṃ, vyāvahārikadharmāviruddhaṃ [86] cāviruddhapadenocyate | niścitam – arthāntarasaṅkārahitam, sādhanakṣamam – sādhanārham, saṃkṣiptam – anativistṛtam, nikhilārtham anavaśeṣitavaktavyam, deśakālāvirodhi | madhyadeśīyaṃ kramukakṣetrādi | śaratkālīnam āmraphalasāhasram apahṛtam ity evamādivirodharahitam | śiṣṭaṃ vyaktārtham |
sthāvaraviṣayāvedanapatrikālekhane viśeṣaḥ
jātiḥ saṃjñādhivāsaś ca pramāṇaṃ kṣetranāma ca |
pitṛpaitāmahaṃ caiva pūrvarājānukīrtanam |
sthāvareṣu vivādeṣu daśaitāni nivedayet ||
iti smaraṇāt | deśasthānādayaḥ sthāvareṣv evopayujyante | adhivāsaḥ – samīpadeśavāsī janaḥ | pramāṇam – gocarmādiparimāṇam | kṣetranāma – śālyādikṣetraviśeṣaṃ vyaktārtham | etad uktaṃ bhavati – samāmāsādīnāṃ yasmin vyavahāre yāvad rūpaṃ yujyate tatra tāval lekhanīyam iti yājñavalkyavacanasya tātparyārthaḥ | hārīto ‘pi ¦
āsanaṃ śayanaṃ yānaṃ kāṃsyatāmram ayomayam |
dhānyam aśmamayaṃ vastraṃ dvipadaṃ ca catuṣpadam ||
maṇimuktāphalādīni hīrakaṃ raupyakāñcanam |
yadi dravyasamūhaḥ syāt saṃkhyā kāryā tathaiva ca ||
yasmin deśe ca yat saṃkhyā mānamānena mīyate |
tena tasmin tathā saṃkhyā kartavyā vyavahāribhiḥ || iti |
tena mānena mītvā saṃkhyā kāryety arthaḥ |
anādeyavyavahāraḥ
[87]
kātyāyanas tu viśeṣam āha |
deśakālavihīnaś ca dravyasaṃkhyāvivarjitaḥ |
kriyāpramāṇahīnaś ca pakṣo ‘nādeya iṣyate ||
ayam arthaḥ – yatra yatra yad upayujyate tatra tatra tal lekhanīyam | tadabhāve sādhyasiddhikaraṇāśakter upayogavihīnaḥ pakṣo ‘nādeya eveti | kriyāpramāṇam – sādhyaparimāṇam, prayojanarahitatvād iti lakṣaṇahīno ‘pi pakṣo heya eva | “aprasiddhanirābādhanirarthakaniṣprayojanāsādhyaviruddhāḥ pakṣābhāsāḥ” iti smaraṇāt | etac candrikākāro vyācaṣṭe | yad āha candrikākāraḥ – tatrāprasiddho bṛhaspatinā nirūpitaḥ |
na kenacit smṛto yas tu so ‘prasiddha iti smṛtaḥ |
tatrodāharaṇam – palasahasrakṛtas tālo gṛhīta iti | nirābādho nāma nirupadravaḥ | yathā – ayam asmadbhavanapradīpaprabhayā svabhavane (svagṛhe) vyavaharatīti | nirarthakaniṣprayojanau tu bṛhaspatinā darśitau |
svalpāparādhaḥ svalpārtho nirarthaka iti smṛtaḥ |
kāryabādhāvihīnas tu vijñeyo niṣprayojanaḥ ||
ṛṇādānādyaṣṭādaśapadānantarbhūto nirarthakaḥ |
vākpāruṣyādibhiḥ sādhyasiddhyanupayuktaiḥ sahitaḥ pakṣo niṣprayojana ity arthaḥ | asādhyaviruddhāv api tenaiva darśitau | [88]
mamānena pradātavyaṃ śaśaśṛṅgakṛtaṃ dhanuḥ |
asaṃbhāvyam asādhyaṃ tu pakṣam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ ||
yasminn āvedite pakṣe prāḍvivāke ‘tha rājani |
purarāṣṭraviruddhaṃ syād viruddhaḥ so ‘bhidhīyate || iti |
vijñānayoginā tu anyathā vyākhyātam – “pakṣavad avabhāsamānāḥ pakṣalakṣaṇarahitāḥ pakṣābhāsāḥ” iti pakṣābhāsalakṣaṇam uktvā –
aprasiddhaṃ nirābādhaṃ nirarthaṃ niṣprayojanam |
asādhyaṃ vā viruddhaṃ vā pakṣābhāsaṃ vivarjayet ||
iti viśeṣalakṣaṇāny uktvā tāni vyākhyātāni | vyākhyāprakāras tu, yad āha vijñānayogī – aprasiddham – madīyaṃ śaśaviṣāṇaṃ gṛhītvā na prayacchatītyādi | nirābādham – asmadgṛhapradīpaprakāśena ayaṃ svagṛhe vyavaharatītyādi | idam udāharaṇam ubhayoḥ samānam | nirarthakam – abhidheyarahitaṃ kacaṭatapaṃ gajaḍadabam ityādi | niṣprayojanam yathā – ayaṃ devadatto ‘smadgṛhasaṃnidhau sasvaram adhīta ityādi | etad ubhayoḥ samānam | asādhyam yathā – ahaṃ devadattena sabhrūbhaṅgaṃ hasita ityādi | viruddhaṃ yathā – aham anena śapa ityādi | purādiviruddhaṃ vā | asādhyasyodāharaṇaṃ candrikākāreṇa nirarthakodāharaṇaṃ kṛtam | svalpārtho nirarthakasmṛter na doṣaḥ | atrāprasiddhodāharaṇam asādhyasyodāharaṇaṃ kṛtam |
mamānena pradātavyaṃ śaśaśṛṅgakṛtaṃ dhanuḥ |
iti vacanān na doṣaḥ | evaṃ vijñānayoginā lokaprasiddham apekṣya hetvābhāsā vivṛtāḥ | [89]
candrikākāreṇa tu vacanānurodhena vyākhyātam itīyān bhedaḥ | eteṣām asādhyādīnāṃ devadattena sabhrūbhaṅgaṃ hasitam ityādīnāṃ sādhanatvāsaṃbhavād asādhyatvam | alpakālatvān na sākṣisaṃbhavaḥ | likhitaṃ dūrata eva alpatvān na divyam iti |
nāradādibhis tv anye bhāṣādoṣā darśitāḥ | te ‘pi sarve pakṣābhāsalakṣaṇalakṣitatvād grāhyāḥ | granthavistarabhayān na prapañcitāḥ | ata eva yogīśvareṇa pakṣalakṣaṇarahitānāṃ pakṣavad avabhāsamānānāṃ pakṣābhāsatvaṃ siddham eveti na pṛthak pakṣābhāsā uktāḥ | tathāpi diṅmātram uktam ity avagantavyam |
yat tu pitāmahenoktam
anekapadasaṃkīrṇaḥ pūrvapakṣo na sidhyati |
iti, yady atra padaśabdo vastuvācakaḥ syāt tadā na doṣaḥ, madīyam anena hiraṇyaṃ vāso rūpakādi cāpahṛtam ity evaṃvidhasyādṛṣṭatvāt | yadi ca atra padaśabdaḥ sthānavācī ṛṇādānādipadasaṃkare pakṣābhāva iti cet, tad api na, madīyaṃ rūpakam anena vṛddhyā gṛhītaṃ suvarṇaṃ cāsya haste nikṣiptam madīyaṃ kṣetram ayam apaharati cetyādīnāṃ pakṣatvam iṣata eva | kiṃ tu kriyābhedāt krameṇa vyavahārabhedo na yugapad ity etāvat,
bahupratijñaṃ yat kāryaṃ vyavahāreṣu niścitam |
kāmaṃ tad api gṛhṇīyāt rājā tatvabubhutsayā ||
iti kātyāyanasmaraṇāt, anekapadasaṃkīrṇaḥ pūrvapakṣaḥ yugapan na sidhyatī vacanārthaḥ | bhūmiśabdaḥ phalakādīnām upalakṣakaḥ |
[90]        pratijñādoṣanirmuktaṃ sādhyaṃ satkāraṇānvitam |
likhitaṃ lokasiddhaṃ ca pakṣaṃ pakṣavido viduḥ ||
lokasiddham – vyāvahārikadharmāviruddham, lokaprasiddam ity evaṃvidhārthasya pratijñādoṣanirmuktam ity anenaiva gatārthatvāt |
abhiyogacāturvidhyam
ayaṃ pakṣaḥ śaṅkābhiyogatathyalabdhābhyarthanapunarnyāyātmakatvena caturvidhaḥ |
śodhayet pūrvavādaṃ tu yāvan nottaradarśanam |
mohād vā yadi śāṭhyād vā yan noktaṃ pūrvavādinā |
uttarāntargataṃ vāpi tadgrāhyam ubhayor api ||
uttarāntargataṃ nāma uttare dīyamāne vādinā procyamānam ity arthaḥ | bṛhaspatis tu viśeṣam āha |
abhiyoktāpragalbhatvāt vaktuṃ notsahate yadā |
tasya kālaḥ pradātavyaḥ kāryaśaktyanurūpataḥ || iti |
athottaravādaḥ
evaṃ niścite pūrvapakṣe uttaraṃ dātavyam ity āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
viniścite pūrvapakṣe grāhyāgrāhyaviśeṣite |
pratijñāte sthirībhūte lekhayed uttaraṃ tataḥ ||
uttaralakṣaṇam
pakṣasya vyāpakaṃ sāram asaṃdigdham anākulam |
avyākhyāgamyam ity etad uttaraṃ tadvivo viduḥ || iti |
[91] avyākhyāgamyam ity atra prasiddhaprayogeṇa duḥśliṣṭavibhaktisamāsādhyāhārābhidhānena vā adeśabhāṣābhidhānena vā | śeṣaṃ sugamam | saṃgrahakāreṇāpi |
ananyārtham abhinnārthaṃ nyāyāgamasamanvitam |
anyūnānadhikaṃ bhraṣṭam ananyākṣarasaṃbhavam ||
bhāṣāyām iti patreṣu likhitāyām anantaram |
pratyarthinaś śrutārthasya kālo ‘yaṃ dātum uttaram ||
ananyārthādilakṣaṇānvitaṃ yathā bhavati tathā likhitāyāṃ bhāṣāyām ity arthaḥ | ayaṃ ca kālaniyamo gavādivivādeṣv eva |
gobhūhiraṇyastrīsteya pāruṣyātyayikeṣu ca |
sāhaseṣv abhiśāpe ca sadya eva vivādayet ||
abhiśāpo – vadhānuśaṃsanam | tasya pāruṣyabhedatve ‘py ādarāt punarvacanam | yājñavalkyo ‘py āha |
sāhasasteyapāruṣyago’bhiśāpātyaye striyām |
vivādayet sadya eva kālo ‘nyatrecchayā smṛtaḥ ||
anyatra ṛṇādānādāv ity arthaḥ |\
ṛṇopanidhiniṣepadānasaṃbhūyakarmaṇām |
samaye dāyabhāge ca kālaḥ kāryaḥ prayatnataḥ ||
samayaḥ – samayānapākarma ¦ ayam arthaḥ – sadyaḥ kṛteṣu sadya eva vivādayet | kālātīteṣu kālaṃ dadyād iti | ata eva pitāmahaḥ |
[92]        sadya kṛteṣu kāryeṣu sadya eva vivādayet |
kālātīteṣu vā kālaṃ dadyāt pratyarthinaṃ prabhuḥ || iti |
pratyarthipadam arthino ‘py upalakṣakam | yathāha nāradaḥ |
matir notsahate yasya vivāde vaktum icchataḥ |
tasya kālaḥ pradātavyo hy arthipratyarthinor api ||
kālaparimāṇam āha sa eva |
sadyo  vaikāhapañcāhau tryahaṃ vā gurulāghavāt |
bhaven māsaṃ tripakṣaṃ vā saptāhaṃ vā ṛṇādiṣu || iti |
kātyāyanas tu viśeṣam āha |
yāvān yasmin samācāraḥ pāramparyakramāgataḥ |
taṃ pratīkṣya yathānyāyam uttaraṃ dāpayen nṛpaḥ ||
uttarabhedāḥ tallakṣaṇāni ca
idam uttaraṃ mithyāsaṃpratipattipratyavaskandanaprāṅnyāyarūpeṇa caturvidham | mithyālakṣaṇam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
abhiyukto ‘bhiyogasya yadi kuryād apahnavam |
mithyā tat tu vijānīyād uttaraṃ vyavahārataḥ || iti |
tat tu mithyottaraṃ caturvidham |
mithyaitan nābhijānāmi tadā tatra na sannidhiḥ |
ajātaś cāsmi tatkāla iti mithyā caturvidhā ||
saṃpratipattyuttarasya lakṣaṇam āha sa eva |
sādhyasya satyavacanaṃ pratipattir udāhṛtā | iti |
[93] ubhayor udāharaṇam – rūpakaśataṃ mahyaṃ dhārayatīty ukte satyaṃ dhārayāmīti saṃpratipattiḥ | nāhaṃ dhārayāmīti mithyā | pratyavaskandanaṃ nāma gṛhītaṃ pratidattaṃ pratigraheṇa vā labdham | yathāha nāradaḥ |
arthinā likhito yo ‘rthaḥ pratyarthigaditaṃ tathā |
prapadya kāraṇaṃ brūyāt pratyavaskandanaṃ smṛtam || iti |
prāṅnyāyottayaṃ tu yatrābhiyukta eva brūyāt – asminn arthe ‘ham anenābhiyuktaḥ tatra cāyaṃ vyavahāramārgeṇa parājita iti | yathoktaṃ hārītena |
asminn arthe mamānena vādaḥ pūrvam abhūt tadā |
jito ‘yam iti ced brūyāt prāṅnyāyaḥ syāt tad uttaram ||
uttarābhāsāḥ
evam uttaralakṣaṇe sthite uttaralakṣaṇarahitānāṃ uttaravad avabhāsamānānāṃ uttarābhāsatvam arthasiddham | ato yogīśvarādibhiḥ spaṣṭasiddhatvān noktam | tathāpītarasmṛtiṣu spaṣṭam udāharaṇāt diṅmātram udāhriyate — aprasiddhaviruddhātyalpātibhūrisaṃdigdhāsaṃbhāvyā-vyaktānyārthātidoṣavad avyāpakavyatyastapadanigūḍhārthākulavyākhyāgamyāsārānuttara-lakṣaṇā uttarābhāsāḥ | lāñchānāvayavasaṃsthānasaṃkhyāsamayānabhijñoktam adeśabhāṣayoktaṃ cāprasiddham |
pratidattaṃ mayā bālye pratidattaṃ mayā na hi | iti |
[94] evamākāreṇa pūrvāparaviruddhābhidhānaṃ viruddhottaram ity arthaḥ | atyalpaṃ punar ayaṃ purā mayā yācita iti vaktavye purā mayāyam ity etāvaty ukte tad iti | atibhūri – gṛhītam ity etāvanmātreṇa satyottare vaktavye kāryaṃ tena kṛtaṃ mayā tu purā gṛhītam ity etad adhikaṃ bhūri |
deyaṃ mayeti vaktavye mayā deyam itīdṛśam |
saṃdigdham uttaraṃ jñeyaṃ vyavahāre budhais tathā || iti |
mayādeyam ity atrākāraśleṣasaṃbhedād adeyam ity avagamāt saṃdigdham evaṃ vidham iti | asmābhir deyaṃ dravyaṃ asmatprapautrapautreṇa dattam ity evaṃvidham uttaram asaṃbhāvyam | avyaktaṃ tu sukhenābhidhātum aśakyam | arthino yad asadvṛttakathana eva paryavasitaṃ tad anyārtham | atyuktyā roṣavad atiroṣavat | śataṃ deyam ity ukte dviśatakam ityādikam |
asmai dattaṃ mayā sārdhaṃ sahasram iti bhāṣite |
pratidattaṃ tadardhaṃ yat tad ihāvyāpakaṃ smṛtam ||
pūrvavādī kriyāṃ yāvat samyaṅ naiva nivedayet |
mayā gṛhītaṃ pūrvaṃ no tadvyastapadam ucyate ||
pūrvapakṣaniścayād arvāk dīyamānaṃ mithyetyādyuttaraṃ vyastapadam ity arthaḥ |
tat kiṃ tāmarasaṃ kaścid agṛhītaṃ na dāsyati |
nigūḍhārthaṃ tu taj jñeyam uttaraṃ tryavahārataḥ || iti |
tāmarasaśabdasya sārvabhaumaprayogāsiddhatvāt | vakroktyādimac ca nigūḍhārtham ity arthaḥ | ananvitānekapadārthavad ākulārtham iti | yathā |
kiṃ tenaiva sadā deyaṃ mayā deyaṃ bhaved iti |
[95] ityādi | vyākhyāgamyam – svarūpato duravabodham | kākadantasadbhāvottaravan niṣprayojanam asāravad ity arthaḥ | ekasyāṃ bhāṣāyāṃ vadaty asatyaṃ mithyetyādyanekottaram anuttaram iti | yathāha nāradaḥ |
pakṣaikadeśe yat satyam ekadeśe ca kāraṇam |
mithyā caivaikadeśe ca saṃkarāt tad anuttaram ||
uttarāṇāṃ sāṃkarye anuttaraṃ nānyatheti jñāpanārthaṃ saṃkarād iti hetuprayogaḥ |
na caikasmin vivāde tu kriyā syād vādinor dvayoḥ |
na cātra siddhiri ubhayoḥ na caikatra kriyādvayam ||
iti kātyāyanokteḥ | mithyākāraṇayoḥ saṃkare arthipratyarthinor dvayor api kriyā prāpnoti |
mithyā kriyā pūrvavāde kāraṇe prativādini |
iti smaraṇāt | tad ubhayam ekasmin vyavahāre viruddham | yathā – rūpakaśataṃ suvarṇaṃ cānena gṛhītam ity abhiyoge rūpakaśataṃ na gṛhītaṃ suvarṇaṃ ca gṛhītaṃ pratidattaṃ ceti | kāraṇaprāṅnyāyasaṃkare tu pratyarthina eva kriyādvayam |
prāṅnyāyapratyavaskande pratyarthī nirdiśet kriyām | iti |
yathā – suvarṇaṃ pratidattaṃ rūpakaśate vyavahāramārgeṇa parājita iti | atra prāṅnyāye jayapatreṇa vā prāṅnyāyadarśibhir bhāvayitavyam | kāraṇottare tu sākṣilekhakādibhir bhāvayitavyam ity avirodhaḥ | evam uttaratritayasaṃkare ‘pi draṣṭavyam | yathānena suvarṇaṃ rūpakaśataṃ vastrāṇi ca gṛhītānīty abhiyoge satyaṃ suvarṇaṃ gṛhītaṃ pratidāsyāmi rūpakaśataṃ tu na gṛhītaṃ vastuviṣaye pūrvanyāyena [96] parājita iti | evaṃ catuṣkasaṃkare ‘pi | eteṣāṃ cānuttaratvaṃ yogapadyena tasya tasyāṅgasya tena tena vinā asiddheḥ krameṇottaratvam eva | etad uktaṃ bhavati – yatra punaḥ sarvathā ekam asaṃkīrṇam uttaraṃ na labhyate tatrāgatyā ekaikāṃśam uttaraṃ kṛtvā krameṇaikaikapratijñāyā ekaikam uttaraṃ grāhyam | anyathā tatra nirṇayābhāvāt | ata evoktam “saṃkarāt tad anuttaram” iti | uktavidhayā saṃkare nirākṛte syād evottaram ity abhiyogaḥ | yatra saṃkare yasya prabhūtārthaviṣayatvaṃ tatkriyopādānena pūrvaṃ vyavahāraḥ pravartayitavyaḥ paścād alpaviṣayottaropādānena | yatra saṃpratipatter uttarāntarasya ca saṃkaraḥ tatra uttarāntaropādānena vyavahāro draṣṭavyaḥ | saṃpratipattau kriyāyā abhāvāt | hārītas tu |
mithyottaraṃ kāraṇaṃ ca syātām ekatra ced ubhe |
satyaṃ cāpi sahānyena tatra grāhyaṃ kim uttaram ||
ity uktvā |
yat prabhūtārthaviṣayaṃ yatra vā syāt kriyāphalam |
uttaraṃ tatra vijñeyam asaṃkīrṇam ato ‘nyathā ||
ity uktam |
ato ‘nyathā saṃkīrṇaṃ bhavatīti śeṣaḥ | ata eva pakṣavyāpakānekottaraviṣaye nāradaḥ |
mithyākāraṇayor vāpi grāhyaṃ kāraṇam uttaram |
iti kāraṇasyānanyathāsiddhatvāt | mithyāto gurutvād ity abhiprāyaḥ | ataś cottarāntarasaṃnipāte ‘pi gurutaraṃ grāhyam | asmin [97] vacane mithyākāraṇayoḥ udāharaṇamātratvāt | yatra punaḥ vādiprativādināṃ traivarṇikatvaṃ tatra varṇānukrameṇa gauravāpekṣayā vyavahāro nirṇetavyaḥ | tathā ca hārītaḥ |
varṇināṃ saṃkaro yatra brāhmaṇādikrameṇa tu |
vyavahāras tu nirṇeyo gauravāpekṣayā bhavet || iti |
gauravāpekṣayā brāhmaṇādikrameṇa, brāhmaṇakṣatriyayor vivāde brāhmaṇasyālpataraṃ sādhyam, kṣatriyasya bahutaram, tathāpi varṇagauravāpekṣayā alpīyaḥsādhyam eva grāhyam iti vacanārthaḥ | yatra punaḥ saṃkarottare tulyabalatayā na pūrvoktakramahetur asti tatrāpy aicchikakrameṇottaraṃ grāhyam, nirṇayasyāvaśyakatvāt | tathā hi – prāṅnyāyasaṃkare tadubhayanirṇāyakajayapatrādilikhitasākṣyādisaṃbhave vyavahāranirṇaye pratyarthinānyatarapakṣam avalambya sthātavyam, nobhayam iti yathāruci śabdasyārthaḥ | evaṃ candrikāyām api pratyavaskandaprāṅnyāyasaṃkare pratyarthinā yathāruci sthīyatām ity atra yathāruci śabdārtho ‘nusandheyaḥ | nanv evaṃ saṃpratipattyuttarasya pakṣopamardakatvābhāvād anuttaratvam iti cet, maivam, saṃpratipatter api sādhyatvena upadiṣṭasya pakṣasya siddhatvena upanyāsasādhyatvanirākaraṇād evottaratvam iti | nanu kāraṇamithyottare tu pūrvoktanyāyaprasaraḥ | “iyaṃ gaur madīyā amukasmin kāle naṣṭā asya gṛhe dṛṣṭā” iti śṛṅgagrāhikayā vadati | anyas tu “mithyaitat etatpradarśitakālāt pūrvam eva sthitā madgṛhe jātā ca” iti vadati | idaṃ tāvat pakṣanirākaraṇasamarthatvān nānuttaram | nāpi mithyaiva, kāranopanyāsāt | nāpi kāraṇam, ekadeśasyābhyupagamābhāvāt | [98] tasmād etat sakāraṇamithyottaram | atra prativādinaḥ kriyā “kāraṇe prativādinaḥ” iti vacanāt | nanu, “mithyākriyā pūrvavāde” iti pūrvavādinaḥ kasmāt kriyā na bhavati | tasya ca śuddhasyāviṣayatvāt | nanv evam “kāraṇe prativādini” iti vacanaṃ śuddhakāraṇaviṣayaṃ kasmān na bhavatīti cet, maivam sarvasyāpi kāraṇottarasya mithyāsahacaritarūpatvāt | śuddhakāraṇasyottarasyābhāvāt | prasiddhakāraṇottare pratijñātārthe ekadeśasyābhyupagamena ekadeśasya mithyātvam | yathā “satyaṃ rūpakaśataṃ gṛhītaṃ na dhārayāmi dattatvāt” ity ekadeśasyābhyupagamo ‘sti, prakṛtodāharaṇe tu pratijñārthasyaikadeśasyābhyupagamo nāstīti śeṣaḥ | evaṃ nirūpitam uttaraṃ prativādinā svata eva deyam |
tatrābhiyogānugatam uttaraṃ prativādinā |
niṣkṛṣyārthaṃ pradeyaṃ syāt doṣasaṃjñāvivarjitam ||
iti smṛteḥ | yadā punaḥ svayam eva dadāti uttarapradānakālaś cātikrāntaḥ, tadā –
pūrvapakṣe yathārthaṃ tu na dadyād uttaraṃ yadā |
pratyarthī dāpanīyaḥ syāt sāmādibhir upakramaiḥ || iti |
dāpanīyo nṛpeṇeti śeṣaḥ |
yathārtham uttaraṃ dadyāt atha tad dāpayen nṛpaḥ |
iti smṛteḥ | sāmādisaptopāyāḥ prakaraṇādāv evoktāḥ | etair apy upāyair asādhyaṃ pratyāha hārīraḥ |
[99]        upāyaiś codyamānais tu na dadyād uttaraṃ tu yaḥ |
atikrānte saptarātre jito ‘sau dātum arhati ||
iti uttarasvarūpanirūpaṇam
atha pratijñottaranirūpaṇānantaraṃ pramāṇanirūpaṇaprakāro nirūpyate -
evam uttarapatre niveśite sādhyasiddheḥ sādhanāyattatvāt sādhananirdeśaṃ kaḥ kuryād ity apekṣāyāṃ yājñavalkyaḥ |
tato ‘rthī lekhayet sadyaḥ pratijñātārthasādhanam ||
tataḥ – uttarānantram arthī sādhyavān, sadyaḥ anantaram eva lekhayet | pratijñatārthaḥ – sādhyaḥ sādhyate aneneti sādhanam – pramāṇam | pratijñātārthasādhanam arthī lekhayed ity anena prāṅnyāyasyaiva sādhyatvāt kāraṇavādy eva arthīti sa eva lekhayet | mithyottare pūrvavādy evārthī sa eva sādhanaṃ nirdiśed ity uktam ity abhiprāyaḥ | arthigrahaṇena saṃpratipattyuttare tu sādhyābhāvena bhāṣottaravādinoḥ dvayor apy arthitvābhāvāt sādhananirdeśa eva nāstīti tāvataiva vyavahāraḥ parisamāpta iti gamyate | ata eva hārītaḥ |
prāṅnyāyakāraṇoktau tu pratyarthī nirdiśet kriyām |
mithyoktau pūrvavādī tu pratipattau na sā bhavet || iti |
iti pratipattiḥ | atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
ubhayor likhite vākye prārabdhe kāryanirṇaye |
ayuktaṃ tatra yo brūyāt tasmād arthāt sahāyataḥ ||
[100] iti | anenaiva hi vacanena manyukṛtavivādeṣu pramādābhidhāne prakṛtād apy arthād dhīyata iti gamyate | yathāham anena śirasi pādena tāḍita ity āvedanasamaye abhidhāya bhāṣākāle hastena vā pādena vā tāḍita iti vadan na kevalaṃ daṇḍyaḥ, api tu parājita eva | asminn arthe ‘rthinaḥ pratyarthinaś ca pramādaparihārārtha evopadeśo yājñavalkyena kṛtaḥ |
abhiyogam anistīrya nainaṃ pratyabhiyojayet | iti |
abhiyujyata ity abhiyogo ‘parādhaḥ | tam abhiyogam anistīrya – aparihṛtya enam abhiyoktāraṃ na pratyabhiyojayet – aparādhena na saṃyojayet | yady apīdaṃ pratyavaskandanarūpaṃ pratyabhiyogaḥ, tathāpi svābhiyogopamardanarūpatvān na niṣedhaniṣayaḥ | idaṃ pratyarthinam adhikṛtyoktam (?), arthinaṃ pratyāha sa eva |
abhiyuktaṃ ca nānyena noktaṃ viprakṛtaṃ nayet | iti |
anyena abhiyuktam – anistīrṇābhiyogam anyo ‘rthī nābhiyojayet | kiṃ ca āvadenasamaye yad uktaṃ tad viprakṛtaṃ viruddhasvabhāvaṃ na nayet | etad uktaṃ bhavati – yad vastu yena rūpeṇa vedanasamaye niveditaṃ tad vastu bhāṣākāle tathaiva lekhanīyam | nānyatheti |
nanu
pratyarthini ‘grato lekhyaṃ yad āveditam arthinā |
ity atredam uktam, kim arthaṃ punar ucyate “noktaṃ viprakṛtaṃ nayet” [101] iti; ucyate, “yathāveditam arthinā” ity anena āvedanasamaye yad vastu niveditaṃ tad eva bhāṣāsamaye ‘pi lekhanīyam | etasmin pade vastvantaraṃ nety uktam | yathā anena rūpakaśataṃ vṛddhyā gṛhītam ity āvedanasamaye pratipādya pratyarthisaṃnidhau bhāṣāsamaye vastuśataṃ vṛddhyā gṛhītam iti na vaktavyam | tathā sati padāntarāgamane ‘pi vastvantaragamanād dhīnavādī daṇḍyaḥ syād iti | yatra tu rūpakaśataṃ vṛddhyā gṛhītvā ayaṃ na prayacchatīty āvedanakāle ‘bhidhāya bhāṣākāle rūpakaśataṃ balād apahṛtavān iti vadati, tadā sāhasādipadāntaragamakam “noktaṃ viprakṛtaṃ nayet” ity anenoktam iti paunaruktyam | etad eva spaṣṭīkṛtaṃ nāradena |
pūrvavādaṃ parityajya yo ‘nyam ālambate punaḥ |
padasaṃkramaṇāt jñeyo hīnavādī sa vai naraḥ || iti |
atrāpavādam āha sa eva |
kuryāt pratyabhiyogaṃ tu kalahe sāhaseṣu ca || iti |
pratyaparādhasaṃbhava iti śeṣaḥ | kalahe pāruṣyadvaya ity arthaḥ |
pañcavidhā hīnavādinaḥ
nāradas tu hīnavāditvaṃ pañcadhety āha |
anyavādī kriyādveṣī nopasthātā niruttaraḥ |
āhūtavyapavāyī ca hīnaḥ pañcavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
anyavādī – anyathāvādī, tathā hīnasya lakṣaṇam āha |
pūrvavādaṃ parityajya yo ‘nyam ālambate punaḥ |
padasaṃkramaṇāṅ jñeyo hīnavādī sa vai naraḥ ||
[102] iti | padasaṃkramaṇam – rṇādānādipadānyatamaṃ padaṃ vihāya anyatamapadasvīkārapūrvam āvedanam | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
śrāviyitvā tadā kāryaṃ tyajed anyad vaded asau |
anyaprakṣāśrayas tena kṛto vādī sa hīyate ||
samayābhihitaṃ kāryam abhiyujya paraṃ vadet |
vibruvaṃś ca bhaved evaṃ hīnaṃ tam api nirdiśet ||
lekhayitvā tu yo vākyaṃ nyūnaṃ vāpy adhikaṃ punaḥ |
vaded vādī sa hīyeta nābhiyogaṃ tu so ‘rhati ||
abhiyogaḥ – pūrvapakṣaḥ taṃ kartuṃ nārhatīty arthaḥ |
kriyādveṣitayā hīnasya lakṣaṇam āha ¦
sabhyāś ca sākṣiṇaś caiva kriyā jñeyā manīṣibhiḥ |
tāṃ kriyāṃ dveṣṭi yo mohāt kriyadveṣī sa ucyate || iti |
anupasthātṛlakṣaṇam āha |
āhvānād anupasthānāt sadya eva prahīyate || iti |
āhvānānantaram anāgamane tadānīm eva hīno bhavatīty arthaḥ | niruttaratayā hīnaṃ nirūpayati |
brūhīty ukto hi na brūyāt sadyo bandhanam arhati |
dvitīye ‘hani durbuddher vidyāt tasya parājayam ||
parājayo – hīnatā | āhūtavyapavāyī tu abhiyogaparihārārtham āhvānaṃ budhvā pracchannacārī | ataiṣāṃ tu uttarottarasya hīnatāgurutvajñāpanārthaṃ hīnaḥ pañcavidhaḥ smṛta ity uktam | na punaḥ pañcavidha eva hīna ity avadhāraṇārtham, vidhāntarāṇām api smaraṇāt || [103]
hīnavādināṃ pañcānāṃ krameṇa daṇḍaḥ
hīnatāyās tu gurutvajñāpanaṃ daṇḍabhūyastvajñāpanārtham | uktaṃ ca kātyāyanena |
anyavādī paṇān pañca kriyādveṣī paṇān daśa |
nopasthātā daśa dvau ca ṣoḍaśaiva niruttaraḥ ||
āhūtavyapavāyī tu paṇān grāhyas tu viṃśatim |
prakārāntareṇa hīnas tenaiva darśitaḥ |
śrāvitavyavahārāṇām ekaṃ yatra prabhedayet |
vādinaṃ lobhayec caiva hīnaṃ tam api nirdiśet ||
bhayaṃ karoti bhedaṃ vā bhīṣaṇaṃ vā nirodhanam |
etāni vādino ‘rthasya vyavahāre sa hīyate ||
arthavyavahāre bhayādīni samastāny asamastāni vā yena vādinā kṛtāni mahīyata ity arthaḥ, bhīṣaṇam – mukhāntareṇa bhayotpādanaṃ iti na paunaruktyam ||
tripakṣānantaraṃ hīnavādinaḥ parājayaḥ
atra nārado viśeṣam āha |
prapalāyan tripakṣeṇa maunakṛt saptabhir dinaiḥ |
sākṣibhinnas tatkṣaṇena pratipannaś ca hīyate || iti |
sākṣibhede viśeṣam āha sa eva |
sākṣiṇas tu samuddiśya yas tu tān na vivādayet |
triṃśadrātrāt tripakṣād vā tasya hāniḥ prajāyate || [104]
kāmata iti śeṣaḥ | akāmatas tu,
ācārakaraṇe divye kṛtvopasthānanirṇayam |
nopasthito yadā kaścit chalaṃ tatra na kārayet ||
ācārakaraṇam – vyavahāranirṇayaḥ | tatrāpi viśeṣo darśitaḥ |
hīno ‘nyavākyena budhaḥ tasyoddhāraṃ vidur budhāḥ |
svavākyabhinno yas tu syāt tasyoddhāro na vidyate ||
manur api |
ākārair iṅgitair gatyā ceṣṭayā bhāṣaṇena ca |
netravaktravikāraiś ca gṛhyate ‘ntargataṃ manaḥ ||
duṣṭam iti śeṣaḥ |
sandhiprakāraḥ
bṛhaspatis tu sandhiprakāram āha |
pūrvottare tu likhite prakṛte kāryanirṇaye |
dvayoḥ santaptayoḥ sandhiḥ syād ayaḥpiṇḍayor iva ||
sākṣisabhyavikalpas tu bhavet tatrobhayor api |
ḍolāyamānayoḥ sandhiṃ prakuryātāṃ vicakṣaṇau ||
pramāṇasaṃmato yatra bhedaś śāstracaritrayoḥ |
tatra rājājñayā sandhir ubhayor api śasyate || iti |
ekasya vādina ekānte parājayād arvāk sandhiṃ prakuryātāṃ tau vicakṣaṇāv ity arthaḥ |
ubhayoḥ sandhyanaṅgīkāre sabhyakṛtyam
ubhayoḥ sandhyanaṅgīkāre sabhyakṛtyam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
[105]        śodhite likhite samyag iti nirdoṣa uttare |
pratyarthino ‘rthino vāpi kriyā kāraṇam iṣyate ||
pratyarthino ‘rthino vāpi sādhyasiddhaye pramāṇalekhanaṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | etad eva candrikākārādayaḥ pratyākalanam ity āhuḥ | kiṃ tatpramāṇam ity apekṣite yājñavalkya āha |
pramāṇaṃ likhitaṃ bhuktiḥ sākṣiṇaś ceti kīrtitam |
eṣām anyatamābhāve divyānyatamam ucyate || iti |
nārado ‘pi |
laukikadivyapramāṇāni
kriyā tu dvividhā proktā mānuṣī daivikī tathā |
mānuṣī lekhysākṣibhyāṃ dhaṭādir daivikī smṛtā ||
lekhyasākṣigrahaṇaṃ bhukter apy upalakṣaṇam | tasyā api mānuṣasaṃbandhitvāviśeṣāt | dhaṭādayo bṛhaspatinā darśitāḥ |
dhaṭo ‘gnir udakaṃ caiva viṣaṃ kośaś ca pañcamaḥ |
ṣaṣṭaṃ ca taṇḍulāḥ proktaṃ saptamaṃ taptamāṣakam ||
aṣṭamaṃ phālam ity uktaṃ navamaṃ dharmajaṃ bhavet |
divyāny etāni sarvāṇi nirdiṣṭāni svayambhuvā ||
śapathās tu tenaiva darśitā |
satyaṃ vāhanaśastrāṇi gobījakanakāni ca |
devatā pitṛpādāś ca dattāni sukṛtāni ca ||
caśabdād anyeṣām api putraśiraḥsparśanādīnāṃ saṃgrahaḥ |
[106]
śapathabhedāḥ
saptarṣayas tathendrādyāḥ puṣkarārthe tapodhanāḥ |
śepuḥ śapatham avyagrāḥ parasparaviśuddhaye || iti |
yady api aśapathānām api divyaśabdavācyatvam asti |
yasmād devaiḥ prayuktāni puṣkarārthe manīṣibhiḥ |
parasparaviśuddhyarthaṃ tasmād dviyāni nāmataḥ || iti |
tathāpi guruṣu divyaśabdaḥ laghuṣu śapatha iti kecit, tan na –
divākṛte kāryavidhau grāmeṣu nagareṣu ca |
saṃbhave sākṣiṇāṃ caiva daivī na bhavati kriyā ||
aśeṣamānuṣābhāve divyenaiva vinirṇayaḥ |
saṃbhave sākṣiṇāṃ prājño daivikīṃ tu vivarjayet ||
iti vacaneṣu divyaśapathayor ekavākyatayopādanāt, tathaiva lokaprasiddeś ca, kiṃ tu –
arthānurūpāḥ śapathāḥ smṛtāḥ satyadhaṭādayaḥ |
sākṣī yatra na vidyeta vivāde vadatāṃ nṛṇām ||
tadā divyaiḥ parīkṣeta śapathaiś ca pṛthagvidhaiḥ ||
iti divyaśapathayor bhedagrahaṇaṃ tayor na bhedakathanāya, api tu śapatho laghuṣu prāyika iti darśayitum ity avagantavyam | kātyāyanādayas tu –
yady ekadeśaṃ vyāptāpi kriyā vidyeta mānuṣī |
sā grāhyā na tu pūrṇāpi daivikī vadatāṃ nṛṇām || [107]
ekadeśe mānuṣasaṃbhave ekadeśavibhāvitanyāyena viśeṣaṇāṃśasiddhiḥ | yad vā alpaśapathenāpi kāryety abhiprāyaḥ |
yady eko mānuṣīṃ brūyād anyo brūyāt tu daivikīm |
mānuṣīṃ tatra gṛhṇīyāt na tu daivīṃ kriyāṃ nṛpaḥ ||
svalpāpi mānuṣī yatra daivīṃ tatra na kalpayet || iti |
ataḥ sarvathā mānuṣāsaṃbhave divyam ity utsargaḥ | kvacid asyāpavādam āha sa eva |
prakrānte sāhase vāde pāruṣye daṇḍavācike |
balodbhaveṣu kāryeṣu sākṣiṇo divyam eva vā ||
ṛṇādānādiṣu viśeṣam āha sa eva |
ṛṇe lekhyaṃ sākṣiṇo vā yuktileśādayo ‘pi vā |
daivikī ca kriyā proktā prajānāṃ hitakāmyayā ||
pramāṇair hetunā vāpi divyenaiva tu niścayaḥ |
sarveṣv arthavivādeṣu sadā kuryān narādhipaḥ ||
likhitaṃ sākṣiṇo bhuktiḥ pramāṇaṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam |
anumānaṃ vidur hetuṃ tarkaṃ caiva manīṣiṇaḥ || iti |
evaṃ dṛṣṭapramāṇenāsiddhau adṛṣṭapramāṇam āśrayaṇīyam, nānyatheti tātparyārthaḥ | kvacid apavādam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
prathame vā tṛtīye vā pramāṇaṃ daivamānuṣam |
uttare syāc caturthe tu sa sākṣijayapatrakam ||
mithyāsaṃpratipattyavaskandanaprāṅnyāyarūpacaturvidhottareṣu prathame mithyottare tṛtīye kāraṇottare daivikī mānuṣī ca kriyā [108] kāryā, caturthe prāṅnyāyottare sākṣijayapatrayor anyatarāvalambanenaiva nirṇayaḥ, tatra divyāvatāra ity akṣarārthaḥ | saṃpratipattau bhāṣāyām uttare ca sādhyārthābhāvāt dvipād vyavahāro ‘yam |
dvipādvyavahāraṇirṇayaḥ
tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ ||
mithyoktau sa catuṣpāt tu pratyavaskandane tathā |
prāṅnyāye ca sa vijñeyo dvipāt saṃpratipattiṣu ||
yena saṃpratipattau kriyānirdeśādyabhāvāt uttarānta eva vyavahāraḥ samāpyate | evaṃ ca mithyākāraṇayor mānuṣī daivikī vā, prāṅnyāye tu mānuṣy eva, satye tu na kadācid iti tātparyārthaḥ |
kvacit sākṣiṣi satsv api avaśyaṃ divyaniyamaḥ
kvacid divyaniyamam āha |
mahāśāpābhiśapteṣu nikṣepaharaṇe tathā |
divyaiḥ kāryaṃ parīkṣeta rājā satsv api sākṣiṣu ||
kātyāyano ‘pi |
prāṇāntikavivāde ca vidyamāneṣu sākṣiṣu |
divyam ālambate vādī na pṛcchet tatra sākṣiṇaḥ ||
bṛhaspatir api |
likhite sākṣivāde ca saṃdeho jāyate yadā |
anumāne ca saṃbhrānte tatra daivaṃ viśodhanam || [109]
etac ca vākpāruṣyādivyatiriktaviṣayam |
vākpāruṣye mahīvāde niṣiddhā daivikī kriyā | iti |
tatra tān na kalpayed ity abhiprāyaḥ |
vākpāruṣye ca bhūmau ca divyaṃ na parikalpayet |
iti smaraṇāt, idaṃ tu vākpāruṣye ‘lpe, mahati vākpāruṣye divyavidhānāt | bhūmāv iti sthāvaropalakṣaṇam | ata eva pitāmahaḥ |
sthāvaravivāde divyābhāvaḥ
sthāvareṣu vivādeṣu divyāni parivarjayet |
sākṣibhir likhitenātha bhuktyā cainaṃ prasādhayet ||
etad vacanaṃ vijñānayoginā parājayaviṣaye avaṣṭambhaviṣayam ity uktam | anyathā vyavahārocchedaprasaṅgād iti | cadrikākārādibhis tu mānuṣāsaṃbhave hetunā nirṇayaḥ, tadasaṃbhave rājājñayeti mantavyam iti | etac ca dharmasthānavihīneṣu vivādeṣu rājājñārūpapramāṇanirūpaṇāsaṃbhavād dheyam (dhyeyam?) | na ca rājājñāpramāṇasya niravakāśatvam iti vācyam | “uttaraḥ pūrvabādhakaḥ” iti tadvyākhyānāvasare pūrvam eva prapañcitatvāt | ata eva vijñāneśvaramatam eva samyak | vyāso ‘pi |
na mayaitat kṛtaṃ lekhyaṃ kūṭam anyena kāritam |
adharīkṛtya tat patram arthe divyena nirṇayaḥ ||
pūgaśreṇigaṇādīnāṃ yā sthitiḥ parikīrtitā |
tasyāstu sādhanaṃ lekhyaṃ na divyaṃ na ca sākṣiṇaḥ || iti | [110]
divyaniṣedho lekhyasya gurutvakathanārthaḥ |
dvāramārgakriyābhogajalavāhādike tathā |
bhuktir eva tu gurvī syāt na lekhyaṃ na ca sākṣiṇaḥ ||
dattādatte ca bhṛtyānāṃ svāmināṃ nirṇaye sati |
vikrayādānasaṃbandhe krītvā dhanam ayacchati ||
dyūtasamāhvye lekhyadivyayor anavakāśaḥ
dyūte samāhvaye caiva vivāde sampasthite |
sākṣiṇaḥ sādhakaṃ proktaṃ na divyaṃ na ca lekhyakam ||
atrāpi sākṣiṇa eva garīyāṃsa ity abhiprāyaḥ | evam uktapramāṇavyavasthā parīkṣakaiḥ kalpanīyā | yat āha nāradaḥ |
pūrvavāde ‘bhilikhite yathākṣaram aśeṣataḥ |
arthī tṛtīyapāde tu kriyāyāḥ pratipādayet ||
pūrvavādo – bhāṣā, abhilikhitam – pūrvapakṣābhibhāvakatayā likhitam – uttaram iti yāvat | kriyāpramāṇaṃ tṛtīyapādaḥ | pratyākalitākhyaḥ bhāṣottarapratyākalitākhyapādatraye sati svakārtham arthī pramāṇena sādhayed ity arthaḥ |
[111]
kriyāsvarūpabhedau
kriyāyāḥ bhedam āha vyāsaḥ |
kāryaṃ tu sādhyam ity uktaṃ sādhanaṃ tu kriyocyate |
dvividhā sā punar jñeyā mānuṣī daivikī tathā ||
bṛhaspatis tu |
dviprakārā kriyā proktā mānuṣī daivikī tathā |
ekaikānekadhā bhinnā munibhis tatvavedibhiḥ ||
kātyāyano ‘pi |
pañcaprakāraṃ daivaṃ syāt mānuṣaṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam || iti |
tatra pañcaprakāram iti na niyamyate | taṇḍulataptamāṣādividhānāntarasyāpi smṛtyantare darśanāt, trividham iti tu niyamārtham, smṛtyantaravirodhāt,
likhitaṃ sākṣiṇo bhuktiḥ pramāṇaṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam |
iti nāradokteḥ,
likhitaṃ sākṣinaś cātra dvau vidhī parikīrtitau |
ity uktam | likhitasākṣiṇoḥ bhuktito garīyastvābhiprāyaṃ veditavyam |
lekhyanirūpaṇam
likhitasya dvaividhyam āha saṃgrahakāraḥ |
rājakīyaṃ jānapadaṃ likhitaṃ dvividhaṃ smṛtam |
rājakīyaṃ pañcavidham
rājakīyaṃ śāsanajayapatrājñāpatraprasādalekhyabhedāt pañcavidham | śāsanaṃ nāma yatra bhūmyādikaṃ nibandhaṃ vā datvā [112] tāmrapaṭṭe paṭe vā sthānavaṃśāsisaṃyuktaṃ dharaṇīvarāhapratipādakavacanāśīrvādapūrvakam ātmapitṛpitāmahaprapitāmahādīnāṃ śauryādivarṇanadvārā ātmānam abhilekhya pratigrahaparimāṇādikaṃ likhitvā samāmāsapakṣāhaḥsaṃpradānagrāmādi likhitvā āgaminṛpabodhanārthaṃ “dātuḥ pālayituḥ svargaṃ hantur narakam eva vā” |
ṣaṣṭhir varṣasahasrāṇi dānacchedaphalaṃ likhet |
sāmānyo ‘yaṃ dharmasetur narāṇām
ityādi lekhanīyam | tato rājā svahastasaṅketaṃ likhet, tad rājaśāsanam iti | tac ca pratigrahītre ‘rpaṇīyam | tasyopayogitvāt | tad api śāsanadānaṃ na dānasiddhyartham, tasya pratigraheṇaiva siddheḥ | kiṃ tu dattasya sthairyakaraṇārtham | sthiratve akṣayaphalaśruteḥ |
jayapatrikā nāma –
yad vṛttaṃ vyavahāre tu pūrvapakṣottarādikam |
kriyāvadhāraṇopetaṃ jayapatre ‘khilaṃ likhet ||
ādiśabdena sākṣivacanasabhyaprāḍvivākakulānāṃ śāstrasya mataṃ svahastenānyahastena vā likhed iti taj jayapatram | anyad api jayapatraṃ bṛhaspatir āha |
anyavidyādihīnebhyaḥ uttareṣāṃ pradīyate |
vyakānuvādasaṃsiddhaṃ tac ca syāj jayapatrikam ||
uttareṣām ahīnavādyādīnām, yathāha vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
prāḍvivākādihastāṅkaṃ mudritaṃ rājamudrayā |
siddhe ‘rthe vādine dadyāt jayine jayapatrikām || iti
[113]
ājñāpatrikā
ājñāpatraṃ tu |
sāmanteṣv atha bhṛtyeṣu rāṣṭrapālādikeṣu vā |
kāryam ādiśyate yena tad ājñāpatram ucyate ||
prajñāpatraṃ tu |
ṛtvikpurohitācāryamānyeṣv abhyarhiteṣu tu |
kāryaṃ nivedyate yena patraṃ prajñāpanātmakam ||
prasādapatram
prasādalekhyaṃ tu |
deśādikaṃ yasya rājā likhitena prayacchati |
senāśauryādinā tuṣṭaḥ prasādalikhitaṃ hi tat || iti |
jānapadasya lakṣaṇam āha vyāsaḥ |
likhej jānapadaṃ lekhyaṃ prasiddhaṃ sthānalekhakaḥ |
rājavaṃśakramayutaṃ varṣamāsārdhvāsaraiḥ ||
pitṛpūrvaṃ nāmajātidhanikarṇikayor likhet |
dravyabhedaṃ pramāṇaṃ ca pṛthvīṃ cobhayasaṃmatām ||
caśabdena sākṣideśācārādi gṛhyate, ata eva nāradaḥ |
lekhyaṃ tu sākṣimat kāryam aviluptakramākṣaram |
deśācārakramayutaṃ samagraṃ sarvavastuṣu || iti |
yājñavalkyas tu viśeṣam āha |
samāpte ‘rthe nāma svahastena niveśayet |
mataṃ me ‘mukaputrasya yad atropari lekhitam || [114]
vyāso ‘pi |
sākṣiṇas tu svahastena pitṛnāmapūrvakaṃ likheyuḥ | iti |
te ca guṇaiś ca saṃkhyayā samā eva, na viṣamāḥ | saṃkhyāsāmyam adṛṣṭārthaṃ prābalyārthaṃ vā | guṇasāmyaṃ dṛṣṭārtham | sākṣiṇa iti bahuvacanaṃ gurukāryalekhyaviṣayam |
uttamarṇādhamarṇau ca sākṣiṇau lekhakas tathā |
samavāyena caikeṣāṃ lekhyaṃ kurvīta nānyathā ||
iti hārītena lekhyamātre sākṣināv iti dvitvokteḥ | ataś ca uttamarṇādhamarṇasākṣilekhaka-lekhanīyarūpapañcavidhagamakārūḍhatvāt tadabhedāt patrasya lekhyaṃ pañcārūḍham iti laukikaprasiddhiḥ | nanu “patraṃ hi lipisaṃyuktam ucyate” ity asya liper lekhanīyārthanirūpakatayā tadabhedāt patrasya tadārūḍhatvoktiḥ pañcasaṃkhyoktiś cāyukteti cet, maivam, yathā lekhyasya lekhakanirūpakatayā tadabhede ‘pi tadgataviśeṣākārasvīkāreṇa lekhakārūḍhatvoktir aviruddhā tathaivātreti | tady api svahastalekhye lekhakābhāvāt pañcārūḍhatvaṃ nāsti, tathāpi tasminn avasthādvayavivakṣayāvasthitaṃ gamakatvam avalambya taduktir na viruddhā | na ca vācyam – svahastalekhyasākṣyabhāvapakṣe pañcārūḍhatvaṃ gauṇam iti, sākṣikṛtyaṃ svakṛtalipāv eva tiṣṭhati | svasyaiva sākṣitvāṅgīkārāt | kecit tv anyakṛtalekhye pañcārūḍhatvaṃ patrasyety āhuḥ | candrikākārādayas tu anyakṛtalekhyasya uttamarṇādhamarṇasākṣidvayalekhakarūpapañcapuruṣārūdhatvāt pañcārūḍhaṃ patram iti lokavyavahāraḥ, sākṣisaṃkhyādhikye tv ayaṃ vyavahāro gauṇa iti mantavya ity āhuḥ | tasmin mate pañcaśabdaḥ puruṣamātraparo na tu [115] gamakaparaḥ | atra patrasya pañcārūḍhatve puruṣamātraṃ na nimittam, api tu tadgatagamakatvam, ataś cetarapramāṇāpekṣayā patrasya prābalyasiddhiḥ | kiṃ ca sākṣisaṃkhyādhikye tv ayaṃ vyavahāro gauṇa ity asamañjasam | ṣaḍādisaṃkhyāviśiṣṭasākṣimatpatrasthale ‘pi śate pañcāśannyāyena mukhyatāyāṃ gauṇatvāṅgīkārokter ayuktatvāt | kecit tu tanmatānuvartina evaṃ tatparihāram āhuḥ: eko ‘py ubhayasaṃmata iti vacanāt śrutādhyayanasaṃpannasya tasya vādino ‘py ekasyaiva sākṣitvāṅgīkārāt | puruṣacatuṣṭayārūḍhatve pañcārūḍhatvoktir gauṇīti | tat tu pūrvāparaviruddhābhidhānam ity anādartavyam | tathāpi pūrvoktadoṣaduṣṭatvād asmaduktaprakāra eva samyak | nāradas tu viśeṣam āha |
alipijño ṛṇī yaḥ syāt lekhayet svamataṃ tu saḥ |
sākṣiṇā sākṣino ‘nyena sarvasākṣisamanvitaḥ |
vijātīyalipijño ‘pi svayam eva likhet padam ||
padam iti svasaṅketam ity arthaḥ | tadanantarakṛtyaṃ yājñavalkya āha |
ubhayābhyarthitenaitan mayāpy amukasūnunā |
likhitaṃ hy amukeneti lekhakas tv antato likhet ||
vyāso ‘pi |
mayobhayābhyarthitenāmukenāmukasūnunā |
svahastayuktaṃ svaṃ nāma lekhako ‘nte tato likhet |
evaṃ jānapade lekhye vyāsenābhihito vidhiḥ || iti |
lekhyam aṣṭavidham
etaj jānapadaṃ lekhyam aṣṭavidham | vibhāgapatra-dānapatra-[116]saṃvitpatra-sthitipatra-sandhipatra-viśuddhipatra-ādhipatra-krayapatrabhedāt |
vibhāgapatradānapatrādikramaḥ
vibhāgapatrasvarūpaṃ tu nārada āha |
bhrātaraḥ saṃvibhaktā ye svarucyā tu parasparam |
vibhāgalekhyaṃ kurvanti bhāgalekhyaṃ tad ucyate || iti |
dānapatrasvarūpam sa evāha |
bhūmiṃ datvā tu yat patraṃ kuryāc candrārkasākṣikam |
anācchedyam anāhāryaṃ dānapatraṃ tad ucyate || iti |
saṃvitpatraṃ tu pitāmaha āha |
grāmo deśaś ca yat kuryān mate lekhyaṃ parasparam |
rājāvirodhi dharmārthaṃ saṃvitpatraṃ vadanti tat ||
sthitipatram āha bṛhaspatiḥ|
pūgaśreṇyādikānāṃ tu samayasya sthiteḥ kṛtam |
sthitipatraṃ tu tat proktaṃ manvādismṛtivedibhiḥ || iti |
sandhipatrasvarūpam āha hārītaḥ |
sandhipatraṃ tu vijñeyam arthipratyarthinor yadā |
parasparānumatyā ca nirmitaṃ tu sasākṣikam || iti |
viśuddhipatrasvarūpaṃ tu sa evāha |
abhiśāpe samuttīrṇe prāyaścitte kṛte janaiḥ |
viśuddhipatraṃ vijñeyaṃ lekhyasākṣisamanvitam || [117] iti |
ādhipatram āha yamaḥ |
ādhiṃ kṛtvā tu yo dravyaṃ prayuṅkte svadhanaṃ dhanī |
yat tatra kriyate lekhyam ādhipatraṃ tad ucyate ||
krayapatrasvarūpam āha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
krīte krayaprakāśārthaṃ dravye yat kriyate kvacit |
vikretranumataṃ kretur jñeyaṃ tat krayapatrakam || iti |
eteṣv evāṣṭasu patreṣu anvādhipatragopyādhipatrakralekhyasīmāpatrādīnāṃ yathāyogam antarbhāvaḥ kāryaḥ | etad apy aṣṭavidhaṃ patraṃ cirakācirakabhedena dvividham | dvividham api svahastakṛtam anyahastakṛtaṃ ceti dvividham | cirakaṃ nāma putrapautrādidṛṣṭaṃ sākṣyādipañcārūḍhaṃ tadānīṃ gamakānāṃ vidyamānatvāc cirakam iti | tadanyad acirakam |
tad ayam atra niṣkarṣaḥ – patraṃ dvividham  rājaśāsanaṃ jānapadaśāsanaṃ ceti | rājaśāsanaṃ pañcavidham – śāsanajayapatrājñāpatraprajñāpatraprasādalekhyabhedāt | śāsanaṃ dvividham – bhūmyādiviṣayatvena nibandhanaviṣayatvena ca | jānapadam aṣṭavidham – vibhāgadānasaṃvitsthitisandhiviśuddhādhikrayabhedāt | etat sarvaṃ svahastakṛtam anyahastakṛtaṃ ceti dvividham | svahastakṛtaṃ sākṣimattvāsakṣimattvābhyāṃ dvividham |
svahastilikhitaṃ patraṃ sākṣyabhāve ‘pi tadbali | iti |
lekhyaprayojanam āha marīciḥ |
sthāvare vikrayādhāne vibhāge dāna eva ca |
[118]        likhitenāpnuyāt siddhim api saṃvādam eva ca ||
lekhyāntarotpattiviṣayaḥ
lekhyasya nāśe vā kāryākṣamatve vā lekhyāntaram utpādanīyam |
deśāntarasthe durlekhye naṣṭonmṛṣṭe hṛte tathā |
bhinne dagdhe tathā chinne lekhyam anyat tu kārayet ||
iti yājñavalkyasmṛteḥ | deśāntarasthe – sarvathānetum aśakyasthāne sthite | durlekhye – durbodhākṣare | bhinne – dvidhā jāte | chinne – śīrṇe kṛte | hṛte taskarādinā | etac cārthipratyarthinoḥ parasparānumatau satyām | asatyāṃ tu vyavahāraprāptau deśāntarasthapatrānayanāya ādhyapekṣayā kālo dātavyaḥ | durdeśāvasthite naṣṭe vā patre sākṣibhir eva vyavahāranirṇayaḥ kartavyaḥ | yathāha nāradaḥ |
lekhye deśāntaranyaste śīrṇe durlekhite hṛte |
satas tatkālakaraṇam asato draṣṭṛdarśanam || iti |
sato vidyamānasyānayanāya kālaṃ karaṇaṃ kālāvadhir dātavyaḥ | asataḥ punar avidyamānasya pūrvaṃ ye draṣṭāraḥ sākṣiṇas taiḥ darśanaṃ vyavahārapīrasamāpanaṃ kāryam |
alekhyasākṣike daivīṃ vyavahāre vinirdeśet (diśet?) |
iti smaraṇāt | iyam eva vyākhyā samīcīnā | kālāntare dhane deye lekhyāntaraṃ tu kāryam eveti candrikākāravyākhyānaṃ tu dhanasya kālāntardeyatve patrāntarakaraṇam utsargataḥ prāptam ity avaseyam |
[119]
lekhyaparīkṣā
lekhyaṃ lekhyācāreṇa sākṣyaṃ sākṣyācāreṇa vicāreyed ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
rājā kriyāṃ samāhūya yathānyāyyaṃ vicāreyet |
lekhyācāreṇa likhitaṃ sākṣyācāreṇa sākṣiṇaḥ || iti |
rājñā sākṣiṇa āhūya sākṣyācāreṇa likhitaṃ vicārayet | yad vā lekhyācāreṇa likhitaṃ vicārayet | asmin vyākhyāne kriyām iti lakṣaṇe dvitīyā | kriyānirṇayārthaṃ likhitaṃ sākṣiṇaś ca vicārayed iti | kriyāṃ samādāyeti pāṭhe ṛjur evānvayaḥ | lekhyācāram āha sa eva |
varṇavākyakriyāyuktam asaṃdigdhaṃ sphuṭākṣaram |
ahīnakramacihnaṃ ca lekhyaṃ tat siddhim āpnuyāt ||
duṣṭalekhyam
kriyānāma – sādhyam | duṣṭalekhyasvarūpam āha hārītaḥ |
mumūrṣubālabhītārtastrīmattavyasanāturaiḥ |
niśopadhibalātkāraiḥ kṛtaṃ lekhyaṃ na sidhyati ||
bālastrīśabdāv asvatantropalakṣakau | yathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
mattenopadhibhītena tathonmattena pīḍitaiḥ |
strībhir bālāsvatantraiś ca kṛtaṃ lekhyaṃ na sidhyati || [120] iti |
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
lekhyadośās tu ye kecit sākṣiṇaś caiva ye smṛtāḥ |
vādakāle tu vaktavyāḥ paścād uktān na dūṣayet ||
lekhyasaṃdehe nirṇayanimittāni
lekhyasaṃdehe nirṇayanimittāny āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
saṃdigdhalekhyaśuddhiḥ syāt svahastalikhitādibhiḥ |
yuktiprāptikriyācihnasaṃbandhāgamahetubhiḥ ||
śuddham aśuddhaṃ veti saṃdigdhasya lekhyasya śuddhiḥ svahastalikhitādibhiḥ syāt | svahastena yal likhitaṃ lekhyāntaraṃ tena śuddhiḥ | yadi sadṛśākṣarāṇi bhavanti | tadā śuddhiḥ syād ity arthaḥ | ādiśabdāt sākṣilekhakasvahastalikhitāntarasaṃvādāc chuddhir iti yuktyā prāptiḥ | deśakālapuruṣāṇāṃ dravyeṇa saha saṃbandhaḥ prāptiḥ | asmin kāle asya puruṣasya idaṃ dravyaṃ ghaṭata iti yuktiprāptiḥ | kriyā – tatsākṣyupanyāsaḥ | cihnam asādhāraṇaṃ śrīkārādi tatsaṃbandhaḥ | arthiprathyarthinoḥ pūrvam api parasparaviśvāsena dānagrahaṇasaṃbandha āgamaḥ | asya etāvato ‘rthasya saṃbhāvitaprāptyupāyā eva hetavaḥ | ebhir hetubhiḥ saṃdigdhalekhyaśuddhiḥ syād ity arthaḥ |
sākṣibhir lekhyanirṇayaḥ
yadā tu lekhyasaṃdehe nirṇayo na jāyate tadā sākṣibhiḥ nirṇayaḥ kāryaḥ | yathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
dūṣite patrake vādī tadārūḍhāṃs tu nirdiśet | [121]
iti smaraṇāt | yat tu hārītavacanam,
na mayaitat kṛtaṃ patraṃ kūṭam etena kāritam |
adharīkṛtya tat patram arthe divyena nirṇayaḥ ||
iti, tat tu sākṣyabhāvaviṣayam |
śāsanaśuddhi
śānanam adhikṛtyāha prajāpatiḥ |
kāryo yatnena mahatā nirṇayo rājaśāsane |
rājasvahastatanmudrālekhakākṣaradarśanāt ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
mudrāśuddhaṃ kriyāśuddhaṃ bhuktiśuddhaṃ sacihnitam |
rājasvahastasaṃśuddhaṃ śuddhim āyāti śāsanam || iti |
kriyāśuddham apaśabdānvayādirahitam |
viṃśativarṣopabhogānantaraṃ lekhyaśuddhiḥ
jānapadalekhyam api kvacid bhuktyā śuddhim āyātīty āha sa eva |
śaktasya saṃnidhāv artho yasya lekhyena bhujyate |
varṣāṇi viṃśatiṃ yāvat tat patraṃ doṣavarjitam ||
smṛtyantare tu |
atha viṃśativarṣāṇi ādheḥ bhukteḥ sa nirṇayaḥ |
yena lekhyena tatsiddhiṃ lekhyaṃ doṣavivarjitam ||
sīmāvivāde nirṇīte sīmāpatraṃ vidhīyate |
tasya doṣāḥ pravaktavyāḥ yāvad varṣāṇi viṃśatiḥ ||
[122]
patrasākṣimṛtāv api lekhyasiddhivivekaḥ
tadapavādam āha sa eva |
yadi labdhaṃ bhavet kaṃcit prajñaptir vā kṛtā bhavet |
pramāṇam eva likhitaṃ(?) mṛtā yady api sākṣiṇaḥ ||
prajñaptim eva prapañcayati |
darśitaṃ pratikālaṃ yad grāhitaṃ smāritaṃ tathā |
lekhyaṃ sidhyati sarvatra mṛteṣv api ca sākṣiṣu ||
bṛhaspatis tu viśeṣam āha |
anyanāmāṅkalekhane divyena nirṇayaḥ
strībālārtān lipyavijñān vañcayanti svabāndhavāh |
lekhyaṃ kṛtvānyanāmāṅkaṃ jñeyaṃ yuktyāgamais tu tat ||
jñātvā kāle deśakārye kuśalāḥ kūṭakārakāḥ |
kurvanti sadṛśaṃ lekhyaṃ tad yatnena vicāreyet || iti |
prajāpatis tu |
yannāmagotrais tattulyarūpaṃ lekhyaṃ kvacid bhavet |
agṛhīte dhane tatra kāryo divyena nirṇayaḥ ||
tattulyarūpam – avipratipannalekhyāntaratulyarūpam | agṛhīte (dhane) prativādini dṛḍha ity arthaḥ |
lekhyavirodhe nirṇayaḥ
lekhyānāṃ mitho virodhe bādhakanirṇayārtham āha vyāsaḥ |
svahastakāj jānapadaṃ tasmāt tu nṛpaśāsanam |
pramāṇataram iṣṭaṃ vyavahārārtham āgatam ||
[123]
lekhyasākṣyoḥ madhye lekhyasya prābalyam
lekhyaprābalyam āha saṃvartaḥ |
lekhyasyopari yat sākṣyaṃ kūṭaṃ tad abhidhīyate |
adharmasya hi tad dvāraṃ tato rājā nivartayet ||
bṛhaspatir api |
vācakair yatra sāmarthyam akṣarāṇāṃ vihanyate |
kriyāṇāṃ sarvanāśaḥ syād anavasthā na jāyate ||
kriyāṇāṃ lekhyakriyāṇām | kātyāyano ‘pi |
na divyaiḥ sākṣibhir vāpi hīyate likhitaṃ kvacit |
lekhyadharmaḥ sadā śreṣṭho hy ato nānyena hīyate ||
lekhyena tu hīyata ity āha sa eva |
tad yuktaḥ pratilekhyena tadviśiṣṭena vā sadā |
lekhyakriyā nirasyeta nirasyānyena na kvacit ||
anyena sākṣyādinety arthaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha vyāsaḥ |
adṛṣṭaṃ śrāvitaṃ lekhyaṃ pramītadhanikarṇikam |
abandhalagnakaṃ caiva bahukālaṃ na sidhyati |
atra na sidhyatīty etat purastāt “paśyato ‘bruvato bhūmeḥ” ityādivacanavyākhyānāvasare nivedayiṣyāmaḥ |
bhukter api nirṇāyakatvam
bhukter api nirṇāyakatvam āha nāradaḥ |
kṛtvottaraṃ kriyāvāde lekhyaṃ sādhanam uddiśet |
sāmantalakṣaṇopetā bhuktir vā cirakālikī || [124]
atra sādhanaśabdena sākṣiṇa ucyante, vāśabdaḥ parasparam abhisaṃbadhyate | likhitaṃ vā sākṣiṇo vā bhuktaṃ vā samuddiśed ity arthaḥ | kaiścid viśeṣaṇair yuktāhā bhukteḥ prāmāṇyaṃ darśayati kātyāyanaḥ |
pramāṇeṣu bhukteḥ pramāṇatrayam
likhitaṃ sākṣiṇo bhuktiḥ pramāṇatrayam iṣyate |
pramāṇeṣu sthirā bhuktiḥ sallekhyā saṃmatā nṛṇām || iti |
sallekhyam anavadyalekhyam | nanu likhitasya sākṣiṇāṃ ca śabdābhivyaktadvāreṇa śabdāntarbhāvād yuktaṃ prāmāṇyam | bhuktes tu kathaṃ prāmāṇyam it ced, ucyate | bhuktir api kaiścid viśeṣaṇair yuktā svatvahetubhūtakrayadānādikam avyabhicārād anumāpayati | anyathānupapadyamānā kalpayatīty anumāne ‘rthāpattau vāntarbhavatīti pramāṇam eva |
bhukter eva prābalyasthalāni
kvacid bhukter eva śraiṣṭhyam itarābhyām ity āha sa eva |
rathyādinagaradvārajalavāhādisaṃśaye |
bhukter eva tu gurvī syāt pramāṇeṣv iti niścayaḥ || iti |
sthāvareṣu viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
vidyamāne ‘pi likhite jīvatsv api ca sākṣiṣu |
viśeṣataḥ sthāvarāṇāṃ yan na bhuktaṃ na tat sthiram || [125]
kasyāścid bhukteḥ kāryāntaram āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
paśyato ‘bruvato bhūmer hānir viṃśativārṣikī |
pareṇa bhujyamānāyā dhanasya daśavārṣikī ||
pareṇa asaṃbandhena bhujyamānāṃ bhuvaṃ dhanaṃ vā paśyataḥ abruvataḥ madīyeyaṃ bhūḥ tvayā na bhoktavyety apratiṣedhatas tasyā bhūmeḥ viṃśativārṣikī viṃśativarṣopabhoganimittā hānir bhavati dhanasya tu hastyaśvāder hāniḥ daśavārṣikī |
nanv eta anupapannam, na hy apratiṣedhāt svatvam api gacchati | apratiṣedhasya dānavikrayādivat svatvanivṛttihetutvasya lokaśāstryor aprasiddhatvāt | nāpi viṃśativarṣabhogāt svatvam | upabhogasya svatve apramāṇatvāt | pramāṇasya prameyaṃ pratyutpādakatvāt | rikthakrayādiṣu svatvakārakahetṣv apāṭhāc ca | tathā hi – “svāmī rikthakrayasaṃvibhāgaparigrahādhigameṣu | brāhmaṇasyādhikaṃ labdham | kṣatriyasya vijitam, nirviṣṭaṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ” (GDh 10.39–42) ity aṣṭāv eva svatvakāraṇahetūn gautamaḥ paṭhati | na bhogam | na cedam eva vacanaṃ viṃśativarṣopabhogasya svatvāpattihetutvaṃ pratipādayatīti yuktam | svatvasya svatvahetūnāṃ ca lokasiddhatvena śāstraikasamadhigamyatvābhāvasya dhanārjananayavyutpādyatvāt | etac ca vibhāgaprakaraṇe nipuṇataram upapādayiṣyate | gautamavacanaṃ tu niyamārtham | api ca –
anāgamaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte bahūny abdaśatāny api |
coradaṇḍena taṃ pāpaṃ daṇḍayet pṛthivīpatiḥ ||
ity etad apy anāgamopabhogasya svatvahetutve virudhyate | na ca “anāgamaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte” ity etat parokṣabhogaviṣayam | “paśyato ‘bruvataḥ” [126] ity etat pratyakṣaviṣayaṃ yuktaṃ vaktum | “anāgamaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte” iti aviśeṣābhidhānāt |
nopabhoge balaṃ kāryam āhartrā tatsutena vā |
paśustrīpuruṣādīnām iti dharmo vyavasthitaḥ ||
iti kātyāyanavacanāc ca | samakṣabhoge ca hānikāraṇābhāvena hāner asaṃbhavāt | na caitan mantavyam, ādhipratigrahakrayeṣu pūrvasyāḥ kriyāyāḥ prābalyāpavādena bhūmiviṣaye viṃśativarṣopabhogayuktāyā dhanaviṣaye daśavarṣopabhogayuktāyāḥ uttarasyāḥ kriyāyāḥ prābalyam anenocyata iti | yatas teṣu uttaraiva kriyā tatvato nopapadyate | svayam etac cādheyaṃ deyaṃ vikreyaṃ ca bhavati | na cāpi tasya vikrītasya dattasya vā svatvam asti asvatvasya dāne pratigrahe ca daṇḍaḥ smaryate –
adeyaṃ yaś ca gṛhṇāti yaś cādeyaṃ prayacchati |
ubhau tau coravac chāsyau dāpyau cottamasāhasam || iti |
atha ādhyādīnāṃ trayāṇām apavādate asya vacanasya –
ādhisīmopanikṣepajalabāladhanair vinā |
tathopanidhirājastrīśrotriyāṇāṃ dhanair api ||
ity ādhyādiṣu paśyato ‘bruvato ‘pi bhūmeḥ viṃśater dhanasya daśabhyo varṣebhya ūrdhvam apy upacayahānir bhavatīti uttare vacane apavādo nopapadyate | tasmād bhūmyādīnāṃ hānir anupannaiva (anupapannaiva?) | nāpi vyavahārahāniḥ | yataḥ –
[127]        upekṣā kurvatas tasya tūṣṇīṃ bhūtasya tiṣṭhataḥ |
kāle ‘tipanne pūrvoktavyavahāro na sidhyat || iti |
nāradena upekṣāliṅgābhāvakṛtavyavahārahānir uktā | na svabhāvakṛtā | tathā manunāpi |
ajaḍaś ced apaugaṇḍo viṣaye cāsya bhujyate |
bhagnaṃ tad vyavahāreṇa bhoktā tad dhanam arhati ||
iti vyavahārato bhaṅgo darśito na vastuto vyavahārabhaṅgaḥ | evaṃ bhoktā kila vadati – “ajaḍo ‘yam apaugaṇḍo bālaḥ asya saṃnidhau viṃśativarṣāṇi anākrośaṃ mayā bhuktam | tatra bahavaḥ sākṣiṇaḥ santi | yady asya svam anyāyena mayā bhujyeta tadāyaṃ kim ity etāvantaṃ kālam udāsta” iti | tatra cāyaṃ niruttaro bhavati | evaṃ niruttarasyāpi vāstavo vyavahāro bhavaty eva,
chalaṃ nirasya bhūtena vyavahārān nayen nṛpaḥ |
iti niyamāt |
atha matam – yady api na vastuhāniḥ, nāpi vyavahārahāniḥ, tathāpi paśyataḥ abruvataḥ apratiṣedhataś ca vyavahārahāniśaṅkā bhavatīti tannivṛttaye tūṣṇīṃ na sthātavyam iti upadiśyata iti |
tac ca na, smārtakālasahitāyā bhukteḥ vyavahārahāniśaṅkākāraṇatvābhāvāt | tūṣṇīṃ na sthātavyam ity etāvanmātrābhidhitsāyāṃ viṃśatigrahaṇam avivakṣitaṃ syāt |
athocyeta viṃśatigrahaṇaṃ ūrdhvaṃ patradoṣodbhāvananirākaraṇārtham yathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
śaktasya saṃnidhāv artho yasya lekhyena bhujyate |
viṃśadvarṣāṇy atikrāntaṃ tatpatraṃ doṣavarjitam || [128] iti |
tad api na, ādhyādiṣv (?) api viṃśater ūrdhvaṃ patradoṣodbhāvanasya tannirāsakaraṇasya ca samatvenāpavādāsaṃbhavāt | tathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
atha viṃśatiparvāṇi ādhibhuktiḥ suniścitā |
tena lekhyena tatsiddhiḥ lekhyaṃ taddoṣavarjitam ||
sīmāvivāde nirṇīte sīmāpatraṃ vidhīyate |
tasya doṣāḥ pravaktavyāḥ yāvad varṣāṇi viṃśatiḥ || iti |
ataś ca dhanasya daśavārṣikīty etad api pratyuktaṃ veditavyam | etena candrikārāroktam “hāniś cātra likhitabalena ātmīyatvaprasādhanamātrasyābhipretā | na punar bhūmyādau tatphale vā svatvasya veti | nopekṣāmātreṇa svatvam apaitīty uktatvāt” ity apāstam | viṃśativārṣiṅīty atra vacane viṃśatigrahaṇena viṃśater ūrdhvaṃ patradoṣodbhāvananirākaraṇārthatvasya pratyuktatvāt |
bhogabalābalavyavasthā
tad ayam atra niṣkarṣaḥ – bhūmer dhanasya ca na svarūpahāniḥ | nāpi vyavahārahāniḥ | kiṃ tu phalanānir iha vivakṣitā | nirākrośaviṃśativarṣopabhāgād ūrdhvaṃ yady api svāmī vyavahārataḥ kṣetraṃ labhate, tathāpi phalānusaraṇaṃ na labhate | apratiṣedhalakṣaṇāt svāparādhād asmāc ca vacanād eva |
parokṣabhoge tu viṃśater ūrdhvam api phalānusaraṇaṃ labhata eva | paśyata iti vacanāt | pratyakṣabhoge ca sākrośe abruvata iti vacanāt | viṃśateḥ prāk pratyakṣe nirākrośe ca labate | viṃśatigrahaṇāt | ataś ca svāmyupekṣālakṣaṇasvāparādhāt | asmāc ca vacanād viṃśater ūrdhvaṃ naṣṭaṃ phalaṃ na labhate | anāgamabhoge tu – [129]
bhoktā –
anāgamaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte bahūny abdaśatāny api |
coradaṇḍena taṃ pāpaṃ daṇḍayet pṛthivīpatiḥ ||
ity anena proktaṃ daṇḍaṃ nārhati, hānir viṃśativārṣikīty anena taduktadaṇḍasyāpodyatvād iti sthitam iti vijñāneśvaraḥ [on YDh 2.24] | etena daśavārṣikīty etad api vyākhyātam | ata eva vyāsaḥ |
varṣāṇi viṃśatir yasya bhūmir bhuktā parair iha |
sati rājñi samarthasya tasya seha na sidhyati ||
atrāpavādam āha manuḥ |
saṃprītyā bhujyamānāni na naśyanti kadācana |
dhenur uṣṭro vahann aśvo yac ca damyaḥ prayujyate ||
ata eva manuḥ |
ādhiś ca sīmātmadhanaṃ nikṣepopanidhistriyaḥ |
rājasvaṃ śrotriyadravyaṃ nopabhogena naśyati ||
ādheḥ bhuktau ādhitvaupādhika eva bhoga ity upekṣāyām api na puruṣāparādhaḥ | sīmnaḥ cirakṛtatuṣāṅgārādigopyacihaiḥ susādhanatvād upekṣā saṃbhavati | nātmadhanasya bandhubhir upayoge saṃbandhitvenopekṣā saṃbhavaḥ | kecin nāṭadhanaṃ – śaulūṣadham ity āhuḥ | teṣām abhinayādyabhyāsādiniviṣṭacittatvād yuktam upekṣaṇam | nikṣepopanidhyor lakṣaṇam aṣṭādaśapadanirūpaṇāpasare nirūpitam | tayor upanidhānanikṣepaṇakāle bhukteḥ pratiṣiddhatvāt pratiṣedhātikaramopabhoge sodyalābhād upekṣopapattiḥ | strīṇām aprāgalbhyād ajñānāc ca [130] śrotriyasyādhyayanādhyāpanatadanuṣṭhānavyākulatvāt rājño bahukāryavyākulatvād upekṣā yuktaivety evamādiṣu sarvatropekṣākāraṇasaṃbhavāt pratyakṣabhoge nirākrośe ca na kadācid api phalahāniḥ | kasyāścid bhukteḥ prāmāṇyam āha pitāmahaḥ |
sāgamā dīrghakālā ca vicchedoparavojjhitā |
pratyarthisaṃnidhānā ca bhuktiḥ pañcavidhā smṛtā ||
uparavaḥ – ākrośaḥ | pañcavidheti – pañcāṅgeti yāvat | ata eva vyāsaḥ |
sāgamo dīrghakālaś ca vicchedoparavojjhitaḥ |
pratyarthisaṃnidhānaś ca pañcāṅgo bhoga iṣyate ||
iti vadan ekāṅgavaikalye ‘py aprāmāṇyam eva bhogasyeti darśayati, tathā ca nāradaḥ |
saṃbhogaṃ kevalaṃ yas tu kīrtayen nāgamaṃ kvacit |
bhogacchalāpadeśena vijñeyaḥ sa tu tatkaraḥ ||
tena sabhyasaṃnidhāv āgamādikam apy aṅgaṃ kīrtanīyam ity abhiprāyaḥ | ata evāha kātyāyanaḥ |
praṇaṣṭāgamalekhyena bhogārūḍhena vādinā |
kālaḥ pramāṇaṃ dānaṃ ca kīrtanīyāni saṃsadi || iti |
dānagrahaṇam āgamopalakṣaṇārtham | caśabdaḥ sātatyādiviśeṣaṇagrahaṇārthaḥ | tad ayam atra niṣkarṣaḥ – bhogaikapramāṇavādinā bhogākhyapramāṇaṃ tadviśeṣaṇāni āgamadīrghakālādīni ca kīrtanīyāni | kīrtitāni ca tadvipratipattau sādhanīyāni | kīrtanamātreṇa niścayābhāvāt |
[131]
āgamabhyuktyoḥ parasparasāpekṣatvam
evaṃ ca dīrghakālabhuktir āgamānugṛhītā pramāṇam | āgamas tu dīrghakālabhuktyanugṛhīta iti | ata eva pitāmahaḥ |
nāgamena vinā bhuktiḥ nāgamo bhuktivarjitaḥ |
tayor anyonyasaṃbandhāt pramāṇatvaṃ vyavasthitam || iti |
anenaivābhiprāyeṇa bṛhaspatir api |
bhuktyā kevalayā naiva bhūmiḥ siddhim avāpnuyāt |
āgamenāpi śuddhena dvābhyāṃ sidhyati nānyathā ||
kevalayā – āgamarahitayety arthaḥ | ata eva hārītaḥ |
na mūlena vinā śākhā antarikṣe prarohati |
āgamas tu bhaven mūlaṃ bhuktiḥ śākhā prakīrtitā ||
stokabhuktyabhāve āgamadaurbalyam
āgamasya bhoganirapekṣatvena prāmāṇyaṃ nāstīty āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
āgame ‘pi balaṃ naiva bhuktiḥ stokāpi yatra no | iti |
yasminn āgame svalpāpi bhuktir nāsti tasminn āgame balasaṃpūrṇatā naivāsti | ayam abhisandhiḥ – svasvatvanivṛttiḥ parasvatvāpādanaṃ ca paro yadi dānaṃ svīkaroti tadā saṃpadyate | tathā svīkāraś ca trividhaḥ mānaso vācikaḥ kāyikaś ceti | tatra mānaso mamedam iti saṅkalparūpaḥ | vācikas tu mamedam iti abhivyāhārollekhī savikalpakapratyayaḥ | kāyikaḥ punar upādānābhimarśanādirūpo ‘nekavidhaḥ | tatra ca niyamaḥ smaryate –
[132]        dadyāt kṛṣṇājinaṃ pucche gāṃ pucche kariṇaṃ kare |
kesareṣu tathaivāśvaṃ dāsīṃ śirasi dāpayet || iti |
tatra trividho’pi svīkāraḥ udakadānānantaram eva | kṣetrādau punaḥ phalopabhogavyatirekeṇa kāyikasvīkārāsaṃbhavāt svalpenāpy upabhogena bhavitavyam | anyathā dānavikrayādeḥ saṃpūrṇatā na bhavatīti phalopabhogalakṣaṇakāyikasvīkāravikalatayā āgamo durlabho bhavatīti | atrāgamasya daurbalyamātram ity āh nāradaḥ |
ādau tu kāraṇaṃ dānaṃ madhye bhuktis tu sāgamā || iti |
ayam arthaḥ – ādye puruṣe sākṣilekhyābhyāṃ bhāvita āgamo bhogād apy adhiko balavān | pūrvakramāgatas tu bhogo vināpi bhāvitā āgamac caturthapuruṣe likhitena bhāvitā āgamād balavān | madhye tu bhogarāhitād āgamāt stokabhogasahito ‘py āgamo balavān iti | atredam upatiṣṭhate yājñavalkyoktam |
āgamo ‘bhyadhiko bhogād vinā pūrvakramāgatāt | iti |
nanu ‘vinā pūrvakramāgatād’ ity anena āgamanirapekṣatvena prāmāṇyam uktaṃ bhogasya, tac ca ‘āgamo ‘bhyadhiko bhogād’ ity anena virudhyate it cet — maivam, smārtāsmārtakālaviṣayatvād vacanasya, āgamo ‘bhyadhiko bhogād iti smārtakālaviṣayam | ‘vinā pūrvakramāgatād’ ity etad asmārtakālaviṣayaṃ veditavyam | ataś ca smaraṇayogyakāle yogyānupalabdhyā āgamābhāvaniścayasaṃbhavāt āgamajñānasāpekṣasyaiva bhogasya prāmāṇyam | asmartakāle tu [133] yogyānupalabdhyabhāvena āgamābhāvaniścayāsaṃbhavāt āgamajñānanirapekṣa eva sātatyaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭo bhogaḥ pramāṇam | ata evoktaṃ kātyāyanena |
smārtakāle kriyā bhūmeḥ sāgamā bhuktir iṣyate |
asmārte ‘nugamābhāvāt krakāt tripuruṣāgatā || iti |
anugamābhāvaḥ – yogyānupalabdhyabhāvenāgamābhāvaniścayāsaṃbhavaḥ | svatvahetupratigrahakrayādir āgamaḥ | nanu yac ca –
anyāyenāpi yad bhuktaṃ pitrā pūrvatamais tribhiḥ |
na tac chakyam apākartuṃ kramāt tripuruṣāgamam || iti |
pitreti sahārthe tṛtīyā | kramāt tripuruṣāgamam iti tu smārtakālopalakṣaṇam | tripuruṣavivakṣāyām ekavarṣābhyantare ‘pi puruṣatrayātikramasaṃbhavād dvitīye varṣe nirāgamasyāpi bhogasya prāmāṇyaprasaṅgāt |
smārtakāle kriyā bhūmeḥ sāgamā bhuktir iṣyate | iti |
smṛtivirodhaś ca syāt | kiṃ tu anyāyenāpi bhuktam apahartuṃ na śakyam | kiṃ punar anyāyāniścaya ity evaṃ param | yat tūktam –
yad vināgamam atyantaṃ bhuktaṃ pūrvais tribhir bhavet |
na tac chakyam apākartuṃ kramāt tripuruṣāgatam ||
iti, tatrātyantam āgamaṃ sātatyādiguṇaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭam upalabhyamānaṃ vineti vyākhyeyam | na punar āgamasvarūpaṃ vineti | āgamasvarūpābhāve bhogaśatenāpi na svatvaṃ bhavatīty uktaṃ vijñānayoginā |
[134]
smārtakālapramāṇam
smārtakālasvarūpam āha pitāmahaḥ |
triṃśatsamā yā tu bhuktā bhūmir avyāhatā paraiḥ |
bhuktiḥ sā pauruṣī jñeyā dviguṇā tu dvipauruṣī ||
tripauruṣī tu triguṇā tata ūrdhvaṃ cirantanī |
atra nāradaḥ |
anāgamaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte bahūny abdaśatāny api |
coradaṇḍena taṃ pāpaṃ daṇḍayet pṛthivīpatiḥ || iti |
anāgamam iti siddhavan nirdeśād anvāhitādīnāṃ bhoge daṇḍa iti jñāpitam | anyathā pūrvoktavacanavirodhāt | niścitāgamāś ca tenaiva darśitāḥ |
anvāhitaṃ hṛtaṃ nyastaṃ balāvaṣṭabdhayācitam |
apratyakṣaṃ ca yad bhuktaṃ ṣaḍāgamavivarjitāḥ ||
anvāhitam – anyasmai dātum arpitam | balāvaṣṭabdham – rājaprasādādibalena (?) bhuktam | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
yā rājñā krodhalobhābhyāṃ chalān nyāyena vā kṛtā |
pradattā cāpi tuṣṭena na sā siddhim avāpnuyāt ||
yā khalv anyasya bhūmiḥ krodhādinā rājñā parabhogyatayā kṛtā tuṣṭyā ca anyasmai dattā sā cirantanabhogenāpi na bhoktur siddhyatīty arthaḥ | pūrvasyāpavādam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
yo ‘bhiyuktaḥ paretaḥ syāt tasya rikthi tam uddharet |
na tatra kāraṇaṃ bhuktir āgamena vinā kṛtā || [135]
abhiyuktaḥ – akṛtavyavahāranirṇaya eva | paretaḥ – paralokaṃ gataḥ | tadā tasya rikthi – putrādiḥ |tam āgamam uddharet | pūrvābhiyogena bhogasya sāpavādatvāt | ata evoktaṃ nāradena |
tathārūḍhavivādasya pretasya vyavahāriṇaḥ |
putreṇa so ‘rthaḥ saṃśodhyo na taṃ bhogān nivartayet || iti |
evaṃ ca vijñānayoginā smārtakālaḥ śatavarṣaparyantaḥ “śatāyuḥ puruṣaḥ” iti śruteḥ ity uktam | candrikākāreṇa tu pañcādhikaśatavarṣaparyantaṃ smārtakāla ity uktam | tat tu prāyikābhiprāyikam | navativarṣaparyantāyās tripuruṣyāḥ pratipāditatvāt |
atra matāntaram
atra varadarājaḥ – viṃśatāv āgamaprābalyam | bhogasya tadānuguṇyam | dvitīye bhukteḥ prābalyam | caturthapuruṣe pañcāṅgabhoga eva pramāṇam | nāgamāpekṣeti siddham iti |
atra bṛhaspatis tu viśeṣam āha ¦
trivarṣaṃ bhujyate yena samagrā bhūr avāritā |
tasya sā nāpahartavyā kṣamāliṅgaṃ na ced vadet ||
catuṣpāddhanadhānyādi varṣād dhānim avāpnuyāt ||
etad vacanaṃ bhukter ādarārthaṃ yathāśrutaṃ vyākhyeyaṃ bahupratipakṣatvād iti varadarājaḥ |
iti likhitabhuktinirūpaṇam
[136]
atha sākṣiprakaraṇam
(sākṣisvarūpam)
sākṣisvarūpam āha nāradaḥ |
brāhmaṇāḥ kṣatriyā vaiśyāḥ śūdrā ye cāpy aninditāḥ |
prativarṇaṃ bhaveyus te sarve sarveṣu vā punaḥ || iti |
atra sati saṃbhave vyavahartuḥ savarṇā eva sākṣiṇa ity arthaḥ | ata eva nāradaḥ |
śreṇiṣu śreṇipuruṣāḥ vargeṣu vargiṇaḥ |
bahirvāsiṣu bāhyāḥ syuḥ striyaḥ strīṣu ca sākṣiṇaḥ || iti |
etad apy upalakṣaṇam | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
tapasvino dānaśīlāḥ kulīnāḥ satyavādinaḥ |
dharmapradhānā ṛjavaḥ putravanto dhanānvitāḥ ||
tryavarāḥ sākṣiṇo jñeyāḥ śrautasmārtakriyāparāḥ |
yathājāti yathāvarṇaṃ sarve sarveṣu vā smṛtāḥ || iti |
traya avarā nyūnā eṣāṃ te tryavarāḥ | tribhyo ‘rvāṅ na bhavanti | jātayo – mūrdhāvasiktādayaḥ | mūrdhāvasiktānāṃ mūrdhāvasiktāḥ sākṣiṇaḥ | atra varṇāḥ – brāhmaṇādayaḥ | atra brāhmaṇānāṃ brahmanā eva sākṣiṇo bhavanti | evaṃ kṣatriyeṣv api draṣṭavyam | atrāpavādam āha nāradaḥ |
śreṇyādiṣu tu vargeṣu kaścid vai dveṣyatām iyāt |
tasya tebhyo na sākṣyaṃ syāt dveṣṭāraḥ sarva eva te || [137] iti |
śreṇyādigrahaṇaṃ varṇādipradarśanārtham | dveṣyādigrahaṇaṃ pramādālasyavipralipsādīnām upalakṣaṇārtham |
sākṣiparīkṣā
yathāha pitāmahaḥ |
niyamenaiva saṃśodhyāḥ sarva eva hi sākṣiṇaḥ |
vipralipsādayo doṣā vijñyāś carmacakṣuṣā ||
prativākyam apekṣyaṃ syāt prāmāṇyaṃ dharmaniścitam || iti |
ādiśabdena pramādālasyadveṣādayo gṛhyante | prativākyaṃ prāmāṇyam apekṣyam | na tu pratipuruṣam, avasthābhedena vipralipsādīnāṃ saṃbhāvayituṃ śakyatvāt |
sākṣiyogyāḥ
iti ? | ata eva manuḥ |
āptāḥ sarveṣu varṇeṣu kāryāḥ kāryeṣu sākṣiṇaḥ |
sarvadharmavido ‘lubdhān viparītāṃs tu varjayet || iti |
yat tu saṃgrahakāraḥ |
kartavyāḥ sarvakāryeṣu tribhyaḥ ārabhya sākṣiṇaḥ |
dvyekayoḥ pratiṣedhaḥ (?) syād eko ‘py ubhayasaṃmataḥ || iti |
apiśabdād ubhayasaṃmatatve dvayor api grahaṇam | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
uttamarṇādhamarṇau dvau sākṣiṇau lekhakas tathā |
samavāyena caiteṣāṃ lekhyaṃ kurvīta nānyathā || [138] iti |
atra sākṣiṇāv iti dvitvoktiḥ | yat tūktaṃ bṛhaspatinā –
nava sapta pañca va syuś catvāras traya eva vā |
ubhau vā śrotriyau śantau naikaṃ pṛcchet kadācana || iti |
tac ca ubhayasaṃmatavyatiriktaviṣayam | nava pañca sapta trayāṇāṃ viṣamasaṃkhyātve ‘pi saṃkhyāsāmyapratipādanaṃ prābalyārtham ity uktatvād adoṣaḥ | navādisaṃkhyānām apavādam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
ekasyāpi sākṣitvam
ubhayānumataḥ sākṣī bhavaty eko ‘pi dharmavit | iti |
jñānapūrvakaṃ nityanaimittikadharmānuṣṭhātā dharmavit | ata eva śaṅkhalikhitau: “maulāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ sākṣyarthaṃ viditavantaḥ śrautasmārtakriyāyuktāḥ vigatadveṣamatsarāḥ apravāsino yuvāno lobhamohavivarjitāḥ tryavarāḥ navasaṃkhyākā na jātu kūṭatām pratipadyante” iti | ata eva nāradaḥ |
jñātvā kāryaṃ deśakālau kuśalāḥ kūṭakārakāḥ |
kurvanti sadṛśaṃ lekhyaṃ naite syuḥ sākṣiṇo yathā ||
viparītāṃs tu varjayed ity uktam |
varjanīyasākṣiṇaḥ
tān āha manuḥ |
nārthasaṃbandhino nāptāḥ na sahāyā na vairiṇaḥ |
na dṛṣṭadoṣāḥ kartavyā na vyādhyārtā na dūṣitāḥ ||
[139]        na sākṣī nṛpatiḥ kāryaḥ na kāmukakuśīlavāḥ |
na śrotriyo na liṅgastho na saṅgebhyo vivarjitaḥ ||
na vṛddho na śuśur naiko nāntyo na vikalendriyaḥ |
nādhyadhīno na vaktavyo na dasyur na vikarmakṛt ||
nārto na matto nonmatto na kṣuttṛṣṇopapīḍitaḥ |
na śramārto na kāmārto na kruddho nāpi taskaraḥ || iti |
arthasaṃbandhinaḥ – pratipādyārthasaṃbandhinaḥ – pratibhūprabhṛtaya ity arthaḥ | yad vā vipratipadyamānārthasaṃbandhinaḥ sahāyāḥ | dūṣitāḥ mahāpātakair abhiyuktāḥ | śrotriyo vedoktakarmānuṣṭānatatparaḥ na tu vedapāṭhakaḥ | tasya sākṣitvenābhidhānāt | liṅgastho brahmacārī | saṅgebhyo vivarjitaḥ vānaprasthaḥ | eka iti ubhayānanumataḥ | ekasyobhayānumatasya eko ‘py ubhayasaṃmata iti sākṣitvābhidhānāt | antyaḥ caṇḍālādiḥ | etac caṇḍālādivyatiriktavyavahāraviṣayam | tatkulavyavahāreṣu tadīyānām eva sākṣitvaniyamāt | “tatkulīnās tatkulavyavahāre sākṣiṇaḥ” iti smṛteḥ | vaktavyaḥ bodhyaḥ | dasyuḥ paśyato haraḥ | mattādīnāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ pūrvam evoktam | taskaraḥ suruṅgāder nirmātā | asākṣiṇāṃ pāñcavidhyam āha nāradaḥ |
asākṣiṇaḥ
asākṣy api vinirdiṣṭaḥ śāstre pañcavidho budhaiḥ |
vacanād doṣato bhedāt svayamuktir mṛtāntaraḥ ||
ayam arthaḥ – asākṣihetūnāṃ pañcavidhatvāt tanmūlena asākṣy api pañcavidha iti | vacanād asākṣiṇo yājñavalkya āha |
[140]        śrotriyās tāpasā vṛddhāḥ ye tu pravrajitādayaḥ |
asākṣiṇas te vacanān nātra hetur udāhṛtaḥ || iti |
tāpasaḥ – vānaprasthaḥ | ādiśabdena pitrā vivadamānānāṃ grahaṇam | yathāha śaṅkhaḥ: “pitrā vivadamānagurukulavāsivānaprasthanirgandhā asākṣiṇaḥ” iti | nāradena doṣād asākṣiṇo darśitāḥ |
stenāh sāhasikāś caṇḍāḥ kitavā vadhakās tathā |
asākṣiṇas tu duṣṭatvāt teṣu satyaṃ na vidyate || iti |
bhedād asākṣiṇāṃ ca svarūpaṃ tenaiva darśitam |
sākṣiṇāṃ likhitānāṃ ca nirdiṣṭānāṃ ca vādinām |
teṣām eko ‘nyathāvādī bhedāt sarve na sākṣiṇaḥ || iti |
atra likhitagrahaṇāt akṛtasākṣiṇāṃ bhede ‘pi na sākṣyahānir ity uktam | svayamuktasvarūpaṃ sa evāha |
svayamuktir anirdiṣṭaḥ svayam evaitya yo vadet |
sūcīty uktaḥ sa śāstreṣu na sa sākṣitvam arhati || iti |
mṛtāntarasyāpi lakṣaṇam āha vyāsaḥ |
arthī yatra na vidyeta tatra sākṣī mṛtāntaraḥ |
pratyarthī vā mṛto yatra tatrāpy evaṃ prakalpayet ||
ayam arthaḥ – arthinā vā pratyarthinā vā sākṣiṇo “yūyam atrārthe (?) sākṣiṇaḥ” iti yo ‘rthaḥ śrāvayitavyo bhavet tasmin arthini prathyarthini vā asati mṛte vā sākṣī mṛtāntara iti |
[141]         yat tu vijñānayoginoktaṃ “tatra tu mumūrṣuṇā svasthena vā pitrā putrādayaḥ śrāvitāḥ amī sākṣiṇaḥ iti atra mṛtāntaro ‘pi sākṣī” (on YDh 2.69) | yathāha nāradaḥ |
mṛtāntaro ‘rthini prete mumūrṣuśrāvito dṛḍhaḥ | iti |
tat tu śrāvitasya sākṣitvaṃ nāpagatam ity evaṃ param | na punar mṛtāntarasyāpi sākṣitvavidhānārtham ity avagantavyam | mṛtāntarasya sākṣitvāsaṃbhavāt | nanu bhedād asākṣiṇa ity anupapannam | “sākṣidvaidhe bahūnāṃ vā guṇavattamānāṃ vā vacanaṃ grāhyam” iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | samasaṃkhyānāṃ vā samaguṇānāṃ vā dvaidhe yuktisadhrīcīnaṃ vacanaṃ grāhyam ity arthād uktaṃ bhavatīti nibandhanakāravacanād iti cen, maivam | bahavaḥ sākṣiṇo militvā sākṣyabhāvam ekasmin niveśya saṃsadi anena yad yuktaṃ tad asmākaṃ saṃmatam ity avocan, tadāsāv eko ‘pi visaṃvādī, sarve te sākṣiṇo visaṃvādina ity evaṃparaṃ vacanam iti na kaścid virodhaḥ |
sākṣivibhāgādi
sākṣī tu dvividhaḥ – darśanaśravaṇabhedāt | yathāha pitāmahaḥ |
samakṣadarśanāt sākṣyaṃ śravaṇāc caiva sidhyati | iti |
sa ca dvividhaḥ kṛtākṛtabhedāt | akṛtaḥ ṣaḍvidhaḥ yathāha nāradaḥ |
grāmaś ca prāḍvivākaś ca rājā ca vyavahāriṇām |
kāryeṣv adhikṛto yaḥ syād arthinā prahitaś ca yaḥ ||
kulyāḥ kulavivādeṣu vijñeyās te ‘pi sākṣiṇaḥ || [142] iti |
prāḍvivākagrahaṇaṃ lekhakasyāpy upalakṣaṇārtham |
lekhakaḥ prāḍvivākaś ca sabhyāś caivānupūrvaśaḥ |
nṛpe paśyati tat kāryaṃ sākṣiṇaḥ samudāhṛtāḥ || iti |
kṛtasākṣiṇāṃ pañcānāṃ svarūpam āha nāradaḥ |
likhitaḥ smāritaś caiva yadṛcchābhijña eva ca |
gūḍhaś cottarasākṣī ca sākṣī pañcavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
teṣāṃ svarūpam |
arthinā svayam āhūtaḥ yo lekhye saṃniveśyate |
