sa sākṣī likhito nāma smāritaḥ patrakād ṛte || iti |
patrakād ṛte – patrakaṃ vihāya svakāryasiddhyarthaṃ dṛṣṭvā punaḥ punaḥ smāryate sa sākṣī smārita ity arthaḥ, yadṛcchayāgataḥ sākṣī kriyate yaḥ sa yadṛcchābhijñaḥ –
prayojanārtham ānītaḥ prasaṃgād āgataś ca yaḥ |
dvau sākṣiṇau cālikhitau pūrvapakṣasya sādhakau ||
smāritayadṛcchābhijñayoḥ patrānārūḍhatve ‘pi prasaṅgād āgatatvaprayojanārtham ānītatvābhyāṃ bheda ity arthaḥ |
arthinā svārthasiddhyarthaṃ pratyarthivacanaṃ sphuṭam |
yaḥ śrāvyate sthito gūḍho gūḍhasākṣī sa ucyate || iti |
sākṣiṇām api yaḥ sākṣyam upary upari bhāṣate |
śravaṇāc chrāvaṇād vāpi sa sākṣyuttarasaṃjñitaḥ || iti |
likhite tu viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
sudīrgheṇāpi kālena niścitaḥ siddhim āpnuyāt |
[143]        ātmanaiva likhej jñātam ajñas tv anyena lekhayet ||
yat punas tenaivoktam |
aṣṭamād vatsarāt siddhiṃ smāritasyeha sākṣiṇaḥ |
ā pañcamāt tathā siddhiṃ yadṛcchopagatasya tu ||
ā tṛtīyāt tathā varṣāt siddhiṃ gūḍhasya sākṣiṇaḥ |
ā ca saṃvatsarāt siddhiṃ vadanty uttarasākṣiṇaḥ || iti |
tat tu na niyamārtham | at evāha sa eva |
atha vā kālaniyamo na dṛṣṭaḥ sākṣiṇaṃ prati |
smṛtyapekṣaṃ hi sākṣitvam āhuḥ śāstravido janāḥ ||
yasya nopahatā buddhiḥ smṛtitantreṣu sākṣiṇaḥ |
sudīrtheṇāpi kālena sa sākṣī sākṣyam arhati || iti |
tatra nirduṣṭāḥ sākṣiṇaḥ santi cen nirdeṣṭavyāḥ sadoṣeṣu sākṣiṣu dūṣayitavyā ity āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
sākṣiṇo ‘rthisamuddiṣṭān satsu doṣeṣu dūṣayet |
aduṣṭān dūṣayan vādī tatsamaṃ daṇḍam arhati ||
tatsamo duṣṭasākṣidaṇḍasamaḥ | doṣās tu vivādakāla eva vaktavyāḥ nānyatrety (?) kātyāyanaḥ |
lekhyadoṣās tu ye kecit sākṣiṇāṃ caiva ye smṛtāḥ |
vādakāle tu vaktavyā paścād uktaṃ na dūṣayet ||
na dūṣayed ity anena daṇḍābhāvo ‘pi pratipāditaḥ | yata āha sa eva |
[144]        ukte ‘rthe sākṣiṇo yas tu dūṣayet prāgdūṣitān |
na ca tatkāraṇaṃ brūyāt prāpnuyāt pūrvasāhasam ||
kiṃ ca,
nāvedyena pramāṇaṃ tu doṣeṇaiva tu dūṣayet |
mithyābhiyoge daṇḍyaḥ syāt sādhyārthāc cāhi hīyate || iti |
ayam arthaḥ – doṣeṇaiva tu dūṣayet | na tu guṇābhāvena tasya vacanaprāmāṇyavighātakāritād ity abhiprāyaḥ | ata eva ṛṇādānādau niṣiddhetarasyāpi sākṣitvam abhyanujñātam | ato na guṇābhāvodbhāvanamātreṇa ṛṇādānādau nirdiṣṭasākṣiṇām asākṣitvasiddhiḥ | kiṃ tu dāsakitavatvādiniṣedhanimittotthāpanenety avagantavyam | sāhasādau na tāvanmātreṇa, kiṃ tu ajñānāsatyaśīlatvādidoṣodbhāvanenety avagantavyam |
anyais tu sākṣibhiḥ sādhye dūṣaṇe pūrvasākṣiṇām |
anavasthā bhaved doṣaḥ teṣām apy anyasaṃbhavāt ||
evaṃ prativādināṃ dūṣaṇapratipādanam | na prakaṭadūṣaṇaṃ kāryam | vaiyarthyāt | sabhyair eva tatprasiddher ity arthaḥ | yadi punar api prasiddaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ kīrtitam, tatra tadasādhayato daṇḍam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
asādhayan damaṃ dāpyaḥ pratyarthī sākṣiṇaḥ sphuṭam |
bhāvinaḥ sākṣiṇo varjyāḥ sākṣidharmanirākṛtāḥ ||
sphuṭaṃ yathā ca bhavati tathā sākṣidoṣam asaṃbhāvayan ity arthaḥ | doṣitvena sādhitāḥ sākṣiṇo varjyāḥ na tu daṇḍyā ity abhiprāyaḥ |
[145]        pratyarthino ‘rthino vāpi sākṣidūṣaṇasādhane |
na tu sākṣyabhiyogaḥ syāt vyavahārāntaraṃ tathā || iti |
pramāṇadoṣavivādanirṇayaḥ pūrvavyavahāramadhya eva kartavya ity abhiprāyaḥ | na ca vyavahāramadhye vyavahārāntaranirṇayasya anucitatvād ayuktam iti vācyam, pramāṇadoṣavivādanirṇayasya pūrvavyavahāraśeṣatvena tatraiva kāryatvāt | pṛthakphalābhāvena vyavahārāntaratvābhāvāc ceti | sākṣiṇaḥ sarve yasya vādinaḥ prativādino vā sādhyoktiṃ satyām ūcuḥ tasya jaya ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
sākṣiphalam
yasyocuḥ sākṣiṇaḥ satyāṃ pratijñāṃ vijayī bhavet | iti |
bṛhaspatir api |
yatrādoṣaḥ pratijñārthaḥ sākṣibhiḥ prativarṇitaḥ | iti |
pratijñā – sādhyoktiḥ | yasya punaḥ pratijñāṃ sākṣiṇaḥ asatyām ūcuḥ tasya parājaya ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
anyathāvādino yasya dhruvas tasya parājayaḥ | iti |
kvacid anṛtasākṣyabhyanujñā
nanu yājñavalkīyavacane: “varṇināṃ hi vadho yatra sākṣye tatrānṛtaṃ vadet” iti sākṣiṇām anṛtavacanena varṇināṃ vijayitvāt satyavacanenaiva vijayitvam iti na niyamyata iti pratīyata iti cen,
maivam, tatrānṛtavacanasya vihitatvāt prāyaścittād adoṣa iti | na cātra anṛtavacanaṃ na vihitam iti vācyam | uttarārdhe [146] “tatpāvanāya nirvāpyaś caruḥ sārasvato dvijaiḥ” iti anṛtavacanajanitaduritāpanodanaprāyaścittasmaraṇāt |
nanv evam anṛtavacanasya vihitatvāt tatprāyaścittam anupapannam iti cet,
ucyate, atra vijñānayogino (on YDh 2.83cd) matam – uttarārdhapūrvārdhayor ekavākyatāṅgīkārāt uttarārdhārthānusāreṇa anṛtavacanajanitaduritanivṛttyarthaṃ sārasvataś caruḥ kāryaḥ iti | bhagavatpādamataṃ tu kṣāmavatīṣṭivat sārasvataḥ, ayam arthaḥ – tatpāvanāya śuddhyartham – doṣiṇāṃ saṃrakṣaṇārtham, etatparyantam ekaṃ vākyam, yatra varṇināṃ vadhas tatra tatpāvanāya sākṣyam anṛtaṃ vadet ato naimittikaṛtur iti sārasvataś carur naimittika iti | atra naimittikatve tatpāvanāya iti padaṃ nānvīyād iti anyatheti padam adhyāhṛtya vyācaṣṭe ṭīkākāraḥ | ayam arthaḥ – satyam eva vaditvā sārasvataś carur naimittikaḥ | na ca satyavacanānantaraṃ prāṇivyāpāde jāte tadapanodanāya sārasvataś caruḥ prāyaścittamātram iti vācyam | anṛtavacanasya vihitatvāt, vihitānuṣṭhāna eva prāyaścittasmaraṇāt | atra kecid etad vacanam anyathā paṭhitvā vyācakṣate – tatvāvanāya nirvāpyata iti | tatvāvanāya – satyasaṃrakṣaṇāya | asminn anṛte ‘pi sārasvataś carur naimittika eva, tatvāvanāyety asyāyam arthaḥ – tatvasya avanaṃ rakṣaṇam | tādṛśe samaye satyam anṛtavacanenaiva setsyatīty abhiprāyaḥ | kriyāgrahaṇamātre caturthī | yatra kecit sākṣiṇaḥ sādhyoktiṃ satyāṃ kecid asatyām ūcuḥ tatra manur āha |
sākṣidvaividhye nirṇayaḥ
bahutvaṃ parigṛhṇīyāt sākṣidvaidhe narādhipaḥ |
sameṣu ca guṇotkṛṣṭān guṇadvaidhe dvijottamān || [147]
nārado ‘pi |
sākṣiviprapatipattau tu pramāṇaṃ bahavo yataḥ |
tatsāmye śucayo grāhyāḥ same tu śucimattarāḥ ||
tatsāmyāt sākṣisāmye ca vivādo yatra dṛśyate |
sūkṣmatvāt sādhyadharmasya sādhyaṃ vyāvartayet tataḥ | iti |
sākṣibhir anyūnādhikam eva vaktavyam
pṛṣṭād ūnādhikaṃ na kartavyam iti kātyāyana āha |
nirdiṣṭeṣv arthajāteṣu sākṣī cet sākṣya āgate |
na brūyād akṣarasamaṃ na tan nigaditaṃ bhavet ||
sākṣye – sākṣyavāde | āgate – prāpte | akṣarasamam akṣarānurūpaṃ pṛṣṭārtham iti yāvat | pṛṣṭārthāsamarpakam apārtham ity arthaḥ | evaṃ pṛṣṭād ūnādikaṃ ca na vaktavyam |
ūnam abhyadhikaṃ vārthaṃ vibrūyur yatra sākṣiṇaḥ |
tad apy ayuktaṃ vijñeyaṃ eṣa sākṣiviniścayaḥ ||
kūṭasākṣicihnāni
viṣṇuḥ: “svabhāvād vikṛtau mukhavarṇavināśe ca asaṃbaddhapralāpe ca kūṭasākṣiṇaṃ vindyāt” iti | svabhāvavikṛtim āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
deśād deśāntaraṃ yāti sṛkkiṇī parileḍhi ca |
lalāṭaṃ svidyate yasya mukhaṃ vaivarṇyam eti ca ||
abhiyoge ‘tha sākṣye vā duṣṭaḥ sa parikīrtitaḥ || iti |
[148]
jānato ‘kathayituḥ kūṭasākṣidaṇḍaḥ
na dadāti hi yaḥ sākṣyaṃ jānann api narādhamaḥ |
sa kūṭasākṣiṇāṃ pāpaiḥ tulyo daṇḍena caiva hi ||
yo vipratipadyamānam arthaṃ jānann api daurātmyāt sākṣyaṃ nāṅgīkaroti “nāham atra sākṣī” iti asau kūṭasākṣivad daṇḍyaḥ tadvat pāpīti cārthaḥ | bṛhaspatis tu viśeṣam āha |
āhūto yas tu nāgacchet sākṣī rogavivarjitaḥ |
ṛṇaṃ damaṃ ca dāpyaḥ syāt tripakṣāt paratas tu saḥ ||
tathā ca manuḥ |
tripakṣād abruvan sākṣyam ṛṇādiṣu naro yadi |
tad ṛṇaṃ prāpnuyāt sarvaṃ daśabandhaṃ ca sarvataḥ ||
agadaḥ – svasthaḥ | daśabandhaśabdena daśamo ‘ṃśa ucyate | sa tu daṇḍo rājagrāhyaḥ | yat tu,
na kālaharaṇaṃ karyaṃ rājñā sākṣiprabhāṣaṇe |
mahān doṣo bhavet kāle dharmavyāvṛttilakṣaṇaḥ ||
iti, tat spaṣṭasākṣiviṣayam | ata āha kātyāyanaḥ |
samyak kriyāparijñāne jñeyaḥ kālas tu sākṣiṇām |
saṃdigdhaṃ patrasākṣyaṃ tu spaṣṭaṃ sadyo vivādayet || iti |
yājñavalkyas tu kūṭasākṣiṇo viśeṣam āha |
kūṭasākṣidaṇḍaḥ
pṛthak pṛthak daṇḍanīyāḥ kūṭakṛt sākṣiṇas tathā |
vivādadviguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ vivāsyo brāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || [149] iti |
asyārthaḥ – ye dhanādānādinā kūṭaṃ kūṭasākṣyaṃ kurvantīti kūṭakṛt sākṣiṇo mithyāvādina iti yāvat, te rājñā pṛthag daṇḍanīyāḥ | brāhmaṇas tu nirvāsyaḥ – rāṣṭrān nirvāsyaḥ na daṇḍyaḥ | etac ca lobhādikāraṇaviśeṣaparijñāne anabhyāse ca boddhavyam | yathāha manuḥ |
lobhāt sahasraṃ daṇḍyaḥ syāt mohāt pūrvaṃ tu sāhasam |
bhayād vai madhyamo daṇḍo maitryā pūrvaṃ caturguṇam ||
kāmād daśaguṇaṃ pūrvaṃ krodhāt tu triguṇaṃ param |
ajñānāt dviśate pūrṇe bāliśyāc chatam eva tu ||
etaddaṇḍasya kālaḥ
atra lobho ‘rthalipsā | moho viparyayajānam | bhayaṃ saṃtrāsaḥ | maitrī snehātiśayaḥ | kāmaḥ strīvyatikarābhilāṣaḥ | krodho ‘marṣaḥ | ajñānam asphūṭajñānam | bāliśyaṃ jñānānutpādaḥ | etat sarvaṃ saptāhādisākṣyavihitakālapratīkṣaṇānantaraṃ veditavyam | yadi sākṣidvaidhe guṇavattamānāṃ samasaṃkhyākānām api arthipratyarthinor anyataro ‘nyatarasya sākṣiṇo vacanaṃ nānumanyate tatra vādānantaraṃ saptāhadvisaptāhtrisaptāhaṣaṭcatvāriṃśaddvisāvadhi-rājakadaivikapratīkṣaṇaṃ kāryam | dravyatāratamyād ity nibandhanakāraḥ | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
saptāhāt tu pratīyeta yatra sākṣy anṛtaṃ vadet |
rogo ‘gnir jñātimaraṇaṃ dvisaptāhāt trisapta vā ||
ṣaṭcatvāriṃśake vāpi dravyajātyādibhedataḥ || [150]
dravyabhedaḥ – dravyatāratamyam | jātibhedaḥ – jātitāratamyam ity arthaḥ | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “śatanāśe ṣaṭcatvāriṃśaddivasapratīkṣaṇam | dviśatanāśe trisaptāhapratīkṣaṇam | pañcaśatanāśe dvisaptāhapratīkṣaṇam | sahasranāśe saptāhapratikṣaṇam” iti |
atra paṇavivekaḥ
atha śatādisaṃkhyāsaṃkyeyatvaṃ tāmrikapaṇānām ity āha bhāruciḥ | suvarṇamāṣāṇām ity āha varadarājaḥ | daṇḍavidhāne “sahasraṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍyaḥ” ityādau sahasrasaṃkhyāsaṃkyeyatvaṃ tāmrikapaṇānām ity āha vijñanayogī | bhārucis tu – sahasrasaṃkhyāsaṃkhyeyatvaṃ suvarṇamāṣāṇām ity āha | atra deśato vyavasthā |
kūṭasākṣidaṇḍavivekaḥ
kauṭasākṣyaṃ tu kurvāṇān trīn varṇān dhārmiko nṛpaḥ |
pravāsayed daṇḍayitvā brāhmaṇaṃ tu vivāsayet || iti |
etac cābhyāsaviṣayam, kurvāṇān iti vartamānanirdeśāt | kṣatriyādīn trīn varṇān daṇḍayitvā pravāsayet – mārayet pravāsaśabdasyārthaśāstre māraṇe prayogāt | asya kāṇḍasyārthaśāstrarūpatvāt | tatrāpi pravāsanaṃ oṣṭhacchedanaṃ jihvācchedanaṃ prāṇaviyojanaṃ ca kauṭasākṣyaviṣayānusāreṇa draṣṭavyam | brāhmaṇaṃ tu daṇḍayitvā vivāsayet – svarāṣṭrān nirvāsayet | [151]
yad vā vivāsanam – vāsaso rāhityaṃ nagnīkaraṇam ity arthaḥ | atha vā vasaty asminn iti vāso – gṛham; vivāsayet – bhagnagṛhaṃ kuryād iti yāvat | brāhmaṇasyāpi lobhādiviśeṣāparijñāne anabhyāse ca tatra tatrokārthadaṇḍa eva | abhyāse tv arthadaṇḍo vivāsanaṃ ca | na ca brāhmaṇasyārthadaṇḍo nāstīti mantavyam, arthadaṇḍābhāve śārīradaṇḍe ca niṣiddhe svalpe ‘py aparādhe nagnīkaraṇaṃ gṛhabhaṅgo ‘ṅkanaṃ vivāsanaṃ daṇḍābhāvo vā prasajyeran |
mahaty aparādhe brāhmaṇasyāpi dhanadaṇḍaḥ śārīradaṇḍaś cāsti
caturṇām api varṇānāṃ prāyaścittam akurvatām |
śārīraṃ dhanasaṃyuktaṃ daṇḍaṃ dharmyaṃ prakalpayet ||
iti smaraṇāc ca,
sahasraṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍyaḥ guptāṃ viprāṃ balād vrajan |
iti smaraṇāc ca | tatrāpi jātidravyānubandhādyapekṣayā vivāsanaṃ nagnīkaraṇaṃ gṛhabhaṅgo deśān nirvāsanaṃ ceti vyavasthā draṣṭavyā | lobhādikāraṇaviśeṣāparijñāne anabhyāse cālpaviṣaye kauṭasākṣye brāhmaṇasyāpi kṣatriyādivad arthadaṇḍa eva | mahāviṣaye tu deśān nirvāsanam eva | abhyāse sarveṣāṃ nagnīkaraṇaṃ gṛhabhaṅgaḥ deśān nirvāsanaṃ dhanadaṇḍaś ca militāś ca | yad pay uktaṃ manunā,
rāṣṭrād enam bahiṣkuryāt samagradhanam akṣatam |
iti, tat prakṛtasāhasaviṣayam, na sarvasāhasaviṣayam |. śārīradaṇḍo brāhmaṇasya na kadācid bhavati |
na jātu brāhmaṇaṃ hanyāt sarvapāpeṣv avasthitam || [152] [MDh 8.380]
iti sāmānyena manuvacanāc ca |
brāhmaṇasya vadhād bhūyān adharmo vidyate bhuvi |
tasmād asya vadhaṃ rājā manasāpi na cintayet || iti | [MDh 8.381]
iyaṃ viśuddhir uditā pramāpyākāmato dvijam |
kāmato brāhmaṇavadhe niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate ||
iti yājñavalkyavacanāc ca | ataś caturṇām api varṇānām ity atra caturṇāṃ varṇānāṃ dhanadaṇḍaḥ | śārīrasahitadhanadaṇḍas tu brāhmaṇavyatiriktatraivarṇikānām avagantavyaḥ |
nanu dvitīyakāṇḍe ‘rthaśāstram ity uktam | arthaśāstre ātatāyino brāhmaṇasya hananaṃ vidhīyate |
guruṃ vā bālavṛddhau vā brāhmaṇaṃ vā bahuśrutam |
ātatāyinam āyāntaṃ hanyād evāvicārayan ||
ātatāyivadhe doṣo hantur bhavati kaścana |
pracchannaṃ vā prakāśaṃ vā manyus taṃ manyum ṛcchati ||
tathā –
ātatāyinamāyāntam api vedāntagaṃ raṇe |
jighāṃsantaṃ jighāṃsīyān na tena bhrūṇahā bhavet || iti |
bhrūṇo – brāhmaṇaḥ | ātatāyitvaṃ kūṭasākṣiṇo ‘py asti | yathāha viṣṇuḥ: “paradārābhimarśakaḥ parakṣetrāpahārī udyatāsiḥ agnido garadaḥ paradravyāpahārī mahābhiyogeṣu kūṭasākṣī mithyāmahābhiyogī vety ātatāyinaḥ” iti | atra paraśabdena brāhmaṇa ucyate | [153] brāhmaṇadārābhimarśī brāhmaṇakṣetrāpahārī brāhmaṇadhanāpahārī brāhmaṇe mahāpātakābhiyoktā śatrapāṇibrāhmaṇe brāhmaṇe mahābhiyoge kūṭasākṣī brahmagṛheṣv agnidaḥ brāhmaṇe garadaś ceti | atra garadatvaṃ auṣadhyādinā nivṛtte viṣe | anyathā mahāpātakitvaprasaṅgāt |
udyatāsiviṣāgniś ca śāpodyatakaras tathā |
ātharvaṇena hantā ca piśunaś cāpi rājani ||
bhāryātikramakārī ca randhrānveṣaṇatatparaḥ |
evamādyān vijānīyāt sarvān evātatāyinaḥ || iti |
ataś ca,
agnido garadaś caiva śastrapāṇir dhanāpahaḥ |
kṣetradārāpahartā ca ṣaḍ ete ātatāyinaḥ ||
iti upalakṣaṇaṃ veditavyam |
dharmaśāstrārthaśāstrayor balābalavivecanā
atra kecid evaṃ parihāram āhuḥ – dharmaśāstrāntargatam eva arthaśāstram iha vivakṣitam, na uśanasādinirmitam arthaśāstram | ataś ca dharmaśāstravirodhe arthaśāstraṃ durbalam | yathoktaṃ yājñavalkyena |
arthaśāstrāt tu balavad dharmaśāstram iti sthitiḥ | iti |
yady api samānakartṛkatayā arthaśāstradharmaśāstrayoḥ svarūpato viśeṣo nāsti, tathāpi prameyasya dharmaprādānyāt arthasyāprādhānyāt dharmaśāstraṃ balavad ity abhiprāyaḥ | ataś ca dharmaśāstrārthaśāstrayor virodhe arthaśāstrasya bādha eva, na viṣayavyavasthā nāpi vikalpa iti |
tad ayuktam – anayor ekaviṣayatvasaṃbhavena [154] virodhābhāvān na balābalacintāvatarati | tathā hi –
śastraṃ dvijātibhir grāhyaṃ dharmo yatroparudhyate |
ity upakramya –
ātmanā svaparitrāṇe dakṣiṇānāṃ ca saṅgare |
strīvittādivipattau ca ghnan dharmeṇa na dūṣyate ||
ity ātmano rakṣaṇe dakṣiṇādīnāṃ yajñopakaraṇānāṃ ca yuddhe ca strībrāhmaṇahiṃsāyāṃ ca ātatāyinam akūṭaśāstreṇa ghnan na daṇḍabhāg ity uktvā, tasya arthavādārtham idam ucyate – “guruṃ vā bālavṛddhau vā” ityādi | gurvādīn atyantāvadhyān apy ātatāyino hanyāt kim utānyān iti vāśabdagrahaṇāt “api vedāntapāragam” ity atrāpy apiśabdaśravaṇān na gurvādīnāṃ vadhyatvapratītiḥ | tathā ca sumantuḥ –
mahaty aparādhe ‘pi brāhmaṇasya śarīradaṇḍābhāvaḥ
ātatāyivadhe na doṣo ‘nyatra gobrāhmaṇebhyaḥ | iti |
yat tu bhavadevenoktam – ātatāyivadhe doṣo nety anvayaḥ, gośabdena goḥ śṛṅgasthānaṃ brāhmaṇaśabdena brāhmaṇarūpavedavākyam ity uktam, tat tu sakalasmṛtivirodhāt kliṣṭakalpanayā heyam | yat tv aparārkeṇoktam,
udyatāsiṃ ca viṣadaṃ śāpodyatakaraṃ tathā |
ity ātatāyilakṣaṇe udyatāsiśāpodyatakarapadadvayasāmarthyād vadhe vyāpriyamāṇā brāhmaṇā apy ātatāyino vadhā iti tatsakalavidvadasaṃmatatvād dheyam |
[155]        ācāryaṃ ca pravaktāraṃ pitaraṃ mātaraṃ gurum |
na hiṃsyād brāhmaṇaṃ gāṃ ca sarvāṃś caiva tapasvinaḥ ||
iti manuvacanāc ca brāhmaṇavadhe doṣaḥ smaryate | ataś cedaṃ vacanam “brāhmaṇo na hantavyaḥ” iti sāmānyaśāstreṇa niṣedhasya siddhatvād ātatāyināṃ hiṃsāpratiṣedhenārthavad iti |
nātatāyivadhe doṣo hantur bhavati kaścana |
iti vacanaṃ brāhmaṇavyatiriktaviṣayam eva | brāhmaṇasya kūṭasākṣye yathārhaṃ deśān niṣkāsanaṃ grāmān niṣkāsanaṃ nagnīkaraṇam aṅkanaṃ gṛhabhaṅgo ‘rthadaṇḍaś ca kāryāḥ | kadācid api brāhmaṇasya śārīradaṇḍo nāstīti sarvasmṛtisiddham ity avagantavyam ity alaṃ vistareṇa |
sākṣyanuyogaprakāraḥ
sākṣyānuyogaprakāram āha manuḥ |
devabrāhmaṇasāṃnidhye sākṣyaṃ pṛcchen nṛpo dvijān |
udaṅmukhān prāṅmukhān vā pūrvāhṇe vai śuciḥ sucīn ||
nāradas tu |
āhūya sākṣiṇaḥ pṛcchet niyamya śapathair bhṛśam |
samastān viditācārān vijñātārthān pṛthak pṛthak ||
bhayāvahaiḥ śapathaiḥ satyaniṣṭhān kṛtvā pratyekaṃ pṛcched ity arthaḥ | śapathās tenaiva darśitāḥ |
varṇabhedena satye vastubhedaḥ
satyena śāpayed vipraṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāhanāyudhaiḥ |
[156]        gobījakāñcanair vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ || iti |
ataś ca dvijān iti dvijagrahaṇaṃ sākṣimātropalakṣakam iti mantavyam, śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakair iti śūdrasya śāpavidhānāt | atra manunā viśeṣaḥ smṛtaḥ |
brūhīti brāhmaṇaṃ pṛcchet satyaṃ brūhīti pārthivam | iti |
vaiśyapraśnaṃ viśeṣānukteḥ gobījakāñcanair viyogaḥ syād yady anyathā brūyād iti | śūdre tu praśnavākyaprakāro manunā prapañcitaḥ |
brahmaghnā ye smṛtāś caurā ye ca strībālaghātinaḥ |
mitradruhaḥ kṛtāghnāś ca te te syur vadato mṛṣā ||
janmaprabhṛti yat kiṃcit puṇyaṃ bhadra tvayā kṛtam |
tat te sarvaṃ śuno gacched yadi brūyās tvam anyathā ||
yamo vaivasvato devo yas tavaiṣa hṛdi sthitaḥ |
tena ced avivādas te mā gaṅgāṃ mā kurūn gamaḥ ||
nagno muṇḍaḥ kapālena bhikṣārthī kṣutpipāsitaḥ |
adhaḥ śatrugṛhaṃ gacchet yat sākṣyam anṛtaṃ vadet ||
pañca paśvanṛte hanti daśa hanti gavānṛte |
śatam aśvānṛte hanti sahasraṃ puruṣānṛte ||
hanti jātān ajātāṃś ca hiraṇyārthe ‘nṛtaṃ vadet |
sarvaṃ bhūmyanṛte hanti mā sma bhūmyanṛtaṃ vadeḥ ||
etān doṣān avekṣya tvaṃ sarvān anṛtabhāṣaṇe |
yathāśrutaṃ yathādṛṣṭaṃ satyam evāñjasā vadeḥ ||
kurūn kurukṣetram | añjasā śuddhena hṛdayena | jātān ajātān [157] iti svakula iti śeṣaḥ | ayaṃ ca śūdrapraśne vidhir anāpadi hīnavṛttyupajīvināṃ dvijānām api bhavati, teṣām alpaśapathena nityantum aśakyatvāt | ata eva manuḥ |
ye ‘nupātāḥ svakarmabhyaḥ parapiṇḍopajīvinaḥ |
dvijatvam abhikāṅkṣante tāṃś ca śūdravad ācaret ||
sākṣye iti śeṣaḥ | tatra kātyāyanaḥ |
sabhānataḥsthais tu vaktavyaṃ sākṣyaṃ nānyatra sākṣibhiḥ |
sarvasākṣyeṣv ayaṃ dharmo ‘nyatra syāt sthāvareṣu tu ||
anyatra sthāvare ‘pīty arthaḥ |
vadhe cet prāṇināṃ sākṣyaṃ vādayec chavasaṃnidhau |
tadabhāve tu cihnasya nānyathaiva pravādayet ||
cihnasya śṛṅgādeḥ |
sākṣyavādaprakāraḥ
sākṣyavādaprakāram āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
vihāyopānaduṣṇīṣaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ bāhum uddharet |
hiraṇyagośakṛddarbhān samādāya ṛtaṃ vadet ||
dakṣiṇaṃ pāṇim uddharet prāvṛtaṃ vastraṃ yajñopavītavat kuryād ity arthaḥ | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “dakṣiṇaṃ bāhum uddharate ‘vadhatte savyam iti yajñopavītam” iti | tathā ca vasiṣṭhaḥ:
prāṅmukho yaḥ sthitaḥ sākṣī śapathaiḥ śāpitaḥ svakaiḥ |
hiraṇyagośakṛddarbhān upaspṛśya vaded ṛtam || [158] iti |
ṛtaṃ satyam | svakān śapathān āha manuḥ |
satyena pūyate sākṣī dharmaḥ satyena vardhate |
tasmāt satyaṃ hi vaktavyaṃ sarvavarṇeṣu sākṣibhiḥ || iti |
vadan sākṣy anṛtaṃ pāśair badhyate vāruṇair naraḥ |
vivaśaṃ śatam ājāti tasmāt sākṣī vaded ṛtam ||
ātmaiva hy ātmanaḥ sākṣī gatir ātmā tathātmanaḥ |
māvamaṃsthās tvam ātmānaṃ nṛṇāṃ sākṣiṇam uttamam ||
śatam ājāti śatajanmaparyantam ity arthaḥ | vivaśam iti kriyāviśeṣaṇam | nārado ‘pi |
kuberādityavaruṇaśakravaivasvatādayaḥ |
paśyanti lokapālāś ca nityaṃ divyena cakṣuṣā ||
bṛhaspatir api |
kūṭasabhyaḥ kūṭasākṣī brahmahā ca samāḥ smṛtāḥ |
bhrūṇahā vittahā caiṣāṃ nādhikaḥ samudāhṛtaḥ || iti |
vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
atha ced anṛtaṃ brūyāt sarvato ‘medhyabhakṣaṇam |
mṛtau narakam āyāti tiryag gacched anantaram ||
amedhyabhakṣaṇayutaṃ narakaṃ mṛto gacchatīty arthaḥ \
kūṭasākṣye doṣāḥ
vyāso ‘pi |
pīḍyante vāruṇaiḥ pāśaiḥ sākṣiṇo ‘nṛtavādinaḥ |
ṣaṣṭir varṣasahasrāṇi narake vasatir druvam ||
[159]        teṣāṃ varṣaśate pūrṇe pāśa ekaḥ pramucyate |
kāle ‘tīte muktapāśaḥ tiryagyoniṣu jāyate ||
kūṭasākṣyaṃ prakṛtya vasiṣṭhaḥ |
sūkaro daśavarṣāṇi daśavarṣāṇi gārdabhaḥ |
śvā caiva daśavarṣāṇi bhāso varṣāṇi viṃśatim ||
kṛmikīṭapataṅgeṣu catvāriṃśat tathaiva ca |
mṛgas tu daśavarṣāṇi jāyate mānavas tataḥ ||
mānuṣyaṃ tu yadāpnoti mūko ‘ndhas tu bhavet tu saḥ |
dāridryaṃ tu bhavet tasya punarjanmani janmani ||
bubhukṣitaḥ śatrugṛhe bhikṣate bhārayā saha |
jñātvā tv anṛtato doṣān jñātvā satye ca sadguṇān ||
śreyaskaram ihāmitre satyaṃ sākṣī vaded ataḥ ||
atrāpśabdo ‘dhyāhartavyaḥ | amitre ‘pi sākṣye satyaṃ vaded ity arthaḥ | evaṃ saṃśodhanam etair eva vacanaiḥ kāryam |
kvacitsākṣye pratyavāyābhāvaḥ
kvacid viṣaye ‘pavādam āha manuḥ |
śūdraviṭkṣatraviprāṇāṃ yatrartokau bhaved vadhaḥ |
tatra vaktavyam anṛtaṃ tad dhi satyād viśiṣyate || iti |
tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
varṇināṃ hi vadho yatra vadet sākṣyaṃ tathānṛtam | iti |
sākṣyabhāve ‘py agnidatvādivivādeṣūlkāhastādicihnāny eva sākṣikāryaṃ kurvantīty āha
nāradaḥ |
ulkāhasto ‘gnido jñeyaḥ śastrapāṇiś ca ghātakaḥ |
keśakeśigṛhītaś ca yugapat pāradārikaḥ ||
[160]        kuddālapāṇir vijñeyaḥ setucchettā samīpagaḥ |
tathā kuṭhārapāṇiś ca vanacchettā prakīrtitaḥ ||
pratyakṣacihnair vijñeyo daṇḍapāruṣyakṛn naraḥ |
asākṣipratyayā hy ete pāruṣye tu parīkṣaṇam || iti |
hārītas tu |
kaścit kṛtvātmanaś cihnaṃ dveṣāt param upadravet |
yuktihetvarthasambandhais tatra yuktaṃ parīkṣaṇam ||
tathā ca nāradaḥ |
yāty acoro ‘pi coratvaṃ coraś cāyāty acoratām |
acoraś coratāṃ prāpto māṇḍavyo vyavahārataḥ ||
ataḥ parīkṣaṇam āvaśyakam ity abhiprāyaḥ | ayam abhisandhiḥ – atrākrośābhāvaviśiṣṭā eva likhitādayaḥ pramāṇapadavīm avagāhante, nānyathā | ata evāha viṣṇuḥ: “āsiddham anāsiddham eva” iti | etad vyācaṣṭe bhāruciḥ: “āsiddhaṃ sarvalikhitaṃ bhuktiprabhṛti avaruddhaṃ āsamantāt siddhaṃ na bhavati kadāpi siddhaṃ na bhavatīty arthaḥ” | yat tu nāradenoktam,
vaktavye ‘rthe na tiṣthantam utkrāmantaṃ ca tad vacaḥ |
āsedhayed vivādārthī yāvad āhvānadarśanam || iti |
etad vyākhyātam candrikākāreṇa: (p. 33) “saṃdighārthanirṇaya udāsīnaṃ tannirṇayāya pravartitavyam ityādi cārthino vacanam [161] avamanyamānaṃ pratyarthinaṃ svayaṃ cārthī āsedhayet | rājājñayā vyavahāradarśanaparyantaṃ nirodhayet” iti svarūpāsedhanam uktam | tat tu pratyarthigṛhītapratyarpaṇiyadravyadvārā sa evāsedhata iti svarūpāsedhamātraparaṃ veditavyam | bhārucis tu –
vaktavye ‘rthe na tiṣṭhantam utkrāmantaṃ ca tad vacaḥ |
āsedhayed vivādārthī vādī tatprativādinam ||
“siṣādhayiṣitārthaviṣaye āsedhayet – tadgṛhītaṃ svaṃ siṣādhayiṣitārthaṃ rājājñayāvarodhayet | tadabhāve tadgrahītāraṃ tannāśakaṃ vāvarodhayet | iti mukhyavṛttyā dravyāsedhaparam idaṃ vacanam” ity āha | ato bhārucivyākhyānam eva samyak, viṣṇuvacanānurodhitvāt |
nanv evam ayuktaṃ viṣṇuvacanānurodhābhāvāt | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “āseddhuḥ phalam evāsedhya” iti |
maivam | bhavatā vaiṣṇavavacanatātparyāparijñānāt | tattātparyaṃ tu likhitāsedhāt phalāsedhanaṃ balavat, sarveṣāṃ phalārtham eva pravṛtteḥ | phalāsedhākaraṇe tatpūrvakṛtā likhitādyāsedhā aprayojakā eveti tatprābalyajñāpanārthatvāt tadvacanasya iti | tathā ca gautamasūtram: “phalāsedhaḥ kauttikavat” iti |
tasyārtho vivṛto nibandhakāreṇa – atra dvitīyārthe vatiḥ kauttikaḥ – kuttājīvakaḥ, yathā āsedhaviṣayaḥ | evaṃ sarvatra phalam evāsedhaviṣaya iti | yathā kauttikaḥ kuttākāreṇa gṛhītapāribhāṣikārthātiriktopacitadravyasvīkāradaśāyām āsedhyaḥ | [162] evaṃ sarveṣu vyavahāreṣu phalasvīkāradaśāyām āsedhaḥ kartavya iti | phalasvīkāradaśāyāṃ phalam evāsedhyaṃ na svarūpam iti tātparyam | kauttikagrahaṇaṃ tu kuttāviṣaye itaravyavahāravat pūrvakālāsedhā na santi | kiṃ tu kuttāyāṃ phalam evāsedhaviṣaya iti tāvanmātraviśeṣajñāpanārtham ity avagantavyam |
āsedhākrośabalābalam
nanv āsedho nāma phalasyāvarodhaḥ | madhyasthajanagocare vivādāspadībhūtadravyanidhāpanam iti yāvat | phalānutpattau tatsvīkārodyogoparodhaḥ | phalanāśakaranodyoge svarūpāsedho rājājñayā nirodhanam – āsedhaḥ | ākrośas tu tādṛśo na bhavati | kiṃ tu – phalaṃ na bhoktavyam | patraṃ na lekhanīyam | sākṣibhir na bhavitavyam | mayā sārdhaṃ nyāyanirṇayāyāgantavyam | anyathā rājājñām ullaṅghitvān ityādyākārakavāgvyāpārarūpo na kāyikavyavahārarūpaḥ | ataś cāsedhād ākrośo durbala eva | tata āseṣedha eva vyavahārabījaṃ nākrośa iti cet,
maivam, ākrośāsedhayoḥ viṣayabhedo na svarūpabhedaḥ | durbalenārthinā kṛtoparodha ākrośapadālabhipyaḥ (-dābhilapyaḥ?) | balavatā tu kṛta āsedhapadābhidheya iti | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “durbalaprabalakṛtav (āv?) ākrośāsedhau” iti |
ayam arthaḥ – “durbalasya balaṃ rājā” iti rājapuruṣair durbalenāpi kārita āsedha ākrośamūlo ‘pi prabalakṛtatvād āsedha eveti | durbalenaivākrośa iti lakṣaṇaṃ suṣṭu, paratrāsedhā ākrośasyeyān viśeṣaḥ – ākrośaḥ kālāntare dharmanirṇayānantaraṃ phalagatam api svatvam ākarṣayati | [163] āsedhas tv āsedhasamay eva phalam apy uparuṇaddhīti | atrāhuḥ – balavatā āsedha eva kartavyo nākrośa iti bhārucyādayaḥ | atrākrośakartuḥ nirūpaṇaṃ sphuṭatvān na kṛtam, ataś cāsedhasadbhāve tu tadabhāvaviśiṣṭatvarūpadharmopamardanadvārā viśiṣṭaṃ kṛtsnaṃ pramāṇam upamṛdnāty ākrośa iti |
kuttālekhabalavicāraḥ
nanu likhitādīnāṃ prāmāṇyam āgamanirūpaṇadvārā, āgamābhāve tatprāmāṇyaṃ praśithilamūlatayā dṛḍhaṃ na bhavati, āgamās tu keṣāṃcin mate sapta | tathā ca smṛtiḥ: “sapta vittāgamā dharmyāḥ” iti | gautamādīnāṃ (GDh 10.39) mate tu rikthakrayasaṃvibhāgaparigrahādhigamapratigrahanirveśādayo ‘ṣṭa vittāgamā iti | bhāradvājamate tu parivṛtter api vittāgamarūpatayā  nigaditatvāt tayā sārdhaṃ nava vittāgamāḥ | eteṣāṃ madhye kuttākārākāritaparibhāṣitārthasyāparigaṇanād āgamaprakāranirūpaṇāśaktau kuttālekhasya tadārūḍhasākṣiṇāṃ ca daurbalyaṃ prāptam |
anāgamena yo bhu(ṅkte?) bahūny abdaśatāny api |
coradaṇḍena taṃ pāpaṃ daṇḍayet pṛthivīpatiḥ ||
iti smṛteḥ | āgamaśūnyāyā bhukteḥ pramāṇakoṭiniveśābhāvaḥ pratīyate | yat tu bhogāgamayoḥ sāmyaṃ bṛhaspativacanāt pratīyate, tat tu kevalabhukteḥ daurbalyajñāpanaparatayā na viruddham | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhuktyā kevalayā naiva bhūmisiddhim avāpnuyāt |
āgamenāpi śuddhena dvābhyāṃ siddhim avāpnuyāt || [164]
tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
āgamo dīrghakālaś ca vicchedoparavojjhitaḥ |
pratyarthisaṃnidhānaṃ ca pañcāṅgo bhoga iṣyate || iti |
atra “pañcāṅgo bhoga iṣyate” iti vadann ekāṅgavaikalye ‘py aprāmāṇyaṃ bhogasyeti darśayatīty uktaṃ prāk, ataś cāgamanirūpaṇe tadabhāvapratīteḥ mūlaśuddhyabhāvāt tadgamakapramāṇadārḍhyābhāva iti atrocyate – kvacit paribhāṣāpi vittāgama eva yathā –
ādhiḥ praṇaśyed dviguṇe dhane yadi na mokṣyate |
ity atra dhanadvaiguṇyanibandhanaparibhāṣā vittāgamamadhye ‘parigaṇitāpi sopādhikaṃ svatvam avagamayatīti vijñāneśvaraḥ | candrikākāras tu – dhanadvaiguṇyaparibhāṣā parivṛttirūpeṇa tilavrīhivinimayavat svatvāpadikety āha | vijñāneśvaramatāvalambanena paribhāṣāyā vācikadānāntargatatayā svatvāpādakatvam asti | aupādhikasthake ‘py upādhinivṛttau svatvasya dṛḍhatvāt | tathā hi – kṣetrād etasmād etāvad dhanam asmabhyaṃ dīyatām avaśiṣṭaṃ gṛhyatām | yad vā – etāvad dravyaṃ gṛhītvā tad ṛṇaṃ saṃśodhyatām avaśiṣṭaṃ gṛhyatāṃ iti paribhāṣāyām avaśiṣṭadravye svatvanivṛttiḥ parasvatvāpattiparyantā bhavaty eveti dṛśyate | atha vā bhārucimatāvalambanena kuttākārakāritāparibhāṣāvaṣṭambhitamānasikadānāntatayā svatvāpādakatvaṃ paribhāṣāyāḥ samasti |
nanu saṃkalpamātrāt svatvaṃ notpadyate kiṃ tu saṃkalpāt svatvam apaiti | svatvotpattis tv ārjanād eveti dhanārjananayasiddhatvāt tadvyākopo mānasikadānāntatayā paribhāṣāyāḥ svatvahetutvoktāv iti cet,
maivam, yadi dhanārjananayaḥ [165] svatvāpāyahetumānasikakriyaiveti syāt tadāyaṃ doṣaḥ prāduṣyāt, na ca tathā, svatvāpāyahetuḥ kvacit saṃkalpaḥ kvacin mahāpātakādir ity uktam | ataś ca tasyāpy adhikaraṇasya laukikavyavahārānurodhitayā svatvasyāpi laukikatvāc ca | yathā loke dṛṣṭaṃ tathā svīkartavyam | kuttādivyavahāre mānasikī kriyaiva svatvahetur iti saṃmataṃ laukikānām | tathā ca dṛśyate – bahuśo bahavaḥ kāyasthāḥ kṣetrādikaṃ deśagrāmādikaṃ vā kuttākāreṇa gṛhṇantaḥ pākṣikāpacayabhārasahiṣṇavo dṛśyante | tatra grāmādikṣetrādideśādyupacitapāribhāṣikadravye teṣāṃ vyavahārāṇāṃ svāmyaṃ vidyata eva | sa cāgamaḥ sādhīyān eva |
nanu kuttākāreṇa gṛhītadravya upacayo vidyate cet tam upacayaṃ kuttāpradātāra uttamapadābhilapyāḥ svīkurvanti paridṛśyante ca,
maivam, yadi kauttikas tv apacayabhārasahiṣṇur na bhavet tadāpy uttamasyāpy upacayāpāyasahiṣṇutvaṃ na syāt | kauttikatvaprasiddhir yāma(dugdha)viruddhabhogapradānāny athākaraṇamūlā | ata evoktaṃ viṣṇunā: “kauttiko ‘pacayabhārasahiṣṇur uttamas tūpacayāpāyasahiṣṇuḥ” iti |
atra bhāruciḥ: “kuttopajīvī – kauttikaḥ | kuttāpradātottamaḥ | kuttā nāma – gṛhakṣetrārāmagrāmadeśādipadārthasamṛddhaphalaprāptyarthaṃ yasmai kasmai cid vyavahāriṇe tadgṛhādipadārthajātasaṃdānam” iti |
saṃdānaṃ nāma – samyagdānaṃ na bhavati, aupādhikadānarūpatvāt kuttāyāḥ, api tu saṃdānam – bandhanam | uttamasyāpy upacayānaṅgīkāreṇa kauttika eva bandhanarūpatvāt saṃdānam | kuttety upacaryate | sadānaṃ nāma – tyāgamātram iti kecit, atrāyaṃ viśeṣaḥ – yady anayoḥ kauttikottamayor [166] vidyate samayaḥ “upacayaś cen mayottamena soḍhavyaḥ apacayaś cet tvayā kauttikena soḍhavyaḥ” iti, tadā saṃvidanusaraṇe upacaye vidyamāne ‘pi viṣṇuvacanānusāreṇa pādamātropacaya eva svāmyaṃ kauttikasya |
tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “pādamātropacaye kauttikasya svāmyam” iti | atra bhāruciḥ: “so ‘yam upacayaḥ kuttātaḥ pādamātraṃ cet tasminn evopacaye uttamo manasā imam upacayaṃ kauttiko gṛhṇīta, anyadāyanāśayoḥ svāmyadhīnatvaṃ na tu kauttikasya | pādādhikaya eva svāmyāt tasyti smarati | tadā tasmin pādamātropacitadravye uttamasya svatvanivṛttiḥ parasvatvāpattiparyantā bhavatīti tātparyam” iti |
ayam āśayaḥ – mānasikam api dānaṃ tyāgātmakaṃ svatvāpādakam iti vaiṣṇavaṃ matam iti | uttamo manasāyaṃ kauttika imam upacayaṃ gṛhṇātv iti vadatā bhārucinā yāvaty upacaye dravye uttamasya mānasikatyāgaḥ tāvaty api svatvaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ pādam iti matam, tato nyūnena na bhavitavyam iti |
atra kecid āhuḥ – anenaiva nyāyena kuttāyāḥ apacaye vidyamāne ‘pi pādamātram uttamena soḍhavyam | tato nyūnaṃ dūrata eva, tatrādhikam aicchikam iti viṣṇuvacanasya tātparyārtha iti |
iti mānuṣapramāṇanirṇayaḥ
atha divyapramāṇanirūpaṇam
tatra nāradaḥ |
yathā vidhānena sadā pañca divyāni sarvataḥ |
dadad rājābhiyuktāya pretya svarge ‘bhinandati || [167]
pañca divyāni – tulāgnir āpo viṣaṃ kośa iti | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
tuyāgnyāpo viṣaṃ kośo divyān āhur viśuddhaye | iti |
anena abhiyokṝṇām etāni divyāni bhavatīty arthād uktaṃ bhavati | yathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
na kaścid abhiyoktāraṃ divyeṣu viniyojayet |
abhiyuktāya dātavyaṃ divyaṃ divyaviśāradaiḥ || iti |
kaścit – sabhāpatyādiḥ | divyaviśāradāḥ – prāḍvivākādayaḥ | anenārthipratyarthnor anyonyecchayā divyāṅgīkāreṇāyaṃ niyama ity arthād uktaṃ bhavati | uktaṃ ca yājñavalkyena |
rucyā vānyataraḥ kuryād itaro vartayec chiraḥ | iti |
śiro nāma vyavahārasya caturthaḥ pādo jayaparājayalakxaṇaḥ | tena ca daṇḍo lakṣyate | rucyā – abhiyoktrabhiyuktayoḥ parasparānumatyā | anyataraḥ – abhiyoktā abhiyukto vā divyaṃ kuryāt | itaraḥ abhiyuktaḥ abhiyukto vā | śiraḥ – āśrīram arthadaṇaṃ vā | vartayet – aṅgīkuryāt | pañca divyānīti mahābhiyogaviṣayam | yad āha yājñavalkyaḥ: “mahābhiyogeṣv etāni” iti | etāni mahābhoyogeṣv eva nānyatreti niyamyate |
dhaṭo ‘gnir udakaṃ caiva viṣaṃ kośas tathaiva ca |
taṇḍulāś caiva divyāni saptamaṃ taptamāṣakam || iti |
idam api phāladharmayor upalakṣaṇam | yathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
dhaṭo ‘gnir udakaṃ caiva viṣaṃ kośaś ca pañcamaḥ |
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu taṇḍulāḥ proktaṃ saptamaṃ taptamāṣakam ||
[168]        aṣṭamaṃ phālam ity uktaṃ navamaṃ dharmajaṃ smṛtam |
divyāny etāni sarvāṇi nirdiṣṭāni svayaṃbhuvā || iti |
nanv alpābhiyoge ‘pi kośo ‘sty eva, “kośam alpe ‘pi dāpayet” iti smaraṇāt,
satyam, kośasya tulādiṣu pāṭho na mahābhiyogeṣv eveti niyamārthaḥ, kiṃ tu sāvaṣṭambhābhiyogeṣu prāptyarthaḥ, anyathā śaṅkābhiyoga eva syāt ||
avaṣṭambhābhiyuktānāṃ dhaṭādīni vinirdiśet |
taṇḍulāś caiva kośaś ca śaṅkāsv eva na saṃśayaḥ ||
iti smaraṇāt | śaṅkāsv iti viśvāsādīnām upalakṣaṇārtham | yathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
śaṅkāviśvāsasaṃdhāne vibhāge rikthināṃ tathā |
kriyāsamūhakartṛtve kośam eva pradāpayet || iti |
atra kośaḥ śirorahitaḥ |
śiraḥsthāyivihīnāni divyāni parivarjayet |
dhaṭādīni viṣāntāni kośa eko ‘śrāḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
śaṅkābhiyogādiviṣaya ity śeṣaḥ |
mahātatvābhiyoge tu kośo ‘pi siraḥsthāyivihīno varjyaḥ “śīrṣakasthe ‘bhiyoktari” iti pañcadivyaśeṣatayā yājñavalkye noktatvāt | mahābhigoyeṣv api rājaprāḍvivākasabhāvyatiriktasabhāyāṃ dhaṭādidivyapañcake na śiraḥkalpanam asti |
vāgdaṇḍo dhigdamaś caiva viprāyattāv ubhau smṛtau |
arthadaṇḍavadhāv uktau rājāyattāv ubhāv api ||
rājaprāḍvivākasabhāyām eva daṇḍasya vyavahāraśiraḥsthānīyasyoktatvāt | tataś ca –
[169]        vāgdaṇḍo dhigdamaś caiva viprāyattāv ubhau smṛtau |
arthadaṇḍavadhāv uktau rājāyattāv ubhāv api ||
rājaprāḍvivākasabhāyām eva daṇḍyasya vyavahāraśiraḥsthānaniyamasyoktatvāt | tataś ca –
śiraḥsthāyivihīnāni divyāni parivarjayet |
ityādivacanaṃ rājaprāḍvivākādisabhāyām eveti mantavyam | dharmajaṃ tu divyam alpaviṣayam eva | tasya prāyaścittādau śaṅkābhiyoge yuktatvāt | yathāha pitāmahaḥ |
hantṝṇāṃ yācamānānāṃ prāyaścittārthināṃ nṛṇām |
saṃdigdheṣv abhiśastānāṃ dharmādharmyaparīkṣaṇam ||
etāni ca divyāni śapathāś ca yathāsaṃbhavam ṛṇādhānādiṣu vivādeṣu prayoktavyāni |
sthāvaraviṣaye likhitasyābhāve divyāvasaraḥ
yat pitāmahavacanam,
sthāvareṣu vivādeṣu divyāni parivarjayet | iti |
tadā likhitasādhyādisadbhāve divyāni parivarjayed iti vyākhyeyam |
nanu vivādāntareṣv api pramāṇāntarasaṃbhave divyānām anavatāra eva,
satyam, ṛṇādānādiṣu vivādeṣūktalakṣaṇasākṣyupanyāse arthinā kṛte ‘pi pratyarthī yadi daṇḍābhyupagamāvaṣṭambhena divyam avalambate tadā divyam api bhavati, sākṣiṇām atiśayadoṣasaṃbhavāt | divyasya nirdoṣatvena vastutaḥ sarvaviṣayatvāt | [170]
yathāha nāradaḥ |
tatra satye sthiro dharmo vyavahāraś ca sākṣiṇi |
devasādhye pauruṣeyīṃ na lekhyaṃ vā prayojayet || iti |
sthāvareṣu tu pratyarthinā daṇḍāvaṣṭambhanena divyālambane kṛte ‘pi sāmantādiduṣṭapramāṇasaṃbhave divyaṃ na grāhyam ity evam artham,
sthāvareṣu vivādeṣu divyāni parivarjayet |
iti pitāmahavacanam, nātyantikadivyanirākaraṇārtham, likhitasāmantādyabhāve sthāvaravivādeṣv anirṇayaprasaṅgāt | yat tu,
lekhyaṃ yatra na vidyeta na bhuktir na ca sākṣiṇaḥ |
na ca divyāvatāro ‘sti pramāṇaṃ tatra pārthivaḥ || iti |
divyāvatārābhāvasya sthāvaravivādasyaiva viṣayatvāt | tatra nirṇāyakaṃ rājājñeti sthāvaravivādeṣv anirṇayaprasaṅgād ity uktir ayukteti na vācyam | etac candrikākāra-vijñāniyogimatabhedena vyavasthā pūrvam evokteti nātra prapañcyate | divyānāṃ kālaviśeṣam āha –
divyānāṃ kālaviśeṣaḥ
pitāmahaḥ |
pūrvāhṇe ‘hni parīkṣā syāt pūrvāhṇe ca dhaṭo bhavet |
madhyāhne tu jale deyaṃ dharmatattvam abhīpsatā ||
divasasya tu pūrvāhṇe kośaśuddhir vidhīyate |
rātrau tu paścime yāme viṣaṃ deyaṃ saśītalam || iti |
anuktavelāviśeṣāṇāṃ taṇḍulataptamāṣaprabhṛtīnāṃ pūrvāhṇa eva pradhānaṃ parikīrtitam iti sāmānyanāradasmaraṇāt | tathā ca hārītena viśeṣo darśitaḥ |
āgneḥ śiśirahemantau varṣāś caiva prakīrtitāḥ |
[171]        śaradgrīṣme tu salilaṃ hemantaśiśire viṣam ||
caitro mārgaśiraś caiva vaiśākhaś ca tathaiva ca |
ete sādhāraṇā māsā divyānām avirodhinaḥ ||
kośas tu sarvadā deyaḥ tulā syāt sārvakālikī ||
divyapratiṣedhakālaḥ
kośagrahaṇaṃ sarvaśapathānām upalakṣaṇam | taṇḍulādīnāṃ punar viśeṣeṇābhidhānāt sārvakālikatvam | keṣāṃcit kālānāṃ pratiṣedhaḥ |
na śītatoye śuddhiḥ syān noṣṇakāle ‘gniśodhanam |
na prāvṛṣi viṣaṃ dadyāt pravāte na tulāṃ tathā ||
nāparāhṇe na saṃdhyāyāṃ na madhyāhne kadācana |
śītaḥ – śītakālaḥ | atha vivādinor jātyādito divyavyavasthām āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
ṛṇādikeṣu kāryeṣu saṃvivāde parasparam |
dravyasaṃkhyā(dinā)nvitā deyā puruṣāpekṣyā tathā ||
puruṣāpekṣayā – vādinor jātyāpekṣayety arthaḥ |
varṇabhedena divyam
yathāha sa eva |
brāhmaṇasya dhaṭo deyaḥ kṣatriyasya hutāśanaḥ |
vaiśyasya salilaṃ deyaṃ śūdrasya viṣam eva tu ||
sādhāraṇaḥ samastānāṃ kośaḥ prokto manīṣibhiḥ | iti |
anityā ceyaṃ vyavasthā | yathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
sarveṣu sarvadivyaṃ vā viṣavarjaṃ dvijottame || [172] iti |
atra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
tulā strībālavṛddhāndhapaṅgubrāhmaṇarogiṇām |
agnir jalaṃ vā śūdrasya yavāḥ sapta viṣasya vā ||
strī iti strīmātram, jātivayo’vasthāviśeṣānādareṇa “bāla ā ṣoḍaśād varṣāt” | tathā vṛddhaḥ – āśītikāparaḥ | etac ca savratānām atyantārtānāṃ stryādīnāṃ veditavyam | yathā pitāmahaḥ |
savratānāṃ bhṛśārtānāṃ yācitānāṃ tapasvinām |
strīṇāṃ ca na bhaved divyaṃ yadi dharmān apekṣate || iti |
strīṇāṃ tu na viṣaṃ proktaṃ na cāpi salilaṃ smṛtam |
dhaṭakośādibhis tāsām antas tatvaṃ vicārayet ||
iti viṣasalilavyatiriktadhaṭakośādibhiḥ śuddhividhānāt | strīṇām iti bālānām upalakṣaṇam | savratānām ityādisāmānyenābhidhānāt | brāhmaṇādīnām api na sārvakālikas tulādiniyamaḥ |
sarvavarṇānāṃ kośāc chuddhiḥ
sarveṣām eva varṇānāṃ kośāc chuddhir vidhīyate |
sarvāṇy etāni sarveṣāṃ brāhmaṇasya viṣaṃ vinā || iti |
tad ayam atra niṣkarṣaḥ – varṣāsv agnir eva sarveṣām, hemantaśiśirayos tu kṣatriyāditrayāṇām agniviṣayor vikalpaḥ, brāhmaṇasya tv agnir eva na kadācid viṣam, grīṣmaśaradayos tu salilam eva, sādhāraṇe kāle bahudivyasamavāye tulādiniyama iti |
[173]
puruṣaviśeṣe divyaviśeṣaniṣedhaḥ
lohakārādīnāṃ tu viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
na lohaśilpinām agniḥ salilaṃ nāmbusevinām |
mantrayogavidāṃ caiva viṣaṃ dadyāc ca na kvacit ||
taṇḍulair na niyuñjīta vraṇinaṃ mukharogiṇam |
kuṣṭhināṃ varjayed agniṃ salilaṃ śvāsakāsinām ||
pittaśleṣmavatāṃ nityaṃ viṣaṃ tu parivarjayet |
madyapastrīvyasanināṃ kitavānāṃ tathaiva ca ||
kośaḥ prājñair na dātavyo ye ca nāstikavṛttayaḥ | iti |
nārado ‘pi |
mahāparādhe nirdharme kṛtaghne śīlakutsite |
nāstike dṛṣṭadoṣe ca kośadāne vivarjayet ||
evaṃ ca klībāder agniḥ kośaś ca varjyaḥ | yathāha nāradaḥ |
klībāturān satyahīnān paritaś cārditān narān |
strībālavṛddhāṃś cāndhāṃś ca parīkṣeta dhaṭe sadā ||
nityā ceyaṃ vyavasthā | sadety abhidhānāt | atra viśeṣam āha sa eva |
yeṣu vādeṣu divyāni pratiṣiddhāni yatnataḥ |
kārayet svajanais tāni nābhiśastaṃ tyajen manuḥ ||
na tyajet uktaprakāreṇāviśodyeti śeṣaḥ |
dravyasaṃkhyāviśeṣeṇa divyaviśeṣah
kātyāyanas tu viśeṣam āha |
sarvadravyapramāṇaṃ tu jñātvā divyena yojayet |
[174]        jñātvā saṃkhyāṃ suvarṇānāṃ śatanāśe viṣaṃ smṛtam ||
atra suvarṇānāṃ saṃkhyāṃ jñātvā apahṛtadravyasya suvarṇātmakatayā saṃkhyāsaṃkhyetatām kṛtvety arthaḥ |
aśītes tu vināśe vai dadyād eva hutāśanam |
ṣaṣṭyā nāśe jalaṃ deyaṃ catvāriṃśati(ke) vai dhaṭam ||
viṃśaddaśavināśe tu kośadānaṃ vidhīyate |
pañcādhikasya vā nāśe tadardhārdhasya taṇḍulāḥ ||
tadardhārdhasya nāśe tu putradāraśirāṃśi ca |
tadardhārdhaya nāśe tu laukikyaś ca kriyāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
atra sarvatra vināśaśabdena apahnavo lakṣyate | tasyaiva pramāṇaprastāvahetutvāt | caśabdena smārtaśapathāḥ samuccitāḥ | yathāha hārītaḥ |
niṣke tu satyavacanaṃ dviniṣke pādalambhanam |
ūnatrike tu puṣpaṃ syāt kośadānam ataḥ param ||
atra niṣkaśabdo laukikaniṣkavacana iti candrikākāraḥ, tathaivātra vyavahārād iti | atra viśeṣam āha viṣnuḥ: “yathārhaṃ samayakriyā | rājadrohasāhaseṣu yathākāmam | nikṣepasteyeṣv arthaparimāṇāt sarveṣu arthajāteṣu mūlyaṃ kanakaṃ parikalpayet | tatra kṛṣṇalone dūrvākaraṃ śāpayet | dvikṛṣṇalone tu tilakaram | trikṛṣṇalone rajatakaram | catuṣkṛṣṇalone suvarṇakaram | pañcakṛṣṇalone sīroddhṛtamahīkaram | suvarṇārdhone kośo deyaḥ śūdrasya | tataḥ paraṃ yathārhaṃ dhaṭāgnyambuviṣāṇām anyatamam, dviguṇārdhe yathāhitā samayakriyā vaiśyasya triguṇārthe [175] rājanyasya | caturguṇārthe brāhmaṇasya | prāgdṛṣṭadoṣabrāhmaṇaṃ divyānām anyatamam eva svalpe ‘py arthe ‘pi dāpayet (kārayet?) | suviditasaccaritraṃ na mahaty arthe ‘pi” iti | etad viṣṇuvacanaṃ kṛtsnaśaḥ sarvasmṛtyanurodhena vyākhyāsyate | samayakriyā – laukikakriyā | sīroddhṛtamahīkaram – lāṅgaloddhṛtaloṣṭakaram ity arthaḥ | atra ūnagrahaṇam adhike śāpanivṛttyartham | atra viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
eṣā saṃkhyā nikṛṣṭānāṃ madhyānāṃ dviguṇā smṛtā |
caturguṇottamānāṃ tu sarvavarṇeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti |
eṣāṃ saṃkhyākṛṣṇalaṃ tu vyāvahārikaniṣkasya viṃśatitamo bhāgaḥ, matāntare tu pañcakṛṣṇalāḥ palam, tatra laukikavyavahāre mānacatuṣtayaṃ kṛṣṇalam ity ucyate, taṇḍulaparimitamānam iti prasiddham | mānava-yājñavalkyayoḥ śāstrayoḥ kākaṇikadvayanyūnamānasaptakaṃ kṛṣṇalam ity ucyate | evaṃ trividheṣu kṛṣṇaleṣū satsu vaiṣṇavadharmaśāstroktakṛṣṇalam evāśrayitum ucitam iti | kākiṇikatrayaṃ nyūnamānaṣaṭkaṃ kṛṣṇalam evātra svata āśriyate, mānavakṛṣṇalaṃ tu kartṛguṇādibhir āśriyate | tatra yāvatāpahṛtadravyeṇa kṛṣṇalapūrtiḥ | tatkṛṣṇalāpahāre upoṣitasḥ snāta ārdrapaṭaḥ śūdro vedabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhau dūrvākaraḥ śapathaṃ kuryāt | evaṃbhūtaṃ dvitīyakṛṣṇalād ārabhya ā tṛtīyakṛṣṇalāc chūdraḥ tilakaraḥ śapathaṃ kuryāt tṛtīyakṛṣṇalād ārabhya ā caturthād rajatapāṇiḥ śapathaṃ kuryāt | kṛṣṇalacatuṣṭayād ārabhyāpañcamāc chūdro hiraṇyapāṇiḥ śapathaṃ kuryāt | kṛṣṇalapañcakād ārabhyāṣṭāc chūdro lāṅgaloddhṛtaloṣṭakaraḥ śapathaṃ kuryāt | [176] ṣaṣṭakṛṣṇalād ārabhya daśamānānāṃ daśakād arvāk śūdro nikṛṣṭaś cet putreṇa bhāryayā vā śapathaṃ kuryāt | sacchūdraś ced asminn evārtha sarvaiḥ pātakaiḥ śapathaṃ kuryāt | yady apy atra,
śūdraṃ sarvais tu pātakaiḥ |
putradārān avāpyainaṃ śirāṃsi sparśayet pṛthak | iti |
dravyapramāṇaviśeṣo noktaḥ, tathāpy aucityena kṛṣṇalapañcakaparisamāpter ārabhya daśamānapaṇād arvāg etau bhavitum arhataḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatinā, viṣādīni dharmajāntāni dviyāny uktvoktam –
satyaṃ vāhanaśāstrāṇi gobījakanakāni ca |
devabrāhmaṇapādāṃś ca putradāraśirāṃsi ca |
ete tu śapathāḥ proktāḥ satye ‘rtha sukarāḥ sadā || iti |
evam eteṣāṃ dharmajaṃ pratyalpārthaviṣayatvāt pratyayasvarūpaparāmarśanenaiva sīroddhṛ(śirodhṛ)tamahīṃ prati gurvarthaviṣayatvāvagamāt | paṇadaśakād arvāk kṛṣṇalapañcakād ūrdhvaṃ etāni bhavantīty uktam | paṇānāṃ daśakād ārabhya pañcadaśakād arvāk śūdrasya dharmaśodhanaṃ bhavati | asminn evārthe halopajīvināṃ kṛṣikāṇāṃ phālaṃ dātavyam iti | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ|
śate hṛte ‘pahnute ca dātavyaṃ dharmaśodhanam |
gocārasya pradātavyaṃ sabhyaiḥ phālaṃ prayatnataḥ || iti |
mānānāṃ śate ‘pahnute ca śūdrasya dharmaśodhanaṃ bhavati, mānaśatasya daśapaṇātmakatvāt | paṇadaśakād ārabhya pañapañcadaśakād arvāg dharmapratyayo bhavatīty arthaḥ | asminn evārthe gocarasya phālaṃ [177] gocārasya – kṛṣīvalasya śūdrasyety arthaḥ | “śūdrasya phālaṃ dātavyam” ity gautamasmaraṇāt | pañcadaśapaṇād ārabhya triṃśatpaṇād arvāk śūdrasya kośadānam | triṃśatpaṇād ārabhya catvāriṃśatpaṇād arvāk śūdrasya taṇḍulabhakṣaṇaṃ bhavati | catvāriṃśatpaṇād ārabhya pañcāśatpaṇād arvāk śūdrasya tatptamāṣā bhavanti | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
catuḥśatābhiyoge tu dātavyas taptamāṣakaḥ |
triṃśatas taṇḍulā deyāḥ kośaś caiva tadarthake ||
catuḥśatābhiyoge – śatacatuṣṭayamānasuvarṇaviṣayābhiyoge ity arthaḥ | tatra taptamāṣo dātavyaḥ | teṣāṃ catvāriṃatpaṇātmakatvāt | catvāriṃśatpaṇābhiyoge śūdrasya taptamāṣo dātavyaḥ iti yāvat | triśate taṇḍulā deyā ity atrāpi śatatrayamānasuvarṇe triṃśatpaṇātmake śūdrasya taṇḍulā deyā iti | tathā – “kośaś caiva tadardhake” iti pañcadaśapaṇātmake pañcāśadadhikaśatamānasuvarṇe śūdrasya kośo deya iti | tathā pañcāśatpaṇād ārabhya saptādhikaṣaṣṭipaṇād arvāk śūdrasya tulādhāraṇaṃ bhavati | saptādhikaṣaṣṭipaṇād ārabhya pañcādhikasaptatipaṇād arvāk śūdrasya jalapratyayo bhavati | pañcādhikasaptatipaṇḍād ārabhya śapaṇād arvāk śūdrasya paraśur bhavati | śatapaṇād ārabhya śūdrasya viṣabhakṣaṇam eva | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
viṣaṃ sahasre ‘pahṛte pādone tu hutāśanaḥ |
tripādone (bhāgone) ca salilaṃ ardhe deyo dhaṭaḥ sadā ||
paṇaśatātmakasahasramānasuvarṇe śūdrasya viṣabhakṣaṇaṃ bhavati | mānasahasracaturbhāgahīne pañcasaptatipaṇātmake śatasaptamāne śūdrasya paraśudhāraṇam | tathā sahasramānasuvarṇasya tṛtīyabhāgahīne [178] saptaṣaṣṭipaṇe śūdrasya jalapratyayo bhavati | pañcāśatpaṇātmake sahasramānārdhe śūdrasya tulādhirohaṇaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ |
nanu viṣaṃ sahasra ity ādau sahasrādisaṃkhyānāṃ saṃkhyeyatvena taṇḍulatulitamānasuvarṇam eva grāhyam iti katham avasīyate |
ucyate –
sarvadravyapramāṇaṃ tu jñātvā hetuṃ prakalpayet |
hemapramāṇayuktaṃ tu tathā divyaṃ prayojayet ||
śate viṣaṃ tu pādone dattabhuktatṛtīyake |
āpas tribhāgahīne tu śatārdhe tu tulā smṛtā ||
kośadānaṃ tadardhe vā daśapañcakasaptasu |
tadardhe taṇḍulā jñeyās tadardhe taptamāṣakam ||
iti kātyāyanaḥ | tathā ca |
jñātvā saṃkhyāṃ suvarṇasya śatamāne viṣaṃ smṛtam |
aśītes tu vināśe tu hutabhuk pratyayaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
hutabhuk – paraśuḥ |
ṣaṣṭyā nāśe jalaṃ deyaṃ syāc catvāriṃśake dhaṭaḥ |
triṃśadvināśe vā kośadānaṃ tatra bṛhaspatiḥ ||
pañcārdhaikasya vā nāśe tadardhasya tu taṇḍulāḥ ||
iti vṛddhamanuḥ | anayoḥ kātyāyana-vṛddhamanuvacanayoḥ śatanāśe viṣam uktam | bṛhaspativacane tu sahasranāśe viṣam uktam iti, anena tābhyāṃ mahān virodho ‘stīti pratīyate | tadvirodhaparihārārthaṃ bhūyasāṃnyāyena vacanadvayānusāreṇa bṛhaspativacanaṃ yojanīyam | tathā ca sati dharmaśāstreṣu daṇḍavidhānādau saṃkhyādimātre nirdiṣṭe paṇānāṃ vyavahārārthatayā kḷptatvāt saṃkhyeyatayā prāyeṇa paṇā eva tatra gṛhyanta [179] iti ihāpi kātyāyana-vṛddhamanuvacanagataśatādisaṃkhyānāṃ saṃkhyeyatvena paṇā eva gṛhyante | evaṃ ca bṛhaspativacanagatasahasrasaṃkhyādīnāāṃ saṃkhyeyatayā taṇḍulatulitamānasuvarṇe gṛhīte “mānas taṇḍulamātra syāt daśamānaḥ paṇaḥ smṛtaḥ” ityādivacanadarśanāc chatasahasrayor avirodhaḥ | yad api yājñavalkyavacanam,
nāsahasrād dharet phālaṃ na viṣaṃ na tulāṃ tathā |
atra sahasraśabdaḥ pādonārdhonayor upalakṣaṇārtha iti na virodhaḥ | tatra phālaśabdaḥ paraśuparaḥ | mukhyaphālaśabdasyālpārthaviṣayatvasya darśitatvāt | yat tv atra vṛddhamanunoktam |
aśītes tu vināśe tu hutabhuk pratyayaḥ smṛtaḥ |
yac ca kātyāyanoktam,
śate viṣaṃ tu pādone hutabhuk tatra dīyate |
āpas tribhāgahīne tu śatārdhe tu tulā smṛtā || iti |
etābhyāṃ vacanābhyām evaṃ pratīyate – pañcasaptatyā ārabhya aśīter arvāg agniḥ udakaṃ ca prāptam, evaṃ ṣaṣṭyā ārabhya saptaṣaṣṭipradeśād arvāk jaladhaṭaprāptau putrasahitotkṛṣṭasya puruṣasya laghupratyayo bhavati | aputrasya gurupratyayo bhavatīti vyavasthām āha varadarājaḥ |
tadardhe taṇḍulā deyās tadardhe taptamāṣakam |
iti alpaviṣaye taptamāṣavidhānaṃ cauryādiśaṅkāviṣayam iti avirodhaḥ |
dattasyāpahnavo yatra pramāṇaṃ tatra kalpyate (kalpayet) |
steyasāhasayor divyaṃ svalpe ‘py rthe pradāpayet ||
iti kātyāyanavacanāt | evam ātra siddhāntitārthasya ca viruddhārthapratipādakāni smṛtivacanāni santi, teṣām eva vyavasthāṃ [180] kalpayitvā tadvirodhaparihāraḥ kartavya ity anusaṃdheyam | brāhmaṇasyāgāmikāla ityādi | ayam arthaḥ – bhaviṣyatkāryaviṣaye tu brāḥmaṇasyāpi kośo bhavatīti | śūdrasyet – pūrvoktadravyapramāṇāni śūdraviṣayāṇīty arthaḥ | pūrvoktasarvaviṣayeṣu dviguṇārthe vaiśyasya śapathakriyā bhavati | triguṇārthe rājanyasya śapathakriyā | caturguṇārthe brāhmaṇasya śapathakiryeti draṣṭavyam, yat tūktam bṛhaspatinā |
eṣā saṃkhyā nikṛṣṭānāṃ madhyānāṃ dviguṇā smṛtā |
caturguṇā cottamānāṃ kalpanīyā parīkṣakaiḥ || iti |
tat tūttamād arvācīnānāṃ madhyamād utkṛṣṭānāṃ traiguṇyapratipādanasyāpy upalakṣakaṃ viṣṇuvacanānurodhāt | anenaiva vyākhyānena “eṣā saṃkhyā nikṛṣṭānām” ityādi manuvacanam api vyākhyātam | śūdrād arvācīnānām api śūdravad eva divyāni deyāni |
śūdrāṇāṃ tu sadharmāṇaḥ sarve ‘padhvaṃsajāḥ smṛtāḥ |
iti manuvacanāt | keṣāṃcid brāhmaṇānām api śūdravad eva divyaṃ deyam ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
gorakṣakān vāṇijakān śilpinaś ca kuśīlavān |
preṣyān vārdhuṣikāṃś caiva viprāñ chūdravad ācaret ||
etad uktaṃ bhavati – śūdrasya putradārādiśapathasthāne vaiśyasya gobījakāñcanaiḥ śapathaḥ | kṣatriyasya vāhanaśastraiḥ | brāhmaṇasya satyavākyena | ataś ca yad vṛttaṃ brāhmaṇasya tat prayuktāḥ śapathāḥ kāryā iti | atra vivadante vṛddhāḥ – kecid divyeṣu paṇānāṃ saṃkhyeyatve ‘pi kākaṇikacatuṣṭaye loke paṇavyavahārāt  kākaṇikaḥ paṇacaturbhāga iti vaiyākaraṇaprasiddheś ca kākaṇikacatuṣṭayātmakaṃ [181] suvarṇapaṇam eva sahasrādisaṃkhyāsaṃkhyeyam iti manyante | anye tu tāmrakākaṇikābhiprāyā sarvadivyeṣu saṃkhyeti manyante | apare tu –
mānas taṇḍulamātraṃ syād daśamānaḥ paṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
ityāgamavacanād daśamānātmaka eva paṇa iti manyante | apare –
māṣo viṃśatimo bhāgo jñeyaḥ kārṣāpaṇasya tu |
kākaṇī tu caturbhāgo māṣasyeti prakīrtitaḥ ||
iti auśanasavacanāt,
tathā māṣaṃ budhāh prāhuḥ kākaṇīnāṃ catuṣṭayam |
viṃśatiḥ syāt punas teṣāṃ sa mukhyaḥ paṇa ucyate ||
iti bhāradvājavacanāt,
māṣo viṃśatimo bhāgaḥ paṇasya parikīrtitaḥ |
iti kātyāyanavacanāc (-smaraṇāc) ca viṃśatimāṣāḥ paṇa iti tadviṣayā divyeṣu saṃkhyeti manyante | tad etad asāram –
paṇo viṃśatimāṣas tu divyād anyatra kīrtitaḥ |
iti kātyāyanavacanena divyeṣu viṃśatimāṣasya paṇasya niṣedhāt | tathā jñātvā suvarṇasya saṃkhyām iti smṛteḥ –
sarvadravyapramāṇaṃ tu jñātvā hema prakalpayet |
sarveṣv arthajāteṣu mūlyaṃ kanakaṃ kalpayet ||
ityevamādismṛtibhyaḥ suvarṇasyaiva saṃkhyeyatvāvagamāt | tāmrikakārṣāpaṇasya divyeṣu saṃkhyeyatvaprāpakavacanābhāvāc ca suvarṇapaṇānām eva divyeṣu saṃkhyeyatvam uktam | yat tu – candrikākāreṇa sahasrādisaṃkhyānāṃ laukikaniṣkasya saṃkhyeyatvam ity uktam iti | tat tūktarītyā sahasrādisaṃkhyānāṃ [182] paṇasyaiva saṃkhyeyatvasya nyāyyatvāt niṣkapratipādakavacanābhāvāc ca heyam |
nanu kākaṇikānām aṣṭakaṃ taṇḍulaśabdenocyate | mānaśabdenāpi tad evocyate | tatra daṇḍaprakaraṇe –
māṣān aṣṭau tu mahiṣīṃ sasyaghātasya kākiṇīm |
iti vacanāt,
dāpayet paṇapādaṃ tu gāṃ pādau mahaṣīṃ tathā |
iti vacanāt | paṇapādadvayāṣṭamānamāṣaśabdayor ekārthatvāvagamāt ṣoḍaśamāṣātmakaḥ paṇa iti gamyate | tathā ca carake |
utthitas tv arkajālena trasareṇuka ucyate |
aṣṭakaṃ trasareṇūnāṃ likṣeti parikīrtyate ||
tās tisraḥ sarṣapaḥ proktaḥ kākiṇī syāt tu sā smṛtā |
aṣṭakaṃ kākiṇīnāṃ tu taṇḍulaḥ parikīrtitaḥ ||
taṇḍulaṃ mānakaṃ proktaṃ māṣakaḥ parikīrtyate |
yad vastu smṛtiṣu mānaśabdenocyate tad eva vaidyake mānaśabdenocyate |
taddvayaṃ kṛṣṇalaṃ proktaṃ maṇḍikā kṛṣṇaladvayam |
astikāpaṇaśabdau tu paryāyeṇa prakīrtitau || iti |
atra ṣoḍaśātmakaḥ paṇa iti gamyate | tathā vaikhānasasaṃhitāyām api,
mānas taṇḍulamātraṃ syād aṣṭamāṣaḥ paṇaḥ smṛtaḥ | iti |
tatrāpy aṣṭakam eva kākiṇīnāṃ māṣaśabdena taṇḍulaśabdena vocyate ṣoḍaśamānātmakaḥ paṇa iti,
satyam etat | ṣoḍaśamāṣātmakaḥ daśamāṣātmako vā paṇaḥ saṃkhyeyatvena gṛhyate iti, tatra śodhyasya guṇavattve ṣoḍaśamāṣātmakasya saṃkhyeyatvena grahaṇam | [183] nirguṇasya śodhyatve ‘sya daśamāṣātmakasya paṇasaṃkhyeyatvam iti vyavasthā | abhiśāpavyavasthayā divyavyavasthām āha kātyāyanaḥ |
aparādhaviśeṣeṇa divyasthānāni
indrasthāne ‘bhiśastānāṃ mahāpātakināṃ nṛṇām |
nṛpadrohapravṛttānāṃ rājadvāri prayojayet ||
prātilomyaprasūtānāṃ divyaṃ deyaṃ catuṣpathe |
tato ‘nyeṣu tu kāryeṣu sabhāmadhye vidur budhāḥ ||
anyeṣu kāryeṣu – svalpakāryeṣu – adhikakāryeṣv apy uttamānāṃ satyādipuruṣāntāḥ śapathā eva | divyaṃ deyam iti divyagrahaṇād dhaṭādiviṣāntāny avagantavyāni | indrasthānam iti prakhyātadevatāyatanam upalakṣayati | yathāha nāradaḥ |
sabhārājakuladvāradevatācatvarā iti |
imāni divyāni janasamakṣam eva deyāni | yathāha pitāmahaḥ |
pratyakṣaṃ dāpayed divyaṃ rājā vādhikṛto ‘pi vā |
brāhmaṇānāṃ śrutavatāṃ prakṛtīnāṃ tathaiva ca ||
prakṛtayaḥ – prajāḥ | adhikṛtaḥ – prāḍvivākaḥ | pratigatam akṣam – pratyakṣam | atra viśeṣam āha hārītaḥ |
dhane pramuṣite coraiḥ prahṛte ‘pi dhanī svayam |
parimāṇaṃ svarūpaṃ ca śapathena viśodhayet ||
corair aphṛte dravye taddravye guruparimāṇaṃ (dvarūpaṃ ca) vālpaparimāṇaṃ vānādṛtya śapathenaiva daṇḍapāśikānāṃ dravyaparimāṇaṃ ca dravyavān sādhayed ity arthaḥ |
iti sarvadivyopayoginī mātṛkā samāptā
divyanirmāṇaprakāraḥ
atha divyanirmāṇaprakāra ucyate | atra prajāpatiḥ |
divyeṣu sarvakāryāṇi prādvivākaḥ prakāśayet |
adhvareṣu yathādhvaryuḥ sopavāso nṛpājñayā ||
chittvā yajñīyavṛkṣaṃ tu yūpavan mantrapūrvakam |
praṇamya lokapālebhyaḥ tulā kāryā manīṣibhiḥ ||
atra prāḍvivākagrahaṇād rājaniyuktasabhāyām eva tulā na tu grāmādisabhāsu tatra prāḍvivākābhāvāt | sopavāsa iti pūrvoktaniyamānām upalakṣaṇam | nṛpājñayā tulā kāryety anvayaḥ | chittvā – bhedayitvā | mantrapūrvakam ity atra mantram āha: “saumyo vānaspatyaḥ” iti | saumyaḥ – somadaivatyaḥ | somo dhenum ityādiḥ | vānaspatyo – vanaspatidaivatyaḥ vanaspate śatavalśo virohetyādiḥ | “ṛjvī tulā kāryā khādarī vā taindukī vā” iti viṣṇuḥ | atra prajāpatir viśeṣam āha |
khādiraṃ kārayet tatra nirvraṇaṃ śuklavarjitam |
śuklo – niryāsaḥ |
śiṃśapāṃ tadabhāve tu sālaṃ vā koṭaraṃ vinā |
evaṃvidhāni koṣṭhāni dhaṭārthaṃ parikalpayet ||
caturhastā tulā kāryā pādau vāpi tathāvidhau |
antaraṃ tu tayor hastau bhaved adhyardham eva vā ||
toraṇe ca tathā kārye pārśvayor ubhayor api |
dhaṭād uccatare syātāṃ nityaṃ daśabhir aṅgulaiḥ ||
dhaṭanirmāṇam
[185]        avalambau ca kartavyau toraṇābhyām adhomukhau |
mṛnmayau sūtrasaṃbandhau dhaṭamastakacumbitau || iti |
atra tulārthaṃ vṛkṣacchedanaṃ saparikarāyās tulāyāḥ | ataś ca pādastambārtham api tadvṛkṣacchedanaṃ kāryam ity avagantavyam | “caturhastā tulā kāryā” ity anena parikarāyās tulāyāḥ pramāṇam uktam | tathāvidhāv iti – caturhastapramāṇāv ity arthaḥ | pādāv iti – tulādhāraṇārthakāṣṭadhāraṇārthastambhau | taccaturhastamānau karaṇīyahastadvayād ūrdhvam |
hastadvayanikheyaṃ tu proktaṃ kaṭakayor dvayoḥ |
ṣaḍḍhastaṃ tu tayoḥ proktaṃ pramāṇaṃ tu pramāṇataḥ || iti |
akṣapramāṇaṃ tu tayor antarālābhidhānenaivārthasiddham, tena pādastambhasaktakaṭakād bahir nisṛto yathā bhavati yathā hastadvayāt sardhahastādhiko ‘kṣadaṇḍaḥ kārya ity avagantavyam | hastaḥ punaś caturviṃśatyaṅgulaḥ |
tiryagyavodarāṇy aṣṭau ūrdhvā vā vrīhayas trayaḥ |
pramāṇam aṅgulasyoktaṃ vitastir dvādaśāṅgulam ||
“hastau vitastadvayam” iti smṛteḥ | tulāsthaulye tu viśeṣāsmṛteḥ yavati sthaulye dārḍhyaṃ tāvat sthaulyaṃ kāryam | yad vā – ācārāt sthaulyaviśeṣo veditavyaḥ | etad evābhipretyoktaṃ pitāmahena |
caturhastā tulā kāryā dṛḍhā ṛjvī tathaiva ca |
kaṭakāni ca deyāni triṣu sthāneṣ yatnataḥ ||
kaṭakāni – lohavalayāni | upāntayor madhye ca deyāni | [186] upāntayoḥ śikyadhāraṇārthaṃ madhye ‘kṣakāṣṭhe tulādhāraṇārtham iti vivekaḥ | yathāha pitāmahaḥ |
āyasena tu pādena madhye saṃgṛhya dharmavit |
yojayet tu susaṃyuktāṃ tulāṃ prāgaparāyatām ||
prāgaparāyatām – pūrvapaścimadīrghām ity arthaṃ |
prāṅmukho niścalaḥ kāryaḥ śucau deśe dhaṭaḥ sadā |
śikyadvayaṃ samsajya pārśvayor ubhayor api ||
prāṅmukhān kalpayed darbhān śikyayor iti niścayaḥ |
ekasmiṃs tolayet kartṝn anyasmin mṛttikāṃ śubhām ||
kartur iti pāṭhe tu kartāram – abhiyuktam ity arthaḥ |
piṭakaṃ pūrayet tasmin iṣṭakāgrāvapāṃsubhiḥ |
atra mṛttikeṣṭakāgrāvapāṃsūnāṃ vikalpo veditavyaḥ | etac ca māṣāṇām upalakṣaṇam, “māṣair vā nikaṭaṃ pūrayet” iti smṛtyantaravacanāt |vikaṭam – śikyāntargatapātram |
parīkṣakā niyoktavyās tulāmānaviśāradāḥ |
vaṇijo hemakārāś ca kāṃsyakārās tathaiva ca ||
kāryaḥ parīkṣakair nityam avalambasamo dhaṭaḥ |
udakaṃ ca pradātavyaṃ dhaṭasyopari paṇḍitaiḥ ||
yasmin na plavate toyaṃ sa vijñeyaḥ samo dhaṭaḥ |
dhataṃ tu kārayen nityaṃ patākādhvajaśobhitam ||
tolayitvā naraṃ pūrvaṃ tatas tam avatārayet |
evam uktavidhānāṃ tulāṃ kṛtvā prāḍvivākaḥ kiṃ kuryād ity ata āha |
prāṅmukhaḥ prāñjalir bhūtvā prāḍvivākas tato vadet |
[187]        ehy ehi bhagavan dharma asmin divye samāviśa ||
sahito lokapālaiś ca vasvādityamarudgaṇaiḥ |
āvāhya tu dhaṭe dharmaṃ paścād aṅgāni vinyaset ||
indraṃ pūrve tu saṃsthāpya preteśaṃ dakṣiṇe tathā |
varuṇaṃ paścime bhāge kuberaṃ cottare tathā ||
agnyādilokapālāṃś ca koṇabhāgeṣu vinyaset |
indraḥ pīto yamaḥ śyāmo varuṇaḥ sphaṭikaprabhaḥ ||
kuberaś ca suvarṇābhaḥ agniś caiva suvarṇabhaḥ |
tathaiva nirṛtiḥ śyāmo vāyur dhūmraḥ pradṛśyate ||
īśānas tu bhaved raktaḥ evaṃ dhyāyet kramād imān |
indrasya dakṣiṇe pārśve vasūnā (rādha) vāhayed budhaḥ ||
dharo dhruvas tathā soma āpaś caivānilo ‘nalaḥ |
pratyūṣaś ca prabhāsaś ca vasavo ‘ṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ ||
deveśeśānayor madhye ādityānāṃ tathāyanam |
dhātāryamā ca mitraś ca varuṇo ‘ṃśo bhagas tathā ||
indro vivasvān pūṣā ca parjanyo daśamaṃ smṛtaḥ |
tatas tvṣṭā tato viṣṇur ajaghanyo jaghanyajaḥ ||
ity ete dvādaśādityā nāmabhiḥ parikīrtitāḥ |
atrājaghanyo jaghanyaja iti tvaṣṭṛviṣṇor viśeṣaṇe | ato na saṃkhyātirekaḥ |
agneḥ paścimabhāge tu rudrāṇām ayanaṃ smṛtam |
vīrabhadraś ca śambhuś ca vāgīśaś ca mahāyaśāḥ ||
ajaikpād ahirbudhnyaḥ pinākī cāparājitaḥ |
bhuvanādhīśavaraś caiva kalīpā ca viśāṃpate ||
[188]        sthānur bhagaś ca bhagavān rudrā ekādaś smṛtāḥ |
prajeśarakasor madhye mātṛsthānaṃ prakalpayet ||
brāhmī māheśvarī caiva kaumārī caiva vaiṣṇavī |
vārāhī caiva māhendrā cāmuṇḍāgaṇasaṃvṛtā ||
nirṛter uttare bhāge gaṇēśāyatanaṃ viduḥ |
varuṇasyottare bhāge marutāṃ sthānam ucyate |
gaganaḥ sparśano vāyur anilo mārutas tathā ||
prāṇaḥ prāṇeśajīvau ca maruto ‘ṣṭau prakīrtitāḥ ||
smṛtyantare tu |
āvaho vivahaś caiva udvahaḥ saṃvahas tathā |
nivaho ‘dhivahaś caiva pravaho ‘bhivahas tathā || iti |
etayor vikalpaḥ |
dhaṭasyottaradigbhāge durgām āvāhayed budhaḥ |
etāsāṃ devatānāṃ tu svanāmnā pūjanaṃ viduḥ ||
bhūṣāvasānaṃ dharmāya datvā cārghyādikaṃ kramāt |
bhūṣāvasānam – bhūṣāntam | tad ayam atra niṣkarṣaḥ – tulāyām ehy ehīti mantreṇa dharmam āvāhya āvāhitāya dharmāya arghyapādyācamanīyasnānavastrācamanīyayajñopavitācamanīyam akūṭakanakabhūṣāntaṃ datvā indrādīnāṃ durgāntānām api arghyādibhūṣādisaparyāṃ padārthānusamayena datvā ubhayeṣāṃ gandhupuṣpadhūpadīpanaivedyādīni dadyād iti | gandhapuṣpāṇi dhaṭapūjāyāṃ raktāni kāryāṇi | yathāha nāradaḥ |
raktair gandhaiś ca dhūpaiś ca dadyād dipā[dhūpā]kṣatadibhiḥ |
[189]        arcayet tu dhaṭaṃ pūrvaṃ tathā śiṣṭāṃś ca pūjayet || iti |
indrādīnāṃ raktair anyair vā pūjanam, viśeṣādarśanāt | etat sarvaṃ pūrvāhne kāryam |
pūrvāhne sarvadivyānāṃ pradānam anukīrtitam |
iti nāradavacanāt | etac ca prāḍvivākaḥ kuryād ity āha prajāpatiḥ |
prāḍvivākas tato vipro vedavedāntapāragaḥ |
śrutavṛttopapannaś ca śāntacitto vimatsaraḥ ||
satyasandhaḥ śucir dakṣaḥ sarvaprāṇihite rataḥ |
upoṣitaḥ śuddhavāsāḥ kṛtakāntānudhāvanaḥ ||
sarvāsāṃ devatānāṃ ca pūjāṃ kuryād yathāvidhi |
caturdikṣu tathā homaḥ kartavyo vedapāragaiḥ ||
ājyena haviṣā caiva samidbhir homasādhanaiḥ ||
sāvitryā praṇavenātha svāhāntenaiva bhāvayet || iti |
homaprakāro vijñānayoginoktaḥ: “ṛtvigbhiś caturbhiḥ caturdikṣu laukikāgnau praṇavādikāṃ gāyatrīm uccārya punaḥ svāhākārāntaṃ praṇavam uccārya samidājyacarūn pratyekam aṣṭottaraśataṃ juhuyāt” iti |
yaṃ cārtham abhiyuktaḥ syāl likhitvā taṃ tu patrake |
mantreṇānena sahitaṃ tat kāryaṃ tu śirogatam || iti |
evaṃ homāntāṃ devapūjāṃ vidhāyānantaram abhiyuktam arthaṃ mantrasahitaṃ patre likhitvā tatpatraṃ śodhyaśirogataṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | [190]
mantras tu |
ādityacandrāv analo ‘nilaś ca dyaur bhūmir āpo hṛdayaṃ yamaś ca |
ahaś ca rātriś ca ubhe ca saṃdhye dharmaś ca jānāti narasya vṛttam || iti |
yat tu prajāpatinoktam,
imaṃ mantravidhiṃ kṛtsnaṃ sarvadivyeṣu yojayet |
āvāhanaṃ ca divyānāṃ tathaiva parikalpayet || iti |
asyārthaḥ – dharmāvāhanādi śirasi patrāropaṇāntam anuṣṭhānakāṇḍaṃ sarvadivyasādhāraṇam uktam iti mantavyam | anantaraṃ prāḍvivāko dhaṭam āmantrya taṃ śodhyaṃ punar āropayet |
samayaiḥ parigṛhyātha punar āropayen naram |
nivāte vṛṣṭirahite śirasy āropya patrakam ||
samāḥ –śapathāḥ | śapathāś ca tenaiva darśitāḥ |
brahmaghno yasya lokā ye ye lokāḥ kūṭasākṣiṇām |
tulādhārasya te lokās tulāṃ dhārayato mṛṣā || iti |
punar āropaṇānantaraṃ nāradaḥ |
tasminn eva kṛte lakṣye lakṣyaṃ kṛtvā dvijo vadet |
tvaṃ dhaṭa brahmaṇā diṣṭaḥ parīkṣārthaṃ durātmanām ||
dhakārād dharmamūrtis tvaṃ ṭakārāt kuṭilaṃ naram |
dhṛto bhāvayase yasmāt dhaṭas tenābhidhīyase ||
tvaṃ vetsi sarvabhūtānāṃ pāpāni sukṛtāni ca |
tvam eva deva jānīṣe na vidur yāni mānuṣāḥ ||
[191]        vyavahārābhiyukto ‘yaṃ mānuṣaḥ śuddhim icchati |
tad enaṃ saṃśayād asmād dharmatas trātum arhati || iti |
anantaraṃ tu śodhyas tulām āmantrayet | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
tulādhāraṇavidvidbhiḥ aviyuktas tulāśritaḥ |
pratimānasamībhūto rekhāṃ kṛtvāvatāritaḥ ||
tulābhimantraṇam
tvaṃ tule satyadhāmāsi purā devair vinirmitā |
tat satyaṃ vada kalyāṇi saṃśayān māṃ vimocaya ||
yady asmi pāpakarmāhaṃ tato māṃ tvam adho naya |
śuddhaś ced gamayordhvaṃ māṃ tulām ity abhimantrayet ||
atra nāradaḥ |
śikyadvayaṃ samāsajya dhaṭakarkaṭayor dṛḍham |
ekatra śikye puruṣam anyatra tulaec chilām ||
dhaṭakarkaṭayoḥ – karkaṭaśṛṅgopamayoḥ lohavalayayor ubhayoḥ koṭilagnayoḥ |
dhārayed uttare pārśve puruṣaṃ dakṣiṇe śilām |
śiro ‘vasthitapatrakaṃ dhaṭaṃ punar āropya vināḍīpañcakaṃ yāvat tathaivāvasthāpayet |
vināḍyādilakṣaṇam
vināḍyaḥ pañca vijñeyāḥ parīkṣyāḥ kālakovidaiḥ | iti |
daśagurvakṣaroccāraṇakālaḥ prāṇaḥ, ṣaṭprāṇā vināḍiḥ, uktaṃ ca jyotiḥśāstre |
daś gurvakṣarāḥ prāṇāḥ ṣaṭprāṇā syād vināḍikā |
tāsāṃ ṣaṣṭyā ca ghaṭikā tatṣaṣṭyā ahar ucyate || iti |
anena jyotiḥśāstraparicayavān ekaś ca tulākovideṣu kāryaḥ |
[192]
tulāyāṃ śuddhyaśuddhiprakāraḥ
śuddhyaśuddhivinirṇayaprakāram āha prajāpatiḥ |
tulito yadi vardheta sa śuddhaḥ syān na saṃśayaḥ |
samo vā hīyamāno vā na viśuddho bhaven naraḥ ||
yat tu pitāmahenoktam,
hīyamāno na śuddhaḥ syād eteṣāṃ tu samā śuciḥ |
iti, tan matāntaragrahaṇaṃ pūjanārtham, na tu vikalpārtham | tathānyatra |
kaśācchede tulābhaṅge dhaṭakarkaṭayos tathā |
rajjucchede ‘kṣabhaṅge ca tathaivāśucim ādiśet || iti |
tathāpi dṛśyamāne kāraṇe yeṣāṃ bhaṅgaḥ nādṛṣṭavaśāt, tadā samādhānaṃ kṛtvā punar āropaṇaṃ kartavyam |
śikyādicchedanabhaṅge ca punar āropayen naram |
iti smṛtyantaravacanam | ata evāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
dhaṭe niyuktas tulito hīnaś ced dhānim āpnuyāt |
tatsamas tu punas taulye vardhito vijayī bhavet ||
śikyacchede ‘kṣabhaṅge vā dadyāc chikyaṃ punar nṛpaḥ |
sākṣiṇo brāhmaṇāḥ śreṣṭhāḥ yathādṛṣṭārthavādinaḥ ||
jñātinaḥ śucayo lubdhā niyoktavyā nṛpeṇa tu |
teṣāṃ vacanato gamyaḥ śuddhyaśuddhivinirṇayaḥ ||
ṛtvijāṃ dakṣiṇā dātavyety āha pitāmahaḥ |
ṛtvikpurohitācāryān dakṣiṇābhis tu toṣayet | iti |
evaṃ kārayitvā rājā bhuktvā bhogān manoramān |
mahatīṃ kīrtim āpnoti brahmabhūyāya kalpate ||
[193]
dhaṭasaṃrakṣaṇaprakāraḥ
dhaṭasaṃrakṣaṇaprakāram āha pitāmahaḥ |
viśālām ucchritāṃ śubhrāṃ dhaṭaśālāṃ tu kārayet |
yatrastho nopahanyeta śvabhiś caṇḍālavāyasaiḥ ||
tatraiva lokapālādīn sarvadikṣu niveśayet |
trisaṃdhyaṃ pūjayec caināṃ gandhamālyānulepanaiḥ ||
kavāṭabījasaṃyuktāṃ paricārakarakṣitām |
mṛtpānīyāgnisaṃyuktām aśūnyāṃ kārayen nṛpaḥ || iti |
bījāni yavavrīhyādīni | vijñānayogī tu “yadā tūktalakṣaṇaṃ dhaṭaṃ tathaiva sthāpayitum icchati tadā śvavāyasādyupaghātanirāsārthaṃ kavāṭādisahitāṃ śālāṃ kuryāt” iti |
iti dhaṭavidhiḥ
athāgnividhiḥ
athāgnividhir ucyate |
agner vidhiṃ pravakṣyāmi yathāśākhapracoditam |
kārayen maṇḍalāny aṣṭau purastān navamaṃ tathā ||
āgneyaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tv ādyaṃ dvitīyaṃ vāruṇaṃ smṛtam |
tṛtīyaṃ vāyudaivatyaṃ caturthaṃ yamadaivatam ||
pañcamaṃ tv aindradaivatyaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tūcyate budhaiḥ |
kauberam ucyate ṣaṣṭhaṃ saptamaṃ somadaivatam ||
ādityam aṣṭamaṃ caiva navamaṃ sarvadaivatam |
etāni maṇḍalāny aṣṭāv iti śāstravido viduḥ ||
etāni nava maṇḍalāni prakhyātadevatāyatanādivihitadeśe prāgapavargāṇi gomayena kāryāṇi | atra hārītaḥ |
[194]        aṣṭābhir maṇḍalaiḥ proktam aṅgulīnāṃ śatadvayam |
ṣaṭpañcāśatsamadhikaṃ bhūmes tu parikalpanā ||
ṣoṣaśāṅgulakaṃ jñeyaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tāvad antaram |
aṅgulapramāṇaṃ tulāprakaraṇe darśitam | pitāmahenāpy uktam |
dvātriṃśadaṅgulaṃ prāhur maṇḍalaṃ maṇḍalāntaram |
aṣṭābhir maṇḍalair evam aṅgulānāṃ śatadvayam ||
ṣaṭpañcāśatsamadhikaṃ bhūmes tu parikalpanā |
kartuṃ padasamaṃ kāryaṃ maṇḍalaṃ tu pramāṇataḥ ||
tatra navamaṃ maṇḍalam aparimitāṅgulapramāṇakaṃ taṃ vihāyāṣṭabhir saṇḍalaiś cāṣṭābhiś cāntarālaiḥ pratyekaṃ ṣoḍaśāṅgulapramāṇair aṅgulānāṃ ṣaṭpañcāśatsamadhikaṃ śatadvayaṃ saṃpadyate | tatrāpi saptaiva gantavyāni maṇḍalāni | katham | prathame tiṣṭhati navame kṣipatīti |
nanu,
dvātriṃśadaṅgulaṃ prāhur maṇḍalaṃ maṇḍalāntaram |
aṣṭābhir maṇḍalair evam aṅgulānāṃ śatadvayam ||
catvāriṃśatsamadhikaṃ bhūmer aṅgulamānataḥ |
iti nāradoktiḥ katham iti ced,
ucyate – avasthānamaṇḍalaṃ vihāya gantavyamaṇḍalābhiprāyaṃ tat | ayam arthaḥ – avasthānamaṇḍalāt ṣoḍaśāṅgulāt maṇḍalāntaram anyamaṇḍalaṃ dvitīyādy ekam ekaṃ dvātriṃśadaṅgulaṃ sāntarālam | tad evam avasthānamaṇḍalaṃ ṣoḍaśāṅgulaṃ gantavyāni ca saptamaṇḍalāni sāntarālāni catvāriṃśadaṅgulāni evam aṣṭabhir maṇḍalaiś catvāriṃśatsamadhikaśatadvayaṃ bhūmer aṅgulam iti | atra dviyamṛtṛkoktasādhāraṇadharmeṣv anuṣṭhiteṣu [195] tulāprakaraṇoktadharmāvāhanādiśiraḥpatrāntavidhyante sati agnividhau viśeṣam āha prajāpatiḥ |
paścime maṇḍale tiṣthet prāṅmukhaḥ prāñjaliḥ śuciḥ |
tiṣṭhet – śodhyaḥ | śodhanaprakāram āha |
lakṣayeyuḥ kṣatādīni hastayos tasya hāriṇaḥ |
lakṣaṇam – cihnam | yathāha nāradaḥ |
hastakṣateṣu sarveṣu kuryād dhaṃsapadāni ca | iti |
alaktakarasādinā vraṇakiṇasthāneṣu haṃsapādākāreṇa aṅkayitvety arthaḥ | tataḥ kim ity apekṣite yājñavalkyaḥ |
karau vimṛditavrīhī lakṣayitvā tato nyaset |
saptāśvatthasya parṇāni tāvacchubhrāṇi veṣṭayet ||
veṣṭayīta sitair hastau sapabhiḥ sūtratantubhiḥ ||
iti nāradavacanād aśvatthaparṇam upari sapta śamīparṇāni sapta dūrvāparṇāni dhadhyakṣatāṃś ca vinyaset | tathā ca pitāmahaḥ |
sapta pippalapatrāṇi śamīpatrāṇy athākṣatān |
dūrvāyāḥ sapta parṇāni dadhyaktān akṣatāṃs tathā ||
vinyased ity śeṣaḥ | kusumāni ca vinyaset | tathā ca pitāmahaḥ |
sapta pippalapatrāṇi akṣatān sumano dadhi |
hastayor nikṣipet tatra sūtreṇāveṣṭitaṃ tathā || iti |
sumanasaḥ – puṣpāṇi | yat tūktaṃ hārītena,
ayas taptaṃ tu pāṇibhyām arkaparṇais tu saptabhiḥ |
antarhitaṃ haran śuddhas tv adagdhaḥ saptame pade ||
[196] iti, tat tu aśvatthaparṇānām abhāve arkapatraviṣayaṃ veditavyam aśvatthapatrāṇāṃ pitāmahapraśaṃsāvacanena mukhyatvāvagamāt –
pippalāj jāyate vahniḥ pippalo vṛkṣarāṭ smṛtaḥ |
atas tasya tu patrāṇi hastayor nikṣiped budhaḥ ||
iti vijñānayogī | apare tu śamīpatrādīnām arkapatrapakṣe ‘nupraveśo nāstīty evaṃparam ity āhuḥ | atra prajāpatiḥ |
aśrihīnaṃ samaṃ kuryād aṣṭāṅgulam ayomayam |
piṇḍaṃ tu tapayed agnau pañcāśatpalikaṃ smṛtam ||
atra vyāsaḥ |
prāṅmukhas tu tatas tiṣṭhet prasāritakarāṅguliḥ |
ārdravāsāḥ śuciś caiva śirasy āropya patrakam ||
jātyaiva lohakāro yaḥ kuśalaś cāgnikarmaṇi |
dṛṣṭaprayogaś cānyatra tenāyo ‘gnau tu dāpayet ||
agnivarṇam ayaḥpiṇḍaṃ sasphuliṅgaṃ surañjitam |
pañcāśatpalikaṃ bhūyaḥ snāpayitvā śucir dvijaḥ ||
tṛtīyatāpe tapyantaṃ brūyāt satyapuraskṛtam | iti |
dṛṣṭaprayogaś cānyatrety anena yathā loke lohaśuddhyarthaṃ sutaptaloham udake nikṣipya punaḥ santāpena lohakāryasiddhyarthaṃ kriyate tathātrāpi kartavyam iti vadataḥ candrikākārasya mate tṛtīyatāpe tapyantam ityādi vacanāni nyāyasiddhārthānuvādīnīti mantavyam | satyapuraskṛtam, tatsvarūpam āha pitāmahaḥ |
śṛṇv imaṃ mānavaṃ dharmaṃ lokapālair adhiṣṭhitam |
tvam agne sarvadevānāṃ pavitraṃ paramaṃ mukham |
[197]        tvam agne sarvabhūtānāṃ hṛdistho vetsi ceṣṭitam ||
atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
tasyety uktavato lohaṃ pañcāśatpalikaṃ samam |
agnivarṇaṃ nyaset piṇḍaṃ hastayor ubhayor api ||
tasya – kartuḥ | iti – evaṃ prakāreṇa |
tvam agne sarvadevāṇāṃ antaś carasi sākṣivat |
tvam eva deva jānīṣe na vidur yāni mānavāḥ ||
vyavhārābhiśasto ‘yaṃ mānuṣaḥ śuddhim icchati |
pāpaṃ punāsi vai yasmāt tasmāt pāvaka ucyase ||
pāpeṣu darśayātmānam arciṣmān bhava pāvaka |
atha vā śuddhabhāvena śīto bhava hutāśana || iti |
atra vyāsaḥ |
eṣa tvāṃ dhārayati satyenānena mānavaḥ |
satyavākyasya cāsya tvaṃ śīto bhava hutāśana |
mṛṣāvākyasya pāpasya daha hastau ca māyinaḥ || iti |
tadanantarakṛtyam āha prajāpatiḥ |
amuṃ mantraṃ ca patrastham abhiyuktaṃ yathārthataḥ |
saṃśrāvya mūrdhni tasyaiva nyased evaṃ yathākramam ||
nyāsaprakāram āha manuḥ |
ity uktvā hastayos tasya pradadyāt piṇḍapāvakam |
suprataptam asaṃbhugnaṃ visphuliṅgāgnyuddhṛtam ||
asaṃbhugnam animnapradeśaṃ svarūpeṇāvasthitam ity arthaḥ |
dṛḍhaṃ gṛhītvā sandaśaiḥ vidyujjālasamaprabham | [198] iti |
utkaṭaṃ dhṛtaṃ vilīnaṃ na bhavatīty anuddhṛtam | tataḥ śodhyakṛtyam āha yamaḥ |
hastābhyāṃ tu samādāya prāḍvivākasamīpataḥ |
sthitvaikasmin tato ‘nyāni vrajet sapta tv ajihmagaḥ ||
ekasminn iti – paścime prathame maṇḍale | ajihmagaḥ – akuṭilagatiḥ |
asaṃbhrāntaḥ śanair gacched akruddhaḥ so ‘nalaṃ prati |
yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
sa tam ādāya saptaiva maṇḍalāni śanair vrajet | iti |
saḥ – puruṣaḥ | tam – taptalohapiṇḍam | evakāreṇa maṇḍaleṣv eva padanyāsaḥ | maṇḍalānatikramaṃ ca darśayati pitāmaḥaḥ |
na maṇḍalam atikrāmet nāpy arvāk sthāpayet padam | iti |
maṇḍalaṃ cāṣṭamaṃ gatvā tato ‘gniṃ visṛjen naraḥ |
yas tv antarā pātayati daṇḍyaś ca sa vibhāvayet ||
punas taṃ hārayed agniṃ sthitir eṣā dṛḍhīkṛtā ||
prathamamaṇḍale sthitasya kartur abhimantraṇam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
tvam agne sarvabhūtānām antaś carasi pāvaka |
sākṣī matpuṇyapāpānāṃ brūhi satyaṃ kave mama ||
satyam brūhi darśayety arthaḥ | atrāyaṃ prayogaḥ – sutaptam ayaḥpiṇḍaṃ udake lohaśuddhyarthaṃ nikṣipya punaḥ santāpyodake nikṣipya tṛtīyatāpe sandaṃśena gṛhītvā punar apy ānīya tvam agne sarvabhūtānām ity abhimantraṇaṃ brūyāt | asminn avasare prāḍvivāko maṇḍalād dakṣiṇapradeśe laukikam agnim upasamādhāya agnaye pāvakāyety ājyena [199] aṣṭottaraśataṃ juhuyāt | asminn evāgnau ayaḥpiṇḍatāpa iti candrikākāraḥ | aṣṭame maṇḍale sthitvā navame agnityāge kṛte kiṃ kuryād iti yājñavalkyaḥ |
muktvāgniṃ mṛditavrīhir adaṇḍyaḥ śuddhim āpnuyāt |
atra kātyāyanas tu viśeṣam āha |
na skhalaty abhiśastaś cet sthānād anyatra dahyate |
adagdhaṃ taṃ vidur devāḥ tasya bhūyo ‘pi dāpayet ||
sthānād anyatra – hastābhyām anyatrety arthaḥ | prajāpatis tu |
antarā patite piṇḍe sandehe vā punar bhavet |
hṛte tu mardayet paścāt vrīhīn vāpy atha vā yavān ||
yadā gacchato ‘ntarā – madhye aṣṭamamaṇḍalād arvāg eva piṇḍaḥ patitaḥ vrīhiyavavimardane kṛte daṇḍyādaṇḍyatvanirṇayābhāvena saṃdeho bhavati tadā punar bhavet | dvitīyaprayoge ‘pi yadā saṃdeho jāyate | tadā punaḥpunar hared iti vacanābhāve ‘pi nyāyataḥ prāptaṃ dhāraṇam | yathāha vijñānayogī “punaḥ punar hared ity arthaprāptam uktam” (on YDh 2.107cd) iti | atra śuddhasya rājñā viśuddhipatraṃ deyam | aśuddhasya daṇḍapūrvakaṃ tyāga eva | tatra nāradaḥ |
anena vidhinā dharyo hutāśanasamaḥ sadā |
ṛte grīṣmād ahar vyaktakāle ‘nyasmin suśītale || iti |
hutāśanasamas taptāyaḥpiṇḍaḥ |
ity agnividhiḥ
jalavidhiḥ
atha jalavidhir ucyate | atra candrikāyām – jalavidhir viṣavidhiś ca pratipāditau | yathoktaṃ candrikākāreṇa – [200] jalaviṣayor utsannānuṣṭhānatvāt tadvidhim anākhyāya kośavidhir ucyata iti | utkalādiṣu kvacid deśeṣu jalavidher eva prāmāṇikatvena vyavahriyamāṇatvāt | śūrasenamāgadhādiṣu kvacid deśeṣu viṣavidher eva prāmāṇikatvena parigṛhītatvāt | ataḥ kramaprāpto jalavidhir ucyate | tatra prajāpatiḥ |
toyasyātha pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ dharmyaṃ purātanam |
ṛtānṛta(śubhāśubha)parīkṣārthaṃ brahmaṇā vihitaṃ svayam ||
śarān vai pūjayed bhaktyā vaiṣṇavaṃ ca dhanus tathā |
maṇḍayet puṣpadhūpaiś ca tataḥ karma samārabhet || iti |
tatra kātyāyanaḥ |
śarāṃś cānāyasair agraiḥ prakurvīta viśuddhaye |
veṇukāṇḍamayaiś caiva kṣeptā ca sudṛḍhaḥ kṣipet ||
kṣeptā kṣatriya eva –
kṣeptā tu kṣatriyaḥ proktaḥ tadvad abrāhmaṇo ‘pi vā |
akrūrahṛdayaḥ śāntaḥ sopavāsas tataḥ kṣipet ||
iti pitāmahavacanāt | atra nāradaḥ |
krūraṃ dhanuḥ saptaśataṃ madhyamaṃ ṣaṭśataṃ smṛtam |
mandaṃ pañcaśataṃ proktaṃ eṣa jñeyo dhanurvidhiḥ ||
saptaśatam iti | aṅgulānāṃ saptādhikaṃ śataṃ krūram ity arthaḥ | evaṃ ṣaṭśataṃ pañcaśatam |
madhyamena tu cāpena prakṣipec ca śaratrayam |
hastānāṃ tu śate sārdhe lakṣyaṃ kṛtvā vicakṣaṇaḥ ||
nyūnādhike tu doṣaḥ syāt kṣipataḥ sāyakāṃs tathā ||
[201] iti |
nātikrūreṇa dhanuṣā kṣepayitvā śaratrayam |
pānīyamajjanaṃ kāryaṃ śaṅkā yatra vijñāyate ||
yatra śaṅkā vijñāyate tam abhiyuktam ity arthaḥ | lakṣanirdeśārthaṃ toraṇaṃ kāryam | tathāha sa eva |
toraṇaṃ tatra kartavyaṃ lakṣyalakṣaṇasaṃdhaye |
lakṣyalakṣaṇayoḥ saṃdhir abhisaṃdhiḥ – parijñānam iti yāvat | tat toraṇaṃ ca nimajjanasthānasamīpe samadeśe śodhyakarṇapramāṇocchritaṃ kāryam | yathāha nāradaḥ |
gatvā tu tajjalasthānaṃ taṭe toraṇam ucchritam |
kurvīta karmamātraṃ tu bhūmibhāge same śucau || iti |
atra nāradaḥ |
brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyo rāgadveṣavivarjitaḥ |
nābhimātrajale sthāpya puruṣaṃ stambhavat sthale ||
tasyorū pratisaṃgṛhya nimajjed abhiśāpavān |
atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
satyena mābhirakṣa tvaṃ varuṇety abhiśāpya kam |
nābhidadhnodakasthasya gṛhītvorū jalaṃ viśet || iti |
kaṃ – udakam | satyena mābhirakṣa tvaṃ varuṇety mantrataḥ abhiśāpo ‘bhimantraṇam | jalaṃ viśed iti nimajjed ity arthaḥ | etac ca varuṇapūjāyāṃ satyām | yathāha nāradaḥ |
gandhamālyaiḥ surabhibhiḥ madhukṣīraghṛtādibhiḥ |
varuṇāya prakurvīta pūjām ādau samāhitaḥ ||
[202] iti | tathā sarvadivyasādhāraṇadharmeṣu dharmāvāhanādisakaladevatāpūjāhomamantraka-pratijñāpatraśironiveśanānteṣu satsu ca | tathā –
toya tvaṃ prāṇināṃ prāṇaḥ sṛṣṭer ādyaṃ tu kāraṇam |
śuddheś ca kāraṇaṃ proktaṃ dravyāṇāṃ dehināṃ tathā ||
atas tvaṃ darśayātmānaṃ śubhāśubhaparīkṣaṇam | iti |
prāḍvivākenodakābhimantraṇe kṛte śodhyaḥ satyena mābhirakṣa tvaṃ varuṇety udakaṃ prārthayed iti | atra prajāpatiḥ |
tṛṇaśaivālarahite jalūkāmatsyavarjite |
nābhimātre jale majjen na cāgādhe na cālpake ||
āviśed amale nityam ūrmipaṅkavivarjite |
āhāryaṃ varjayet toyaṃ śīghragāsu nadīṣu ca ||
āhāryam – dhaṭādibhir uddhṛtam | anena alpavegāsu nadīṣu hradeṣu sarastaṭākādau ca yat toyaṃ muktadoṣaṃ tat viśodhakam ity arthād uktaṃ bhavati |
sthāpayet prathamaṃ toye stambhavat puruṣaṃ nṛpaḥ |
āśritya yaṃ nimajjanti mānavāḥ śuddhikāṅkṣiṇaḥ || iti |
atra smṛtyantare viśeṣa uktaḥ |
udake prāṅmukhas tiṣthed dharmasthūṇāṃ pragṛhya ca | iti |
atra śaṅkhalikhitau: “nābhipramāṇodakastho yajñīyavṛkṣodbhavāṃ dharmasthūṇām avaṣṭabhya prāṅmukhas tiṣṭhet” | tasyorū – abhimatapuruṣasya ūrū iti | abhimatapuruṣaḥ pūrvoktalakṣaṇaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
iṣūn na prakṣiped vidvān mārute vāti vai bhṛśam |
[203]        viṣame bhūpradeśe ca vṛkṣasthānusamākule || iti |
atra pitāmahaḥ |
gantuś cāpi ca kartuś ca samaṃ gamanamajjanam |
gacchet toraṇamūlaṃ tu lakṣyasthānāj jayī (javī?) naraḥ ||
asmin gate dvitīyo ‘pi vegād ādāya sāyakam |
gacchet toramūlaṃ tu tataḥ sa puruṣo gataḥ ||
āgatas tu śaragrāhī na paśyati yadā jale |
antarjalagataṃ samyak tadāśuddhiṃ vinirdiśet ||
anyathā na viśuddhaḥ syād ekāṅgasyāpi darśane |
sthānāc cānyatra gamanād yasmin pūrve niveśitaḥ ||
ekāṅgasyāpīti karṇādyabhiprāyam |
śiromātraṃ tu dṛśyeta na karṇau na ca nāsikā |
apsu praveśane yasya śuddhaṃ tam abhinirdiśet ||
iti viśeṣābhidhānāt | atra jayasvarūpanirdhāraṇam āha nāradaḥ |
pañcāśato yāvakānāṃ yau syātām adhikau jale |
tau tatra viniyoktavyau śarānayanakarmaṇi ||
tatrāyaṃ prayogakramaḥ – prāḍvivāko jalāśayasaṃnidhau toraṇaṃ vidhāya uktapramāṇadeśaṃ lakṣyaṃ nidhāya toraṇasaṃnidhau dhanuḥśarān saṃpūjya jalāśaye varuṇam āvāhya pūjayitvā tattīre dharmādidevānāṃ āvāhanādyupacārair iṣṭvā śodhyaśirasi pratijñāpatram ābadhya prāḍvivāka eva “toyatvaṃ prāṇinaḥ prāṇaḥ” ityādimantreṇa jalam abhimantrya satyenetyādimantreṇa śodhyakartṛkajalābhimantraṇānantare [204] nābhimātrodakāvasthitasya balīyasaḥ puruṣasya dharmasthūṇāyā vā samīpaṃ śodhyaṃ prāpayet | tadanantaraṃ triṣu śreṣu mukteṣu eko vegavān madhyamaśarapātaṃ gatvā tad ādāya tatraiva tiṣṭhati | anyas tu puruṣo vegavān śaramokṣaṇasthāne toraṇamūle tiṣthati | evaṃ sthitayoḥ tṛtīyasyāṃ karatālikāyāṃ viśodhyo nimajjati | tatsamakālam eva toraṇamūle sthito ‘pi drutataraṃ madhayaśarapātasthānaṃ gacchati | śaragrāhī ca tasmin prāpte drutataraṃ toraṇamūlaṃ prāpyāntarjalagataṃ yadi na paśyati tadā śuddho bhavatīti | sārdhaśatahastaparimitadeśe lakṣyaṃ kṛtvā javinoḥ puruṣayoḥ lakṣyatoraṇaparyantaṃ pradhāvanānudhāvane kārye | sārdhaśatahastaparimitadeśanyūnādhikabhāve doṣaśravaṇād etad eva pradhānam iti kecit | dhanuḥśarādipūjāsmaraṇāt tin(tan?)niyamārtham ity apare |
iti jalavidhiḥ
atha viṣavidhiḥ
atha viṣavidhir ucyate | tatra prajāpatiḥ |
viṣasyādhya pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ cakṣaṇacoditam |
abhiyuktāya dātavyaṃ yavamātraṃ pradīyate ||
yavāḥ sapta pradātavyā atha vā ṣaṭ ca saṃkyayā |
śṛṅgiṇo vatsanābhasya haimasya ca viṣasya ca ||
cāritāni ca jīrṇāni kṛtrimāṇi tathaiva ca |
bhūmijātāni sarvāṇi viṣāṇi parivarjayet ||
nāradas tu |
bhraṣṭaṃ ca cāritaṃ caiva dhūpitaṃ miśritaṃ tathā |
[205]        kālakūṭam alābhuṃ ca viṣaṃ yatnena varjayet || iti |
cāritaṃ – caritvāvaśiṣṭam |
auṣadhāny api ratnāni mantrayogāt viṣāpahān |
kartuḥ śarīrasaṃsthāṃś ca parīkṣeta mahīpatiḥ || iti |
saptādisaṃkhyāvyavasthā ṛtubhedena draṣṭavyā | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
varṣe caturyavamātrā graiṣme pañca yavāḥ smṛtāḥ |
haimantake sapta yavāḥ śarad alpā tato ‘pi ca ||
dadyād dhi sopavāsāya devabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhau | iti |
alpā – ṣaḍyavety arthaḥ | tata ity anena saptasaṃkhyāparāmarśāt | hemanta ity anena śiśirasyāpi grahaṇam | hemantaśiśirayoḥ samāsena hemantaśiśirāvṛt prīṇāmīty ādau prāyaśaḥ sahabhāvena śravaṇāt | “vasantasya sarvadivyasādhāraṇatvāt tatrāpi sapta yavāḥ” iti vijñāneśaḥ |(on YDh 2.211cd) yavapramāṇaṃ tu |
jālāntaragate bhānau yat sūkṣmaṃ dṛśyate rajaḥ |
prathamaṃ tat pramāṇānāṃ rasareṇuṃ pracakṣate ||
trasareṇavo ‘ṣṭa vijñeyā likṣaikā parimāṇataḥ |
tā rājasarṣapās tisraḥ te trayo gaurasarṣapaḥ ||
sarṣapāḥ ṣaḍyavo madhyaḥ triyavāḥ kṛṣṇalāḥ smṛtāḥ |
pañcakṛṣṇalako māṣaḥ te suvarṇaṃ tu ṣoḍaśa || iti |
hārītena viśeṣāntaram uktam |
pūrvāhṇe śītale deśe viṣaṃ deyaṃ ghṛtaplutam |
[206] iti | atra pūrvāhṇaśabdaḥ parisaṃkhyāparaḥ,
nāparāhṇe na madhyāhne na saṃdhyāyāṃ tu dharmavit |
iti kālāntaraniṣedhasmaraṇāt | ghṛtaṃ ca viṣāt triṃśadguṇaṃ grāhyam,
pūrvāhṇe śītale deśe viṣaṃ deyaṃ hi dehinām |
ghṛtena yojitaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ ghṛtaṃ triṃśadguṇānvitam ||
iti kātyāyanasmaraṇāt | pitāmahena prayogakrama uktaḥ |
sopavāsāya deyaṃ syād viṣaṃ brāhmaṇasaṃnidhau |
sūpopahāramantraiś ca pūjayitvā maheśvaram ||
devānāṃ saṃnidhau caiva dakṣiṇābhimukhe sthite |
udaṅmukha prāṅmukho vā dadyād vipraḥ samāhitaḥ || iti |
tad ayam atra niṣkarṣaḥ – prāḍvivākaḥ sopavāso mahādevaṃ ṣoḍaśopacārair abhyarcya tatpurataḥ śārṅgaṃ haimavataṃ vā viṣaṃ ghṛtaplutaṃ saṃsthāpya dharmādyāvāhanādiśraḥpatrāropaṇāntaṃ divyamātṛkāntaṃ kṛtvā viṣam abhimantrya dakṣiṇābhimukhāya svayaṃ prāṅmukha udaṅmukho vā viṣaṃ prayacchati | sa ca śodhyo viṣam abhimantrya bhakṣayed iti kramaḥ | prāḍvivākakartṛkaviṣābhimantraṇamantraḥ –
tvaṃ viṣa brahmaṇā sṛṣṭa parīkṣārthaṃ durātmanām |
pāpānāṃ darśayātmānaṃ śuddhānām amṛtaṃ bhava ||
mṛtyumūrtir viṣa tvaṃ hi brahmaṇā parikīrtitā |
trāyasvainaṃ naraṃ pāpāt satyenāsyāmṛtaṃ bhava || iti |
śodhyakartṛkaviṣābhimantraṇamantras tu –
tvaṃ viṣa brahmaṇaḥ putraḥ satye dharme vyavasthitaḥ |
trāyasvāsmān abhīśāpāt satyena bhava me ‘mṛtam ||
[207] iti | atra śodhyaś ca kuhakādibhyo rakṣaṇīya ity āha pitāmahaḥ |
trirātraṃ pañcarātraṃ vā puruṣaiḥ svair adhiṣṭhitam |
kuhakādibhayād rājā dhārayed divyakāriṇam || iti |
tathā viṣam api rakṣaṇīyam ity āha sa eva |
śārṅgaṃ haimavataṃ śastaṃ gandhavarṇarasānvitam |
akṛtrimam asaṃmūḍham amantropahitaṃ bhavet | iti |
uktaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭaṃ yathā bhavet tathā rakṣaṇīyam ity arthaḥ |
bhakṣite tu yadā svastho mūrchāchardivivarjitaḥ |
nirvikāro dinasyānte śuddhaṃ tam abhinirdiśet || iti |
etac ca dināntakālarūpakālapratīkṣaṇaṃ yāvatkāle viṣaṃ jīryati tāvanmātropalakṣakam, na tu niyāmakam | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
evam uktvā viṣaṃ śārṅgaṃ bhakṣayed dhimaśailajam |
yasya vegair vinā jīryec chuddhiṃ tasya vinirdiśet || iti |
yadā viṣam atimātraṃ prayuktaṃ syāt tadā karatālikāśatapañcakaparimitakālaṃ pratīkṣya tadanantaraṃ cikitsā kartavyety āha pitāmahaḥ |
pañcatālaśataṃ kālaṃ nirvikāro yadā bhavet |
tadā bhavati saṃśuddhaḥ tadā kuryāc cikitsikam || iti |
atra vijñānayoginoktam: “viṣe pīte yāvatkaratālaśatapañcakaṃ tāvat parīkṣaṇīyaḥ, anantaraṃ cikitsanīyaḥ” iti mukhyaṃ pratīkṣaṇakālam uktvā “nirvikāro dinasyānte ityādiśuddhivacanaṃ [208] svalpamātraviṣayam” iti | tan mandam |
evam uktvā viṣaṃ śārṅgaṃ bhakṣayed dhimaśailajam |
yasya vegair vinā jīryec chuddhiṃ tasya vinirdiśet ||
iti yājñavalkyavacane viṣajīraṇapratipādanam,
tadā bhavati saṃśuddhas tadā kuryāc cikitsitam |
iti pitāmahavacane cikitsāpratipadanam api virundhyāt | ataḥ cikitsāpratipādakavacanaṃ karatālikāśatapañcakāvacchinnakālapratīkṣaṇadvāreṇātimātraprayuktaviṣayam ity asmaduktaiva viṣayavyavasthā samyak | yac ca “viṣavego nāma dhātoḥ dhātvantaraprāptiḥ” (on YDh 2.111) ity uktaṃ vijñānayoginā, tat prāyikābhiprāyikam ity avagantavyam, ādyaviṣavegasyaivaṃrūpatvāsaṃbhavāt | tataś ca viṣasya dhātusaṃkrāmo vega ity etāvad vivakṣitaṃ vijñānayoginety avagantavyam | “tvagasṛṅmāṃsamedo’sthimajjāśuklāni dhātavaḥ” |
evaṃ suddhasya śuddhipatraṃ rājñā dātavyam | itarasya daṇḍādi kartavyam iti |
iti viṣavidhiḥ
atha kośavidhiḥ
prāḍvivākaḥ kṛtopavāso durgādityādīn ugradevān snāpayitvā tadudakaṃ tāmrapātrādau saṃgṛhya taddevān gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ saṃpūjya tatsnānodakaṃ divyadeśaṃ nītvā dharmāvāhanādi śirasi patrāntaṃ sarvadivyasādhāraṇavidhiṃ vidhāya śodhyaṃ ca prāṅmukhaṃ kṛtvā gomayānuliptamaṇḍalāntaḥsthitaṃ kṛtvā pūrvanihitodakāt prasṛtitrayaṃ śodhyaṃ pāyayet | tathā ca pitāmahaḥ |
prāṅmukhaṃ kāriṇaṃ kṛtvā pāyayet prasṛtitrayam |
[209]        pūrvoktena vidhānena snātam ārdrāmbaraṃ śucim ||
pūrvoktena – sarvadivyasādhāraṇyenoktena | kāriṇam – divyakāriṇam | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
devān ugrān samabhyarcya tatsnānodakam āharet |
saṃśrāvya pāyayet tasmāj jalāc ca prasṛtitrayam || iti |
nāradas tu |
tam āhūyābhiśaptaṃ tu maṇḍalābhyantarsthitam |
ādityābhimukhaṃ kṛtvā pāyayet prasṛtitrayam || iti |
hārītas tu |
gomayenānuliptāyāṃ bhūmau śodhyaṃ tu pāyayet | iti |
yājñavalkyas tu śuddhiprakāram āha |
arvāk caturdaśād adhno yasna no rājadaivikam |
vyasanaṃ jāyate ghoraṃ sa śuddhaḥ syān na saṃśayaḥ || iti |
ūrdhvaṃ punar avadher no doṣa ity āha nāradaḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ yasya dvisaptāhād vaikṛtaṃ tu mahad bhavet |
nābhiyojyaḥ sa viduṣā kṛtakālavyatikramāt || iti |
etan mahābhiyogaviṣayam, “mahābhiyogeṣv etāni” iti prastutyābhidhānāt | avadhyantarāṇi pitāmahenoktāny alpaviṣayāṇi, “kośam alpe tu dāpayet” iti smaraṇāt |
trirātrāt saptarātrād vā daśāhād vā dvisaptakāt |
vaikṛtaṃ yasya dṛśyeta pāpakṛt sa udāhṛtaḥ || iti |
dvisaptakād iti yājñavalkyavacanena samānārthatvān mahābhiyogaviṣayam | etad evoktaṃ vijñānayoginā “mahābhiyogoktadravyād [210] arvācīnadravyaṃ tredhā vibhajya trirātrādipakṣatrayaṃ vyavasthāpanīyam” (on YDh 2.113) iti | daivikāny āha pitāmahaḥ |
kṣayātisāravisphoṭavātāsthiparipīḍanam |
netrarug jalarogāś ca tathonmādaḥ prajāyate ||
śirorug bhujabhaṅgaś ca vyādhayo daivikā nṛṇām || iti |
rājakam – rājadaṇḍaḥ | yathāha viṣṇuḥ |
yasya paśyed dvisaptāhāt trisaptāhāt tathāpi vā |
rogo ‘gnir jñātimaraṇaṃ rājadaṇḍam athāpi vā ||
tam aśuddhaṃ vijānīyāt tathā śuddhaṃ viparyayāt |
rājadaṇḍaśabdaḥ arthabhraṃśadhanakayayor upalakṣakaḥ |
rogo ‘gnir jñātimaraṇam arthabhraṃśo dhanakṣayaḥ |
