iti nāradokteḥ | “arthaśabdaḥ putrādivacanaḥ” iti candrikākāraḥ | ata eva svīyasyāpi janasya sarvasya madhye yadā yasya kasyacid vaikṛtaṃ bhavet sa hīyata ity āha pitāmahaḥ |
na tasyaikasy kiṃ tv evaṃ sarvasya yadi tad bhavet |
rogo ‘gnir jñātimaraṇaṃ saiva tasya vibhāvanam ||
pitāmahena viśeṣa uktaḥ |
bhakto yo yasya devasya pāyayet tasya taṃ naram |
samabhāve tu devānām ādityasya tu pāyayet ||
durgāyāḥ pāyayec corān ye ca śastropajīvinaḥ |
bhāskarasya tu tat toyaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ tan na pāyayet ||
durgāyāḥ snāpayec chūlam ādityasya tu maṇḍalam |
itareṣāṃ tu devānāṃ snāpayed āyudhāni tu || [211]
atra niṣkarṣaḥ – ugradevābhiṣekajalaṃ prāḍvivāko jaladivyaprakaraṇoktena “toya tvaṃ prāṇināṃ prāṇaḥ” ity anena mantreṇābhimantrya abhimantraṇānantaraṃ śodhyena “satyena mābhirakṣa tvaṃ varuṇa” ity abhimantritaṃ prasṛtitrayaṃ pāyayet | saṃśrāvyety ubhayāmantraṇam ucyate | ata eva nāradaḥ |
ādityābhimukhaṃ kṛtvā toyaṃ saṃśrāvya pāyayet \
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
saṃśrāvya pāyayet tasmāj jalāt tu prasṛtitrayam | iti |
kiṃ saṃśrāvyety apekṣite, nādadaḥ |
enaś ca śrāvayet pātuḥ pāyayet prasṛtitrayam | iti |
enaḥ – pāpm | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ |
visaṃvaden naro [visaṃvāde naro] lobhāc chalī bhavati durmatiḥ |
smṛtyantare ‘pi |
ātmanaḥ kāmakāreṇa kośaṃ pītvā visaṃvadet |
daridro vyādhito mūḍhaḥ saptajanmani jāyate ||
iti |
evaṃ śrāvatayaḥ śodhyaḥ śrutvedaṃ na mayā kṛtam |
iti vacan bhavet | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “ugradevān samabhyarcya tatsnānodakāt prasṛtitrayaṃ pibet | idaṃ mayā na kṛtam iti vyāhared devatābhimukham” iti | evaṃ kṛte pūrvoktakāleṣu vikārābhāve śuddho bhavati | anyathā daṇḍa ity vagantavyam |
iti kośavidhiḥ
atha taṇḍulavidhiḥ
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
sopavāsaḥ sūryagrahe taṇḍulān bhakṣayec chuciḥ |
śuddhiḥ syāc chuklaniṣṭhīve raktamiśre tu doṣakṛt ||
atra kevalaraktād api doṣabhāktvaṃ caśabdārthaḥ | atra divyasādhāraṇadharmāvāhānādihoma-śiraḥpatrāropaṇādikaṃ kṛtvā ārdravāsasaḥ śodhyasya taṇḍulabhakṣaṇaṃ kāryam | tat tu cauryābhiyoga eva yathāha pitāmahaḥ |
taṇḍulānāṃ pravakṣyāmi vidhiṃ bhakṣaṇacoditam |
caurye tu taṇḍulā deyā nānyasminn iti niścayaḥ ||
taṇḍulān kṣālayec chubhrān śāleyāny asya kasyacit |
mṛnmaye bhājane kṛtvā ādityasyāgrataḥ śuciḥ ||
snānodakena saṃmiśrān rātrau tatrādhivāsayet |
prāṅmukhaṃ doṣiṇaṃ snātaṃ śiroroptapatrakam ||
taṇḍulān bhakṣayitvā tu patre niṣṭhīvayet tataḥ |
pippalasya tu nānyasya abhāve bhūrja eva yat ||
lohitaṃ yadi dṛśyeta hanus tālu ca śīryate |
gātraṃ ca kampate yasya tam aśuddhaṃ vinirdiśet || iti |
bhakṣayitveti svārthe ṇyantād bhakṣidhātor hetur māṇṇic | anyathā nirdiśed ity asya bhakṣayitvety asya ca smanārthatvapratīter bhakṣaṇaṃ prāḍvivākasyaiva syān na śodhyasyeti hetumaṇṇijeva samyak | ādityasyeti pratimārūpasyādityasya, rātrau tatrādhivāsayed iti svārasyāt | tatratyety ādityagṛhe | śālivyatiriktataṇḍulaniṣedhas tv adṛṣṭārthaḥ | atra candrikākāraḥ: [213]
“caurye tu taṇḍulā deyā nānyasminn iti niścayaḥ |
iti | atrānyasmin – strīsaṃgrahaṇād anyataravivāde, na dhanavivāde | dhanavivāde tu cauryād anyatrāpi taṇḍulā deyāḥ | “tadardhārdhasya taṇḍulā” iti kātyāyenoktatvāt | na ca tadvacanaṃ cauryaviṣayam evāstv iti vācyam, dattasyāpahnavo yatrety upakramavirodhāpatteḥ” ity āha |
iti taṇḍulavidhiḥ
atha taptamāṣavidhiḥ
atra pitāmahaḥ |
taptamāṣasya vakṣyāmi vidhim uddharaṇe śubham |
kārayed āyasaṃ pātraṃ tāmraṃ vā ṣoḍaśāṅgulam ||
caturaṅgulamātraṃ tu mṛnmayaṃ vātha maṇḍalam |
pūrayed ghṛtatailābhyāṃ viṃśatyā vā palais tu tat ||
gavyaṃ ghṛtam upādāya tad agnau tāpayec chuciḥ|
maṇḍalam – parimaṇḍalam – vartulam iti yāvat | ghṛtatailābhyāṃ pūrayed ekaḥ pakṣaḥ | gavyaghṛtena vā pūrayitvā laukikam agniṃ divyadeśe pratiṣṭhāpya tatra tāpayet | pakṣadvaye ‘pi tāpe vartamāne dharmāvāhanādisodhyaśiraḥpatrāropaṇāntasarvadivyasādhāraṇavidhiṃ vidadhyāt | ghṛtatailapūraṇapakṣe pitāmahena viśeṣa uktaḥ |
suvarṇamāṣakaṃ tasmin sutapte nikṣipet tataḥ |
aṅguṣṭhāṅguliyogena uddharet taptamāṣakam ||
tasminn iti – ghṛtatailābhyāṃ pūrite pātre –
karāgraṃ yo na dhunuyād visphoṭo vā na jāyate |
śuddho bhavatu dharmeṇa nirvikārakarāṅguliḥ || [214]
ity atra śuddhividhānam uddhared iti vacanāt pātrād utkṣepaṇamātra eva, na bahiḥprakṣepaṇe | gavyaghṛtapūraṇapakṣe pitāmahena viśeṣa uktaḥ |
suvarṇe rājate tāmra āyase mṛnmaye ‘pi vā |
gavyaṃ ghṛtam upādāya tad agnau tāpayec chuciḥ ||
sauvarṇīṃ rājatīṃ tāmrīm āyasīṃ vā suśobhitām |
salilena sakṛd dhautāṃ mudrikāṃ tatra nikṣipet ||
bhramadvīcītaraṅgāḍhye na nakhasparśagocare |
parīkṣetārdrapatreṇa carukāraṃ saghoṣakam ||
tataś cānena mantreṇa sakṛt tad api mantrayet |
paraṃ pavitram amṛtaṃ ghṛta tvaṃ yajñakarmasu ||
daha pāvaka pāpaṃ tvaṃ himaśītaṃ śucer bhava |
śuceḥ – śuddhasya | pāpam – pāpakarmāṇam | anantarakṛtyam āha prajāpatiḥ |
upoṣitaṃ tataḥ snātam ārdravāsasam āgatam |
grāhayen mudrikāṃ tāṃ tu ghṛtamadhyagatāṃ tathā ||
pradeśinīṃ ca tasyātha parīkṣeyuḥ parīkṣakāḥ |
yasya visphoṭakā na syuḥ śuddho ‘sāv anyathāśuciḥ || iti |
pradeśiṇīṃ prīkṣeyur iti vacanāt pradeśiṇyaiva mudrikoddhāraṇam | atra prayogakramaḥ –
dharmāvāhanādiśiraḥpatrāntaṃ sarvadivyasādhāraṇaṃ vidhiṃ vidhāya daha pāvaketi ghṛtānumantraṇamantreṇa prāḍvivāko tailaṃ gavyaghṛtaṃ vābhimantrya tvam agne sarvabhūtānām iti mantreṇāgnyabhimantraṇaṃ kṛtavataḥ śodhyasya mudrikātaptamāṣayor uddharaṇānte pradeśinīṃ parīkṣakāḥ [215] parīkṣeyur iti | taptamāṣoddharaṇe parvāṅgalīparīkṣaṇam | mudrikoddharaṇe tu pradeśinīmātraparīkṣaṇam iti vivekaḥ |
iti taptamāṣavidhiḥ
atha phālavidhiḥ
atha phālavidhir ucyate | tatra bṛhaspatiḥ |
āyasaṃ dvādaśapalaṃ ghaṭitaṃ phālam ucyate |
aṣṭāṅgulaṃ bhaved dīrghaṃ caturaṅgulavistaram ||
agnivarṇaṃ tu tac coto jihvayā lelihet sakṛt |
adagdhaś cec chucir bhūyād anyathā tu sa hīyate ||
atra coragrahaṇaṃ śodhyopalakṣaṇārtham iti candrikākāraḥ | vijñāneśvara-varadarājaprabhṛtayas tu phālavidhiś corasyaiva, na śodhyamatrasyeti vadanti | atra vidvāṃso vidāṃ kurvantu | tatrāyaṃ paryogakramaḥ – uktaparimāṇaṃ phālaṃ divyadeśe pratiṣṭhitāgnau nikṣipye dharmāvāhanādi śodhyaśirasi patrāropaṇāntaṃ kṛtvā sarvadivyasādhāraṇe ‘nuṣṭhite sarvaṃ kuryāt |
iti phālavidhiḥ
atha dharmavidhiḥ
atra pitāmahaḥ |
adhunā saṃpravakṣyāmi dharmādharmaparīkṣaṇam |
rājataṃ kārayed dharmam adharmaṃ sīsakāyasam ||
sīsakamiśram āyasam iti kecit | sīsakaṃ vā āyasaṃ vety apare ||
hantṝṇāṃ yācamānānāṃ prāyaścittārthināṃ nṛṇām |
hantṝṇām iti sāhasābhiyogeṣu | prāyaścittārthinām iit – pātakābhiyogeṣu | pakṣāntaram āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
[216]        likhed bhūrjapuṭe vāpi dharmādharmau sitāsitau |
abhyukṣya pañcagavyena gandhamālyaiḥ samarcayet ||
sitapuṣpas tu dharmaḥ syād adharmo ‘sitapuṣpadhṛt |
evaṃ vidhāyopalipya piṇḍayos tāni dhāpayet ||
gomayena mṛtā vāpi piṇḍau kāryau samau tataḥ |
mṛdbhāṇḍake ‘nupahate sthāpyau cānupalakṣitau ||
upalipte śucau deśe devabrāhmaṇasaṃnidhau |
samarcayet tato devān lokapālāṃś ca pūrvavat ||
dharmāvāhanapūrvaṃ tu pratijñāpatrakaṃ likhet |
yadi pāpavimukto ‘haṃ darmaś cāyātu me kare || iti |
abhiśasto ‘bhimantrayet |
abhiśastas tayoś caikaṃ pragṛhṇītāvilambitaḥ |
dharme gṛhīte śuddhiḥ syād adharme tu sa hīyate || iti |
prajāpatinā viśeṣa uktaḥ |
vastradvaye lekhanīyau dharmdharmau sitāsitau |
japahomādikair mantraiḥ gāyatryā sahitais tathā ||
āmantrya pūjayed gandhaiḥ kusumaiś ca sitāsitaiḥ |
abhyukṣya pañcagavyena mṛtpiṇḍāntaritau tataḥ ||
samau kṛtvā tu mṛtpiṇḍau sthāpyau cānupalakṣitau |
tataḥ kumbhāt piṇḍam ekaṃ pragṛhṇītāvilambitaḥ ||
dharme gṛhīte suddhiḥ syāt saṃpūjyeta parīkṣakaiḥ |
adharme tu gṛhīte tu daṇḍyo nirvāsya eva ca ||
iti dharmavidhiḥ
[217]
tāni divyāni |
atha śapathāḥ
te ca manvādibhir uktā iti pūrvamevoktāḥ | tathāpi vispaṣṭārtham ucyate | yathāha viṣṇuḥ |
niṣke tu satyavacanaṃ dviniṣke pādalambhanam |
trikād arvāk chiraḥ puṣpaṃ kośadānam ataḥ param ||
satyena śāpayed vipraṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāhanāyudhaiḥ |
gobījakāñcanair vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃsarvais tu bhedakaiḥ ||
ityādayaḥ | atra śuddhivibhāvanā manunoktā |
na cārtim ṛcchait kṣipraṃ sa jñeyah śapathaiḥ śuciḥ | iti |
ārtiḥ – pīḍā “yasya no rājadaivikam” ity uktā | kālaniyamaś ca trirātraprabhṛtikālagaurava-lāghavaparyālocanayā draṣṭavyaḥ | yady api śapathānām api divyaśabdavācyatvam asti, tathāpi kālāntaravinirṇayanimittatvena samantaranimittebhyo dhaṭādibhyo bhedena vyapadeśaḥ smṛtiṣu brāhmaṇaparivrājakanyāyena kṛtaḥ | atrāpi viśeṣam āha hārītaḥ |
kośataṇḍuladharmāṃs tu dharmasaṃbhavam eva ca |
putradārādiśapathān sarvakālaṃ prayojayet ||
nanv ākrośābhāvaviśiṣṭā likhitādayaḥ pramāṇapadavīm agāhanta ity uktam | tan na saṃgacchate | ākrośābhāvasya sarvadā sākṣisādhyatvaniścayābhāvāt | divyenaiva sādhyatve tatra mānuṣaṃ pramāṇaṃ niravakāśaṃ syāt tathāi – ākrośābhāvaḥ sākṣisādhyo na bhavati | kenacit kadācit kathaṃcid api kṛtasyākrośasya sarvathā carmacakṣuṣāṃ divyajñānaśūnyānāṃ niścetum aśakyatvāt | kiṃ tu [218] devadattaḥ svayam evārthī na kvacit kadācit kenacit kathaṃcid apy āsedhanīya iti kṛtvā svayam evāsedhābhāvaṃ jānāti | tathaiva pratyarpya sarvaprakāreṇāsedhya iti sa evāsedhyaṃ samyag jānāti |
nanv āsedhaḥ sasākṣikaḥ | tādṛśasākṣyabhāvād evāsedhābhāvaḥ samyag udbhāvayituṃ śakyaḥ | tādṛśasākṣyabhāvād evāsedhābhāvaḥ samyag udbhāvayituṃ śakyaḥ |
maivam | na hy abhāvasya sākṣitvām kvacid dṛṣṭam |
nanv anupalabdhipramāṇad evāsedhābhāvaḥ sādhayituṃ śakyate | liṅgānām anupalabdhipramāṇānugrāhakatayā prāmāṇikam evāsedhābhāvajñānam,
iti cen na | sākṣyanupalabdhyā sākṣyābhāva eva jñātuṃ śakyo nāsedhābhāvaḥ vaiyadhikaraṇyābhāvāt |
nanu sākṣyanupalabdhyā sakṣyabhāvo nirṇīyate tena sākṣyabhāvena āsedhābhāvo nirṇetuṃ śakyata ity cet,
satyam | sasākṣikāsedhasthale prāmāṇikatvam āsedhābhāvasādhanasya bhavatu | asākṣikāsedhasthale tu pūrvoktam evāvatiṣṭhata iti na kaścid virodhaḥ | kiṃcāsedhānāṃ sasākṣikatvaprasiddhiḥ prāyikābhiprāyā | loke āsedhāḥ sasākṣikā asākṣikā api santi | dṛśyante ca laukikavyavahāreṣu | āsedhagamakasākṣyabhāvād evāsedhābhāva iti niścaye dharmasya tattvaṃ nihitaṃ guhāyāṃ īśvara eva jānātīti sa sākṣikadharmavyāhatiḥ syāt |
nanu sarveṣāṃ mānuṣavigrahvatām āśayadoṣasadbhāve sasākṣike ‘pi prāmāṇike vyavahāre sākṣiṇāṃ vā lekhakādīnāṃ vā mūlasvamino vāpy āśaye katipayāviśeṣā udbhāvayitum aśakyā iti sarvatra divyam eva pravartate | ato mānuṣapramāṇaṃ niravakaśaṃ syād iti cen,
maivam | mānuṣapramāṇānāṃ pariśuddhānāṃ sadbhāve ‘py āśayadoṣodbhāvanadvārā teṣāṃ mūlaśaithilym ādādya divyāṅgīkārair nirṇetṛbhiḥ sabhyaiḥ prāḍvivākena ca vyavahārasya sottaratvam [219] āpādyāvaṣṭambhena nirṇayaḥ kartavyaḥ | ata evoktaṃ pitāmahena |
sottarānuttaratvena vyavahāro dvidhā mataḥ | iti |
etad vyācaṣṭe nāradaḥ: “sapaṇāpaṇabhedād vyavahāraḥ” iti | dviprakāra iti śeṣaḥ | ataś ca mānuṣapramāṇadūṣaṇārtham āśayadoṣōdghāṭanena divyāvalambane paṇabandhaṃ datvā vyavahāram avaṣṭambhāvaśyaṃ divyaṃ deyam eveti dhyeyam |
nirṇayakṛtyam
atra nirṇayakṛtyam āha saṃgrahakāraḥ |
uktaprakārarūpeṇa svamatasthāpitāḥ kriyāḥ |
rājñā parīkṣya satyaiś ca sthāpyau jayaparājayau ||
yo yathoktānyatamayā kriyayārthaṃ prasādhayet |
bhāṣākṣarsamaṃ sādhyaṃ sa jayī parikīrtitaḥ ||
asādhayan sādhayitvā viparītārtham ātmanaḥ |
dṛṣṭakāraṇadoṣo vā yaḥ punaḥ sa parājayī || iti |
vyāsaḥ |
jitaṃ tu daṇḍayed rājā jetuḥ pūjāṃ pravartayet |
pūjā ca gandhamālyavastrādinā kāryā |
jetuḥ pravartayet pūjāṃ gandhamālyāmbarādibhiḥ |
iti smṛteḥ | yat tu kātyāyanoktam,
śatārdhaṃ dāpayec chuddham aśuddho daṇḍabhāg bhavet | iti |
yad api viṣṇunoktam: “etair divyajayāvadhāraṇe śatārdhaṃ daṇḍyaḥ śuddhaḥ | aśuddho daṇḍabhāk” iti, tat tu śuddhasya daṇḍavidhānaṃ sapaṇavyavahāra eva | [220] yathā samanantaram eva viṣṇuḥ: “sottara eva vyavahāre śuddhasya daṇḍaḥ” iti | sa tu daṇḍo ‘rthadaṇḍa ity arthasiddham | aśuddhasya daṇḍaprakāram āha kātyāyanaḥ |
viṣe toye hutāśe ca tulākośe ca taṇḍule |
taptamāṣakadivye ca kramād daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet ||
sahasraṃ ṣāṭchataṃ caiva tathā pañcaśatāni tu |
catustridvyekam evaṃ ca hīnaṃ hīne(tu) ca kalpayet || iti |
“ayaṃ daṇḍo ‘nihnave bhāvito dadyāt’ ityuktadaṇḍenāpi samuccīyate” iti vijñāneśaḥ (on YDh 2.113) | sahasrādisaṃkhyāsaṃkhyeyatvaṃ paṇānām eveti bhāruciḥ |
pūjāpravartanānantarakṛtyam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
siddhenārthena saṃyojyo vādī satkārapūrvakam |
lekhyaṃ svahastasaṃyuktaṃ tasmai dadyāt tu pārthivaḥ || iti |
lekhyaṃ jayapatram ucyate | jayapatre ca yad vaktavyaṃ tad akhilaṃ lekhyavidhāv uktaṃ tatrānusaṃdheyam |
iti śrīvīragajapatigauḍeśvara-navakoṭikarṇāṭakaluburigeśvara-
jamunāpurādhīśvara-huśanasāhisuratrāṇaśaraṇarakṣaṇa-
śrīdurgāvarputra-paramapavitracaritra-rājādhirājarājaparameśvara-
śrīpratāparudramahādeva-mahārājaviracite
smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe pramāṇanirṇayo nāma
tṛtīyollāsaḥ [caturthollāsaḥ?]
[221]
pañcamollāsaḥ
atha aṣṭādaśapadāni
atha sarvāsu smṛtiṣu pramāṇanirūpaṇānantaram evāṣṭādaśapadanirūpaṇam | pramāṇanirūpaṇānantaraṃ prameyanirūpaṇaṃ nyāyyam iti tadanantaram eva aṣṭādaśapadākhyaṃ prameyaṃ nirūpyeta |
ṛṇādānam
tatra ṛṇādāna eva mānuṣadivyātmakasakalapramāṇasādhyatvena vyavahārasya nirūpaṇāt prathamyād uddeśakrameṇa prathamam ṛṇādānākhyaṃ vivādapadaṃ nirūpyate | ata evoktaṃ “teṣām ādyam ṛṇādānam” iti | atra ṛṇādānavidhiḥ saptavidhaḥ | īdṛśam ṛṇaṃ deyaṃ īdṛśam ṛṇam adeyam anenādhikāriṇā deyam asmin samaye deyam anena prakāreṇa deyam ity adhamarṇe pañcavidhaḥ | uttamarṇe tu dānavidhiḥ ādānavidhiś ceti dvividhaḥ | yathoktaṃ nāradena |
ṛṇaṃ deyam adeyaṃ ca yena yatra yadā bhavet |
dānagrahaṇadharmāś ca ṛṇādānavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
tatra ṛṇapradānapūrvakatvād itareṣāṃ prathamaṃ tatpradānaprakāram āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
aśītibhāgo vṛddhiḥ syāt māsi māsi sabandhake |
varṇakramāc ca tadvat tricatuḥpañcakam anyathā ||
viśvāsārtham adharmarṇenottamarṇe yad ādhīyate | tadādhir atra bandhakam ucyate | sabandhake prayoge prayuktadravyasyāśītitamo bhāgo vṛddhir dharmyā bhavati | tena paṇaviṃśatyāḥ paṇapādo māsi māsi vṛddhir bhavati | tathā ca manuḥ |
vasiṣthavacanaproktāṃ vṛddhiṃ vārdhuṣike śṛṇu |
[222]        pañcamāṣās tu viṃśatyā evaṃ dharmo na hīyate ||
tathā ca gautamaḥ |
kusīdavṛddhir dharmyā syād viṃśateḥ pāñcamāṣikī |
daśamāṣasya paṇatve paṇaviṃśatitamo bhāgo māṣaḥ | tadardhaṃ taṇḍulo bhavati | viṃśatimāṣasya paṇatve taṇḍulaparimitaṃ bhavati | ṣoḍaśamānatve paṇasya paṇasya daśaguṇaṃ kalpyam | anyathā – bandharahite prayoget | varṇānām – brāhmaṇādīnāṃ caturṇāṃ krameṇa dvicatuḥpañcakaṃ śataṃ dharmyaṃ bhavati | brāhmaṇe ‘dhamarṇe dvikaṃ śatam | kṣatriya ‘dhmarṇe trikaṃ śatam | vaiśya ‘dhamarṇa catuṣkaṃ śatam | śūdre ‘dhamarṇe pañcakam | māsi māsi bhavati | dvicatuḥpañcāsmin vṛddhiḥ dīyata iti – dvicatuḥpañcakaṃ śatam | etad uktaṃ bhavati – samānajātīye sarvatra trikaṃ śatam eva nyāyyam | tathā ca kṣatriyasya kṣatriye ‘dhamarṇe dvikaṃ śatam | vaiśye ‘dhamarṇe trikaṃ śatam | śūdre ‘dhamarṇe catuṣkaṃ śatam | tathā vaiśyasya vaiśye ‘dhamarṇe dvikaṃ śatam | śūdre ‘dhamarṇe trikaṃ śatam | tathā śūdrasya śūdre ‘dhamarṇe dvikaṃ śatam iti | anantaraṃ trikaṃ śatam ekāntare catuṣkaṃ śataṃ dharmyaṃ bhavatīti | tathā ca manuḥ |
dvikaṃ trikaṃ catuṣkaṃ ca pañcakaṃ ca śataṃ tathā |
māsasya vṛddhiṃ gṛhṇīyād varṇānām ānupūrvyaśaḥ || iti |
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
vṛddhiś caturvidhā proktā pañcadhānyaiḥ prakīrtitā |
ṣaḍvidhānyaiḥ samākhyātā tattvatas tān nibodhata ||
kāyikā kālikā caiva cakravṛddhis tathāparā |
kāritātha śikhāvṛddhir bhogalābhas tathaiva ca || [223]
kāyikādīnāṃ svarūpam āha sa eva |
kāyikā karmasaṃyuktā māsagrāhyā tu kālikā |
vṛddher vṛddhiś cakravṛddhiḥ kāritā ṛṇinā kṛtā ||
pratyahaṃ gṛhyate yā tu śikhāvṛddhis tu sā smṛtā |
śikheva vardhate nityaṃ śiraśchedān nivartate ||
mūle datte tathaivaiṣā śikhāvṛddhis tathā smṛtā |
gṛhāt toṣaḥ phalaṃ kṣetrād bhogalābhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || iti |
gṛhāt toṣa ity anena nivāsanibandhano gṛhasakāśāt saṃtoṣaḥ | kṣetrāt saṃbhūtadhānyādiphalaṃ ca bhogalābhākhyā vṛddhir iti | “pratyahaṃ gṛhyate” ity anena saṃpratipannadinasaṃkhyāviśiṣṭavṛddhigrahaṇāl lābho darśitaḥ | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
ekāntenaiva vṛddhiṃ tu śodhayed yatra carṇikam |
pratikālaṃ dadāty eva śikhāvṛddhis tu sā smṛtā ||
ṛṇinā svecchayā kṛtā vṛddhiḥ kāritā | svārthe ṇic | ata eva parapreraṇayā tu kāritā vṛddhiḥ niṣiddhaiveti candrikākāraḥ | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
ṛṇikena tu yā vṛddhir adhikā saṃprakalpitā |
āpatkālakṛtā nityaṃ dātavyā kāritā tu sā ||
anyathā kāritā vṛddhir na dātavyā kathaṃcana | iti |
ayam atra niṣkarṣaḥ – vṛddhir dvividhā, kṛtā kāritā ceti | kṛtā tūttamarṇādhamarṇābhyām | sā ca kāyikātibhedāt ṣaḍvidhā | kāritā tu āpatkālakṛtā uttamarṇādhamarṇavyatiriktair madhyasthair anāpatkāle ‘pi madhyasthaiḥ kṛtā evaṃ kāritavṛddhidvaye anāpatkālakṛtā [224] niṣiddhā | adharmyā – na grahītavyety arthaḥ |
kāyikāṃ bhogavṛddhiṃ ca kāritāṃ ca śikhātmikām |
catuṣṭayīṃ vṛddhim āhuś cakravṛddhyā tu pañcamīm ||
iti bṛhaspativacanasya tātparyam iti mantavyam | vṛddher api pratimāsaṃ mūlabhāvena punar vṛddhiś cakravṛddhiḥ | māsagrāhyā – pratimāsaṃ labhyā vṛddhiḥ kālikā | aśvamahiṣīdāsyādīn adhikṛtya vāhanadohanādikāyikavyāpāra eva yatra vṛddhitvena kalpitaḥ sā kāyiketi | paśvādidravyasya tu vṛddhyarthaṃ prayuktasya tadīyā santatir eva vṛddhir ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ: “santatis tu paśustrīṇām” iti | striyo – dāsyaḥ | santatyabhāve tu prayuktasya paśvādeḥ puṣṭir anāśo vā lābhaḥ | kṣīrārthināṃ paricaryārthinām ity arthaḥ | adhamarṇaviśeṣe pratimāsaṃ vṛddheḥ parimāṇāntaram āha sa eva |
kāntāragās tu śatakaṃ sāmudrā viṃśakaṃ śatam |
dadyur vā svakṛtāṃ vṛddhiṃ sarve sarvāsu jātiṣu ||
kāntāram araṇyaṃ gacchatīti kāntāragāḥ | samudraṃ gacchantīti sāmudrāḥ | tebhyo daśakaṃ śataṃ viṃśakaṃ śataṃ ca dharmyaṃ bhavati māsi māsīty anuṣajyate | kāritāyāṃ vṛddhau sarve brāhmaṇādayaḥ | ayam arthaḥ – kāntāragantṛbhyo daśakaṃ śatam | samudragantṛbhyo viṃśatikaṃ śatam uttamarṇa ādadyāt | mūlavināśasyāpi śaṅkitatvād iti | kvacid akṛtāpi vṛddhir bhavati | yathāha nāradaḥ |
na vṛddhiḥ prītidattānāṃ syād anākāritā kvacit |
anākāritam apy ūrdhvaṃ vatsarārdhād dvivardhate || [225]
anākāritam akṛtam ity arthaḥ | atra viṣṇuḥ |
yo gṛhītvā ṛṇaṃ pūrvaṃ yo dāsyāmīti sāmakam |
na dadyāl lobhataḥ paścāt tadā vṛddhim avāpnuyāt ||
samam eva sāmakam – akṛtavṛddhim iti yāvat | kātyāyanas tu viśeṣam āha |
paṇyaṃ gṛhītvā yo mūlyam adatvaiva diśaṃ vrajet |
ṛtutrayasyopariṣṭāt tad dhanaṃ vṛddhim āpnuyāt ||
apratiyācitaviṣayam etat |
yo yācitakam ādāya tam adatvā diśaṃ vrajet |
ūrdhvaṃ saṃvatsarāt tasya tad dhanaṃ vṛddhim āpnuyāt ||
yaḥ punaḥ svadeśe sthita eva yācito ‘pi yācitakaṃ na dadyāt taṃ yācanakālam ārabhya vṛddhiṃ dāpayed rājā,
svadeśe ‘vasthito yas tu na dadyād yācitaḥ kvacit |
taṃ tato ‘kāritāṃ vṛddhim anicchantaṃ ca dāpayet ||
iti smaraṇāt | atra vṛddheḥ parimāṇam “aśītibhāgo vṛddhiḥ syāt” ityādivacanaiḥ pratipāditam | yathāha manuḥ |
kṛtānusārād adhikā vyatiriktā na sidhyati |
kusīdapadam āhus taṃ pañcakaṃ śatam arhati ||
kusīdam āhur ity ucchāstravṛddhiṃ niṣedhati | śāstrakṛtavṛddhyanusāro vṛddhigrahaṇo yo laukikānāṃ samācāraḥ sa kṛtānusāraḥ | tasmād adhikā vṛddiḥ na sidhyati | ata evāha viṣṇuḥ: “vṛddhiṃ dadyād akṛtām api | saṃvatsarātikrame yathābhihitām” iti | yathābhihitāṃ dharmaśāstre ity śeṣaḥ | na ca pratyarthinā [226] madhyasthena vā arthinā vā abhihitā, adharmarādyaṅgīkārāyattaparimāṇasyātrāsaṃbhavāt | anyasya puruṣabuddhikalpitasya śāstrabāhyasyāyuktatvāt śāstroktam eva parimāṇaṃ grāhyam iti tātparyam | smṛtyantare viśeṣa uktaḥ |
prītidattaṃ na vardheta yāvan na pratiyācitam |
yācyamānam adattaṃ ca vardhate pañcakaṃ śatam || iti |
anākāritavṛddher apavādam āha nāradaḥ |
paṇyamālā bhṛtir nyāso daṇḍo yatra prakalpitaḥ |
vṛthādānākṣikapaṇā vardhante nāvivakṣitāḥ ||
vṛthādānaṃ naṭādibhyo dattam | ākṣikapaṇaḥ – akṣasaṃbandhipaṇaḥ | saṃvarto ‘pi |
na vṛddhiḥ strīdhane lābhe nikṣepe ca yathāsthite |
saṃdigdhe prātibhāvye ca yadi na syāt svayaṃkṛtā ||
svayaṃkṛteti vadan strīdhanādāv api kṛtā vṛddhir deyeti darśayati | niṣepe ca yathāsthita iti vadan ayathāsthitatve vyaktyanyatvādikaraṇe vṛddhir bhavatīti darśayati | kātyāyano ‘pi |
carmasasyāsadyūtapaṇyamūlyeṣu sarvadā |
strīśulke ca na vṛddhiḥ syāt prātibhāvyāgateṣu ca ||
ataḥ sarvadeti vadan pratiyācanāder vidyamānatve ‘pi nāsty akṛtā vṛddhir iti darśayati | gautamena tu veśeṣa uktaḥ: “bhuktādhir na vardhate” iti | bhukto vastālaṅkārādir iha bhuktādhiḥ | yājñavalkyenāpi |
dīyamānaṃ na gṛhṇāti prayuktaṃ yat svakaṃ dhanam |
madhyasthasthāpitaṃ yac ca vardhate na tataḥ param ||
[227] iti | vṛddhyuparamāvadhim āha nāradaḥ |
ṛṇānāṃ sārvabhaumo ‘yaṃ vidhir vṛddhikaraḥ smṛtaḥ |
deśācārasthitis tv anyā yatrarṇam avatiṣṭhate ||
dviguṇaṃ triguṇaṃ caiva tathānyasmiṃś caturguṇam |
tathāṣṭaguṇam anyasmin deśe deśe ‘vatiṣṭhate ||
yad deyam ṛṇaṃ tad vardhamānaṃ kvacid deśe dviguṇaṃ kvacit triguṇaṃ kvacic caturguṇam | prayuktadravyabhedena vṛddhivyavasthām āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
hiraṇye dviguṇā vṛddhis triguṇā vastrakupyake |
dhānye caturguṇā proktā daś vāhye laveṣu ca ||
uktā pañcaguṇā śāke bījasthā ṣaḍguṇā sthitā |
lavaṇasnehamadyeṣu vṛddhir aṣṭaguṇā smṛtā ||
guḍe madhuni caivoktā prayukte cirakālikā |
kupyam – trapusīsakam | śadaḥ – kṣetrutthaphalam | yady api dhānyam api kṣetrotthaphalam, tathāpi śadaśabdena kṣetrotthapuṣpaphalādikam ucyate gobalīvardanyāyena | lavo – meṣalomacāmarādi | śākavat kārpāse ‘pi ṣaḍguṇaiva vṛddhiḥ | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
śākakārpāsabījeṣu ṣaḍguṇā vṛddhir iṣyate |
tailasyāpi madyavad aṣṭaguṇā vṛddhiḥ | kātyāyanenāpi |
tailānāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ madyānām atha sarpiṣām |
vṛddhir aṣṭaguṇā jñeyā guḍasya lavaṇasya ca ||
yat tu “triguṇaṃ dhānyarasādiḥ” iti viṣṇuvacanam, tad ubhayasaṃmatyā deśabhedena vā vyavasthākalpanena dhānyaṃ caturguṇam iti pūrvoktena na viruddham | dhānyenaiva rasā vyākhyātāḥ | “puṣpaphalāni ca” iti smṛtyantaraṃ tatra deśe triguṇaṃ deyam avatiṣṭhate tadviṣayam | daridrādhamarṇaviṣayaṃ [228] vā | suvarṇavan madyādīnāṃ dvaiguṇyam eva | yathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
maṇimuktāpravālānāṃ suvarṇaracitasya ca |
tiṣṭhati triguṇā vṛddhiḥ || iti |
tatra manuḥ |
kusīdavṛddhir dvaiguṇyaṃ nātyeti sakṛd āhitā |
kusīdaṃ nāmādhikaṃ grahīṣyāmīti yad alpaṃ dīyate tat | tad dvaiguṇyaṃ samabhivyāhārād dhiraṇyam evābhipretam | sakṛd āhitā – puruṣāntaram asaṃkrāmitā | yad vā savṛddhikaṃ mūlīkṛtā tādṛśamūlena saha dviguṇatvaṃ nātikrāmatīty arthaḥ | evaṃ traiguṇyādāv api sakṛd āhitā vṛddhir nātikrāmati | atra vijñānayogī (on YDh 2.39cd):
kusīdavṛddhir dvaiguṇyaṃ nātyeti sakṛd āhitā |
iti | sakṛd āhṛtety api pāṭho ‘sti upacayārtham prayuktaṃ (pravṛttaṃ) dhanam – kusīdam | tasya vṛddhiḥ – dvaiguṇyaṃ nātyeti – nātikrāmati | yadi sakṛd āhitā – sakṛt pravṛttā | puruṣāntarasaṃkramādinā prayogāntarakaraṇe dvaiguṇyam atyeti | sakṛd āhṛteti pāṭhe tu śanaiḥ śanaiḥ pratidinaṃ pratimāsaṃ prativatsaraṃ vā adhamarṇād āhṛtā dvaiguṇyaṃ nātyetīti vyākyeyam ity āha | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “cirasthāne dvaiguṇyaṃ pryogasya” iti | prayogasyety ekavacananirdeśāt prayogāntarakaraṇe dvaiguṇyātikramo ‘bhipretaḥ | cirasthāna iti nirdeśāc chanaiḥ śanair vṛddhigrahaṇe dvaiguṇyatikramo darśitaḥ ||
vṛddhyuparamāpavādaḥ
kvacid vṛddhyuparamāpavādaḥ | yathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
tṛṇakāṣṭheṣṭakāsūtrakiṇvacarmāsthivarmaṇām |
hetipuṣpaphalānāṃ ca vṛddhis tu na nivartate || [229]
carma – bāṇādinivārakaḥ, phalakaḥ | varma – kavacam | puṣpaphalayos tu vṛddhyanivṛttiḥ pratidānavelāyām atyantāsamṛddhādhamarṇaviṣaye veditavyā | anyathā pūrvoktavacanavyākopaprasaṅgāpatteḥ | viṣṇur api: “kiṇvakārpāsamātracarma-varmāyudheṣṭakāṅgārāṇām akṣayā” iti | atra kārpāsasya ṣaḍguṇābhidhāyakavacanavirodhaḥ pūrvavat parihāryaḥ | vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
dantacarmāsthiśṛṅgāṇāṃ mṛnmayānāṃ tathaiva ca |
akṣayā vṛddhir eteṣāṃ puṣpamūlaphalasya ca ||
manus tu kvacit pratiṣedham āha |
nātisāṃsatsarīṃ vṛddhiṃ na cādṛṣṭāṃ vinirharet | iti |
adṛṣṭāṃ dharmaśāstreṣu | ṣaṭkaṃ śatam ity evamādīty arthaḥ |
kecid adṛṣṭām ity etat padam anyathā varṇayanti |
hiraṇyadhānyavastrāṇāṃ vṛddhir dvitricaturguṇā |
ghṛtasyāṣṭaguṇā vṛddhis tāmrādīnāṃ caturguṇā ||
tailānāṃ ṣaḍguṇā vṛddhiḥ strīpaśūnāṃ tu santatiḥ |
caturguṇā syāt kośānāṃ kārpāsasya caturguṇā ||
kāṣṭhānāṃ candanādīnāṃ vṛddhir aṣṭaguṇā bhavet |
evaṃ vṛddhividhiḥ proktā nāsti vṛddis tataḥ parā ||
na vṛddher vṛddhir astīti dharmakārānuśāsanam |
na cānyasaṃśritā vṛddhir iti vṛddheś ca karhicit ||
ity atra vṛddhisaṃśritavṛddhīnāṃ śāstraniṣedhāt śāstrādṛṣṭatvam iti | atra candanādīnām ity ādiśabdas tu gandhamātropalakṣakaḥ | tāmrādīnām [230] ity ādiśabdena suvarṇarajatavyatiriktānāṃ grahaṇam | kośaśabdo dhānyānām upalakṣakaḥ | kārpāsaśabdagrahaṇena taṇḍulaprabhṛtīnām upalakṣaṇam | dhānyasya triguṇapañcaguṇayor vyavasthā pūrvam evoktā | nātisāṃvatsarīm ity asyārthaḥ – śūdrādhamarṇe pratimāsaṃ pañcaśataṃ gṛhṇīyād iti yā vṛddhiḥ prāg uktā sā brāhmaṇādhamarṇe kathaṃcid aṅgīkāravaśāt gṛhyamāṇā saṃvatsaraṃ yāvad grahītavyā, na tataḥ param iti |
apare tu – taddvaiguṇyād vṛddhigrahavidhānāt saṃvatsarād ūrdhvam api vṛddhigrahaṇe doṣābhāvāt abhyudayārthī cet saṃvatsarād ūrdhvaṃ yā vṛddhis tāṃ na nirharet – na gṛhṇīyād iti | ayaṃ prakāraḥ candrikākārādīnām abhipretaḥ | ādyas tu aparārkādīnām abhipretaḥ |
yathāruci svīkāryam | atra viṣṇuḥ |
steyaṃ brahmasvaviṣaye suvarṇābharaṇe tathā |
paścāt tat tena dātavyaṃ tasmād ekādaśādhikam ||
ayam artho brāhmaṇasaṃbandhisuvarṇavyatiriktadravyāpahāre | kṣatriyasuvarṇāpahāre ca | tathā ca pitāmahaḥ |
sadyaḥ syād dvādaśaguṇaṃ coritaṃ ratnahāṭakam |
brāhmaṇasvaṃ ca rūpyādi sadyo ‘py ekādaśādhikam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
kupyaṃ pañcaguṇaṃ bhūmiḥ tathaivāṣṭaguṇā matā |
sadya eveti vacanāt sadya eva pradīyate || iti |
bṛhaspatis tu viśeṣam āha |
śikhāvṛddhiṃ kāyikāṃ ca bhāgalābhaṃ tathaiva ca |
dhanī tāvat samādadyād yāvan mūlaṃ na śodhitam ||
[231]        śikhāvṛddhyādīnāṃ svarūpaṃ pūrvam evoktam ||
na śodhitaṃ na pratidattam ṛṇikenety arthaḥ | ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
ādhis tu bhujyat tāvad yāvan na pratidīyate | iti |
atra hārītaḥ |
bandhaṃ yathā sthāpitaṃ syāt tathaiva paripālayet |
anyathā naśyate lābho mūlaṃ vā tadvyatikramāt ||
yathā – yena prakāreṇa gopyatvena bhogyatvena vā sthāpitam – adhamarṇenādhīkṛtam | tathaiva – gopyam ādhiṃ gopyatvenaiva bhojyam ādhiṃ bhogyatvenaiva dhanī pālayet | anyathā – gopyaṃ bhogyatvenaiva bhogyaṃ vā gopyatvenaiva pālitaṃ cet | samayātikramāl lābho naśyati, mūlaṃ vā dravyaṃ naśyatīty arthaḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ: “gopyādhibhoge no vṛddhiḥ” iti | bhoge balād iti śeṣaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
no bhoktavyo balād ādhiḥ bhuñjāno vṛddhim utsṛjet | iti |
ayam arthaḥ – bhogapratiṣedhaṃ kurvantam ādhātāram ākramya gopyādhiṃ bhuñjānasyātyantāparādhitvāt alpabhoga eva sarvanāśakaḥ syād iti | yas tu balātkāreṇātyantaṃ bhuṅkte tasya mūlahānir eveti | “mūlaṃ vā” iti hārītavacane vikalpārthaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
yaḥ svāminānanujñātam ādhiṃ bhuṅkte vicakṣaṇaḥ |
tenādhivṛddhir moktavyā tasya bhogasya niṣkṛtiḥ ||
bhogyādhau tu bhogyatvena pālite lābhasyaiva nāśaḥ | samayātikramamātreṇa mūlanāśapakṣānavatārāt | na ca bhogyādhau vṛddhyabhāvāl lābhanāśapakṣasyāpy anavatāra iti vācyam | bhogasyāpi lābhatvāt | [232] atha vā yatrādhātā ādāv upabhogaṃ vṛddhidānam apy ādhyābhyupagacchati tatra prāptā vṛddhir ity avagantavyam | ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
gopyādhibhoge no vṛddhiḥ sopakāre ‘pi hāpte |
naṣṭo deyo vinaṣṭaś ca daivarājakṛtād ṛte ||
gopyādhes tāmrakaṭāhāder upabhoge vṛddhir na bhavati | tathā sopakāre – upakārakāriṇi balīvardādau | bhogyādhau savṛddhike hāpite | hānim – vyavahārākṣamatvam | gamite no vṛddhir iti saṃbandhaḥ | naṣṭo – vikṛtiṃ gataḥ chidrabhedādinā | pūrvavat kṛtvā deyaḥ | vṛddhisadbhāve vṛddhir api hātavyā | vinaṣṭaḥ – ātyantikanāśaṃ prāptaḥ | so ‘pi deyo mūlyādidvāreṇa | taddāne savṛddhikamūlyaṃ labhate yadi tadā mūlyanāśaḥ,
vinaṣṭe mūlanāśaḥ syād daivarājakṛtād ṛte |
iti nāradavacanāt | daivam – agnyudakadeśopaplavādi | daivakṛtād rājakṛtād vā vināśād vinā svāparādhakṛtād vināśe savṛddhikaṃ mūlyaṃ dātavyam | adhamarṇenādhyantaraṃ vā dātavyam | yathāha |
srotasāpahṛte kṣetre rājñā caivāpahārite |
ādhir anyo ‘tha dātavyo deyaṃ vā dhanino dhanam || iti |
tathā ca nāradaḥ |
yady ādher mūlanāsaḥ syād daivarājakṛtāt kvacit |
ādhyarthas tv ṛṇinā deyas tv aśaktau bhogato bhavet || iti |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ yāvān bhogas tāvān vṛddhitvena datavya ity arthaḥ | bhāradvājas tu viśeṣam āha |
pracchādyādhim ṛṇī kuryāt krayārthe balavac ca yaḥ |
daṇḍaṃ sa triguṇaṃ datvā punar ādhyarthako bhavet || [233]
atra vyāsaḥ |
grahītṛdoṣān naṣṭaś cet bandho hemādiko bhavet |
ṛṇaṃ salābhaṃ saṃśodhya tanmūlyaṃ dāpayed dhanī ||
atra bhāradvājaḥ |
ādheḥ praveśane kāle mohān necchati ced dhanī |
bhogo nāsty evāta ūrdhvaṃ na vardhayati tad dhanam ||
bhogyādhikyaṃ ca bhogyādher hrāsaṃ ca na vicārayet |
lekhye tu likhite yāvat tāvad bhoktavyam eva tu ||
ataś ca |
rakṣyamāṇo ‘pi yatrādhiḥ kālenāyād asāratām |
ādhir anyo ‘tha vā kāryo deyaṃ vā dhanino dhanam || iti |
jaṅgamaviṣaya evam avagantavyam | ata evāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
ādhir bandhaḥ samākhyātaḥ sa ca proktaś caturvidhaḥ |
jaṅgamaḥ sthāvaraś caiva gopyo bhogyas tathaiva ca ||
ayam arthaḥ – ādhir dvividhaḥ: sthāvaro jaṅgamaś ca | ata evāha nāradaḥ |
ādhis tu dvividhaḥ prokto jaṅgamaḥ sthāvaras tathā |
sa cāpi dvividhaḥ prokto gopyo bhogyas tathaiva ca || iti |
atra bhogyo dvividhaḥ sapratyayabhogyādhiḥ apratyayabhogyādhiś ceti | savṛddhikamūlyāpākaraṇārtho yaḥ sa sapratyayabhogyādhir ity ucyate | vṛddhimātrāpākaraṇārtho yaḥ so ‘pratyayabhogyādhir ity ucyate | tatra sapratyayabhogyādhim āha vyāsaḥ |
kāṃcid vṛddhiṃ samābhāṣya dravyam ādāya tattvataḥ |
[234]        matkṣetraṃ bhuṅkṣva vṛddhyartham adhikaṃ mūlanāśanam |
ityādipratyayādhiḥ syād dvaiguṇye niṣkrayo bhavet ||
savṛddhimūlyāpākaraṇārtho yaḥ sa sapratyayabhogyādhir iti vacanārthaḥ | athāpratyayabhogyādhim āha kātyāyanaḥ |
dravyaṃ gṛhītvā vṛddhyarthaṃ bhogayogyaṃ dadāti cet |
jaṅgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ vāpi bhogyādhiḥ sa tu kathyate ||
mūlyaṃ tadādhikaṃ datvā svakṣetrādhikam (tatkṣetrā-) āpnuyāt |
vṛddhimātrāpākaraṇārthe bhogyādhau adhamarṇa uttamarṇataḥ prāptaṃ mūlyaṃ datvā svaṃ kṣetrādikam āpnuyāt | eṣo ‘pratyayabhogyādhir iti vacanābhiprāyaḥ | atra bharadvājaḥ |
pratyayādhau tu bhoktavyā vṛddhir yā pūrvalekhitā |
tāvad eva tu bhoktavyam iti śāstraviniścayaḥ ||
yat tu tatrādhikaṃ vṛddher deyaṃ tad ṛṇine punaḥ |
hīnaṃ yāvat tu tad vṛddhe tāvat saṃpūrayed ṛṇae ||
sapratyayabhogyādhau niṣkrayakāle savṛddhikamūlyasyāparyāptaṃ pūrayet | adhikaṃ ced ādadyād iti vacanasya tātparyārthaḥ | atra yamaḥ |
vaiśākhādyāsu (?) bhogyādher āṣāḍhyāṃ niṣkrayo bhavet |
hīnaṃ yad dhanino doṣād etat pūrṇam arhati ||
hīnasyāpūraṇe vṛddhiś cakravṛddhyā vivardhate |
sarvādhīnāṃ balād bhogān niṣkrayo nāsti tattvataḥ ||
balād bhukte sakāle vā niṣkrayāt triguṇo damaḥ |
bharadvājaḥ |
svāminā cānanujñāta ādher ādhiṃ karoti cet |
[235]        svadhanāt sa tu hīnaḥ syāt karoty āpadi pūrvavat ||
āpadi – āpatkāle pūrvavat – svadhanaṃ hīyety arthaḥ | dāsyādyādhau vetanam, śakaṭādyādhau nāśakādikam | na tu dāsyādhikṛtāvaghātādes taṇḍulādikam ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
akāmam ananujñātam ādhiṃ yat karma kārayet |
bhoktā karmaphalaṃ dāpyo vṛddhiṃ vā labhate na saḥ || iti |
karmānusāreṇeti draṣṭavyam | dāpyo – bandhasvāmine rājñeti śeṣaḥ |
yas tv ādhikarma kurvāṇaṃ vācā daṇḍena karmabhiḥ |
pīḍayed bhartsayen naiva prāpnuyāt pūrvasāhasam ||
atra kurvāṇam iti śānacā(?) kurvaty ādhau pīḍanabhartsanakāriṇo ‘pi na daṇḍa iti sūcitam |
balād akāmaṃ yatrādhim anisṛṣṭaṃ praveśayet |
prāpnuyāt sāhasaṃ pūrvaṃ dhātāpy ādhim āpnuyāt ||
anisṛṣṭam – anāhitam | gopyādhau balād alpasyāpy anāhitasya bhoge bhogakartur daṇḍaḥ sarvamūlanāśaḥ syād iti tātparyārthaḥ | vañcanayā tu kṛte bhoge bhogānusāreṇa mūlanāśaḥ | anyathā balād grahaṇasyānarthakyāpattir iti candrikākāraḥ – ādhisaṃrakṣaṇakāram āha hārītaḥ |
bandhaṃ yathā sthāpitaṃ syāt tathaiva pratipālayet |
ādhigrahaṇād ūrdhvam ardhe nāśahrāsavikārāsāratvavyaktyantaratvādayo yathā na bhavanti tathā pratyarpaṇaparyantaṃ dhanī yatnenādhiṃ pālayed ity arthaḥ | evaṃ pālyamāne py ādhau daivādivaśān nāśo yadi bhavet [236] tadāpi na kaścid doṣo dhanina ity āha sa eva |
daivarājopaghāte tu na doṣo dhaninaḥ kvacit | iti |
atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
sa ced dhanikadoṣeṇa nipated vā mriyeta vā |
ādhim anyaṃ sa dāpyaḥ syād ṛṇān mucyete sarṇikaḥ || iti |
dhanikadoṣād anyatrādhyapacāre ṛṇika ṛṇān mucyate | tenānyam adhikam ādhisamaṃ vārtham ṛṇiko dhanine rājñā dāpya ity arthaḥ | ādhisiddhiṃ praty āha nāradaḥ |
ādhis tu dvividhaḥ proktaḥ sthāvaro jaṅgamas tathā |
siddhir asyobhyasyāpi bhogo yady asti nānyathā ||
siddhir ādhitasiddhiḥ | anyathā – vinā bhogam adhamarṇoddiṣṭasya uttamarṇasvīkāramātreṇeti yāvat | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
maryādācihnitaṃ kṣetraṃ gṛhaṃ vāpi yadā bhavet |
grāmādayaś ca likhyante tadā siddhim avāpnuyāt ||
anena lekhyārūḍhatvam api ādhisiddhau nimittam iti sūcitam | mukhyaṃ prayojanaṃ bhoga eva | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ |
yayor nikṣipta ādhis taṃ vivadetāṃ yadā narau |
yasya bhuktir jayas tasya balātkāraṃ vinā kṛtā ||
dvayor api bhuktisadbhāve bṛhaspatir āha |
kṣetram ekaṃ dvayor bandhe dattaṃ yat samakālikam |
yena bhuktaṃ bhavet pūrvaṃ tasya tat siddhim āpnuyāt ||
etac cobhayoḥ patrārūḍhatve veditavyam | yathāha vasiṣṭhaḥ |
tulyakāle nisṛṣṭānāṃ lekhyānām adhikāriṇā |
[237]        yena bhuktaṃ bhavet pūrvaṃ tasyādhir balavattaraḥ ||
bhogyādhau bhogasya pradhānakāraṇatvāt prāthamyanibandhanabalavattā yuktety abhiprāyaḥ | bhogāviśeṣe tu |
yady ekadivase tau tu bhoktukāmāv upasthitau (māv upāgatau) |
vibhajyādhiṃ samaṃ tena bhoktavya iti niścayaḥ ||
tenety ekavacanaṃ dvivacanasyopalakṣam, dvābhyām ity arthaḥ | yad vā – teneti tarhīty arthe nipātaḥ | bhogyādau viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
ādhim ekaṃ dvayor yatra kuryāt ko ‘tra patir bhavet |
tayoḥ pūrvakṛtaṃ grāhyaṃ tatkartā coradaṇḍabhāk ||
pūrvakṛtam – pūrvam upādānādinā siddham | upādānādau yaugapadye ‘py āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
tulyakālopasthitayor dvayor api samaṃ bhavet |
upasthitayoḥ – upādānādikaṃ kartum iti śeṣaḥ | gopyādhau lekhyam eva prabalaṃ pramāṇam ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
ādhānaṃ vikrayo dānaṃ lekhyasākṣikṛtaṃ yathā |
ekakriyāviruddhaṃ tu lekhyaṃ tatrāpahārakam ||
lekhyam – lekhyakṛtam | apahārakaṃ balavat | lekhyasiddhatvāviśeṣe ‘pi balābalaviśeṣam āha sa eva |
anirdiṣṭaṃ ca nirdiṣṭam ekatra ca vilekhitam |
viśealikhitaṃ jyāya iti kātyānayo ‘bravīt ||
anirdiṣṭaṃ nāmādātur ādhikaraṇakāle yad yad dhanaṃ nirūpitasvarūpaṃ tad dhanaṃ nirdiṣṭam | tadviparītam anirdiṣṭam ity ucyata iti | ādhikaraṇakāle nirdiṣṭatvasāmye ‘py āha [238] sa eva |
yas tu sarvasvam ādiśya prāk paścān nāmacihnitam |
ādadyāt tat kathaṃ tu syāc cihnitaṃ balavattaram ||
ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
ādhau pratigrahe krīte pūrvā tu balavattarā | iti |
“ādhyādiṣu triṣu pūrvam eva kāryaṃ balavat | anyathā ekam eva kṣetram ekasyādhiṃ kṛtvā punar anyasyāpy ādhau yat kim api gṛhṇāti | atra pūrvasyaiva tad bhavati nottarasya | evaṃ pratigrahe kraye ca |
nanv āhitasya tadānīm asvatvāt punar ādhānaṃ na saṃbhavati | evaṃ dattasya ca krītasya ca dānakrayau nopapadyete, tasmād idaṃ vacanam anarthakam ity ced,
ucyate – asvāmitve lobhāt kaścin mohād vā punar ādhānādikaṃ karoti tatra pūrvakaṃ balavad iti nyāyamūlam evedaṃ vacanam ity acodyam” iti vijñāneśaḥ | yady apy ādhikaraṇena na svāmibhāvo nivartate, tathāpi pratibadhyate | tataś ca pratibaddhasvāmibhāvena kṛta ādhivikrayaḥ pareṇa kṛta ivāsiddhatvād durbalo bādhyata eva | evaṃ dānakrayau | anenābhprāyeṇa vasiṣṭho ‘pi |
yaḥ pūrvataram ādhāya vikrīṇīte tu taṃ punaḥ |
kim etayor balīyaḥ syāt prāktanaṃ balavattaram ||
ataś cādhau parikrīta ity atra ādhāv ity atra viṣayasaptamī | ādhipratigrahakrayayoś ca balavattāvicāraṇe pūrvasyābalavattvam iti keṣāṃcin matam | ādhipratigrahakrayāṇāṃ yaugapadye ‘py āha sa eva |
kṛtaṃ yatraikadivase dānam ādhānavikrayam |
trayāṇām iti saṃdehe kathaṃ tatra vicintayet ||
[239]        trayo ‘pi tad dhanaṃ darmyaṃ vibhajeyur yathāṃśataḥ |
ubhau kriyānusāreṇa vibhāgena pratigrahaḥ || iti |
atra krayaśabdo dhanavacanaḥ, karaṇavyutpatter āśrayaṇāt | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
kṛtaṃ ced ekadivase vikrayādhipratigraham |
trayāṇām api saṃdehe kathaṃ tatra vicāraṇā ||
trīṇy eva hi pramāṇāni vibhajeyur yathāṃśataḥ |
ubhau cārthānusāreṇa tribhāgena pratigrahī || iti |
pratigraha iti pāṭhe niṣpadyata iti śeṣaḥ |
ādhiḥ praṇśyed dviguṇe dhane yadi na mokṣyate |
kāle kālakṛto naśyet phalabhogyo na naśyati ||
atrādhipraṇāśo nāmādhamarṇasaṃbandhaṃ parityajya dhanikasaṃbandhāpattiḥ | etad uktaṃ bhavati – prayukte dravye svakṛtayā vṛddhyā kālakrameṇa dviguṇībhūte yady ādhir adhamarṇena dravyādānena na mokṣyate | tadā naśyati – adharmarṇasya svaṃ dhanaprayoktuḥ svaṃ bhavatīti | kālakṛto ‘py evam eva | kālakṛtaḥ – kṛtakālaḥ | āhitāgnyāditvāt pūrvanipātaḥ | sa tu kāle nirūpite prāpte naśyet | dvaiguṇyāt prāg ūrdhvaṃ vā phalabhogyaḥ – phalaṃ bhogyaṃ yasya sa phalabhogyaḥ | kṣetrārāmādiḥ | sa kadācid api na naśyati kṛtakālasyābhogyasya tatkālātikramanāśa ity uktaḥ “kāle kālakṛto naśyet” iti akṛtakālasya bhogyasya nāśābhāva uktaḥ | phalabhogyo na naśyatīti pāriśeṣyād ādhiḥ praṇāsyed ity etad akṛtakālagopyādhiviṣayam avatiṣṭhate | dvaiguṇyādikrameṇa nirūpitakālātikrameṇa ca vināśe [240] caturdaśadinapratīkṣaṇaṃ kartavyam |
hiraṇye dviguṇībhūte pūrṇe kālakṛtāvadheḥ |
bandhakasya dhanī svāmī dvisaptāhaṃ pratīkṣya ca ||
tadantarā dhanaṃ datvā ṛṇī bandham avāpnuyāt |
iti bṛhaspativacanād dhanam atra savṛddhikamūlyaṃ vivakṣitam | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
gopyādhiṃ dviguṇād ūrdhvaṃ mocayed adhamarṇakaḥ | iti |
dviguṇād ūrdhvam – prayuktadhane śāntalābhe satīty arthaḥ | mocayet – savṛddhikamūlyadāneneti śeṣaḥ | lābhaśāntitaḥ pūrvakālamadhye ‘pi savṛddhike dhane datte bandhāvāptiḥ daṇḍāpūpanyāyasiddhā ca |
tadantarā dhanaṃ datvā ṛṇī bandham avāpnuyāt |
gopyādhiṃ dviguṇād ūrdhvaṃ modayed adhamarṇakaḥ ||
iti vacanadvayasya dvaiguṇyānantaram evādhir moktavyaḥ madhye dviguṇam eva dātavyaṃ dhanaṃ na tu yathākālaprāptavṛddhiyuktadvaiguṇyāt pūrvaṃ na dātavyam ity evaṃ vyākhyānaṃ na yujyate candrikākāra-kulārkavyākhyāviruddham iti vācyam | bhārucimatānusāreṇa vyākhyānād idam eva vyākhyānaṃ samyak | sthāvarasyādhau dhanadvaiguṇyaṃ gopyasyāpādakam | uttamarṇas tu gopyalābhārdhaṃ dhanaṃ prayuktavān | na vṛddhyartham | ato yadā kadācid api prayuktadravyadāne dviguṇam eva dhanaṃ dātavyam | dviguṇānantaram eva parasvatvāpattir nāsti –
bandhakasya dhanī svāmī dvisaptāhaṃ pratīkṣya tu | iti |
dhanino bandhakasvāmitvaṃ caturdaśadinānantaram evety ato na dvaiguṇyānantaraṃ bandhanasvāmitvam | ato dvauguṇyāt pūrvam api dviguṇam eva [241] dātavyam iti bhārucimatatatvam | ataś candrikākārādimatam asamañjasam iti dhyeyam | phalabhogyādhimūlamātraṃ datvā phalakālānte vartamānam āpnuyāt ṛṇī –
jyeṣṭhāvadhiṃ samāsādya mocayed bhogyam āhitam | iti |
āhitam – ādhīkṛtam ity arthaḥ | etac ca sthāvaraviṣayam | svarūpeṇa bhogyavastrādau na kālavyavasthā | tatra bṛhaspatiḥ |
dhanaṃ mūlīkṛtaṃ datvā yadādhiṃ prārthayed ṛṇī |
tadaiva tasya moktavyam anyathā doṣabhāg dhanī ||
atra doṣaḥ – steyadoṣaḥ | deśakālavyavadhānena dhanikāsaṃnidhau dhanikakuṭumbenāpi so ‘rtho moktavyaḥ |
prayojake sati dhanaṃ mūle nyasyādhim āpnuyāt |
nyasya – datvā | paribhāṣite viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
paribhāṣya yadā kṣetraṃ pradadyād dhanike ṛṇī |
tvayaitac chāntalābhe ‘rthe moktavyam iti niścitam ||
praviṣṭe sodaye dravye pradātavyaṃ tvayā mama | iti |
tvayaitad ityādiparibhāṣya yadā ṛṇagrahaṇakāle kṣetrādyādhiṃ pradadyāt | prāguktavidhayādhilābha ity arthaḥ | ādhilābho ‘nayaiva paribhāṣayā lābhaśānteḥ paścād api siddhyatīty āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
yadā tu dviguṇībhūtam ṛṇam ādhau tadā khalu
mocya ādhis tadutpanne praviṣṭe dviguṇe dhane || [242]
tatra viṣṇuḥ: “gṛhītadhanapraveśārtham eva yat tu sthāvaraṃ dattaṃ tad gṛhītadhanapraveśe dadyāt” iti | evaṃvidham ādhiṃ laukikāḥ kṣayādhim ācakṣate | kecid idam eva paribhāṣitādhim, kecit saṃvidādhim āhuḥ ||
“nanv ādhiḥ praṇaśyed ity anupapannam, adhamarṇasya svatvanivṛttihetor dānavikrayāder abhāvāt | dhanikasya ca svatvahetoḥ pratigrahakramyāder abhāvād iti cen,
maivam | ādhīkaraṇam eva loke sopādhikasvatvanivṛttihetuḥ ādhisvīkāraś ca sopādhikasvatvāpattihetuḥ prasiddhaḥ | atra dhanadvaiguṇye nirūpitakālaprāptau ca dravyasyātyantanivṛtteḥ anena vacanena adhamarṇasya ātyantikī svatvanivṛttiḥ uttamarṇasyātyantaṃ svatvaṃ bhavati” iti vijñāneśaḥ | candrikākāras tu (Vya p. 142) “dhanadvaiguṇyam avadhibhūtakālātikramaṇaṃ ca svatvadhvaṃsakaṃ yady api loke dvaiguṇyāder na tathā prasiddhir asti, tathāpi dravyavinimayasya tathā prasiddhir asti | tilavikrayapratiṣedhād vikrayākaraṇe ‘pi vinimaye tilānāṃ svatvanivṛttidarśanāt | tataś cātrāpi kṛtakālāvadhau ṛṇī grahaṇakāla eva yady aham iyatā kālena na dadāmi ādhir evānṛṇyāya tava bhaviṣyatī dhanikarṇikayor vinimayasaṃpratipatter jātatvād avadhibhūtakāle svatvadhvaṃso yukta eva” iti |
ayam abhisaṃdhiḥ – vijñāneśvaramate vācaniko ‘tra svatvadhvaṃsaḥ parasvatvāpattiś ca | candrikākāramate naiyāyikaḥ, krayapratigrahādyabhāve vinimayenauvādhau dhanikasya svatvāpattiḥ | vrīhyādāv iva tilavinimayakartarīti nyāyapratipādanāt |
apare tv āhuḥ – paribhāṣāvaśād dviguṇadhanasya mūlyatvena krayāntādhir bhaviṣyatīti sopādhikakraya iti svatvasya laukikatvād vācanikatvaṃ na [243] yujyate | vinimayasya svatvāpādakatvam “svāmī rikthakrayasaṃvibhāgaparigrahādhigameṣu” ityādivacanasya niyamaparatvāt pāribhāṣikakrayānta iti bhāruci-aparārkādīnāṃ matam iti krayānte vācikadānānto veti |
ayam āśayaḥ – ādhisthale vinimaya eva na saṃgacchate | paribhāṣāvaśāt dīpotsavādisamaye etad dṛḍham ahaṃ dāsyāmi anyathāyam ādhis tava bhaviṣyatīti tatra paribhāṣayaiva dhanasya sopādhikrayadravyatayā pratīteḥ | yad vā “kāle kālakṛto naśyet” ity atrāpi paribhāḍayaivādhināśaḥ pratīyate | tasmāt paribhāṣā nāma vācanikadānam iti dānam eva svatvāpādakam | ato dānāntatayā ādhiḥ svatvāpādakaḥ | anenaivābhiprāyeṇoktaṃ vijñānayoginā – “vacanāt svatvam” iti | vacanaṃ paribhāṣā – vācanikadānam iti yāvat | anyathā – krayāntapakṣe sthāvaradṛśyādhau |
jñātyādhipratyayenaiva smārakakraya iṣyate |
iti vakṣyamāṇanyāyena jñātyādhyāsedhā na lageyuḥ | na ca tathā saṃbhavati | ādhir nāma bandhakaṃ | ṛṇadāne grahaṇe vā ṛṇaṃ na dātavyaṃ na grahītavyaṃ cety evam āsedhaḥ saṃbhavatīti dṛśyādhau jñātyādhyāsedhā na santi, te ‘pi na lagayur evety ato vacanikadānaprakṣa eva samyag iti | anvādhisvarūpam āha prajāpatiḥ |
dhanī dhanena tenaiva paramādhiṃ nayed yadi |
kṛtvā tadādhilikhitaṃ pūrvaṃ vāsya samarpayet || iti |
“yad bandhasvāmini dhanaṃ prayuktaṃ tat tulyenaiva dhanena param – dhanikāntaram ādhiṃ nayet | na tv adhikena” iti candrikākāraḥ (Vya p. 143) | gopyādher bhogyādhitvam apy āha viṣṇuḥ: [244] “pāribhāṣiko ‘pi gopyādhir bhogyādhir api bhavati” iti | apiśabdaḥ kālādīn samuccinoti | pāribhāṣikaḥ – paribhāṣayā prāptaḥ | yathā – ayam ādhis tvatprayuktadhane dviguṇībhūte yadi na mocyeta tad dinam ārabhya dviguṇadhanasya bhogya iti gopyabhogyādhiḥ | dīpotsavādisamaye tatprayuktaṃ mūlyam eva dhanaṃ yadi na dīyate tadāprabhṛti prayuktadhanam ārabhya vā ayam ādhir bhogyādhir iti kālakṛtabhogyādhiḥ | pāribhāṣika iti vacanenedaṃ jñāpyate – bhogyādhir api kālādhir bhaviṣyati | yathā – ayaṃ bhogyādhiḥ svaprayuktadhanavṛddhyarthadīpotsavakālaparyantam anubhujyatāṃ tadā yadi mūlyaṃ na dāsyāmas tadā ādhis tava bhaviṣyatīti bhogyakālādhiḥ | bhogyato ‘py ādhim apy āha gautamaḥ | tathā ca gautamasūtram: “bhogyādhir api gopyādhir bhavatīti pāribhāṣikatvā vyavahārasya” iti | pāribhāṣikatvaṃ paribhāṣānibandhanam ity arthaḥ | athāyaṃ bhogyādhir dīpotsavakālaparyantaṃ prayuktaṃ dhanaṃ vṛddhyartham upabhujyatām, tadānīṃ prayuktadhanāpradāne dīpotsavaprabhṛti na bhujyatām | tataḥ paraṃ prayuktadhane vṛddhyā dviguṇībhūte dvaiguṇyānantaraṃ tad dhanaṃ yadi na dāsyāmaḥ tadādhis tava bhaviṣyatīti | anvādhir apy evam evohyaḥ | khaṇḍādhir api dīpotsavaparyantaṃ māsamātraṃ māsadvayaparyantaṃ vā pāribhāṣikavṛddhyā yāvad vṛddhaṃ dhanaṃ tāvad dhanaṃ mūlīkṛtya taddhanasyādhakiraṇaṃ kaṇḍādhir iti | atra viśeṣam āha viṣṇuḥ: “sa kvacit kriyāntaḥ” iti | ayam arthaḥ – ayaṃ pūrvoktādhiḥ | kriyāntaḥ kvacit viṣayabhedena dīpotsavādisamaye dviguṇadravyāpradāne taddviguṇadravyasya [245] ayam ādhir vikrīta iti | kriyāntakālādhidviguṇādhiś ca bhogyādhir api kriyāntaḥ | yathā – ayam ādhir vṛddhyartham anubhūyatāṃ dīpotsavādisamaye mūlyaṃ yadi na dīyate tadā mūlyasyaiva ayam ādhir vikriyata iti | eṣu krayāntasaṃkarādiṣu paribhāṣāvaśāt krayāntatāyāṃ teṣāṃ krayadharmāḥ |
pūrvāhṇe grāmamadhye ca jñātisāmantasaṃnidhau |
hiraṇyodakadānena ṣaḍbhir gacchati medinī ||
ityādidharmaḥ saty eva | ato jñātyādibhiḥ pratyayenaivetyādivakṣyamāṇanyāyena jñātyādyanumatyā bhāvyam | yadā tu śāntalābhe dhane bandhakasya tadaivopasthitasya mocanāt prāggrahaṇasya maraṇādir bhavet tadā kiṃ kartavyam ity ata āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
hiraṇye dviguṇībhūte mṛte naṣṭe ‘dhamarṇake |
dravyaṃ tadīyaṃ sāmgṛhya vikrīṇīta sasākṣikam ||
rakṣed vā kṛtamūlyaṃ tu daśāhaṃ janasaṃsadi |
ṛṇānurūpaṃ parato gṛhītvānyāṃs tu varjayet || iti |
kātyāyanas tu |
ādhātā yatra na syāt tu dhanī bandhaṃ nivedayet |
rājñas tataḥ sa vikhyāpya vikrayaṃ kurute dhanī ||
savṛddhikaṃ gṛhītvā tu śeṣaṃ rājany athārpayet ||
rājany athārpayed iti jñātyādipratyāsannābhāvaviṣayam | tatsadbhāve tatraivārpaṇasya nyāyyatvāt | ayam abhisaṃdhiḥ – jñātyādāv arpaṇānto vidhiḥ bahumūlyādiviṣaya eva, roṣārpaṇādes tatraiva saṃbandhāt | samamūlye tu sasākṣikaṃ vikrayī svayam eva gṛhṇīyāt | svalpamūlye [246] tv evaṃ vikrayī gṛhītvā ṛṇaśeṣam ṛṇī dānādhikāriṇi kadācid āgate tato gṛhṇīyāt | anāgate tu māstu ityādyamukavacanaṃ tathaivohyam iti | yadā tv ādhātṛsukṛtam anadhikṛtya ṛṇaṃ gṛhītvā dvaiguṇye jāte ‘pi śāntalābhaṃ dhanaṃ na vardhate | na cādher ādhānāntaraṃ vikrayaṇaṃ vā dhaninā kartuṃ na śakyata iti buddhyā ṛṇapradāne ‘pi vilambam evāvalambate | yad vā dhanī svacchāśayatvāt pra(su?)kṛtādhim eva gṛhṇāti tadādhamavarṇo na dhanaṃ prayacchati tadānīm asya rājñā dāpya ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
caritrabandhakakṛtam sa vṛddhyā dāpayed dhanam | iti |
caritram eva bandhakaṃ caritrabandhakaṃ caritraśabdena gaṅgāsnānāginhotrādijanitam apūrvam ucyate | yatra tad evādhīkṛtya yad dravyam ātmasāt kṛtaṃ tatra tad eva dviguṇībhūtaṃ dātavyaṃ nādhināśa iti | atra ādhiviṣaye kātyāyanaḥ |
ādhiṃ duṣṭena lekhyena bhuṅkte yam ṛṇikād dhanī |
nṛpo dhanaṃ dāpayitvā ādhilekhyaṃ vināśayet ||
dravyabhoganiṣpattyarthaṃ vṛddhihānir apy anusaṃdheyā | dhanapradāne bandhaṃ vā lagnakaṃ vā gṛhṇīyād ity uktam | tatra bandhasvarūpam uktvā lagnakasvarūpam āha |
darśane prayaye dāne ṛṇidravyārpaṇe tathā |
catuṣprakāraḥ pratibhūḥ śāstradṛṣṭo manīṣibhiḥ ||
pratyayo – viśvāsaḥ | dānam – ṛṇāpākaraṇārtham arthārpaṇam | ṛṇino dravyaṃ ṛṇidravyam | tasyārpaṇaṃ gṛhopakaraṇāder arpaṇam | [247]
catuṣprakārasvarūpam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
āhaiko darśayāmīti sādhur eṣo ‘paro ‘bravīt |
dātāham etad draviṇam arpayāmy aparo ‘bravīt ||
eko – darśanapratibhūḥ | aham enaṃ prapalāyanapravṛttaṃ darśayiṣyāmīti prātibhāvyaṃ darśayiṣyann (bhajann?) āha | aparaḥ – pratyayaḥ pratibhūviśeṣaḥ sādhur avañcako matpratyayenāsya dhanaṃ dehīti brūte | dānapratibhūr ayaṃ yadā na dadāti draviṇaṃ gṛhītaṃ savṛddhikaṃ tadā tasya draviṇasya ahaṃ dāteti vadati | aparaḥ – ṛṇidravyārpaṇapratibhūḥ yadāyaṃ gṛhītaṃ dhanaṃ na dadāti tadāham etadīyārtham arpayāmīti | ayam eva ṛṇipratibhūdānapratibhuvor bhedaḥ | vivādanirṇayāya dṛṣṭam apy adṛṣṭaṃ vā pramāṇaṃ yatra kālavyavadhānena bhaviṣyati tatrāpi pramāṇāya pratibhūr bhāvyaḥ | dāsādau viśvāsāya coratvādisaṅkāyāṃ pratibhūr bhāvyaḥ | yad āha kātyāyanaḥ |
dāsopasthānavādeṣu viśvāsaḥ śapathāya ca |
lagnakaṃ kārayed evaṃ yathāyogaṃ viparyaye ||
viparyaye – kāryavyatyāsaviṣaye | yathāyogam – yathāsaṃbhavam | lagnakaḥ – pratibhūḥ | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
darśanapratibhūr yas tu kāle ca darśayet |
yady asau darśayet tatra moktavyaḥ pratibhūr bhavet || iti |
adarśane manuḥ |
yo yasya pratibhūs tiṣṭhed darśanāyeha mānavaḥ |
adarśayaṃś ca taṃ tasya prayacchet sadhanaṃ nṛṇām || [248]
ṛṇam atra savṛddhikam iti | “pratibhūputradeyatve avṛddhikam” iti smaraṇāt | kātyāyanas tu viśeṣam āha |
naṣṭasyānveṣaṇārthaṃ tu deyaṃ pakṣatrayaṃ param |
yady asau darśayet tatra moktavyaḥ pratibhūr bhavet ||
kāle ‘py atīte pratibhūḥ yadīdaṃ nava darśayet |
sa tam arthaṃ pradāpyaḥ syād ṛṇe caivaṃ vidhiḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
atra pakṣatrayam iti avadhibhūtakālopalakṣaṇārtham | yathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
naṣṭasyānveṣaṇe kālaṃ dadyāt pratibhuve dhanī |
deśānurūpataḥ pakṣaṃ māsaṃ sārdham athāpi vā ||
ataḥ pakṣatrayād ūrdhvaṃ deyam iti niyamārthaṃ vacanam | ṛṇidravyārpaṇapratibhūḥ pramāṇakaraṇe vivādāspadaṃ dhanaṃ dadyāt | ṛṇiny apratikurvati tad dravyam arpayet | abhayam – pratibhayopasthitau tatpratīkāraṃ ācaret | pramāṇapratibhūḥ pramāṇakaraṇaṃ vivādāspadaṃ dhanaṃ dadyāt | dāsyādyapahṛtaṃ punar alabdhaṃ ced viśvāsapratibhūr mūlyadvāreṇa dadyād ityādyās tatra tatra kalpanīyāḥ |
darśanapratibhūr yatra mṛtaḥ prātyayiko ‘pi vā |
na tatputrā ṛṇaṃ dadyur dadyur dānāya ye sthitāḥ ||
yadā darśanapratibhūḥ prātyayiko ‘pi vā pratibhūr diṣṭaṃ gataḥ tadā tayoḥ putrāḥ prātibhāvyāyātaṃ patiṛkam ṛṇaṃ na dadyuḥ | yas tu dānāya sthitaḥ pratibhūr diṣṭaṃ gataḥ tasya putrā dadyuḥ | putraśabdena pautrādīnāṃ vṛddhidānapratiṣedhena mūlyadānapratiṣedha ity avagantavyam,
[249]        ṛṇaṃ paitāmahaṃ pautraḥ prātibhāvāgataṃ sutaḥ |
samaṃ dadyāt tu tatputro na dadyād iti niścayaḥ ||
iti vyāsavacanāt | prātibhāvyavyatiriktaṃ paitāmaham ṛṇaṃ pautraḥ samam – yadgṛhītaṃ tāvad eva dadyāt na vṛddhim | tathā suto ‘pi prātibhāvyāgataṃ pitryam ṛṇaṃ samam eva dadyāt | tayoḥ pautraputrayoḥ sutau prapautrapautrau ca prātibhāvyāyātaṃ ṛṇaṃ yathākramagṛhītadhanau dāpyāv iti vacanārthaḥ | adhamarṇasya vittahīnatve khādakatve ca pratibhūr vittavān smṛtaḥ | tasya putreṇa mūlyam eva dātavyaṃ na vṛddhiḥ |
khādako vittahīnaś cet lagnako vittavān yadi |
mūlaṃ tasya bhaved deyaṃ na vṛddhiṃ dātum arhati ||
yatra darśanapratibhūḥ pratyayapratibhūr vā bandhakaṃ paryāptaṃ gṛhītvā pratibhūr jātaḥ tatra tatputrā api tasmād eva bandhakāt prātibhāvyāyātam ṛṇaṃ dadyur eva |
gṛhītvā bandhakaṃ yatra darśane yaḥ sthito bhavet |
vinā pitrā dhanāt tasmād dāpyaḥ syāt tad ṛṇaṃ sutaḥ || iti |
darśanagrahaṇaṃ pratyayasyāpy upalakṣakam | vinā pitreti pitari prete dūradeśaṃ gate vā | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
ādyau tu vitathe dāpyau tatkālāveditaṃ dhanam |
uttarau tu visaṃvāde tau vinā tatsutau tathā ||
ādyau - darśanapratyayapratibhuvau | vitathe aham enaṃ darśayiṣyāmi sādhur eṣa ity evaṃvidhayoḥ pratibhūvākyayor mithyātve | uttarau dānadvaryārpaṇapratibhuvau | vasaṃvāde – śāṭhyādinā dhane ṛṇike nāpravṛtte | tau vinā – uttarayoḥ pravāse vā maraṇe vā jāte | [250]
yathāha manuḥ |
ādātari punar dātā vijñātaprakṛtāv ṛṇam |
paścāt pratibhuvi prete parīpset kena hetunā ||
narā diṣṭadhanaś cet tu pratibhūḥ syād alaṃdhanaḥ |
svadhanād eva tad dadyān nirādiṣṭa iti sthitiḥ ||
nirādiṣaṃ nitarāṃ samarpitaṃ bandhutvena dhanaṃ yasyāsau nirādiṣṭadhanaḥ | alaṃdhanaḥ – paryāptadhanaḥ | atra pratibhūśabdena darśanapratibhuvaḥ putra upalakṣyate | anyathā paścāt pratibhuvi preta ity uktir virundhyād ity anekavyākhyātṛsaṃmatam | anekapratibhū(viṣaye)sthale viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
bahavas tu yadi svāṃśair dadyuḥ pratibhuvo dhanam |
ekacchāyāśriteṣv eṣu dhanikasya yathāruci ||
yady ekasmin prayoge dvau bahavo vā pratibhuvaḥ tadā tadṛṇaṃ vibhajya svāṃśair dadyuḥ | ekacchāyāśriteṣu – ekasyādharmarṇasya chāyāsādṛśyaṃ tām āśritāḥ adhamarṇo yathā kṛtsnadravyadānāya sthitaḥ tathā dānapratibhuvo ‘pi pratyekaṃ kṛtsnadravyadānāvisthitāḥ | evaṃ darśane yathāruci | pratyaye ca teṣv ekacchāyāśriteṣu dhanikasyottamarṇasya yathāruci – yathākāmam | ataś ca dhaniko vittādyapekṣayā yaṃ svārthaṃ prārthayate sa eva kṛtsnaṃ dadyāt, nāṃśataḥ | ekacchāyāśriteṣu yadi kaścid deśāntaraṃ gataḥ tatputraś ca saṃnihitaḥ tadā dhanikecchayā sa taṃ dāpyaḥ | mṛte tu kasmiṃscit svapitryaṃ savṛddhikaṃ dāpyaḥ | yad āha kātyāyanaḥ |
ekacchāyāpraviṣṭānāṃ dāpyo yas tatra dṛśyate |
proṣite tatsutaḥ sarvaṃ pitraṃśaṃ (pitryāṃśaṃ) tu mṛte tu saḥ ||
[251] iti | tatra viśeṣam āha hārītaḥ |
sarve pratibhuvo dāpyāḥ prātibhāvye pramoṣite |
ayam arthaḥ – caturvidhe prātibhāvye mithyābhūte sati taddhanaṃ rājñā dhanine dāpyāḥ pratibhuva iti vacanārthaḥ pūrvam evokta iti neha prapañcitaḥ | tad ayam atra niṣkarṣaḥ – ṛṇādānasamaye viśvāsārthaṃ bandhakaṃ lagnako vā kāryaḥ | atra bandhakaṃ gopyādhir bhogyādhiś ceti dvividham | tatra bhogyādhir dvaiguṇyanibandhanaḥ kālakṛtaś ceti dvividhaḥ | kālakṛtaḥ savṛddhiko ‘vṛddhikaś ceti dvividhaḥ | bhogyādhis tu sapratyayo ‘pratyayaḥ kṣayādhir anvādhiś ceti caturvidhaḥ | tatra gopyādhir yadi bhujyate tadā na vṛddhiḥ | atibhukto mūlanāśaḥ | ṛṇāparyāptaṃ ced api mūlanāśa eva ṛṇikena vāvaśiṣṭaṃ dātavyam | adhikaṃ dviguṇaṃ gṛhītvā avaśiṣṭaṃ ṛṇine tadabhāve tajjñātiṣu dvaiguṇyadhanaṃ dātavyam | dviguṇād ūrdhvam eva dhanaṃ datvā ādhir moktavyaḥ | kṣayādhau tu dviguṇe dhane tacchuddhabhogāt prāpte tadādhir moktavyaḥ | kālakṛte dvividhe kāle prāpte ādhir moktavyaḥ | bhogyādhāv atibhuktau bhuktānusāreṇa dhanaṃ dāpayet | anvādhis tu gṛhītasamadhasyaiva | tadadhike tu na sidhyati | sarvaṃ pāribhāṣikaṃ cet sidhyaty eva | kriyāntagopyādhau tu dṛśyasmakālam evādhiḥ sidhyati pāribhāṣikatvād iti tanmataṃ dūṣitam adhastāt | (saṃkarādayas tu) saṃskārādayas tu paribhāṣāvaśād eva siddhāḥ | lagnako ‘pi viśvāsapratibhūr viśvāsāpanaye dhanaṃ dāpyaḥ | darśanapratibhūs tu tadabhāve dhanaṃ dāpyaḥ | ubhayos tu deśāntaragatayoḥ mṛtayor vā tatputreṇa taddhanaṃ mūlamātram eva dātavyam | dānapratibhuvā ṛṇike [252] naṣṭe daridre vā jāte savṛddhikaṃ dhanaṃ dātavyam | tadabhāve tatputreṇāpi savṛddhikam eva dātavyam itīyaṃ gamanikā | atra ṛṇādānapratidānavidhim āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
yācamānāya dātavyam aprakālam ṛṇaṃ kṛtam |
pūrṇāvadhau śāntalābham abhāve ca pituḥ sutaiḥ ||
aprakālam – aprakṛṣṭakālam adīrghakālam iti yāvat | kṛtaṃ dīpotsavakāle pratideyam ṛṇam ity evaṃ sāvadhikatvena kṛtam | ayam arthaḥ – dīrghakālam ṛṇaṃ yācñānantaraṃ dātavyam | sāvadhikatvena kṛtam ṛṇam avadhau prāpte deyam | vṛddhyartham ṛṇaṃ lābhaśāntyanantaraṃ dātavyam | ṛṇikābhāve ‘pi tatsutair apy evam eva dātavyam, anyathā doṣasmaraṇāt |
tapasvī cāgnihotrī ca ṛṇavān mriyate yadi |
tapasyā cāgnihotraṃ ca sarvaṃ tad dhanino bhavet || iti |
tenāvaśyam ṛṇāpākaraṇaṃ kartavyam ity arthaḥ | tatra sasākṣikam eva kartavyam | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
sākṣimac ca bhaved yad vā tad dātavyaṃ sasākṣikam | iti |
sāmānyokteḥ pūrvasākṣyabhāve sākṣyantarasamakṣaṃ dhanaṃ dātavyam iti | atra viseṣam āha pitāmahaḥ |
avibhaktā ṛṇaṃ dadyuḥ pitryaṃ mātṛkam eva vā |
tadabhāve vibhaktāś ca na tu tena pratiśrutam ||
mātāpitṛsaṃbandhidravyābhāve tābhyāṃ pratiśrutaṃ na deyam iti varadarājaḥ | vyāsas tu |
ṛṇaṃ paitāmahaṃ pautraḥ prātibhāvyāgataṃ sutaḥ |
samaṃ dadyus tatsutau tu na dāpyāv iti niścayaḥ || [253]
prātibhāvyāgatam api pautreṇāpi dātavyam iti pradīpaḥ |
avibhakte kuṭumbārthaṃ yad ṛṇaṃ tu kṛtaṃ bhavet |
dadyus tad rikthinaḥ prete proṣite vā kuṭumbini ||
nāradaḥ |
pitṛvyeṇāvibhaktena bhrātrā vā yad ṛṇaṃ kṛtam |
mātrā vā yat kuṭumbārthe dadyus tad rikthino ‘khilam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
pitṛdravyaṃ bhrātṛputradāsaśiṣyānujīvibhiḥ |
yad gṛhītaṃ kuṭumbārthe tad gṛhī dātum arhati ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
kanyāvaivāhikaṃ caiva pretakārye ‘pi yat kṛtam |
etat sarvaṃ pradātavyaṃ kuṭumbena kṛtaṃ ca yat ||
atra manuḥ |
ṛṇaṃ dātum aśakto yaḥ kartum icchet punaḥ kriyām |
sa datvā nirjitāṃ vṛddhiṃ karaṇaṃ parivartayet ||
parivaryated iti – punar lekhyādikriyāṃ vartamānavatsarādicihnitāṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | atrāpi viśeṣam āha sa eva |
adarśayitvā tatraiva hiraṇyaṃ parivartayet |
yāvatī saṃbhaved vṛddhis tāvatīṃ dātum arhati ||
hiraṇyaśabdena nirjitā vṛddhir ucyate | nirjitāṃ vṛddhim adatvety arthaḥ | tatraiva – pūrvakṛtakaraṇa eva parivartayed iti – vṛddhiṃ mūlyatvenāropayed ity arthaḥ | yathāśakti stokaṃ datvā lekhyam upagatākhyam uttamarṇād grahītavyam | uttamarṇenāpi tad avaśyaṃ deyam,
[254]        dhanī copagataṃ dadyāt svahastaparicihnitam |
iti smṛteḥ | ayaṃ ca pakṣaḥ putrābhāve veditavyaḥ | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “asamagradāne lekhyāsaṃnidhāne cottamarṇaḥ svalikhitaṃ dadyāt” | ṛṇikāyeti śeṣaḥ | patrasadbhāve tu pratidattadravyaṃ patrapṛṣṭhe lekhyaṃ syān nyūnadravyaṃ viśodhayet | ayam arthaḥ – nyūnaṃ yāvadgṛhītadravyam | kṛtsnapratidānāśaktau śaktyanusāreṇa kiṃcid dattam iti viśuddhaye – prajñaptaya ity arathaḥ | kṛtsnadravyapradāne patraṃ chettavyam ity arthaḥ prāptam | tad eva spaṣṭīkṛtaṃ nāradena |
gṛhītvopagataṃ dadyād ṛṇikāyodayaṃ dhanī | iti |
udayam – vṛddhyarthaṃ gṛhītvā upagatam – upagatārthasaṃketaṃ patrapṛṣṭhe upagatapatraṃ vā ṛṇikāya dadād iti | kecid etad vacanaṃ pratidinaṃ vā pratimāsaṃ vā paribhāṣayā svīkartavyadravyaviṣayam iti manyante | apare tu udayam ṛṇikenopārjitaṃ gṛhītvā dhanī ṛṇikāyopagataṃ kārayet balātkāreṇāpi,
dhanadānāsahaṃ baddhvā svādhīnaṃ karma kārayet |
iti kātyāyanasmaraṇāt iti | karmakaraṇānarhaṃ tu bandhanāgāre vāsayet |
aśaktau bandhanāgāre pravaeśo brāhmaṇād ṛte | iti |
ataś ca samajātim api parīkṣīṇaṃ yathocitaṃ karma kārayet | brahmaṇagrahaṇam utkṛṣṭajāter upalakṣaṇam | ataś ca kṣatriyādir api parīkṣīṇo vaiśyādeḥ śanair dāpya iti | etad eva spaṣṭīkṛtaṃ manunā |
karmaṇāpi samaṃ kuryād dhaniko nādhamarṇikaḥ |
samāpakṛṣṭajātiś ced dadyāc chreyāṃs tu tac chanaiḥ ||
[255] iti | atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
nānarṇasamavāye tu yad yat pūrvam ṛṇaṃ bhavet |
tat tad evāgrato deyaṃ rājñe syāc chrotriyād anu ||
śrotriyo ‘tra brāhmaṇamātraśālī na tu śrutādhyayanaśālī | rājādyapekṣayā utkṛṣṭajātiparatvena atra śrotriyapadaprayogāt | gṛhītajātikramo ‘tra grāhyaḥ | brāhmaṇasya pūjyatayā anugrāhyatvāt | evaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ vaiśyaśūdrayor vā yugapad upasthāne vā varṇakrameṇa dātavyam, pūrvapūrvasya śreṣṭhatvena anugrāhyatvāt | evaṃkrameṇa ṛṇāpākaraṇe kriyamāṇe “hīnajātiṃ parīkṣīṇam” ityādy anusaṃdheyam iti mantavyam | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
ekāhe likhitaṃ yatra tatra kuryād ṛṇaṃ samam |
grahaṇaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ lābham anyathā tu yathākramam ||
anyathā – ekāhādivyatirekeṇa aharbhedād ity arthaḥ | atra kvacid apavādam āha sa eva |
yasya dravyeṇa yat paṇyaṃ sādhitaṃ yo vibhāvayet |
tad dravyam ṛṇikenaiva dātavyaṃ tatra nānyathā ||
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
yācamānāya dātavyam aprakālam ṛṇaṃ kṛtam |
pūrṇāvadhau śāntalābham abhāve ca pituḥ sutaiḥ ||
tathā ca nāradaḥ |
icchanti pitaraḥ putrān svārthahetor yatas tataḥ |
uttamarṇādhamarṇābhyāṃ mām ayaṃ mokṣayiṣyati ||
ataḥ putreṇa jātena svārtham utsṛjya yatnataḥ |
ṛṇāt pitā mocanīyo yathā na narake patet ||
[256] iti | atroktam ānṛṇyam “jāyamāno vai brāhmaṇas tribhir ṛṇavā jāyate” iti śrutyuktam adhamarṇaṃ pūrvam evoktam | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
nāprāptavyavahāras tu pitary uparate kvacit |
kāle tu vidhinā deyaṃ vaseyur narake ‘nyathā ||
anuparate ‘pi pitari sutaiḥ pitṛkṛtam ṛṇaṃ pitari pratidānāsamarthe deyam ity āha sa eva |
vidyamāne ‘pi rogārte svadeśāt proṣite ‘tha vā |
viṃśatsaṃvatsarād deyaṃ ṛṇaṃ pitṛkṛtaṃ sutaiḥ ||
viṃśatsaṃvatsarāt – pravāsād ārabhyetyādi śeṣa iti candrikā | bṛhaspatis tu |
sāṃnidhye ‘pit pituḥ putraiḥ ṛṇaṃ deyaṃ vibhāvitam |
jātyandhapatitonmattakṣayaśvitrādirogiṇaḥ ||
tathā ca nāradaḥ |
pitary uparate putrā ṛṇaṃ dadyur yathāṃśataḥ |
vibhaktā vāvibhakā vā yo vā tām udvahed dhuram ||
ptṛṇe vidyamāne tu na ca putro dhanaṃ haret |
deyaṃ tad dhanikadravyaṃ mṛte gṛhṇaṃs tu dāpayet ||
mṛte pitari dravyaṃ gṛhṇaṃs tu dāpyate – mṛte pitari dravyaṃ gṛhṇann eva putro dāpyata ity arthaḥ | pitṛkṛtasvakṛtarṇasamavāye tu ātmīyavat pitryaṃ deyam | pitṛkṛam ādau deyam,
ādau pitṛkṛtam deyaṃ paścād ātmīyam eva ca |
ṛṇam ātmīyavat pitryaṃ putrair deyaṃ vibhāvitam ||
iti bṛhaspatismaraṇāt | adeyam āha sa eva |
saurākṣikavṛthādānakāmakrodhapratiśrutam |
[257]        prātibhāvyaṃ daṇḍaśulkaśeṣaṃ putrair na dāpayet ||
sauram – surāsaṃpādananimittakam | ākṣikam – dyūtaparājayanimittakam | vṛthādānam – nartakādidattam | śeṣaṃ sugamam | vṛthādānaviṣaye smṛtyantaram |
dūrte vandini malle ca kuvaidye kaitave śave |
cāṭacāraṇayor eṣu dattaṃ bhavati niṣphalam ||
cāraṇāḥ – gāyakāḥ | cāṭāḥ – pūrvam uktāḥ | kātyāyanena pratiśrutam uktam |
likhitaṃ muktakaṃ cāpi deyaṃyat tu pratiśrutam |
parapūrvastriyai tat tu vindyāt kāmaśrutam ṛṇam ||
muktakam – lekhanarahitam | tathā krodhapratiśrutaṃ ca tenaivoktam |
yasya hiṃsāṃ samutpādya krodhād dravyaṃ vināśya vā |
uktaṃ tuṣṭikaraṃ yat tu vindyāt krodhapratiśrutam ||
ayam arthaḥ – yasya hiṃsāṃ dhanavināśaṃ vā krodhāt kṛtvā tatsantoṣāya dravyaṃ dāsyāmīti pratiśrutaṃ tad ṛṇaṃ krodhajātam iti | atra prātibhāvyaṃ darśanapratibhūdeyaṃ vivakṣitam | tathā ca manuḥ |
prātibhāvyaṃ vṛthādānam ākṣikaṃ saurikaṃ ca yat |
daṇḍaśulkāvaśeṣaṃ ca na putro dātum arhati ||
darśanaprātibhāvye tu vidhiḥ syāt pūrvacoditaḥ | iti |
pūrvodito vidhiḥ prātibhāvyaṃ putro dātum arhatīti vidhiḥ | daṇḍaśulkāveśeṣam iti padadvayasyārthas tu uśanasā spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ |
daṇḍaṃ vā daṇḍaśeṣaṃ vā śulkaṃ taccheṣam eva vā |
na dātavyaṃ tu putreṇa yac ca na vyāvahārikam || [258]
na vyāvahārikam – saurikam ity arthaḥ |
atha pautreṇa deyam ucyate | tatra manuḥ |
sadoṣaṃ vyāhataṃ pitrā naiva deyam ṛṇaṃ kvacit |
sadoṣam – śūrādivyasananimittatvena doṣayuktam | pitrā vyāhatam – nirākṛtam | pitrā svadṛṣṭam apahnutaṃ vā pramāṇasādhitaṃ ced deyam,
putrapautrai ṛṇaṃ deyaṃ nihnave sākṣibhāvitam |
iti yājñāvalkyokteḥ | atra na deyam ity adeyamātropalakṣaṇam | tenānyad api putreṣūktaṃ pautreṣv api draṣṭavyam | pauradeyaṃ tu tatra kātyāyanaḥ |
pitrā dṛṣṭam ṛṇaṃ yat tu kramāyātaṃ pitāmahāt |
nirdoṣaṃ noddhṛtaṃ putrair deyaṃ pautrais tu tad bhṛguḥ ||
asyārthaḥ – pitṛkṛtam – svapitṛkṛtatvena pitrā niḥsaṃdigham āgatam | pitāmahāt kramaprāptaṃ nirdoṣam – adeyatvāpādakadoṣarahitam | putrair noddhṛtam – putrair duścikitsarogādivaśād anapākṛtam | tathā ca sa eva |
yad deyaṃ pitṛbhir nityaṃ tadabhāve tu taddhanāt |
taddhanaṃ putrapaurair vā deyaṃ tatsvāmine tadā ||
pitṛdhanasadbhāve tasmād eva ṛṇam | dhanābhāve ‘pi putratvāt pautratvād deyam ity arthaḥ | pautrais tu prātibhāvyaṃ punaḥ sarvaprakāram api na deyam ity uktaṃ prāk | atra viṣṇunā viśeṣa uktaḥ: “dhanagrāhiṇi prete pravrajite dvidaśāḥ samāḥ proṣite vā tatputrapaurair dhanaṃ deyam, nātaḥ param anīpsubhiḥ” iti | pravrajite – saṃnyasta ity arthaḥ | dvidaśāḥ – viṃśatiḥ | samāḥ – saṃvatsarāḥ | dhanagrāhiṇi viṃśativarṣavyāpitayā pravāse kṛta ity arthaḥ | “nātaḥ param anīpsubhiḥ” ity asyāyam arthaḥ – [259] putrapautrebhyaḥ param ūrdhvam | ye jātāḥ taiḥ prapautrādibhir anīpsubhir na deyam iti | etac ca viṃśativarṣād ṛṇādānaṃ pūrvam ayācitadravyaviṣayam | yācitaṃ ced dātavyam eva | yathāha hārītaḥ |
yāvat sthitipramāṇaṃ syāl likhitaṃ nirapākṛti |
apākaraṇaṃ nāmātra dṛṣṭadhanapraveśalekhanaṃ pratipatraṃ sākṣyādisaṃyaksthale vyavahāraniveśanaṃ cety evamādi | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ |
lekhasya pṛṣṭhe ‘bhilikhed yad vā datvarṇiko dhanam |
dhanī vopagataṃ dadyāt svahastaparicihnitam ||
yadā adhamarṇikaḥ sakalam ṛṇaṃ dātum asamarthaḥ tadā śaktyanusāreṇa datvā pūrvakṛtalekhasya pṛṣṭhe abhilikhed etāvan mayā dattam iti | uttarmarṇo vā upagataṃ prāptaṃ dhanaṃ tasyaiva lekhasya pṛṣṭhe dadyād abhilikhet etāvan mayā labdham iti svahastalikhitākṣaracihnitam | yad vā – upagataṃ preveśapatraṃ svahastalikhitacihnitam adhamarṇāyottamarṇo dadyāt | etac ca pūrvam eva praticoditam api spaṣṭārthaṃ prasaṅgād uktam ity apaunaruktyam | ṛṇe tu kṛtsne datte lekhyaṃ kiṃ kartavyam ity āha sa eva |
datvarṇaṃ pāṭayel lekhyaṃ śuddhyai vānyatra (-nyat tu?) kārayet |
krameṇa sakṛd eva vā kṛtsnam ṛṇaṃ datvā pūrvakṛtalekhyaṃ pāṭayet – bhindyāt | yadā tu durgadeśāvasthitaṃ lekhyaṃ naṣṭaṃ vā tadā śuddhyai – adhamarṇatvanivṛttyartham anyal lekhyaṃ kārayet | uttamarṇo ‘pi śuddhipatram adhamarṇāya dadyād ity arthaḥ | sasākṣika ṛṇe kṛtsnaṃ dātavyam ity āha sa eva |
[260]        sākṣimac ca bhaved vā tad dātavyaṃ sasākṣikam | iti |
yat tu sasākṣikam ṛṇaṃ tat pūrvasamakṣam eva dadyāt | ṛṇādāne ṛṇe tatputraḥ pautro naptā dāyabhāk ca patnyādiś ca ṛṇāpākaraṇe ‘dhikāriṇaḥ | teṣāṃ kramas tu – prathamaṃ putraḥ tadabhāve pautraḥ tadabhāve naptā ceti krama it niṣkarṣaḥ | kartrantarasamavāye kramam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
rikthagrāhī ṛṇaṃ dāpyo yoṣidgrāhas tathaiva ca |
putro ‘nanyāśritadravyaḥ putrahīnasya rikthinaḥ || iti |
asyārthaḥ – putrahīnasya – yogyaputrādividhurasya rikthino dhanavato mṛtasya yad ṛṇaṃ tad rikthagrāhī dāpyaḥ | rājeti śeṣaḥ | prāptinaśe (nāśe?) taddāyaharo bhrātrādir dāpanīya ity arthaḥ | tadabhāve yoṣidgrāhaḥ | yaś ca tisṝṇāṃ svairiṇīnām antimāṃ yaś ca punarbhuvāṃ tisṝṇāṃ svairiṇānāṃ prathamāṃ tadīyāṃ yoṣitav atikrāntaniṣedhaḥ praigṛhṇāti sa yoṣidgrāhaḥ | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
parapūrvāḥ striyas tv anyāḥ sapta proktā yathākramam |
punarbhūr dvividhā tāsāṃ svairiṇī tu caturvidhā ||
kanyā vākṣatayonir vā pāṇigrahaṇadūṣitā |
punarbhūḥ prathamaṃ proktā punaḥsaṃskārakarmaṇi ||
deśadharmānepekṣā strī gurubhir yā pradīyate |
utpannasāhasānyasmai sā dvitīyā prakīrtitā ||
strīprasūtāprasūtā va patyāv eva tu jīvati |
kāmāt samāśrayed anyaṃ prathamā svairiṇīti sā ||
kaumāraṃ patim utsṛjya yā tv anyaṃ puruṣaṃ śritā |
[261]        punaḥ patyur gṛhaṃ yāyāt sā dvitīyā prakīrtitā ||
mṛte bhartari tu prāptān devarādīn apāsya vā |
upagacchet paraṃ kāmāt sā tṛtīyā prakīrtitā ||
prāptā deśād dhanakrītā kṣutpipāsāturā tu yā |
tavāham ity upagatā sā caturthī prakīrtitā ||
antimā svairiṇīnāṃ yā prathamā ca punarbhuvām |
ṛṇaṃ tayoḥ patikṛtaṃ dadyād yas tām upāśnute ||
yā tu saptadinenaiva sāpatyā vānyam āśrayet |
sā vā dadyād ṛṇaṃ bhartur utsṛjed vā tathaiva tām ||
adhanasya hy aputrasya mṛtasyāpaiti yaḥ striyam |
ṛṇaṃ voḍhuḥ sa bhajate naiva cānyadhanaṃ smṛtam || iti |
atra dhaneti prakṛtadhanayuktety (?) arthaḥ sa – evaṃvidhayoṣidgrāhaḥ | ṛṇaṃ dāpyaḥ tadabhāve anyāśritadravyaḥ | anyam āśritam aprāptaṃ atd dravyaṃ yasya so ‘nyāśritadravyaḥ – svagāmidhana ity arthaḥ | tato nañsamāse tadviparītaḥ | paraprāptadāyaḥ klībatvādidoṣair dāyaharaṇāyogyaṃ yo vidhīyate sa ca dāpya iti | yat tu rikthagrahābhāve yoṣidgrāhaḥ yoṣidgrāhābhāve anyāśritadravyaḥ putraḥ tadabhāve putrapautrahīnasya prapautrādayo rikhino yadi syuḥ ta eva dāpyāḥ | yadi prapautrādīnāṃ rikthagrahaṇaṃ nāsti tai ṛṇaṃ na deyam ity evaṃ vyākhyāna, “putrahīnasya rikthinaḥ” ity atra rikthaśabdena yoṣidgrāha evocyate,
ṛṇaṃ voḍhuḥ sa bhajate naiva cānyadhanaṃ smṛtam |
iti nāradavacanāt | ataś ca yoṣidgrāhābhāve putraḥ ṛṇaṃ dāpyaḥ putrābhāve yoṣidgrāha iti parasparaviruddham | tathā hi – yoṣidgrāhābhāve [262] putra ity ukteḥ yoṣidgrāhasya mukhyatā putrasyānukalpatā bhavati “somābhāve pūtikān abhiṣuṇoti” itivat | evaṃ sthite putrābhāve yoṣidgrāha ity ucyamāne pūrvoktamukhyānukalpavirodhāpatteḥ | anyonyavyāghātaḥ | api caivaṃ sati yadobhau saṃnihitau tadā kaścid api dāpyo na syāt | anyatarābhāvam upajīvyaiva anyatarasya dāpanābhidhānāt | ubhayasaṃnidhau ca anyatarābhāvāsaṃbhavāt | ataś ca ṛṇāpākaraṇam eva apākṛtaṃ bhavet ity abhisaṃdhāya –
dhanastrīhāriputrāṇām ṛṇabhāg yo dhanaṃ haret |
putro ‘sutaḥ strīdhaninoḥ strīhārī dhanaputrayoḥ ||
iti nāradavacanam avalambya virodhaparihāraḥ kṛtaḥ | ataś ca vyākhyānadvaye “putrahīnasya rikthinaḥ” iti bahuvacanāntapadam aparākaiḥ vyākhyāyate | dhanastrīhāriputrāḥ kasya dāpyā ity ākāṅkṣayā uttamarṇasya dāpyāḥ | tatputrādeḥ | putrādyabhāve kasya dāpyā ity ākāṅkṣāyāṃ “putrahīnasya rikthinaḥ” iti putrādyanvayahīnasya uttamarṇasya yo rikthī rikthagrahaṇayogyaḥ sapiṇḍādiḥ – tasya rikthino dāpyā iti | atra “rikthinaḥ” iti ṣeṣṭhyekavacanam | etadvyākhyānakramaṃ vijñānayoginā pūrvācārecchayānugacchatā adhikṣepasamādhānābhyām atikleśam āśritya kṛtam | yat tu – pūrvatra putrapautraiḥ ṛṇaṃ deyam iti pitṛkṛtarṇāpākaraṇe klaibyādidoṣarāhityenāṃśagrahaṇa evārhāḥ putrāḥ pautrāś cādhikāriṇo darśitāḥ | svarasataḥ putrādiśabdānām api kulaputrādipartvasya ucitatvāt | atredam āśaṅkyate – yadā na santi putrādayaḥ kas tadīyam ṛṇam apākuryād ity asyām ākāṅkṣāyām anyān kartṝn kramikān nirūpayitum idaṃ vacanam ārabhyata iti maduktarītyā [263] ṛjumārgeṇa vyākhyāne siddhe vakramārgānusaraṇaṃ heyam | kātyāyanas tu viśeṣam āha |
bālaputrādikartā ca trātāraṃ yānyam āśritāḥ |
āśritas tadṛṇaṃ dadyāt bālaputrādiviśrutam ||
dadyāt – dāpayed ity arthaḥ | etad api spaṣṭārtham uktam | patiṛṇāpākaraṇe patnyāś cādhikārāt bālānām anadhikāraś ceti | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
pitṛvyabhātṛputrastrīdāsaśiṣyānujīvibhiḥ |
yad gṛhītaṃ kuṭumbārthe tad grāhī dātum arhati ||
tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
proṣitasya mṛte vāpi kuṭumbārtham ṛṇaṃ kṛtam |
dātuḥ strīmātṛśiṣyair vā dādyāt putreṇa vā bhṛguḥ ||
nārado ‘pi |
putriṇī tu samutsṛjya putraṃ strī yānyam āśrayet |
tasyā dravyaṃ (dhanaṃ) haret sarvaṃ niḥsvāyāḥ putra eva tu ||
upaplavanimittaṃ ca vidyād āpatkṛtaṃ tu tat |
kanyāvaivāhikaṃ caiva pretakāryaṃ tu yat kṛtam ||
etat sarvaṃ pradātavyaṃ kuṭumbena kṛtaṃ prabhoḥ |
kuṭumbārtham aśakte tu gṛhītaṃ vyādhitena vā ||
kuṭumbabharaṇāśakte kuṭimbini sati prabhor deyam ity arthaḥ | etac ca pratiśrutānumoditaviṣayam,
deyaṃ pratiśrutaṃ yat syād yac ca syād anumoditam |
iti kātyāyanasmaraṇāt | atra striyās tu viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
bhartukāmena yā bhartrā uktā deyam ṛṇaṃ tvayā |
aprasannāpi sā dāpyā dhanaṃ yady āśritaṃ striyā || [264]
apratipannā cet – patidhanābhāve ‘pi karmakarāpi sevayā dhanaṃ saṃpādya ṛṇāpākaraṇaṃ kartavyam | patidhanasaṃbandhe tu yato rikthaṃ tata eva ṛṇāpākaraṇanyāyāt autsargikarṇāpākaraṇe adhikāro vidhavāyā ity āha aparārkaḥ | candrikākāras tu – atra arthād evānāśritabhartṛdhanāpi apratipannā cet na dāpyeti gamyate iti | atrottamarṇasantāne nāradaḥ |
brāḥmaṇasya tu yad deyaṃ sānvayasya na cāsti naḥ |
nirvapet tu sakulyeṣu tadabhāve ‘sya bandhuṣu ||
yadā tu na sakulyāḥ syuḥ na ca saṃbandhibāndhavāḥ |
tadā dadyād dvejebhyas tu teṣv asatsv apsu nikṣipet ||
atra brāhmaṇagrahaṇaṃ agner apy upalakṣaṇam | nikṣepo nāma homaḥ | yathāha saṃgrahakāraḥ |
dravyam yady adhamarṇasthaṃ kvacid brāhmaṇagaṃ bhavet |
sutādibrāhmaṇāntānāṃ rikthabhājām asaṃbhave ||
palāśasy palāśena juhuyān madhyamena tu |
yat kusīdam iti prāsyed atha vāpsv eva tad dhanam || iti |
yat kusīdaṃ mayy anenetyādimantra ucyate ||
iti śrīvīragajapatigauḍeśvara-navakoṭikarṇāṭakaluburigeśvara-
jamunāpurādhīśvara-huśanasāhisuratrāṇaśaraṇarakṣaṇa-
śrīdurgāvarputra-paramapavitracaritra-rājādhirājarājaparameśvara-
śrīpratāparudramahādeva-mahārājaviracite
smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe ṛṇādānākhyapadasya vilāsaḥ
athopanidhyākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
pūrvam upacayāpekṣayā parahaste dattam ṛṇam, tadanapekṣayā rakṣaṇārtham evānyahaste dattaṃ dravyam upanidhir iti ṛṇādānānantaram upanidher avasaraḥ | upanidhir nāma karaṇḍasthasvarūpasaṃkhyāviśeṣakathanarahitaṃ mudritam anyahaste rakṣaṇārthaṃ visrambhād arpyate | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhājanastham anākhyāya haste ‘nyasya yad arpyate |
dravyaṃ tad aupanidhikaṃ pratideyaṃ tathaiva tat || iti |
nāradas tūpanidhinikṣepayor bhedam āha |
asaṃkhyātam avijñātaṃ samudraṃ yan nidhīyate |
taj jānīyad upanidhiṃ nikṣepaṃ gaṇitaṃ vidhuḥ || iti |
atra viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
kulaje vṛttasaṃpanne dharmajñe satyavādini |
mahāpakṣe dhanī nyāyye nikṣepaṃ nikṣiped budhaḥ || iti |
mahāpakṣe – bandhuśālini | nyāsasya tu svarūpam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
rājacorādiṣu bhayād dāyādānāṃ ca vañcanāt |
sthāpyate ‘nyagṛhe dravyaṃ nyāsaḥ sa parikīrtitaḥ || iti |
upanidhinikṣepāṇāṃ svarūpam aṣṭādaśapadasvarūpanirūpaṇāvasare kathitam api vyavahitatvā saṃnidhānārtham avagantavyam | etac ca [266] yathādattaṃ tathāiva pratideyam, “pratideyaṃ tathaiva tat” iti yājñavalkyasmṛteḥ | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
sasākṣikaṃ raho dattaṃ dvividhaṃ samudāhṛtam |
putravat paripālyaṃ tad vinaśyad (-śyaty?) anapekṣayā ||
dadato yad bhavet puṇyaṃ haimārūpyāmbarādikam |
tat syāt pālayato nyāsaṃ yathaiva śaraṇāgatam ||
bhartṛdrohe yathā nāryāḥ puṃsaḥ putrasuhṛdvadhe |
doṣo bhavet tathā nyāse bhakṣitopekṣite nṛṇām ||
nyāsadravyaṃ na gṛhṇīyāt tannāśas tv ayaśaskaraḥ | iti |
atra nyāsapadena upnidhyādayaḥ prabhedā gṛhyante | rāmāyaṇe tu,
pāduke cāsya rājyāya nyāsaṃ datvā punaḥ punaḥ |
iti pādukayoḥ nyāstvakathanam, tathaiva pretideyam iti:
nyāsadravyaṃ na gṛhṇīyāt tannāśas tv ayaśaskaraḥ |
ityādidharmāṇāṃ tatra pratidānārtham ity avagantavyam | atra pratiprasavam āha sa eva |
daivarājopaghātena yadi tannāśam āpnuyāt |
grahītṛdravyasahitaṃ tatra doṣo na vidyate ||
grahītur iti śeṣaḥ | grahītṛdravyasahitam ity etasya upekṣādyabhāvaniścāyakatvenoktatvāt grahītṛdravyasattve ‘pi tannāśakāraṇāntarādyapekṣāsadbhāve doṣa evety avagantavyam | daivarājagrahaṇaṃ grahītṛsamādheyanimittopalakṣaṇārtham | yājñavalkyo viśeṣam āha |
na dāpyo ‘pahṛtaṃ tat tu rājadaivikataskaraiḥ | [267]
jihmakāritam iti śeṣaḥ | ajihmakārite tu dāpya eva yathāha nāradaḥ |
grahītuḥ saha yo’rthena naṣṭaḥ sa dāyinaḥ |
daivarājakṛte tadvan na cet taj jihmakāritam || iti |
yata āha sa eva ¦
bhreṣaś cen mārgite datte dāpyo daṇḍyaś ca tatsamam |
svāminā mārgite – yācite yadi na dadāti tadā taduttarakālaṃ yady api rājādibhiḥ bhreṣo nāśaḥ saṃjātaḥ, tathāpi dravyaṃ mūlyakalpanayā dhanine grahītā dāpyaḥ rājñā ca tatsamaṃ daṇḍyaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhedenopekṣayā nyāsaṃ grahītā yadi nāśayet |
yācyamāno na dadyād vā dāpyas taṃ sodayaṃ bhavet || iti |
atrāha candrikākāraḥ – (Vya, p. 179) nāśe mūlyadvāreṇa dāpyaḥ anāśe tu svarūpata eva | (a?)kṛtavṛddhiprakaraṇe sāmānyenoktaṃ vṛddhiparimāṇaṃ nāśe grāhyam | adāne tu –
yācyamānaṃ na ced dadyād vardhate pañcakaṃ śatam |
iti viśeṣeṇa tatraivoktam | vṛddhiparimāṇam ūhyam | bhedenopekṣayeti vadan svīyadravyeṇa sahaivopekṣayā nāśe tu sodayavidhir neti darśayati | tena tatra “samaṃ dāpya upakṣitaḥ” iti smṛtyantaravihitamūlyamātram eva deyam | evaṃ ca yācyamānādattasya daivarājakṛte ‘pi nāśe sthāpakāya mūlamātraṃ deyaṃ pratyarpaṇavilambamātrāparādhena savṛddhikanyāsadānāyogāt | rājñe ca [268] tatsamo daṇḍo deya iti |
kecid etad vacanam anyathā vyācakṣate |
bhedenopekṣayā nyāsaṃ grahītā yadi nāśayet |
yācyamāno na dadyād vā dāpyas taṃ sodayaṃ bhavet || iti |
bhedaś chalanam | bhedena vā upakṣayā vā nyāsasya grahītā nyāsaṃ yadi nāśayet | nyāsagrahaṇaṃ nikṣepopanidhyor upasaṃgrahaṇārtham | mūlyasamam eva dāpyaḥ | samam eva daṇḍyaḥ | “yācyamāno na dadyād vā” ity atra vāśabdaḥ tuśabdārthaḥ | yadi yācyamānāvasare idānīṃ na dīyate cet vṛddhir dātavyety uktaṃ tena grahītrā yady aṅgīkriyate tadānāṃ nyāyata eva sodayaṃ dadyāt, grahītā yadi kāraṇāntarād vyāsaṅgavaśād vā pratyarpaṇāsahiṣṇutayā lobhād vā yācanānantaraṃ na dadāti tasya ṛṇādānapadāntarbhāvāt,
yācyamānam adattaṃ ced vardhate pañcakaṃ śatam |
iti kātyāyanaḥ pravartata iti āhuḥ |
tad asat, ṛṇādānopanidhyoḥ padayor bhedasya prakaraṇādu darśitatvāt | vṛddhiprakaraṇadharmā atraiva prasarantīti | ata evāha vyāsaḥ |
bhakṣite sodayaṃ dāpyaḥ samaṃ dāpya upekṣite |
kiṃcin nyūnaṃ dāpyaḥ syād dravyam ajñānanāśitam ||
atrāha varadarājaḥ: “bhakṣite sodayaṃ dāpyaḥ” iti upekṣayā nāśe samaṃ dāpyaḥ, apekṣyā nāśe caturthāṃsanyūna iti brahmavitpravara iti | tatraivoktaṃ varadarājena –
krayumūlyaṃ krayārthaś ca nikṣepo ‘pahnave tathā |
cakravṛddhyā vivardheta yāvat pañcaguṇaṃ bhavet || [269]
krayamūlyakrayārthau – kretṛvikretṛsaṃśayamūlakau |
nikṣepādharato ‘dhyardhaṃ trimāsāt triguṇaṃ bhavet |
ata ūrdhvaṃ vivardheta cakravṛddhivyavasthayā ||
upādhibhis tu yaḥ kaścit paradravyaṃ labhen naraḥ |
sahasā yaḥ sa hantavyaḥ prakāśaṃ vividhaiḥ .... || iti |
nīcaviṣayam etad iti varadarājaḥ | nīcaviṣaye ‘py upanidhivyatirikteti bhāruciḥ | atra viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
grahītāpahnute yatra sākṣibhiḥ śapathena vā |
vibhāvya dāpayen nyāsaṃ tatsamaṃ vinayaṃ tathā ||
etac ca sasākṣikaviṣayam | asākṣike rahasi sthāpitaṃ rahasy eva grāhyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
yo yathā nikṣiped dhaste yam arthaṃ yasya mānavaḥ |
sa tathaiva grahītavyo yathā dāyas tathā grahaḥ ||
dāyaḥ – sthāpanam | graho – grahaṇam | rahodatte nirṇayam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
rahodatte vidhau yatra visaṃvādaḥ prajāyate |
vibhāvakaṃ tatra divyam ubhayor api ca smṛtam ||
nidhir upanidhiḥ | ubhayor madhe ekasyety arthaḥ | atra nāradaḥ |
eṣa eva vidhir dṛṣṭo yācitānvāhitādiṣu |
śilpiṣūpanidhau nyāse pratinyāse tathaiva ca || iti |
eteṣu yācitādiṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ – upanidher yaḥ pratidānādividhiḥ sa eva veditavyaḥ | yācitaṃ nāma vivāhādyutsaveṣu vastrālaṅkārādi yācayitvā nītaṃ tathaiva dravyam anu – paścād anyasya haste svāmine dehīti nihitam | anvāhitanyāsalakṣaṇaṃ [270] tu kaiścid uktam – nyāso nāma gṛhasvāmino darśayitvā tatparokṣam eva gṛhajanahaste prakṣepaḥ | gṛhasvāmine nivedya samarpaṇīyam iti | evaṃ suvarṇakārādihaste kaṭakādinirmāṇāya nyastasya suvarṇāder nyāsaśabdavācyatām āha | pratinyāsas tu parasparaprayojanāpekṣayā tvayedaṃ madīyaṃ rakṣaṇīyaṃ mayedaṃ tvadīyaṃ rakṣyate iti paribhāṣāmūlakaḥ | bṛhaspatis tu viśeṣam āha |
anvāhite yācitake śilpinyāse sabandhake |
eṣa evodito dharmas tathaiva śaraṇāgate || iti |
śilpiviṣaye jīrṇavastrādāv upaghāte na doṣaḥ | nūtanavastrādāv upaghāte samaṃ dāpyaṃ kuvindādau rajakādau ca | “rājadaivopaghāte ca na doṣaḥ” ityādau vyavahāre pūrvoktanayair eva nirṇayaḥ kāryaḥ | rajakādau viśeṣa uttaratra vakṣyate ||
iti śrīpratāparudradevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇde upanidhyākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
atha saṃbhūyasamutthānākyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
atra nāradaḥ |
vaṇikprabhṛtayo yatra karma saṃbhūya kurvate |
saṃbhūya ca samutthānaṃ vyavahārapadaṃ smṛtam ||
ṛṇādāne prayuktadhanād utpannopacayasya ekaniṣṭhatvam atrānekaniṣṭhatvam iti ṛṇādānānantaram asya padasyāvasaro nyāyyaḥ | tathāpy etad anantaram upanidher avakāśābhāvād uktanyāyena ṛṇādānānantaram evopanidheḥ praveśa iti paścād asya padasyāvasara iti | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
phalahetor upāyena karma saṃbhūya kurvatām |
ādhārabhūtaḥ prakṣepas tenottiṣṭheyur aṃśataḥ ||
ādhārabhūtaḥ – upāyabhūtaḥ | prakṣepo – dravyaprakṣepaḥ |
same ‘tirikto hīna vā yasyāṃśo yatra yādṛśaḥ |
āyavyayau yathāvṛddhis tasya tatra tathā nidhiḥ ||
alpāṃśasyālpaḥ, mahāṃśasya mahān ity arthaḥ|
āyavyayajñaiḥ śucibhiḥ śūraiḥ kāryā sahakriyā |
kulīnadakṣānalasaiḥ prājñair bhāṇḍakavedibhiḥ |
nāṇakavedibhir iti kvacit pāṭhaḥ | nāṇakas tattaddeśīyavibhinnamudrāṅkitā dīnārādayaḥ | vyāsas tu saṃbhūyakāriṇāṃ kāryam āha |
asamakṣaṃ samakṣaṃ vā vañcayantaḥ parasparam |
nānāpaṇyānusārāt te prakuryuḥ krayavikrayau ||
[272]        saṅgopayanto bhāṇḍāni śulkaṃ dadyuś ca te ‘dhvani |
anyathā dviguṇaṃ dāpyāḥ śulkasthānād bahiḥ sthitāḥ || iti |
atha vā sarvānujñayā sarveṣāṃ kāryam eka eva kuryāt |
bahūnāṃ saṃmato yas tu dadyād eko dhanaṃ naraḥ |
karaṇaṃ kārayed bhāve sarvair eva kṛtaṃ bhavet ||
saṃbhūyakāriṇāṃ mitho vivāde vyāsa eva |
parīkṣakāḥ sākṣiṇaś ca ta evoktāḥ parasparam |
saṃdigdhārthe vañcanāyāṃ na ced vidveṣasaṃyutāḥ |
yadā tu vidveṣasaṃyutāḥ tadāpy āha sa eva |
yaḥ kaścid vañcakas teṣāṃ vijñātaḥ krayavikraye |
śapathaiḥ sa viśodhyaḥ syāt sarvavāde ‘py ayaṃ vidhiḥ ||
viśodhyaḥ sabhyair iti śeṣaḥ | etac ca sāṃyātrikaviṣayam | itaratra sākṣyantarasadbhāvād iti lakṣmīdharaḥ | vañcakatve pramāṇasiddhe sati “jihmaṃ tyajeyur nirlābham” iti yājñavalkīyam avatiṣṭhate | jihmo – vañcakaḥ | taṃ lābharahitaṃ kṛtvā bahiṣkuryuḥ saṃbhūyakāriṇa ity arthaḥ | tasyāpavādam āha nāradaḥ |
kriyāhānir yadā tatra daivarājakṛtā bhavet |
sarveṣām eva sā proktā kalpanīyā yathāṃśataḥ ||
anirdiṣṭo vāryamāṇaḥ pramādād yas tu nāśayet |
tasyāṃśaṃ yad amantratvād gṛhṇīyus te tato ‘param ||
anidiṣṭaḥ – aniyukto vā vāryamāṇo vety arthaḥ | atra veśeṣam āha sa eva |
daivarājabhayād yas tu svaśaktyā paripālayet |
[273]        tasyāṃśaṃ daśamaṃ dadyād gṛhṇīyus te tato ‘param ||
kātyāyano ‘pi |
corataḥ salilād agner dravyaṃ yas tu samāharet |
tasyāṃśo daśamo deyaḥ sarvadraveṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti |
atra viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
samavetais tu yad dattaṃ prārthanīyaṃ tathaiva tat |
na yācate ca yaḥ kaścil lābhāt sa parihīyate || iti |
kṛṣikādisaṃbhūyopajīviṣu viśeṣam āha hārītaḥ |
bāhyabijātyayād yasya kṣetrahāniḥ prajāyate |
tenaiva sā pradātavyā sarveṣāṃ kṛṣijīvinām ||
bāhyabījagrahaṇaṃ kṛṣisādhanānām upalakṣaṇārtham | śilpiṣu viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
hemakārādayo yatra śilpaṃ saṃbhūya kurvate |
karmānurūpanirveśaṃ labheraṃs tu yathāṃśataḥ ||
nirveśo – bhṛtiḥ | aṃśaprakāram āha kātyāyanaḥ |
śikṣakā bījakuśalā ācāryaś ceti śilpinaḥ |
ekadvitricaturbhāgān hareyus te yathottaram ||
harmyādinirmātṝṇāṃ viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
harmyaṃ devagṛhaṃ vāpīṃ cārmikopaskarādi ca |
saṃbhūyakurvatāṃ caiṣāṃ pramukho hy aṃśam arhati ||
ṛtvijāṃ tu viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
ṛtvijaḥ samavetās tu yathā sattre nimantritāḥ |
kuryur yathārhataḥ karma gṛhṇīyur dakṣiṇāṃ tathā || [274]
sattraṃ - yāgaḥ | tatra yajamānānām eva ṛtviktvād ṛtvijām abhāvāt teṣām ṛtvijāṃ tv adhvaryubrāhmaṇādiviśeṣopādānena vihitāṃ dakṣiṇāṃ adhvaryubrāhmaṇādaya eva gṛhṇīyuḥ – samuditadakṣiṇāṃ vibhajya gṛhṇīyur ity arthaḥ |
yasya karmaṇi yās tu syur uktāḥ pratyaṅgadakṣiṇāḥ |
sa eva tā ādadīta bhajeran sarva eva na ||
kvacit sarva eva vā iti pāṭhaḥ | tac ca anekakartṛkadakṣiṇāviṣayam | tac ca prātisvikam eva | yathā stotrakāriṇa eva chandogā bhajeran | pradhāndakṣiṇās tv aṃśakalpanayā | tatra viśeṣam āha sa eva |
sarveṣām ardhino mukhyās tadardhenārdhino ‘pare |
tṛtīyinas tṛtīyāṃśāś caturthāṃśās tu pādinaḥ ||
mukhyā – adhvaryubrahmādayaḥ | apare – dvitīyāḥ prastorādayaḥ | tṛtīyā parihartrādayaḥ | caturthzā grāvastudādayaḥ | ardhinaḥ pādinaḥ tṛtīyina iti yaugikyaḥ saṃjñāḥ | “adhvaryur gṛhapatiṃ dīkṣayitvā brāhmaṇaṃ dīkṣayati” ityādivākye ardhino dīkṣayantīti maitrāvaruṇādiṣv ardhyādisaṃjñāḥ śrūyante | tāś ca yaugikya iti tadbalenoktāṃśakalpanāniyamo ‘pi gamyata ity arthaḥ | atra manuḥ |
ṛtvig yadi mṛto yajñe svakarma parihāpayet |
tasya karmānurūpeṇa deyo ‘ṃśaḥ sahakartṛbhiḥ ||
