sahakartṛbhiḥ – saṃbhūyakāribhir ity arthaḥ | kartṛbhiḥ saha yajamānena deya ity apare | karmaṇi ṛtvik parityaktāṃśakartṛbhiḥ saha deya [275] ity anye | pakṣatraye ‘py ayam arthaḥ – svakarmaikdeśaṃ kartuḥ yā dakṣiṇā tasya caikāntarakartuś ca dakṣiṇākāle saṃbhūyakārisaṃgho yajamāno vā tat tat karmānusāreṇārpayed iti | asya kvacid apavādam āha sa eva |
dakṣiṇāsu ca dattāsu svakarma parihāpayet |
kṛtsnam eva labhetāṃśam anyenaiva tu kārayet ||
anyena – svagaṇavartināṃ madhye pratyāsannena | yena kenacid anyena kāryamāṇe ādhvaryavādisamākhyābādhāpatteḥ | evam ṛtvigantare mṛte ‘py ūhyam | atra nāradaḥ |
ṛtvijāṃ vyasane ‘py evam anyas tatkarma nistaret |
labheta dakṣiṇābhāgaṃ tasmāt saṃpratikalpitam ||
yat tu bṛhasptinoktam,
evaṃ kriyāpravṛttānāṃ yadi kaścid vipadyate |
tadbandhunā kriyā kāryā sarvair vā sahakāribhiḥ ||
iti, yac ca śaṅkhenoktam: “tatra ced anupravṛtte savane ṛtvik mriyeta tasya sagotraḥ śiṣyo vā tatkāryam anupūrayet | atha ced abāndhavas tato ‘nyam ṛtvijaṃ vṛṇuyāt” iti, tat sarvam avāntaragaṇaśūnyartvikkartṛkayajñaviṣayam iti mantavyam – iti candrikākāraḥ (Vya p. 187) | atra viśeṣam āha viṣṇuḥ: “athartviji mṛte paścād anyaṃ vṛṇuyāt, pūrvavṛtasyaiva dakṣiṇā” | paścād āhūtaḥ kiṃcil labhate ceti | yat kiṃcic chabdārtham āha kauṭilyaḥ: “agniṣṭomādiṣu dīkṣaṇīyāyā ūrdhvaṃ yājako ‘vasannaḥ, pañcamam aṃśam anya āhūto labheta | somavikryād uṛdhvaṃ caturtham | pravargyodvāsanād ūrdhvaṃ tṛtīyam | agniṣṭomīyād ūrdhvam ardham | prātaḥsavanād ūrdhvaṃ pādonam | [276] mādhyaṃdinasavanād ūrdhvaṃ samagraṃ nītāsu dakṣiṇāsu bhavati” iti varadarājaḥ | nartakādīnāṃ madhye tālajñasya viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
nartakānām eṣa eva dharmaḥ sadbhir udāhṛtaḥ |
tālajño labhate ‘dhyardhaṃ (atyardhaṃ) gāyakānāṃ samāṃśinām ||
gāyakānāṃ nartakānāṃ samāṃśināṃ madhye tālajñaḥ iha atyartham – ardhenādhikam aṃśaṃ labhata ity arthaḥ | coreṣu saṃbhūyakāriṣu viśeṣam āha sa eva |
svāmyājñayā tu yac coraiḥ paradeśāt samāhṛtam |
rājñe datvā tu ṣaḍbhāgaṃ bhajeyus te yathāṃśataḥ ||
caturo ‘ṃśān bhajen mukhyaḥ śūras tryaṃśam avāpnuyāt |
samarthas tu hared dvyaṃśaṃ śeṣās tv anye samāṃśinaḥ || iti |
paradeśād vairideśād ity arthaḥ | durghaṭadeśād āhṛtaviṣayam etat | sulabhadeśād āharaṇe kātyāyanaḥ |
pararāṣṭrād dhanaṃ yat syāc coraiḥ svāmyājñayā hṛtam |
rājño daśāṃśam uddhṛtya vibhajeran yathāvidhi ||
pūrvokta “caturo ‘ṃśān” ityādividhim anatikramyety arthaḥ |
teṣāṃ cet prasṛtānāṃ yo grahaṇaṃ samavāpnuyāt |
tanmokṣaṇārthaṃ (-ṇāya) yad dattaṃ tasya kāryā samakriyā || iti |
etad vacanaṃ pāribhāṣiketaraviṣayam | pāribhāṣikate ‘pi svabhāgānusāreṇa na bhavatīty evaṃparam |
iti śrīpratāparudramahādevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇde saṃbhūyasamutthānākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
[277]
atha dattāpradānikaṃ nāva vivādapadaṃ nirūpyate
tatra nāradaḥ |
datvā dravyam asamyag yaḥ punar ādātum icchati |
dattāpradānikaṃ nāma tad vivādapadaṃ smṛtam || iti |
ādhyupanidhicauryāgatādīnām adeyatvapratipādanāt trayāṇāṃ ṛṇādānopanidhisaṃbhūyotthānānāṃ śeṣatayā saṃgatiḥ | yadi api strīdhanānām adeyatvapratipādanād dāyavibhāgaśeṣatāpy asti, tathāpi bhūyasāṃ nyāyena trayāṇāṃ śeṣateti dhyeyam | atra manuḥ |
adeyam atha deyaṃ ca dattaṃ cādattam eva ca |
vyavahāreṣu vijñeyo dānamārgaś caturvidhaḥ ||
adeyam – dānānarham | deyam – dānārham | dattam – sthiram | adattam – nivartanīyam | adeyādīnāṃ svarūpam upoddghātaprakaraṇe pratipāditam iti smāritam | evaṃ caturvidho dānamārgaḥ |
atra aṣṭāv adeyāny āha | tatra tāvad adeyasya svarūpabhedān āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
sāmānyaṃ putradārādi sarvasvaṃ nyāsayācitam |
pratiśrutaṃ tathānyasya nadeyaṃ tv aṣṭadhā smṛtam || iti |
adeyaṃ nāma dānārham | ata eva taddāne ‘pi parasvatvāpattiparyantā svatvanivṛttir nāsti | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “adeyadānasya niṣiddhatvāt parasvatvānupapattiḥ” | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “adeyaṃ na deyam iti, deyaṃ dānārham ity arthaḥ” | tathā ca hārītaḥ |
adeyadānaṃ jaihmyaṃ ca kalañjasya niṣevaṇam |
[278]        madyapasya mukhāsvādaḥ catvāry evaṃ samāni ca || iti |
atrāhuḥ – kalañjasya niṣevaṇam āsvāda eva, madyapasya mukhāsvāda iti tatsaṃniyogaśiṣṭatvāt | kecit tu kalañjanaṃ gṛñjanam ity āhuḥ | apare tv āhuḥ: bhavatu vā pātityahetubhūtakalañjabhakṣaṇasaṃniyogaśiṣṭatvam adeyadānasya tāvanmātreṇāsya pāpādhikyaṃ nāsti | prāyaścittavidhiparyālocanayaiva pāpaparijñānam āhur ācāryā iti | tathā ca manuḥ adeyāny aṣṭāv upakramya,
yo dadāti sa mūḍhātmā prāyaścittīyate naraḥ | iti |
prāyaścittasvarūpam āha viṣṇuḥ |
jaihmye trirātram upoṣya pañcagavyena śudhyati | iti |
tathā ca madyapasya mukhāsvādaṃ kṛtvā ekarātram upavāsaḥ | matyā pañcagavyam iti | adeyadāne kṛcchratrayam iti | gautamas tv adeyadāna sāntapanam ity āha | kalañjabhakṣaṇe pātityaṃ sarvasmṛtisiddham | adeyeṣu madhye putrakalatrādīnām adeyatvāt taddāne prāyaścittam uktam | ataś ca adeyadravyadānaṃ niṣidhyate | atra kecid āhuḥ: adeyadravyadānaniṣedhaḥ sahetuka eva | na tu nirhetukaḥ | tathā hi – sāmānyadravyam itarānujñāṃ vinā na deyam | tadanujñā ced deyam eva | putradārādidravyasya tu dāne kṛte ‘pi svatvasyānapāyāt | svatvaṃ mānasikā kriyā sakalparūpatayā nāpaiti | kiṃ tu mahāpātakādinā tadgataṃ svatvam apaitīti dhanārjananayasiddham | at evāha viṣṇuḥ: “putradārādidātā patito bhavati” iti dātṛśabdo vikretur apy upalakṣakaḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “anāpadi putradārādidāne ṣaḍvārṣikaṃ caret” | tat tu durbhikṣakālaviṣayam [279] | samantarakālam āha: “dvādaśavārṣikaṃ caret” | yat tu vasiṣṭhenoktam – “āpadi putraṃ mātāpitarau dadyātāṃ hareyātām” ity uktvā “putraṃ pratigrahīṣyan bandhūn āhūya rājani nivedya svaniveśanasya madhye adūrabāndhavam asaṃnikṛṣṭaṃ vyāhṛtībhir hutvā pratigṛhṇīyāt” (VaDh 15.6) iti | āpacchabdārtham āha sa eva: “putrābhāva eva vāpad” iti | vāśabdena samuccayārthena durbhikṣakāle ‘tyantauṭumbabharaṇāśaktiḥ samuccīyate | tathā cāpadīti viśeṣaṇaṃ pratigrahītur dātur api samānam | ato vaidikatvād asya dānasa pūrvoktaṃ na viruddham | nyāsas tu mūlasvāminā anujñāto dātvaya eva | anyasya pratiśrutam api tadanujñayā deyam eva | yācitam api tathaiva | etac ca sarvasaṃmatam |
sarvasvaṃ tu kim artham adeyam | na putradārādivan mahāpātakarūpaniyatopāyakatvābhāvāt | mānasikyā tu kriyayā svatvam udety eva | yathā sarvasvadakṣiṇādau ṛtvijāṃ svatvam udeti | mahāsāhasādau daṇḍatayā gṛhītasarvasve rājasvatvam udety eva | kiṃ ca viraktasya sarvasvatyāgo na niṣiddhaḥ | atas tattyāganiṣedhe kāraṇaṃ vaktamyam |
atra kecid āhuḥ – sarvasya tyāge jīvikāyā aniṣpatteḥ dharmasādhanasya śarīrasyānirvāhāt | tannirvāhārthaṃ sarvasya tyāgo na yuktaḥ | kiṃ tu svajīvikārthaṃ kiṃcit gṛhītvāvaśiṣṭam eva dātavyaṃ viraktānām apīti |
tad asaṃgatam | viraktānāṃ śarīradhāraṇasya bhikṣāṭanādijīvikayā niṣpādayituṃ śakyatvāt |
nanu sarvasvadānasya vihitatve,
āpady api viraktau ca sarvasvaṃ na pradīyate |
iti hārītavacanaṃ niravakāśaṃ syāt | atra jñāpakam apy āhur [280] lakṣmīdharaprabhṛtayaḥ: “viśvajiti sarvasvaṃ dadyāt,” “na kesariṇo dadyāt” iti kesaridānaniṣedhaḥ sarvasvadānaṃ niṣedhatīti |
atra kecit “viśvajiti sarvasvam” ity atra sarvaśabdaḥ kesarivyatiriktasarvaparaḥ | atra svaśabdasyātmīyavācitayā ātmīyādīnāṃ putrādīnām adeyatvād eva svatvanivṛtter abhāvād ity āhuḥ |
tan na, “sarvasvaṃ dadyāt” iti vidhiḥ niṣedhaśirasko bhavaty eva sarvasvadānaṃ vidhātum ity āhur ācāryāḥ | sarvasvadakṣiṇāvidhayas tv adeyadravyavyatiriktaviṣayāḥ | putradārādau tu ārjyārjakabhāvasaṃbandharūpasya svatvasya vidyamānatvāt | putradārādikaṃ svam eva | tac ca mahāpātakādinā nivartata ity uktaṃ prāk | ataś ca sarvaśabdaḥ saṃkucadvṛttir na bhavati | ata eva audumbarī sarvā veṣṭayitavyety atra sarvaśabdaḥ saṃkucadvṛttir na bhavatīti pratipāditaṃ guruṇā | rājñā sarvasvaharaṇaṃ sāhaseṣu vihitam | balātkāreṇa gṛhīte ‘pi dravye svatvam utpadyate | kiṃ ca na tatpradānam, api tu daṇḍyatayā sarvasvagrahaṇam | prakṛte tu dānavicāro dānaviṣaya iti tatrāsmākam anāsthā | kiṃ ca –
anvāhitaṃ yācitakam ādhiḥ sādhāraṇaṃ ca yat |
nikṣepaḥ putradārāś ca sarvasvaṃ cānvaye sati ||
āpatsv api na kaṣṭāsu vartamānena dehinā |
adeyāny āhur ācāryā yac cānyasmai pratiśrutam ||
iti nāradavacana-bṛhaspativacanayor ānurūpyāya “sāmānyaṃ putradārādi” ityādibṛhaspativacane “āpady api viraktā” iti vacane ca “anvaye sati” iti viśeṣaṇam aṅgīkartavyam | anvaye – vaṃśaparamparāyā bījabhūta vastuni vidyamāne na sarvasvadānaṃ kartavyam iti | viraktānām(?) [281] api santatisadbhāve sarvasvadānaṃ neṣyata (na yujyata) eva |
niṣedhavidhayas tu sasantatikasyaiva sarvasvadānaniṣedhaparā iti cet, satyam | candrikākāra-kulārka-vijñānayogi-prabhṛtīnāṃ matam etat |
lakṣmīdharādayas tu adeyadravyatyāge vācaniko niṣedhaḥ | na tu naiyāyikaḥ | anyathā prāyaścittavidhānaṃ daṇḍavidhānaṃ vyāhanyeta | tathā ca pitāmahaḥ |
yo dadāti sa mūḍhātmā prāyaścittīyate naraḥ | iti |
śaṅkho ‘pi: “adeyaṃ datvā tatparāvartya trirātram upavaset” iti prāyaścittaṃ smṛtavān |
gṛhṇāty adattaṃ yo mohād yaś cādeyaṃ prayacchati |
daṇḍanīyāv ubhāv etau dharmajñena mahībhujā ||
adattagrahaṇam adeyasyāpy upalakṣaṇārtham | tenādattapratigrahītur adeyapratigrahītuś ca daṇḍo ‘nekavacanokta iti mantavyam | gṛhītasyāparāvartanam api mahīkṣitā kāryam iti adattādeyagrahaṇāt gamyate | adattenādeyena ca dānasiddher abhāvāt parasvatvānutpatter ity āha candrikākāraḥ | tataś ca vaidho ‘yaṃ sarvasvatyāganiṣedha iti lakṣmīdharādīnāṃ matam eva samyak |
nanu sāmānyādidravyasvāmino ‘nujñāne dātuṃ yuktam ity uktam prāk |
tan na, lakṣmīdharādimate mūlasvāmina eva tatrādhikāro na tadanujñāmātreṇetarasyeti dhyeyam | loke kuttākhyo vyavahāraḥ mūlasvāminaḥ sakāśād gṛhakṣetrādikaṃ gṛhītvā tadupacayāpacayau kauttikasya ma(mai)yaiva soḍhavyāv iti pāribhāṣikaḥ samasti | aparaś ca kuttākhyo mūlasvāmina aurdhvadaihikaṃ tatkṛtam [282] ṛṇaṃ ca tadīyagṛhakṣetrādinā nirvartya saṃśodhya avaśiṣṭam asti cet, grahītavyaṃ nāsti cen, nāstīti pāribhāṣikaḥ |
sa ca kuttākhya upacayapadābhilapyastasyopacayākhyapāribhāṣikatvenaupādhikasvatva-saṃkramopāditayā tatprakaraṇe ṛṇādānākhye vivādapade gopyādyadhikāre ‘ntabhāva evāstv iti cet,
maivam, ṛṇādānākhyaṃ vivādapadaṃ śodhyamocyakoṭidvayāvalambanena pravṛttam | śodhyam abandhakam ṛṇam | mocyaṃ sabandhakaṃ gopyādhiprakṛtikam | evaṃ dviprakāram ṛṇādānākhyaṃ vivādapadam | loke upacayāparaparyāyakuttārūpasya śodhyamocyarūpaprakārdvayāsaṃbhavāt | kiṃ ca ṛṇādāne uttamarṇagata upacayaḥ kuttātmake tv adharmaṇagata iti na tatrāntarbhāva aupacayikasyeti |
apara āhuḥ – gopyādhāv antarbhāvo vācya iti | devadattakṛtarṇāpakaraṇe jāte prakṛtavivāde mocanīyaviṣayatāsti | kiṃ tu yāvajjīvānuṣṭheyapitṛkriyākaraṇasyādhirūpeṇa svīkārāt tanmukhena na vimocyatvam | upanayanasaṃskārasyādhyāpanakriyāṅgatvena tannivṛttyā nivṛttau pravṛttāyāṃ gurur abhyuddeyaḥ pravayāḥ pratyuddeya ityādyupanītadharmāṇāṃ yāvajjīvānuvṛtteḥ tanmukhenopanayanasyāpi yāvajjīvānuvṛttivat –
maivam | prakṛtasya vyavahārasyāhitadravyaviṣayatvābhāvāt | pratyuta pākṣikāpacayabhārasahitatvād ādhau tadabhāvāt | kuttākhyasyaupacayikasya ṛṇādāne ‘natarbhāvayitum aśakyatvāt | dattāpradānike ‘ntarbhāvo nyāyyaḥ | dattāpradānikaṃ caturvidhaṃ dānam avalambyāvatiṣṭhate | dattam adattaṃ deyam adeyaṃ ceti dānamārgaś caturvidha ity uktaṃ prāk | dattaṃ dvividhaṃ sopādhikaṃ nirupādhikaṃ ceti | kuttātmakaṃ dānaṃ [283] sopādhikam iti kṛtvā sopādhikadattarūpatvenaupacayikasya tatraivāntarbhāva iti rahasyam | devadattasya ṛṇāpakaraṇaṃ taduddeśaprasaktadevapitṛkriyākaraṇaṃ viṣayīkṛtya pravṛttam aupacayikaviṣayaṃ dattāpradānike ‘ntarbhavati |
kramāgataṃ gṛhakṣetraṃ pitryaṃ paitāmahaṃ tathā |
putrapautrasamṛddhasya na (hya) deyam ananujñayā ||
atra manuḥ |
dharmārthaṃ yena yad dattaṃ kasmaicid yācate dhanam |
paścāc ca na tathā tat syād adeyaṃ tena tad bhavet ||
dānārham ekavidham āha nāradaḥ |
kuṭumbabharaṇadravyaṃ yat kiṃcid atiricyate |
tad deyam uparudhyānyad dadad āgaḥ samāpnuyāt ||
atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
sarvasvaṃ gṛhavarjaṃ tu kuṭumbabharaṇādikam |
yad dravyaṃ tat svakaṃ deyam adeyaṃ syād ato ‘nyathā ||
svakam – svārjitam |
na dāreṣu na putreṣu na bandhuṣv anapekṣakāḥ |
sarvakāryeṣu puruṣāḥ svadravye prabhaviṣṇavaḥ ||
svārjitadravye prabhaviṣṇava ity arthaḥ | vyāsaḥ |
andhādiṣu śataṃ dānam anantaṃ duhitur bhavet |
pitrye śataguṇaṃ dānaṃ sahasraṃ mātur ucyate ||
bhaginyāṃ śatasāhasraṃ sodarye dattam akṣayam ||
dakṣaḥ |
mātāpitror guror mitre vinīta copakāriṇi |
[284]        dīnānāthaviśiṣṭeṣu dattaṃ tu phalavad bhavet ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
śūdre tv ekaguṇaṃ dānaṃ vaiśye dviguṇam eva ca |
kṣatriya triguṇaṃ prāhuḥ brāḥmaṇe ṣaḍguṇaṃ (bhavet) smṛtam ||
śrotriya tac ca sāhasram upādhyāye tu taddvayam |
ācārye triguṇaṃ jñeyam āhitāgniṣu taddvayam ||
ātmike śatasāhasram anantaṃ tv agnihotriṇī |
somape śatasāhasram anantaṃ brahmavādini ||
iti vā pāṭhe | ātmike – ātmajñānaniṣṭhe | bhāradvājaḥ |
paṇyaṃ mūlyaṃ bhṛtis tuṣṭyā snehāt pratyupakārataḥ |
dānam ekātmakaṃ prāhuḥ tad bhavet toyapūrvakam ||
vinā toyapradānena dharmadānaṃ na vidyate |
atra āpastambaḥ: “sarvāṇy udakapūrvāṇi dānāni” iti | atra gautamaḥ: “svastvācya bhikṣādānaṃ appurvam | tathātithiṣu (dadātiṣu) caivaṃ dharmeṣu (dharmyeṣu)” | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
strīdhanaṃ strīsvakulyebhyaḥ prayacchet taṃ tu varjayet |
strīdhane striyā evādhikāraḥ nānyasyety arthaḥ |
kulyābhāve tu bandhubhyaḥ tadabhāve dvijātiṣu |
nāradas tu sāptavidhyam āha |
paṇyamūlyaṃ bhṛtis tuṣṭyā snehāt pratyupakārataḥ |
strīśulkānugrahārthaṃ ca dattaṃ saptavidhaṃ smṛtam || [285]
paṇyasya krītasya mūlyatvena dattam, kṛtakarmaṇe bhṛtitvena, tuṣṭyā cāraṇādibhyaḥ, senāt suhṛdbhyaḥ, upakurvate pratyupakārarūpeṇa, pariṇayanārthaṃ kanyājñātibhyaḥ śulkatvena, yac ca adṛṣṭārthaṃ dattaṃ tad etat saptavidham api dattam eva na prayāharaṇīyam | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhṛtis tuṣṭyā paṇyamūlyaṃ strīśulkam upakāriṇe |
śraddhānugrahasaṃprītyā dattam aṣṭavidhaṃ smṛtam ||
manur api |
mātāpitror gurau mitre vinīte copakāriṇe |
dīnānāthaviśiṣṭebhyo dattaṃ tu saphalaṃ bhavet ||
saudāyikaṃ samāyātaṃ śauryaprāptaṃ ca yad bhavet |
strījñātisvābhyanujñātaṃ dattaṃ siddhim avāpnuyāt ||
sthāvareṣu viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pratigrahaḥ prakāśaḥ syāt sthāvarasya viśeṣataḥ |
deyaṃ pratiśrutaṃ caiva datvā nāpaharet punaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
svecchayā yaḥ pratiśrutya brāhmaṇāya pratigraham |
na dadyād ṛṇavad dāpyaḥ prāpnuyāt pūrvasāhasam ||
pratiśrutasyādānena dattasyācchādanena ca |
kalpakoṭiśataṃ martyaḥ tiryagyonau ca jāyate ||
pratiśrutyāpy akarmasaṃyuktāya na dadyāt | tathā ca hārītaḥ |
vacasā yat prtiśrutya karmaṇā nopapāditam |
ṛṇaṃ tad dharmasaṃyuktam iha loke paratra ca ||
ṛṇavat pratiśrutam ity arthaḥ | atha ṣoḍaśātmakam adattam ity āha nāradaḥ |
adattaṃ tu bhayakrodhaśokavegasamanvitaiḥ |
[286]        tathotkocaparīhāsavyatyāsacchalayogataḥ ||
bālamūḍhāsvatantrārtamattonmattāpavarjitaiḥ |
kartā mamāyaṃ karmeti pratilābhecchayā ca yat ||
apātre pātram ity uktvā kārye cādharmasaṃyute |
dattaṃ yat syād ap jñānād adattaṃ ṣoḍaśātmakam ||
atra bhayakrodhaśokaśabdaiḥ vegaśabdaḥ pratyekaṃ saṃbadhyate | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
kāmakrodhāsvatantrārtaklībonmattapramohitaiḥ |
vyatyāsaparihāsāc ca yad dattaṃ tat punar haret ||
bhāradvājaḥ |
ākrośād arthahīnānāṃ pratīkāraṃ ca yad bhayāt |
pradīyate tat kartṛbhyo bhadānaṃ tad ucyate ||
utkocalakṣaṇaṃ nārada āha |
yat tu kāryasya siddhyartham utkocā sā prakīrtitā |
utkocaśabdaḥ strīliṅgo ‘py asti | tallakṣaṇāntaram api tenaivoktam |
stenasāhasadurvṛttapāradārikaśaṃsanān |
darśaṇād dhṛtanaṣṭasya tathā tasya pravartanāt ||
prāptam etais tu yat kiṃcit tad utkocākhyam ucyate ||
ata viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
niyukto yas tu kāryeṣu sa ced utkocam āpnuyāt |
sa dāpyas taddhanaṃ kṛtsnaṃ damaś (deyaṃ) caikādaśādhikam ||
aniyuktas tu kāryārtham utkocaṃ yam avāpnuyāt |
kṛtapratyupakārārthaḥ tasya doṣo na vidyate ||
śeṣaṃ sugamam | asvatantrā nāradenoktāḥ |
asvatantrāḥ prajāḥ sarvāḥ svatantraḥ pṛthivīpatiḥ |
[287]        asvatantrāḥ smṛtāḥ śiṣyā ācāreṣu svatantratā ||
asvatantrāḥ striyaḥ putrā dāso yaś ca parigrahaḥ |
svatantras tasya tu gṛhī yasya syāt tat kramāgatam ||
garbhasthaiḥ sadṛśo jñeya aṣṭamād vatsarāc chiśuḥ |
bāla ā ṣoḍaśād varṣāt paugaṇḍaś ceti śabdyate ||
parato vyavahārajñaḥ svatantraḥ pitarāv ṛte |
jīvato hy asvatantaḥ syāj jarayāpi samanvitaḥ ||
tayor api pitā śreyān bījaprādhānyadarśanāt |
abhāve bījino mātā tadabhāve tu pūrvajaḥ ||
nīconmattāḥ samākhyātā vātapittakaphodbhavāḥ |
śeṣaṃ sugamam | yajñārthaṃ labdhaṃ dhanaṃ dyūtādau viniyuñjanāya dattam ity evaṃ ṣoḍaśaprakāraṃ dattam apy adattam eva pratyāharaṇīyatvāt | ārtadattasyādattatvaṃ dharmakāryaviṣayaṃ kātyāyanavacanāt |
svasthenārtena vā dattaṃ śrāvitaṃ dharmakāraṇāt |
adatvā tu mṛte dāpyaḥ syāt suto nātra saṃśayaḥ || iti |
viṣṇur api “yājanādhyāpanābhyāṃ corād upagatam api pratyāhartavyam” iti | smṛtyantaram api |
adattādāyinaś corāl lipseta brahmaṇo dhanam |
yājanādhyāpanenāpi yathā stenas tathaiva saḥ || iti |
adattādāyī coraḥ | nāradaḥ |
yogāmanavikrītaṃ yogadānaṃ pratiśrutam |
yatra nāpy upadhiṃ paśyet tat sarvaṃ vinivartayet ||
yogaḥ – upādhiḥ yenopādhiviśeṣeṇa ādhikrayavikrayadānapratigrahāḥ [288] kṛtās tadupādhivigame krayādīnāṃ nivṛttiḥ | arthadānādīnāṃ nivartyatvam āha bharadvājaḥ |
prayojanam avīkṣyaiva pātrebhyo yatra dīyate |
tad arthadānam ity āhur aihikaṃ phalam eva ca ||
na tv adṛṣṭaṃ prayojanam ity arthaḥ |
dṛṣṭaprayojanaṃ strīṇāṃ prasaṅgād yat pradīyate |
anarheṣu ca rāgeṇa kāmadānaṃ tad ucyate ||
saṃsadi vrīḍayāśritya parebhyo yat pradīyate |
vrīḍādānam iti prāhuḥ tad dānaṃ tattvadarśinaḥ ||
dṛṣṭvā priyaṃ tathā śrutvā harṣād yac ca prayacchati |
harṣadānaṃ tad ity āhuḥ dṛṣṭam evāsya tat phalam ||
kalyāṇe ca vipattau ca yat priyeṣu pradīyate |
dānaṃ laukikam ity evaṃ dṛṣṭārthaṃ tat pradīyate ||
adattāny āhur etāni dattāny ap manīṣiṇaḥ |
atra nāradaḥ |
gṛhṇāty adattaṃ lobhād yo yaś cādeyaṃ prayacchati |
daṇḍanīyāv ubhāv etau dharmajñena mahīkṣitā ||
bṛhaspatir api |
adattabhoktā danḍyaḥ syāt tathādeyapradāyakaḥ || iti |
lobhād ity anena kāraṇoktyā ajñānād dānāpradānayor doṣa iti sūcitam ||
iti śrīpratāparudradevamahārājaviracit smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe upanidhyākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
[289]
athābhyupetyāśuśrūṣākhyas padasya vidhir ucyate
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
abhyupetya tu śuśrūṣāṃ yas tāṃ na pratipadyate |
aśuśrūṣābhyupetyaitad vivādapadam ucyate || iti |
etat pūrvaṃ pratiśrutārthasya sopādikatve pratyādheyatvam uktam | atra nirupādhikatve ‘pi pratyādheyatvam iti saṅgatiḥ | na cānayor ekaprakaraṇatvam, pūrvaprakaraṇe dattasyādānaṃ dravyaviṣayam | atra tūpetasyākaraṇam upajīvakaviṣayam iti bhinnaviṣayatvāt prakaraṇāntareṇa vyutpādyam iti naikaprakaraṇatvam | atra nāradaḥ |
śuśrūṣakaḥ pañcavidhaḥ śāstre dṛṣṭo manīṣibhiḥ |
caturvidhaḥ karmakaraḥ śeṣā dāsās tu pañcakāḥ ||
vidyā trayī samākhyātā ṛgyajuḥsāmalakṣaṇā |
tadarthaṃ guruśuśrūṣāṃ prakuryāc chāstracoditām ||
vidyāvijñānakāmārthanimittena caturvidhā |
ekaivaṃ punar eteṣāṃ kriyābhedāt prabhidyate ||
vijñānam ucyate śilpaṃ hemakupyādisaṃskṛtiḥ |
nṛttādikaṃ ca tatprāptaṃ kuryāt karma guror gṛhe ||
kaṅkaṇakaṭakādinirmāṇaviṣayaṃ nṛttagītādikaraṇaviṣayaṃ ca cakārāt stambhakumbhādiracanāviṣayaṃ ca vijñānaṃ śilpavijñānam ity ucyate | antevāsiśabdārtham āha nāradaḥ |
svaśilpam icchann āhartuṃ bāndhavānām anujñayā |
[290]        ācāryasya vased ante kālaṃ kṛtvā suniścitam ||
ante samīpe etāvantaṃ kālam asmatsamīpe sthātavyam ity ācāryoktakālaparimāṇaṃ suniścitaṃ kṛtvety arthaḥ | ācāryasyāpi kartavyam āha sa eva |
ācāryaḥ śikṣayed enaṃ svagṛhe dattabhojanam |
na cānyat kārayet karma putravac cainam ācaret ||
nāradaḥ |
ā vidyāgrahaṇāc chiṣyaḥ śuśrūṣet prayato gurum |
etac ca gurudāreṣu guputre tathaiva ca ||
vedatrayādhyāyināṃ trayāṇām apy upadeśaḥ |
samāvṛttaś ca gurave pradāya gurudakṣiṇām |
pratīyāt svagṛhān eṣā śiṣyavṛttir udāhṛtā ||
gṛhān ātmīyagṛhān | pratīyāt – prāpnuyāt | kātyāyanas tu viśeṣam āha |
yas tu na grāhayec chilpaṃ karmāṇy anyāni kārayet |
prāpnuyāt sāhasaṃ pūrvaṃ tasmāc chiṣyo nivartate ||
śikṣito ‘pi śritaṃ kāmam antevāsī samācaret |
tatra karma ca yat kuryād ācāryasyaiva tat phalam ||
tatra hārītaḥ |
gṛhītaśilpaḥ samaye kṛtvācāryaṃ pradakṣiṇam |
śaktitaś cānumānyainam antevāsī nivartate ||
ete śiṣyādayaś caturvidhāḥ – śiṣyo ‘ntevāsī bhṛtako ‘dhikarmakṛt iti | tathā ca,
śiṣyāntevāsibhṛtakāś caturthas tv adhikarmakṛt |
[291]        ete karmakarā jñeyā dāsās tu gṛhajādayaḥ ||
sāmānyam asvatantratvam eṣām āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
jātikarmakṛtas tūkto viśeṣo vṛttir eva ca ||
karmāpi dvividhaṃ jñeyam aśubhaṃ śubham eva ca |
aśubhaṃ dāsakarmoktaṃ śubhaṃ karma kṛtaṃ smṛtam ||
gṛhadvārāśucisthānarathyāvaskaraśodhanam |
guhyāṅgasparśanocchiṣṭaṃ viṇmūtragrahaṇojjhanam ||
iṣṭataḥ svāmināṃ svāṅgair upasthānam athāntataḥ ||
iṣṭataḥ – svāmina icchātaḥ iti | svāṅgaiḥ – svahastādibhiḥ | antata antimasya viṇmūtrojjhanasya upasthānaṃ gharṣaṇam |
aśubhaṃ karma vijñeyaṃ śubham anyad ataḥ param |
ataḥ śiṣyo vedavidyārthī | antevāsī śilpavidhārthī | mūlyena yaḥ karma karoti sa bhṛtakaḥ | karma kurvatām adhiṣṭhātā adhikarmakṛt iti jñeyam | śeṣaṃ sugamam | bhṛtakaś cāpi trividhaḥ |
uttaamas tv āyudhīyo ‘tra madhayas tu kṛṣīvalaḥ |
adhamā bhāravāhī syād ity evaṃ trividho bhṛtaḥ ||
gṛhajātādayas tu |
gṛhajātas tathā krīto labdho dāyād upāgataḥ |
anākālabhṛtas tadvad āhitaḥ svāminā ca yaḥ ||
mokṣito mahataś carṇād yuddhaprāptaḥ paṇe jitaḥ |
tavāham ity upagataḥ pravrjyāvasitaḥ kṛtaḥ ||
bhaktadāsaś ca vijñeyas tathaiva vaḍavāhṛtaḥ |
vikretā cātmanaḥ śāstre dāsāḥ pañcadaśa smṛtāḥ ||
gṛhe dāsyāṃ jāto gṛhajātaḥ | krītaḥ – mūlyena svāmyantarāt | labdhaḥ [292] tata eva pratigrahādinā prāptaḥ | dāyād upagataḥ pitrādidāsaḥ | anākālabhṛtaḥ durbhikṣe yo dāsatvāya maraṇād rakṣitaḥ | āhitaḥ svāminā dhanasaṃgrahaṇenādhī kṛtaḥ | ṛṇamocanena dāsatvam upagato ṛṇadāsaḥ | yuddhaprāptaḥ samare vijitya gṛhītaḥ | paṇe jitaḥ yady asmin vivāde parājito ‘haṃ bhavāmi tadā tvaddāso bhavāmīti paribhāṣya yo jitaḥ | tavāhaṃś cyutyaḥ | dāsa iti svayaṃ pratipannaḥ | pravrajyāvasitaḥ pravrajyātaś cyutaḥ | kṛtaḥ etāvantaṃ kālaṃ tvaddāsa ity abhyupagamaṃ prāptaḥ | bhaktadāsaḥ sarvakālaṃ bhaktārtham eva dāsatvam upagato yaḥ praviṣṭaḥ | vaḍavāhṛtaḥ vaḍavā – gṛhadāsī tayā hṛtaḥ tallobhena tām udvāhya dāsatvena praviṣṭaḥ | ya ātmānaṃ vikrīṇīte so ‘tra vikretety evaṃ pañcadaśa prakārāḥ | evaṃ pañcadaśānāṃ madhye keṣāṃcid dāsatvam uktiprakāram āha nāradaḥ |
tatra pūrvaś caturvargo dāsatvān na vimucyate |
prasādāt svāmino ‘nyatra dāsyam eṣāṃ kramāgatam ||
pūrvaś caturvargaḥ gṛhajātakrītalabdhadāyādupagatānāṃ caturṇāṃ vargaḥ | anyasyāpi dāsatvavimuktiprakāram āha sa eva |
vikrīṇīte svatantraḥ san ya ātmānaṃ narādhamaḥ |
sa jaghanyatamas teṣāṃ so ‘pi dāsyān na mucyate ||
prasādāt svāmina ity etad atrāpy anuṣajyate | tad ayam arthaḥ – gṛhajātādayaḥ ātmavikretṛpañcamāḥ svāmiprasādāt dāsyāt mucyante nānyathā | anākālabhṛtādayas tu svāmiprāṇarakṣaṇād dāsyād vimucyante | tathāha nāradaḥ |
yo vaiṣāṃ svāminaṃ kaścid gopāyet (modayet) prāṇasaṃkaṭāt |
dāsatvāt sa vimucyeta putrabhāgaṃ labheta ca ||
eṣāṃ pañcadaśavidhānāṃ dāsānāṃ madhya ity arthaḥ | etad uktaṃ bhavati – [29] gṛhajātādīnām ātmavikretṛpañcamānāṃ dāsatvāpanaye hetudvayam, svāmiprasādaḥ prāṇasāṃkaṭād rakṣaṇaṃ ca | anākālabhṛtānāṃ tu svāmiprāṇarakṣaṇam eveti | pravrajyāvasite viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
pravrājyāvasito sādo moktavyaś ca na kenacit | iti |
yājñavalkyo ‘pi |
pravrajyāvasito rājño dāsa āmaranāṇtikam |
pravrajyāvasitaḥ – saṃnyāsād bhraṣṭaḥ rājño dāsaḥ | pārthivasyaiva nānyasyety arthaḥ |
rājña eva tu dāsaḥ syāt pravrajyāvasito naraḥ |
na tasya pratimokṣo ‘sti na viśuddhiḥ kathaṃcana ||
iti nāradasmaraṇāt | nara iti sāmānyavacanaṃ kṣatriyavaiśyayor eva paryavasyati, viprasya dāsatvapratiṣedhāt | yathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
pravrajyāvasitā yatra trayo varṇā dvijātayaḥ |
nirvāsyaṃ kārayed vipraṃ dāsatvaṃ kṣatraviḍ bhṛguḥ ||
tathā ca hārītaḥ |
dvāv eva karmacaṇḍālau loke dūrabahiṣkṛtau |
pravrajyāparivṛttaḥ ca vṛthāpravrajitaś ca yaḥ || iti |
ata eva kātyāyanaḥ |
anākālabhṛto dāsyān mucyate goyugaṃ dadat || iti |
tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “adhikādhikadravyadānān mucyate” iti | evam ṛṇadāsādīnām ṛṇādyupādhinivṛttau dāsatvād vimuktir iti nyāyasiddho ‘rthaḥ | tathā hi, durbhikṣe poṣitena (poṣaṇe) kārito [294] dāso godvayārpaṇād vimucyate | āhitadāsas tv ādhātṛgṛhītarṇe pratyarpite sati uttamarṇasya dāsyād vimucyate | ṛṇādāsas tu svakṛtarṇāpākaraṇād vimucyate | paṇajitaraṇaprāptopagatās trayo dāsaviśeṣāḥ | svanirvartyākhilavyāpāranirvartanakṣama-dāsāntarapradānād vimucyante | kṛtas tu dāsaviśeṣo dāsyādher vimucyate, baḍavāhṛtas tu dāsaḥ saṃbhogatyāgād vimucyate iti | dāsābhāsān āha nāradaḥ |
corāpahṛtavikrītā ye ca dāsā hṛtā balāt |
rājñā mocayitavyās te dāsatvaṃ teṣu neṣyate ||
yat tu kātyāyanenoktam,
tavāham iti cātmānaṃ yo ‘svatantraḥ prayacchati |
na sa taṃ (samaṃ) prāpnuyāt kāmaṃ pūrvasvāmī labheta tam ||
tat paradāsatvenāsvatantraviṣayam | kevalāsvatantratve tu dāsapratinidhir vā dāpyaḥ svayam eva vā dāsyaṃ kuryāt | dāsyavimokaprakāram āha nāradaḥ |
svadāsam icched yaḥ kartum adāsaṃ prītamānasaḥ |
skandhād ādāya tasyāsau bhindyāt kumbhaṃ sahāmbhasā ||
sākṣatābhiḥ sapuṣpābhir mūrdhany adbhir apāharet |
adāsa iti coktvā triḥ prādravan (prāṅmukhaṃ) tam athotsṛjet ||
etac ca dāsyā api samānaṃ vidhānam | dāsa ity atra liṅgavacanayor uddeśyatayā avivkṣitatvāt | viṣṇus tu viśeṣam āha: “dāsena yā pariṇītā sā dāsītvam āpadyate” iti | kātyāyano ‘pi |
dāsenoḍhā ca yā dāsī sāpi dāsitvam āpnuyāt |
yad tadbhartā prabhus tasyāḥ svāmyadhīnaḥ prabhus tayoḥ (yataḥ) ||
[295] tayoḥ – dāsadāsyoḥ | svāmī prabhur ity anena dāsasyāpi dāsapatnyāś ca taddhanasya ca prabhur ity arthād uktaṃ bhavati | ata eva nāradaḥ |
dāsasya tu dhanaṃ yat syāt svāmī tasya prabhur mataḥ | iti |
anena dāsadhanasyāpi svāmy eva prabhur iti mantavyam | dāsatvavimokasya phalam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
tataḥ prabhṛti vaktavyaḥ svāmyanugrahapālitaḥ |
bhojyānno ‘tha pratigrāhyo bhavaty abhimataḥ satām ||
dāsīsutāś ca ye jātāḥ tasyāḥ patyā pareṇa vā |
utpādako yadi svāmī na dāsīṃ kārayet prabhuḥ || iti |
vyāsaḥ |
anyadīyā tu yā dāsī dāsy anyasya tu sā bhavet |
śulkaṃ datvā tu tāṃ gacched agatyā dāsyam arhati ||
yas tu śulkaṃ datvā dāsyām apatyam utpāditavān tad apatyaṃ tasyaiva bījaprādhānyāt | śulkam adatvaiva gacchati apatyaṃ cotpannaṃ tad apatyaṃ dāsīsvāmina eveti bhāruciprabhṛtaya āhuḥ | vadarājas tu dvayor ity āha:
ato ‘patyaṃ dvayor iṣṭaṃ pitur mātuś ca dharmataḥ |
iti vadan | dāsyādhikāre viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
varṇānām ānulomyena dāsyaṃ na pratilomataḥ |
rājanyavaiśyaśūdrāṇāṃ tyajatāṃ hi svatantratām ||
samavarṇe tu vipraṃ tu dāsatvaṃ naiva kārayet ||
tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
triṣu varṇeṣu vijñeyaṃ dāsyaṃ viprasya na kvacit |
[296]        varṇānām ānulomyena dāsyaṃ na pratilomataḥ ||
yathā dāragrahaṇam ānulomyena na prātilomyena, dāravad dāsatvam iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | atrāpi viśeṣam āha uśanā |
na gurur na sapiṇḍaś ca na vipro nāntyayonayaḥ |
dāsabhāvaṃ na te ‘rhanti na ca vidyādhiko dvijaḥ ||
ayam arthaḥ – brāhmaṇasya antyayonir na dāsaḥ vidyādhikaś ca | evaṃ kṣatriyāder brāhmaṇaḥ | samavarṇe tu vidyādhiko na dāsaḥ antyayonir api na dāsa iti dhyeyam | atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
śūdraṃ tu kārayed dāsaṃ krītam akrītam eva vā |
dāsyāyaiva hi sṛṣṭaḥ sa svayam eva svayaṃbhuvā ||
iti śrīpratāparudradevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe abhyupetyāśuśrūṣākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
[297]
atha vetanānapākarmākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
atra manuḥ |
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi vetanasyānapākriyām | iti |
ataḥ param – abhyupetyāśruśrūṣākhyasya padasyānantaram ity arthaḥ | pūrvasmin prakaraṇe upagatapaṇajitādīnāṃ pratiśrutanirvāhārthaṃ pratinidhir vā kārya ity uktam | atra tu pratiśrutanirvāhārthaṃ vetanaṃ kartavyam | anyathā tad vedatanaṃ pratiśrutam apaharaṇīyam iti saṃgatiḥ | na cāsya dattāpradānike ‘ntarbhāvaḥ | tatra dattasyādattaprāyatoktā na tu dattasyāpaharaṇam | ataś cānayor bhedaḥ | tad etad anusaṃdhāya,
ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi vetanasyānapākriyām |
ity atra “ataḥ param” iti padaṃ prayuktam ity anusaṃdheyam | tatra nāradaḥ |
bhṛtyāya vetanaṃ dadyāt karmasvāmī yathākramam |
ādau madhye ‘vasāne ca karmaṇi (karmaṇo) yad viniścitam ||
tubhyam idaṃ dāsyāmi iti yad vetanaṃ parimāṇato niścitaṃ tat tredhā vibhajya karmaṇām ādimadhyāvasāneṣu dadyād ity arthaḥ |
nanu karmakarāṇāṃ pūrvaprakaraṇa eva tatsvarūpanirūpaṇāvasare tadbhṛtidānaprakāraś (tadvṛtti-) ca tatraiva vaktavya iti cet,
maivam | śiṣyadāsādisamānayogakṣematayākarmakarāṇāṃ tatra svarūpamātram upodghātatayā nirūpitam, tatra tadvetanapradānāvasarasyānākāṅkṣitatvāt | jijñāsāprasūtaṃ hi vyutpādyam iti nyāyavidaḥ | dattāpradānike yad vuttaṃ (uktaṃ?) tad avalambyaivābhyupetyāśuśrūṣākhyasya padasyotthānam ity uktasaṅgatiḥ | ataś ca [298] tadanusāreṇaiva ākāṅkṣotthānāt paṇajitayuddhaprāptasvayamāgatānāṃ dāsānāṃ vyutpādyatvaṃ tadutthitākāṅkṣatvāt gṛhajātādīnāṃ vyutpādyatvaṃ tatprasaṅgāt karmakarādīnāṃ vyutpādyatvam iti tatsvarūpamātram eva pūrvaprakaraṇe vyutpādyam | na tu tatra bhṛtidānaprakāra iti sa evātra prapañcyate | tatra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
bhṛtāv aniścitāyāṃ tu daśabhāgam avāpnuyuḥ |
lābhagovīryasasyānāṃ vaṇiggopakṛṣīvalāḥ ||
daśabhāgam – daśamabhāgam ity arthaḥ | govīryam – payaḥ yathākrameneti śeṣaḥ | yadi karmasvāmī na dadāti tadā tv āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
dāpyas tu daśamaṃ bhāgaṃ vāṇijyapaśusasyataḥ |
aniścitya bhṛtiṃ yas tu kārayet sa mahīkṣitā ||
bṛhaspatis tu viśeṣam āha |
tribhāgaṃ pañcabhāgaṃ vā gṛhṇīyāt sīravāhakaḥ | iti |
atra vikalpārtham āha sa eva |
vastrācchādabhṛtaḥ sārād bhāgaṃ gṛhṇīta pañcakam |
jātasasyāt tribhāgaṃ tu pragṛhṇīyād atho bhṛtaḥ || iti |
ayam arthaḥ – kṛṣīvalo jātasasyāt pañcamaṃ bhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyat tena yady aśanācchādane nirvartyete, anyathā tṛtīyabhāgam iti pāribhāṣikabhṛtāv api yadi bhṛtyaḥ prvīṇaḥ tadā kiṃcid adhikaṃ deyam | yadā anipuṇaḥ kiṃcin nety āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
deśaṃ kālaṃ ca yo ‘tīyāl lābhaṃ kuryāc ca yo ‘nyathā |
tatra syāt svāminaś chando rikthaṃ deyaṃ kṛta ‘dhike ||
anekabhṛtinirvartye karmaṇi pāribhāṣikabhṛter arpaṇaprakāram āha yājñāvalkyaḥ |
[299]        yo yāvat kurute karma tāvat tasya tu vetanam |
ubhayor apy asādhyaṃ cet sādhye kuryād yathāśrutam ||
yathāśrutam – yathāparibhāṣitam | bṛhaspatis tu viśeṣam āha |
bhṛtakas tu na kurvīta svāminaḥ śāṭyam aṇv api |
bhṛtihātim avāpnoti tato hāniḥ pravartate ||
tato – vyavahārāt | bhṛtakasya svāminaḥ sakāśāt hāniḥ – parājaya ity arthaḥ | parājito bhṛtako hānim avāpnuyāt iti yāvat | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
karmopakaraṇaṃ caiṣāṃ kriyāṃ prati yad āhitam |
āptabhāvena tad rakṣyaṃ na jaihmyena kadācana || iti |
eṣām – karmasvāmināṃ karmopakaraṇaṃ (?) lāṅgalādikaṃ kṛṣyādikriyām uddiśya yad bhṛtyapārśve nihitaṃ tat tena niḥśāṭhyena saṃrakṣyam | anyathā bhṛter hāniḥ syād iti śeṣaḥ | karmakaraṇe tv āha viṣṇuḥ: “bhṛtyo vetanaṃ gṛhītvā yadi karma na karoti vetanād dviguṇaṃ gṛhṇīyāt” iti | tat samarthaviṣayam | yathāha nāradaḥ |
gṛhītvā vetanaṃ karma na karoti yadā bhṛtaḥ |
samarthaś ced damaṃ dāpyo dviguṇaṃ tac ca vetanam ||
atra kātyāyanaḥ |
karmārambhaṃ tu yaḥ kṛtvā siddhaṃ naiva tu kārayet |
balātkārayitavyo ‘sāv akurvan daṇḍam arhati ||
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
bhṛto ‘nārto na kuryād yo darpāt karma yatheritam (yathoditaḥ) |
[300]        sa daṇḍyaḥ kṛṣṇalān aṣṭau na deyaṃ cāsya vetanam ||
nāradaḥ |
kāle ‘pūrṇe tyajan karma bhṛter nāśam avāpnuyāt |
svāmidoṣād apakrāman yāvat kṛtam avāpnuyāt ||
atra viṣṇuḥ: “bhṛtakaś cāpūrṇe kāle karma tyajan sakalam eva mūlyaṃ jahyāt (dadyāṭ) | rājñe paṇaśataṃ dadyāt | svadoṣeṇa ca yad vinaśyet svāmine deyam | anyatra daivarājopaghātāt | svāmī cet bhṛtakam apūrṇe kāle jahyāt tasya sarvam eva mūlyaṃ dadyāt paṇaśataṃ ca rājñe dadyāt | anyatra bhṛtadoṣāt” | yājñavalkyaḥ |
arājadaivikaṃ naṣṭaṃ bhāṇḍaṃ dāpyas tu vāhakaḥ |
prasthānavighnakṛc caiva pradāpyo dviguṇaṃ smṛtam ||
prakrānte saptamaṃ bhāgaṃ caturthaṃ pathi saṃtyajan |
bhṛtim ardhapathe sarvaṃ pradāpyas tyājako ‘pi ca ||
yadā prakrānte gamane prāk prasthānāt tyajati tadā bhṛtyā saha caturthabhāgam | ardhapathe sārdham | yadā punaḥ svāmī bhṛtakaṃ yeṣu sthāneṣu tyajet tadā tenāpi bhṛtakāya pūrvoktaṃ deyam | atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
na tu dāpyo hṛtaṃ corair dagdham ūḍhaṃ jalena vā | iti |
ūḍhaṃ nītam | paṇyastrīṣu nārada āha |
śulkaṃ gṛhītvā paṇyastrī necchantī dviguṇaṃ vahet |
anicchan śulkadātāpi śulkahānim avāpnuyāt ||
bṛhaspatis tasyāpavādam āha |
vyāditā saṃbhramāvyagrā rājadharmaparāyaṇā |
[301]        āmantritā ca nāgacched avācyā baḍavā smṛtā ||
baḍavā – veśyā | atra viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
aprayacchaṃs tathā śulkaṃ anubhūya pumān striyam |
akrameṇa tu saṃgacched ghātadantanakhādibhiḥ ||
ayonau yaḥ samākrāmed bahubhir vāpi vāsayet |
śulkaṃ so ‘ṣṭaguṇaṃ dāpyo vinayaṃ tāvad eva tu ||
matsyapurāṇeṣu viśeṣāntaraṃ darśitam |
gṛhītvā vetanaṃ veśyā lobhād anyatra gacchati |
tāṃ damaṃ dāpayed dadyād uttarasyāpi bhāṇḍakam ||
veśyāpradhānā ye tatra kāmukās tadgṛhoṣitāḥ |
tatsamuttheṣu kāryeṣu nirṇayaṃ saṃśayaṃ viduḥ ||
atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
prabhuṇā viniyuktaḥ san bhṛtako vidadhāti yaḥ |
tadartham aśubhaṃ karma svāmī tatrāparādhnuyāt ||
svāmina eva tatrāparādha ity arthaḥ | atra nāradaḥ |
stomavāhīni bhāṇḍāni pūrṇakālāny upāvahet |
grahītur āvahed bhagnaṃ naṣṭaṃ cānyatra saṃplavaḥ ||
saṃplavaḥ – parasparam anyena vā dravyeṇa saṃghaṭṭanam | atra hārītaḥ |
parabhūmau gṛhaṃ kṛtvā stomaṃ datvā vaset tu yaḥ |
sa tad gṛhītvā nirgacchet tṛṇakāṣṭheṣṭakādikam ||
stomād vinā vasitvā tu parabhūmāv anicchataḥ |
nirgacchaṃs tṛṇākāṣṭhādi na gṛhṇīyāt kathaṃcana ||
yāny eva tṛṇakāṣṭhāni iṣṭakā vā niveśitā |
[302]        (sa) vinirgacchaṃs tu tat sarvaṃ bhūmisvāmini vedayet ||
stomaśabdena paragṛhānubhavārthaṃ tebhyo dakṣiṇocyate |
snehena ca ciraṃ labdhvā mandiraṃ kurute tu yaḥ |
nirgacchatas tasya dārudattastomasya nānyathā ||
anyathā rājā gṛhṇīyād ity arthaḥ |
etadantarbhūtatayā svāmipālavivādākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate | atra nāradaḥ |
gavāṃ śatād vatsatarī dhenuḥ syād dviśate bhṛtiḥ |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ gope saṃdohaś cāṣṭame ‘hani ||
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ vatsatarī – samatikrāntavatsatvāvasthā gaur bhṛtiḥ pālake pālyamānagośatavaśād deyā bhavati | śatāntarādhike tu vatsatarīsthāne savatsā gaur aṣṭame ‘hani saṃdohaś ca bhavatīty arthaḥ | saṃdohaḥ – sarvadohaḥ | yathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
tathā denubhṛtaḥ kṣīraṃ labhetāsyāṣṭame ‘khilam | iti |
gosvāmino ‘numate tu pratyādeyaiva bhṛtir dohanātmikā | atra daśadogdhrīṇāṃ madhye utkṛṣṭāṃ vatsatarīṃ savbhṛtyarthaṃ kṣīrabhṛto gṛhṇīyād ity āha manuḥ |
gopa kṣīrabhṛto yas tu sa duhyād daśātaḥ parām | iti |
parām utkṛṣṭāṃ daśataḥ sārvavibhaktikas tasiḥ nirdhāraṇe, daśānāṃ dogdhīṇāṃ madhye ity arthaḥ | svāmipālakayoḥ kartavyam āha nāradaḥ |
upānayed vā gopālaḥ pratyahaṃ rajanīkṣaye |
samarpitāś ca tā gopaḥ sāyāhne pratyupānayet || [303]
upānayet – svīkuryād ity arthaḥ | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ |
yathārpitān paśūn gopaḥ sāyaṃ pratyarpayet tathā | iti |
gopo gopālaḥ | yāvatyaḥ prātaḥ samarpitāḥ tāvatyaḥ pratyarpaṇīyā ity arthaḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ: “sāyaṃ samarpayet sarvam” iti | sarvāsamarpaṇe tv āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pramādahṛtanaṣṭāṃś ca pradāpyaḥ kṛtavetanaḥ | iti |
kṛtavetanaḥ – pālaka ity arthaḥ | pramādagrahaṇaṃ pālakadoṣopalakṣaṇārtham | tena haraṇamaraṇādiviṣaye nirdoṣaś cet pālako na dāpyaḥ | ata eva manuḥ |
vighuṣyopahataṃ caurair na pālo dātum arhati |
yadi deśe ca kāle ca svāminaḥ svasya śaṃsati ||
vighoṣaṇaṃ kāhalaveṇvādinā bahūnām anubhāvanārtham | deśo yatra svāmī sthitaḥ so ‘bhimataḥ | kālo ‘nativilambitaḥ | evaṃbhūtaṃ deśaṃ kālaṃ vā yadā svadoṣeṇātikramya deśāntare kālāntare vā śaṃsati tadā so ‘parādhānusārād dātum arhatīti hamyate | kṛcidviśeṣe  pālakasya nirdoṣatvam āha vyāsaḥ |
pālagrahe grāmaghāte tathā rāṣṭrasya vibhrame |
yat praṇaṣṭaṃ hṛtaṃ vā syān na pālas tatra kilbiṣī ||
atra viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
naṣṭaṃ vinaṣṭaṃ kṛmibhiḥ śvahataṃ viṣame mṛtam |
hīnaṃ puruṣakāreṇa pradadyāt pāla eva tu ||
naṣṭaṃ – kutra tiṣṭhatīty aviditam | kṛmibhir vinaṣṭaṃ pradhvastam | śvahataṃ śunā hatam | viṣame mṛtaṃ kardamādau mṛtam | puruṣakāreṇa hīnam –bhuktihīnam (śakti-) | [304] ataś ca svāmine nivedanīyam anyathā pālakaḥ kilbiṣī bhaved iti gamyate | etad eva spaṣṭayitum āha sa eva |
syāc ced govyasanaṃ gopo vyāyacchet tatra śaktitaḥ |
aśaktas tūrṇam āgatya svāmine vā nivedayet ||
anyathā karaṇe viṣṇuḥ: “dravyaṃ vinaṣṭaṃ ced vinaṣṭapaśumūlyaṃ svāmine dadyāt | divā paśūnāṃ vṛkādyupaghāte pāle tv apālayati pāladoṣaḥ” iti | divāgrahaṇād rātrau na doṣa iti gamyate | yathāha manuḥ |
ajāvike tu saṃruddhe vṛkaiḥ pāle tv anāyati |
yāṃ prasahya vṛko hanyāt pāle tat kilbiṣaṃ bhavet ||
yāṃ vyaktim ity arthaṃ | anāyati – aprayatamāne udāsīne ity arthaḥ | yad vā anāyati – adhāvati | yat tu nāradoktam,
tāsāṃ ced avaruddhānāṃ carantīnāṃ mitho vane |
yām upetya vṛko hanyān na pālas tatra kilbiṣī ||
asamarthaviṣayam etat | paśupālakaśuddhyaśuddhiviṣayam āha viṣṇuḥ: “paśuṣu vinaṣṭeṣu tadvālaśṛṅgādidarśanāt śudhyati” iti | vālādikaṃ hṛ(mṛ)tapaśvavayavabhūtam araṇye paśvapahāraśaṅkānivṛttyarthaṃ pālako darśayed ity arthaḥ | tac ca liṅgadarśanaṃ sarvabhṛtakānām upalakṣaṇam | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “bhṛtakavināśe bhṛtakaṃ vā tacchiṣṭaṃ vā rājñe nivedayet” iti | rājagrahaṇād rājabhṛtakaviṣayam etat |
iti śrīpratāparudramahādevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe vetanānapākarmākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
[305]
atha asvāmivikrayākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
atra nāradaḥ |
asvāminā kṛto yas tu krayo vikraya eva ca |
akṛtaḥ sa tu vijñeyo vyavahārasya nityaśaḥ ||
pūrvaprakaraṇe vetanam apākaraṇīyam ity uktam | atra tu krītam apākaraṇīyam iti saṃgatiḥ | dattāpradāne tu dattasyādattaprāyatoktā | atra tu krītasyāpākaraṇaṃ na tu krītasyākrītaprāyateti tasmād bhedaḥ | atra (ata) nāradaḥ |
dravyam asvāmivikrītaṃ prāpya svāmī tad āpnuyāt | iti |
asvāmivikrītaṃ svakīyadravyaṃ yasya pārśve dṛṣṭaṃ tasya sakāśāt svāmī tad gṛhṇīyād ity arthaḥ | yat tu candrikākāreṇoktam: “etac ca svāmyananumatyā vikrītadravyaviṣayam” iti, tan na, svāmyanumatau vidyamānāyām asvāmivikrayatvāviṣayatvāt | yat tu manunoktam:
vikrīṇīte parasya svam asvāmī svāmyasaṃmataḥ |
asaṃmataḥ – asamyak jñānavān | svāminaṃ vañcayitveti yāvat | kim idaṃ parasvam ity apekṣite bṛhaspatiḥ |
nikṣepānvāhitanyāsahṛtayācitabandhakam |
upāṃśu yena vikrītam asvāmī so ‘dhidhīyate ||
nikṣepādīnāṃ lakṣaṇāni pūrvam evoktāni | upāṃśu – aprakāśam | kātyāyanaḥ |
asvāmivikrayaṃ dānam ādhiṃ ca vinivartayet | [306]
ādhikrayadānebhyaḥ prāg evāsvāmihaste dṛṣṭaṃ naṣṭam apahṛtaṃ svakīyaṃ dravyam asvāminaḥ sakāśāt svāmī gṛhṇīyād iti daṇḍāpūpanyāyād anena vacanena gamyate | tatra viśeṣam āha viṣṇuḥ: “ajñānataḥ prakāśaṃ yaḥ paradravyaṃ vikrīṇīte tatra tasyādoṣaḥ | svāmī tad dravyam avāpnuyāt” iti | ajñānataḥ – vikretur asvāmitvājñānataḥ | tatra paradravya (kraye) viṣaye tasya kretur adoṣaḥ – taskaradoṣo nāstīty arthaḥ | vikretus tu taddoṣo ‘stīti gamyate | sa tu taskaravad daṇḍyaḥ | na punaḥ kretā, prakāśam ity anena rahasi lobhāt paradravyam iti jñātvā krīṇāti tatra kretur api taskaradoṣo ‘sti | tathā ca smṛtiḥ: “yady aprakāśaṃ prakāśaṃ vā hīnamūlyaṃ vikrīṇīyād tadā kretā vikretā ca coravat śāsyau” iti | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
antar gṛhe bahir grāmān niśāyām asato janāt |
hīnamūlyaṃ ca yat krītaṃ jñeyo ‘sāv upadhikrayaḥ ||
asato janāt – caṇḍālādijanād ity arthaḥ | kecid asadgrahaṇam asvāmitvajñāpakadāsatvādīnām upalakṣaṇārthatvāt kitavādiparam ity āhuḥ | upadhikrayas tu tena doṣāpādaka ity āha sa eva |
yena krītaṃ tu mūlyena tat prāg rājñe niveditam |
na tatra vidyate doṣaḥ stenaḥ syād upadhikrayāt ||
stenaḥ – coraḥ | coravac chāsya ity arthaḥ | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
nāṣṭikas tu prakurvīta taddhanaṃ jñātibhiḥ svakam | iti |
nāṣṭiko naṣṭadhanavān | naṣṭaṃ svakīyaṃ dhanaṃ jñātibhiḥ sākṣibhūtaiḥ prakurvīta sādhayed ity arthaḥ | kretāraṃ pray āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
pūrvasvāmī tu tad dravyaṃ yad āgatya vicārayet |
[307]        tatra mūlaṃ dharśanīyaṃ kretuḥ śuddhis tato bhavet ||
vyāsaḥ |
mūle samāhṛte kretā nābhiyojyaḥ kathaṃcana |
mūlena saha vādas tu nāṣṭikasya tadā bhavet ||
atra kātyāyanas tu viśeṣam āha |
tadānayanakālas tu deyo yojanasaṃkhyayā | iti |
yojanasaṃkhyayā – deśaviprakarṣāpekṣayety arthaḥ | kālaviprakarṣādau pratibhūprakaraṇaṃ draṣṭavyam | atra viśeṣam āha vyāsaḥ |
anupasthāpayen mūlaṃ krayaṃ vāpy aviśodhayet (-dhayat) |
yathābhiyogaṃ dhanine dhanaṃ dāpyo ‘yam añjasā ||
mūlaṃ pūrvavikretāraṃ nāṣṭikaṃ praty āha sa eva |
yadi svaṃ naiva kurute jñātibhir nāṣṭiko dhanam |
prasaṅgavinivṛttyarthaṃ coravad daṇḍam arhati ||
prasaṅgo ‘tiprasaṅgaḥ | atrāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
naṣṭāpahṛtam āsādya hartāraṃ grāhayen naram |
naṣṭam apahṛtaṃ vā anyadīyaṃ krayādinā prāpya hartāraṃ grāhayet | coroddharaṇakāribhiḥ | athāviditadeśāntaraṃ gataḥ kālāntare vā vipannaḥ tadā mūlasamāharaṇāśakteḥ kretāram adarśayitvaiva svayam eva tad dhanaṃ nāṣṭikasya samarpayed iti śrīkararucikādaya āhuḥ | vijñāneśvaras tu – naṣṭam apahṛtaṃ vātmīyaṃ dravyam āsādya kretur hastasthaṃ jñātvā taddhartāraṃ kretāraṃ sthānapālādibhir grāhayet | deśakālātipattau – deśakālātikrame sthalapālādyasaṃnidhānāt tadvijñāpanakālāt palāyanaśaṅkayā (?) svayam eva gṛhītvā tebhyaḥ samarpayed iti vyākhyātavān | yadā [308] mūlopasthāne aśaktaḥ san divyam aṅgīkaroti tadā divyenaiva vinirṇeyaḥ | yadā tu divyena vā krayaṃ na darśayati tadā sa eva daṇḍabhāg bhavatīti vijñānayogi-medhātithi-asahāyadityā (?) āhuḥ | candrikākāra-aparārka-bhārucyādayas tu asvāmivikrayaviṣaye divyāvakāśo (-vatāro) nāstīty āhuḥ | nirṇayas tu rājājñayā samanyūnādhikatvena dhanaṃ vibhajyaiva nirṇaya iti |
pramāṇahīnavāde tu puruṣāpekṣayā nṛpaḥ |
samanyūnādhikatvena samaṃ kuryād vinirṇayam || iti |
puruṣāpekṣayā – nāṣṭikakretṛpuruṣayoḥ sādhutvāpekṣayā | asahāya-medhātithi-prabhṛtīnāṃ tu samanyūnādhikatvena puruṣāpekṣayā sākṣipuruṣāpekṣayā nirṇayaṃ kuryāt | sa ca prakāraḥ sākṣiprakaraṇokto veditavyaḥ | sākṣyādimānuṣābhāve divyam avataratīty evaṃparam etad vacanam ity āhuḥ | atra candrikārāra-bhāruci-aparārkādīnāṃ matam asamyak | yat tūktaṃ hārītena,
prakāśaṃ ca krayaṃ kuryāt sādhubhir jñātibhiḥ svakaiḥ |
na tatrānya kriyā proktā daivikī na ca mānuṣī ||
iti, tat tu “lekhyaṃ yatra na divyate” ityādivacanasamānārthaṃ jñeyam | tadvacanārthas tu divyanirūpaṇaprakaraṇe nirūpitaḥ, ata evāvadhāryaḥ |
iti śrīpratāparudramahādevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe asvāmivikrayākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
[309]
atha vikrīyāsaṃpradānākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
vikrīya mūlyaṃ paṇyena kretur yan na pradīyate |
vikrīyāsaṃpradānaṃ tad vivādapadam ucyate ||
pūrvaprakaraṇe asvāmivikrīte svatvānutpattes tatparāvartanīym ity uktam | atra tu krayāt svatvotpattāv api tasya pratibaddhatvāt parāvartanīyam iti saṃgatiḥ | na ca bhogyādhivat sopādhikatve ‘pi na ṛṇādānāntaḥpātitvam asya | tatra bhogyādhau paribhāṣāmūlaṃ svatvasyaupādhikatvam | atra tu anuśayamūlam ity anayor bhedaḥ | na ca dattāpradānike ‘ntarbhāvaḥ, dānavikrayayor bhedāt | kiṃ ca tatra dattasyādattaprāyatā atra tu na krītasyākrītaprāyatā kiṃ tu vikrītasya paryāvṛttir iti bhedaḥ | ata eva vetanānapākarmādau nāntarbhāvaḥ |
loke ‘smin dvividhaṃ dravyaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ tathā |
krayavikrayadharmeṣu sarvaṃ tat paṇyam ucyate ||
ṣaḍvidhas tasya vibudhair dānādānavidhiḥ smṛtaḥ |
gaṇitaṃ tulitaṃ meyaṃ kriyayā rūpataḥ kriyā ||
etadvyākhyānam uddeśasthala evoktam, ata evāvadhāryam |
vikrīya paṇyaṃ mūlena (mūlyena?) ya kretur na prayacchati |
sthāvarasya kṣayaṃ dāpyo jaṅgamasya kriyāphalam ||
kṣayam – gatabhogādikam ity arthaḥ | sthāvarasyāpi vastrādeḥ kṣayasya dātum aśakyatvāt tadanuguṇadravyaṃ dāpyaḥ | jaṅgamasya dvipadāṃ catuṣpadāṃ ca tatkarmanimittaṃ mūlyaṃ dāpyaḥ | yathā krayakālagṛhītena mūlyena yāvat paṇyam arpaṇakāle arghavaśād adhikaṃ labhyate | sa ca [310] vikretā sodayaṃ tat paṇyaṃ kretāram eva prāpayet | ata eva viṣṇuḥ: “arghavaśād adhikaṃ cet kretāraṃ pratyarpaṇakāle prāpayet” iti | yat tu yājñavalkyenoktam |
gṛhītamūlyaṃ yat paṇyaṃ krītaṃ naiva prayacchati |
sodayaṃ tasya dāpyo ‘sau diglābhaṃ vā digāgate || iti |
tac ca krayakāle pratyarpaṇakāle sāmyaviṣayam, arghasya hrāsavṛddhigrahaṇābhāvāt | sodayasya dāpya iti vṛthā, na nyāyataḥ prāptam | arghasāmye tu paṇyopacayarūpodayāsaṃbhavāt | ataś ca,
nikṣepaṃ vṛddhiśeṣaṃ ca krayavikrayam eva ca |
yācyamānam adattaṃ ced vardhate pañcakaṃ śatam ||
iti akṛtavṛddhiprakaraṇoktaparimāṇā vṛddhiḥ kalpyate | tatsahitaṃ paṇyaṃ dāpya iti | atra nāradaḥ |
sthāyinām eṣa niyamo diglābhaṃ digvicāriṇām |
grāmapaṭṭaṇādau sthitvaiva ye paṇyavikrayakrayarūpādivyavahārān kurvanti te sthāyinaḥ | taditare sāṃyātrikādayo digvicāriṇaḥ | sthāyinām eṣa niyamaḥ iti “sthāvarasaṃkṣayaṃ dāpyaḥ” ityādi | digvicāriṇāṃ tu yat paṇyaṃ yasmin digantare vikretuṃ krītaṃ tat paṇyaṃ tasmin digantare vikrīṇānasya yo lābhas tena sahitaṃ deyam iti diglābhaśabdārthah | kātyāyanas tu |
krītvā prāptaṃ na gṛhṇīyād yo na dadyād adūṣitam |
sa mūlyād daśamaṃ bhāgaṃ datvā svadravyam āpnuyāt ||
aprāpte ‘rthe kriyākāle kṛte naiva pradāpayet |
evaṃ dharmo daśāhaṃ tu parato ‘nuśayo na tu || [311]
dūṣitam – jalādineti śeṣaḥ | arthakriyākālo – dohyavāhyādipaṇyasya dohanavāhanādikālaḥ tasmin prāpte sati adāne vā agrahaṇe vā prakṛtenaiva daśamaṃ mūlyabhāgaṃ pradāpayet | kiṃ tu tam adatvaiva svadravyam āpnuyāt | evam uktadharmo daśāhāt prāg arthakriyākālād ūrdhvaṃ veditavyaḥ | daśāhāt paratas tu anuśayo na kartavyaḥ, anuśayakālasyātītatvāt |
krītvāpy anuśayāt paṇyaṃ tyajed dohyādi yo naraḥ |
aduṣṭam eva kāle tu sa mūlyād daśamaṃ vahet ||
nirdoṣaṃ darśayitvā tu yaḥ sa doṣaṃ prayacchati |
mūlyaṃ taddviguṇaṃ dāpyo vinayaṃ tāvad eva ca ||
upahanyeta vā paṇyaṃ dahyetāpahriyeta vā |
vikretur eva so ‘nartho vikrīyāsaṃprayacchataḥ ||
ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
rājadaivopaghātena paṇye doṣam upāgate |
hāniś ca kretur evāsau yācitasyāprayacchataḥ ||
yācitasyeti vadan ayācitasya vikretur aprayacchato na hānir iti darśayati | ataś ca “sthāvaraś ca kṣayaṃ dāpyaḥ” ityādivacanaṃ yācitaviṣayam iti mantavyam | nāradas tu viśeṣam āha |
dīyamānaṃ na gṛhṇāti krītaṃ paṇyaṃca yaḥ krayī |
sa evāsya bhaved doṣo vikretur yo ‘prayacchataḥ ||
aprayacchato vikretur yo doṣaḥ asya kretuḥ sa evety arthaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
dīyamānaṃ na gṛhṇāti krītaṃ paṇyaṃ ca yaḥ krayī |
[312]        vikrītaṃ ca tad anyatra vikretā nāparādhnuyāt ||
abuddhipūrvakasthale ‘pi parāvartanīyo vyavahāra ity āha sa eva |
mattonmattena vikrītaṃ hīnamūlyaṃ bhayena vā |
asvatantreṇa mugdhena tyājyaṃ tasya punar bhavet ||
tatra nāradaḥ |
dattamūlyasya paṇyasya vidhir eṣa prakīrtitaḥ |
adatte ‘nyatra samayān na vikretur api kramaḥ ||
adattamūlye paṇye ca vāṅmātreṇa kraye kṛta |
na parāvartitavyam ity evamādisamayābhāve sati pravṛttau nivṛttau vā na kaścid doṣa iti | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
satyaṃkārakṛtaṃ dravyaṃ dviguṇaṃ pratidāpayet |
krayaṃ satyaṃ kartuṃ yad vikretṛhaste kṛtaṃ tat satyaṃkārakṛtam | kretṛdoṣavaśena krayāsiddhau tv āha (sa eva) vyāsaḥ |
satyaṃkāraṃ ca yo datvā yathākālaṃ na dṛśyate |
paṇyabhāvena dṛṣṭaṃ tu dīyamānam agṛhṇataḥ ||
lābhārtho vaṇijāṃ sarvaḥ paṇye tu (paṇyeṣu) krayavikrayaḥ |
sa ca lābho ‘rgham āsādya mahān bhavati vā na vā ||
lābhaparimāṇam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
svadeśapaṇye tu vaṇik śataṃ gṛhṇīta pañcakam |
paradeśe tu daśakaṃ yaḥ sadyaḥ paṇyavikrayī ||
tenāyam arthaḥ saṃpannaḥ – anyenāpi krayam arthasaṃpannaḥ anyenāpi krayasaṃbhāṣaṇe kṛte ‘py adattamūlye tato lābhe saty anyenātikrayaḥ kartavyaḥ | ata eva vyāsaḥ |
[313]        tasmād deśe ca kāle ca vaṇig ardhaṃ prakalpayet |
na jaihmyena parvarteta śreyān evaṃ vaṇikpathaḥ ||
iti śrīpratāparudramahādevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe vikrīyāsaṃpradānākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
[314]
atha krītvānuśayākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
krītvā mūlyena yat paṇyaṃ kretā na bahumanyate |
krītvānuśaya ity etad vivādapadam ucyate ||
pūrvasmin prakaraṇe vikrītasya parāvṛttiḥ, atra tu krītasyeti saṃgatiḥ | na cāsya pūrvatrāntarbhāvaḥ, krītavikrītagatatvena bhinnaviṣayatvāt parāvṛtteḥ |
nanu krayavikrayayor vīvadhakalaśatulyatayā parasparāpekṣatvād vaivadhikaṃ paṇyaṃ yuktam iti cet,
satyam | “daśāho ‘nuśayaḥ krayaḥ” ity atra krayaśabdaḥ parivṛttivinimayayor upalakṣakaḥ | tatrāpy anuśayasya tulyatvāt | yathāha bhāradvājaḥ |
saṃdhiś ca parivṛttiś ca vibhāgaś ca samā yadi |
ādaśāhaṃ nivarteta viṣame navavatsarāt || iti |
parivṛttiśabdo vinimayasyāpy upalakṣakaḥ, “sajātīyayor dravyayor vinimayaḥ | vijātīyayos tu parivṛttiḥ” iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt |
na ca parivṛtteḥ kraya evāntarbhāva iti vācyam | kraye tu mūlyaṃ trivarṣaphalam, parivṛttau tu svatas tulyam eva | yady api rikthakrayādigautamasūtre parivṛttivinimayayoḥ parigaṇanābhāvāt svatvahetutā nāstīti pratibhāti, tathāpi tilakrayasya niṣiddhatvāt pratigrahasyātiduṣṭatvāt tilān datvā vrīhyādigrahaṇasthale vinimayaparivṛttyor iva svatvāpādakatvam iti lokaprasiddhiḥ | yat tu “ādhiḥ praṇaśyed dviguṇa” ityādau tilavinimayavad dhanadvaiguṇyaṃ svatvāpādakaṃ na bhavati | api tu krayāntaparyavasānāt svatvāpādakam ity uktam | [315] tat tu vinimayasya svatvāpādakatvaṃ nāstīty evaṃparaṃ na bhavati | kiṃ tu tasmin sthale krayāntaparyavasānād vācikādānāntatayā vā svatvāpādakatvam, anyatra tu tilavinimayādau vinimayaparivṛttyor api krayādīnām iva svatvāpādakatvaṃ lokaprasiddhir(?) nāpahnotuṃ śakyam ity āha bhāruciḥ | vastutas tu – vinimayaparivṛttyoḥ pāribhāṣikyoḥ paribhāṣayā tayor vācīnakadānarūpeṇa svatvahetutvasiddhir asty evety uktam tatraiva | pūrvasmin prakaraṇe vikrīyāsaṃpradānatā atra tu parivṛttyanuśaya it prakaraṇabhedaḥ | krītvānuśaya iti samākhyā tu parivṛttivinimayayor ap lakṣaṇayety āha bhāruciḥ | atrāha nāradaḥ |
kretā paṇyaṃ parīkṣeta prāk svayaṃ guṇadoṣataḥ | iti |
krītvānuśayo mā bhūd iti svayaṃ kretrā prāg eva krayāt paṇyaṃ guṇāḍhyaṃ duṣṭaṃ veti nirṇayārthaṃ parīkṣā kartavyety arthaḥ | bṛhaspatis tu paṇyadoṣaguṇavidām anyeṣām api pradarśayed ity āha |
parīkṣeta svayaṃ paṇyam anyeṣāṃ ca pradarśayet | iti |
atra vyāsas tu viśeṣam āha |
carmakāṣṭheṣṭakāsūtradhānyasya panasasya tu |
vasukupyahiraṇyānāṃ sadya eva parīkṣaṇam ||
vasuśabdo hemavacanaḥ | krayaśabdaḥ (kupyaśabdaḥ?) hemarūpyavyatiriktatrapusīsādivacanaḥ | hiraṇyaśabdo rajatavacanaḥ “tasmād rajataṃ hiraṇyam” iti śruteḥ | atra bṛhaspatiḥ |
parīkṣitaṃ bahumataṃ gṛhītvā na punas tyajet | iti |
tathā ca nāradaḥ |
parīkṣyābhimataṃ krītaṃ vikretur na punar bhavet | [316]
parīkṣāyāṃ kālaniyamam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
tryahaṃ dohyaṃ parīkṣeta pañcāhād vāhyam eva tu |
muktāvajrapravālānāṃ saptāhaṃ syāt parīkṣaṇam ||
dvipadām ardhamāsaṃ tu puṃsāṃ taddviguṇaṃ striyāḥ |
daśāhaṃ sarvabījānām ekāhaṃ lohavāsasām ||
ato vākpaṇyadoṣas tu yadi saṃjñāyate kvacit |
vikretuḥ pratideyaṃ tat kretā mūlyam avāpnuyāt ||
atra nāradaḥ |
avijñātaṃ tu yat krītaṃ duṣṭaṃ paścād vibhāvitam |
krītaṃ tat svāmine deyaṃ paṇyaṃ kāle ‘nyathā tv iti ||
paśvādipaṇyadravyāṇām iti ūrdhvam upacayo vā vṛddhir vā bhaviṣyatīti deśakālavaśāt jñātvā krayādau (pravartitavya) pravṛttam ity āha hārītaḥ |
kṣayaṃ vṛddhiṃ ca jānīyāt paṇyānām āgatiṃ tathā | iti |
apaśakunato ‘pi vikretā svakrītaṃ duṣkrītaṃ manyate tadā krayaḥ parāvartate | yadā bahudoṣatvaṃ jñātvā hiraṇysvalpamūlyena krīṇāti na tatra duṣkrītaṃ manyata iti na tatra parāvartate krayaḥ | yadā krītvāpy anyatra tato ‘py adhikalābhe apaśakunādivyājam utpādayati tadā vyavahāro na parāvartyaḥ | api tu daṇḍyaḥ | yathāha viṣṇuḥ: “lābhād vyājāntaram utpādayati sa daṇḍyaḥ” iti | ata eva kātyāyanaḥ |
paribhuktaṃ tu yad vāsaḥ kliṣṭarūpaṃ malīmasam |
[317]        sadoṣam api tat krītaṃ vkretur na bhavet punaḥ ||
ata eva svayaṃ parīkṣakaḥ parīkṣyaiva gṛhṇāti | paścād visaṃvāde ‘pi na parāvartyo vyavahāraḥ | svasyaiva parīkṣakatvena svāparādhāt | tatrāvikreyāny āha manuḥ |
nānyad anyena saṃsṛṣṭaṃ virūpaṃ vikrayam arhati |
na sāvadyaṃ na ca nyūnaṃ na dūre na tirohitam ||
atra kātyāyanaḥ |
sādhāraṇaṃ tu yat krītaṃ naiko dadyān narādhamaḥ |
nādadyān na ca gṛhṇīyād vikrīyāc ca na caiva hi ||
krītvā mūlyena yat paṇyaṃ duṣkrītaṃ manyate krayī |
vikretuḥ pratideyaṃ tat tasminn evāhny avīkṣitam ||
dvitīye ‘hni dadat kretā mūlyāt tryaṃśāṃśam āharet |
dviguṇaṃ tu tṛtīye ‘hni parataḥ kretur eva tat ||
etad dravyānuśayottarakālaṃ draṣṭavyam | aparārkas tu – ayam evaiṣām anuśayakāla ity āha | vastrāṇām upabhogavaśān mūlyatvaṃ(?) cāha viṣṇuḥ:
“sakṛd dhautasya nāśe mūlyād aṣṭamabhāgo hīyeta | dvir dhautasya nāśe pādahāniḥ | trir dhautasya nāśe tṛtīyāṃśahāniḥ | catur dhautasya nāśe ardhamūlyaṃ hīyate | pañcame arthād aṣṭamabhāgahāniḥ | ṣaṣṭe tv icchāto na śāstrataḥ” | iti
atra viśeṣam āha viṣṇuḥ: “anasāṃ dohyānāṃ balīvardādivāhyānāṃ sīmollaṅghanena parāvṛttiḥ” iti | ayam arthaḥ – samīpagrāmasthānāṃ dūragrāmasthānām api grāmāntare krītānāṃ śakaṭagomahiṣādīnāṃ tadgrāmasīmollaṅghane [318] vyavahārasyāparāvṛttiḥ | sīmollaṅghanāt prāg eva śakaṭādiparīkṣaṇaṃ tataḥ paraṃ nāstīti | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
dravyasvaṃ pañcadhā kṛtvā tribhāgo mūlyam ucyate |
lābhaś caturtho bhāgaḥ syāt pañcamaḥ satyam ucyate ||
etat kuṅkumavāṇijyaviṣayam | “caturtho bhāgo lābhaḥ | pañcamo bhāgaḥ satyaḥ kuṅkumādau” iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | mūlyanirṇaye prajāpatiḥ |
saṃvibhāge vinimaye kṣetrayor ubhayor api |
anusmṛtikṛtā tābhyāṃ kāryasiddhir bhaviṣyati ||
maṇigāndharvavīṇānāṃ nārīṇāṃ mūlyakalpanā |
nṛpājñayāpaṇasthānāṃ gobhūmyor ubhayecchayā ||
gāndharvāḥ – hayāḥ | gośabdo mahiṣyādīnām upalakṣakaḥ | rājājñayeti niyamavyāvartyam āha |
na sāmantair na tadgrāmais tasya mūlyaṃ niyamyate |
kiṃ tu rājñaiveti śeṣaḥ | na tadgrāmīṇair iti – teṣāṃ te pānyādīnām | grāmo grāmasthapuruṣāḥ | vivādasvarūpam āha vṛddhayājñavalkyaḥ |
idam asyeti yat kretā vikretrā saha saṃvadet |
etad eva vikrītasya kṣetrasya mūlyam iti | atra viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
yaḥ kaścid vañcakas teṣāṃ vijñātaḥ krayavikraye |
śapathaiḥ sa viśodhyaḥ syāt sarvavādeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti |
yathā krayasya svatvāpādakatvaṃ lokaprasiddhaṃ tadvad vinimayaparivṛttyor apy astīty āha viṣṇuḥ: “parivṛttivinimayau krayavat” iti krayeṇa tulyaṃ vartate krayavat | yathā kayaḥ svatvāpādakaḥ [319] tathā parivṛttivinimayāv iti vater arthaḥ | ayam arthaḥ – “svāmī rikthakrayasaṃvibhāga-” ityādi gautamasūtram niyamārtham iti vinimayaparivṛttyoḥ krayāntatayā vācanikadānāntatayā vā svatvāpādakatvam iti viṣṇusmṛtes tātparyaṃ varṇayanti bhāruci-asahāyaprabhṛtayaḥ | vijñānayogi-varadarājaprabhṛtayas tu “sapta vittāgamā dharmyāḥ” (cf. p. 72) iti saptasaṃkhyāyā ayogavyavacchedaparatvād vinimayaparivṛttyor api pṛthak svatvāpādakatvam iti viṣṇusmṛter artha ity āhuḥ | gautamasūtraṃ tu purastād vyākhyāsyate | parivṛttivinimayayoḥ svarūpam āha sa eva: “vijātīyayor dravyayor ekaṃ gṛhītvā ekasya pradānaṃ parivṛttiḥ | sajātīyayor dravyayor vinimayaḥ” iti |
nanu tilān datvā tilagrahaṇam eva vinimayaḥ | sa ca na saṃgacchate, prayojanābhād iti cet,
naivam, tilān datvā kālavyavadhānena tilagrahaṇaṃ vinimaya iti | yad vā – tilānāṃ bījatvārhāṇāṃ bījatvena saṃpādanārthaṃ tailajananasamarthais tilaiḥ saha vinimaya iti na kācit kṣatiḥ | yady api suvarṇādikaṃ mūlyaṃ datvā kṣetragṛhādi paṇyaṃ gṛhyate, tathāpi sā na parivṛttiḥ | mūlyāt sakāśāt phalādhikyāt “trivarṣamūlaphalakaṃ kreyam” iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | yāvatā mūlyena kreyaṃ kṣetraṃ tanmūlyaṃ varṣatrayād arvāg eva yāt cet kreyam iti parivartanād vijātīyādhikye mūlyādhikye mūlyasamaya(?) eva bhavati | vinimayas tu tathaiveti teṣāṃ parasparabhedaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha bhāradvājaḥ |
sāṃdhiś ca parivṛttiś ca vibhāgaś ca samā (yadi) api |
ā daśāhān nivartante viṣame navavatsarāt || [320]
sumantur api |
saṃdhiś ca parivṛttiś ca vibhāgaś ca samā yadi |
ā daśāhān nivartyāḥ syur vaiṣamye navavatsarāt || iti |
sāmyapakṣe sāmānyataḥ prāptadaśāhān nivṛttiḥ | vaiṣamye sati navavarṣād ūrdhvaṃ nivṛttir nāstīty evamartham uktā | na ca krayasyaiva daśāhānuśayo na parivṛttyāder iti vācyam, “daśāho ‘nuśayaḥ krayaḥ” iti krayaśabdasya tulyānām arthakriyāṇām upalakṣakatvāt | yat tu vṛddhakātyāyanenoktam,
saṃdhiś ca parivṛttiś ca viṣamā vā tribhogataḥ |
ājñayāpi krayaś cāpi daśābdaṃ vinivartayet ||
tribhogataḥ – tripuruṣataḥ | sa ca varṣamadhye yady āsīt tad atyantavaiṣamyaviṣayam | ājñākrayasamānayogakṣematvokteḥ kvacit parivṛttir api parivartanīyety āha viṣṇuḥ: “abdān maṇyādivinimayaḥ parāvartate | tathaivārdhādhikena miśritā parivartanā” | ardhādhikadravyeṇeti śeṣaḥ | ratnahīrādimiśritadravyaṃ datvā yat kiṃcid dhānyādikaṃ hayādikaṃ vā gṛhṇāti sā parivartanā | tathā ca sumantuḥ |
kraya evaṃ bhaven nyūne viparītau samāv ubhau |
ayam arthaḥ – “ardhān nyūne same vā krayaḥ parāvartate | ardhādhike krayāsiddhiḥ” iti smṛteḥ, parivṛttivinimayau tu viparītau – viṣamau parāvartete | na samau, sāmye parāvṛttyayogāt | ā daśāhān nivartanta iti sarvasamānatvenautsargikatvāt | daśāhasyānuśayakālatvāt | tadvad dhīnakrayaḥ parivṛttiś ca parāvartyete | [321] yathāha viṣṇuḥ: “hīnakrayaparivṛttyor vyāvartanam” iti | hīnakrayo nāmālpadravyenādhikakṣetrādeḥ krayaḥ | parivṛttir apy alpadravyasyādhikadravyeṇa parivṛttiḥ | etac cājñāparivṛttivinimayaviṣayaṃ veditavyam | rucikraye tu svarucyā svatvanivṛttiḥ parasvatāpattiś ca saṃkalpamātrād ity uktaṃ prāk | ataś ca kiṃcid api dravyaṃ mūlye praviṣṭe cet kreyaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅkamaṃ ca kretur eva na tu vikretuḥ, kiṃ tv avaśiṣṭaṃ dravyaṃ dātavyaṃ grahītavyaṃ ca | yathoktaṃ viṣṇunā: “svalpam api dravyaṃ kreyaṃ vyāpnoti” iti | kreyam – sthāvarajaṅgamātmakam | tathā ca sa eva: “sthāvarajaṅgamātmakadravyaṃ kreyam ucyate” iti | ata evāha sumantuḥ |
māṣamātram api dravyaṃ kretur vikretari sthitam |
vyāpnoti sakalāṃ bhūmiṃ kāyam alpaṃ viṣaṃ yathā ||
na caitad vacanaṃ pratibandhkaviṣayam, tatprakaraṇe apāṭhāt | bhūmiviṣayatvāt pratibandhakakrayaviṣayayo na bhavatīti bhārucyādibhir vyākhyātatvāt | varadarājo ‘py evam evāha tena svalpadravyapradāne ‘pi krayasiddhir asty eveti vadan |
nanv arthadattasya cādattasya cāvakrayatvāt kathaṃ svalpadravyapradāne krayasiddhiḥ |
ucyate | avakrayo ‘pi sidhyaty eva, yadi saṃkalpitakāre mūlyaṃ dīyate | anyathā na sidhyatīty abhisaṃdāyāha sa eva |
arthadattam adattaṃ krayam āhur avakrayam |
avakrayo nivarteta yadi kāle na dīyate || iti |
pratibandhakraye viśeṣam āha bhāradvājaḥ |
pratibandhaṃ kraye kṛtvā vikretranuśayo yadi |
[322]        kṣetre sa dāpyas tad dravyaṃ dviguṇaṃ damam arhati ||
yadā kretur eva pratibandhaṃ kṛtvānuśayas tad āha viṣṇuḥ: “anuśayate kretā pratibandho vikretur eva” iti | ayaṃ pratibandhakrayaḥ sthāvare nāsti | yad āha sa eva: “pratibandhakrayo nāsti sthāvare” iti | tat tu pūgādyārāmasthāvaraviṣayam ity eke | apare tu phalitavrīhyādikṣetraviṣayam iti | anye tu sādhāraṇakṣetraviṣayam ity āhuḥ | sarvatra sthāvarakraye jñātisāmantādyanumatyā bhāvyam | tathā ca prajāpatiḥ |
jñātisāmantadhanikān anujñāpya samīpagān |
krayavikrayaṇe kuryāc chrāvayitvā tu sākṣiṇaḥ ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
jñātyādīn ananujñāpya samīpasthān aninditān (atandritān) |
krayavikrayadharmo ‘pi bhūmer nāstīti nirṇayaḥ ||
sumantus tu viśeṣam āha |
jñātyādīn ananujñāpya samīpasthān atandritān (aninditān) |
krayavikrayakartārau tatsamaṃ daṇḍam arhati ||
samīpasthān ity ekagrāmavāsināṃ grahaṇam | bṛhaspatiḥ |
praṣṭavyāh saṃnidhisthāś cet kretrā jñātyādayaḥ smṛtāḥ |
anyathā cet kṛtaṃ karma jñātīcchāṃ darśayet tataḥ ||
“tat karma jñātīn evānusaratīty arthaḥ” iti bhāruciḥ | tatra kālaniyamam āha sa eva |
tripakṣād atha vā māsatritayāt tad avāpnuyāt | iti |
jñātir iti śeṣaḥ | tatra viśeṣam āha (viṣṇuḥ) kātyāyanaḥ |
svagrāme daśarātraṃ syād anyagrāme tripakṣakam |
[323]        rāṣṭrāntareṣu ṣaṇmāsaṃ bhāṣābhede tu vatsaram ||
yathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
jñātyādipratyayenaiva sthāvarakraya iṣyate |
anyathā cet krayo yaḥ syād anyagrāme tripakṣakam ||
atra vyāsaḥ |
bhūmer daśāho ‘nuśayaḥ kretur vikretur eva ca |
tātkālikās tu sāmantās tatkālā dhanikās tathā ||
tātkālikāḥ sapiṇḍāś ca vedanīyāḥ kraye matāḥ |
tatra sāmantān praty āha sumantuḥ |
athodīcyaḥ paścimo ‘pi sarvābhāve ‘pi (tu) dakṣiṇaḥ |
catuḥsāmantasāṃnidhye prācīdig balavattarā ||
udīcī ca pratīcī ca sarvābhāve tu dakṣiṇā |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
sodarāś ca sapiṇḍāś ca sodakāś ca sagotriṇaḥ |
sāmantā dhanikā grāhyāḥ saptaite yonayo matāḥ ||
yonayaḥ – kāraṇāni | atra eteṣāṃ jñātisāmantadhanikānāṃ ājñākraye uktalābhakraye ca praveśeo nāsti | yathāha viṣṇuḥ: “uktalābhe ājñāyāṃ na virodhaḥ” iti | jñātyādikṛta iti śeṣaḥ | ājñākrayoktalābhakrayayoḥ svarūpaṃ sumantunā darśitam |
mūlyasya pādam ardhaṃ vā mūlam (mūlyam) ājñākraye sthitam (smṛtam) |
mūlyaṃ tad āptam akhilaṃ datvā kṣetraṃ samāpnuyāt ||
ā tribhogāt tataḥ kretuḥ parato dṛḍhatām iyāt | [324]
atrāpi viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
palāyite tu karade karapratibhuvā saha |
karārthaṃ karadakṣetraṃ vikrīṇīyuḥ sabhāsadaḥ ||
bhāradvājas tu viśeṣam āha |
ājñādhis tatkrayaś caive kare daṇḍo vidhīyate |
ubhāv anyatra na syātām iti dharmavido viduḥ || iti |
anyatra na syātām iti – āhvāne ‘pi nāgatā ity arthāḥ | athoktalābhakrayaḥ |
kiṃcic ca dravyam ādāya kāle dāsyāmi te kvacit |
no cen mūlam idaṃ tyaktaṃ kedārasyeti yaḥ krayaḥ ||
sa uktalābha ity uktaṃ ukatakāle ‘py anarpaṇāt ||
ataś ca ājñākrayoktalābhakrayoḥ aupādhikasvatvāt jñātyādīnāṃ tatrānavakāśa ity anusaṃdheyam | anena nyāyena jñāyate krayāntagopyādiprabhṛtisaṃkarādikrayeṣu sopādhikasvatvāt | jñātyādikṛto nirodho nāsty eveti mantavyam | kecit tu saṃkarādiṣu jñātyādipratyayena bhāvyam ity āhuḥ |
pūrvāhṇe grāmamadhye tu jñātisāmantasaṃnidhau |
hiraṇyodakadānena ṣaḍbhir gacchati medinī ||
etaddharmaṣaṭkamadhye jñātisāmantasaṃnidhir āvaśyakaḥ | “jñātyādipratyayenaiva sthāvarakraya iṣyate” ityādivacanānurodhāt (-nusārāt) | pūrvāhṇagrāmamadhyayos tu na niyamaḥ | hiraṇyodakadāneneti tu niyamo dharmaḥ, bhūkrayasya niṣiddhatvāt | ataś caitad dharmaṣaṭkaṃ sati saṃbhave aṅgīkartavyam eva | asaṃbhave tu bhūkraye jñātisāmanta- [325] saṃnidhānātmakadharmatrayam avaśyam aṅgīkartavyam iti bhārucer abhiprāyaḥ | ādhunikānāṃ varadarājaprabhṛtīnām api saṃmatam etam | vijñānayoginas tu na saṃmatam | yathāha vijñānayogī: “pūrvāhṇa ity anena dānakāla uktaḥ | jñātīty etat vibhaktāvibhaktatvaniścayārtham uktaṃ padam | sāmantapadaṃ tu gṛhakṣetrādīnāṃ parasparaṃ saṃkīrṇatvaphalābhiprāyam | hiraṇyodakadānenetyādi bhūkrayasya niṣiddhātvād iti sarvam etad dharmaṣaṭkaṃ na niyataṃ svatvasya laukikatvāt” iti | tan na sahante bhārucyādayaḥ | svatvasya laukikatve ‘pi bhūkrayasya niṣiddhatvāt taddānāṅgatayā dharmapañcakasya niyatatvam iti | etad eva (saṣṭīkrtaṃ matam) samyaṅ matam |
atha kṣetraviṣaye anucitamūlyam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
samavetais tu sāmantair abhijñaiḥ pāpabhīrubhiḥ |
kṣetrārāmagṛhādīnāṃ dvipadāṃ ca catuṣpadām ||
kalpitaṃ mūlyam ity āhuḥ bhāgaṃ kṛtvā tad aṣṭadhā |
ekabhāgātiriktaṃ vā hīnaṃ vānucitaṃ smṛtam ||
taddhīnamūlyam anucitamūlyaṃ vety abhidhīyata ity arthaḥ | hīnamūlyaṃ sarvathā pratyāvartanīyam āha sa eva |
samāḥ śatam atīte ‘pi sarvaṃ tad vinivartate |
krayavikrayaṇe krayyaṃ yan mūlyaṃ dharmato ‘rhati ||
tat turye pañcame ṣaṣṭhe saptame ‘ṃśe ‘ṣṭame ‘pi vā |
hīno (hīne) yadi vinivṛtte krayavikāyaṇe sati ||
hīnamūlyaṃ tu tat sarvaṃ kṛtam apy akṛtaṃ bhavet |
[326]        uktād alpatare hīne kraye naiva praduṣyati ||
tenāpy aṃśena hīyeta mūlyataḥ krayavikrayet |
kṛtam apy akṛtaṃ prāhur anye dharmavido janāḥ ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
mūlyaṃ datvādhikaṃ nyūnaṃ mūlyasyānucitaṃ smṛtam |
krayasiddhes (-siddhis) tu naiva syād vatsarāṇāṃ śatair api ||
atra jñātyādipraveśo ‘sty eva, rucikrayatvāt | atra bṛhaspatir viśeṣam āha |
vikrayeṣu ca sarveṣu kūpavṛkṣādi lekhayet |
jalamārgādi yat kiṃcid anyaiś caiva bṛhaspatiḥ ||
kṣetrādyupetaṃ paripakvasasyaṃ vṛkṣaṃ phalaṃ vāpy upabhogayogyam |
kūpaṃ taṭākaṃ gṛham unnataṃ ca (krīte) kretre ca vikretur idaṃ vadanti ||
eṣu krayapatreṣu ālikhiteṣu vikretur bhavantīty arthaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
ardhādhike krayaḥ siddhyed uktalābho daśādhikaḥ (daśābdikaḥ) |
apakrayas (ava-) tribhogena (-bhāgena) sadya eva rucikrayaḥ ||
atra vinimayaparivṛttyoḥ krayadharmān atidiśati hārītaḥ |
mattamūḍhānabhijñātabhītair vinimayaḥ krataḥ (kṛtaḥ?) |
yac cānucitamūlyaṃ syāt tat sarvaṃ vinivartate || [327]
svalpamūlyāt krayasiddhau viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
mūlyāt svalpapradāne ‘pi krayasiddhiḥ kṛtā bhavet |
cakravṛddhyā pradātavyaṃ deyaṃ tat samayād ṛte ||
etāvatā kālena dāsyāmīti samayād ṛte yācyamānam adattaṃ cakravṛddhyā vardhate | samayakaraṇe tasmin kāle pūrvadattaśeaṃ deyam | tasminn api kāle yācyamānaṃ na dattaṃ cec cakravṛddhyā vardata eveti dhyeyam ||
iti śrīpratāparudramahādevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe krītvānuśayākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
[328]
atha samayānapākarmākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
tatra nāradaḥ |
pāṣaṇḍanaigamādīnāṃ sthitiḥ samaya ucyate |
samasyānapākarma tad vivādapadaṃ smṛtam ||
tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “bahubhiḥ sādhubhir mahājanarakṣaṇārthaṃ kṛtā saṃvit samayaḥ | asyānyathā karaṇaṃ samayānapākarma” iti | tathā ca śaṅkhalikhitau: “yāṃ vinopadravo duṣpariharo dharmakāryaṃ ca duḥsādhaṃ sā pāribhāṣikī dharmakriyā | tadanapākaraṇaṃ samayānapākaraṇam” iti | pūrvasmin prakaraṇe krītānuśaye parivṛttyanuśaye ca krītaviṣayaḥ parivṛttiviṣayaś ceti krama uktaḥ | atra tu samasyātikrama ucyata iti saṃgatiḥ | atra samayānapākaraṇaśabde darśaval lakṣaṇety upoddhātaprakaraṇe nirūpitam | atra tu tadupayogitayā kiṃcid āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
rājā kṛtvā pure sthānaṃ brāhmaṇān nyasya tatra tu |
traividyaṃ vṛttimad brūyāt svadharmaḥ paripālyatām || iti |
tad adyam arthaḥ – rājā nṛpatiḥ, svapure durgādau taddeśe ‘vasthānaṃ gṛhārāmakṣetrādikaṃ ca datvā sarvamānyagrāmān datvety arthaḥ | atra brāhmaṇān sthāpayitvā tat brāhmaṇavrātaṃ vedatrayasaṃpannaṃ vṛttimat sarvasaṃpatsamṛddhaṃ kṛtvā svadharmaḥ varṇāśramavihitaḥ śrutismṛtivihito bhavadbhir anuṣṭhīyatām iti tān brūyāt | evam evaṃ niyuktaiḥ yaḥ kṛtaḥ samayaḥ so ‘nullaṅghanīyo rājñety āha | [329] sa eva |
nijadharmāvirodhena yas tu sāmayiko bhavet |
so ‘pi yatnena saṃrakṣyo dharmo rājakṛtaś ca yaḥ ||
tatrāha (atrāha) manuḥ |
yo grāmadeśasaṅghānāṃ kṛtvā satyena saṃvidam |
visaṃvaden naro lobhāt taṃ rāṣṭrād vipravāsayet ||
nigṛhya dāpayed enaṃ samayavyabhicāriṇam |
catuḥsuvarṇaṃ ṣaṇṇiṣkaṃ śatamānaṃ ca rājatam ||
etad anubandhālpatve veditavyam | anubandhādyatiśaye tu yājñavalkyaḥ |
gaṇadravyaṃ hared yas tu saṃvidaṃ laṅghayec ca yaḥ |
sarvasvaharaṇaṃ kṛtvā taṃ rāṣṭrād vipravāsayet || iti |
bṛhaspatiḥ |
yas tu sādhāraṇaṃ hiṃstyāt kṣipet traividyam eva vā |
sākṣikriyāṃ (saṃdhikriyāṃ) vihanyāc ca kṣipraṃ nirvāsyate purāt ||
aruntudaḥ sūcakaś ca bhedakṛt sāhasī tathā |
śreṇīpūganṛpadviṭ cet kṣipraṃ nirvāsyate tataḥ ||
kulaśreṇīgaṇādhyakṣāḥ puradurganivāsinaḥ |
vāgdhigdamaṃ parityāgaṃ prakuryuḥ pāpakāriṇām ||
taiḥ kṛtau yau svadharmeṇa nigrahānugrahau nṛṇām |
tad rājñāpy anumantavyaṃ nisṛṣṭārthā hi te smṛtāḥ ||
vāgdhigdamam iti dvandvaḥ | mukhyānāṃ vivāde viśeṣam āha [330] sumantuḥ |
mukhyaiḥ saha samūhānāṃ saṃvivādo yadā bhavet |
tadā vicārayed rājā svamārge sthāpayec ca tān ||
ayam arthaḥ – yadā naigamādīnām āndolikārohaṇaṃ cāmarādivījanādikaṃ saṃbhāvitaṃ karmāstīti samayas tatkarmakāribhir anuṣṭhīyeta tadā rājñaiva vivādaḥ parisamāpyaḥ | tathaiva pāṣaṇḍādīnāṃ vaiṣṇavānām evotsavādau kuṅkumavasanto ‘sti śaivānāṃ neti vivādaḥ | etādṛśo vivādo rājñaiva parisamāpyaḥ | ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
śreṇīnaigamapāṣaṇḍigaṇānām apy ayaṃ vidhiḥ |
bhedaṃ caiṣāṃ nṛpo rakṣet pūrvavṛttiṃ ca pālayet ||
teṣāṃ svarūpam upodghātaprakaraṇa evoktam | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
ekapātre ‘tra paṅktyāṃ vā na bhoktā yena yo bhavet |
akurvan sa (-van na) tathā daṇḍyaḥ tasya doṣam adarśayan ||
gaṇadravyāpahāre viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
saṃbhūyaikatamaṃ kṛtvā gaṇadravyaṃ haranti ye |
etad aṣṭādaśaguṇaṃ vaṇijaś ca palāyinaḥ ||
dāpyā iti śeṣaḥ | rājñā dāpite viśeṣam āha |
tato labheta yat kiṃcit sarveṣām eva tatsamam |
ṣāṇmāsaṃ māsikaṃ vāpi vibhaktavyaṃ yathāṃśataḥ ||
deyaṃ vā niḥsvavṛddhārtastrībālāturarogiṣu |
sāntānikādiṣu tathā dharma eṣa sanātanaḥ || iti |
tatra kātyāyanaḥ |
yat taiḥ prāptaṃ rakṣitaṃ vā gaṇārthe vā ṛṇaṃ kṛtam |
[331]        rājaprasādalabdhaṃ ca sarveṣām eva tatsamam ||
mahīpatigrahaṇam amātyādīnām upalakṣaṇārtham | samūhākāreṇa nagarādikaṃ praviśya tadākāravicchede sati prātisvikalabdham apy avibhaktārjitadhanavatsādhāraṇam ity arthaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
gaṇam uddiśya yat kiṃcit kratv ṛṇaṃ bhakṣitaṃ (ca yadā) bhavet |
ātmārthaṃ viniyuktaṃ vā deyaṃ tair eva tad bhavet ||
anyathā gaṇenaiva deyam ity arthaḥ | śreṇyādīn adhikṛtyāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
samūhakārya āyātān kṛtakāryān visarjayet |
sadānamānasatkāraiḥ pūjayitvā mahīpatiḥ ||
iti śrīpratāparudradevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe samayānapākarmākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
[332]
atha kṣetrajavivādākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
pūrvasmin prakaraṇe naigamādiviṣayatayā saṃvidvyatikramaḥ | atra tu sīmāviṣayaḥ saṃvidvyatikrama iti yady apy aikādhikaraṇyam eva yuktam, tathāpi strīpuṃsākhyavivādapadopayogitayā gṛhakṣetrādinirṇayopayogitayā ca pṛthagadhikaraṇatā | atra nāradaḥ |
prakāśair aprakāśaiś ca liṅgaiḥ sīmāṃ nayen nṛpaḥ |
arthipratyarthinor yatra sākṣyādipratyayair nayet ||
pakāśair nyagodhāśvatthakiṃśukādivṛkṣalatātṛṇagulmasetuvalmīkakulyādibhir liṅgaiḥ aprakāśaiḥ karīṣāsthyaṅgāraśarkarādibhiḥ bhūmau gūḍhaṃ nikṣiptair liṅgaiḥ | ata eva manuḥ |
etair liṅgair nayet sīmāṃ rājā vivadamānayoḥ |
etaiḥ – pūrvoktaiḥ gūḍhāgūḍhair liṅgaiḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
niveśakāle (nideśa-) kartavyaḥ sīmābandhavinirṇayaḥ |
prakāśopaṃśucihnaiś ca lakṣitaḥ saṃśayāpahaḥ ||
niveśakālo – grāmādinirmāṇakālaḥ | sīmāniyāmako viniyuktaś ca yo niścāyakaḥ | yad vā cihnair viniścayaḥ kartavyaḥ | yathā uttarottaraṃ kālaho (kalaho?) mā bhūd iti | upāṃśucihnāni – guptaliṅgāni | prakāśaliṅgāni guptaliṅgāni smṛtyantare darśitāni | tathā ca smṛtiḥ |
vāpīkūpataṭākāni caityārāmasurālayāḥ |
sthalanimnanadīsrotaḥ śaragulmalatādayaḥ ||
[333]        prakāśacihnāny etāni sīmāyāṃ kārayet sadā |
karīṣāsthituṣāṅgāraśarkarāsthikapālikāḥ ||
sikateṣṭakagovālakārpāsāsthīni bhasma ca ||
etāni kumbhe nikṣipye sīmānteṣu nikhānayet | iti |
kumbha ity ekavacanam avivakṣitam, kumbheṣv ity arthaḥ | ādiśabdena veṇuvalmīkavartva-purātanasetvādīnāṃ grahaṇam | kārpāsāsthituṣāṅgārakārpāsabījāni | eteṣāṃ prayojanam āha manuḥ |
upacihnāni cānyāni sīmāliṅgāni kārayet |
sīmājñāne nṛṇāṃ vīkṣya loke nityaṃ viparyayam ||
viparyayajñānaṃ nivartayed ity arthaḥ | (prātsyanaidhanityādīnāṃ)? sīmānairāsyanityānāṃ svarūpaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ ca śāstrād evoktam | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
tataḥ paugaṇḍabālānāṃ prayatnena pradarśayet |
vārdhakena śiśūnāṃ te darśayeyus tathaiva ca ||
te – paugaṇdabālāḥ svavārdhike śiśūnāṃ darśayeyur ity arthḥ |
evaṃ paramparājñāne sīmābhrāntir na jāyate | iti |
liṅgāny avalambya (?) nirṇayāśaktau manuḥ |
yadi saṃśaya eva syāt liṅgānām api darśane |
sākṣipratyaya eva syāt sīmāvādaviniścaye ||
atra sākṣiṇo viśinaṣṭi bṛhaspatiḥ |
āgamaṃ ca pramāṇaṃ ca bhogakālaṃ ca nāma ca |
bhūbhāgalakṣaṇaṃ caiva ye vidus te ‘tra sākṣiṇaḥ ||
āgamaḥ svatvāpādakaḥ krayādi | pramāṇam – daṇḍādinā etāvān [334] bhūbhāga it parikalpitaṃ pramāṇam | sākṣyabhāve ‘pi sa evāha |
sākṣyabhāve ‘pi catvāro grāmāḥ sīmāntavāsinaḥ |
sīmāvinirṇayaṃ kuryuḥ prayatā rājasaṃnidhau ||
yadi sīmāntagrāmā duṣṭāḥ tadā tatprāntagrāmaiḥ nirṇayaḥ kāryaḥ | yadi te ‘pi duṣṭāḥ te sarve tyājyāḥ anye grāhyā ity āha sa eva |
tyaktvā duṣṭāṃs tu sāmantān anyān maulyādibhiḥ saha |
saṃmiśya (?) kārayet sīmām evaṃ dharmavido viduḥ || iti |
anyaśabdārtham āha nāradaḥ |
nagaragrāmagaṇino ye ca vṛddhodyatā narāḥ |
atra maulānāṃ svarūpam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
ye tatra pūrvasāmantāḥ paścād deśāntaraṃ gatāḥ |
tanmūlatvāt tato maulā ṛṣibhiḥ saṃprakīrtitāḥ ||
niṣpādyamānaṃ yair dṛṣṭaṃ tatkāryasya guṇānvitaiḥ |
vṛddhā vā yadi vāvṛddhās te vṛddhāḥ parikīrtitāḥ ||
upaśravaṇasaṃbhogakāryākhyānopacihnitāḥ |
uddharanti tato yasmād udyatās te tataḥ smṛtāḥ || iti |
sākṣiprabhṛtyuddhṛtaparyantānām abhāve manuḥ |
sāmantānām abhāve tu maulānāṃ sīmasākṣiṇām |
imān apy anuyuñjīta puruṣān vanagocarān ||
imān anuyuñjītety anvayaḥ | anyān kāṣṭhatṛṇādivividhavāhādīn | [335] eteṣāṃ sākṣiṇāṃ niyamapūrvakam eva vaktavyam ity āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
śapataiḥ śāpitāś caiva brūyuḥ sīmāvinirṇayam |
darśayeyuś ca liṅgāni tatpramāṇam iti sthitam ||
satyena śāpayed vipraṃ kṣatriyaṃ vāhanāyudhaiḥ || iti |
ityādinoktavyavasthātikrameṇety arthaḥ | yadā tu sīmāliṅgasākṣiṇo lakṣaṇāni kathaṃcid api darśayituṃ na śaknuvanti kevalaṃ sīmām eva vadanti tān praty āha manuḥ |
śirobhiḥ svair gṛhītāgniḥ sragviṇo (?) raktavāsasaḥ |
sukṛtaiḥ śāpitāḥ svaiḥ svaiḥ nayeyus tat (te?) samañjasam ||
yāvantaḥ sīmāliṅgasākṣiṇaḥ te sarve rohitakusumasragviṇaḥ lohitavasanāḥ kukulāgniṃ śirasi dhārayantaḥ sīmānuyāyino vinā caṅkramaṇena sīmāntaṃ pradarśayantaḥ tāṃ sīmāṃ samañjasam –samyak nirṇayeyur ity arthaḥ | sāmantādīnāṃ sīmonnayane liṅgapradarśanāśaktāv ayam eva vidhir jñātavyaḥ | tatra viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
naikaḥ samunnayet sīmāṃ naraḥ pratyayavān api |
gurutvād asya kāryasya kiryaiṣā bahubhiḥ (bahuṣu) sthitā ||
atrāpavādam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
jñātṛcihnair vinā sādhur eko ‘py ubhayasaṃmataḥ |
raktamālyāmbaradharo mṛdam ādāya mūrdhani ||
satyavrataḥ sopavāsaḥ sīmāntaṃ darśayen naraḥ || iti |
jñātṛcihnaiḥ sākṣyādivanagocarāntacihnaiḥ | prakāśopāṃśuliṅgair vinā abhāve mṛdam ādāyeti | agnivirahitaḥ apakvaḥ mṛṇmayaḥ kukulaḥ | tasmin gaṇādhipaṃ kṛtvā tatra nikṣipya [336] āvāhanādiṣoḍaśopacāraiḥ pūjāṃ kṛtvā paścād darvābhir ekottarasaṃkhyābhiḥ saṃpūjya tadgaṇeśayuktaṃ śirasi dhṛtvā caṅkramamāṇa eva sīmāṃ nayed iti | bahūnāṃ (?) sīmonnayane kukulāgnidhāriṇaṃ caṅkramaṇaṃ vinā mandagamanam ekasya tu tadabhāva iti | atra pratyarthinā śiraḥsthānīyatayā anuyātavyam | atra kuddālādīnākaṭvādikaṃ (?) kāryam | atra manuvacane caṅgramāṇānantaram eva śuddhir iti pratibhāti | tan na | tathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
sīmā caṅgramaṇe kośe pādasparśe tathaiva ca |
tripakṣapakṣasaptāhaṃ daivarājakam iṣyate || iti |
yathāsaṃkhyam iti śeṣaḥ |
nanu sīmonnayanavelāyāṃ pratyarthyanuyāne daivavaśāt skhalitaṃ patanaṃ ca bhavet | kukūlabhaṅgaḥ anyair vā nāśaḥ | āmukakulasya vināyakasya vā bhinnatvam ity evaṃprakāradṛṣṭāpacāradarśane sadya eva jayavyavasthā dṛśyate | tadvat tadabhāve ‘pi sadya eva pratyarthinaḥ parājayavyavasthayā bhāvyam iti tat kathaṃ daivarājakopayātaparijñānārthaṃ tripakṣādipratīkṣaṇam iti cet,
satyaṃ kṣetravivādasya mahatvāt | jayavyavasthā sadyapi (?) | tadabhāve ‘pi sadya eva pratyarthinaḥ parājayavyavasthāpi pratīkṣyaiva | tripakṣapratīkṣaṇam etāvatparyantaṃ pratighāte ca mā bhūd iti dṛṣṭapramāṇebhyaḥ tulādibhyaḥ kukūladhāraṇaṃ (nāstī yatrāste tatra tripakṣaparīkṣṇam uktarītyeti bhārucer abhiprāyaḥ) bhinnatvena smṛtam iti dhyeyam | etat tu bhārucimatam |
vijñānayogiprabhṛtayaś ca kukūladvayam anulikhitavanto mṛdam ādāya mūrdhanīti vacanaṃ mṛtpiṇḍamānaparatvena vyākhyātavantatripakṣapratīkṣaṇam [337] vyākurvate | tanmate sīmānirṇayāya kukūladhāraṇaṃ nāsti | yatrāsti (?) tatra tripakṣapratīkṣaṇam uktarītyeti bhārucer abhiprāyaḥ |
yat tūkaṃ viṣṇunā: “sīmāvivāde maraṇāntikā jayavyavasthā” iti, tat tu viṣayānantareṇa rājñā viṣayāntarasthasya rājñaḥ sīmāvivāde yuddhasādhyaviṣaye kecana traividyavṛddhāḥ vivadamānarājadvayam anumānyatatsīmāparijñātāraṃ kukūladhāraṇādiniyamena nayeyuḥ | yadā satyabhaṅgaḥ (tasyabhaṅgaḥ) sa tu hantavyam iti tadviṣayaḥ |
tatprakāraś ca anekasmṛtisiddhaḥ saṃgṛhya kiṃcid ucyate — rājñoḥ sīmānimitte vivadamānayodhyasthair āgatya tatsīmānirṇayārthaṃ tatparijñātṛpuruṣaṃ yaṃ kaṃcana samāhūya tam ekena nayanena sāñjanam anyena nirañjanam ekena keśabhāgena śikhāvantam anyena visrastakeśam ekena pādena sopānatkam anyena nirupānatkaṃ dhṛtakaupīnaṃ baddhamaunaṃ kṛtvā āmakukūlamadhye jalaṃ nikṣipya tanmadhye gaṇeśam arcayitvā tatkukūle dikpālādyāvāhanaṃ divyamātṛkoktaṃ vidhāya varuṇapūjāṃ kṛtvā dharaṇīvarāham āvāhya “uddhṛtāsī” iti mantreṇa pūjayitvāpaścāllokaṃ nayet | paścād bhāgaṃ na lokitavān apaścāllokaḥ | tasminn ekādaśapadacaṅkramaṇaiḥ sīmām unnayati sati kukūlajalaśoṣo vā kukūlabhaṅgo vā yadi syāt taṃ tathaivānuyāyinaḥ pratyarthino hanyuḥ | atra sādyaskī(?) jayaparājayavyavasthā | na cātra tripakṣapratīkṣaṇam iti | vivādakālam āha manuḥ |
sīmāṃ prati samutpanne vivāde grāmayor dvayoḥ |
jyeṣṭhamāsi nayet sīmāṃ suprakāśeṣu setuṣu ||
[338] iti | kṣetrādinirṇaye sāmantair anyonyasīmānirṇayaḥ kāryaḥ | grāmasāmantādīnāṃ kṣetragṛhasīmādiṣu paricitatvāt | tathāha sa eva |
grāmo grāmasya sāmantaḥ kṣetraṃ kṣetrasya kīrtitaḥ |
grahaṃ grahasya vidviṣṭaṃ sāmantān paribhāvayet ||
tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
sarvasmin sthāvare vāde vidhir eṣa prakīrtitaḥ | iti |
ayam arthaḥ – evam eva kedārārāmodyānadevālayataṭākakūpapravarṣaṇodbhūtajalapravāha-sthānādisīmāvivāde sākṣitaḥ tatsāmantāditaḥ nirṇetā rājā nirṇayet iti | nadyāpahṛtabhūviṣaye nāradaḥ |
ekatra kūlapātaṃ tu bhūmer anyatra saṃsthitiḥ |
nadītīre prakurute tasya tāṃ na vicā(la)rayet ||
tasya nadīvaśāt labdhabhūmikasya tāṃ nadīkṛtabhūmisaṃsthitiṃ pūrvasvāmī na vicālayed ity arthaḥ | etad eva spaṣṭīkṛtaṃ bṛhaspatinā |
anyagrāmāt samāhṛtya dattānyasya yadā mahī |
mahānadyātha vā rājñā kathaṃ tatra vicāraṇā ||
nadyotsṛṣṭā rājadattā yasya tasyaiva sā mahī |
anyathā na bhavel lābho narāṇāṃ rājadaivikaḥ ||
kṣayodayau jīvanaṃ ca daivarājavaśān nṛṇām |
tasmāt sarveṣu kāryeṣu tatkṛtaṃ na vicālayet ||
etac cāluptasasyatīraviṣayam | luptasasyatīraviṣaye manuḥ |
kṣetraṃ sasasyam ullaṅghya bhūmiś chinnā yadā bhavet |
[339]        nadīsrotaḥpravāheṇa pūrvasvāmī labheta tām ||
luptaphalasasyalābhād ūrdhvaṃ prācīnavacanāviṣayatvam eva | rājadattaviṣaye viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
rājñā krodhena lobhena chalān nyāyena vā hṛtā |
pradattānyasya tuṣṭena na sā siddhim avāpnuyāt ||
gṛhādiviṣaye viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
niveśakālād ārabhya gṛhavāryāpaṇādikam |
yena yāvad yathā bhuktaṃ tasya tāṃ na vicārayet (-cālayet) ||
ādiśabdena vātāyanādīnāṃ grahaṇam | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
vātāyanaṃ praṇālī ca tathā nirvyūhavedikā |
catuḥśālasya dhanikāḥ prāṅniviṣṭā na cālayet |
niveśasamayād ūrdhvaṃ naite yogyāḥ kadācana || iti |
vahniśvabhrādīnāṃ viṣaye bṛhaspatiḥ |
viṇmūtrodakavaprāṃś ca vahniśvabhraniveśanam |
aratnidvayam utsṛjya parakuḍyāṃ niveśayet ||
yānty āyānti janā yena paśavaś cānivāritāḥ |
tad ucyate saṃsaraṇaṃ na roddhavyaṃ tu kenacit ||
tathā ca nāradaḥ |
catuṣpathasurasthānarājamārgaṃ na rodhayet |
parakṣetrasya madhye tu setur na pratiṣidhyate ||
mahāguṇo ‘lpabādhaś ced vṛddhir iṣṭā kṣaye satī |
na niṣiddho ‘lpabādhaś ca setuḥ kalyāṇakārakaḥ || [340]
iti yājñavalkyasmaraṇāt |
parabhūmau haret kūpaṃ svalpakṣetre bahūdakam |
svāmino yo nivedyaiva kṣetre setuṃ prakalpayet ||
tatsvāmino bhogalābhas tadabhāve mahīpateḥ |
pūrvapravṛttam utpannam apṛṣṭvā svāminaṃ tu yaḥ ||
setuṃ prakalpayet kaścin na sa tatphalabhāg bhavet |
mṛte ‘pi svāmini punas tadvaṃśye cāpi mānave ||
rājānam āmantrya tataḥ kuryāt setupravartanam ||
kātyāyanaḥ |
asvāmyanumatenaiva saṃskāraṃ kurute tu yaḥ |
kūpodyānataṭākānāṃ saṃskartā labhate na tu ||
aśaktaḥ pretanaṣṭeṣu kṣetrakeṣv anivāritaḥ |
kṣetraṃ ced vikṛṣet kaścid aśnuvīta sa tatphalam ||
asyāpavādam āha sa eva |
vikṛṣyamāṇe kṣetre ca kṣetri(kaḥ) punar āvrajet |
śīlopacāraṃ tat sarvaṃ datvā kṣetram avāpnuyāt ||
tadaṣṭabhāgāpacayād yāvat sapta gatāḥ samāḥ |
samāpteṣv aṣṭame varṣe bhuktakṣetraṃ labhet saḥ ||
aśaktito na dadyāc cet tadarthaṃ yaḥ kṛto vyayaḥ |
tadaṣṭabhāgahīnaṃ tu karṣakaḥ phalam āpnuyāt ||
varṣā hy aṣṭau sa bhoktā syāt parataḥ svāmine tu tat |
nāradaḥ |
saṃvatsareṇārdhakhilaṃ khilaṃ syād vatsarais tribhiḥ |
pañcavarṣo ‘vasānaṃ tat kṣetraṃ syād aṭavī samam ||
[341]        kṣetraṃ gṛhītvā yaḥ kaścin na kuryān na ca kārayet |
svāmine sa damaṃ dāpyo rājñā daṇḍyaś ca tatsamam ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
phālāhatam api kṣetraṃ yo na kuryān na kārayet |
sa pradāpyaḥ kṛṣṭaphalaṃ kṣetram anyena kārayet ||
cirāvasanne daśamaṃ kṛṣyamāṇe daśāṣṭamam |
susaṃskṛteṣu ṣaṣṭaṃ syāt parikalpyaṃ yathāsthiti ||
iti śrīpratāparudramahādevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe sīmāvivādākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
[342]
atha strīpuṃsayogākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
tatra nāradaḥ |
vivādāder vidhiḥ strīṇāṃ yatra puṃsā ca kīrtyate |
strīpuṃsayogasaṃjñaṃ tad vivādapadam ucyate || iti |
atra pūrvaprakaraṇe kṣetrajavivādaḥ kathitaḥ | kṣetraṃ dvividhaṃ sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ ceti | sthāvarakṣetraviṣayavivādanirṇayānantaraṃ jaṅgamātmakastrīrūpakṣetrajavivādanirṇaya it saṃgatiḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
asvatantrāḥ striyaḥ kāryāḥ puruṣais tair divāniśam | iti |
yady api strīpuṃsayoḥ parasparam arthipratyarthityānṛpasamakṣaṃ vyavahāro niṣiddhaḥ, tathāpi pratyakṣeṇa karṇaparamparayā vidite tayoḥ parasparāticāre daṇḍādidānaṃ nijadharmamārgeṇa rājñā kāryam | itarathā doṣa eva rājñaḥ |
dharma ekaḥ patiḥ strīṇāṃ pūrvam eva tu kalpitaḥ |
bahipatnīkṛtaḥ puṃso dharmaś ca bahubhiḥ kṛtaḥ ||
strīdharmaḥ pūrvam evāyaṃ nirmito munibhiḥ purā |
sahadharmacarī bhartur ekā caikasya cocyate ||
eko bhartā hi nārīṇāṃ kaumāra iti laukikaḥ |
āpatsu ca niyogena santānārthe paraḥ smṛtaḥ ||
gaccheta yā tṛtīyaṃ ta (?) yāsyān niṣkṛtir ucyate |
caturthe patitā dharmāt pañcame vardhakī bhavet ||
[343]        evaṃ gate dharmapathe na vṛṇed bahusaṃskṛtām |
alokācaritād asmāt kathaṃ mucyed dhi saṃkarāt ||
īśvara uvāca |
anāvṛtāḥ purā nāryo māsāc chuddhyanti cārtave |
sakṛd uktaṃ tu yā naiva nādharmas te bhaviṣyati ||
iti mahābhāratoktarājadharmamadhye asya strīpundharmajātasyopadeśaḥ kṛto manu-nāradābhyām | yogīśvareṇa tu vivāhaprakaraṇa eva saprapañcaṃ pratipāditam iti punar atroktam | ata evāsmābhir api tanmatānusāribhiḥ na prapañcyate vivādapadam | atra yad bahuvaktavyam ati tad asmābhir apy ācārakāṇḍe vivāhaprakaraṇa eva prapañcitam iti tat evāvadhāryam |
iti śrīpratāparudramahādevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe strīpuṃsayogākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
[344]
atha dāyabhāgākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
puruṣottamabhūmīndratanayena mahībhujā |
