pratāparudradevena dāyabhāgo nirūpyate ||
pūrvaprakaraṇe strīpuṃsayogākhyo dharmaḥ pratipaditaḥ | atra tu strīpuṃsāv evādhikṛtya vibhāgaḥ pravartata iti tayor upajīvyopajīvakatayā saṃgatiḥ | na ca “jāyāpatyor na vibhāgo vidyate” iti smṛteḥ strīpuṃsayor vibhāga eva nāstīti vācyam | strīpuṃsayor vibhāgo vakṣyate | kiṃ ca kvacit puṃsāṃ vibhāgaḥ kvacit strīṇāṃ vibhāgaḥ kvacit strīpuṃsayor iti na kadācit kṣatir iti | dāyo nāma pitāputrasamudāyadravyam,
pitṛdravyaṃ vibhaktavyaṃ dāyam āhur manīṣiṇaḥ |
iti smṛteḥ | vibhaktavyam – vibhāgārham | bṛhaspatir api |
dadāti dīyate pitrā putrebhyaḥ svasya yad dhanam |
tad dāyam ....................... || iti |
pitā putrebhyo yad dhanaṃ dadātīti kartrantapitṛśabdo ‘dhyāhartavyaḥ | evaṃ dāyaśabdaḥ karmany eva vyutpanna iti | anena pitāputrasamudāyaviṣayakaṃ dravyaṃ dāyam iti sāmānyalakṣaṇam | saṃgrahakāro ‘pi |
pitṛdvārāgataṃ dravyaṃ mātṛdvārāgataṃ ca yat |
kathitaṃ dāyaśabdena tadvibhāgo ‘dhunocyate || iti |
bhāruci-aparārkādīnāṃ lakṣaṇam – vibhāgarhaṃ pitṛdravyaṃ dāyam iti | tad eva samyak, dharmavibhāge dravyavibhāge ‘py anugateḥ |
na ca vācyam: dharmāṇām agnihotravaiśvadevādīnāṃ pitṛdravyatvābhāvād vibhaktavyaṃ [345] pitṛdravyam iti lakṣaṇasya tatrānugatir nāstīti, “paitṛkadhanaṃ dvividham, bhoktavyam anuṣṭhātavyaṃ ca” iti viṣṇuvacanena bhoktavyaṃ kṣetragavādikam anuṣṭhātavyam agnihotrādikam iti anuṣṭhātavyasyāgnihotrādeḥ paitṛkatvasya pratipādanāt | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ |
karma smārtaṃ vivāhāgnau kurvīta pratyahaṃ gṛhī |
dāyakālāhṛte vāpi śrautaṃ vaitānikāgniṣu ||
vivāhasaṃbandhād ūrdhvam apy ayam agnir vaivāhiko bhavaty evety āha karkaḥ | dāyakālāhṛte vāpīty anena agnihotrāder vibhāga uktaḥ | tac cāgnihotrādipaitṛkam evāṅgīkartavyam | anyathā karma smārtavivāhāgnāv iti vyāhanyeteti | atrāha karkaḥ:
“abhrātṛkasya vaivāhiko bhrātṝṇāṃ dāyakālāhṛta eveti vyavastheti | ataś ca dāyakāle – dāyavibhāgakāle dāyatvenāhṛte svīkṛte” iti |
bhāruciḥ”
“ajīvadvibhāge śrotriyāgārāj jyeṣṭhenānītam agniṃ bhrātaro vibhajeyuḥ | atra paitṛkatvam agner upacaritam | jīvadvibhāge pitrānītam agniṃ vibhajeyuḥ | pitrānītaḥ paitṛka iti mukhyaṃ paitṛkatvam agneḥ | asmin pakṣe tathāvidhasyaivāgneḥ pitrā svabhrātṛbhya ānītatvāt” iti |
atra kecid āhuḥ: “dāyakālāhṛta ity asya agnihotrasvīkaraṇasya kālāntaram uktam” iti | tan na sahante bhāruciprabhṛtayaḥ, tathā saty asaṃskṛtyāsvīkāraprasaṅgād iti |
kecit tu – “ ‘dāyādy akāla eteṣām’ iti smṛteḥ agnyāharaṇasya kālāntaram uktam” iti | atredaṃ tattvam | vaivāhiko ‘gnir laukiko ‘laukikaś ceti matadvayam | laukikatvapakṣe prākaraṇikasya mantrāmnānasya [346] vaidhadānasiddhyarthatvāt kartṛsaṃskārakatvam eva | nāgnisaṃskārakatvam ahāraṇamātrasya mathanamātrasya vā saṃskārakatvaṃ vaktum ayuktam “mathitenāgninā śrotriyāgārād āhṛtena vā sphaṭikotpannena vā davānalena vā vivāhaḥ kāryaḥ” iti karkabhāṣye āhṛtatvamadhitatvayoḥ (?) sphaṭikotpannatvadāvānalatvābhyāṃ tulyayogakṣematayā pratipādanāt | na ca sphaṭikotpannasya sphaṭikotpattir eva, dāvānalasya vā dāvānalatvam eva saṃskāra iti vaktuṃ yuktam, ato laukika eva vaivāhikāgnir iti | ata eva āpastambena “agnināśe śrotriyāgārān mathanād vāgnim āhṛtya upoṣyāyāś ceti prāyaścittaṃ kṛtvā pūrvavaj juhuyāt” ity uktam | “agnināśe ‘py ayam eva vidhiḥ” iti vṛttikāraḥ | ata eva saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ pṛthag agnihotrakaraṇaṃ ca niṣiddham iti yad vacanaṃ jātaṃ tad upapannaṃ bhavati |
ayam arthaḥ – vibhāgakāle āhṛtasyāgneḥ putrāṇāṃ parasparavibhāgaḥ | vaivāhikāgner alaukikatvapakṣe “bhūr bhuvaḥ suvar om ity agniṃ pratiṣṭhāpayet” iti vidheḥ śrautātideśena alaukikatvam agneḥ | ata evāvibhāgadaśāyām api pṛthagagnihotrakaraṇapṛthagvaiśvadevādi-karaṇavidhaya upapannā bhavantīti | pakṣadvayapratipādako vāśabda ity āhuḥ | ata eva āśvalāyanena “agnināśe yugalojāntasaṃskārakalāpaṃ kṛtvā punar juhuyāt” ity uktam | “śrautaṃ vaitānikāgniṣu” iti pṛthaguktiḥ sarvathā vaitānikāgnīnāṃ vibhāgo nāstīti jñāpanārthā |
atrāhuḥ – vaivāhikāgner alaukikatvapakṣe parasparaṃ pṛthaganuṣṭhānam eva vibhāgaḥ | “laukikatvapakṣe parasparam agnisvīkaraṇam eva vibhāgaḥ” iti [347] lakṣmīdharaprabhṛtrayaḥ | etac ca vibhāgasaṃdehanirṇaye prapañcyate | asahāya-vijñānayogiprabhṛtīnāṃ tu “yat svāmisaṃbandhād eva nimittād anyasya svaṃ bhavati tad dāyaśabdenocyate” iti, tan na sahante bhāruci-aparārkaprabhṛtrayaḥ, “svatvahetūnāṃ krayādīnāṃ tallakṣaṇāsaṃbhavāt | na ca vācyam (?) evakāreṇa krayādayo vyudasyante, kretari dāyādo dāyaṃ gṛhṇātīti laukikaprayogābhāvāt” iti | tarhi strīṇāṃ dāyānarhatvāt, “tasmāt striyo nirindriyā adāyādīḥ” iti śruteḥ | strīdhanaṃ dāyaśabdavācyaṃ na bhavatīti tad uttaratra sphoryate (?) | “vibhāgo nāma dravyadharmayor anyatarasya pṛthakkaraṇam” ity āha bhāruciḥ | vijñānayogī tu “vibhāgo nāma dravyasamudāyaviṣayāṇām anekasvāmyānāṃ tadekadeśeṣu vyavasthānam” ity āha | tan na sahate bhāruciḥ – “dharmavibhāge tadabhāvāt” | dharmavibhāgo nāma dharmamātravibhāgaḥ | pṛthagvaiśvadevapañcamahāyajñānuṣṭhānaṃ paitṛkādikaraṇam | tac ca keṣāṃcid atyantaniḥsvānāṃ dravyābhāvād dharmavibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ ‘vibhāge dharmavṛddhiḥ syāt’ iti gautamasmṛteḥ | dharmavṛddhikāmānāṃ dharmamātravibhāgo vā kartavyaḥ | ata eva viṣṇuḥ: “dharmamātraṃ vā vibhajet” iti | atyantaniḥsvānām iti śeṣaḥ | anena jñāyate paribhāṣāṃ vinā saṃkalpamātreṇāpi vibhāgasiddhiḥ yathā putrikākaraṇaṃ paribhāṣāṃ vinā saṃkalpamātrāt sidhyatīti |
dravyavatāṃ tu dhanavibhāgānantaram eva dharmavibhāgaḥ: “vibhaktāḥ bhrātaraḥ kuryur nāvibhaktāḥ kathaṃcana” iti | vibhaktakartavyatayā dharmān vaiśvadevādikān adhikṛtyoktatvāt | ataś ca niḥsvānām itarānumatyā tadantareṇāpi [348] dharmānuṣṭhānam eva dharmavibhāgaḥ | dhanikānāṃ dhanavibhāgaḥ | evaṃ vibhāgasya dvaividhyam | ata evoktaṃ viṣṇunā: “dvividho vibhāgaḥ karmamūlo dāyamulaś ca” iti | atra – dāyaśabdasya sāmānyavācitve ‘pi viśeṣaparyavasānād dravyavācitvam | atra dharmaśabdena tatsādhanabhūtam agnihotrādikam ucyate | dharmavibhāgo manu-yājñavalkyādismṛtikārāṇān tatsmṛtivyākhyātṝṇām asahāya-medhātithi-vijñānayogīśvara-aparārkāṇāṃ nibandhṝṇāṃ candrikākārādīnāṃ ca saṃmata eva | tathā hi,
vaseyur daśa varṣāṇi pṛthagdharmāḥ pṛthakkriyāḥ |
vibhaktā bhrātaras te ‘pi vijñeyāḥ paitṛkād dhanāt ||
iti dharmavibhāga uktaḥ | tatra svayam evetarānumatiṃ vinā daśavarṣaparyantaṃ pṛthagdharmācaraṇaṃ vibhāgaḥ | yathā,
pitur dravyāvirodhena yad anyat svayam ārjitam |
maitram audvāhikaṃ caiva dāyādānān na tad bhavet ||
iti maitrādivyatirekeṇa yasya kim api nāsti tasya maitrādigrahaṇam eva vibhāgaḥ | tadvad atrāpīty anusaṃdheyam | ata evoktaṃ manunā |
evaṃ (eṣa) strīpuṃsayor ukto dharmo yo ratisaṃjñitaḥ |
āpady apatyaprāptiś ca dāyadharmaṃ nibodhata || iti |
atra bhāruciḥ: “dāyadharmaśabdena dāyavibhāgo dharmavibhāgo lakṣyate” ity āha | dāyavibhāgaṃ dharmavibhāgaṃ mayocyamānaṃ nibodhateti vacanārthaḥ | yady api dāyaśabdena vibhāgārhadravyavācinā dharmasyāpy upasaṃgrahaḥ, tathāpi vispaṣṭārtham uktaṃ dāyadharmam iti | paitṛkād dhanād iti lyablope pañcamī | paitṛkaṃ dhanam [349] upajīvyāpi daśavarṣaparyantaṃ pṛthagdharmācaraṇaṃ vibhāgahetur eveti kaimutikanyāyapradarśanārtham iti kecit | apare tu paitṛkaṃ dhanaṃ vihāyeti lyablopam āhuḥ | anyathā “anupaghnan pitṛdhanam” ityādimanvādivacanavirodhād iti | etad eva samyak | yathāha nibandhanakāraḥ: “ayem eva pakṣo jyāyān” iti |
yasmin kāle yayā bhaṅgyā yair eva kriyate ‘pi ca |
yādṛśasya yadhā yasya yathā śāstraṃ pradṛśyate || iti |
yādṛśasya dāyasya paitṛkamātṛkāder yasmin kāle “mātur nivṛtte rajasi” ityādikaṃ yathā – samaviṣamādibhaṅgyā prakāreṇa yaiḥ – pitṛmātṛbhaginyādibhir vibhāgo vaktavya iti | eva (ṃ?) caturthetikartavyatā kalāpo yasmin vivādapade nirūpyate tad dāyavibhāgo nām vivādapadam | atra saṃgrahakāro viśeṣam āha |
pitṛdravyavibhāgaḥ syāj jīvantyām api mātari |
na svatantratayā svāmyaṃ tasmān mātuḥ patiṃ vinā ||
mātṛdravyavibhāge ‘pi tathā pitari jīvati |
satsv apatyeṣu yasmān na strīdhanasya patiḥ patiḥ || iti |
ekaḥ patiśabdaḥ svāmivācakaḥ | aparo bhartṛvācakaḥ | anena pitari jīvati dravyavibhāgo mātari jīvantyāṃ taddravyavibhāgaḥ putrādeḥ na kārya ity arthād uktaṃ bhavati | tathā ca manuḥ |
ūrdhvaṃ pituś ca mātuś ca sametya bhrātaraḥ saha |
bhajeran paitṛkaṃ riktham anīśās te hi jīvatoḥ || iti |
anīśāḥ – asvatantrā ity arthaḥ | tathā ca hārītaḥ: “jīvati pitari putrāṇām arthādānavisargākṣepeṣv asvātantryam” iti | [350] arthādānam – samakṣopabhogaḥ | visargo – vyayaḥ | ākṣepo – dāsādiparijanasyāparādhe śikṣārthaḥ | asvātantryam – pitur anujñām antareṇa svecchayā arthādānādāv apravṛttiḥ | dharmasvātantryam apy evaṃ pṛthagiṣṭāpūrtādāv apravṛttiḥ | evaṃ ca pitrānujñātena putreṇa dvakarmāgnihotrādi kāryam, nānanujñāteneti candrikākāraḥ | agnihotrādikriyāsv ananujñātasyāpi putrasyādhikāro ‘stīti aparārkaḥ | ācārād vyavasthā | vyavasthitācāradvayasya pūrvam evoktatvān noktam | yat tu devalo ‘pi,
pitary uparate putrā vibhajeran pitur dhanam |
asvāmye tu bhavet teṣāṃ nirdoṣe pitari sthitam ||
iti, atrāsvāyam asvātantryam ity arthaḥ, putrāṇāṃ janmanā svātantryasya siddhatvāt | tad uttaratra prapañcyate | nirdoṣagrahaṇāt pitari sadoṣe sthite ‘pi sati putrāṇāṃ tatsvātantryam iti darśayati | tena pitari sthite ‘py aśaktatvādidoṣe sati arthādānavisargādau jyeṣṭhasya svātantryam | anujānāṃ jyeṣṭhādhīnatvam avagantavyam | ata eva śaṅkhalikhitau: “pitary aśakte tu kuṭumbasya vyavahārān jyeṣṭhaḥ kuryād anantaro vā kāryajñas tadanumatyā” iti | tacchabdena jyeṣṭha ucyate, tadānīṃ tasyaiva svātantryāt | kāryajñapadenāpi tadanantarapadasyānujopalakṣaṇaparatvaṃ sūcyate | “aśaktagrahaṇaṃ ca dīnāder apy upalakṣaṇam” iti candrikākāraḥ | anenāśaktagrahaṇena vārdhakādinā pitary asvatantratām āpanne tadanicchayā taddhanavibhāgaḥ putrāṇām icchayaiva bhavatīti jñāyate | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
[351]        vyādhitaḥ kupitaś caiva viṣayāsaktamānasaḥ |
anyathā śāstrakārī ca na vibhāge pitā prabhuḥ || iti |
api tu putrā eva prabhava iti śeṣaḥ | ata evāha sa eva |
pitaiva vā svayaṃ putrān vibhajed vayasi sthitaḥ | iti |
vayasi sthitaḥ – apratihatasvātantryayuktaḥ | pitaiva vety evākāravāśabdābhyāṃ vyādhitatvādidoṣarāhitye pitur eva vibhāgakaraṇe ‘dhikāraḥ | anyathā putrāṇām ity arthaḥ |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pituḥ putrā vibhajeran samaṃ dhanam |
mātur nivṛtte rajasi prattāsu bhaganīṣu(?) ca |
nivṛtte cāpi ramaṇe pitary uparataspṛhe ||
ramaṇe – ramaṇaviṣaye rantum ity arthaḥ | uparataspṛha ity anena mātur nivṛtte rajasīty anena ca pituḥ patnyantarasvīkārecchāyāṃ vibhāgo nāstīti gamyate | ataś ca “ūrdhvaṃ pituḥ” ity anena eko vibhāgakālaḥ | sa cājīvadvibhāgaḥ | “mātur nivṛtte rajasi” ity anena jīvadvibhāgakālaḥ | evaṃ vibhāgasya kāladvayam uktam | pitur icchāyās tu jīvadvibhāgānatirekān na pṛthak kālabhedaḥ |
vibhāgaphalam āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau |
bhrātṝṇāṃ jīvatoḥ pitroḥ saha bhāvo vidhīyate |
tadūrdhvam api teṣāṃ ca vṛddhyarthaṃ ca saha tribhiḥ ||
kāmaṃ vaseyur ekatra saṃhatā vṛddhim āpadyetan | iti |
pṛthak pṛthag vyayābhāvād iti bhāvaḥ | vibhāge tu dharmo vṛddhim āpadyata iti | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “vibhāge dharmavṛddhiḥ” [352] iti | sā katham ity apakṣite nāradaḥ |
bhrātṝṇām avibhaktānām eko dharmaḥ pravartate |
vibhāge sati dharmo ‘pi bhavet teṣāṃ pṛthak pṛthak || iti |
dharmaḥ – pitṛdevatārcanādijanyaḥ | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
ekapākena vasatāṃ pitṛdevadvijārcanam |
ekaṃ bhaved vibhaktānāṃ tad eva syād gṛhe gṛhe ||
ataś cāvibhaktānām api svasādhyāgnihotrādijanyo dharmaḥ saṃpadyata eva | kiṃ tu vibhāge sati dharmavṛddhir gautamādimata(dvaya)tvenoktey anusaṃdheyam | atra samavibhāgapakṣasya sārvatrikatvenābhidhānād asmin (yasmin) viṣaye yadi pitā svakīyecchayā samavibhāgapakṣam aṅgīkuryāt tadā patnyaḥ putravatyaḥ samānāṃśabhājaḥ kāryā ity āha yājñavakyaḥ |
yadi kuryāt samānāṃśān patnyaḥ kuryāt samāṃśakāḥ |
yati vādhake ‘py ātmanā saha samabhāgapakṣam icchayā svapitā kuryāt tadā tv ātmabhāgena saha tatsamānabhāgaṃ patnī pratigṛhṇīyād iti | anena “jayāpatyor na vibhāgo vidyate” iti āpastambavacanaṃ yatra sahatvacodanā tatraiveti mantavyam iti bhārucir āha | ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhrātṝṇām atha dampatyoḥ pituḥ putrasya caiva hi |
prātibhāvyam ṛṇaṃ sākṣyam avibhaktena tu smṛtam || iti |
atrāha vijñānayogī:
nanu dampatyor vibhāgāt prāk prātibhāvyādipratiṣedho na vidyate, tayor vibhāgābhāvena viśeṣaṇānarthakyāt | vibhāgābhāvas tv āpastambena darśitaḥ: “jāyāpatyor na [353] vibhāgo vidyate” (ĀpDh 2.14.16) iti |
satyam | śrautasmārtāgnisādhyeṣu karmasu tatphaleṣu vibhāgābhāvo na punaḥ sarvakarmasu dravyeṣu vā | tathā hi “jāyāpatyor na vibhāgo vidyate” ity uktvā kim iti na vidyata ity apekṣāyāṃ, hetum uktavān "pāṇigrahaṇād dhi sahatvaṃ karmasu tathā puṇyaphaleṣu ca" (ĀpDh 2.14.17–18) iti | asyārthaḥ – hi yasmāt pāṇigrahaṇād ārabhya karmasu sahatvaṃ śrūyate “jāyāpatī agnīn ādadhīyātām” iti | tasmād ādhāne sahādhikārād, ādhānasiddhāgnisādhyeṣu karmasu sahādhikāraḥ | tathā “karma smārtaṃ vivāhāgnau” (YDh 1.97) ityādismaraṇād vivāhasiddhāgnisādheṣu karmasv api sahādhikāra eva | ataś cobhayavidhāgninirapekṣeṣu karmasu pūrteṣu jāyāpatyoḥ pṛthag evādhikāraḥ saṃpadyate | tathā puṇyādīnāṃ phaleṣu svargādiṣu jāyāpatyoḥ sahatvaṃ śrūyate: “divi jyotir ajaram ārabhetām” ityādi | yeṣu puṇyāpuṇyakarmasu sahādhikāras teṣāṃ phaleṣu sahatvam iti bodhyaṃ, na punaḥ pūrtānāṃ bhartranujñayānuṣṭhitānāṃ phaleṣv api |
nanu dravyasvāmitve ‘pi sahatvam uktam: “dravyaparigraheṣu ca, na hi bhartur vipravāse naimittike dāne steyam upadiśanti” (ĀpDh 2.14.19–20) iti |
satyam | dravyasvāmitvaṃ patnyā darśitam anena na punar vibhāgābhāvaḥ | yasmād “dravyaparigraheṣu ca” ity uktvā tatra kāraṇam uktam: bhartur vipravāse naimittike avaśyakartavye atithibhojanabhikṣāpradānādau hi yasmān na steyam upadiśanti manvādayas tasmād bhāryāyā api dravyasvāmitvam asti | anyathā steyaṃ syād eva (syāt iti) | tasmād bhāryāyā api bhartur icchayā bhāryāyā dravyavibhāgo bhavaty eva na svecchayeti |
aparārkamataṃ tu:
strīṇāṃ dāyavibhāgo nāsty eva “tasmāt striyo nirindriyā adāyādīḥ” iti śruteḥ | ataś ca patyur icchānusāreṇa patnīnām api dhanaṃ dātavyam | samaśabdas tu patyur [354] bhāgān nyūnaṃ na kāryaṃ samam aṃśam adhikāṃśaṃ vā dātavyam | “yadi kuryāt” iti yadiśabdena icchānusārapratipādanād aicchikatvād aṃśadānasyety avagantavyam iti |
atredaṃ tattam – bhārucimate patnīnāṃ bahutvasadbhāve tāsām eva vibhāgaḥ | vijñānayogiprabhṛtīnāṃ mate patnyekaniyato vibhāgo nāsti | kiṃ tu putraiḥ samavibhāgaḥ patnīnām | aparārkādīnāṃ tu mate patnīvibhāgaḥ putrasamavibhāgaś ca nāsti | kiṃ tu patīcchayā deyam iti | atra pakṣatraye varṇato vyavasthām āhur bhāṣyakārāḥ: “brāhmaṇīnām patnīnāṃ svaputraiḥ samavibhāgaḥ | kṣatriyāṇāṃ tu patnīvibhāgo nāsti | na putrasamavibhāgaḥ, kiṃ tu patīcchayā yat kiṃcid deyam iti | vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ patnīvibhāgaḥ | etadvyavasthāyām ācāra eva mūlam” ity āhuḥ |
atra āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau: “jīvati pitari rikthavibhāgo ‘numataḥ prakāśaṃ vā mitho vā dharmataḥ” iti | yo jīvadvibhāgapakṣo ‘numataḥ sa tāvat prakāśaṃ bandhvādijanasamakṣaṃ yathā bhavati tathāt mitho vā rahasi dharmataḥ – dharmaprakāreṇa kārya ity arthaḥ | tam eva prakāram āha kātyāyanaḥ |
sakalaṃ dravyajātaṃ yad bhāgair gṛhṇanti tatsamaiḥ |
pitarau bhrātaraś caiva vibhāgo dharmya ucyate ||
dharmyo dharmād anapetaḥ, “manuḥ putrebhyo dāyaṃ vyabhajat” ity aviśeṣeṇa samavibhāgaśruteḥ | viṣamavibhāgaś ca śāstradṛṣṭo ‘pi lokavirodhāc chrutyantaravirodhāc ca nānuṣṭheya iti samair bhāgair madhyakadravyaṃ gṛhṇantīti niyamyate | ataś cāsmin kaliyuge ananuṣṭheyatvāt jyeṣṭādyuddhārapakṣā na pratipāditāḥ | tathā hi,
asvargyaṃ lokavidviṣṭaṃ dharmam apy ācren na tu | [355]
iti niṣedhāt, yathā,
mahokṣaṃ vā mahājaṃ vā śrotriyāyopakalpayet |
iti vidhāne ‘pi lokavidviṣṭatvān nānuṣṭheyam, yathā “maitrāvaruṇīṃ gāṃ vaś**nūbandhyām ālabheta” iti gavāmbhavidhāne ‘pi lokavidviṣṭatvād ananuṣṭhānam | uktaṃ ca:
yathā niyogadharmo ‘pi nānūbandhyāvadho ‘pi vā |
tathoddhāravibhāgo ‘pi naiva saṃprati vartate || iti |
saṃprati – kaliguye |
yathā āpastambo ‘pi “jīvan putrebhyo dāyaṃ vibhajet samam” samaṃ iti vayam uktvā “jyeṣṭho dādāda ity eke” iti kṛtsnadhanagrahaṇaṃ jyeṣṭhasyety ekīyam amatvenopanyasya, “deśaviśeṣe suvarṇaṃ kṛṣṇā gāvaḥ kṛṣṇaṃ bhaumaṃ jyeṣṭhasya rathaḥ pituḥ paribhāṇḍaṃ ca gṛhe ‘laṅkāro bhāryāyā jātidhanaṃ ca” iti ekīyamatenaivoddhāravibhāgaṃ darśayitvā “tac chāstrair vipratiṣiddham” iti nirākṛtavān |
ataś ca “jyeṣṭhaṃ vā jyeṣṭhabhāgena” ity atra aparārkādivyākhyātṛvyākhyānaskhālityaṃ nodghāṭitam | atra jīvadvibhāge aicchiko vibhāgaḥ | atrāha nāradaḥ |
dvāv aṃśau pratipadyeta vibhajann ātmanaḥ pitā | iti |
etac caikaputraviṣayam | tathāhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau: “sa yady ekaputraḥ syād dvau bhāgāv ātmano gṛhṇīyāt” iti | sa iti prakṛtaḥ pitā parāmṛśyate | “ekaputraḥ syāt” iti gatavayaskaḥ atkrāntaputrāntaralābhakālaḥ | etac ca dhanavibhāga eva na dharmavibhāge, dharmavibhāge aṃśadvayasya prayojanābhāvāt | yatra putrasya pitṛdhane dhanārjanasamarthatayā svāṃśaṃ grahītuṃ necchā tatra yāvat svīkaroti tāvat tasmai datvā pitrā pṛthak kriyā kāryety āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
śaktasyānīhamānasya kiṃcid datvā pṛthak kriyā | [356] iti |
yadā punar jīvati pitari putrakartṛko vibhāgaḥ kriyate tadāpi “sakalaṃ dravyajātam” ityādikātyāyanavacanoktasamavibhāgaprakāreṇaiva kāryaḥ | putrakartṛke jīvadvibhāge prakārāntarapratipādakaśāstrāntarābhāvāt, “tathaivājīvadvibhāge paitṛke vibhajyamāne dāyādye bhrātṝṇāṃ samo vibhāgaḥ” iti paiṭhīnasismaraṇāt, “samāno mṛte pitari vibhāgaḥ” iti hārītavacanāc ca, mṛte pitari bhrātṛbhiḥ kriyamāṇo rikthavibhāgaḥ samavibhāgenaiva kārya ity āthaḥ | dāyādye dāyadhana ity arthaḥ | bhrātṝṇām iti samasvāmyānāṃ samavarṇānām eva | klībādīnāṃ samavarṇānāṃ bhāganirāsasyāsavarṇānāṃ ca tāratamyena bhāgaprāpteś ca vakṣyamāṇatvāt | yathā putrā rikthe samāṃśinaḥ tathā ṛṇe ‘pi samāṃśina ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
vibhajeran sutāḥ pitror ūrdhvaṃ riktham ṛṇaṃ samam | iti |
atra ṛṇaṃ paitṛkaṃ vivakṣitam, apaitṛkasya sahaivāpākaraṇīyatvaniyamāt | ata evāha kātyāyanaḥ |
bhrātrā pitṛvyamātṛbhyāṃ kuṭumbārtham ṛṇaṃ kṛtam |
vibhāgakāle deyaṃ tad ṛkthibhiḥ sarvam eva (tu) tat || iti |
atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
dharmārthaṃ prītidattaṃ ca yad ṛṇaṃ svaniyojitam |
tad dṛśyamānaṃ vibhajen na dānaṃ paitṛkād dhanāt || iti |
yad dharmārthaṃ saṃkalpitaṃ yac ca pitrā prītena dattaṃ yac ca svenaiva pitrā putrair apākaraṇīyam iti niyojitam – tad eva trividham ṛṇaṃ dṛśyamānaṃ jñāyamānaṃ vibhajed evety arthaḥ | [357]
nanu katham,
yadi kuryāt samān aṃśān patnyaḥ kāryāḥ samāṃśakāḥ |
ity atra strīṇāṃ dānānarhatvād aṃśaśabdo ‘nyathā vyākṛtaḥ | kathaṃ tarhi yājñavalkyenoktam,
pitur ūrdhvaṃ vibhajatāṃ mātāpy aṃśaṃ samaṃ haret |
iti, kathaṃ ca vyāsena,
asutās tu pituḥ patnyaḥ samānāṃśāḥ prakīrtitāḥ |
pitāmahyaś ca sarvās tāḥ mātṛtulyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
iti, kathaṃ ca viṣṇunā “mātaraḥ putrabhānusāreṇa (-riṇa) bhāgahāriṇyaḥ anūḍhāś ca duhitaraḥ” iti | tarhi strīṇāṃ dāyānarhatve mātrādīnāṃ duhitrantānām aṃśahāritvoktir na yujyate |
maivam | atra aṃśaśabdo na dāyabhāgavacanaḥ | api tu dravyasamudāyapratīka-mātravacanaḥ | ataś ca noktadoṣa iti kecit | atra mātrādiśabdānāṃ gururūpastrīviśeṣaparatvād ajīvadvibhāge mātāpy aṃśaṃ dāyabhāgaṃ hared ity anye | medhātithimataṃ tu varṇavyavasthayā pūrvam evoktam | “atha bhrātṝṇāṃ dāyavibhāgo yāś cānapatyāḥ striyaḥ tāsām ā putralābhāt” iti vasiṣṭhaḥ (VaDh 17.40–41) | asyārthaḥ – yāḥ pituḥ striyo anapatyāḥ garbhasthāpatyāḥ tāsām ā putralābhāt ā prasavāt sahavāsena sthitānāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ prasūtāpatyaliṅgajānānantaraṃ dāyavibhāga iti |
nanv atra bhrātṝṇām anapatyastrīṇāṃ ca dāyavibhāgo bhavatīti ṛjvarthaḥ kim iti parityajyate |
ucyate – anapatyastrīṇām ā putralābhād iti viruddhārthapratīteḥ, strīṇāṃ dāyānarhāṇāṃ dāyavibhāgāsaṃbhavāc ca parityajyate | ata eva smṛtyantaram:
[358]        janany asvadhanā putraiḥ vibhāge ‘ṃśaṃ samaṃ haret | iti |
asvadhanā – prātisvikastrīdhanaśūnyā jananī putrair eva jīvadvibhāge kriyamāṇe putrāṃśasamam aṃśaṃ hared ity arthaḥ | atra jananīgrahaṇaṃ sapatnyāder upalakṣaṇārtham | mātara putrabhāgānusāribhāgahāriṇya iti | asvadhaneti viśeṣeṇopādānāt svadhane vidyamāne tenaiva jīvanasya svānuṣṭheyasya ca dhanasādhyasya karmaṇaḥ siddhisaṃbhave nāṃśagrahaṇam iti pratīyate | svadhanamātrāj jīvanadhanasādhyakarmaṇoḥ siddhyasaṃbhave svadhanānām api na samabhāgaharaṇam | kiṃ tu yathopayogaṃ nyūnabhāgasyaiva haraṇam iti gamyate | tathā vibhājarāśer atibahutve nirdhanānām api jananyādīnāṃ ca na samāṃśaharaṇam, kiṃ tu yathāsvopayogaṃ samāṃśanyūnasyaivāṃśasya haraṇam ity avagamyate | asvadhaneti viśeṣaṇasyopayogavaśād aṃśagrahaṇaṃ patnyāḥ na punar bhrātṛvad dāyabhāgitvavaśād iti jñāpanārthatvāt | na tu samam iti viśeṣaṇasyopayogāt, asamāṃśasya haraṇe ‘pi avaiyarthyāt | ajīvadvibhāgasthale patīcchayā patnīnām adhikāṃśasyāpi dātum arhatvād ity uktaṃ prāk | asminn ajīvadvibhāgasthale bhrātṝṇāṃ icchānusāreṇa mātur aṃśo dātavyaḥ samo vā adhiko vā | yadīcchā nāsti alpavibhājyarāśer adhikasya prāptasya nivrttyarthatvāt samam iti padam ity anusaṃdheyam | ataḥ etat sarvam anusaṃdhāyaiva yājñavalkyena,
yadi kuryāt samān aṃśān patnyaḥ kāryāḥ samāṃśikāḥ |
ity abhidhāya,
na dattaṃ strīdhanaṃ yāsāṃ bhartrā vā śvaśureṇa vā |
ity abhihitam | strīdhanaṃ dattaṃ cet tāsāṃ patnīnāṃ nāṃśaharatvam iti vacanārthaḥ | ata evoktaṃ candrikākāreṇa: tenātra na mātuḥ svatvavyavarasthāpako(?) dāyavibhāgaḥ, kiṃ tu yāvad artham evārthaharaṇam iti [359] mantavyam iti | vijñānayoginā “bhrātṝṇam atha dampatyoḥ” iti vacanavyākhyāne tasmād bhāryāyā api dravyasvāmitvam asti anyathā steyam syād iti, tat tu dāyaharatvapratipādakaṃ na bhavati kiṃ tv atithibhojanabhikṣāpradānādisvāmyamātram ity anusaṃdheyam | aparārkeṇa tu “yadi kuryāt samān aṃśān” ity atra aṃśaśabdo vibhājyadravyaikadeśaparaḥ | ataś ca patnīnām aṃśaharatvaṃ nāstīti patyur icchayā yat kiṃcid deyam ity evaṃparam iti | ato matatraye ‘pi na dāyabhāktvaṃ strīṇām api tv aṃśaharatvam | tac ca strīdhanasadbhāvatāratamyanibandhanaṃ prāg uktam anusaṃdheyam | bhāṣyakāramate tu śūdrapatnīnāṃ vibhāgo lokācāra siddha iti mantavyam |
yat tu viṣṇunoktam “anūḍhāś ca duhitaraḥ putrabhāgānusāribhāgahāriṇyāḥ” iti, tat tv anūḍheti viśeṣaṇopādānāt svavivāhārthaṃ putrabhāgānusāribhāgagrahaṇaṃ yathāśakti, na punaḥ mātṝṇām iva jīvanārtham aṃśaharatvam iti gamyate | ata eva devalenoktam:
kanyābhyaś ca pitur dravyaṃ deyaṃ vaivāhikaṃ (dhanam) vasu |
vaivāhikaṃ vasu – vivāhaprayojanakaṃ dhanam ity arthaḥ | ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhaginyaś ca nijād aṃśād datvāṃśaṃ tu turīyakam | iti |
asyārthaḥ – bhaginyaś cāsaṃskṛtāḥ saṃskartavyā bhrātṛbhiḥ | kiṃ kṛtvā | nijād aṃśāc caturtham aṃśaṃ datvā | anena duhitaraḥ pitur ūrdhvam aṃśabhāginya iti gamyate | atra nijād aṃśād iti pratyekaṃ parikalpitād aṃśād uddhṛtya caturāṃśo dātavya ity ayam artho na bhavati | kiṃ tu yajjātīyā kanyā tajjātīyaputrabhāgāt [360] caturthāṃśabhāginī sā kartavyā | etad uktaṃ bhavati – yadi brāhmaṇajātīyā sā kanyā tadā brāhmaṇī putrasya yāvān aṃśo bhavati tasya caturthāṃśaḥ tasyā bhavatīti | tad yathā – yadi kasyacid brāhmaṇī patnī tatra caikaḥ putraḥ kanyā caikā tatra tatpitryaṃ sarvam eva dravyaṃ dvidhā vibhajya tatraikaṃ bhāgaṃ caturdhā vibhajya turīyāṃśaṃ kanyāyai datvā śeṣaṃ putro gṛhṇīyāt atha tu dvau putrāv ekā kanyā; tathāpi pitṛdhanaṃ tredhā vibhajya tatraikaṃ bhāgaṃ caturdhā vibhajya turīyāṃśaṃ kanyāyai datvā śeṣaṃ putrau vibhajya gṛhṇītaḥ | atha tv ekaḥ putraḥ dve kanye tadā pitṛdhanaṃ tredhā vibhajya ekaṃ bhāgaṃ caturdhā vibhajya dvau bhāgau dvābhyāṃ kanyābhyāṃ datvā śeṣam aṃśaṃ sarvaṃ putro gṛhṇātīti | evaṃ samānajātīyeṣu samaviṣameṣu bhrātṛbhaginīṣu yojanīyam | yadā tu brāhmaṇīputraḥ ekaḥ kṣatriyā kanyaikā tatrāpi pitryaṃ dravyaṃ saptadhā vibhajya kṣatriyāputrabhāgān trīn caturdhā vibhajya turīyāṃśaṃ kṣatriyākanyāyai datvā śeṣaṃ sarvaṃ brāhmaṇīputro gṛhṇāti | yadā tu dvau brāhmaṇīputrau kṣatriyā kanyaikā tatra pitryaṃ dhanam ekādaśadhā vibhajya teṣu trīn aṃśān kṣatriyāputrabhāgān caturdhā vibhajya caturthāṃśaṃ kṣatriyākanyāyai datvā śeṣaṃ sarvaṃ brāhmaṇīputrau gṛhṇīta | evaṃ jātivaiṣamye bhrātṝṇāṃ bhaginīnāṃ ca saṃkhyāyāḥ sāmye vaiṣamye ca sarvatrohyam |
nanu “datvāṃśaṃ tu turīyakam” iti turīyāṃśavivakṣayā saṃskāramātropayogi dravyaṃ datveti vyākhyānaṃ yuktam, vacanavirodhāt:
svebhyo ‘ṃśebhyas tu kanyābhyaḥ svaṃ dadyur bhrātaraḥ pṛthak |
svāt svād aṃśāc caturbhāgaṃ patitāḥ syur aditsavaḥ || iti | [361]
asyārthaḥ – brāhmaṇādayo bhrātaraḥ brāhmaṇīprabhṛtibhyo bhaginībhyaḥ svajātivihitebhyo ‘ṃśebhyaḥ “caturaṃśān hared vipraḥ” ityādivakṣyamāṇebhyaḥ svāt svād aṃśād ātmīyād ātmīyāt bhāgāc caturtham – turīyaṃ bhāgaṃ dadyuḥ | na ca svātmīyabhāgād uddhṛtya caturthāṃśo deya ity ucyate, kiṃ tu svajātivihitād ekasmād aṃśāt pṛthak pṛthag ekaikasyai kanyāyai caturthāṃśo deya iti | jātivaiṣamye saṃkhyāvaiṣamye ca vibhāgakḷptir uktaiva | “patitāḥ syur aditsavaḥ” iti adāne pratyavāyaśravaṇāt, avaśyadātavyatā pratīyate | atrāpi caturbhāgavacanam avivakṣitam, saṃskāropayogidravyadānam eva vivakṣitam iti cet,
na, smṛtidvaye ‘pi caturthāṃśadānāvivakṣāyāṃ pramāṇābhāvāt, adāte pratyavāyaśravaṇāc ceti |
yad api kaiścid uktam – aṃśadānavivakṣāyāṃ bahubhrātṛkāyā bahadhanatvaṃ bahubhaginīkasya nirdhanatā prāpnotīti,
tad uktarītyā parihṛtam eva | na hy atrātmīyād bhāgād uddhṛtya caturthāṃśasya dānam ucyate, yena tathā syāt | tasmāt pitur ūrdhvaṃ kanyāpy aṃśabhāginī | pūrvaṃ tu yat kiṃcit pitā dadāti tad eva, viśeṣavacanābhāvād iti |
etac ca sarvam asahāya-medhātithi-vijñānayogi-pradīpakārādīnāṃ matam |
etan mataṃ bhāruci-aparārkaprabhṛtayo na manyante, pitur ūrdhvaṃ jīvati vā pitari kanyā nāṃśabhāginī | jīvati pitari pitrā svecchayā putrikāṇāṃ yat kiṃcid dātavyam | pitary uparate bhrātṛbhir apy anūḍhānāṃ saṃskāropayogi apratiṣṭhitānāṃ pratiṣṭhopayogi dravyaṃ dātavyam | na tu tāś caturthāṃśaharā iti | caturthāṃśapratipādakavacanāni tu saṃskāropayogidravyapratipādanaparāṇi pratiṣṭhopayogidravyapratipādanaparāṇi | “anūḍhānām apratiṣṭhitānām evāṃśo dātavyaḥ” iti viṣṇuvacane [362] anūḍhātvāpratiṣṭhitatvaviśeṣaṇa-viśeṣitānām eva bhaginīnām aṃśadānaṃ pratīyate | tac ca pratiṣṭhopayogi vivāhopayogi vā pratīyate | “patitāḥ syur aditsavaḥ” ity adāne pratyavāyaḥ smaraṇaṃ tu pratiṣṭhopayogidravyadānena pratiṣṭhāyā akaraṇe saṃskāropayogidravyadānena saṃskārākaraṇe pratyavāya ity arthakayavagantavyam (?) | jīvati pitari duhitṝṇāṃ yat kiṃcid dānam evam ajīvati pitari | dṛṣṭāthatve siddhe adṛṣṭakalpanā anyāyyā etāsāṃ smṛtīnāṃ nyāyamūlatvād iti bhāruci-aparārka-yajñapatyādīnāṃ matam | ata eva bṛhadviṣṇunoktam: “anūḍhānāṃ ca kanyānāṃ svavittānusāreṇa saṃskāraṃ kuryāt” iti | atra viśeṣam āha śaṅkhaḥ: “vibhajyamāne dāyādye kanyālaṃkāraṃ vaivāhikaṃ strīdhanaṃ ca kanyā labheta” iti | kanyālaṃkāram – kanyayā svadhṛtam alaṃkāraṃ strīdhanam – mātṛdhanam | atra bodhāyanaḥ: “mātur alaṃkāraṃ duhitaraḥ sāṃpradāyikaṃ labherann anyed vā” iti | sāṃpradāyikaṃ mātṛparamparāyātam | anyat taditarat bhrātṛbhiḥ svecchayā dattaṃ kumāryo labheran | duhitaraḥ sāṃpradāyikaṃ labherann ity asyāpavādam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
mātur duhitaraḥ śeṣam ṛṇāt tābhya ṛte ‘nvayaḥ | iti |
mātur dhanaṃ duhitaro vibhajeran yathākramam ṛṇāc cheṣam – tatkṛtarṇāpākaraṇāvaśiṣṭam | etad uktaṃ bhavati – mātṛkṛtam ṛṇaṃ putrair evāpākaraṇīyaṃ na duhitṛbhiḥ | ṛṇāvaśiṣṭaṃ mātṛdhanaṃ duhitaro gṛhṇīyur iti | yuktaṃ caitat |
pumān puṃso ‘dhike śukle strī bhavaty adhike striyāḥ | iti |
stryavayavānāṃ duhitṛṣu bāhulyāt strīdhanaṃ duhitṛgāmi | [363] pitṛdhanaṃ putragāmi, pitravayavānāṃ putreṣu bāhulyād iti | atra gautamena viśeṣo darśitaḥ: “strīdhanaṃ dhuhitṝṇām aprattānām apratiṣṭhitānāṃ ca” iti | asyārthaḥ – prattāprattāsamavāye aprattānām eva strīdhanam, prattāsu ca pratiṣṭhitāpratiṣṭhitāsamavāye apratiṣṭhitānām eveti | aprattā – anūḍhāḥ, apratiṣṭhitā – nirdhanāḥ | duhitrabhāve mātṛdhane ṛṇāvaśiṣṭaṃ ko gṛhṇīyād ity ata āha “tābhya ṛte ‘nvayaḥ” iti | tābhyaḥ – duhitṛbhyo vinā duhitṝṇām abhāve anvayaḥ – putrādiḥ gṛhṇīyāt | etac ca pitror ūrdhvaṃ vibhajerann ity anenaiva siddhaṃ spaṣṭārtham uktam iti vijñānayogī | etac ca “mātur duhitaraḥ śeṣam” iti vacanam anyathā vyākurvanti bhāruciprabhṛtayaḥ: putrābhāve mātṛdhanaṃ duhitaro vibhajetan | tadabhāve svānvayaḥ pitṛvyādiḥ gṛhṇīyāt, “dāyādā ūrdhvam āpnuyuḥ” iti smṛteḥ | ūrdhvam – dhanasvāminaḥ putrikāder abhāva ity arthaḥ | dāyādāḥ – dhanasvāmiputrikāpitṛvyādayaḥ | ata evoktaṃ saṃgrahakāreṇa |
pitṛdvārāgataṃ dravyaṃ mātṛdvārāgataṃ ca yat |
kathitaṃ dāyaśabdena tadvibhāgo ‘dhunocyate || iti |
mātṛdvārāgatadravyasya dāyaśabdavācyatvāt dāyārhatvaṃ putrāṇām eva | na tu strīṇām, “tasmāt striyo nirindriyā adāyādāḥ (-dāyādīḥ)” iti śruteḥ, “strīṇāṃ dāyavibhāgo nāsti nirindriyatvāt” iti gautamasmṛteś ca | bhrātṛdadbhāve duhitṝṇāṃ mātur alaṃkārādikaṃ bhrātṝṇām icchayā yat kiṃcid deyam, tad eva grahītavyaṃ nānyad iti pratipādayantaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha hārītaḥ |
aneḍamūkā jātyandhā vikalāṅgāś ca kanyakāḥ |
[364]        saṃskāryāḥ paitṛkād rikthād bhrātṛbhiḥ manur abravīt ||
aneḍamūkāḥ – vaktuṃ śrotum asamarthāḥ | vikalāṅgāḥ – nyūnāṅgā adhikāṅgāś ca | paitṛkād rikthād iti sāmānyanirdeśāt sarvaṃ vā rikthaṃ bhrātṛbhiḥ varāya datvā saṃskartavyā iti vacanārtharḥ | kecid aneḍamūkatvādidoṣaduṣṭānāṃ vivāhasaṃkāro neti vadanti | tad apāstam iti veditavyam | manur api dāyānarhān āha |
anaṃśau klībapatitau jātyandhabadhirau tathā |
unmattajaḍamūkāś ca ye ca kecin nirindriyāḥ ||
asyārthaḥ – anaṃśau klībapatitāv iti dvitvoktyā dāyārhabhrātṛbhiḥ rikthagrāhair vā yoṣidgrāhair vā poṣyau, jātyandhabadhirāv iti dvitvoktyā tayor aṃśo ‘sty eva, kiṃ tv aṃśayuktāv api poṣyau vivāhasaṃskārasadbhāvāt | tathāśabdaprayogeṇa paṅgvādayaḥ vivāhasaṃskārārhāś ced aṃśaharāḥ poṣyāś ceti rahasyam | unmattajaḍamūkāś ceti samuccayoktyā te ‘pi bhartavyā eva nāṃśaharāḥ | vivāhārhā na ced iti śeṣaḥ | “ye ca kecin nirindriyāḥ” iti strīṇām apy upalakṣaṇaṃ nirindriyāṇāṃ strīṇāṃ sapatnīduhitṛbhaginīprabhṛtīnāṃ puṃsāṃ ca bhrātṛtatsutabhrātṛvyādīnāṃ mātulādīnāṃ ca saṃrakṣaṇaṃ kāryam iti | kecit tu nirindriyāḥ vyādhinā vinaṣṭaghrāṇādīndriyā ity āhuḥ | yat tu nāradenoktam,
pitṛdviṭ patitaḥ ṣaṇḍo yaś ca syād avapātitaḥ |
aurasā api naivāṃśaṃ labheran kṣetrajāḥ kutaḥ ||
avapātitaḥ – mahāparādho bāndhubhiḥ bahiṣkṛtaḥ | patitaṣaṇḍau spaṣṭau | pitṛdveṣo nāmāsau mama pitā nety evaṃrūpaḥ, anyathā pituḥ pakṣapāte putrāṇāṃ dveṣasaṃbhave tatra bhāgasya vihitatvāt | vasiṣṭho ‘pi [365] “anaṃśās tv āśramāntaragāḥ” iti | gṛhāśramam upekṣyeti śeṣaḥ | ata evāha devalaḥ |
mṛte na pitari klībakuṣṭhonmattajaḍādayaḥ (-jaḍāndhakāḥ) |
patitaḥ patitāpatyaṃ liṅgī dāyāṃśabhāginaḥ ||
mṛte pitari klībādayo dāyāṃśino na bhavantīty arthaḥ | liṅgī naiṣthikavanasthādiḥ kṣapaṇakapāśupatādiś ca | patitāpatyam iti pātityadaśāyām utpannaḥ putraḥ | tatpūrvotpannasya putrasya pitṛgatapātityadoṣānuṣaṅgābhāvāt | pitāputrasaṃbandho laukikaḥ pātityādau nivartata iti purastān nivedayiṣyate | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “teṣām evaurasāḥ putrāḥ bhāgahāriṇo na tu patitasya patanīye kṛte karmaṇy anantarotpannāḥ | pratilomāsu strīṣūtpannāś cābhāginaḥ tatpurāḥ paitāmahe 'py arthe" iti | mṛte pitarīty atrāpiśabdo ‘dhyāhārārthaḥ, pitari mṛte ‘py amṛte ‘pīti klībādayo nāṃśaharā iti vyākhyeyam | tathā ca āpastambaḥ: “jīvan putrebhyo dāyaṃ vibhajet samaṃ klībam unmattaṃ patitaṃ ca parihāpya” iti | parihāpya – varjayitvā | caśabdo vivāhānarhāṇām upalakṣakaḥ | candrikākāras tu “mṛte pitarīti vibhāgakālapradarśanārtham uktam” ity āha | tena vibhāgakāle sthite klaibyādiśālinām apy abhāgaharatvam | na punaḥ klaibyabādhiryādiśālinām eveti mantavyam iti | yat tu yājñavalkyenoktam,
aurasāḥ kṣetrajāś caiṣāṃ nirdoṣā bhāgahāriṇaḥ |
iti, tad dvāparādiyugaviṣayam iti mantavyam, kalau kṣetrajaputraniṣedhāt | tathā ca niraṃśakaputrāṇām aṃśagrahaṇavirodhivyādhyādyabhāve paitāmahadhanaprāptiḥ,
[366]        tatputrāḥ pitṛdāyāṃśaṃ labheran doṣavarjitāḥ |
iti devalavacanāt | doṣāḥ klaibyādayaḥ | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
klībo ‘tha patitas tajjaḥ paṅgur unmattako jaḍaḥ |
andhācikitsyarogādyā bhartavyās te (syur) niraṃśakāḥ ||
tajjaḥ – patitotpannaḥ ādyaśabdo nirindriyādisaṃgrahārthaḥ | bharaṇaṃ yāvajjīvam, “yāvajjīvaṃ bhartavyāḥ” iti manusmṛteḥ | bhinnajātīyānāṃ bhāge viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
catustridvyekabhāgāḥ syuḥ varṇaśo brāhmaṇātmajāḥ |
kṣatrajās tridvyekabhāgā viḍjās tu dvyekabhāginaḥ ||
brāhmaṇasya catasro bhāryāḥ | kṣatriyasya tisraḥ | vaiśyasya dve | śūdrasyaikaiva bhāryeti trisro varṇānupūrvyeneti darśitāḥ | tatra brāhmaṇotpannāḥ varṇaśabdena brāhmaṇādivarṇā ucyante | vīpsāyāṃśas pratyayaḥ | ataś ca varṇe varṇe brāhmaṇotpannā yathākramaṃ catustridvyekabhāgāḥ syuḥ | etad uktaṃ bhavati – brāhmaṇena brāhmaṇyam utpannā ekaikaśaś caturaś caturo bhāgān labhante | tenaiva kṣatriyāyām utpannās trīṃs trīn | vaiśyāyām utpannā dvau dvau | śūdrāyām utapannāḥ ekaikam iti | kṣatrajāḥ – kṣatriyotpannāḥ | varṇaśa ity anuvartate | yathākramaṃ tridvyekabhāgāḥ kṣatriyeṇa kṣatriyāyām utpannāḥ pratyekaṃ trīṃs trīn bhāgān labhante | vaiśyāyām utpannā dvau dvau | śūdrāyām utpannā ekam ekam iti | viḍjāḥ – vaiśyajāḥ varṇaśo dvyekabhāginaḥ | vaiśyena vaiśyāyām utpannāḥ dvau dvau labhante | śūdreṇa śūdrāyām ekam ekaṃ labhante, śūdrasyaikaiva bhāryeti bhinnajātīyapurābhāvāt | tatputrāṇāṃ pūrvokta eka eva samavibhāgaḥ | etac ca smṛtyantarānusāreṇoktaṃ yājñavalkyena, tanmate brāhmaṇasya śūdrāvivāhasya niṣiddhatvāt | [367] yata uktaṃ tenaiva |
na tan mama mataṃ yasmāt tatrāyaṃ jāyate svayam | iti |
tad iti śūdrāvivāho brāhmaṇakartṛkaḥ parāmṛśyate | vibhājyadravyam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
paitāmahaṃ ca pitryaṃ ca yac cānyat svayam ārjitam |
dāyādānāṃ vibhāge ‘pi sarvam etad vibhajyate ||
svayam ārjitam – pitrādyavibhaktadravyopayogena svayam ārjitam | tadanyathā svayam ārjitasyāvibhājyadravyatvāt | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitṛdravyāvirodhena yad anyat svayam ārjitam |
maitram audvāhikaṃ caiva dāyādānāṃ na tad bhavet ||
kramād abhyāgataṃ dravyaṃ hṛtam abhyuddharet tu yaḥ |
dāyādebhyo na tad dadyād vidyayā labdham eva ca ||
mātāpitror dravyāvināśena yat svayam ārjitam | maitram – mitrasakāśāl labdham | audvāhikam – vivāhāl labdham, dāyādānām – bhrātrādīnāṃ na tad bhavet | kiṃ ca kramād abhyāgatam – pitṛkramāt kramāyātaṃ yat kiṃcid dravyam anyair hṛtam asāmarthyādinā pitrādibhir anuddhṛtaṃ yaḥ putrāṇāṃ madhye uddharet | tad dāyādebhyo na dadyāt – uddhartaiva gṛhṇīyāt | tatra vijñānayoginā putrāṇāṃ madhye itarābhyanujñayā ya uddharati tad dāyādebhyo na dadyād ity uktam | tan na sahate aparārkaḥ – itarābhyanujñayaiva teṣāṃ tadaṃśe anadhikārād etad vacanavaiyarthyāt | kṣetre turīyāṃśaṃ labhata ity āha śaṅkhaḥ |
pūrvanaṣṭāṃ tu yo bhūmim ekaś ced uddharet kramāt |
yathābhāgaṃ labhante ‘nye datvāśaṃ tu turīyakam || iti |
kramād ity atra abhyāgatam iti śeṣaḥ | yad anyat svayam ārjitam [368] ity asyārtho manunā spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ | tathā hi –
anupaghnan pitṛdhanaṃ śrameṇa yad upārjayet |
śrameṇa – śramajanitakṛṣyadineti yāvat | pitṛdravyam ity atra pitṛgrahaṇam avibhakopalakṣaṇārtham | anupaghnan – apīḍayan | vyāso ‘pi,
anāśritya pitṛdravyaṃ svaśaktyāpnoti yad dhanam |
dāyādebhyo na tad dadyāt ||
ity prāha | prajāpatir api |
vidyāśauryaśramair labdhaṃ strīdhanaṃ mādhuparkikam |
maitram audvāhikaṃ caiva bhrātṛbhir na vibhajyate || iti |
vidyayā – vedādhyayanena adhyāpanena vā vedārthavyākhyānena vā yal labdhaṃ tad api dāyādebhyo na dadyāt | ārjaka eva gṛhṇīyāt | evaṃvidheṣu sthaleṣu dravyasyaikaniṣṭhatve ‘pi vibhaktatvam astīti bhārucinā prāg eva prapañcitam | vidyādhanasvarūpam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
upanyasya tu yal labdhaṃ vidyayā paṇapūrvakam |
vidyādhanaṃ tu tad vidyād vibhāge na niyujyate ||
śiṣyād ārtvijyataḥ praśnāt saṃdigdhapraśnanirṇayāt |
sujñānaśaṃsanād vādāl labdhaṃ prādhyayanāc ca yat ||
vidyādhanaṃ tu tat prāhuḥ vibhāge na niyujyate |
paraṃ nirasya yal labdhaṃ vidyāto dyūtapūrvakam ||
vidyādhanaṃ tu tad vidyān na vibhājyaṃ bṛhaspatiḥ |
vidyāpratijñayā labdhaṃ śiṣyād āptaṃ ca yad bhavet |
ṛtvijā yena yal labdham etad vidyādhanaṃ bhṛguḥ ||
prādhyayanam – ghaṭikāśatakādi nirmāṇam | dhanaṃ vinādhyayanam [369] (annadānādhyayanam) vā | atra ca pitṛdravyāvirodhena yat kiṃcit svayam ārjitam iti sarvaśeṣaḥ | ataś ca pitṛdravyāvirodhena yan maitram ārjitaṃ pitṛdravyāvirodhena yad audvāhikaṃ pitṛdravyāvirodhena yat kramāyātam uddhṛtaṃ pitṛdravyāvirodhena vidyayā yal labdham iti pratyekam abhisaṃbadhyate | tathā ca – pitṛdravyāvirodhena pratyupakāreṇa yan maitraṃ pitṛdravyāvirodhena āsurādivivāhena ca yal labdhaṃ tathā pitṛdravyavirodhena (?) yat kramāyātam uddhṛtaṃ tathā pitṛdravyavyayalabdhayā vidyayā yal labdhaṃ tat sarvaṃ sarvaiḥ bhrātṛbhir vibhajanīyam | tathā pitṛdravyāvirodhenety asya sarvaśeṣatvād eva pitṛdravyavirodhena pratigrahalabdham api vibhajanīyam | asya sarvaśeṣatvābhāve maitram audvāhikam ityādi nārabdhavyam | atha pitṛdravyavirodhenāpi yan maitrādilabdhaṃ tasyāvibhajyatvāya maitrādivacanam arthavad ity ucyate | tathā sati samācāravirodho vidyālabdhe nāradavacanavirodhaś ca |
kuṭumbaṃ bibhṛyād bhrātuḥ yo vidyām adhigacchataḥ |
bhāgaṃ vidyādhanāt tasmāt sa labhetāśruto ‘pi san || iti |
pitṛdravyāvirodhenety asya bhinnavākyatve pratigrahalabdhasyāvibhājyatvam ācāraviruddham āpadyate | etad eva spaṣṭīkṛtaṃ manunā – “anupaghnan pitṛdravyam” ityādy uktaṃ prāk | vidyādhanasyāvibhājyatvalakṣaṇam uktaṃ kātyāyanena |
parabhakto ‘payogena vidyā prāptānyas tu yā |
tayā labdhaṃ dhanaṃ yat tu vidyāprāptaṃ tad ucyate ||
paraśabdo ‘tra avibhaktāpekṣayā yad vyaktyantaraṃ tatra prayujyate | bhaktaśabdo dravyamātropalakṣakatayā prayuktaḥ | ataś ca pitṛdravyāvirodhenety asya sarvaśeṣatā yuktety anusaṃdheyam |
nanu pitṛdravyāvirodhena [370] yan maitrādinā labdhaṃ dravyaṃ tad avibhājyam iti na vaktavyam, vibhāgaprāptyabhāvāt | yad yena labdhaṃ tat tasyaiva svaṃ nānyasyeti prasiddham | prāptipūrvakaś ca pratiṣedhaḥ |
ucyate,
samavetais tu yat prāptaṃ sarve tatra samāṃśinaḥ |
iti prāptasyāpavādaḥ | atra hārītaḥ “yogakṣemaṃ pracārān na vibhajetan” iti | aprāptasya prāptir yogaḥ | yogakṣemaśabdārtham āha laugākṣiḥ |
kṣemaṃ pūrtaṃ yogam iṣṭam ity āhuḥ tatvadarśinaḥ |
avibhājye ca te prokte śayanāsanam eva ca || iti |
tad ayam arthaḥ – yogaśabdena alabdhalābhakāraṇaṃ śrautasmārtāgnyādisādhyam iṣṭaṃ karma lakṣyate | kṣemaśabdena labdhaparipālanahetubhūtaṃ taṭākārāmanirmāṇādi pūrtaṃ karma lakṣyate | tad ubhayaṃ paitṛkam api pitṛdravyavirodhārjitam apy avibhājyam iti |
kecit tu – yogakṣemakāriṇo rājamantripurohitādaya ucyante ity āhuḥ | chatracāmaraśastravāhanaprabhṛtaya ity anye | pracāraḥ – gṛhārāmādiṣu praveśanirgamanamārgaḥ so ‘py avibhājyaḥ | nāradas tu viśeṣam āha |
mātrā ca svadhanaṃ dattaṃ yasmai tu prītipūrvakam |
tasyāpy eṣa vidhir dṛṣṭo mātāpīṣṭe pitā yathā ||
svadhana iti śeṣaḥ | eṣa vidhiḥ – avibhājyatvavidhiḥ pitṛdattaviṣayoktaḥ | yat tu uśanasā kṣetrasyāvibhājyatvam uktam |
avibhājyaṃ sagotrāṇām ā sahasrakulād api |
yājyaṃ kṣetraṃ ca patraṃ ca kṛtānnam udakaṃ striyaḥ || [371]
tad brāhmaṇotpannakṣatriyāputraviṣayam,
na pratigrahabhūr deyā kṣatriyādisutāya vai |
yady apy eṣāṃ pitā dadyāt mṛte viprāsuto haret ||
iti smaranād iti vijñāneśvara-asahāya-medhātithīnām iyaṃ vyākhyā | bhāruci-aparārka-candrikākārādīnāṃ tu yājanasakāśād utpanno lābho vibhajanīyaḥ | kṣetraṃ cākhiladāyādānumatyā vibhajanīyam,
dāyādair nābhyanujñātaṃ yat kiṃcit sthāvare kṛtam |
tat sarvam akṛtaṃ jñeyaṃ yady eko ‘pi na manyate ||
iti prajāpatismaraṇāt |
kaule raikthavibhāve ‘pi na kaścit prabhutām iyāt |
bhoga (yogyas tu) eva tu kartavyo na dānaṃ na ca vikrayaḥ || iti |
kaule – kulakramāgate sthāvarādau na kaścit pitrādir api rikthavibhāge, apiśabdād vikrayādau prabhyutām iyāt | tatra dāyādānumatim antareṇa na vibhāgavikrayadānāni kuryād iti tasyārtha iti vyākhyātavantaḥ | yathoktaṃ manunā |
vastraṃ puṣpam alaṃkāraṃ kṛtānnam udakaṃ striyaḥ |
yogakṣemapracāraṃ ca na vibhājyaṃ pracakṣate || iti |
vastrasyāvibhājyatvaṃ dhṛtānām eva nānyeṣām | “ghṛtānāṃ tu vastrāṇāṃ na vibhāgaḥ” iti śaṅkhalikhitau | pitṛdhṛtānāni tu pitur ūrdhvaṃ vibhajatāṃ śrāddhabhoktre dātavyāni | yathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
vastālaṃkāraśayyādi pitur yad vāhanādikam |
gandhamālyaiḥ samabhyarcya śrāddhabhoktre tad arpayet || iti |
aśvādivāhanānāṃ bahutve tadvikrayopajīvināṃ vibhājyatvam eva | alaṃkāro ‘pi yo yena dhṛtaḥ sa tasyaiva | adhṛte [372] sādhāraṇe vibhājya eva,
patyau jīvati yaḥ strībhir alaṃkāro dhṛto bhavet |
na taṃ bhajeran dāyādāḥ bhajamānāḥ patanti te ||
iti smṛteḥ | atra dhṛtapadopādānād adhṛtānāṃ vibhājyatvaṃ gamyate | kṛtānnam – taṇḍulamodakādi | taṇḍulamodakādīty atra taṇḍulakṛtāni ca tāni modakāni ca taṇḍulamodakāni | yac coktaṃ manunā,
taṇḍulāni ca vastrāṇi alaṃkāraś ca vāhanam |
udakaṃ ca striyaś cāpi na vibhājyāḥ samā api ||
udakam udakādhāraḥ kūpādiḥ | tac ca mūlyādidvāreṇa na vibhājyaṃ paryāyeṇopabhoktavyam | striyaś ca dāsyo viṣamā na mūlyadvāreṇa vibhājyāḥ, paryāyeṇa karma kārayitavyāḥ | atha paitāmahadravye pautrāṇāṃ vibhāge viśeṣaḥ pradarśyate | tatra yājñavalkyaḥ |
pramītapitṛkāṇāṃ tu pitṛto bhāgakalpanā |
avibhaktānāṃ diṣṭaṃ gatānāṃ ye putrās teṣāṃ pitṛto bhāgakalpanā | etad uktaṃ bhavati – yathā avibhaktā bhrātaraḥ putrān utpādya diṣṭaṃ gatās tatraikasya dvau putrāv anyasya trayaḥ aparasya catvāra iti putrāṇāṃ vaiṣamye tatra dvau svapitraṃśam ekaṃ labhete, anye trayo ‘py ekam aṃśaṃ pitryam | catvāro ‘py ekam evāṃśaṃ labherann iti | ata eva kātyāyanaḥ |
sa evāṃśas tu sarveṣāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ nyāyato bhavet | iti |
sa evāṃśaḥ – pitryāṃśaḥ | yady api pitṛbhāgaharatve anekaputrāṇāṃ pitṛbhāgakalpanā svānanurūpā (svāmyānanurūpā), tathāpi vācanikatvād anumantavyā | tathaiva sasutayor avibhaktayoḥ bhrātroḥ madhye kasyacit bhrātā mṛtaḥ tatputras tu pitṛvyeṇa sārdhaṃ vibhajanīyaḥ,
[373]         avibhakte ‘nuje prete tatsutaṃ rikthabhāginam |
kurvīta ..................... ||
iti kātyāyanasmṛteḥ | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “yady ekaḥ pramītaḥ dvau vā pramītau eko vā sthito dvau vā sthitau tatputrā viṣamasamāḥ tatrāpi pitṛto bhāgakalpanā” iti | atrāpi naṣṭānām api putrāḥ pitryān evāṃśān labhanta iti vācanikī vyavastheti vijñāneśaḥ |
aparārka-bhārucyādayas tu —pramītapitṛkāṇāṃ pitṛdvārāgatadravyadāyasya yatheṣṭaviniyogārhasvatvasaṃbhavāt pitṛsvatvasyaiva vibhāga iti pitṛto bhāgakalpaneti nyāyasiddhārthānuvādaḥ;
ata evāha kātyāyanaḥ |
sa evāṃśas tu sarveṣāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ nyāyato bhavet | iti |
— ity āhuḥ |
atra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhūr yā pitāmahopāttā nibandho dravyam eva vā |
tatra syāt sadṛśaṃ svāmyaṃ pituḥ putrasya caiva hi ||
bhūḥ – śālikṣetrādikā | nibandhaḥ – naigamādi – paṇyasthale ekaikasmin paṇye pratidinaṃ pratimāsaṃ vā iyatpaṇyam etasya jīvanārthaṃ dātavyam iti rājāmātyapradhānapuruṣādhikṛto nibandha ity ucyate | dravyam – suvarṇarajatādi spaṣṭam | yat tu pitāmahena pratigrahakrayādinā labdhaṃ tatra pituḥ putrasya ca svāmyaṃ sadṛśaṃ samānam | hi yasmāl lokaprasiddhatvād ity arthaḥ | ataḥ pitur icchayaiva na vibhāgaḥ | nāpi pitṛbhāgadvayam | ataś ca,
vibhāgaṃ cet pitā kuryād icchayā vibhajet sutān |
ity etat svārjitaviṣayam ity avagantavyam | tathaiva,
dvāv aṃśau pratipadyeta vibhajann ātmanaḥ pitā | [374]
ity etad api svārjitaviṣayam –
jīvator asvatantraḥ syāt jarayāpi samanvitaḥ | iti |
etad api pāratantryaṃ mātāpitrārjitadravyaviṣayam | tathaiva “anīśās te hi jīvatoḥ” ity etad api | tathā sarajaskāyāṃ mātarīty etat | saspṛhe ca pitari vibhāgam anicchaty api putrecchayaiva pitāmadravyavibhāgo bhavatīti jñāyate | tathā avibhaktena pitrā paitāmahe dravye dīyamāne vikrīyamāṇe vā putrasya pautrasya prapautrasya niṣedhe ‘py adhikāraḥ | pitrārjite tu na niṣedhādhikāraḥ tatparatantratvāt | anumatis tu kartavyā | paitṛke paitāmahe ca svāmyaṃ yady api janmanaiva, tathāpi paitṛke pitṛparatantratvāt pituś cārjakatvena prādhānyāt pitrā viniyujyamāne svārjite dravye putreṇānumatiḥ kartavyā,
asaṃbhūya sutrān sarvān na dānaṃ na ca vikrayaḥ |
iti smaranāc ca jñāyate | paitāmahe tu dvayoḥ sāmyam aviśiṣṭam iti niṣedhādhikāro ‘py astīti viśeṣaḥ | ata evoktaṃ manunā |
sa paitṛkaṃ pitā dravyam anavāptaṃ yad āpnuyāt |
na tat putrair bhajet sārdham akāmaḥ svayam ārjitam || iti |
asyārthaḥ – yat pitāmahārjitaṃ kenāpy apahṛtaṃ pitāmahair nānuddhṛtaṃ yadi pitoddhārati tat svārjitam iva putraiḥ sārdham akāmataḥ anīhamānaḥ svayaṃ na vibhajed iti darśayatīti vijñāyate | ata eva bṛhaspatiḥ |
dravye pitāmahopātte jaṅgame sthāvare ‘pi vā |
samam aṃśitvam ākhyātaṃ pituḥ putrasya caiva hi || [375] iti |
vyāso ‘pi |
kramāyāte gṛhakṣetre putrapautrāḥ samāṃśinaḥ |
atha vibhāgānantarakālam utpannasya vibhāgakalpanām āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
vibhakteṣu suto jātaḥ savarṇāyāṃ vibhāgabhāk |
asyārthaḥ – vibhakteṣu putreṣu paścāt savarṇāyāṃ bhāryāyām utpanno vibhāgabhāk | vibhajyata iti vibhāgaḥ – bhāgaḥ pitror bhāgaḥ taṃ bhajatīti vibhāgabhāk | pitror ūrdhvaṃ tayor aṃśaṃ labhata ity arthaḥ | asavarṇāyām utpannas tu svāṃśam eva pityāl labhate | mātṛkaṃ tu sarvam evety āha vijñāneśaḥ | ubhayam api sarvam evety āhur aparārkaprabhṛtayaḥ,
ūrdhvaṃ vibhāgāj jātas tu pitryam eva dhanaṃ haret |
iti smṛteḥ, sāmānyena pitror idaṃ pitryam iti vyākhyāṅgīkārāt, tathā,
ānīśaḥ pūrvajaḥ pitroḥ bhātur bhāge vibhaktajaḥ |
iti smaraṇāt | pitroḥ – matāpitroḥ, vibhāge – vibhāgāt pūrvam utpanno na svāmī | vibhaktajaś ca bhrātur bhāge na svāmīti vacanārthaḥ | tathā vibhāgottarakālaṃ pitrā yat kiṃcid ārjitaṃ tat sarvaṃ vibhaktajasyaiva | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ |
putraiḥ saha vibhaktena pitrā yat svayam ārjitam |
vibhaktajasya tat sarvam anīśāḥ pūrvajāḥ smṛtāḥ || iti |
ye ca vibhaktāḥ pitrā saha saṃsṛṣṭāḥ taiḥ sārdhaṃ pitur ūrdhvaṃ vibhaktajo vibhajet | tathāha manuḥ |
saṃsṛṣṭās tena vā ye syur vibhajeta sa taiḥ saha | iti |
yatra tu pituḥ dvau vā trayo vā bahavaḥ putrās tatra [376] katibhiḥ putraiḥ vibhaktaḥ katibhir avibhaktaḥ avibhaktānām eva pitrārjitadravyam uparate pitari vibhājyam | paścād etair vibhaktaḥ pitā cet taddravyaṃ pūrvavibhaktānāṃ paścād vibhaktānāṃ putrāṇām eva vibhājyaṃ na patnyāḥ | patnīduhitara ityādi svāminā saṃkramakramo na pitṛviṣayaḥ, api tu bhrātrādiviṣaya iti purastān nivedayiṣyate | vibhāgasamaye bhrātṛbhāryāyāṃ mātari cāspaṣṭagarbhāyāṃ vibhāgād ūrdhvam utpannasya bhāgakalpanam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
dṛśyād vā tadvibhāgaḥ syād āyavyayaśodhitāt |
etad uktaṃ bhavati – prātisvikeṣu bhāgeṣu tadutpannam āyaṃ praveśya pitṛkṛtam ṛṇam apanīya avaśiṣṭebhyo bhāgebhyaḥ kiṃcit kiṃcid uddhṛtya vibhaktajasya svabhāgasamaḥ kartavya iti | tadvibhāgasyeti tasya pitari prete bhrātṛvibhāgasamaye aspaṣṭagarbhāyāṃ mātari bhrātṛvibhāgottarakālam utpannasya vibhāgas tadvibhāga iti vijñāneśvaraḥ prāha | spaṣṭagarbhāyāṃ tu prasavaṃ vīkṣya kartavya ity āha vasiṣṭhaḥ: “atha bhrātṝṇāṃ dāyavibhāgo yāś cānapatyāḥ striyaḥ tāsām ā putralābhāt” iti gṛhītagarbhṇām ā prasavaṃ pratīkṣaṇīyam iti yojanīyam | avaśiṣṭaṃ pūrvam evoktam iti neha prapañcyate | ayaṃ ca nyāyo deśāntaragatasyāpi samāna ity āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
gotrasādhāraṇān tyaktvā yo ‘nyadeśaṃ samāśritaḥ |
ardhaśas tv āgatasyāśaḥ pradātavyo na saṃśayaḥ ||
gotrasādhāraṇān tyaktvā sarvasahavāsinivāsadeśam utsṛjyety arthaḥ | atyantadīrghakālaproṣitasya sadbhāvājñānataḥ kṛte ‘pi vibhāge tasyāpi bhāga ity āha sa eva |
ṛṇaṃ lekhyaṃ gṛhaṃ kṣetraṃ yasya paitāmahaṃ bhavet |
[377]        cirakālaṃ proṣito ‘pi bhāgabhāg āgatas tu saḥ ||
bhāgabhāg dhanabhāg ity arthaḥ | āgataḥ – vibhāgād ūrdhvam āgataḥ | pautrādau viśeṣam āha sa eva |
tṛtīyaḥ pañcamaś caiva saptamo yo ‘pi vā bhavet |
janmanāmaparijñāne labhetāṃśaṃ kramāgate ||
kramāgatadravyabhāga ity arthaḥ | kecid atra kramāgatasya bhūmātrasyāṃśo nānyasyety āhuḥ | yathāha viṣṇuḥ: “maulāḥ sāmantā anvayinaṃ vidus tasyāgatasya dātavyā gotrajair mahī” iti | kramāgatadravyopalakṣakam ity apare | atra viśeṣam āha bṛhaspatiḥ: “vibhāgād ūrdhvam āgatasya pūrvāgatasya vā svabhāgaṃ grahītuṃ pravṛttasya dṛṣṭādṛṣṭapramāṇenādau tāvad ātmanaḥ parāyattadravye svāmyaṃ sādhayato bhāgaharaṇe ‘dhikāro bhavati, nānyathā” iti | spaṣṭārthaḥ | vibhaktajaḥ putraḥ pitryaṃ mātṛkaṃ ca dhanaṃ sarvaṃ gṛhṇīta | tatra yadi vibhaktaḥ pitā mātā vā vibhaktāya putrāya snehavaśād ābharaṇādikaṃ prayacchati | tatra vibhaktajena dānapratiṣedho na kartavyaḥ | nāpi dattaṃ pratyādātavyam iti| yathāha viṣṇuḥ: “mātāpitṛbhyāṃ yad dattaṃ tat tasyaiva dhanam” iti, “na vibhaktajas tatreṣṭe” iti – vibhaktajasya svaṃ na bhavatīty arthaḥ | pitrā yad dattaṃ tat tasyaiveti nyāyapratipādanāt vibhāgāt prāg api yad dattaṃ tat tasyaiveti siddham |
atha strīdhanavibhāgaḥ | tatra viṣṇuḥ: “saudāyikaṃ strī yathākāmam āpnuyāt” iti | saudāyikaṃ bhartṛdattopalakṣakam | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
yac ca bhartrā dhanaṃ dattaṃ sā yathākāmam āpnuyāt | [378] iti |
saudāyikaṃ nāma –
ūḍhayā kanyayā vāpi patyuḥ pitṛgṛhe ‘pi vā |
bhartuḥ sakāśāt pitror vā labdhaṃ saudāyikaṃ smṛtam || iti |
labdhaṃ dhanam iti śeṣaḥ | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
yat kanyayā vivāhe ca vivāhāt parataś ca yat |
pitṛbhartṛgṛhāt prāptaṃ saudāyikaṃ smṛtam ||
nanu saudāyikaśabdaḥ svārthe taddhitāntaḥ | sudāya eva saudāyikaṃ vinayāditvāt ṭhak |
nanv etad anupapannam – svārthikataddhitāntatvena strīṇāṃ dāyānarhatvāt iti cet,
maivam, strīṇāṃ bhartṛdāyārhatvāt | svārthikāḥ pratyayāḥ prakṛtito liṅgavacanāny ativartanta iti nyāyāt saudāyikaśabdasya niyatanapuṃsakaliṅgatā | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
bhartrā prītena yad dattaṃ striyai tasmin mṛte ‘pi tat |
sā yathākāmam aśnīyāt dadyād vā sthāvarād ṛte || iti |
ataś ca yathākāmam ity anena svātantryam uktam | evaṃ ca saudāyike sthāvaretaraprītidatte strīṇāṃ svātantryam iti mantavyam | puruṣāṇāṃ tu strīdhane sarvatrāsvātantryam eva | yathāha kātyāyanaḥ |
na bhartā naiva ca suto na pitā bhrātaro na ca |
ādāne vā visarge vā strīdhane prabhaviṣṇavaḥ ||
svāmitvābhāvād ity abhiprāyaḥ | strīdhanaṃ tu adhyagnyādikam | tathāha manuḥ |
adhyagnyadhyāhavanikaṃ dattaṃ ca prītipūrvakam |
bhrātṛmātṛpitṛprāptaṃ ṣaḍvidhaṃ strīdhanaṃ smṛtam ||
yac ca vivāhakāle agnāv adhikṛtya mātulādibhir dattaṃ tad adhyagni | [379] tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
vivāhakāle yat strībhyo dīyate hy agnisaṃnidhau |
tad adhyagnikṛtaṃ sadbhiḥ strīdhanaṃ parikīrtitam ||
yat punar nayate nārī nīyamānā pitur gṛhāt |
adhyāhavanikaṃ nāma strīdhanaṃ parikīrtitam ||
prītyā dattaṃ ca yat kiṃcit śvaśrvā vā śvaśureṇa vā |
pādavandanikaṃ caiva prītidattaṃ tad ucyate ||
pādavandanikam – pādavandanāvasare bhrātṛmātṛpitṛbhyaḥ prāptam | yadā kadā vā jīvanārtham iti śeṣaḥ | ṣaḍvidhaṃ ityādi nyūnasaṃkhyā vyavacchedārtham, nādhikasaṃkhyāvyavacchedāya | ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
pitṛmātṛpatibhrātṛdattam adhyagny upāgatam |
ādhivedanikādyaṃ ca strīdhanaṃ parikīrtitam ||
ādhivedanikam – adhivedananimittam “adhivinnastriyai dadyāt” iti smṛteḥ | ādyaśabdena rikthakrayasaṃvibhāgaparigrahādhigamaprāptam etad dhanam | aparam api strīdhanam āha sa eva |
bandhudattaṃ yathā śulkam anvādheyakam eva ca |
bandhubhiḥ kanyāyā mātṛbandhubhiḥ pitṛbandhubhiś ca yad dattam | śulkaṃ nāma yad gṛhītvā kanyā dīyate tac chulkam iti vijñāneśaḥ | candrikākāras tu,
“gṛhopaskaravāhyānāṃ dohyābharaṇakarmaṇām |
mūlyaṃ labdhaṃ ca yat kiṃcic chulkaṃ tat parikīrtitam ||
iti, gṛhopaskarādīnāṃ mūlyaṃ kanyāgatatvena varādisakāśāt kanyārpaṇopādhitayeti śeṣaḥ” iti | anvādheyaṃ [380] nāma pariṇayanād anu paścād vā āhitaṃ dattam | uktaṃ ca kātyāyanena |
vivāhāt parato yac ca labdhaṃ bhartṛkulāt striyā |
anvādheyaṃ tu tad dravyaṃ labdhaṃ pitṛkulāt tathā || iti |
strīdhanaṃ parikīrtitam iti saṃbandhaḥ | atra bhāruciḥ “śulkaśabdena kanyāmūlyam ucyate | tat tu āsurādivivāha eveti tat tu niṣiddham” ity āha | ayam abhisandhiḥ – ārṣavivāhe gomithunaṃ gṛhītvā kanyā dīyate, “ārṣe gomithunena godvayena vā” iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | tad eva kanyāyā mātuḥ strīdhanam iti aniṣiddhatā | yad vā niṣiddham astv āsurādivivāhe draviṇādānam | atra niṣiddhatvāniṣiddhatvacintā na prastutā, api tu vibhājyatvāvibhājyatvacinteti na kaścid virodhaḥ | “na bhartā naiva tanayo na pitā” ityādvacanasya phalam āha sa eva:
yadi hy ekataro hy eṣāṃ strīdhanaṃ bhakṣayed balāt |
savṛddhikaṃ pradāpyaḥ syād daṇḍaṃ caiva samāpnuyāt ||
tad eva yady anujñāpya bhakṣayet prītipūrvakam |
mūlyam eva pradāpyaṃ syād yady asau dhanavān bhavet ||
dhanavān bhaved ity abhidhānāt, nirdhano mūlyamātram api na dāpya ity arthaḥ | anujñāpya bhakṣaṇe ‘pi mulyadānābhidhānād etad uktaṃ bhavati – strīdhane bhartur asvātantryaṃ na punaḥ pāratantryamātram | bhāryāyās tu vivāhasaṃskṛtāyāḥ bhartur dhane nityaparatantraṃ svāmitvaṃ saṃpadyate | tena bhartur bhāryātvam ekavidhaṃ na bhavatīty avagantavyam | ata eva strīdhanabhoge ‘py anarhatāṃ patyur āha devalaḥ |
vṛttir ābharaṇaṃ śulkaṃ lābhaś ca strīdhanaṃ bhavet |
[381]        bhoktry etat svayam evedaṃ patir nārhaty anāpadi ||
vṛtha mokṣe ca bhoge ca striyai dadyāt savṛddhikam || iti |
vṛttir vartanārthaṃ pitrādinā dattam | śulkaṃ kathitam | labhyata iti lābhaḥ | etad uktaṃ bhavati – gaurīvratādyarthaṃ striyā yal labhyate tad api strīdhanam iti | yad vā lābhaḥ – vṛddhiḥ pūrvoktastrīdhanaṃ parikalpitavṛddhimūlatvena vyavāhriyate | sā ca vṛddhir lābhaśabdenocyate | yady api prayuktadhanasvāmina eva kalpitā vṛddhiḥ, tathāpi dhanaprayoge strīṇām anadhikārāt patyur eva tadadhikārāt tathā śaṅkā mā bhūd ity upadiṣṭam | svayam evety evakāro ‘patyādīnāṃ vyudāsārthaḥ | vṛthā – āpadaṃ vinety arthaḥ | mokṣas tyāgaḥ | āpadaṃ vineti vadan āpadi tu patir eva strīdhanaṃ bhoktum arhati nānya iti darśayati | āpan nāma kuṭumbabharaṇārthe dravyābhāvaḥ | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
durbhikṣe dharmakārye ca vyādhau sapratirodhake |
gṛhītaṃ strīdhanaṃ bhartā na striyai dātum arhati ||
“dharmakārye – nitye naimittike kāmye ca, kvacic chāntike grahayajñādau | pratirodhake – dhanadānaṃ vinā nivārayitum aśakyadhanikādyāsedhādau” iti candrikākāraḥ | “bandīgrahaṇavigrahādau dravyāntarābhāvaḥ” iti vijñāneśaḥ | atra manuḥ viśeṣam āha |
yās tāsāṃ syur duhitaras tāsām api yathārhataḥ |
mātāmahyā dhanāt kiṃcit pradeyaṃ prītipūrvakam || iti |
yathārhataḥ – śīlopayogadāridryāpekṣayety arthaḥ | tāsām – bhaginīnāṃ durhitara ity arthaḥ |
nanu bhaginīduhitṝṇāṃ bhrātṛbhaginī sadvāve mātāmahīdhane svāmitāvāvāt kim iti kiṃcit pradīyata iti |
satyam, prītipūrvakaṃ ity uktatvān na doṣaḥ | yathā paitṛke dhane [382] kanyānāṃ dāyārhatvābhāve ‘pi vacanabalāt “patitāḥ syur aditsavaḥ” iti nindāsmaraṇāc ca saṃskāropayogi pratiṣṭhopayogi ca dravyaṃ deyam iti pratīyate tathehāpīty arthaḥ | viṣṇus tu viśeṣam āha: “yautakaṃ mātuḥ kumārīdāya eva” iti na sahodarāṇām iti śeṣaḥ | yautakam anyonyānvitayoḥ bandhūvarayor deyaṃ yat taddhanam | yutayor iti vyutpattyā yautakam | gautamas tu viśeṣam āha: “strīdhanaṃ duhitṝṇām aprattānām apratiṣṭhitānāṃ ca” iti saudāyikādi strīdhanaṃ kumārīṇām apratiṣṭhitānām anūḍhānāṃ ca duhitṝṇāṃ svaṃ bhavatīty arthaḥ | ataś ca tad dhanaṃ tāv iva duhitaro gṛhṇīyur yathāṃśam ity abhiprāyaḥ | iyam aparākānusārad gautamavacanavyākhyā | vijñāneśvarānusāreṇa tu pūrvam eva vyākhyātam | bhāryāyā ūrdhvaṃ kanyābhāve bhartuḥ bhāryārikthaṃ bhavet | tathā cāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
aprajastrīdhanaṃ bhartuḥ brāhmādiṣu caturṣv api |
duhitṝṇāṃ prasūtā cec cheṣeṣu pitṛgāmi tat ||
asyārthaḥ – aprajastriyāḥ pūrvoktāyāḥ brāhmadaivārṣaprājāpatyeṣu caturṣu vivāheṣu bhāryātvaṃ prāptāyā atītāyāḥ pūrvoktaṃ saudāyikaṃ dhanaṃ bhartur bhavati | tadadbhāve tatpratyāsannānāṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ bhavati | śeṣeṣu āsuragāndharvarākṣasapaiśāceṣu vivāheṣu tat – aparajastrīdhanaṃ pitṛgāmi – mātā ca pitā ca pitarau taugacchatīti pitṛgāmi | ekaśeṣanirdiṣṭāyā api mātuḥ prādhānyāt prathamaṃ dhanagrahaṇam | pitā mātrety ekaśeṣe mātur eva prādhānyāt | tadabhāve tatpratyāsannānāṃ dhanagrahaṇam | sarveṣv eva vivāheṣu prasūtā apatyavatī strī ced duhitṝṇāṃ tad dhanaṃ bhavati | atra duhitṛśabdena duhitṛduhitara ucyante | sākṣād duhitṝṇām “mātur duhitaraḥ śeṣam” [383] ity atra uktatvāt, ataḥ svamātṛdhanaṃ mātari vṛttāyāṃ duhitaro gṛhṇanti | tatra coḍhānūḍhāsamudāye anūḍhā gṛhṇāti | tadabhāve pariṇītā | tatrāpi pratiṣṭhitāpratiṣthitāsamavāye ‘pratiṣṭhitā gṛhṇāti |
etad vijñāneśvaramataṃ bhāruci-aparārka-candrikākārādayo na manyante, vijñāneśvareṇa svamatimātraparikalpitatvāt, anekādhyāhārparikalpitatvāc ca, gautamavacane ca “strīdhanaṃ duhitṝṇām aprattānām apratiṣṭhitānāṃ ca” iti sāmānyenābhidhānāc ceti | etac ca śulkavyatirekeṇa | śulkaṃ tu sodaryāṇām eva “bhaginī śulkaṃ sodaryāṇām uṛdhvaṃ mātuḥ” iti gautamavacanāt | mātur ūrdhvam ity anvayaḥ | sarvāsāṃ duhitṝṇām abhāve duhitur duhitaro gṛhṇanti | “duhitṝṇāṃ prasūtā cet” ity asmād vacanāt | tāsāṃ bhinnamātṛkāṇāṃ viṣamāṇāṃ samavāye mātṛdvāreṇa bhāgakalpanā “pratimātṛ svabhāvaḥ” iti gautamasmaraṇāt | svabhāvaḥ – svatvam | pratimātṛ – mātaraṃ mātaraṃ mātṛsvatvānusāri tāsāṃ svatvam ity arthaḥ | anapatyahīnajātistrīdhanaṃ tu bhinnodarāpy uttamajātistrīduhitā gṛhṇāti | tadabhāve tadapatyam | tathā ca manuḥ |
striyās tu yad bhaved vittaṃ pitrā dattaṃ kathaṃcana |
brāhmaṇī tad dharet kanyā tadapatyasya vā bhavet || iti |
brāhmaṇīgrahaṇam uttamajātyupalakṣaṇam iti vijñāneśaḥ | ataś ca anapatyavaiśyādhanaṃ kṣatriyakanyā gṛhṇāti, duhitṝṇāṃ putrāṇāṃ ca mātṛdhanasaṃbandhāt | tathā ca manuḥ |
jananyāṃ saṃsthitāyāṃ tu samaṃ sarve sahodarāḥ |
bhajeran mātṛkaṃ rikthaṃ bhaginyaś ca sanābhayaḥ || iti |
mātṛkaṃ rikthaṃ sarve sahodarāḥ samaṃ bhajeran | sanābhayo [384] bhaginyaś ca samaṃ bhajerann iti saṃbandhaḥ | na punaḥ sahodarā bhaginyaś ca saṃbhūya samaṃ vibhajeran iti, pūrvoktakramapratipādakavacanavirodhāt, vibhāgakartṛtvānvayenāpi caśabdopapatteś ca | yathā devadattaḥ pacatu yajñadattaś ceti | samagrahaṇam uddhāranivṛttyarthaṃ | sodaragrahaṇaṃ bhinnodaranivṛttyartham | ata eva viṣṇuḥ: “bhaginīśulkaṃ mātuḥ sodarāṇām eva” iti | ayam arthaḥ – bhaginīśulkātmakaṃ strīdhanaṃ mātur eva | mātur abhāve sodarāṇām eva na bhinnodarāṇām ity arthaḥ | yat tu gautamasūtram: “bhaginīśulkaṃ sodarāṇām ūrdhvaṃ mātuḥ” iti, tat mātur ūrdhvaṃ sodarāṇām ity anvayaparam | tathāha bodhāyanaḥ: “strīdhanaṃ mātṛgāmi tadabhāve sodarabhrātṛgāmi” iti strīdhanaṃ kanyāśulkam | ataś ca “kanyāśulkaviṣayasodarāsodaravibhāge sodarāṇām api kiṃcid deyam” ity asahāyavyākhyānam asahāyam | bhaginīśūlkaṃ sodarāṇām “ūrdhvaṃ mātuḥ” ityādiṣu smṛtiṣu bhaginīśulkarūpe sarvasmin dhane sodarāṇām eva svāmyapratipādanāt | putrāṇām abhāve pautrāḥ pitāmahadhanahāriṇa iti vacobhaṅgyāha gautamaḥ: “ṛṇapradātāraś ca rikthabhājaḥ ṛṇaṃ pratikuryuḥ” iti, “putrapautraiḥ ṛṇaṃ deyam” iti pautrāṇām api pitāmahyṛṇāpākaraṇe adhikārāt |
nanv evaṃ “mātāmahyāṃ vṛttāyāṃ tadaurdhvadaihike dauhitrasyaivadhikārāt putrapautradravyasamudāyenaiva aurdhvadaihikakriyāṃ kuryuḥ” iti viṣṇuvacanavirodhaḥ syād iti cet,
maivam | ṣaḍaśaśrāddheṣv eva putrapautradhanasaṃsargaḥ, tat pretatvanivṛtter ubhayākāṅkṣitatvād iti bhārucinā viṣayavyavasthāyāḥ kṛtatvāt | pautrāṇām apy abhāve vibhāgakramam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
atītāyām aprajasi bāndhavāḥ tad avāpnuyuḥ | [385]
asyārthaḥ – tat pūrvoktastrīdhanam aprajasi anapatyāyām – duhitṛdauhitraputrapautrarahitāyāṃ striyām atītāyāṃ bāndhavāḥ – bhartrādayo gṛhṇantīti | yathāha manuḥ |
brāhmadaivārṣagāndharvaprājāpatyeṣu yad dhanam |
atītāyām aprajasi bhartur eva tad iṣyate || iti |
yat tu kātyāyanenoktam |
bandhudattaṃ tu bandhūnāṃ hy abhāve bhartṛgāmi tat |
tad apy uktapañcavidhetaravivāhasaṃskṛtastrīdhanaviṣayam | anyathā śulkaṃ śulkadātur eva syāt | “bhaginīśūlkaṃ sodarāṇām ūrdhvaṃ mātuḥ” iti gautamavacanavirodhaḥ syāt | śulkākhyastrīdhanasya dātāro varādayaḥ teṣāṃ dātṛtve ‘pi taddhanaṃ na bhavati | kiṃ tu svāmināṃ sodara(rya)bhrātṝṇām, teṣāṃ tanmāturabhāve bhavatīty arthaḥ | ata eva bhaginīśulkaṃ nāmārṣavivāhe gomithunam eva nāsurādivivāhe | tatra taddātur eva taddhanavidhānāt | yat tu bhārucivyākhyānaṃ tat prauḍhivādamātram ity anusaṃdheyam | yat tu vaivāhikaṃ śulkaṃ prakṛtvya śāṅkhenoktam: “svaṃ ca śulkaṃ voḍhā” iti — voḍhā varaḥ, svaṃ svayam eva śulkaṃ gṛhṇīyād iti — tad aparisamāpte vivāhe mṛtāyāṃ vadhvāṃ draṣṭavyam | vivāhasamāptiḥ – vivāhahomaparisamāptiḥ | anenaivābhiprāyeṇa yājñavalkyenāpy uktam: “mṛtāyāṃ dattam ādadyāt” iti | dattaṃ śulkam alaṅkārādikaṃ vā ādadyāt voḍheti śeṣaḥ | vāgdattā yā saṃskārāt prāk mriyate tadviṣayaṃ vacanam ity āha | gauṇamātṛparigaṇanapūrvakaṃ taddhanahartṝn āha [386] bṛhaspatiḥ |
mātṛṣvasā mātulānī pitṛvyastrī pitṛṣvasā |
śvaśrūḥ pūrvajapatnī ca mātṛtulyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ ||
yadāsām auraso na syāt suto dauhitra eva vā |
tatsuto vā dhanaṃ tāsāṃ svastrīyādyāḥ samāpnuyuḥ || iti |
svasrīyo dhanasvāmino bhāgineyaḥ | sa ca svamātṛṣvsur dhanam āpnuyāt | evam ādyaśabdaparigṛhītā yathākramaṃ svīyamātṛtulyāyā dhanaṃ samāpnuyuḥ | evam eva sapatnīsantāno ‘py upamātṛdhanaṃ tatsantānatadbhrātrādyabhāve samāpnuyāt | ādhivedanike strīdhane viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
adhivinnastriyai dadyād ādhivedanikaṃ samam |
na dattaṃ strīdhanaṃ yasyai datte tv ardhaṃ prakīrtitam ||
yasyā upari vivāhāntaraṃ sā adhivinnā strī | tasyai adhivinnastriyai | ādhivedanikam adhivedananimittaṃ dhanam adhivedanaprayojanakaṃ “prayojanam” iti ṭhak | yāvad adhivedanārthaṃ vyayīkṛtaṃ tāvad dadyāt | yasyai bhartrā śvaśureṇa vā strīdhanaṃ na dattaṃ syāt | datte strīdhane ādhivedanikasya dravyasyārdhaṃ dadyāt | yāvat pūrvadattam ādhivedanikasamaṃ bhavati tāvad deyam ity arthaḥ | atra strīdhanasya duhitrādisaṃbandhaḥ pratyāsattitāratamyanyāyanibandhano na vācanikaḥ | pratyāsattitāratamyaṃ ca “pumān puṃso ‘dhike śukle strī bhavaty adhike striyā” iti vijñāneśvareṇa pratipāditam ity uktaṃ prāk | atra vivadante vṛddhāḥ – strīdhanaṃ dāyaśabdavācyaṃ na veti, “tasmāt striyo nirindriyaḥ adāyadīḥ” iti śruteḥ strīṇāṃ dāyānarhatvāt | strīdhanavibhāgo na dāyadhanavibhāgaḥ kiṃ tu taddhanavibhāga iti vyavahāraḥ | [387] yat tūktaṃ saṃgrahakāreṇa,
pitṛdvārāgataṃ dravyaṃ mātṛdvārāgataṃ ca yat |
kathitaṃ dāyaśabdena tadvibhāgo ‘dhunocyate ||
iti, tat tu yathā pitṛdvārāgataṃ dravyaṃ dāyaśabdena kathitaṃ dāyaśabdavācyaṃ tadvan mātṛdvārāgatam api dāyaśabdavācyam | ataś caikaśabdasyārthadvayāṅgīkāre śaktigauravaṃ syād iti anyatra vṛttyantaraṃ svīkāryam | ataḥ svamātṛdvārāgataṃ dravyaṃ dīyate dadātīti vā vyutpattyā gauṇavṛttyā dāyaśabdārtha ity āhur bhāruci-aparārka-someśvarācāryaprabhṛtayaḥ | vijñāneśvara-asahāya-medhātithiprabhṛtayas tu “tasmāt striyo nirindriyāḥ” iti śruter nirindriyaśvaṇam atyantanirindriyaviṣayaṃ na bhavati | api ty alpendriyaparam,
pumān puṃso ‘dhike śukle strī bhavaty adhike striyāḥ |
ity atra adhikatālaptvapratīteḥ | na kevalam atyantanirindriyatvaṃ strīṇām iti dāyārhatvaṃ strīṇām apy asti | kiṃ tu pitṛputravibhāge putrāṇāṃ prādhānyāt tatraiva strīṇāṃ dāyānarhatvād yat kiṃcit prītidānārhatvaṃ strīṇām apy astīty evaṃ parāśrutiḥ | ata eva saṃgrahakāravacanam pitṛdvārāgataṃ dravyaṃ mātṛdvārāgataṃ cety ubhayaṃ dāyaśabdavācyam iti svaraso ‘rthaḥ saṃpadyata iti gatam api spaṣṭārthaṃ punar uktam |
atha dvyāmuṣyāyaṇasya vibhāge viśeṣaḥ kathyate | tasya svarūpam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
aputreṇa parakṣetre niyogotpaditaḥ sutaḥ |
ubhayor apy asau rikthī piṇḍadātā ca dharmataḥ || iti |
ataś ca dvipitṛko dvyāmuṣyāyaṇaḥ dvayor api rikthahārī [388] piṇḍadātā ca | parakṣetra ity asyāyam arthaḥ – parasya kṣetraṃ bhāryā | tasyāḥ parakṣetratvaṃ vāgdānamātreṇa | na tu pariṇayanena | pariṇītaparakṣetre niyogasya niṣiddhatvāt | tathā hi manuḥ |
devarād vā sapiṇḍād vā striyā samyaṅ niyuktayā |
prajepsitābhigantavyā santānasya parikṣaye || iti |
vidhavāyāṃ niyuktas tu ghṛtākto vāgyato niśi |
ekam utpadayet putraṃ na dvitīyaṃ kathaṃ ca na ||
ityevaṃ niyogam upanyasya svayam eva niṣedhati:
nānyasmin vidhavā nārī niyoktavyā dvijātibhiḥ |
anyasmin viniyuñjānā dharmaṃ hanyuḥ sanātanam ||
nodvāhikeṣu mantreṣu niyogaḥ kīrtyate kvacit |
na vivāhavidhāv uktaṃ vidhavāvedanaṃ punaḥ ||
ayaṃ dvijair hi vidvadbhiḥ paśudharmo vigarhitaḥ |
manuṣyāṇām api prokto vaine rājyaṃ praśāsati ||
sa mahīm akhilāṃ bhuñjan rājarṣipravaraḥ purā |
varṇānāṃ saṃkaraṃ cakre kāmopahatacetanaḥ ||
tataḥ prabhṛti yo mohāt pramītapatikāṃ striyam |
niyojayaty apatyārthe garhante taṃ hi sādhavaḥ || iti |
na hi vihitapratiṣiddhatvād vikalpaḥ, niyoktṝṇāṃ nindāśravaṇāt, strīdharme vyabhicarasya bahudoṣaśravanāt, saṃyamasya praśastataratvāc ca | yathāha manur eva |
kāmaṃ tu kṣapayed dehaṃ puṣpamūlaphalāśanaiḥ |
na tu nāmāpi gṛhṇīyāt patyau prete parasya tu || [389] iti |
apatyalobhād yā tu strī bhartāram ativartate |
seha nindām avāpnoti paralokāc ca hīyate ||
iti putrārhaṃ puruṣāntarāśrayaṇaṃ niṣedhati | tasmād vihitapratiṣiddhatvād vikalpa iti vaktum ayuktam | na ca “apatyalobhād yā tu strī” iti sati bhartarīty vācyam,
mṛte bhartari mriyeta śayyāṃ vā paripālayet |
iti viṣṇuvacanāt | atra mṛte bhartari jīvati yadā tatparāyaṇā bhartrekaparatantrā strīdharmoktaprakāreṇa mriyeta, mṛte bhartari tatparāyanā strīdharmāvyudāsārthaṃ śayyāṃ vā paripālayed ity uktam | tatprakāro vakṣyate | ato vāgdattaviṣayamiti vijñāneśaḥ | bhārucyādayas tu na sahante,
apatyalobhād yā tu strī bhartāram ativartate |
iti vacanaṃ jīvadbhartṛkāviṣayam,
nānyasmin vidhavā nārī niyoktavyā dvijātibhiḥ |
iti sārthavādakavacanaṃ devarādivyatirikānyaparam | niyoktur nindāśravaṇaṃ devarādivyatirikteṣu ye niyoktāras tadviṣayam | strīṇāṃ vyabhicārasya bahudoṣaśravaṇaṃ niyogavyatiriktavyabhicāraviṣayam | ataś ca,
ayaṃ dvijair hi vidvadbhiḥ paśudharmo vigarhitaḥ |
iti paśudharmadṛṣṭāntokter aicchiko vyabhicāro devarādivyatiriktaniyogaś ca niṣidhyate | devarādivyatiriktaniyogaḥ paśudharmatulyaḥ | ataś ca na śayyāparipālanaputrotpādanayor vikalpaḥ | kiṃ tu putravatyā śayyāparipālanaṃ duhitṛmatyā vā | tadabhāve niyogād apy apatyotpādanam āvaśyakam, [390]
prajepsitādhigantavyā santānasya parikṣaye |
iti vacanāt | śayyāparipālanāt santānanirvāha eva śreyān ity aparārka-bhāruci-someśvarādīnāṃ matam | etan niyojanaṃ kaliyuge niṣiddham api yugāntarābhiprāyeṇoktam | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
yasyā mriyeta kanyāyā vācā satye kṛte patiḥ |
tām anena vidhānena nijo vindeta devaraḥ ||
yathāvidhy adhigatyaināṃ śuklavastrāṃ śucismitām |
mitho bhajetāprasavāt sakṛt sakṛd ṛtāv ṛtau ||
yasmai vāgdattā kanyā sa pratigrahamātreṇaivāsyāḥ patir ity asmād eva vacanād avagamyate | tasmin mṛte devaras tasya jyeṣṭhaḥ kaniṣṭho vā nijaḥ sodaro vindeta pariṇayet | yathāvidhi – śāstram anatikramya pariṇīyānena vidhānena ghṛtābhyaṅgavāṅniyamādinā śuklavastrāṃ śucismitāṃ manovākkāyasaṃyatāṃ mitho rahasi ā garbhagrahaṇāt sakṛt sakṛd ṛtāv ṛtau ekaikaṃ vāraṃ gacchet | ayaṃ ca vācaniko vivāhaḥ niyuktābhigamanāṅgaghṛtābhyaṅgādiniyamavad iti mantavyam | ato na devarasya bhāryātvam āpādayati | atas tadutpannam apatyaṃ kṣetrasvāmina eva bhavati | na devarasya, saṃvid asti ced ubhayor api | etad vāgdattāviṣayakaniyojanaṃ vijñānayogimatānusāreṇoktam | bhārucyādīnāṃ tu mate vidhavāniyojanam apy asti vāgdattāniyojanam apy astīti dhyeyam |
mukhyagauṇaputrāṇāṃ svarūpam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
auraso dharmapatnījaḥ tatsamaḥ putrikāsutaḥ ||
kṣetrajaḥ kṣetrajātas tu sagotreṇetareṇa vā |
gṛhe pracchanna utpanno gūḍhajaḥ sa sutaḥ smṛtaḥ |
kānīnaḥ kanyakājāto mātāmahasuto mataḥ ||
[391]        akṣatāyāṃ kṣatāyāṃ vā jātaḥ paunarbhavaḥ sutaḥ |
dadyān mātā pitā vā yaṃ sa putro dattako bhavet ||
krītaś ca tābhyāṃ vikrītaḥ kṛtrimaḥ syāt svayaṃkṛtaḥ |
dattātmā tu svayaṃ datto garbhe vinnaḥ sahoḍhajaḥ ||
utsṛṣṭo gṛhyate yas tu so ‘paviddho bhavet sutaḥ ||
aurasaḥ – urasā jātaḥ, sa ca dharmapatnījaḥ putro mukhyaḥ | tatsamaḥ aurasasamaḥ putrikāputraḥ,
abhrātṛkāṃ pradāsyāmi tubhyaṃ kanyām alaṃkṛtām |
asyāṃ yo jāyate putraḥ same putro bhaved iti ||
iti vasiṣṭhavacanāt | putrikāputra ity atra ṣaṣṭhīsamāso gamyate | putrikaiva putraḥ – putrikāputra iti karmadhārayo ‘pi vavakṣitaḥ | yathāha gautamaḥ: “tṛtīyaḥ putrikaiva” iti | tṛtīyaḥ putraḥ putrikivety arthaḥ | kṣetrajaḥ – kṣetrajātaḥ, niyogād utpannaḥ | sagotreṇetareṇa vety uttareṇānvayaḥ | itareṇa – sapiṇḍena devareṇa vā | gūḍhajo gūḍhotpannaḥ pitṛgṛhe pracchanam utpanna ity arthaḥ | savarṇatvaniścaye satīti śeṣaḥ | evaṃ kānīnādāv apy ūhyam |
mātā pitā vā dadyātāṃ yam adbhiḥ putram āpadi |
sadṛśaṃ prītiyuktaṃ ca sa jñeyo dattakaḥ sutaḥ ||
āpadgrahaṇād anāpadi na deyaḥ | dātur ayaṃ pratiṣedhaḥ | tathā ekaputro na deyaḥ, “na tv ekaṃ putraṃ dadyāt pratigṛhṇīyād vā” iti vasiṣṭhasmaraṇāt | anekaputrasadbhāve ‘pi jyeṣṭho na deyaḥ |
jyeṣṭhena jātamātreṇa putrī bhavati mānavaḥ | iti |
tasyaiva putrakāryakaraṇe mukhyatvāt | putrapratigrahaprakāram āha [392] vasiṣṭhaḥ: “putraṃ pratigrahīṣyan bandhūn āhūya rājani cāvedya niveśanamadhye vyāhṛtibhir hutvā adūrabāndhavam asaṃnikṛṣṭaṃ gṛhṇīyāt” iti | adūrabāndhavam asaṃnikṛṣṭaṃ gṛhṇīyād ity anena atyantadeśabhāṣādiviprakṛṣṭasya pratiṣedhaḥ | asaṃnikṛṣṭam ity anena jñātiniṣedhaḥ | krītas tu putraḥ tābhyām – mātāpitṛbhyāṃ mātrā pitrā vā vikrītaḥ | pūrvavad ekaṃ putraṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ ca varjayitvā āpadi savarṇa ity eva | yat tu manunoktam |
krīṇīyād yas tv apatyārthaṃ mātāpitror yam antikāt |
sa krītakaḥ sutas tasya sadṛśo ‘sadṛśo ‘pi vā || iti |
tat guṇaiḥ “sadṛśo ‘sadṛśo ‘pi vā” iti vyākhyeyam, na jātyā, “sajātīyeṣv ayaṃ proktaḥ’ ity upasaṃhārāt | “kṛttrimaḥ syāt svayaṃkṛtaḥ” kṛttrimas tu putraḥ svayaṃ putrārthinā dhanakṣetrādipradarśanādipralobhanaiḥ putrīkṛto mātāpitṛvihīnaḥ, tatsadbhāve tatparatantratvāt | dattātmā tu putro yo mātāpitṛvihīnaḥ tābhyāṃ tyakto vā tavāhaṃ putro bhavāmīti svayaṃdatta upanītaḥ | teṣāṃ vibhāgaprakāram āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
piṇḍado ‘ṃśaharaś caiṣāṃ pūrvābhāve paraḥ paraḥ | iti |
eteṣāṃ pūrvoktānāṃ putrāṇāṃ dvādaśānāṃ pūrvapūrvasyābhāve uttarottaraḥ piṇḍadaḥ aṃśaharo – dhanaharo veditavyaḥ | aurasasya dhanagrahaṇe prāpte manur apavādam āha |
putrikāyāṃ kṛtāyāṃ tu yadi putro ‘nu jāyate |
samas tatra vibhāgaḥ syāt jyeṣṭhatā nāsti hi striyāḥ || iti |
tathā anyeṣām ap purvasmin saty uttareṣāṃ putrāṇāṃ caturthāṃśabhāgitvam [393] uktaṃ vasiṣṭhena: “kasmiṃścit pratigṛhīte aurasa utpadyeta caturthāṃśabhāgī syād dattakaḥ” iti | dattakagrahaṇaṃ krītakṛttrimādīnāṃ pradarśanārtham, putrīkaraṇāviśeṣāt | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
utpanne tv aurase putre caturthāṃśaharāḥ sutāḥ |
asavarṇāḥ savarṣāsu grāsācchādanabhājanāḥ || iti |
asavarṇāḥ – kṣetrajadattakādayaḥ | te caurase sati caturthāṃśaharāḥ | caturthāṃśo nāma caturthasya yo ‘ṃśaḥ samatvena parikalpyate tattulyo ‘ṃśaḥ – pañcamāṃśa ity arthaḥ,
pañcamāṃśaharā dattakṛttrimādisutāḥ punaḥ |
iti smṛteḥ | punar iti – paścād utpanne aurasa ity arthaḥ | asavarṇāḥ – kānīnagūḍhotpanna-sadhoḍhapaunarbhavāḥ | teṣāṃ yady api savarṇatvādiniścaye kānīnatvādivyapadeśaḥ, tathāpi saṃdighe ‘pi savarṇatve ‘savarṇatvavyapadeśaḥ | yad api manunoktam,
eka evaurasaḥ putraḥ pitryasya vasunaḥ prabhuḥ |
śeṣāṇām ānṛśaṃsyāt tu pradadyāt tu prajīvanam ||
iti, tad api dattakādīnām aurasapratikūlatve nirguṇatve ca veditavyam iti vijñāneśaḥ | someśvaras tu “dattādivyatiriktānāṃ kānīnagūḍhotpannasahoḍhapaunarbhavānām eva jīvanadānam iti śeṣaśabdārthaḥ” ity āha | bhārucis tu “ ‘eka evaurasaḥ putraḥ’ ityādivacanāt ekaputraviṣaye dattādisvīkāro ‘sti, tathā ca dattaputrādisvīkārāt pūrvaṃ sthitasya putrasya dattādīnāṃ prajīvanapradānaṃ nānyeṣām” [394] ity āha | ayam eva vakṣaḥ śreyān | kṣetrajasya viśeṣo darśito manunā |
ṣaṣṭhaṃ tu kṣetrajasyāṃśaṃ pradadyāt paitṛkād dhanāt |
auraso vibhajan dāyaṃ pitryaṃ pañcamam eva vā || iti |
dvādaśavidhaputrāṇāṃ madhye ṣaṇṇām eva dāyabhāktvam | uttareṣām adāyabhāktvam |
aurasaḥ kṣetrajaś caiva putrī dattrima eva ca |
gūḍhotpanno ‘paviddhaś ca dāyādā bāndhavāś ca ṣaṭ ||
kānīnaś ca sahoḍhaś ca krītaḥ paunarbhavas tathā |
svayaṃdattaś ca śaudraś ca ṣaḍ adāyādabāndhavāḥ ||
ubhayavidhasya ṣaṭkasya bāndhavatvaṃ samānam | ataś ca samānagotratvena ca sapiṇḍatvena codakapradānādhikāritvaṃ vargadvayasyāpi samānam eva | kiṃ tu svapitṛsamānodakasapiṇḍānāṃ saṃnihitavirkthahārābhāve pūrvaṣaṭkasyaiva rikthaharatvaṃ netaraṣaṭkasyeti dhyeyam |
nanv evam,
gotrarikthe janayitur na bhajed dattrimaḥ sutaḥ |
gotrarikathānugaḥ piṇḍo vyapaiti dadataḥ svadhām || iti |
manuvacanād dattrimasya svajanakagotrasāpiṇḍyayoḥ nivṛttau katham “dattrimo ‘pi svajanayituḥ svadhāṃ kuryāt” iti viṣṇuvacanam iti cet,
ucyate | tat tu dattrimajanakasya santatyabhāve veditavyam | upariṣṭāt prapañcyate | ataś caurasavyatiriktānāṃ putrapratinidhīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ rikthahāritvaṃ pūrvasya pūrvasyābhāve avaśiṣṭam | aurasasya tu
eka evaurasaḥ putraḥ pitryasya vasunaḥ prabhuḥ |
ity anenaiva rikthabhāktvamuktam |
yat tu, [395]
sarvāṃs tāṃs tena putreṇa putriṇo manur abravīt |
iti, tad api bhrātṛputrasya putrīkaraṇasaṃbhave anyeṣāṃ putrīkaraṇaniṣedhārtham, na punaḥ putratvapratipādānāya | tatsuto gotrajo bandhur ity anena virodhāt | candrikākāras tu “praśaṃsārthaparaṃ vacanam” ity āha | dhāreśvara-devasvāminau tu vijñānayogimatānuvartināv eva | yathoktaṃ devasvāminā: ubhayatrāpi “dady ekaputrā bahavaḥ,” “bhrātṝṇām ekajātānām” iti vacanadvaye bhrātṛsute putrapratinidhitayā kathaṃcit kartuṃ śakye sati tadanyo na pratinidhiḥ | anulomajānāṃ tu mūrdhāvasiktādīnām aurase ‘py antarbhāvāt teṣām apy abhāve kṣetrajādīnāṃ dāyaharatvaṃ boddhavyam | śūdrāputras tv aurado ‘pi kṛtsnaṃ bhāgam anyābhāve ‘pi na labhate | yad āha manuḥ |
yady api syāt tu satputro yady aputro ‘pi vā bhavet |
nādhikaṃ daśamād dadyāc chūdrāputrāya dharmataḥ || iti |
satputro vidyamānadvijā(ti)putraḥ | śūdradhanavibhāge viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
jāto ‘pi dāsyāṃ śūdreṇa kāmato ‘ṃśaharo bhavet |
mṛte pitari kuryus taṃ bhrātaras tv ardhabhāginam ||
abhrātṛko haret sarvaṃ dhitṝṇāṃ sutād ṛte ||
duhitṛsutasadbhāve ‘py ārdhabhāgika eva dāsaputra ity arthaḥ | atra śūdragrahaṇāt traivarṇikasya dāsyām utpannaḥ pitur icchayāpy aṃśaṃ na labhate | nāpy artham, kṛtsnaṃ dūrata eva | kiṃ tu jīvanamātraṃ labhate ||
iti śrīpratāparudradevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe apratibandhadāyaprakaraṇam
[396]
atha apratibandhadāyaḥ
atha vibhaktasyāputrasya svaryātasyāsaṃsṛṣṭino dhanaṃ ko gṛhṇīyād ity ata āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
patnī duhitaraś caiva pitarau bhrātaras tathā |
tatsuto gotrajo bandhuḥ śiṣyaḥ sabrahmacāriṇaḥ ||
eṣām abhāve pūrvasya dhanabhāg uttarottaraḥ |
svaryātasya hy aputrasya sarvavarṇeṣv ayaṃ vidhiḥ || iti |
ayaṃ ca patnyādiṣu svāmitāsaṃkramakramaḥ pratyāsattitāratamyarūpanyāyanibandhano vācanikaḥ | svasvāmibhāvasaṃbandhasya vācanikatvābhāvāt | tathā hi, svatvaṃ laukikaṃ laukikakriyājanyatvāt vrīhyādivat | yūpāhavanīyācāryakādīnāṃ laukikānāṃ na laukikatakṣaṇādikriyāmātrajanyatā | kiṃ tu mantrādiniyamopetatakṣaṇādijanyateti na vyabhicāraḥ | yas tu vaidhadānapratiyogini pratigrahe mantraniyamaḥ sa dānāpūrvotpattyartho na svatvotpattyarthaḥ | avaidhadānapratiyoginy amantrake pratigrahe svatvotpattidarśanād iti prapañcitam lipsāsūtre | yat tu vijñānayoginā svatvaṃ laukikaṃ laukikakriyāsādhanatvāt vrīhyādivad ity uktam, tad udgranthaṃ sthūlam iva pratibhāti | lipsāsūtre hi guruṇā laukikakriyāsādhyatvenaiva svatvasya laukikatvam uktam | yatheṣṭaviniyogārhatvaṃ svatvam iti svatvalakṣaṇam ity uktvā ārjanāt svatvaṃ nāma kaścana saṃbandha ity anena granthena | asyārthaḥ – ārjanaṃ kartṛkarmaṇoḥ saṃbandhakaraṃ sakarmakatvāt | na ca grāmaṃ tyajatītyādau vyabhicāra iti vācyam | na hy atra pāramārthikaḥ saṃyogādisaṃbandhaḥ sādhyate kiṃ tu kartṛkarmagataḥ [397] kaścid atiśayo 'rjanajanmā sādhyate | sa caudāsīnyapracyutirūpatvāt. saṃbandharūpa iti gīyate | grāmaṃ tyajatītyādāv api grāmato ‘tiśayo vibhāgarūpaḥ kriyāgarbho ‘tiśayo ‘sty eva | na ca pratigrahādijanyo hastasaṃyogādir eva atiśayo ‘stv iti vācyam, vyabhicārāt, janmādau tadasaṃbhavāt | na cākarmakatvāj janikriyāyāṃ karmagatātiśayajanakatvam iti vācyam | arjayatiśabdavācyatvadaśāyāṃ sakarmakatvāt | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ – janidhātuḥ kartṛgatakriyārthaḥ syāt, prāg asataḥ kriyāśrayatvāyogāt, labdhasattākasya punarjananābhāvāt | ato janayitṛkriyāyogaṃ janyasya svātantryavivakṣayā janidhātur āheti gurumatatatvam | yathaikam eva jñānaṃ ghaṭo bhātīty atrākarmakaṃ ghaṭaṃ jānīhīty atra sakarmakam | gurumate vittibhātyor abhedaḥ | yathoktaṃ guruṇā: “dhīr eva hi bhānam” iti | sa cārjanajanyo ‘tiśayaḥ svatvam ity ucya iti mīmāṃsakarahasyam | ataś codganthatā sphuṭaiva | sthūlatvaṃ ca dhanalābhaśiṣyopāstyādisādhane ācāryaniyoge paśuvṛṣṭyādisādhane citrākārīryādiniyoge ca vyabhicārāt | na ca yathāhavanīye laukikajvalanarūpeṇa laukikapākasādhanatā nālaukikasaṃskāranicayayuktarūpeṇety anaikāntikatāparihāraḥ suvacaḥ tathāpūrve ‘py anaikāntikatāpattiparihāraḥ suvaca iti vācyam | tasmin sarvātmanaivālaukike ‘pūrve laukikālaukikarūpabhedakalpanasyāśakyatvāt | yat tūnnītaṃ citrāpūrvakāryatayā śāstraikasamadhigamyam | na ca tadrūpeṇa paśvādisādhanam api tu kṛtaṃ sat siddhatayā, na ca tadrūpaṃ śāstraikasamadhigamyaṃ kāryakāraṇānumānavedyatvāt, ato yūpāhavanīyāditulyatayā nānaikāntikateti pratipādayituṃ śakyata iti |
tad asat, yūpāhavanīyādau laukike rūpe niyamavaiyarthyād [398] alaukika(tva)m api kalpayituṃ śakyate | sarvātmanaivālaukikarūpe madhye laukika(tva)rūpakalpanā hetvābhāvān na śakyeti | utpannasyāpūrvasya kāryakāraṇānumānavedya(tva)rūpam api nāsti tadapekṣāyā anutthānāt | utthitau vā vedavedyam apūrvam utpannaṃ paśvādeḥ phalasyotpatteḥ iti vedavedyatvākārollekhenaivānumānam apūrvāṃśaṃ spṛśatīti na kācit kṣatiḥ |
yathoktaṃ guruṇa navamādye nibandhane – “vedavedyatvādārasya laukikatve ‘pi vedavedyaṃ svarūpam alaukikam eva” iti asya granthasyābhiprāyam āha nāthaḥ – liṅvācyam apūrvam ity evam ākāraṃ jñānaṃ liṅpṛṣṭhabhāvena liṅvedyatvākārollekhenaiva vāpūrvaṃ spṛśatīti naitādṛśajñānavedyatve vedaikavedyatālakṣaṇāpūrvatā vihatiḥ | na hi vedavedyatākārollekhena jāyamānasya yūpāhavanīyāder vedaikavedyatāvihatir iti sthūlatvaṃ hetor iti siddham | nānvācāryakaṃ laukikam adhyāpanakartry ācāryaśabdaprayogāt | ato liṅvātvam abhimataṃ dūranirastaṃ syāt | yathoktaṃ vṛddhaiḥ |
loke kvāpy aprayuktatvād vidhisādhye prayogataḥ |
alaukikārthaṃ yūpādipadaṃ kāmaṃ prasidhyatu ||
nālaukikārtham ācāryapadam etad dvayātyayāt |
mukhaṃ gurumatasyāddhaṃ (-ārdhaṃ) khaḍitaṃ (kim u) kila paṇḍitaiḥ ||
maivam,
upanīya tu yaḥ śiṣyaṃ vedam adhyāpayet dvijaḥ |
sakalaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca tam ācāryaṃ pracakṣate || iti |
smṛtir abhiyuktaśrutismṛtiprayuktācāryapadārthasaṃśayanivṛttyarthaṃ praṇīteti tāvad āstheyam | sā ca nyāyato vivicyamānā yādṛśe ‘rthe paryavasyati tādṛśe smṛtes tātparyaṃ vācyam | tatra yady api [399] “kriyayogaḥ śabdārthaḥ” iti bhāti | tathāpi na tasyāḥ kriyāmātraparatvṃ | (?) tathātve lakṣaṇānvyākhyānamātraparatvaṃ syāt | na caitat saṃbhavati saṃśayanivṛttyartham “upanīya tu yaḥ śiṣyaṃ dvijo ‘dhyāpati śrutam” iti lakṣaṇam avakṣyat | tadanupayoginī vidhyuktir ayuktā satī vaktur anāptatām āpādayeta | vidheyasyaiva tu lakṣaṇatve vivakṣite vidhyuktir āñjasīti vidhisiddham evādhyāpanarūpam ardhaṃ yas tv ityādinā nirdiśya lakṣaṇaṃ kṛtam upeyam | na ca vākyabhedāpatter ubhayavivakṣānupapattiḥ, vaktṛtantre vākye vākyabhedasya codanāsūtre arthapadaṃ prayuñjānena sūtrakāreṇa doṣatayā khyāpitatvā | tuśabda upādhyāyalakṣaṇāt kevalādhyāpayād vyāvṛttipratipādakaḥ |
nanv adhyāpanasya vidheyatvam eva nāsti anyataḥ prāptatvād iti can,
maivam, anyataḥ prāptivādi praptavyaḥ (?) | kim adhyayanavidhitaḥ prāptir āhosvid vṛttita iti | nādyaḥ adhyayanavidher niradhikāratayā svaviṣayānuṣṭhāne ‘py aśaktatvam uktaṃ śāstrādau guruṇā tat tata evāvadhāryam | na dvitīyaḥ vṛttyarthatāpy adhyāpanasyācāryakadvārikaiveti nācāryakotpattyarthatayā tadvidhiṃ vahantīti tenānyataḥ prāptyabhāvād vidhyabhāve ‘dhyāpanasyāprāptasya lakṣaṇatvenākhyānam ayuktam ity adhyāpanasya vidheyatvasiddheḥ | yad vā smṛteś ca lokavyavahāramātraphalānarhatvād ācāryapadārthanirṇayāpekṣavaradānavidhyanuṣṭhānaphalataivopeyā | tatra “ācāryāya varo deyaḥ” iti śraute varadānavidhau ācāryaśabdārthaḥ sampradānatayopadiśyate | na ca saṃpradānāvasthāyām adhyāpanakriyāyoga ācāryaśabdārthaḥ, tasyātītatvāt | na ca svarūpamātram arthaḥ, tasya vidhiṃ vināpi lābhe vidhyānarthakyāt | na ca bhūtapūrvakriyāyogāvasthālakṣaṇā  śrutismṛtiprayuktā | ācāryapadārthatatvanirṇayārthaṃ [400] prayuktāyā abhiyuktaśruter jaghanyārthaprakāśanaparatvasvīkārasyānucitatvāt | anavagatasya ca tadupalakṣyatvānarhatvāt | pañjarasthaḥ siṃha ity atra pañjaraopalakṣyasiṃhatvākāravad iha bhūtakriyāyogopalakṣaṇasyākāraviśeṣasyābhāvāt | ataḥ kriyāyogātirikta saṃpradānāvasthānuvṛttyarham adṛṣṭam ācāryaśabdavācyam iti varadānavidhibalād avagate tadutpādakakriyāprekṣāyāṃ smṛter lakṣaṇabhūtakriyāvidhiparatatvam āśriyate | tathā ca tadanuguṇā mūlaśrutir upanīyādhyāpayed ity anumeyā |
nanv ācāryakaraṇavidhiprayuktatvam adhyayanasya sādhayitum ācāryatvakāmo ‘dhyāpayed ity ādhikāravidhirūpavedānumānaṃ (?) sādhayitavyam | kim ity upanīyādhyāpayed ity ācāryakasya liṅarthatvena viniyogamātravedānumānam iti cen,
maivam | varadānavidhyanupraviṣṭācāryalakṣaṇānvākhyānamātraparāyāḥ smṛtes tadapekṣitālaukikācāryapadārthotpādakakriyāviniyogamātropalakṣaṇatā svīkāryā | na tadviṣyādhikāravidhiparatā gauravāpatteḥ, apekṣābhāvāc ca | ācāryatvaṃ nāma ācāryaśabdapravṛttinimittam, tvatalāder (?) niṣkṛṣṭapravṛttinimittavacanasvabhāvatvāt | tac cācāryatvam upanayanāṅgakādhyāpanasādhyam adṛṣṭam iti tanmātram eva vidhir anujānātu na tato ‘tiriktam iti varadānavidhyapekṣāpūraṇārthaṃ viniyogakriyāvidhiparatvamātram apekṣitaṃ smṛteḥ | na tato ‘tiriktam adhikāraviṣayāpūrvaphalam | ataś cāpekṣābhāvo gauravaṃ ca spaṣṭam | kiṃ cācāryakasya sukhaduḥkhanivṛttyor anyataratvābhāvena svargāditulyanaiyogikaphalatvaṃ kalpayituṃ na śakyate | liṅvācyatve tu sukhaduḥkhanivṛttyor anyataratvābhāve ‘pi vedaikagamyatṛtīyaprayojanatvam ācāryasya samastīti pratyakṣavede ‘pi tanmātraparatādhyāpyānādividhisiddhaiveti nānumeye [401] kācid anupapattiḥ |
nanv evaṃ bhavanāthenopanīyādhyāpanenācāryakaṃ bhāvayed iti vacanavyaktir upanyasteti cet,
