bhavanāthasyāyam abhiprāyaḥ – yady api tad adṛṣṭaṃ kriyāphalaṃ liṅartha evety upanīyādhyāpayed ity evaṃrūpaiva pāramārthakī vacanavyaktiḥ | tathāpi tasyādṛṣṭasyācāryakapadābhilapyatayā smṛtisiddhatvāt tena ca rūpeṇa varadānādilābhakaratayādhyāpakābhīṣṭatvāt kāmyatayaivārthāt svaviṣaye ‘dhyāpane adhikārāpādakatā | na nityādiniyogavan niyogatvenaiveti prakaṛayitum adhyāpanasyaupādānikakaraṇānvayavivaraṇārthā vacanavyaktiḥ adhyāpanenācāryakaṃ bhāvayed ity evaṃrūpopanyastā | ata evedṛśīṃ vacanavyaktim upanyasyāha śālikānāthaḥ: “arthād ācāryībubhūṣor adhikāraḥ" iti | ārthikam adhikāralābhaṃ prakaṭayitum eva tadanuguṇā vacanavyaktir upanyastety abhiprāyaḥ |
nanv evaṃ varadānavidhyapekṣitācāryakotpattyarthakriyāviniyogaparatvād vākyasy etadvidhyanuvādatā liṅaḥ prāpnotīty ācāryakasya liṅvācyatvam abhimataṃ na sidhyet | yady api saṃnidhisamāmnānābhāvān na grāhakāvasthavidhyanuvādatā, tathāpy ādānavākyasthaliṅvat prayojakavasthavidhyanuvādatā syād eva |
maivam, ādhānasya hi saṃskārasya jvalanagatatvena liṅvācyatvāsaṃbhavāt liṅaḥ prayojakāpūrvānuvādatā svīkṛtā | adhyāpanajanyasya tv ācāryasaṃskārasyādhyāpayitṛgatatvena liṅvācyatvaṃ saṃbhavaty eveti na liṅo ‘nuvādakatā svīkāryā |
nanv evam apy ācāryakasaṃskārasya sādhikāraviddhyanupraveśitvād ṛtviksaṃskāraval lābhakaratve ‘pi svayam adhikārāpādakatvāsaṃbhavāt nādhyāpane ‘pi prayojakateti adhyayanaprayojakatvam abhimataṃ dūranirastaṃ [402] syāt | anyathā ṛtviksaṃskārasyāpi yājane yajane ‘pi prayojakatā syāt |
maivam, sādhikāravidhyanupraveśino ‘py ācāryakasya varadānādilābhakaratayādhyāpakābhīṣṭasya vastuvṛttyā adhyāpanādhikārapādakatvena prayojakatvasaṃbhavāt | ṛtviksaṃskārasya tu lābhakaratve ‘pi yajamānādhikārādhīnatatkartṛkavaraṇādisaṃpādyatayānyādhīnatvena svecchayā saṃpādayitum aśakyatvāt na svātantryeṇādhikārāpādakateti na prayojakatāsaṃbhava iti bhedaḥ | ataḥ paramārthato vacanavyaktiḥ upanīyādhyāpayed ity evaṃrūpaiva bhavanāsthasyābhipreteti mantavyam | ataś ca laukikārthakriyāsādhanatvādihetor vyabhicāraḥ siddhaḥ |
kecid evaṃ parihāranti – kriyā ārjanādikā sādhanaṃ yasya svatvasya tallaukikārthakriyāsādhanam iti bahuvrīhiḥ | laukikārthakriyāsādhyatvād ity arthaḥ | ato nodgranthatā na vyabhicāraś ca vijñāneśvaramate ‘pīty anusaṃdheyam iti |
tad asat | tatpuruṣasamāsaṃ parityajya jaghanyabahuvrīhisamāsāśrayaṇaṃ paṅkabhiyā palāyamānasyāśrucipatanasamānam iti | evaṃ ca svatvasya laukikatve siddhe “svāmī rikthakrayasaṃvibhāgaparigrahādhigameṣu brāhmaṇasyādhikaṃ labdhaṃ kṣatriyasya vijitaṃ nirviṣṭaṃ vaiśyaśūdrayoḥ” iti gautamīyā dharmāḥ dṛṣṭārthā dhanārjanopāyāḥ | rikthādayas tu pañca sarvasādhāraṇāḥ | rikthaṃ nāma rikthārjanam – pitrādidhane svāmitvāpādakaṃ putrādijanmeti yāvat | tathā ca paitṛkadhanalābhahetutvenoktaṃ gautamenaiva “utpattyaivāyaṃ svāmitvaṃ labhate ity ācāryāḥ” iti | utpattyaiva – mātṛgarbhe śarīrotpattyaivety arthaḥ | ata eva viṣṇuḥ “janmanā svatvam āpadyate” iti | “putrasyaiva na tu putrikāyāḥ” iti bhāruciḥ | saṃvibhāgaḥ – putrādidhanaviśeṣaniṣṭhasvāmitvasaṃpādako [403] vibhāga iti candrikākāraḥ | vijñāneśvaras tu apratibandho dāyo rikthaṃ sapratibandho dāyaḥ saṃvibhāgaḥ iti | riktaśabdasya yady api sapratibandho ‘pi dāyo ‘rthaḥ, “rikthagrāha ṛṇaṃ dāpyaḥ” iti darśanāt | tathāpy apratibandho dāyo vivakṣyate | anyathā vibhāgaśabdapaunaruktyāt | vibhāgaśabdena taduttarakālīnaḥ sapratibandho dāyo lakṣyate | vibhāgasya svatvahetutvābhāvād ity āha | etan na sahante bhārucyādayaḥ – apratibandhe dāye saṃbandhātiriktaṃ janmāprekṣyate, sapratibandhe dāye tu pratibandhābhāvo na kāraṇaṃ tucchatvād iti | parigrahaḥ– kānanādigatajalatṛṇakāṣṭhāder anyenāsvīkṛtasya svīkāraḥ | adhigamo nidhyādir upalabdhaḥ | eteṣu putrādiḥ kretā saṃvibhaktā parigrahītādhigantā vā yathākrameṇa pitrādidhanasya krītasya saṃvibhaktāṃśasya parigṛhītasyādhigatasya ca svāmī bhavati | tathā labdhaṃ pratigṛhītaṃ brāhmaṇasyādhikaṃ prātisvikam ārjanam | evaṃ kṣatriyasya vijitaṃ jayalabdham | evaṃ vaiśyasya nirviṣṭaṃ kṛṣyādibhṛtirūpeṇa yal labdham | śūdrasyāpi nirviṣṭaṃ dvijaśuśrūṣādinā vṛttirūpeṇa yal labdhaṃ prātisvikam ity evam arjananibandhanārthāyā gautamasmṛter artho vijñeyaḥ | ataś cayaṃ dharmasmṛtiḥ sādhuśabdanibandhanārthavyākaraṇādismṛtivat smṛtety arthaḥ |
atredaṃ tattvam – lokaprasiddhān eva svatvopāyān pratigrahādīn anūdya brāhmaṇādivarṇollekhenādṛṣṭārthatayā niyantuṃ gautamena yatnato vacanam ārabdham iti | tathā ca,
yad garhitenārjayanti karmaṇā brāhmaṇa dhanam |
tasyotsargeṇa śudhyanti japyena tapasaiva ca || iti |
śāstraikasamadhigamye svatve garhitenāsatpratigrahavāṇijyādinā [404] labdhasya svatvam eva nāstīti tatputrāṇāṃ tad avibhājyam eva | yadā tu svatvaṃ laukikaṃ tadā asatpratigrahalabdhasyāpi svatvāt tatputrāṇāṃ tad vibhājyam eva | tasyotsargeṇa śudhyantīti prāyaścittam ārjayitur eva | tatputrādīnāṃ tu dāyatvena svatvam iti na teṣāṃ doṣasaṃbandhaḥ,
sapta vittāgamā dharmyā dāyo lābhaḥ krayo jayaḥ |
prayogaḥ karmayogaś ca satpratigraha eva ca ||
iti smaraṇāt | svatvasya laukikatve kvacid eva parasvatvāpattiḥ saṃkalpamātreṇa svatvanivṛttiś ca | kvacit tu mahāpātakenaiva svatvanivṛttiḥ na saṃkalpamātrāt | ataś ca pitāputrasaṃbandho bhartṛbhāryāsaṃbandhaś ca mahāpātakādau nivartata iti prapañcitam lipsāsūtre |
nanu svatvaṃ laukikam astu svāmyaṃ tv alaukikam | yathāha nyāyapūrvakaṃ saṃgrahakāraḥ |
vartate yasya yaddhaste tasya svāmī sa eva na |
anyasvam anyahasteṣu cauryādeḥ kiṃ na vartate |
tasmāc chāstrata eva syāt svāmyaṃ nānubhavād api || iti |
tasmāc chāstraikasamadhigamyaṃ svāmyaṃ na punar mānāntaragamyam ity arthaḥ |
maivam, yasmāt svāmyasvatvayos tulyayogakṣemayor ekataram adhikṛtya sādhite laukikatve dvayor api sādhitam evety avagantavyam | ato yathāgranthaṃ laukikakriyāsādhyatvam eva avyabhicārāt svatvasya laukikatve hetur iti yuktam utpaśyāmaḥ | tathā ca svatvasya laukikatvāt naiyāyika eva svāmyasaṃkramakramo na vācanika iti siddham | tad uktaṃ vijñānayoginā yat tu – “patnī duhitaraḥ” ityādismaraṇaṃ tad api svāmisaṃbandhitayā bahuṣūpaplavamāneṣu [405] vyāmohanivṛttaye pratyāsattitāratamyanyāyanibandhanasya svāmitāsaṃgramakramasya nibandhanam iti |
nanu patnī duhitaranyāyāvalambanena patnyāḥ patyaṃśaharatvaṃ syāt | tarhi avibhaktapatnīnām api “śarīrārdhaṃ smṛtā jāyā” ityādivakṣyamāṇanyāyasya tulyatvāt patnyaṃśaharatvaṃ syād iti cet,
maivam | vibhaktapatnīnāṃ pratyekaniyatadravyasadbhāvāt tatraiva patnīduhitaranyāyaḥ | avibhaktapatnīnāṃ tu patyur niyatadravyābhāvāt samuditadravya patyaṃśagrāhitvasyāśakyatvāt patnīduhitaranyāyo vibhaktaviṣaya iti dhyeyam |
nanu yady evaṃ syāt tadā avibhaktānāṃ sarveṣāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ svaryātānām aṃśagrāhitvaṃ tatpatnīnāṃ na syāt | tathā smaryate – “avibhaktapatnīnāṃ taddāyaharatvam” iti sarveṣāṃ bhrātṝṇām abhāve iti śeṣaḥ |
atrocyate – avibhaktapatnīnāṃ dāyasya pratyekaparyavasitatvena niyatatvasiddher avibhaktatve ‘pi vibhaktavan nyāyasya suprasaratvāt patnīnām evāṃśaharatvam | tatra duhitṛmatpatnyaduhitṛmatpatnīviveko na kartavya iti vaiṣṇavaṃ matam | patnīduhitara ityādau patnīduhitaretyasyaikadeśasyānukaraṇam | patnīduhitaraś cety atra pratyāsattitāratamyanyāyaṃ sūcayati manuḥ |
pitā hared aputrasya rikthaṃ bhrātara eva ca | iti |
tatra kramo na vivakṣitaḥ vāśabdaprayogāt | asya tātparyārthaṃ saṃgrahakāra āha |
a(vi)śeṣātmajahīnasya mṛtasya dhanino dhanam |
kenedānīṃ grahītavyam ity etad adhunocyate ||
asyārthaḥ – mukhyagauṇaputrahīnasya dhanavato mṛtasya dhanam idānīṃ tanmaraṇānantaraṃ kena hartavyam ity ākāṅkṣāyāṃ pitrādinā hartavyam [406] ity etat adhunā pitrādyapekṣayā bahuvidhopakārakāsannajanābhāve manunocyate iti | ata eva pitrādibhyo gauṇaputrāṇām āsannatvaṃ jñātvā saṃgrahakāreṇa “pitā hared aputrasya” ity asya aśeṣatmajahīnasyeti tātparyam uktaṃ tad anavadyam eva | kiṃ tu gauṇaputrāṇāṃ dṛṣṭādṛṣṭopakārakatvena pitrādyapekṣayā agresaratvāt tadapekṣayāsannataratvam | tathā patnyā api dṛṣṭādṛṣṭopakāreṇa śrutismṛtyādiparyālocanayā pitrādyapekṣayā agresaratvāt tadapekṣayāsannataratvam asti | ataś ca patnyā apy abhāve “pitā hared aputrasya” ity etan manunocyata ity evaṃ tātparyam ūhyate | ata eva gauṇapurābhāve dṛṣṭādṛṣṭopakārakatvalakṣaṇasaṃbandhenānyāpekṣayā patnyāḥ pratyāsannatvam abhidhāya bṛhaspatinā tiṣṭhatsv api pitrādiṣu patnyā eva patidhanabhāgitvaṃ darśitam |
āmnāye smṛtitantre ca lokācāre ca sūribhiḥ |
śarīrārdhaṃ smṛtā jāyā puṇyāpuṇyaphale samā ||
yasya noparatā bhāryā dehārdhaṃ tasya jīvati |
jīvaty ardhaśarīre ‘pi katham anyaḥ samāpnuyāt ||
kulyeṣu vidyamāneṣu pitṛbhrātṛsanābhiṣu |
asutasya pramītasya tatpatnī bhāgahāriṇī ||
atra dvitīyārdhena “śarīrārdhaṃ smṛtā” ityādinā dṛṣṭādṛṣṭopakārasaṃpādane pitrādibhyaḥ patnyāḥ pratyāsannatvam abhihitam | avayavārthas tu – āmāne vede | “ardho vā eṣa ātmano yat patnī” ityādau ātmano dehārdhasyety arthaḥ | smṛtitantre dharmaśāsre |
pataty ardhaśarīreṇa yasya bhāryā surāṃ pibet |
patitārdhaśarīrasya niṣkṛtir na vidhīyate || [407]
ityādau | lokācāre lokācārānumatārthaśāstra ity arthaḥ |
śarīrārdhamayīṃ jāyāṃ ko vihāsyati paṇḍitaḥ |
ity evamādau puṇyāpuṇyaphale karmaṇi sahādhikārāt | asutasya – mukhyagauṇasutahīnasya | patnī – yajñādhikārāpādakapraśastabrāhmādivivāhasaṃskṛtā “patyur no yajñasaṃyoge” iti pāṇinismṛteḥ | tena krītā bhāryā patnīpadena vyāvartitā, tasyāḥ patnītvāyogāt | tathā ca smṛtyantaram |
krayakrītā tu yā nārī na sā patny abhidhīyate |
na sā daive na sā pitrye dāsīṃ tāṃ kavayo viduḥ |
patnītvābhāve kevaladṛṣṭopakārakatvam eva nādṛṣṭopakārakatvaṃ striyā iti darśayituṃ dāsīṃ vidur ity uktam | “atra kecit vivāhasaṃskṛtā jāyā patnīty ucyate” iti candrikākāroktam ity anupapannam | vivāhasaṃskārasya patnītvopapādakatvābhāvāt | patnītvaṃ nāma patibhāryāsaṃbandhavyatirekeṇa na kiṃcid asti | tac cārjyārjakarūpakriyāgarbhaḥ saṃbandhaḥ | sa ca laukika eva | ata eva mahāpātakādau bhāryātvasya nivṛttiḥ | bhūtapūrvagatyā bhāryābhimānaḥ | na ca vivāhe mantraniyamo bhāryātvotpādakaḥ, tasya vaidhadānasiddhatvād ity uktaṃ lipsāsūtre guruṇety āhuḥ |
tan na, guruṇā tu patnīgataṃ svatvam eva laukikam ity uktaṃ na patnītvam, svatvapatnītvayor bhedāt, yajñasaṃyogāt patnī, svāmisaṃbandhāt svam iti | mahāpātakādau bhāryātvasyāpi viyoga iti gurugranthasyāyam arthaḥ – bhāryātvaṃ nāma svatvam, na tu patnītam, anyathā prāyascitte kṛte punaḥ patnītvaṃ na syād ity uktaṃ bhārucinā | ayam evābhiprāyaḥ candrikākārādīnām iti suṣṭhūkaṃ brāhmādivivāhe saṃskṛtā [408] jāyā patnīty ucyata iti | ataś ca patyuḥ pitrye karmaṇy api bhrātrādyapekṣayā agresaratvaṃ patnyā evāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
putrābhāve tu patnī syāt patnyabhāve tu sodaraḥ | iti |
piṇḍadāna iti śeṣaḥ | atra vṛddhamanuḥ |
aputrā śayanaṃ bhartuḥ pālayantī vrate sthitā |
patny eva dadyāt tat piṇḍaṃ kṛtsnam aṃśaṃ hareta ca ||
uttarārdhe pāṭhakramād arthakramo ‘vagantavyaḥ | patnī bhartraṃśaṃ pūrvaṃ labhate paścāt piṇḍaṃ dadyāt | na punas tasyāṃ satyāṃ bhrātrādir ity arthaḥ |
piṇḍado ‘ṃśaharaś caiṣāṃ pūrvābhāve paraḥ paraḥ |
ity atrāpy evam eva vyākhyeyam, aṃśaharaṇasyaiva piṇḍadānanimittatvenoktatvāt | śayanaṃ pālayantī – susaṃyatey arthaḥ | kṛtsnam aṃśaṃ labheteti kṛtsnaśabdārtham āha prajāpatiḥ |
jaṅgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ hema raupyadhānyarasāmbaram |
ādāya dāpayec chrāddhaṃ māsaṣāṇmāsikadikam ||
pitṛvyagurudauhitrān bhartṛśvaśuramātulān |
pūjayet kavyapūrtābhyāṃ vṛddhānāthātithīṃs tathā ||
rūpyam – trapusīmādikam | kavyam – pitrarthasaṃkalpitam annam | pūrtaṃ khātādi | etad uktaṃ bhavati – sthāvareṇa sahitaṃ kṛtsnam aṃśam ādāya dhanasādhyas tryadhikāraśrāddhapūrtādikaṃ patyur ātmanaś ca śreyaḥsādhanaṃ dharmajātaṃ patynyā gṛhītadhanānusāreṇa sādhyam iti | yat tu bṛhaspatinoktam,
yad vibhaktadhanaṃ kiṃcid ādhyādi vividhaṃ smṛtam |
taj jāyā sthāvaraṃ muktvā labheta mṛtabhartṛkā || [409]
yat kiṃcid ādhyādi vividhaṃ sthāvarajaṅgamātmakaṃ bhartṛsvāmikaṃ smṛtaṃ tat sarvaṃ vibhaktaviṣaye patnī labhata ity arthaḥ | vibhaktagrahaṇād avibhaktaviṣaye tu sahavāsina eva bhrātrādayo mṛtasyāputrasya dhanaṃ labherann iti gamyate | etat pūrvam eva sayuktikam uktam apy atra jñāpitam | taj jāyā sthāvaraṃ muktvety etat duhitṛrahitapatnīviṣayam | patnīmātraviṣayatve,
jaṅgamaṃ sthāvaraṃ hemakupyadhānyarasāmbaram |
ādāya dāpayec chrāddhaṃ māsaṣāṇmasikādikam ||
iti pūrvoktavacanavirodhaḥ syād iti candrikākāraḥ | atra candrikākārasyāyam āśayaḥ – duhitṛrahitaduhitṛsahitapatnyoḥ saṃnipāte duhitṛsahitāyā eva patnyāḥ sthāvaram, na duhitṛrahitāyāḥ | yadā duhitṛrahitaiva patnī syāt tadā tasyā eva sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ ca nānyasyāḥ duhitṛsahitāyā mātrādeḥ | tasyās tu patnyapekṣayā bahiraṅgatvasyoktatvād iti | na ca tadvirodhaparihārāya vibhaktapatnyaṃ śaviṣayaṃ(sa-?) cedaṃ vacanam astv iti vācyam | yata evaṃprakārāṃ vyavasthāṃ nirākartum āha sa eva |
vṛttasthāpi kṛte ‘py aṃśe na strī sthāvaram arhati | iti |
santnavṛttibhūtasthāvarārhatā santānaśālitvāyatteti tacchūnyā strī vṛttasthāpi vibhaktaviṣaye ‘pi sthāvaraṃ nārhatīti vacanārthaḥ |
mṛte bhartari bhartraṃśaṃ labhate kulapālikā |
yāvajjīvaṃ hi tatsvāmyaṃ dānādhamanavikraye || [410]
ādhamanam ādhiḥ | strīṇāṃ tu vibhaktadaśāyām api bharaṇam eva | saṃbhogārtham ānītā strīty ucyate |
krayakrītā tu yā nārī saṃbhogārthaṃ sutārthinā |
gṛhītā vānyadīyā vā saiva strī parikīrtyate ||
anyadīyā – parakāntā “yoṣidgrāha ṛṇaṃ dāpyaḥ” ity atra yoṣicchabdārthatayā nirūpitā | tasyāḥ sriyā nāṃśabhāgitvam ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
svaryāte svāmini strī tu grāsācchādanabhāginī |
avibhaktadhanāṃśaṃ tu prāpnoty āmaraṇāntikam ||
uttarārdhaḥ patnīviṣayaḥ | avibhaktāyāḥ patnyā apy aṃśo ‘sti | yathāha sa eva |
aputrā śayanaṃ bhartuḥ pālayantī vrate sthitā |
bhuñjītāmaraṇaṃ kṣāntā dāyādā ūrdhvam āpnuyuḥ ||
etac ca bharaṇākṣameṣu śvaśurādiṣu vijñeyam | yathāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
pradadyād vatsare piṇḍaṃ kṣetrāṃśaṃ vā yadṛcchayā | iti |
jīvanamātrasādhanaṃ dravyaṃ piṇḍaśabdārthaḥ | jīvanamātrasādhanasya dravyasya svalpām iyattām āha nāradaḥ |
āḍhakāṃs tu catustriṃśac catvāriṃśat paṇāṃs tathā |
pratisaṃvatsaraṃ sādhvī labheta mṛtabhartṛkā ||
vatsare vatsare iti prativatsaram | āḍhako ‘ṣṭonadviśataprasṛtiparimitadhānyacayaḥ | paṇaḥ kārṣāpaṇaḥ vyāvahārikaniṣkāśītibhāga ity apare | strībhyo yad dattaṃ tat paripālanīyaṃ ity āha kātyāyanaḥ |
sthāvarāj jīvanaṃ strībhyo yad dattaṃ śvaśureṇa tu |
na tac chakyam apākartum uttaraiḥ śvaśure mṛte || [411]
śvaśuragrahaṇaṃ bharaṇakāriṇām upalakṣaṇārtham | sthāvaragrahaṇaṃ ca dhanagrahaṇasyopalakṣaṇārtham | tena dhanam api jīvanārthaṃ strībhyo yad dattaṃ tan nāpākartum itaraiḥ śakyam ity avagantavyam | tad apavādam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
bhoktum arhati kḷptāṃśaṃ guruśuśrūṣaṇe ratā |
na kuryād yadi śuśrūṣāṃ celapiṇḍe niyojayet ||
kḷptāṃśam apahṛtyeti śeṣaḥ | atra yāvajjīvabharaṇavṛttim āha viṣṇuḥ: “pratisaṃvatsaraṃ catvāriṃśatpaṇāś caturviṃśadāḍhakāḥ | atha vā yāvajjīvaṃ śataṃ kārṣāpaṇās tadardhaṃ vā” iti | akāryakāriṇīnām api kḷptāṃśaharaṇaṃ karyam ity āha sa eva: “nirmaryādānāṃ kḷptāṃśaharaṇaṃ kāryam” iti | nirmaryādāḥ vyabhicāriṇyaḥ | tatbharaṇam | yat tu manunoktam,
evam eva vidhiṃ kuryād yoṣitsu patitāsv api |
vastrānnam āsāṃ deyaṃ tu vaseyuś ca gṛhāntike || iti |
tad bhartṛkartṛkaviṣayam | evaṃ strīṇāṃ yad bharaṇapratipādakaṃ vacanajātaṃ tad avibhaktapatnīviṣayaṃ ca veditavyam | yac ca,
aputrā śayanaṃ bhartuḥ pālayantī vrate sthitā |
bhuñjītāmaraṇaṃ kṣāntā dāyādā ūrdhvam āpnuyuḥ || iti |
sthāvaraṃ jaṅgamaṃ caiva kupyadhānyasāmbaram |
mṛte bhartari bhartraṃśaṃ labhate kulapālikā ||
yāvijjīvaṃ tu tatsvāmyaṃ dānādhamanavikraye | [414]
nanv evaṃ svayam eva pitā śrāddhadāne dṛṣṭopakāraka iti duhitrapekṣayā āsannataratāt patnyabhāve duhituḥ kathaṃ dhanagrahaṇam iti ven,
maivam,
tasyām atmani tiṣṭhantyāṃ katham anyo dhanaṃ haret |
ity anenaivoktatvāt | tathā hi yady api dṛṣṭopakārasaṃbandhena pituḥ sakāśād vyavahitā, tathā śarīrasaṃbandhenāvyavahitety ubhayathā duhitaivāgresarī |
evaṃ tarhi duhitrabhāve “pitā haret” ity asyāvasaraḥ syāt,
maivam, adhunāpi na tasyāvasaraḥ syāt | duhitrabhāve dauhitrasya tatkoṭitvena pitrādyapekṣayāsannatvāt,
aputrapautrasantāne dauhitrā dahan āpnuyuḥ |
pūrveṣāṃ tu svadhākāre pautrā dauhitrakā matāḥ ||
iti viṣṇusmaraṇāc ca | atra dhāreśvara-devasvāmi-devarāta-śrīkarādayo duhitṛgāmidhanam iti vidhāyakaṃ vacanajātaṃ putrikāviṣayam evety āhuḥ | tanmataṃ dūṣayati candrikākāraḥ | tathā ca candrikākāragranthaḥ: “dhāreśvara-devasvāmi-devarātamataṃ pratitantrasiddhāntānabhijñānonmādakalpitaṃ nirastaṃ veditavyam” iti | pratitantrasiddhāntaḥ – svasiddhāntaḥ | vijñānayogīśvareṇāpi dūṣitam | yathā mitākṣarāgranthaḥ: “na caitat putrikāviṣayam, “tatsamaḥ putrikāsutaḥ” iti putrikāyās tatsutasya ca aurasasamatvena putraprakaraṇe ‘bhidhānt” iti | dhāreśvarādīnām ayam abhisaṃdhiḥ – gauṇaputrāṇām aurasena saha daśavidhatvam eva | putrikāsutasya svayaṃkṛtasutasya svamatikalpitatvāt putratvaṃ nāsti | kiṃ tu dāyabhāktvamātram | ataś caiva putrikaiva sutaḥ putrikāsuta iti putrikāyāḥ sutāḥ putrikāsuta iti samāsadvayāṅgīkāre ‘pi pitrikāyāḥ sutasya pautratulyatvaṃ putrikārūpasutasya tu [415] putratulyatayā patnyā apy antaraṅgatayā pūrvaṃ putrikāyā dhanaprāptiḥ | putravat tadanantaraṃ patnyā iti nyāyapratipādanārthaṃ patnī duhitaraś cet cakāraḥ | duhitara iti bahuvacanam aputrikā duhitṛputrikākaraṇaduhitṛputrikāsutajananīrūpaduhitṝṇām upasaṃgrahārtham | ataś ca,
aśeṣātmajahīnasya mṛtasya dhanino dhanam |
ityādi saṃgrahakārādivacaneṣv aśeṣātmajapadena putrikā anupasaṃgrahāt tadarthaṃ putrikāsuta iti vacane tu aurasād ardhadhanabhāktvam api siddham iti | ataś ca tridhānāṃ putrikāṇāṃ madhe putrikākaraṇaduhituḥ samuditadravasyārtham | itarayos tu duhitror ardham iti | etac ca dhāreśvarādīnām abhimatam iti yogyam ity ullikhitam |
tan na saṃgacchate – putrikāyāḥ suta iti putrikaiva suta ity atra sutaśabdasya dāyabhāktvamātreṇa gauṇārthatā na yujyate, pitur aurdhvadaihikādiputrakṛtye aurasābhāve tasyaivādhikārasmaraṇāt | gauṇasutatvaṃ nāma anaurastvam iti | ataś ca putrasamānayogakṣematayā pūrvam eva dāyagrahaṇasya pratipādanād ity alam ativistareṇa |
kecit tu patnīduhitaraś caivety evakāreṇa patnyā yajñasaṃyogavatyā duhitara eva, na striyāḥ | cakārāt patyā apīty āhuḥ |
tan na, tathā ca sati dhanasya duhitṛgāmitvānantaraṃ patnyabhigāmitvaṃ syāt tathā ca pūrvoktanayavirodhaḥ syād iti | atra caśabdād duhitrabhāve dauhitro dhanabhāk tatkoṭitvāt | tathāha viṣṇuḥ |
aputrapautrasantāne dauhitrā dhanam āpnuyuḥ |
pūrveṣāṃ tu svadhākāre pautrā dauhitrkā matāḥ || iti |
manur api |
akṛtā vā kṛtā vāpi yaṃ vindet sadṛśaṃ sutam |
[416]        pautrī mātāmahas tena dadyāt piṇḍaṃ dhanaṃ haret || iti |
tadabhāve pitarau – mātāpitarau dhanabhājau | mātṛśabdasya dvandve pūrvaṃ nipātād dvandvabdhakatvād ekaśeṣasya | vigrahavākyasthamātṛśabdasya pūrvasmaraṇāt pāṭhakramād evārthakramāvagamāt | dhanasaṃbandhe ‘pi kramāpekṣāyāṃ pratītikramānurodhenaiva prathamaṃ mātā dhanabhāk | tadabhāve piteti gamyate | kiṃ ca pitā putrāntareṣv api sādhāraṇaḥ | mātā tu na sādhāraṇā iti pratyāsattyatiśayaḥ |
anantaraṃ sapiṇḍādyaḥ tasya tasya dhanaṃ haret |
iti vacanān mātur eva prathamaṃ dhanagrahaṇam | mātāpitor mātur eva pratyāsattyatiśayād dhanagrahaṇaṃ yuktam | tadabhāve pitā dhanabhāg iti vijñāneśvaramatam | candrikārāreṇa tu pitur eva prathamaṃ dhanagrahaṇādhikāraḥ pratipādyate, tadabhāve pitṛgāmi tadabhāve mātṛgāmīti viṣṇusmṛteḥ | candrikākāramatād vijñānayogimatam eva samyak, nyāyamūlatayā pratipādanāt | anena śrīkaroktaṃ pitror vibhajya dhanagrahaṇaṃ nirastaṃ veditavyam |
pitrabhāve bhrātaro dhanabhājaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
pitā hared aputrasya rikthaṃ bhrātara eva vā | iti |
yat punar dhāreśvareṇoktam,
anapatyasya putrasya mātā dāyam avāpnuyāt |
mātary api ca vṛttāyāṃ pitur mātā dhanaṃ haret ||
iti manuvacanāt, jīvaty api pitari mātari ca vṛttāyāṃ pitur eva tu mātā pitāmahī dhanaṃ haret | na pitā, yataḥ pitṛgṛhītaṃ dhanaṃ vijātīyeṣv api putreṣu gacchati | pitāmahīgṛhītaṃ tu sajātīyeṣv eva gacchatīti pitāmahyena gṛhṇātīti | etad vijñānayogī na [417] manyate, vijātīyaputrāṇām api dhanagrahaṇasyoktatvāt, “catustridvyekabhāgāḥ syuḥ” ityādinā | bhrātṛṣv api sodarāḥ prathamaṃ gṛhṇīyuḥ, bhinnodarāṇāṃ mātrā viprakarṣāt | sodarāṇām abhāve bhinnodarā dhanabhājaḥ | bhrātṝṇām abhāve tatputrāḥ pitṛkrameṇa dhanabhājaḥ, pitṛto bhāgakalpaneti vacanāt | bhrātṛputrāṇām abhāve gotrajā dhanabhājaḥ pitāmahī sapiṇḍāḥ samānodakāś ca | tatra pitāmahī prathamaṃ dhanabhāk |
mātary api ca vṛttāyāṃ pitur mātā dhanaṃ haret |
iti mātranantaraṃ pitāmahyā dhanagrahaṇe prāpte pitrādīnāṃ bhrāgṛsutaparyantānāṃ baddhakramatvena madhye anupraveśābhāvāt, pitur mātā dhanaṃ hared ity asya vacanasya dhanagrahaṇādhikāraprāptimātrāparatvāt, utkarṣe tatsutānantaraṃ pitāmahī gṛhṇāti avirodhād iti vijñānayogimatam | etan na sahate candrikākāraḥ,
mātary api ca vṛttāyāṃ pitur mātā dhanaṃ haret |
iti kramasyokteḥ, “aputradhanaṃ patnyabhigāmi tadabhāve mātāpitarau gṛhṇīyātām, tadabhāve pitur mātā bhrātaraḥ sapiṇḍāḥ” iti viṣṇusmṛteś ca mātrānantaraṃ pitāmahyā dhanaṃ bhāktvam iti | atra vijñānayogimatam eva samyag ity āhuḥ | pitāmayāś cābhāve samānagotrajāḥ sapiṇḍāḥ pitāmahādayo dhanabhājaḥ, bhinnagotrāṇāṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ bandhuśabdena grahaṇāt | tatra pitṛsantānābhāve pitāmahaḥ pitṛvyās tatputrāś ca krameṇa dhanabhājaḥ | pitāmahasantānābhāve tatprapitāmahī pitāmahaḥ tatputrāḥ tatsūnavaś ceti | evam ā saptamāt sagorāṇāṃ sapiṇḍānāṃ dhanagrahaṇaṃ veditavyam |
anantaraḥ sapiṇḍādyas tasya tasya dhanaṃ haret | [418]
iti smaraṇāt, teṣām abhāve samānodakā dhanabhājaḥ | te ca sapiṇḍānām upari sapta veditavyāḥ. janmanāmajñānāvadhikā vā | yad āha manuḥ |
sapiṇḍatā tu puruṣe saptame vinivartate |
samānodakabhāvas tu nivarteta caturdaśāt |
janmanāmasmṛter eke tatparaṃ gotram ucyate || iti |
anena saṃgrahakāroktakramaḥ,
tāsāṃ duhitrabhāve ‘pi mātā dhanam avāpnuyāt |
vidyamāne ‘pi pitari sapatnīsutasantatau ||
tādṛṅmātur abhāve ‘pi pitur mātā hared dhanam |
vidyamāne ‘pi pitari kṣatriyāsutasantatau ||
pitāmahyā abhāve ‘pi pitā dhanam avāpnuyāt ||
iti nirasto veditavyaḥ | dhāreśvarotprekṣitanyāyamūlatvād asya kramasya viśvarūpādibhir evādhyāhārādīn avalambya dūṣitatvān nāsmābhir dūṣyate, pūrvoktanyāyavirodhāc ca | yat tu tenaivoktam,
sodaryāḥ santy asodaryā bhrātaro dvividhā yadi |
vidyamāne ‘py asodarye sodaryā eva bhāginaḥ ||
iti, tat samyaṅnyāyamūlatvād ādaraṇīyam | bāndhavās tv āsannatarakrameṇaiva smṛtyantare darśitāḥ |
ātmapitṛṣvasuḥ putrā ātmamātṛṣvasuḥ sutāḥ |
ātmamātulaputrāś ca vijñeyā ātmabāndhavāḥ ||
pituḥ pitṛṣvasuḥ putrāḥ pitur mātṛṣvasuḥ sutāḥ |
pitur mātulaputrāś ca vijñeyāḥ pitṛbāndhavāḥ ||
mātuḥ pitṛṣvasuḥ putrāḥ mātur mātṛṣvasuḥ sutāḥ|
māturmātulaputrāś ca vijñeyā mātṛbāndhavāḥ || [419] iti |
eteṣāṃ gotrajābhāve dahasaṃbandhaḥ | tatra cāntaraṅgatvāt prathamam ātmabāndhavā dhanabhājaḥ, tadabhāve pitṛbāndhavāḥ, tadabhāve mātṛbāndhavā iti kramo veditavyaḥ | na cātra pituḥ sakāśān mātur evābhyarhitatvā tadbāndhavānāṃ pitṛbāndhavebhyaḥ pūrvam eva dhanabhāktvam iti vācyam,
pituḥ sakāśād anyebhyo yebhyo mātā garīyasī |
iti smaraṇān mātur evābhyarhitatvaṃ na mātṛbāndhavānām iti | pitṛbāndhavebhyaḥ paścād eva mātṛbāndhavānāṃ dhanabhāktvam iti yuktam utpaśyāmaḥ | tadabhāve ācāryaḥ |
upanīya tu yaḥ śiṣyaṃ vedam adhyāpayed dvijaḥ |
sakalaṃ sarahasyaṃ ca tam ācāryaṃ pracakṣate |
iti anena yonisaṃbandhavad vidyāsaṃbandho ‘pi dhanabhāktvahetur iti sūcitam | tadabhāve śiṣyaḥ, śiṣye ‘pi vidyāsaṃbandhasya vidyamānatvāt | ata eva āpastambaḥ: “putrābhāve yaḥ sapiṇḍaḥ pratyāsannaḥ tadabhāve ācāryaḥ tadabhāve ‘ntevāsī” iti | anenāsmin vacane putrābhāve pratyāsanna ity anena yonisaṃbandho dhanabhāktve hetuḥ tadabhāve ācārya ityādinā vidyāsaṃbandho dhanabhāktve nimittam iti | śiṣyābhāve sabrahmacārī bhrātṛtulyaḥ | tadabhāve brāhmaṇadravyaṃ yaḥ kaścic chrotriyo gṛhṇīyāt “śrotriyā brāhmaṇasyānapatyasya rikthaṃ bhajeran” iti gautamasmaraṇād iti vijñāneśaḥ | bhārucyādayas tu sabrahmacāriṇāṃ bhrātṛtulyatayā tatputrāṇāṃ tatpatnyādīnām abhāve śrotriyabrāhmaṇagāmitvam āhuḥ | asahāyādayas tu yonisaṃbandhānantaraṃ vidyāsaṃbandhād ācāryagāmi tv etad dhanam | [420] tadabhāve ācāryaputragāmi tadabhāve tatpatnīgāmi tadabhāve sabrahmacārigāmi tadabhāve sacchrotriyabrāhmaṇagāmi tadabhāve śtrotriyamātragāmi tadabhāve brāhmaṇamātramāmīty āhuḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
sarveṣām apy abhāve tu brāhmaṇā rikthabhāginaḥ |
traividyāḥ śucayo dāntās tathā dharmo na hīyate || iti |
na kadācid api brāhmaṇadravyaṃ rājā gṛhṇīyāt,
nāhāryaṃ brāhmaṇadravyaṃ rājñā nitym iti smṛteḥ |
iti manuvacanāt | nāradenāpy uktam |
brāhmaṇārthasya tannāśe dāyādaś cen na kaścana |
brāhmaṇāyaiva dātavyam enasvī syān nṛpo ‘nyathā || iti |
yat tu manunoktam,
itareṣāṃ tu varṇānāṃ sarvābhāve haren nṛpaḥ |
iti śūdravyatiriktavarṇānāṃ kṣatriyavaiśyānām eva dhanaṃ sabrahmacāripartyantānām abhāve rājā tad dhanaṃ haret na brāhmaṇa ity arthaḥ | śūdrasya tu bhrātṛparyantābhāve rājagāmi dhanam |
śūdrasyaikodarābhāve rājā tad dhanam āpnuyāt | iti |
evaṃ yonisaṃbandhavidhyāsantatyoḥ prādhānyena yonisantateḥ dāyaharaṇam uktvā vidyāsaṃbandhasantateḥ dāyakramam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
vānaprasthayatibrahmacāriṇāṃ rikthabhāginaḥ |
krameṇācāryasacchiṣyā dharmabhrātrekatīrghinaḥ ||
vānaprasthasya – yateḥ brahmacāriṇaś ca krameṇa pratilomakrameṇa ācāryasacchiṣyadharmabhrātrekatīrthinaḥ rikthasya – dhanasya bhāginaḥ | brahmacārī dvividhaḥ – upakurvāṇaś ca naiṣṭhikaś ca | upakurvāṇasya dhanaṃ [421] mātrādaya eva gṛhṇanti | naiṣṭhikasya dhanam ācārya iti, tatra vidyāsaṃbandhasyaiva yonisaṃbandhād garīyastvāt | yates tu dhanaṃ sacchiṣyā gṛhṇanti | yatiś caturvidhaḥ – kuṭīcakabahūdakahaṃsaparamahaṃsabhedāt | kuṭicakabahūdakahaṃsānām ācāryābhāve śiṣyasya dhanagrahaḥ | paramahaṃsasya tu ācāryābhāvāt śiṣya eva gṛhṇāti | vānaprasthasya dhanaṃ dharmabhātrekatīrthī gṛhṇāti | ekatīrthī ekāśramī | dharmabhrātā pratipanno bhrātā ekaguruśiṣyatayā bhrātṛtvena svīkṛta ity arthaḥ | dharmabhrātā cāsāv ekatīrthī ca iti viśeṣaṇasamāsa iti vijñāneśaḥ | “anaṃśās tv āsramāntaragatāḥ” iti vasiṣṭhasmaraṇaṃ tu icchayā rikthasaṃbandho nāstīty evaṃparam,
ahno māsasya ṣaṇṇāṃ vā tathā saṃvatsarasya vā |
arthasya nicayaṃ kuryāt kṛtam āśvayuje tyajet ||
iti vānaprastham adhikṛtyokteḥ,
kaupīnācchādanārthaṃ ca vāsāṃsi bibhṛyāt tadā |
yogasaṃbhārabhedāṃś ca gṛhṇīyāt pāduke tathā ||
iti yatim adhikṛtyokteḥ | naiṣṭhikasyāpi śarīrayātrārthaṃ vastrādisaṃbandho ‘sty evety etad vibhāgakathanaṃ yuktam evety avagantavyam | atrāha bhagavān lakṣmīdharaḥ: “aśeṣātmajahīnasya mṛtasyāsaṃsṛṣṭino dhanaṃ prathamaṃ patnyabhigāmi tadabhāve duhitṛgāmi tadabhāve cakārād dauhitragāmi tadabhāve mātāpitarau hareyātāṃ tadabhāve bhrātṛgāmi tadabhāve bāndhavo yathākramaṃ gṛhṇīyuḥ” iti | prathamaṃ rikthasyātmavarge patnyādau saṃkramas tadanantaraṃ pitṛvarge pitṛpitṛvyatatputrādau saṃkramas tadanantaraṃ pitāmahavarge āsaptamaṃ saṃkramaḥ tadanantaraṃ samānodakeṣu tadabhāve ātmabandhuṣu tadabhāve pitṛbandhuṣu [422] tadabhāve mātṛbandhuṣu śrotriyānteṣu saṃkrama iti | evaṃ sthite patnyanantaraṃ duhitṛgāmi dhanaṃ tadduhitṝṇāṃ sāpatyānapatyatām anapekṣyaiva saṃkrāmati | tathā ca duhitara iti vacanaṃ sārthakaṃ bhavati | ata eva bhrātara iti bahuvacanam api sāpatyānapatyabhrātṛtvavivekan anapekṣyaiva prayuktam | ata eva patnīty ekavacanam | sāpatyānapatyapatnīdvayasaṃnipāte sāpatyāyāḥ sthāvaṃ nānapatyāyāḥ | ata eva tatsuta ity atrāpi bhrātṛsutānāṃ sāpatyānapatyānāṃ saṃnipāte sāpatyasyaiva rikthagrahaṇam | evam uttaratrāpi | sabrahmacāriṇām iti bahuvacanaṃ tu ekavāsinām iva anekavāsinām apy ādarārtham | tac ca duhitṛsvāmyatiriktaṃ sapratibandham api dauhitrasadbhāve duhitṛgāmitvāvasthāyām apratibandhadāyatām āpadyate | cakāreṇānuktasamuccayārthena samuccitadauhitrasyāpi samakālam eva svatvaprāptir iti jñāpayaty evakāraḥ | tathaiva “bhrātaras tathā” iti tathāśabdaḥ tatsutapadenānvīyamāno yathāśabdasaṃbandho ‘nveti yattador nityasaṃbandhāt |
tathā cāyam anvayaḥ: pitṛgāmitvānantaraṃ dāyasya bhrātṛśabdavācyānāṃ tatsutānām apratibandhenaiva dāyādhikāraḥ | tathā tatputrasadbhāve ‘py apratibandha eva dāya iti | yat tu vijñānayoginoktam – “pitarāv ity atra ekaśeṣamahimnā pūrvaṃ mātṛgāmi dhanaṃ tadabhāve pitṛgāmi” iti, tan na, bahuvacanavad dvivacanasyāpi samaprādhānyasya dyotakatvāt tayos tadrikthe tulyam eva svāmyam | kiṃ tu “pumān puṃso ‘dhike śukle” iti vacanāt, “bījagrahaṇānuvidhāyinam aṃśaṃ gṛhṇīyād iti vaiṣṇavavacanānurodhena tādṛgaṃśagrahaṇasya nyāyyatvāt” iti somaśekharaḥ | tan na | tathā sati pitṛvarge bhrātṛputrānte māsulādiṣu mātravayavānuvṛttes tatraiva dāyagrahaṇaṃ syān na prapitāmahavarge | yathāha bhāruciḥ viṣṇuvacanavyākhyānāvasare (?) [423] : “bījaśabdaḥ piṇḍavācī” iti | atra nirvāpyapiṇḍānvaya eva vivakṣitaḥ | mātuḥ pitrā sāpiṇḍyāt | ubhayagrahaṇāt pitur eva prādhānyaṃ tadabhāve mātur eveti | ayam evāśayaḥ candrikākārodāhṛtaviṣṇavavacanasyāpi dhyeyam |
atredaṃ tattvam — yathā pitṛdravye putrāṇāṃ dāyasvīkāro ‘pratibandhaḥ | putraḥ putratvenaiva pitṛdravyasvāmi duhitrādisthale ‘pi putrasantatisadbhāve tatsvāmitvaṃ tatputratvenaiva | ata uktaṃ tatsutā iti |
nanv atra tacchabdenāputrabhrātaiva parāmṛśyate | na tu bhrātṛmātram iti tatputrasyāpratibandhadāyārhatā nāstīti pratibhātīti cet,
maivam | bhrātṛśabdasya saṃbandhiśabdatvād aputrasya bhrāteti gamyate bhrātṛpadena | na tu tatpadena aputrasaṃbandhaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭabhrātṛparāmarśaḥ tāvatparyantaṃ śabdatātparyābhāvāt | na cātra rikthagrāhitayāpi tadbhrātṛsutānāṃ tatkṛtam ṛṇam apākaraṇīyaṃ syāt tathātve “raktagrāhī ṛṇaṃ dāpyaḥ” it sāmānyena smaraṇaṃ vyāhanyeta | na ca tathāṅgīkāraḥ sarvalokasiddhatvāt tadṛṇāpākaraṇasyeti vācyam, bhūtapūrvagatyā tadīyarikthagrāhitvāt teṣāṃ tadṛṇam apākaraṇīyam | atredam upatiṣṭhate vaiṣṇavaṃ vacanam: “dauhitrāntānām apāye mātāpitarau hareyātām” iti | “patnī duhitaraś caiva” iti yājñavalkīyavacanagatacakārānukṛṣṭānāṃ dauhitrāntānām abhāve mātāpitarau piṇḍānurodhena dhanaṃ hareyātām | dauhitrasaṃkrāntyanantaraṃ tatputragāmy eva dhanaṃ na mātāpitṛgāmi |
atredaṃ tattvam — sapratibandhasthale ‘pi putrasantatisadbhāve ‘pi apratibandha eva dāya iti cakāraivakārābhyāṃ yājñavalkīyavacanatāparyam avadhāryam | “rikthagrāhī ṛṇaṃ [424] dāpyaḥ” iti vacanabalād vāstavaṃ rikthagrāhitvam avalambya tadṛṇasaṃśodhanaṃ nyāyyam | svāmyaṃ tv apratibandham eva | ataś ca dauhitrasyāpratibandho dāyagrahaḥ na duhituḥ | yadi duhitur api syāt dauhitrasaṃkrāntarikthaṃ tadabhāve mātāpitṛgāmi syāt | tac ca sarvavidvadasaṃmatam | santatihīnāyā duhituḥ putrikārūpasantatihīnāyā vā rikthasaṃkrāntau tanmṛtyantaraṃ tadduhiṝr vā tajjñātīn vā rikthaṃ prāpnuyāt | tac ca nyājjyaṃ na bhavati | tathā ca smaryate |
aputrāyāś ca duhituḥ pitṛrikthaṃ haranti te |
pitṛbhrātṛsutādyāś ca gotrajā naiva bāndhavāḥ || iti |
bāndhavā mātulādayaḥ paitṛṣvaseyādayaś ca | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “anapatyarikthaṃ na bāndhavagāmi” iti | ayam arthaḥ – anapatyānāṃ strīṇām anapatyasya vā rikthaṃ sapratibandho dāyaḥ sagotrān jātīn eva saṃkrāmati | na tv anapatyānāṃ putrikāsantatiyuktānāṃ vā duhitṝṇāṃ jñātīn teṣāṃ sagotratvābhāvād iti | ata eva patnīviṣaye hārītaḥ |
aputrā śayanaṃ bhartuḥ pālayantī vrate sthitā |
bhuñjītāmaraṇāt kṣantā dāyādā ūrdhvam āpnuyuḥ || iti |
atra “aputrāyāḥ” iti smṛteḥ,” “anaptyarikthaṃ na bāndhavagāmi” iti smṛter aputrānapatyaśabdayoḥ ekārthatvāṅgīkārād duhitur api putrikāsantatisahitāyās tadduhitranantaraṃ tatputrikāgāmi na bhavati dhanam iti | ata evāha sa eva: “na putrikāgāmi na bāndhavagāmi kiṃ tv aputrasya rikthino jñātayo dhanaṃ hareyuḥ” iti |
atra kecid āhuḥ – “patnī duhitaraś caiva” ity atra cakāreṇānukṛṣṭo dauhitraḥ | evakāreṇāvadhāraṇārthenāvadhāritaḥ | ataś ca dauhitragāmy api dhanaṃ dauhitrābhāve [425] mātāpitṛgāmy eva na tatputragāmy eveti traividyavṛddhavyavahārasiddham | ataś ca duhitṛgāmi sat dauhitram eva saṃkrāntaṃ tatsutasaṃbhave tam eva kaṭākṣīkaroti tad riktham | iyāṃs tu viśeṣaḥ – dauhitrāntānām abhāve dauhitraputraṃ na saṃkrāmati, kiṃ tu tato ‘py antaraṅgatvāt mātāpitarāv evāvalambate riktham |
nanv evaṃ duhitrabhāve tatsaṃkrāntyabhāvād dauhitrasaṃkrānter mātāpitṛsaṃkrānter āsannatvād duhitranantaraṃ mātāpitarāv evāvalambatām iti cet,
maivam | dauhitrasya mātāpitṛto ‘pi pratyāsattir asti |
aputrapautrasantāne dauhitrā dhanam āpnuyuḥ |
pūrveṣāṃ tu svadhākāre pautrā dauhitrakā matāḥ ||
iti viṣṇuvacanam |
akṛtā vā kṛtā vāpi yaṃ vindet sadṛśaṃ sutam |
pautrī mātāmahas tena dadyāt piṇḍaṃ dhanaṃ haret ||
iti manuvacanam | atrākṛtā veti dṛṣṭāntartham | kṛtāyāḥ putrikāyāḥ sutasya putratvena ardhāṃśabhāgitvād apratibandhadāyārhatvasyokteḥ | yathākṛtāyāḥ putreṇa mātāmahaḥ pautrī tathā kṛtāyā api putreṇeti | anena,
kuryān mātāmahaśrāddhaṃ niyamāt putrikāsutaḥ |
ity api parāstam | putrikāsutasya putramadhye pāṭhāt pautra (patnīpautra) eva putrikāsutaśabdavācya iti | anayor mānavavaiṣavayoḥ vacanayoḥ tātparyavarṇane vivadante vṛddhāh | dauhitrasya mātāmahaśrāddhaṃ sakāraṇam eva, pitṛśrāddhavan niṣkāraṇaṃ na bhavatīti | tathā hi – “yata ādadīta sa tasmai śrāddhaṃ kuryāt” iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt, [426]
śrāddhaṃ mātāmahānāṃ tu avaśyaṃ dhanahāriṇā |
dauhitreṇārthaniṣkṛtyai kartavyaṃ vidhivat sadā ||
iti vyāsasmaraṇāt, dhanagrahaṇanibandhanaṃ dauhitrasya mātāmahaśrāddhakaraṇam iti | yat tu pulastyenoktam,
mātuḥ pitaram ārabhya trayo mātāmahāḥ smṛtāḥ |
teṣāṃ tu pitṛvac chrāddhaṃ kuryur duhitṛsūnavaḥ ||
iti, tat tu pitṛśrāddhenānuśiṣṭamātāmahaśrāddhaviṣayam ity avagantavyam | yathoktaṃ pitāmahena |
pitaro yatra pūjyante tatra mātāmahā api |
aviśeṣeṇa kartavyaṃ viśeṣān narakaṃ vrajet || iti |
vyāso ‘pi |
pitur mātāmahāṃś caiva dvijaḥ śrāddhena tarpayet |
anṛṇaḥ syāt pitṝṇāṃ tu yajvanāṃ lokam ṛcchati || iti |
skānde purāṇe ‘pi |
kṛtvā tu paitṛkaṃ śrāddhaṃ pitṛprabhṛtiṣu triṣu |
kuryān mātāmahānāṃ ca tathaivānṛṇyakāraṇāt || iti |
yat tūktam,
kuryān mātāmahānāṃ tu niyamāt putrikāsutaḥ |
ubhayor atha saṃbandhāt kuryāt sa ubhayoḥ kriyāḥ ||
atra kecit – dvividho hi putrikāputraḥ eko mātāmahasaṃbandhaḥ, aparaḥ pitṛmātāmahasaṃbandhaḥ | mātāmahasaṃbandhena mātāmahaśrāddhaṃ kartavyam | ubhayasaṃbandhenobhayoḥ kriyāḥ kāryā iti | ayam āśayaḥ – putrikāyāḥ suta iti ṣaṣṭhisamāsāśrayaṇāt tasya mātāmahaśabdavācyatvāt | . . . . putrikāsuta iti karmadhārayasamāsāśrayaṇe tu [427]
asyāṃ yo jāyate putraḥ sa me putro bhaviṣyati |
iti paribhāṣāvaśāt putrikāputrasya mātāmahasaṃbandhaḥ | itarasya tūbhayasaṃbandhitvam iti |
atrocyate – dauhitrasya mātāmahaśrāddhe putravad adhikāra ity āha viṣṇuḥ: “dauhitrasya mātāmahaśrāddhaṃ niṣkāraṇam” iti | kāraṇam – rikthagrahaṇātmakam | dauhitrasya mātāmahaśrāddhe nityavad adhikāra ity arthaḥ | atra bhāruciḥ – “niṣkāraṇam iti vadatā viṣṇunā bhaṅgyantareṇa samantarakartṝṇāṃ putrādīnāṃ vidyamānatve dauhitrasya kartṛtvasaṃkrāntiḥ” | atrādiśabdena patnī vivakṣitā, yady agnividyāsādhyakarmasu strīṇām adhikāras tatrāpi,
patny eva dadyāt tat piṇḍaṃ kṛtsnam aṃśaṃ labheta ca |
ityevamādivacanabalāt tatra tāsām adhikāraḥ | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “nityavad adhikāro dauhitrāṇāṃ mātāmahaśrāddhe” iti | nityavad iti svārthe vatiḥ | ataś ca,
akṛtā vā kṛtā vāpi yaṃ vindet sadṛśaṃ sutam |
pautrī mātāmahas tena dadyāt piṇḍaṃ hared dhanam |
iti patnīśabdavācyabhāryāputrikāsutaviṣayaṃ putrikākaraṇaputrikāsutaviṣayaṃ ca veditavyam |
nanv evam akṛtā vā kṛtā vetyādi vacanaṃ putrikākaraṇaviṣayaṃ kṛtetipadena, akṛtetipadena tu gāndarvādivivāhoḍhāputrikāviṣayam | “pautrī mātāmahas tena” iti vacanasāmarthāt pautratvam ubhayor eva putrikāyāḥ putratvāt tatputrasya dauhitratvāt | gāndharvavivāhe mātāmahasāpiṇḍyasagotratvayor anivṛtteḥ tatra pautratvam iti tayor eva mātāmahaśrāddhe nityavad adhikāraḥ na dauhitramātrasyeti cet,
maivam | “dadyāt piṇḍaṃ hared dhanam” iti dhanaharaṇasya patnīmulakatvāt patnīpadena yajñasaṃyogapratipādakena gāndharvādivivāhoḍhāparyudāsāt [428] tatpautrāṇāṃ dūrata eva patnīmūlakadhanagrahaṇam iti | kiṃ ca pautrīti sāmarthyāt gāndarvādivivāhoḍhaputrikāputre ‘pi nāsti dauhitratvāt tasya kiṃ tūpacāra eva | ataḥ pūrvoktam eva samyak |
nanv evaṃ “yo yata ādadīta sa tasmai śrāddhaṃ kuryāt” iti vaiṣṇavavacanam, “dauhitrasya mātāmahaśrāddhaṃ niṣkāraṇam” iti ca vaiṣṇavam eva, ubhayor virodha iti cet,
maivam | atra bhagavator bhārucer matam avatiṣṭhet – “yaḥ śrāddhādhikārī yato yasmāt sakāśād (?) dhanam ādadyāt tena militena dravyeṇa tasmai tadarthaṃ tatpratinidhir bhūtvā kuryāt” iti | ayam āśrayaḥ – bahuputrasthale bahudauhitrasthale pitur mātāmahasya vā aurdhvadaihikakriyāṇāṃ madhye navaśrāddhaṣoḍaśaśrāddhānāṃ niṣpādane na bahūnām adhikāraḥ kiṃ tv ekasyaiveti prakaraṇasāmarthād uktaṃ bhārucineti dhyeyam | someśvaras tu – “vacanasyābhidhānaśaktyā prakaraṇaṃ bādhitvā yo yat ādadīta sa tasmai śrāddhaṃ kuryād iti vacanaṃ putradauhitravyatiriktarikthagrāhaviṣayam” ity āha | etac ca samantaram evoktaṃ viṣṇunā: “uddhṛtadravyād ekenaiva śaktena śrāddhaṣoḍaśakaṃ kāryam” iti | ṣoḍaśaśrāddhagrahaṇaṃ navaśrāddhānām upalakṣakam | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “samuditadravyeṇa navaśrāddhaṃ ṣoḍaśaśrāddhaṃ na kuryāt” iti cakārah panthāparivyayaṇaṃ samuccinoti | samuditaśabdena jñāyate – ekasyaivādhikāro nānyeṣām iti | viṣṇuvacane śaktapadaṃ sāmarthyam adhikāraṃ ca gamayati | tathā cāyam arthaḥ – ekaśabdo mukhyavācī mukhyo jyeṣṭho ‘dhikṛtaś cet sa eva adhikārī | anyathā samanantaranyāyasiddhārthaḥ | śakto dṛḍhāṅgaḥ | ekaḥ dauhitrāṇāṃ madhye sa evādhikārīti | asmin prakaraṇe paṭhitatvā yo yata ādadīteti vacanam etadanurodhena vyākhyātaṃ bhārucinā | nyāyaniṣṭhena bhagavatā someśvareṇa nyāyataḥ prakaraṇam ullaṅghitam iti dhyeyam | ataś ca yāni rikthagrāhakatṛkaparāṇi – [429]
śrāddhaṃ mātāmahānāṃ tu avaśyaṃ dhanahāriṇā |
ityādīni, tāni ṣoḍaśaśrāddhaviṣayāṇīti dhyeyam |
śrāddhaṃ mātāmahānāṃ tu avaśyaṃ dhanahāriṇā |
dauhitreṇārthaniṣṅrtyai kartavyaṃ vidhivat sadā || iti |
someśvara-bhāruci-matāvalambanena ayam asyārthaḥ – arthaśabdena prayujanam uddeśyam – ṛṇam iti yāvat | tasya niṣkṛtyai – ānṛṇyāya bahuṣūpaplavamāneṣu dauhitreṣu eka eva śakto dhanahārī dhanaṃ hṛtvā āhṛtadravyeṇa ṣoḍaśaśrāddhaṃ navaśrāddhāni panthāparivyayaṇaṃ kuryāt | putreṣv asaṃnihiteṣu avidyamāneṣu patnyām avidyamānāyāṃ samantarakartṛṣu vidyamāneṣu itarasya vyavahitakartur adhikāraniṣedhāt | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “sati kartary anyasya kartṛtvaṃ samanantarakartary anantarakartṛtvaṃ na smṛtam” iti | śrāddha iti śeṣaḥ | saṃskārakarmaṇīti kecit | tathā ca vyāsaḥ |
pitṝn mātāmahāṃś caiva dvijaḥ śrāddhena tarpayet |
anṛṇaḥ syāt pitṝṇāṃ tu yajñalokaṃ samṛcchati || iti |
atrānṛṇyam “tribhi ṛṇavā jāyate brahmacaryeṇa ṛṣibhyo yajñena devebhyaḥ prajayā pitṛbhyaḥ” iti pratītaṃ dauhitrasyāpi pautravan mātāmahaprajārūpatvena siddhaṃ nānyathā | duhitus tv agnividyāsādhyakarmaṇy anadhikārāt dauhitrasyaiva tatrādhikāra iti duhitṛdvāyā dauhitrasya sampādanād ānṛṇyaṃ mātāmahasya | ata eva pautrād dauhitrasya vyavadhānam | ata eva pautravad dauhitrasya sākṣād apratibandhena dāyasvīkārārhatā nāsti, kiṃ tu duhitṛdvārā | tato dauhitro [430] ‘pīṣatkalpaḥ pautra eveti tasyāpy ānṛṇyaṃ putravan mātāmahaurdhvadaihikakriyākaraṇāt setsyatīti | ata eva śrutiḥ:
duhitā putrakalpā ca pautrā dauhitrakāḥ smṛtāḥ | iti |
na caiṣā putrikākaraṇaviṣayeti śaṅkanīyam, kalpappatyayānanvayāt (?) | putrikākaraṇe putrikaiva putra iti na putrakalpatā putrikāyāḥ | ata eveṣatkalpaputraḥ putrikā īṣatkalpapu(pra?)pautro dauhitra eveti siddham | yat tu saṃniyogaśiṣṭamātāmahaśrāddhapratipādakaṃ vacanam –
pitaro yatra pūjyate tatra mātāmahā api |
aviśeṣeṇa kartavyaṃ viśeṣān narakaṃ vrajet || iti |
tat tu jīvaputrājīvaputramātāmahadvayasādhāraṇam | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
dvau daive prāk trayaḥ pitrye udag ekaikam eva vā |
mātāmahānām apy evaṃ tantraṃ vā vaiśvadaivikam || iti |
kuryān mātāmahaśrāddhaṃ niyamāt putrikāsutaḥ |
ityādivacanam etadvacanāvirodhy (-canānurodhy) eva | vijñānayogiprabhṛtayas tu saṃniyogaśiṣṭaṃ śrāddhaṃ mātāmahoddeśyaṃ pākṣikam ity āhuḥ | ataś ca dauhitrasya mātāpitṛto ‘pi dṛṣṭādṛṣṭopakārakatayā pratyāsattyatiśayāt tadgāmy eva dhanam iti siddham | etac ca lakṣmīdharācāryamatam atigambhīraṃ diṅmātram udāhṛtam |
atha patnīduhitaranyāyasyāpavādam āha viṣṇuḥ: “saṃsṛṣṭadhanaṃ na patnyabhigāmi” iti | atra bhāruciḥ: “avibhāgadaśāyām iva saṃsṛṣṭidaśāyām api dhanaṃ anekapuruṣasvatvasamāveśād ekapuruṣāpāyena tatsvatvanivṛttāv api puruṣāntarasvatvānāṃ tathaivāvasthānāt ko [431] gṛhṇīyād ity apekṣāyā anutthānāt tādṛgapekṣopanipātinaḥ patnīduhitaranyāyasya bādhaktvenānyasaṃsṛṣṭinyāyasyāvatāraḥ” iti | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ – vibhāgottarakālaṃ punar dravyāṇi miśrīkṛtya saṃsārayātrāyām anuvartamānāyāṃ prāpnuvann upacayo ‘pacayo vā yathājāto ‘nubhāvya iti saṃvidaṃ kṛtvā saṃsarge pravṛtteḥ pākṣikāpacayabhārādyupagamasāhasaśālitvaṃ saṃsṛṣṭinyāya iti | evam anena nyāyena saṃsṛṣñīnāṃ patnīduhitrapekṣayā tatkramapatitāsaṃsṛṣṭipitrādyapekṣayā ca prābalyam iti naiyāyiko 'yaṃ saṃsṛṣṭīnāṃ svatvasaṃkramakramaḥ | saṃsṛṣṭī nāma vibhaktad’avyaṃ vibhaktena dravyāntareṇ punar miśrīkṛtaṃ saṃsṛṣṭaṃ tad asyāstīti saṃsṛṣṭī tasyāputrasya dhanam itaraḥ saṃsṛṣṭī gṛhṇīyāt | na patnyādir ity arthaḥ | saṃsṛṣṭitvaṃ na sarveṣām api tu pitṛbhrātṛpitṛvyāṇām eva | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
vibhakto yaḥ punaḥ pitrā bhrātrā vaikatra saṃsthitaḥ |
pitṛvyeṇāpi vā prītyā sa tatsaṃsṛṣṭa ucyate || iti |
viṣṇur api “pitṛvyapitṛbhrātṛbhir eva saṃsargo nānyaiḥ” iti | atra bhāruciḥ: “vaikalpiko ‘yaṃ saṃsargavidhiḥ” iti | ayam arthaḥ – pitṛvyeṇa vā pitrā vā bhrātṛbhir vā svarucyā saṃsargaḥ “pitṛvyeṇātha vā prītyā” iti prītipadasmaraṇād iti | ata eva saṃbhūya samutthāne ‘py etasyāntarbhāvo nāsti | tatra patnīduhitaranyāya evāvatarati | “saṃbhūyakāriṇāṃ madhye mṛtasya patnyādir eva dhanāṃśabhāgīti” viṣṇusmaraṇāt | ayam arthaḥ – “pitṛvyapitṛbhrātṛbhir eva saṃsargo nānyair iti saṃsṛṣṭadhanaṃ na patnyabhigāmi” iti viṣṇusmaraṇaṃ patnīduhitaranyāyasya bādhakaṃ saṃniyāmakaṃ pitrādibhir eva saṃsargo nānyair iti saṃbhūyakāriṇām ayaṃ nyāyo nāvataratīti | atra [432] viśeṣam āha viṣṇuḥ: “saṃsṛṣṭīnāṃ piṇḍakṛd aṃśahārī” iti | atra bhāruciḥ “piṇḍado ‘ṃśaharaś caiṣām ity atra piṇḍadatvam evāṃśagrahaṇe prayojakam” iti | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ – piṇḍado ‘ṃśaharaś caiṣām ity atra pāṭhakramād arthakramo balīyān ity aṃśahartvam eva piṇḍadatvaprayojakam iti sakalasmṛtisiddham | tathāpy asaṃsṛṣṭhisthale pākṣikāpacayabhārābhyupagamasāhasaśālitvarūpanyāyasya piṇḍadatvarūpāntaraṅganyāyo bādhaka iti pardarśanamātrapara ity uktam | na tu vastuvṛttyā piṇḍadatvam aṃśagrahaṇaprayojakam iti | ato ‘smin prakaraṇe saṃsṛṣṭinyāyāntaraṅganyāyau yathārthaṃ pravartete | ataś ca kvacit saṃsṛṣṭinyāyena saṃsṛṣṭina eva dhanagrāhitvaṃ kvacid antaraṅganyāyenaivāsaṃsṛṣṭina eva dhanagrāhitvam uktam | evaṃ traividhye ‘pi na patnyādir dhanagrāhīti pratipadanyāyaphalaṃ siddham | ataś ca saṃsṛṣṭino ‘putrasyāpitṛkasya dhanaṃ pitṛvyagāmy eveti viṣṇuvacanasyārthaḥ | ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ: “saṃsṛṣṭinas tu saṃsṛṣṭī” iti | yatra punaḥ pitṛvyasodarau saṃsṛṣṭau tatra saṃsṛṣṭidhanaṃ saudaragāmy eva | na pitṛvyagāmīty āha yājñavalkyaḥ: “sodarasya to sodaraḥ” iti | sodarasya saṃsṛṣṭasya dhanaṃ sodara eva gṛhṇīyāt | saṃsṛṣṭipitṛvyādis tu saṃsṛṣṭo ‘pi na gṛhṇīyāt | tasyaiva tatpiṇḍadānādhikārād iti vacanārthaḥ | saṃsṛṣṭino maraṇānantaraṃ jātasya putrasyaivāṃśo dātavyaḥ na grahītavya ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
dadyāc cāpaharec cāṃśaṃ jātasya ca mṛtasya ca | iti |
yatra punaḥ bhinnodarā bhrātaraḥ kecana saṃsṛṣṭāḥ sodarabhrātaro na santi pitṛvyādayo ‘pi saṃsṛṣṭāḥ tatra bhinnodarabhrātṛgāmy eva dhanam ity āha yājñavalkyaḥ | [433]
anyodaryas tu saṃsṛṣṭī nanyodaryadhanaṃ haret | iti |
asaṃsṛṣṭīti śeṣaḥ | tathā hi viṣṇuḥ: “bhinnodarāṇāṃ saṃsṛṣṭināṃ gṛhṇīyuḥ” | atra bhāruciḥ – bhinnodarāṇām iti nirdhāraṇe ṣaṣṭhī | bhinnodarāṇāṃ madhye saṃsṛṣṭina eva dhanaṃ gṛhṇīyuḥ | ayaṃ bhāvaḥ – yady api bhinnodarāṇāṃ saṃsṛṣṭinām asaṃsṛṣṭīnāṃ ca tatpiṇḍadāne ‘dhikāras tulya eva, piṇḍadāne jyeṣṭhakaniṣṭhatvādivivekānapekṣayā adhikārasya tulyatvād ity ukteḥ | piṇḍadānādhikārarūpāntaraṅganyāyataulye ‘pi pākṣikāpacayabhārābhyupagamasāhasaśālitva-rūpanyāyasyādhikasya vidyamānatvāt tatraiva dhanagrāhitvam iti na kācid anupapattiḥ |
nanu pitrā bhrātrā pitṛvyeṇa ca saṃsṛṣṭadhanaṃ na pitṛgāmi nāpi pitṛvyagāmi api tu bhrātgāmy evety uktam | evaṃ ca sati vācanikasvatvasaṃkramaḥ syāt, sa naiyāyika iti prāg uktaṃ nirundhyāt | ataḥ saṃsṛṣṭiviṣaye ‘pi pitrādyapekṣayā bhrātuḥ prāthamye nyāya eva vaktavyaḥ |
ucyate – uktaṃ tāvad vibhaktānāṃ punaḥ punaḥ saṃsargapravṛttiḥ pākṣikāpacayabhārābhyupagamasāhasapūrviketi | bhrātṝṇām eva ca saṃsargapravṛttis tādṛśī na pituḥ | pitāputrayor asaty api saṃsarge ‘nyatarāpacayanibandhanāpacayasaṃkrānter avarjanīyatvena kṛtākṛtaprasaṅgitvāt | śrūyate ‘pi |
tathā pitā putraṃ kṣita upadhāvati |
tathā putraḥ pitaraṃ kṣita upadhāvati || iti |
ato bhrātṝṇām eva saṃsarge pravṛttiḥ pākṣikāpacayabhārābhyupagamasāhasapūrvikā na pitur iti bhrātṛprāthamyaṃ naiyāyikam eva |
nanv evaṃ putrād vibhaktasya pituḥ svabhrātṛbhiḥ saṃsṛṣṭasya [434] maraṇe taddhanasya bhrātṛgāmitvam eva syāt na putragāmitvam iti |
maivam, patnīduhitaranyāyavat saṃsṛṣṭanyāyasyāputraviṣayatvāt | yathāha saṃsṛṣṭiprakaraṇe nāradaḥ |
bhrātṝṇām aprajāḥ preyāt kaścic cet pravrajeta vā | iti |
devalo ‘pi ¦
tato dāyam aputrasya vibhajeran sahodarāḥ | iti |
śaṅkho ‘pi: “aputrasya svaryātasya bhrātṛgāmi dravyam” iti | putro vidyamāno vibhakto na saṃsṛṣṭaḥ ity uktam cet, kim asaṃsṛṣṭaḥ putro na putraḥ, putrasya hi putratvenaiva prābalyaṃ na saṃsṛṣṭatvena vā vibhaktatvena vā “aṅgād aṅgāt saṃbhavasi” iti mantravarṇāt tasyātmatayā nirūpitatvāt, dhanasvāminā mṛtasyātmabhūte tasmin vartamāne saty anyasya taddhanagrāhitvaśaṅkānudāyāt | evaṃ ca yathā vibhakto ‘pi putraḥ putratvenaiva patnyādyapekṣayā prabalaḥ | tathaiva saṃsṛṣṭo ‘pi putraḥ putratvenaiva saṃsṛṣṭabhrātrapekṣayā prabala iti tadgāmy eva dhanam |
nanu pitṛvyādibhiḥ saṃsṛṣṭasya pitur dhanaṃ putraikaniyatam ity ukte kim athaṃ saṃsargaḥ pitrā tadbhrātrādīnām iti cet,
maivam | jīvaddaśāyām upacayārtham eva saṃsargavidhānaṃ na tu bhāvimaraṇābhisaṃdhinā | ato maraṇānantaraṃ nyāyato vivicyamānaṃ svatvaṃ yatra paryavasitaṃ syāt tad eva grāhyam iti | mṛte pitari saṃsṛṣṭe tatsaṃsṛṣṭaiḥ pitṛvyādibhiḥ saṃsṛṣṭidaśāyāṃ bhuktāvaśiṣṭaṃ dhanam apākṛtāvaśiṣṭam ṛṇaṃ putrair eva vibhaktair apy asaṃsṛṣṭibhir api svīkāryam iti na kaścid virodhaḥ | antaraṅganyāyenāsaṃsṛṣṭinām eva dhanagrāhitvam āha yājñavalkyaḥ: “asaṃsṛṣṭy api cādadyāt” [435] iti | apiśabdena “sodarasya tu sodaraḥ” ity atra sodaro ‘nukṛṣyata iti bhāruciḥ | lakṣmīdharas tu – “apiśabdena “saṃsṛṣṭo nānyamātṛjaḥ” ity anyamātṛjapadasāmarthyāt sodara eva samuccīyate” ity āha | tad ayam arthaḥ – saṃsṛṣṭino dhanam asaṃsṛṣṭasodara eva gṛhnīyāt | anyamātṛjas tu saṃsṛṣṭo ‘pi na gṛhṇīyāt | asaṃsṛṣṭinaḥ sodarasya pākṣikāpacayabhārābhyupagamasāhasaśālitvābhāve ‘pi pitṛpiṇḍadānādhikāras tasyaiveti taduktiḥ | anenaiva nyāyena ekodarāṇām api saṃsṛṣṭasya madhyamasya maraṇe kaniṣṭhasyāsaṃsṛṣṭinaḥ tad aurdhvadaihikādhikārāt saṃsṛṣṭajyeṣṭhasya vidyamānatve ‘pi tasya na madhyamāṃśagrāhitvam iti dhyeyam |
atra kecid āhuḥ – “asaṃsṛṣṭy api cādadyāt saṃsṛṣṭa” iti saṃsṛṣṭapadaṃ sodaravāci saṃsṛṣṭadhanavadvācīty arthadvayam āhuḥ | avasite tātparye saṃsṛṣṭapadam āvṛttyā vākyadvaye ‘py anvitam astu vākyabhede ca virūpārthatā na doṣa iti vijñānayogino matam iti |
nanu pitrā saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ putrāṇāṃ dhanagrāhitvam | asaṃsṛṣṭānāṃ putrāṇāṃ pitṛdhanagrāhitvaṃ nāsti | yathā avibhaktajaputrasya pitṛdhanagrāhitvam, nānyeṣāṃ putrāṇām iti |
maivam |
vibhakteṣu suto jātaḥ savarṇāyāṃ vibhāgabhāk |
ūrdhvaṃ vibhāgāj jātas tu pitryam eva hared dhanam ||
iti |
putraiḥ saha vibhaktena pitrā yat svayam ārjitam |
vibhaktajasya tat sarvam anīśāḥ pūrvajāḥ smṛtāḥ ||
ityādivacanaśatebhyaḥ vibhaktasya pitṛdhanagrāhitvaṃ pratīyate | etādṛśaṃ saṃsṛṣṭaputrasya pitṛdhanaprāpakaṃ vacanam ekam api na pradṛśyate | [436]
nanv evaṃ vibhaktasya pitṛdhanasvāmitvaṃ vācanikaṃ syād iti pūrvoktaṃ virudhyeta iti cet,
maivam | atra vibhāgo vācanikaḥ, “vibhāge dharmavṛddhiḥ syāt” ityādivacanād, dharmavṛddhikāmānāṃ vibhāgaḥ kārya iti vibhāgasya vācanikatvapratīteḥ | ato vibhaktajasya svāmitvaṃ naiyāyikam, tathā hi – vibhaktasya pitṛdravyasvīkārasamaye itare vibhaktā bhrātaraḥ tad dravyaṃ samam amśaṃ svayam api yadi gṛhṇīyuḥ tadā vibhaktajasyālpīyān eva vibhāgaḥ syād iti viṣamavibhāgaḥ syāt | taddoṣaparijihīrṣayā yadi te sarve vibhaktajena sārdhaṃ punar vibhāgaṃ kuryus tadā pūrvavibhāgasya pitṛkṛtasyānarthakyaṃ syāt | bhrātaraḥ saṃsṛṣṭāṃśam avayutya saṃsṛṣṭino datvā pitṛdravyam eva gṛhṇīyur iti | ato yuktaṃ saṃsṛṣṭinām asaṃsṛṣṭināṃ putrāṇāṃ pitṛdravya tulyam eva svāmyam iti | etad evābhipretyāha bhāruciḥ – saṃsṛṣṭānām asaṃsṛṣṭānāṃ putrāṇāṃ pitṛkṛtarṇāpākaraṇaṃ tulyatayā nyāyyam iti pitrārjitadravyasyādhikye lobhād vibhāgāpekṣāyām apy apacayabhārasahiṣṇutvābhāvāt | apacaye satyapravṛtteḥ vibhāgo nāsti | kiṃ tu vibhaktajasyaiva pitṛdravyam iti pitṛdhanagrahaṇe manuvacanaṃ jñāpakam ity āhuḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
saṃsṛṣṭās tena ye vā syur vibhajeta sa taiḥ saha |
asyārthaḥ – ye ca vibhaktāḥ pitrā saha saṃsṛṣṭās taiḥ sārdhaṃ pitur ūrdhvaṃ vibhaktajo vibhajet | asaṃsṛṣṭais tu bhrātṛbhiḥ saha vibhaktasya na vibhāga iti tātparyāya iti | yat tu manunaivoktaṃ saṃsṛṣṭivibhāgaṃ prakramya,
eṣāṃ jyeṣṭhaḥ kaniṣṭho vā hīyetāṃśapradānataḥ |
mriyetānyataro vāpi tasya bhāgo na lupyate ||
[437]        sodaryā vibhajeyus taṃ sametya sahitāḥ samam |
bhrātaro ye ca saṃsṛṣṭāḥ bhaginyaś ca sanābhayaḥ || iti |
tad vijñānayogī vyācaṣṭe – yeṣāṃ bhrātṝṇāṃ saṃsṛṣṭānāṃ madhye jyeṣṭhaḥ kaniṣṭho madhyamo vā aṃśapradānataḥ – aṃśapradāne | sārvavibhaktikas tasiḥ, vibhāgakāla iti yāvat | hīyeta svāṃśaḍ bhraśyeta āśramāntaragrahaṇena vā brahmahatyādinā vā maraṇena vā tadā tasya bhāgo na lupyate | ataḥ pṛthag uddharaṇīyo na saṃsṛṣṭino gṛhṇīyur ity arthaḥ | tasyoddhṛtasya viniyogam āha “sodaryā vibhajeyus tam” iti tam uddhṛtaṃ bhāgaṃ sodaryāḥ sahodarā asaṃsṛṣṭā api sametya deśāntaragā api samāgamya sahitāḥ saṃbhūya samam – nyūnādhikavibhāga(bhāva)rahitam | ye ca bhrātaro bhinnodarāḥ saṃsṛṣṭās te ca sanābhayo bhaginyaś ca gṛhṇīyur ity artha iti | tad ayam arthaḥ – bhinnodarasaṃsṛṣṭinām apacayabhārasahiṣṇutvam aṃśagrahaṇe nimittam | ubhayanimittaṃ saṃsṛṣṭasyaikodarābhāve veditavyam | bhaginīnāṃ tu saṃsṛṣṭadhanavibhāgasamaye dāyavibhāgasamaya iva yat kiṃcit prītyā deyaṃ na tu vibhāgaḥ, tāsāṃ saṃsargāprasakteḥ | prasaktānām eva vibhāgaḥ | ataś ca bhinnodarāṇāṃ saṃsṛṣṭānām asaṃsṛṣṭānām ekodarāṇāṃ samavibhāga iti siddham |
aparārka-candrikākārādayas tu – saṃsṛṣṭāputradravyaṃ prathamato bhrātṛgāmi tadabhāve pitṛgāmi tadabhāve vṛttasthapatnyabhigāmīti śaṅkhoktavācanikakrameṇa patnīduhitara iti naiyāyikakramo bādhyate | ataḥ “sodaryā vibhajeyus tam” iti vacanaṃ kramaparm ity āhuḥ |
tan na, śaṅkhoktakramasya naiyāyikatvaṃ pratipāditaṃ prāk | śaṅkhavacane patnīgrahaṇāt [438] saṃsṛṣṭadhanavibhāgasamaye patnyā api bhaginīnām iva yat kiṃced deyam ity dhyeyam | ataś bhāruci-vijñānayogimatam eva samyak |
ata sarva(varṇa)vibhāgaśeṣaḥ kiṃcid ucyate | tathā ca manuḥ |
ṛṇe dhane ca sarvasmin pravibhakte yathāvidhi |
paścād dṛśyeta yat kiṃcit tat sarvaṃ samatāṃ nayet ||
kātyāyanas tu viśeṣam āha |
pracchāditaṃ ca yad yena punar sādya tatsamam |
bhajeran bhrātṛbhiḥ sārdham abhāve hi pituḥ sutāḥ ||
pitur abhāve sarve sutā eva tadāsāditaṃ vibhajerann ity arthaḥ | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
anyonyāpahṛtaṃ dravyaṃ vibhakte yat tu dṛśyate |
tat punas te samair aṃśair vibhajerann iti sthitiḥ || iti |
atra samair iti vadatā uddhāravibhāgo niiddhaḥ | vibhajerann iti vadatā yena yad dṛśyate tat tenaiva na grāhyam iti darśitam | anena vacanenaiva samuditadravyāpahāre dāyādānāṃ na doṣa iti jñāyata iti bhāruci-aparārka-someśvarādaya āhuḥ | vijñāneśvaras tu – nanu manunā samuditadravyāpahāre jyeṣṭhasyaiva doṣo na kanīyasāṃ doṣa iti darśitam |
yo jyeṣṭho hi nivurvīta lobhād bhrātṝn yavīyasaḥ |
sa jyeṣṭhaḥ syād abhāgaś ca niyantavyaś ca rājabhiḥ || iti |
naitat jyeṣṭamātraviṣayaṃ sarveṣām yavīyasām api | tathā ca śrutiḥ: “yo vai bhāginaṃ bhāgannudate cayate vainaṃ sa yadi vainaṃ na cayate ‘tha putram atha pautraṃ cayate” iti | bhāginam – bhāgārhaṃ bhāgānnudate bhāgād [439] apākaroti bhāgaṃ tasmai na prayacchati sa bhāgān muktaḥ enaṃ netāraṃ cayane nāśayati doṣiṇaṃ karoti | yadi taṃ na nāśayati | athāsya putraṃ pautraṃ vā nāśayatīti jyeṣṭhaviśeṣam antareṇaiva sādhāraṇadravyāpahāre doṣaḥ śruta ity āha | atra bhrārucyādimatam eva samyak | manusmṛtiśrutyor bhāgamātrapradānaviṣayatvād avaluptavibhāgaviṣayatvād iti | yat tūktaṃ kātyāyanena,
vibhaktenaiva yat prāptaṃ dhanaṃ tasyaiva tad bhavet |
hṛtaṃ labdhaṃ ca yan naṣṭaṃ prāg uktaṃ ca punar bhajet ||
mitho ‘pahṛtam ityādinā prāguktasyāpi punar upādānaṃ dārḍhyārtham | tathā ca durvibhaktaṃ mitho ‘pahṛtam anyonyāpahṛtaṃ naṣṭaṃ durlabdhaṃ ca samatāṃ nayed ity arthaḥ |
anyonyāpahṛtaṃ dravyaṃ durvibhaktaṃ ca yad bhavet |
paścāt prāptaṃ vibhajyeta samabhāgena tad bhṛguḥ || iti |
durvibhaktaṃ śāstroktaprakāram antareṇa viṣamatayā vibhaktam | naṣṭaṃ tu nikṣepādinā naṣṭaṃ paścāl labdham | durlabdhaṃ (durlabhaṃ?) tu asādhyajanasthitam ṛṇādikam | evam anyāpahṛtadurvibhaktanaṣṭamithopahṛtadurlabdhānāṃ vibhāgānantaraṃ jāyamānānāṃ bhrātṛbhiḥ samāṃśenaiva vibhāgaḥ kartavya iti śāstramaryādā | atha vibhāgasaṃdehe nirṇayaprakāram āha nāradaḥ |
sākṣitvaṃ prātibhāvyaṃ ca dānaṃ grahaṇam eva ca |
vibhaktā bhrātaraḥ kuryur nāvibhaktāḥ parasparam ||
bṛhaspatiḥ |
pṛthagāyavyayadhanāḥ kusīdaś ca parasparam |
[440]        vaṇikpathaṃ ca ye kuryuḥ vibhaktās te na saṃśayaḥ || iti |
viṣṇur api: “krayavikrayadānagrahaṇaprātibhāvyasākṣitvasambhūyakāritvanidhyādhānādikaṃ parasparakṛtaṃ vibhāgahetuḥ” iti | ayaṃ krayavikrayādhikārahetuḥ | ataś ca sākṣitvaprātibhāvyadānagrahaṇādīni parasparam eva na kāryāṇi | bhrātṝṇāṃ madhye itarābhyanujñayā ekasya vibhaktapitṛvyādikaṃ prati prātibhāvyādeḥ vihitatvāt | tathā ca smṛtiḥ |
itareṇānujānānaḥ prātibhāvyaṃ haret paraḥ | iti |
anenābhiprāyeṇāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhrātṝṇām atha dampatyoḥ pituḥ putrasya caiva hi |
prātibhāvyam ṛṇaṃ sādhyam avibhakte na tu smṛtam || iti |
parasparam iti śeṣaḥ | ata evāha sa eva |
vibhāganihnave jñātibandhusākṣyabhilekhyakaiḥ |
vibhāgabhāvanā jñeyā gṛhakṣetraiś ca yautakaiḥ ||
vibhāgasya nihnave apalāpe jñātibhiḥ pitṛsaṃbandhibhiḥ vibhaktapitṛvyādibhiḥ bandhubhiḥ mātṛsaṃbandhibhiḥ mātulādibhiḥ pūrvoktalakṣaṇaiḥ lekhyyena ca vibhāgapatreṇa vibhāgabhāvanā vibhāganirṇayo jñātavyaḥ | tathā ca yautakaiḥ pṛthakkṛtaiḥ gṛhakṣetraiś ca | cakāreṇa pṛthak pṛthak kṛṣyādipravartanaṃ pṛthak pṛthak pañcamahāyajñādidharmānuṣṭhānaṃ samuccīyate | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
vibhāgadharmasaṃdehe dāyādānāṃ vinirṇayaḥ |
jñātibhir bhāgalekhyena pṛthak kāryapravartanā || iti |
atra likhitasākṣyādeḥ jñāpakahetutvam, vibhāgasaṃdehe siddhasyaiva vibhāgasya jñāpakatvāt | kārakahetūnāṃ tv avidyamānasyāpi [441] vibhāgasya niṣpādakatvaṃ purastān nivedayiṣyate | pṛthakkṣetraiś ca yautakair iti cakārasamuccitārthas tu daśavarṣaparyantāvasthitāḥ kārakā iti ca purastān nivedayiṣyate |
nanv asmin vacanadvaye lekhyasākṣibhyāṃ tulyatayā liṅgānāṃ gamakatvam uktam |
tan na saṃgacchate |
liṅgānāṃ tarkarūpeṇa pramāṇānugrāhakatayā tadvat pramāpakatvāyogād iti cet,
maivam, asmin vivādapade liṅgānām api pramāpakatvam eva na tv itarasaptadaśavibhāgapadeṣv iva liṅgānāṃ pramāṇānugrāhakatvam | tathā hi – vibhāgārheṣu bhrātṛṣu parasparam ṛṇaprātibhāvyasākṣyadānapratigrahapitṛdevārcanakriyāḥ ṣoḍhā darśana uktāḥ hastādiliṅgatulyā na bhavanti | tataś caitāni “nāvibhaktāḥ kathaṃcana” iti smṛtivaśād avibhaktānāṃ niṣiddhāni “vibhāganihnave” ityādivacane sākṣilikhitasamānayogakṣematayā liṅgāny uktāni bhavanti | itareṣu vivādapadeṣu likhitasākṣyādīnām eva pramāpakatāt itareṣāṃ tadanugrāhakatvam, atra tu na tatheti | kiṃ cānenaiva vacanena jñāyate  asmin viṣaye liṅgānām api lekhyasākṣibhyām antareṇāpi pramāpakatvam abhyupagatam iti | ata eva bṛhaspatiḥ |
sāhasaṃ sthāvarasvāmyaṃ prāgvibhāgaś ca rikthinām |
anumānena vijñeyaṃ na syur yasya ca sākṣiṇaḥ ||
na syur iti – likhitasākṣiṇāv antareṇety arthaḥ | sākṣigrahaṇaṃ prabalapramāṇasyopalakṣaṇam | ata eva lekhyam api saṃgṛhītam | ata evānantaram uktaṃ tenaiva |
teṣām etāḥ kriyāḥ loke pravartante svarikthiṣu |
vibhaktān avagaccheyuḥ lekhyam apy antareṇa tān || [442]
lekhyagrahaṇaṃ sākṣiṇām upalakṣaṇam |
kecid āhuḥ – atra vibhāganihnave lekhya(likhita)sākṣibhyāṃ tulyabalatvaṃ liṅgānām apy avagantavyam | ata evāhaitadvyākhyāne candrikākāraḥ — pravartante vyaktāḥ samastā iti śeṣa iti |
tan na | parasparakartṛkasākṣitvaprātibhāvyādīnāṃ jñāpakahetubhyo vilakṣaṇatvenoktatvāt | sākṣigrahaṇenaiva jñātyādīnāṃ taṭasthasākṣiṇām api sākṣitve ‘pi vibhāganirṇaye teṣāṃ prābalyajñāpanārthaṃ pṛthag grahaṇam iti vijñāneśaḥ | sākṣigrahaṇaṃ kṛtasākṣiparam iti kecit | bṛhaspatiḥ |
ekapākena vasatāṃ pitṛdevadvijārcanam |
ekaṃ bhaved vibhaktānāṃ tad eva syād gṛhe gṛhe ||
evaṃ ca pṛthak pṛthag vaiśvadevādikāryapravartanam avibhakteṣv avidyamānaṃ vibhaktatvam avagamayatīti vibhāgasaṃdehanirṇayayuktir yuktety anavadyam iti candrikākāraḥ | asyāyam āśrayaḥ – pitṛdevadvijārcanam ity atra pitṛśabdena pratyabdikam ucyate | amāvāsyāśrāddhādīnām avibhaktānāṃ madhye itarābhyanujñayā itarasyādhikārāt | atra devaśabdena tatsaṃniyogaśiṣṭaṃ vaiśvadevaśrāddham ucyate | na tu devayajñādikam, tasyāvibhaktānām api vihitatvāt,
avibhaktaiś ca kartavyā vaiśvadevādikāḥ kriyāḥ |
iti smaraṇād iti | etac ca vaivāhikāgnir yeṣāṃ mate alaukikaḥ | laukikatvapakṣe tu vibhāgānantaram eva agnihotravaiśvadevādikāḥ kāryā iti teṣāṃ kārakahetavo vaiśvadevādikāḥ | ubhayeṣāṃ pratyābdikaṃ kārakahetur (?) iti |
atra kecid āhuḥ – candrikākāreṇa śreṣṭhanirṇāyakapramāṇābhāve liṅgānāṃ praveśa ity uktaṃ tac ca (tat tu) likhitasākṣisadbhāve tābhyāṃ nirṇaya autsargika iti teṣāṃ prāthamyam (prādhānyam) uktam | [443] na tu pramāpakatve likhitasākṣiliṅgānām | vibhāganihnavasthale liṅgād itaraprābalyasya smṛtyācārayor vedānumāpakatve tulye ācāramūlavedānumānāt smṛtimūlavedānumānasya sukaratvavad ity anusaṃdheyam iti |
tan na, candrikākārasya liṅgaśabdena hetur abhipretaḥ | sa ca jñāpaka eva | kārakahetūnāṃ likhitād atiśayitvān na tulyateti | tatra kātyāyanaḥ |
vaseyur daśa varṣāṇi pṛthakdharmāḥ pṛthakkriyāḥ |
bhrātaras te ‘pi vijñeyā vibhaktāḥ paitṛkād dhanāt ||
bhrātṛśabdo ‘tra rikthasaṃbandhyupalakṣaṇārtham | paitṛkagrahaṇaṃ dāyagrahaṇopalakṣaṇārtham iti candrikākāraḥ |
tan na, svatvānutpatter iti vakṣyate |
nanu kāryānuṣaṅgād vā aśaktyā vā daśavarṣaparyantaṃ dāyagrahaṇābhāve vyavahāraḥ siddha eveti pūrvaprakaraṇoktaṃ virundhyād iti cet,
maivam | paramārthato dāyagrahaṇābhāve,
paśyato ‘bruvato bhūmer hānir viṃśativārṣikī |
pareṇa bhujyamānāyā dhanasya daśavārṣikī ||
itivat chalānusāreṇa vibhaktā eveti candrikākāraḥ | atra chalaṃ svopekṣānibandhanam |
nanv evaṃ paśyato ‘bruvato bhūmer ity atra na vyavahārahāniḥ svasvarūpahāniḥ | api tu phalahānir ity uktaṃ vijñānayoginā | tadvad atrāpi phalahānir eva na svarūpahāniḥ na vyavahārahānir iti cet,
maivam | bhūmer hānir ity atra karmaṇi ṣaṣṭhīvidhānād viṃśativārṣiko bhogo bhūmiṃ hanyād iti vākyārthasya niṣpannatvāt | daśavārṣiko bhogo dhanaṃ hanyād iti svarūpahānir evoktā |
nanu,
chalaṃ nirasya bhūtena vyavahārān nayen nṛpaḥ | [444]
iti tatvānusaraṇenaiva nyāyaḥ kartavya iti cet,
maivam | atra chalamatatvātmakaṃ vivakṣitam | na tu tatvātmakam | tatvātmakachalāvalambanena tu vyavahāranirṇayadarśanāt | tathā hi,
bhūtachalānurodhena dvigatiḥ samudāhṛtaḥ |
iti vyavahārasya chalāvalambanam apy ekā gatir uktā | anyathā,
dharmaś ca vyavahāraś ca caritraṃ rājaśāsanam |
catuṣpād vyavahāro ‘yam uttaraḥ pūrvabādhakaḥ ||
iti smṛtyā uttareṣāṃ vyavahāracaritrarājaśāsanānāṃ dharmabādhakatvaṃ chalānusaraṇanibandhanaṃ nirundhyāt | etasya chalānusaraṇasya tātvikatve smṛtikāraṇām aprāmāṇikatvaṃ syāt | ata eva chalaṃ nirasya bhūtenety atra chalagrahaṇam atatvātmakachalaviṣayam iti mantavyam | ata eva candrikākāra-vijñānayogibhyām –
nihnave bhāvito dadyād ekadeśavibhāvitaḥ |
iti vacanavyākhyānāvasare chalānusaraṇam atra kāryam ity uktam | vijñānayogināpi,
sthāvareṣu vivādeṣu divyāni parivarjayet |
iti atra avaṣṭambhaviṣaye divyaṃ nāstīti vadatā, “paśyato ‘bruvato bhūmeḥ” ity atrāpi tu phalahānir iti vadatā, chalānusāreṇa nirṇayo ‘ṅgīkṛta iti diṅmātram udāhṛtam |
tatra kecid āhuḥ – daśavarṣaparyantaṃ pṛthakkriyākaraṇaṃ pṛthagdharmānuṣṭhānaṃ ca rikthakrayasaṃvibhāgaparigrahādyanyatamatvābhāvāt svatvahetutvābhāvāt pṛthakkriyasya pṛthagdharmakasya puruṣasya kathaṃ svatvāpādakam iti cet,
ucyate – vibhaktāḥ paitṛkād dhanād iti vacanād avagamyate svatvam | tathā hi – [445] vibhāgo nāma – samudāyadravyaviṣayāṇām anekasvāmyānām ekaikatrāvasthāpanam ity uktaṃ prāk | tac ca vacanād avagamyate | yathā –
ādhiḥ praṇaśyed dviguṇe dhane yadi na mokṣyate |
ity atra vācanikasvatvanivṛttiḥ parasvatvāpattir ity uktaṃ vijñānayoginā | candrikākāreṇāpi chanānusāreṇa vibhāgo nāstīti vadatā vācanikasvatvāpattir ity uktam |
nanv evaṃ candrikākāreṇa ādhiḥ praṇaśyed dviguṇa ity atra tilavinimayādidṛṣṛāntena ādher vinimayāntam aṅgīkṛtya tasya vinimayasya svatvahetutā lokasiddhety uktam iti evam atrāpi lokasiddhyāśrayeṇa prakārāntareṇaudāsīnyasvopakṣānibandhanasvīkāro ‘pi svatvahetur bhavatv iti vācyam, paśyato ‘bruvato bhūmer ity anenaiva dattotaratvāt | kiṃ ca tatrāpi candrikākārasya vācanikadānāntatvaṃ vā vinimayadravyasya krayamūlyatayā krayāntatvaṃ vā svīkartavyam ity uktaṃ prāk | svatvasya vācanikatvaṃ nāma pāribhāṣikatvam ity uktam ādhiḥ praṇaśyed ityādivacanavyākhyānāvasare | tac ca pāribhāṣikatvam atra na vaktuṃ śakyate | deśavarṣaparyantaṃ tūṣṇīm evāvasthitatvāt | paramārthato ‘pi dāyagrahaṇābhāva iti candrikākāragranthasyāyam arthaḥ – paramārthato vastuvṛttyā | dāyagrahaṇaṃ vibhāgaḥ | tasyābhāve ‘pi paitṛkād dhanād bhrātaro vibhaktā eveti | ayam āśayaḥ – yatheṣṭaviniyogārhatvam eva svatvam | tac ca janmanaiva siddham | kārakahetūnāṃ parasparakartṛkasākṣitvaprātibhāvya-dānagrahaṇakrayavikrayasaṃbhūyakāritvanidhyādīnāṃ sadbhāve saty eva vibhāgotpattiḥ | teṣāṃ kārakatvenaiva jñāpakatvābhyupagamāt | [446]
vibhaktā bhrātaraḥ kuryuḥ nāvibhaktāḥ parasparam |
iti avibhaktānāṃ niṣiddhāḥ santo vibhāgaṃ niṣpādya jñāpayanti | yathā dvaitīyīkasādhyānubandhāḥ śāstrabhedam utpādya bhedajñāpakā iti kārakahetutvenābhimatā mīmāṃsakaiḥ tadvad atrāpīti mantavyam | atra janmanaiva svatvam utpannaṃ bhrātṝṇām, tathāpi vibhāgakārakahetvabhāvād daśavarṣaparyantam avaṣṭambhahetūnāṃ vibhāgakārakatve siddhe svatvasyāpi vibhāgaḥ siddhaḥ saṃsphuratīti candrikākārasyābhimatiyogyaṃ matam anūditam | ataś ca teṣāṃ liṅgānāṃ sadbhāve saṃvibhāgo ‘vaśyam astīty apiśabdaṃ prayuñjānasya bhāva iti |
yat tu candrikākāreṇoktam – daśavarād arvāg ityādinā granthakalāpena – vibhāgasaṃdehe divyānavatārāt punar vibhāgaḥ kartavya iti liṅgānāṃ sāmarthyam asāmarthyaṃ coktam, tat tu daśavarṣād arvāg api liṅgānāṃ sākṣilikhitapramāṇābhyāṃ sāmarthyaṃ tulyam eva, kiṃ tu daśavarṣasyopari liṅgānāṃ prābalyāt likhitasākṣisadbhāve ‘pi tadapekṣā nāstīty evaṃparam | divyānavatāras tu vācanikaḥ, “sarvābhāve ‘pi punar vibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ” iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | sarveṣāṃ likhitādijñāpakahetūnāṃ kārakahetūnāṃ  cābhāve | vibhāgaśabdaḥ patnīvibhāgavad asamartheṣu bhrātṛṣu daridreṣu svarucyā yat kiṃcid dātavyam ity evaṃparaḥ iti someśvarādaya āhuḥ |
tan na, sarvābhāve divyānavatārāt svarucipakṣasyānavatārāc chuddha eva vibhāgaḥ kartavya ity āha bhāruciḥ | ayam eva pakṣaḥ samyak |
kecit tu someśvarādīnām abhisaṃdhim evam āhuḥ – vyapagate vibhāgasaṃdehe vibhāgasya siddhatvaṃ bhrātaraḥ proṣyā iti yat kiṃcid deyam ity āhur iti sarvam anavadyam | etac candrikākārābhimatiyogyaṃ matadvayam anūditam | [447]
vaseyur daśavarṣāṇi pṛthagdharmāḥ pṛthakkriyāḥ |
vibhaktā bhrātaras te ‘pi vijñeyāḥ paitṛkād dhanāt ||
ity anena dravyābhāve ‘tyantaniḥsvānāṃ dharmavibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ | “vibhāge dharmavṛddhiḥ syāt” ityādismṛtibhyaḥ | ataś ca pitṛdravyāvirodhena daśavarṣaparyantaṃ ye pṛthagdharmāḥ pṛthakkriyāḥ te vibhaktā eva | dharmavibhāgasya itarābhyanujñām antareṇāpy ekenaiva svīkartuṃ śakyatvād ity uktaṃ bhārucinā | etādṛśasya vibhāgaśabdavācyatvam apy astīty uktaṃ prakaraṇādāv eva | pitṛdravyāvirodhenārjite dravye dāyādānām adhikāra ity uktam | ataś ca pitṛdravyāvirodhenārjitasya sadbhāve ‘pi tasyāvibhājyatvāt dharmavibhāgātmaka evātra vibhāgo ‘vatiṣṭhata ity avagantavyam | paitṛkād dhanād iti lyablope pañcamī |
atredaṃ bhārucer matatattvam — “vaseyur daśavarṣāṇi” ity atra lyablope pañcamyāśrayaṇāt pitṛdhanaṃ vihāya ye daśavarṣaparyantaṃ dharmavibhāgavantaḥ teṣāṃ tadūrdhvaṃ maitrādinā yal labdhaṃ tad evāvibhājyaṃ daśavarṣamadhe labdhaṃ maitrādikaṃ vibhājyam eva | avibhāgadaśāyāṃ svayam ārjitaṃ maitrādikaṃ vibhājyam iti kaimutikanyāyasiddham | yathāha viṣṇuḥ: “apitryaṃ gārbhaṃ dhārmaṃ maitraṃ vaidyam ākasmikam ā daśābdaṃ pravibhājyam ata ūrdhvaṃ sarvam vibhājyam” iti | atrāha bhāruciḥ – apitryam avidyamānapitṛdravyam | etat tritayaviśeṣaṇam | gārbhaṃ strīdhanam | dharmam iṣṭāpūrtādikam | maitraṃ mitrasakāśāl labdham | vaidyaṃ vidyāto labdham | ākasmikam akasmāl labdhaṃ nidyādikam | pratigrahādinā labdham | etat [448] pañcavidhadravyamadhye uttaratrayaṃ dharmavibhāgābhāve ‘vibhaktatvā vibhājyam | daśavarṣaparyantāvasthitirūpa-dharmavibhāgasadbhāve ‘py avibhājyam eva iti | ayam āśayaḥ – ā daśavarṣam iti dharmavibhāgopalakṣaṇam iti | na caitad vacanam,
saṃdhiś ca parivṛttiś ca vibhāgaś ca samā api |
ā daśāhān nivartante viṣamā navavatsarāt ||
iti viṣamavibhāgasya navavarṣaparyantaṃ nivṛttipratipādakabharadvājavacanānusāreṇa navavarṣād upari viṣamavibhāgo na parāvartata ity evamparam iti vācyam, etadvacanasya tatparatvābhāvāt | tathā hi – etadvacanaṃ vibhāgasaṃdehaṃ prakramyoktam | vacanasāmarthyaṃ cāpi tathaiva pratibhāti |
vaseyur daśavarṣāṇi pṛthag dharmāḥ pṛthakkriyāḥ|
vibhaktā bhrātaraḥ   ............... ||
iti, pṛthagdharmakatvapṛthakkriyatvalakṣaṇaviśeṣaṇaviśiṣṭadaśavarṣaparyantaṃ vasatir vibhāgahetuḥ pratipāditaḥ | viṣamavibhāgapratipādanaparatve vyāhanyeta | vibhaktā bhrātara iti liṅgasāmarthyāl liṅgī vibhāgo vyāhanyeta, vibhāgaliṅgānāṃ parasvatvāpādakatvāyogāt | chalānusāreṇāpi vyavahārasya nyāyyatvād iti tatra lyablope pañcamy eva samāśrayaṇīyeti |
tan na, vibhāgaliṅgānāṃ kārakarūpahetutvād vibhāgotpādane sāmarthyasyokteḥ, svatvasyāpi putrāṇām janmanaiva siddhatvāt, tattvātmakachalānusaraṇasya nyāyyatvenodāhṛtatvāt |
tad ayam atra niṣkarṣaḥ – vibhāgasaṃdehe kvacit likhitena nirṇayaḥ, kvacit sākṣibhiḥ, kvacit jñātibhiḥ, kvacid bandhubhiḥ, kvacin miśritair [449] nirṇayaḥ kartavyaḥ | eteṣām abhāve kārakahetubhir nirṇayaḥ | ubhayasadbhāve kārakahetur autsargika eva | jñāpakahetubhis tu daśavarṣaparyantaṃ parivartitair eva nirṇayo nānyaiḥ | daśavarṣaparyantāvasthitānāṃ jñāpakahetūnāṃ kārakatvātmanāvasthitaiḥ kārakahetubhir eva nirṇaya ity arthād uktam | iyāṃs tu viśeṣaḥ – svabhāvataḥ kārakahetubhiḥ sadya eva vibhāgasiddhes tannirṇayaḥ, jñāpakaheturūpakārakāṇāṃ daśavarṣaparyanta iti | sarvābhāve divyānavatārāt śuddho vibhāgaḥ kartavyaḥ | uktahetubhiḥ vibhārasiddhāv api vyavahartṛbhrātṛbhyo yat kiṃcid deyam iti sarvam anavadyam ||
iti śrīpratāparudamahādevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇḍe dāyabhāgākhyapadasya vilāsaḥ |
[450]
atha sāhasākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
pūrvaṃ trayodaśasu vivādapadeṣu deyādeyavicāraḥ kṛtaḥ | yathāha viṣṇuḥ: “vyavahāro dvirutthānaḥ” iti | dviprakāreṇa – deyamūlatayā daṇḍamūlatayotthānam | dvirutthāna iti vyavahārasya kāraṇadvayaṃ kathitam | tathā gautamasūtram: “dvirutthāno dvigatiḥ” iti | vyavahāra ity anuṣajyate | tatra nibandhanakāreṇa ṛṇādānādidāyavibhāgāntānāṃ deyanibandhanatā sāhasādipañcakasya daṇḍanibandhantvam iti dviruttāhatety artha iti | yady api manvādibhiḥ,
teṣām adyam ṛṇādānaṃ nikṣepo ‘svāmivikrayaḥ |
saṃbhūya ca samutthānaṃ dattasyānapakarma ca ||
vetanasaiva cādānaṃ saṃvidaś ca vyatikramaḥ |
krayavikrayānuśayau vivādaḥ svāmipālayoḥ ||
sīmāvivādadharmaś ca pāruṣye dāṇdavācike |
steyaṃ ca sāhasaṃ caiva strīsaṃgrahaṇam eva ca ||
padāny aṣṭādaśaitāni vyavahāre vidur budhāḥ ||
ity evamuktaprakāreṇa kramikāṇi vyavahārapadāny uktāni | atra vākpāruṣyadaṇḍapāruṣya-strīsaṃgrahaṇānantaraṃ dāyavibhāga kramikaḥ [451] nibandhanakāreṇa tu – trayodaśavivādapadaṃ dāya ity uktam | ubhayor mahān virodhaḥ | sa parihriyate – tathoktaṃ nāradena |
ṛṇādānaṃ hy upanidhiḥ saṃbhūyotthānam eva ca |
dattasya punar ādāna aśuśrūṣābhypetya ca ||
vetanasyānapākarma tathaivāsvāmivikrayaḥ |
vkrīyāsaṃpradānaṃ ca prītyānuśaya eva ca ||
samayasyānapākarma vivādaḥ kṣetrajas tathā |
strīpuṃsayoś ca saṃbandho dāyabhāgo ‘tha sāhasam ||
vākpāruṣyaṃ tathā proktaṃ daṇḍapāruṣyam eva ca |
dyūtaṃ prakīrṇakaṃ caivaṃ .................. || iti |
nāradavacanānusāri nibandhanakāravacanam | ataś ca tadvyākhyeyasyāpi gautamasūtrasya nāradavacanānusāritvam eva | etadanusāreṇaivāsmābhir apy uktā vivādapadānāṃ saṃgatiḥ | ataś ca ṛṇādānādidāyavibhāgāntānāṃ deyanibandhantvena pratipādanaṃ sāhasādipañcakasya daṇḍanibandhanatvam iti sūcayitum “atha sāhasam” ity atha śabdaḥ prayuktaḥ | ataś ca deyapratipādanānantaraṃ daṇḍapratipādanasyāvasara iti saṃgatiḥ | atrāpi vākpāruṣyadaṇḍapāruṣyāṇāṃ parasparaṃ bhedābhāve ‘pi daṇḍālpatvamahatvārthaṃ pṛthag grahaṇam | ataś ca prakaraṇatrayasyāpi saṃgatir uktaiva | anena vijñānayoginā yad uktaṃ vivādapadānāṃ parasparaṃ saṃgatir nāstīti, tatparās taṃ veditavyam | a
tra kātyāyanaḥ |
sahasā yat kṛtaṃ karma tat sāhasam udāhṛtam | iti |
etad evāha nāradaḥ |
sahasā kriyate karma yat kiṃcid baladarpitaiḥ |
[452]        tat sāhasam iti proktaṃ saho balam ihocyate || iti |
sādhāraṇaparadhanayor haraṇaṃ balāvaṣṭambhena kriyamāṇaṃ sāhasam ity arthaḥ | ata evāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
sāmānyadravyaprasabhaharaṇāt sāhasaṃ smṛtam | iti |
etac caturvidham ity āha viṣṇuḥ: “paradārābhimarśaṃ steyam ubhayaṃ pāruṣyaṃ parahiṃsā ca” iti | bṛhaspatis tu |
manuṣyaharaṇaṃ cauryaṃ paradārābhimarśanam |
pāruṣyam ubhayaṃ ceti sāhasaṃ tu caturvidham ||
iti vacanadvayakramasya prayojanābhāvād avivakṣitam iti mantavyam | yat tu śaṅkhalikhitoktam: “cauryapāruṣyahiṃsāh sāhasapadavācyāḥ” iti, tatra strīsaṃgrahaṇasya cauryānatirekāt strīsaṃgrahaṇasteye cauryapadena saṃgṛhīte iti mantavyam |
tat punas trividhaṃ jñeyaṃ prathamaṃ madhyamaṃ tathā |
uttamaṃ ceti śāstreṣu tasyoktaṃ lakṣaṇaṃ pṛthak ||
phalamūlodakādīnāṃ kṣetropakaraṇasya ca |
bhaṅgākṣepo ‘samarthādyaiḥ prathamaṃ sāhasaṃ smṛtam ||
vāsaḥ paśvannapānānāṃ gṛhopakaraṇasya ca |
etenaiva prakāreṇa madhyamaṃ sāhasaṃ smṛtam ||
vyāpādo viṣaśastrādyaiḥ paradārābhimarśanam |
prāṇoparodhi yac cānyad uttamaṃ sāhasaṃ smṛtam ||
tasya daṇḍakiryāpekṣā prathamasya daśāparaḥ |
madhyamasya tu śāstrajñaiḥ dṛṣṭaḥ pañcaśatāparaḥ ||
[453]        uttame sāhase daṇḍaḥ sahasrāpara ucyate |
vadhaḥ sarvaharaṇaṃ purān nirvāsanaṃ tu vā ||
tadaṅgaccheda ity ukto daṇḍa uttamasāhase ||
ayam arthaḥ – aśītyadhikasahasradaṇḍo yatroktaḥ sa uttamasāhasasaṃjñakaḥ | catvāriṃśadadhikapañcaśataparimito daṇḍo madhyamasāhasasaṃjñakaḥ | saptatyadhikaśata-dvayaparimito daṇḍo ‘dhamasāhasasaṃjñakaḥ iti | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
sāśītipaṇasāhasro daṇḍa uttamasāhasaḥ |
tadardhaṃ madhyamaḥ proktas tadardham adhamaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
etad vacanaṃ kecid evaṃ vyācakṣate –
sāśītipaṇasāharadaṇḍa uttamasāhasaḥ |
iti daṇḍavidhāyakaṃ vacanaṃ na tu saṃjñāvidhāyakam | asmin mate daṇḍa uttamasāhase iti saptamyantāni padāni | paṇānāṃ sahasraṃ pramāṇam asyeti sāhasraḥ | aśītyā saha vartata iti sāśītiḥ | uttamasāhase aśītyadhikapaṇasahasro daṇḍa ity arthaḥ | uttamaviṣayasāhasam uttamasāhasam | madhyamaviṣayasāhasaṃ madhyamasāhasam adhamaviṣayasāhasam adhamasāhasam | tasyaiva prathamasāhasam iti nāmāntaram | tathā cāyam arthaḥ:
uttamapuruṣasaṃbandhinaḥ kṣetrāder apahāraḥ tathā madhyamapuruṣasaṃbandhinaḥ tathā nikṛṣṭapuruṣasaṃbandhinaḥ kṣetrāder apahāraḥ tathā uttamadravyasya aśvasuvarṇamaṇyāder apahāraḥ madhyamasya mahiṣīvrīhigodhūmādeḥ nikṛṣṭasya dravyasya mṛnmayaghaṭāder aparāraḥ yathākramam uttamamadhyamādhamasaṃjñako bhavati | tad uttaratra procyate | “daṇḍa uttamasāhasaḥ” ityādi sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ śateṣu lakṣaṇayā śabda ity āhuḥ | [454] prathamasya daśāpara iti kṣudradravyeṣu daśāparamūlyeṣu tanmūlyadviguṇāṣṭa-guṇaṣoḍaśaguṇadvātriṃśadguṇacatuḥṣaṣṭiguṇaparyanto daṇḍaḥ | kṣudradravye śūdravaiśyakṣatriyabrāhmaṇatapasviṣu māṣamūlyena vyavasthāniyamaḥ | evaṃ śatāpar ity atrāpy ūhyam| sahasramūlye tadaṅgachedādi yojyam iti |
atra śatādisaṃkhyāsaṃkhyeyasya sāśītipaṇasāhasra ityādivad viśeṣānupādānāt kriyāpekṣayā mṛnmayabhāṇḍadiṣu tāvatsaṃkhyakaraṇamūlyakalpanāyā anucitatvā paṇāṣṭamo bhāgo māṣa eva kākaṇikā vā kalpyate | aṣṭamāṣaparimitaḥ paṇa evāsmin vyavahārakāṇḍe svīkaraṇīya iti divyamātṛkāyām uktam ata evāvadhāryam |
atra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
dhigdaṇḍas tv atha vāgdaṇḍo dhanadaṇḍo vadhas tathā |
yojyā vyastāḥ samastā va hy aparādhavaśād iha || iti |
asyārthaḥ – dhigdaṇḍo dhig iti manasā kutsanam | vāgdaṇḍas tu paruṣavacanātmakaḥ | dhanadaṇḍaḥ prasiddhaḥ | vadhadaṇḍaḥ śarīraprāṇaviyojanaparyantaś ca ete caturvidhā daṇḍāḥ vyastāḥ ekaikaśaḥ samastāḥ dvitricaturā vā aparādhānusāreṇa yojyāḥ | tadyojanakramam āha manuḥ |
dhigdaṇḍaṃ prathamaṃ kuryād vāgdhaṇḍaṃ tadanantaram |
tṛtīyaṃ dhanadaṇḍaṃ tu vadhadaṇḍam ataḥ param || iti |
evaṃ prativyakti daṇḍavidhānaṃ vidhātum aśakyam iti sāmānyatas tad upāyam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
jñātvāparādhaṃ deśaṃ ca kālaṃ balam athāpi ca |
[455]        vayaḥ karma ca vittaṃ ca daṇḍaṃ daṇḍyeṣu pātayet ||
aparādhaṃ jñātvā tadanusāreṇa daṇḍyeṣu daṇḍārheṣu daṇḍo yojya iti | yathāha viṣṇuḥ: “uttamamadhyamādhamadravyāṇāṃ haraṇe mūlyānusārato daṇḍaḥ” iti | asyārtho manunā spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ | yathā –
hiraṇyaratnakauśeyastrīpuṃśā gajavājibhiḥ |
devabrāhmaṇarājñāṃ ca dravyaṃ vijñeyam uttamam || iti |
madhyamadravyaṃ tu |
vāsaḥ kauśeyavarjaṃ ca govarjaṃ paśavas tathā |
hiraṇyavarjaṃ lohaṃ ca madhyamaṃ vrīhayas tathā ||
vrīhiśabdo yavagodhūmānām upalakṣakaḥ |
mṛdbhāṇḍāsanakhaṭvāsthidārucarmatṛṇādi yat |
śibirānnaṃ kṛtānnaṃ ca kṣudradravyam udāhṛtam || iti |
evaṃ triṣu dravyeṣu prathamamadhyamādhamasāhasavad daṇḍanīya ity autsargiko ‘rthaḥ | sa evāha manuḥ |
sāhaseṣu ya evoktaḥ triṣu daṇḍo manīṣibhiḥ |
sa eva daṇḍaḥ steye ‘pi dravyeṣu triṣv anukramāt || iti |
sāhasasteyayor daṇḍaikyād ekam eva padam, tathāpi tanmate pṛthagabhidhānād daṇḍātideśo na viruddha iti rahasyam | viṣṇus tu jātipragraheṇāpi viśeṣam āha: “ukto daṇḍaḥ śūdrād vaiśyakṣatriyabrāhmaṇānāṃ viduṣāṃ steye dvigunottarāṇi kilbiṣāṇi” iti | ayam arthaḥ – yasminn apahāre yo daṇḍaḥ uktaḥ sa śūdrakartṛke apahāryadravyasya aṣṭaguṇa āpādanīyaḥ | vaiśyakartṛke apahāre ṣoḍaśaguṇaḥ | kṣatriyakartṛke apahāre dvātriṃśadgunaḥ | brāhmaṇakartṛke [456] apahāre catuḥṣaṣṭiguṇo daṇḍa āpādanīyaḥ | evaṃ viṭchūdrādikartṛke ‘pahāre daṇḍa ūhanīyaḥ,
brāhmaṇe śataguṇo vā pūrṇaṃ vāpi śataṃ bhavet |
iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | nikṛṣṭeṣu dviguṇaṃ kalpayet “nikṛṣṭe dviguṇaṃ kalpayet” iti tenaivoktatvāt | nikṛṣṭo jātyā ācāreṇa dhanena veti nibandhanakāraḥ | na ca divyamātṛkāyāṃ dviguṇārthe yathābhihitā samayakriyā vaiśyasya triguṇārthe rājanyasya caturguṇārthe brāhmaṇasyeti viṣṇuvacane śūdravaiśyakṣatriyabrāhmaṇānām ekadvitricatuḥsaṃkhyayā divyakriyāyāṃ tāratamyam uktam, atrāpi daṇḍakalpanam aṣṭamāṃśaṣoḍaśāṃśacaturviṃśadvāviṃśakalpanayā bhāvyam iti vācyam | daṇḍavidhāyakadivyavidhāyakaśāstrayor vedamūlatvena nyāyamūlatvābhāvāt vācanikam idaṃ tāratamyam | atra viśeṣam āha viṣṇuḥ: “daśakumbhīdhānyaharaṇe ekādaśaguṇaṃ dāpyaḥ” iti | “viṃśatidroṇakaṃ kumbhīkhārīparyāyaḥ” iti candrikākāraḥ | taddviguṇāpahāre ekādaśaguṇaṃ tad dhanaṃ dāpayitvā aṅgachedanavadharūpadaṇḍā yojyā yathāyogam | atra manuḥ viśeṣam āha |
puruṣāṇāṃ kulīnānāṃ nārīṇāṃ ca viśeṣataḥ |
ratnānāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ haraṇe vadham arhati ||
kulīnānāṃ tu daṇḍāntaram āha sa eva |
puruṣaṃ harato daṇḍa ukta uttamasāhasaḥ |
stryaparādhe tu sarvasvaṃ kanyāṃtu harato vadhaḥ || iti |
kṣudradravyāṇāṃ tu māṣānyūnamūlyānāṃ mūlyāt pañcaguṇo daṇḍaḥ | yathāha nāradaḥ |
kāṣṭhabhāṇḍatṛṇādīnāṃ mṛnmayānāṃ tathaiva ca |
[457]        veṇuvaiṇavabhāṇḍānāṃ tathā snāyvasthicarmaṇām ||
śākhānām ārdramūlānāṃ haraṇe phalamūlayoḥ |
gorasekṣuvikārāṇāṃ tathā lavaṇatailayoḥ ||
pakvānnānāṃ kṛtānnānāṃ matsyānām āmiṣasya ca |
sarveṣām alpamūlyānāṃ mūlyāt pañcaguṇo damaḥ || iti |
yad api viṣṇuvacanam “kṣudradravyāpahāre mūlyād dviguṇo damaḥ” iti, etad alpaprayojanaśarāvādiviṣayam iti veditavyam |
viṣṇus tu viśeṣam āha: “maṇīnāṃ prāṇakalañjāpahāre hastachedo vadhaḥ kukūlārohaṇam” iti | prāṇo nāma caturmāṣātmakaḥ parimāṇaviśeṣaḥ | kalañjo nāma dharaṇam | yathāha viṣṇuguptaḥ |
pañcaguñjalako māṣaḥ prāṇas teṣu caturguṇaiḥ |
kalañjo dharaṇaṃ prāhuḥ maṇimānaviśāradāḥ || iti |
atra kecic caturguṇaiḥ iti padasāmarthyād viṃśatiguṇātmako māṣo na bhavati, vidheyasyaiva prādhānyāt | pradhānaparāmarśasya nyāyyatvāt, ṣaḍaśamāṣātmakaḥ prāṇa ity āhuḥ | etad eva samyak, tathā vyavahārāt | kalañjas tu prāṇadvitayam “kalañjaḥ prāṇayugyakam” iti lakṣaṇāt | lokācārato vyavasthā | etat sarvaṃ daṇḍakathaṃ balāvaṣṭambhe veditavyam, sāhasaṃ prakṛtya manvādibhir uktatvāt | yat tu manunoktam,
syāt sāhasaṃ tva anvayavat prasabhaṃ karma yat kṛtam |
niranvayaṃ bhavet steyaṃ vañcayitvāpakarṣaṇam ||
kṛtvāpahnūyeta yadīti pāṭhāntaram | tathā ca kātyāyanaḥ |
sānvayas tv apahāro yaḥ prasahya haraṇaṃ ca yat |
sāhasaṃ ca bhaved evaṃ steyam uktaṃ vinihnave || [458] iti |
etac ca vyākhyātṛbhir medhātithi-asahāya-yajñapati-prabhṛtibhir vijñāneśvara-prabhṛtibhiś ca vyākhyātam | anvayavad anvayayuktaṃ dravyarakṣaṇarājādhyakṣādisamakṣam | prasabhaṃ balāvaṣṭambhena yat paradhanaharaṇādikaṃ kriyate tat sāhasam | anyathā kriyamāṇaṃ cauryapadābhilipyaṃ steyam ity etāvad eva lakṣaṇam | balāvaṣṭambhena rājādyasamakṣam api sāhasam evety uktaṃ prāk vakṣyate ca | ataś ca medhātithyādivyākhyānaṃ svamatikalpitam iti mantavyam | ata evāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
sāṃprataṃ sāhasasteyaṃ śrūyatāṃ krodhalobhajam | iti |
sāhasaṃ steyaṃ sāhasalakṣaṇasteyam iti candrikākāraḥ | atra vyāsaḥ |
jñātvā tu ghātakaṃ samyak sasahāyaṃ sabāndhavam |
hanyāc citravadhopāyair udvejanakarair nṛpaḥ || iti |
smṛtyantare ‘pi |
bandīgrahāṃs tathā rājakuñjarāṇāṃ ca hāriṇaḥ |
prasahya ghātinaś caiva śūlān āropayen narān || iti |
bṛhaspatir api |
prakāśavadhakāryeṣu tathā copāṃśughātakān |
jñātvā samyag dhanaṃ hṛtvā hantavyā vividhair vadhaiḥ || iti |
atra vividhair vadhair udvejanakarair ity anena udvejanakaraṇaṃ vividhopāyānāṃ vikalpaḥ | upāṃśughātajñānaprakāram āha sa eva |
ghātaḥ saṃdṛśyate yatra ghātakas tu na dṛśyate |
pūrvam evānumānena jñātavyaḥ sa mahībhṛtā ||
vijñeyaḥ sādhusaṃsargaiḥ cihnair oṣṭhena vā punaḥ |
eṣo ‘pi ghātakānāṃ tu taskarāṇāṃ bhaved iti || [459]
atra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
grāhakair gṛhyate coro loptreṇātha padena vā |
pūrvakarmāparādhe ca tathā cāśuddhavāsakaḥ ||
ayam arthaḥ – yaś coro ‘yam iti janair vikhyāpyate asau grāhakaiḥ – rājapuruṣadaṇḍapāśakarādibhiḥ grahītavyaḥ | loptram – gṛhītadravyaikadeśaḥ cihnaṃ vā khanitrādi | padaṃ coreṇa bhūmau prakṣiptapadacihnaṃ prāk prakhyātacauryaḥ pūrvakarmāparādhī | aśuddhavāsaka asahasthānavāsavān | kiṃ ca,
anyo ‘pi śaṅkayā grāhyaḥ jātināmādinihṇavaiḥ |
dyūtastrīpānasaktāś ca śuṣkabhinnamukhasvarāḥ ||
paradravyagṛhāṇāṃ ca pṛcchakā gūḍhacāribhiḥ |
nirāyā vyayavantaś ca vinaṣṭadravyavikrayaḥ ||
ityādicoragrahaṇopāyāḥ mānavīyadharmaśāstre pratipāditāḥ | lokaprasiddhatvān neha prapañcyante | etair liṅgair ayam eva cora iti niścityādhaḥ pātayituṃ na śakyate | tathāha nāradaḥ |
anyahastāt paribhraṣṭam akāmād utthitaṃ tu vā |
corair vāpi pratikṣiptaṃ loptraṃ yatnāt parīkṣayet || iti |
tathā |
asatyāḥ satyasaṃkāśās tathyāś cātathyasaṃnibhāḥ |
dṛśyante vividhā bhāvās tasmād yuktaṃ parīkṣaṇam || iti |
sākṣyādimānuṣapramāṇair dhaṭādidivyapramāṇaiś ca coratvasaṃdehanirākaraṇadvārā coratvaṃ nirṇetavyam iti kim arthaṃ lokasiddhacoragrahaṇopāyapratipādanāya svānubhavenety uparatam | atra steyamātraviṣayam āha manuḥ | [460]
dvividhāṃs taskarān vidyāt paradravyahāriṇaḥ |
prakāśāṃś cāprakāśāṃś ca cāracakṣur mahīpatiḥ ||
bṛhaspatir api |
prakāśāś cāprakāśāś ca taskarā dvividhā smṛtāḥ |
prajñāsāmarthyamāyābhiḥ prabhinnās te sahasradhā ||
dvividhasyāpi corasya lakṣaṇam āha nāradaḥ |
prakāśavañcakās tatra kūṭamānatulās tathā |
utkocakāḥ sopadhikāḥ kitavāḥ paṇyayoṣitaḥ ||
pratirūpakarāś caiva maṅgalādeśavṛttayaḥ || iti |
maṅgalādeśena jīvantīti maṅgalādeśavṛttayaḥ | yathāha vyāsaḥ |
strīpuṃsau vañcayantīha maṅgalādeśavṛttayaḥ |
gṛhṇanti chadmanā hy artham anāryās tv āryaliṅgakāḥ || iti |
śeṣaṃ sugamam | atra viṣṇuḥ: “ete doṣānurūpata eva daṇḍyāḥ” iti | ete – prakāśataskarāḥ | na punar dhanānurūpata ity arthaḥ | kṛṭamānāḥ kūṭatulāḥ utkocajīvinaḥ kapaṭopāyāḥ kitavāḥ paṇyayoṣitaḥ pratirūpakarāḥ – mithyānāṇakādikāriṇaḥ maṅgalādeśavṛttayaś cakāreṇa samuccitāḥ | naigamādyāś ca bhūridhanā api dhanadaṇḍyā api tu doṣānusāreneti – asmin doṣe etāvān daṇḍa iti mūlyakalpanayā vidhīyata ity arthaḥ |
prachannataskarāṇāṃ svarūpam āha viṣṇuḥ: “doṣānuviddhāḥ prachannāḥ gūḍhataskarāḥ | | utkṣepakaḥ saṃdhibhettā paśustrīgranthibhedakāḥ parasparaṃ daṇḍyāḥ” iti | doṣā rātriḥ | ayam evārthaḥ spaṣṭīkṛto nāradena | [461]
sādhanādyanvitā rātrau vicaranty avibhāvitāḥ |
avijñātanivāsāś ca jñeyāḥ prachannataskarāḥ || iti |
rātrāv iti prāyikābhiprāyam, divāpy araṇyādāv avibhāvkānāṃ saṃbhavāt | teṣāṃ bhedam āha sa eva |
utkṣepakaḥ saṃdhibhettā pānthamuḍ granthibhedakaḥ |
strīpuṃsoś ca paśusteyī coro navavidhaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
