utkṣepako nāma dhaninām avadhānābhāvam avadhārya antikasthaṃ dhanam udgṛhṇann iti | yad vā nāṇakādisaṃdarśanārthaṃ dattaṃ tatkṣaṇād eva hastalāghavenānyathayati sa ity āha bhāruciḥ | saṃdhibhettā gṛhasaṃcaraṇasthāne dṛṣṭasaṃdhāv avasthāya bhittibhettā – suraṅgādinirmāteti yāvat | pānthamuṭ pathikānāṃ kāntārādau dhanaṃ muṣṇātīti pānthamuṭ – paśyatohara ity arthaḥ | granthibhedakaḥ paridhānādigranthiṃ bhinatti dhanaṃ grahītum | śeṣaṃ sugatam | teṣāṃ madhye utkṣepakagranthibhedakayor daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
utkṣepakagranthibhedau karasaṃdaṃśahīnakau |
karasaṃdaṃśo nāma tarjanyaṅguṣṭhātmakaḥ paradravyādānahetur vivakṣitaḥ | saṃdhibhettur daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
saṃdhiṃ bhittvā tu ye cauryaṃ rātrau kurvanti taskarāḥ |
teṣāṃ chitvā nṛpo hastau tīkṣṇaśūle niveśayet ||
tan iti śeṣaḥ | vyāsas tu viśeṣam āha |
saṃdhiṃ chitvānekavidhaṃ dhanaṃ prāpnoti vai gṛhāt |
pradāpya svāmine sarvaṃ tīkṣṇaśule niveśayet ||
paśyatoharasya daṇḍam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
tathā pānthamuṣo vṛkṣe galaṃ badhvāvalambayet | [462]
manus tu viśeṣam āha |
aṅgulī granthibhedasya bhedayet prathamagrahe |
dvitīye hastacaraṇau tṛtīye vadham arhati || iti |
aṅgulī tarjanyaṅguṣṭhau |
prathame granthivedānām aṅgulyaṅguṣṭhayor vadhaḥ |
iti bṛhaspatismaraṇāt | strīpūmastenayor daṇḍam āha vyāsaḥ |
strīhartā lohaśayane dahyate vā kaṭāgninā |
narahartā hastapādau chittvā sthāpyaś catuṣpathe ||
gostenasya daṇḍam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
gohartur nāsikāṃ chittvā badhvāmbuni niveśayet |
aśvapaśustenayor daṇḍam āha vyāsaḥ |
aśvāpaharaṇe pādau kaṭiṃ chittvā pramāpayet |
paśuhartus tv agrapādaṃ tīkṣṇaśastreṇa kartayet ||
atra nāradas tu viśeṣam āha |
mahāpaśuṃ stenayato daṇḍa uttamasāhasaḥ |
madhyamaṃ madhyamapaśau pūrvaṃ śūdrapaśau (kṣudra-?) hṛte ||
madhyamaṃ madhamasāhasaṃ | pūrvaṃ prathamasāhasam | suvarṇādisteye daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
suvarṇarajatādīnām uttamānāṃ ca sāhasāḥ |
ratnānāṃ caiva sarveṣāṃ śatād abhyadhike vadhaḥ ||
pañcāśatas tv abhyadhike hastachedanam iṣyate |
śateṣv ekādaśaguṇaṃ mūlyād daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet ||
aṅgabhaṅgavadhāntadaṇḍavidhānaṃ sarvaṃ rājanyavaiśyaśūdrādīnām eva na tu brāhmaṇasya | tathā ca yamaḥ | [463]
na śārīro brāhmaṇasya daṇḍo bhavati karhicit |
tathāparādhaṃ vipraṃ tu vikarmāṇy api kārayet |
avadhyā brāhmaṇā gāvo loke ‘smin vaidikī śrutiḥ || iti |
vigarhitaṃ karma paśvādipālanam iti candrikākāraḥ | kārāgṛhanivāsa iti bhārucyādayaḥ | ata evāha hārītaḥ |
dvijātīnām avadhyatvān muṇḍanaṃ vadha iṣyate | iti |
ātatāyino ‘pi brāhmaṇasya vadho na vidyata iti pratipāditaṃ prakaraṇādau | yat tu viṣṇunoktaṃ yatra vadhaś coditaḥ tatrendriyanirodhaḥ kartavyaḥ | tānīndriyāni daśa | jñānendriyāṇi pañca karmendriyāṇi pañca vākpāṇipādapāyūpasthaśrotratvakcakṣurjihvāghrāṇātmakāni | teṣāṃ nirodhaḥ kartanaṃ iti kecit |
tan na, pūrvavacanavirodhāt | kiṃ tu nirodhanaṃ nāma śṛṅkhalādibandhaḥ pāṇipādasya | vāṅnirodho mukhabandhaḥ | yat tu sumantunoktam: “vadhapratinidhiḥ brāhmaṇasya cakṣurnirodhaḥ” iti | nirodhaḥ utpāṭanam | na cāsya vacanasya cakṣur iti viśeṣopādānena viśeṣaparatvāt pūrvavacanasyātropasaṃhāra iti vācyam | ata evāhuḥ mahāparādhe brāhmaṇasya vadhapratinidhitven śiromauṇḍyaṃ deśavipravāsaḥ kārāgṛhabandhaḥ netrotpāṭanam iti yathākramam aparādhatāratamyena yojanīyam iti bhāruciprabhṛtayaḥ | candrikākāras tu,
“na śārīro brāhmaṇasya daṇḍo bhavati karhicit | iti
karhicitpadasāmarthyāt akṣinirodho nāmākṣinibandhanam akṣicarmasevanaṃ na tūtpāṭanam” ity āha | tad eva samyag iti vṛddhāḥ | mahāparādhe striyā api aṅgacchedādivadhānto daṇḍo ‘stīty āha yājñavalkyaḥ | [464]
vipraduṣṭāṃ striyaṃ caiva puruṣaghnīm agarbhiṇīm |
setubhedakarīṃ cāpsu śilāṃ badhvā praveśayet ||
vipraduṣṭāṃ viśeṣeṇa praduṣṭām atiduṣṭāṃ bhrūṇaghnīṃ garbhapātinīm iti yāvat | yā ca puruṣasya hantrī | kiṃ ca viṣāgnidāṃ patigurujināpatyapramāpaṇīm |
vikarṇakaranāsoṣṭhīṃ kṛtvā gobhiḥ pramāpayet |
atrāpy agarbhiṇīm ity anvartate iti vijñāneśaḥ | gobhir atidurdāntaiḥ balīvardaiḥ pramāpayen mārayed ity arthaḥ | atra coropakāriṇāṃ daṇḍam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhaktāvakāśāgnyudakamantropakaraṇavyayān |
datvā corasya cāhantur jānanto dama uttamaḥ ||
asyārthaḥ – bhaktam annam avakāśo nivāsasthānam | agniḥ kare śītāpanodanādyartham | udakaṃ tṛṣitasya | mantraḥ coreṇa sahālocanam anena cauryaprakāropadeśā lakṣyante | upakaraṇaṃ cauryasādhanaṃ suruṅgādinirmāṇārthaṃ khnitrādidānam | vyayaḥ pātheyam | jānann apīty anena ajānatāṃ madhyamasāhasam iti bhāruciḥ,
śaktāś ca ya upekṣante te ‘pi taddoṣabhāginaḥ |
iti nāradasmaraṇāt | coropekṣādyuttamasāhasa eva daṇḍa iti gamyate | tatrāpi jñānata eva | ajñānatas tu madhyamasāhasam iti bhāruciḥ | anenaivābhiprāyeṇāha kātyāyanaḥ |
ārambhakaḥ sahāyaś ca tathā mārgopadeśakaḥ |
āśrayaḥ śastradātā ca bhaktadātā vikarmaṇām ||
yad vopadeśakaś caiva tadvināśapradarśakaḥ |
upekṣākāryayuktaś ca doṣavaktānumodakaḥ ||
[465]        yathāśaktyanurūpaṃ tu daṇḍam eṣāṃ prakalpayet || iti |
atra yājñavalkyas tu viśeṣam āha |
yaḥ sāhasaṃ kārayati sa dāpyo dviguṇaṃ damam |
yaś caivam uktvāhaṃ dātā kārayet sa caturguṇam || iti |
dāpya ity arthaḥ | avijñātakartṛke hanane hantṛjñānopāyam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
avijñātahatasyāśu kalahaṃ sutabāndhavāḥ |
praṣṭavyā yoṣitaś cāsya parapuṃsi ratāḥ pṛthak ||
ajñātapuruṣeṇa ghātitasya saṃbandhinaḥ sutā pratyāsannāś ca bāndhavāḥ kenāsya kalaho jāta iti kalaham āśu praṣṭavyāḥ | śeṣaṃ sugamam | atra pratiprasavam āha manuḥ |
dvijo ‘dhvagaḥ kṣīṇavṛttiḥ dvāv ikṣū dve ca mūlake |
ādadānaḥ parakṣetrān na daṇḍaṃ dātum arhati ||
caṇakavrīhigodhūmayavānāṃ mudgamāṣayoḥ |
aniṣiddhair grahītavyā muṣṭir ekā pathi sthitaiḥ || iti |
strīsaṃgrahe viśeṣam āha saṃvartaḥ |
necchantyā yāni cihnāni balātkārakṛtāni ca |
punaḥ punaḥ prasaṅgeṣu nārīṇāṃ tāni śṛṇvataḥ ||
nakhadantakṣatā kṣāmā sakacagrahapīḍitā |
sadyo vidhvaṃsitā nārī balātkāreṇa dūṣitā ||
uccair vikrośayantī ca rudatī lokasaṃnidhau |
tasyā nāma vadantī ca yathāhaṃ tena dūṣitā ||
śoced evaṃvidhair liṅgair vraṇīkṛtapayodharā |
chinnālaṅkārakeśaiś ca mukulīkṛtalocanā ||
[466]        rājñā sabhyaiḥ sabhāṃ nītvā svayam anviṣya tat kṣaṇāt |
yad brūyāt sahajaṃ vākyaṃ tat kartavyaṃ prayatnataḥ ||
vivāde sākṣiṇām atra na kurvīta parigraham |
vartanād abhiśastasya na divyaṃ datum arhati ||
cihnair eva atra rājñā nirṇetavyam | na divyaṃ na ca sākṣiṇaḥ iti vadataś candrikākārasyāyam āśayaḥ – na ca sarvadā asmin viṣaye sākṣidivyayor anavatāra iti | kiṃ tu rājñā sabhyaiś ca nirṇaye kṛte sākṣidivyayor apekṣā nāsti | tadā tayoar anavatāraḥ | yadā tu kāntā vṛṣasyantī kapaṭasvayaṃkṛtakucataṭanakhakṣatādharadantakṣatādibhiḥ vipralambhayati tadā sākṣidivyayor avakāśa iti |
vidhavāgamane viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
svachandavidhavāgāmī vikruṣṭe nābhidhāvakaḥ |
akāraṇena vikroṣṭā caṇḍālaś cottamāṃ spṛśan ||
śūdrapravrajitānāṃ ca daive pitrye ca bhojakaḥ |
ayuktaṃ śapathaṃ kurvan ayogyo yogyakarmakṛt ||
vṛṣaśūdrapaśūnāṃ ca puṃstvasya pratighātakṛt |
sādhāraṇasyāpalāpī dāsīgarbhavināśakṛt ||
pitāputrasvasṛbhrātṛdampatyācāryaśiṣyakāḥ |
eṣām apatitānyonyatyāgī ca śatadaṇḍabhāk ||
niyogaṃ vinā svecchayā vidhavāṃ gacchatīti svachandavidhavāgāmī | svakulaiś corādibhayād vikruṣṭe ākrośe tannivāraṇasamartho ‘pi nābhidhāvatīti kruṣṭe nābhidhāvakaḥ | ayuktaṃ śapathaṃ karotīty ayuktaśapathakṛt | puṃstvasya prajājananaśakteḥ vināśakaḥ | vṛkṣakṣudrapaśūnām iti pāṭhe hiṅgvādyauṣadhaprayogeṇa vṛkṣādeḥ [467] phalaprasūnānāṃ pātayiteti vijñāneśaḥ | ete paṇaśataṃ daṇḍārhāḥ pratyekam iti | yat tu bṛhaspatinoktam,
sahasā kāmayed yas tu dhanaṃ tasyākhilaṃ haret |
utkṛtya liṅgavṛṣaṇau bhrāmayed gardhabhena tu ||
tat tu brāhmaṇīṃ kāmayamānasya brāhmaṇetarasya viṣaye | kāmayet parastriyam iti śeṣaḥ | manus tu viśeṣam āha |
sacihnaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ kṛtvā svarāṣṭrād vinivāsayet |
brāhmaṇaṃ punar mahaty aparādhe ‘pi na ghātayet | api tu lalāṭe ‘ṅkayitvā svarāṣṭrān nirvivāsayet | aṅkanaṃ ca bhagākāreṇa kāryam | tathā ca vṛddhavasiṣṭhaḥ |
surāpāne dhvajāṅkanaṃ steye ca svapadāṅkanam |
brahmahatyāyām gardabhāṅganaṃ gurutalpagamane bhagāṅkanam || iti |
manus tu viśeṣam āha |
gurutalpe bhagaḥ kāryaḥ surāpāne surādhvajaḥ |
steye ca śvapadaṃ kāryaṃ brahmahaṇy aśirāḥ pumān ||
aśirāḥ pumān – kabandhaḥ | śeṣaṃ sugamam | rājapatnīgamane viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
kṣetraveśmavanagrāmavivītakhaladāhakāḥ |
rājapatnyabhigāmī ca dagdhavyāḥ sakaṭāgninā ||
kaṭair vīraṇatṛṇamayaiḥ ghāsanirmitair dāmabhiḥ veṣṭayitvā dagdhavyā ity arthaḥ | kṣetrāder dāhakānāṃ maraṇadaṇḍaprasaṅgāt viśeṣārtho ‘yam ārambhaḥ | nāradas tu paradāragamanasya trividhyam uktvā daṇḍaviśeṣam āha |
trividhaṃ tat samākhyātaṃ prathamaṃ madhyottamam |
adeśakālasaṃbhāṣā nirjane ca parastriyam ||
[468]        kaṛākṣavīkṣaṇaṃ hāsyaṃ prathamaṃ sāhasaṃ smṛtam |
preṣaṇaṃ gandhamālyānāṃ dhūpabhūṣaṇavāsasām ||
pralobhanaṃ cānnapānair madhyamaṃ sāhasaṃ smṛtam |
sahāsanaṃ vivikte tu parasparam upāśrayaḥ ||
keśākeśigrahaś caiva samyak saṃgrahaṇaṃ smṛtam ||
ayam arthaḥ – adeśe ārāmādau akāle andhakāre nirjane vijane sallāpanaṃ karoti sahaikamañcādau ciram avatiṣṭhate so ‘pi saṃgrahaṇe pravṛttaḥ | etac ca śaṅkyamānapuruṣaviṣayam itarasya na doṣaḥ | tathāha manuḥ |
yas tv anākāritaḥ pūrvaṃ vibhāṣetāpi kāranāt |
na doṣaṃ prāpnuyāt kiṃcin na hi tasya vyatikramaḥ || iti |
etac ca kaṭākṣavikṣaṇādivyatiriktasahaikamañcasthitimātraviṣayam | tathā ca viṣṇuḥ: “saṃlobhanāpāṅgadarśanavihasanasahaikatranivāsāḥ saṃgrahagamagāḥ” iti | tathā ca gautamaḥ: “paradārābhimṛṣṭaḥ stambhaś ced grāhyaḥ” iti | stabdhaḥ sparśane kṣamate | tathā ca manuḥ |
striyaṃ spṛśed adeśe yaḥ spṛṣṭo vā marṣayet tadā |
parasparasyānumate sarvaṃ saṃgrahaṇaṃ smṛtam || iti |
viṣṇus tu viśeṣam āha: “mohād iyaṃ mayā bhuketi yo vadati sa tu grāhyaḥ” iti | moho darpādīnām upalakṣakaḥ | yathāha manuḥ |
darpād vā yadi vā mohāt ślāghayā vā svayaṃ vadet |
pūrvaṃ mayeyaṃ bhukteti tac ca saṃgrahaṇaṃ smṛtam || iti |
tathā ca gautamaḥ: “pratiṣedhe pumān daṇḍyaḥ tadardhaṃ strī” iti | asyārtho vivṛto nibandhanakāreṇa – “patipitrādibhir yena [469] saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ niṣedhyaṃ tatra pravartamānā strī śatapaṇaṃ daṇḍyā | puruṣo ‘py evaṃ niṣiddhaḥ san pravartamāno dviśataṃ daṇḍyaḥ” iti |
viṣṇus tu viśeṣam āha: “pratiṣiddhe pravartamānayoḥ strīpuṃsayoḥ saṃgrahaṇr varṇānusāreṇa daṇḍaḥ” iti | etac cāvarodhastrīviṣayam iti bhāruciḥ | jātivyavasthayā daṇḍam āha viṣṇuḥ: “guptaparadārābhigamane sāśītipaṇasāhasram” iti | etac ca gurusakhībhāryādivyatiriktaviṣayaṃ draṣṭavyam, yathā –
mātā mātṛṣvasā śvaśrūr mātulānī pitṛṣvasā |
pitṛvyasakhiśiṣyastrī bhaginī tatsakhī snuṣā ||
duhitācāryabhāryā ca sagotrā śaraṇāgatā |
rājñī pravrajitā dhātrī sādhvī varṇottamā ca yā ||
āsām anyatamāṃ gacchan gurutalpaga ucyate |
śiśnasyotkartanāt tatra nānyo daṇḍo vidhīyat ||
iti nāradasmṛteḥ | atra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
sajātyām uttamo daṇḍa ānulomye tu madhyamaḥ |
prātilomye vadhaḥ puṃso nāryāḥ karṇādikartanam || iti |
ayam arthaḥ – caturṇām api varṇānāṃ balātkāreṇa sajātigupaparadādābhimarśane sāśītipaṇasāhasraṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | yadā tv ānulomyena hīnavarṇastriyaṃ guptām abhigacchati tadā madhyamasāhasaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | yadā punaḥ savarṇām aguptām ānulomyena guptāṃ vā vrajati, yathāha manuḥ |
sahasraṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍyo guptāṃ viprāṃ balād vrajan |
śatāni pañca daṇḍyaḥ syād icchantyā saha saṃgataḥ ||
sahasraṃ brāhmaṇo daṇḍaṃ dāpyo gupte tu te vrajan |
[470]        śūdrāyāṃ kṣatriyaviśoḥ sahasraṃ tu bhaved damaḥ || iti |
te kṣatriyavaiśyajāye | prātilomye utkṛṣṭavarṇastrīgamane kṣatriyādeḥ puruṣasya vadhaḥ | etac ca guptādiviṣayam | yathāha viṣṇuḥ: “rājanyavaiśyau brāhmaṇīṃ guptāṃ sevamāno kaṭāgninā dagdhavyau” iti | aguptāgamane viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
brāhmaṇīṃ yady aguptāṃ tu sevetāṃ vaiśyapārthivau |
vaiśyaṃ pañcaśataṃ kuryāt kṣatriyaṃ tu sahasrakam || iti |
śūdraṃ praty āha gautamaḥ: “aguptām apy utkṛṣṭavarṇāṃ śūdro gacchel liṅgachedanam arhati” iti | atrāpiśabdo vyutkrameṇa saṃbandhanīyaḥ | liṅgachedanam arhatīti tena sarvasvāpahārasamuccayaḥ siddhaḥ,
vadhaḥ sarvasvāpahāro guptāṃ tu vrajato ‘sya ca | iti |
asya śūdrasyety arthaḥ |
śūdro guptam aguptaṃ vā dvaijātaṃ varṇam āviśet |
guptau liṅgāṅgasarvasve aguptau dravyamātrakam ||
iti hārītasmṛteḥ | hīnavarṇaṃ vrajantyāḥ striyāḥ karṇādichedanaṃ kāryam iti | yathāha viṣṇuḥ: “ānulomyena vā asavarṇaṃ vā vrajantyāḥ nāsādeḥ kartanaṃ vadhadaṇḍo vā kalpyaḥ” | atra bhāruciḥ,
ānulomyena striyā nāsādikartanam |
asavarṇānugamane vadhadaṇḍaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
iti smṛtim āha | ayaṃ ca daṇḍasyāpy upadeśo rājña eva, tasyaiva pālane ‘dhikārāt | na dvijātimātrasya, brāhmaṇaḥ parīkṣārtham api śastraṃ nādadīteti śastragrahaṇaniṣedhāt,
dhigdaṇḍo vāgdamaś caiva viprāyattāv ubhāv imau | [471]
iti niṣedhāc ca | atra viśeṣam āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau: “kṣatriyavaiśyayor anyonyastryabhigamane daṇḍyāv ubhau” iti | atra vijñāneśaḥ: “yathākramaṃ sahasraśatapaṇātmakau daṇḍau veditavyau | yathāha manuḥ:
vaiśyaś cet kṣatriyāṃ guptāṃ vaiśyāṃ vā kṣatriyo vrajet |
yo brāhmaṇyām aguptāyāṃ tāv ubhau daṇḍam arhataḥ” ||
ity āha | bhārucis tv anyathā vyācaṣṭe: “vaiśyasya bhāryāyāṃ yaḥ kṣatriyaḥ vrajati tasyaiva bhāryāyāṃ vaiśyo vrajati cec chatapaṇātmako daṇḍo veditavyaḥ | tathā anyasya yasya kasyacid vaiśyasya bhāryāyāṃ kṣatriyo gacchati sahasrapaṇān daṇḍyaḥ | kṣatriyāyāṃ vaiśyo gacchan sahasrapaṇān daṇḍyaḥ” iti | kanyāyāṃ tu viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
alaṃkṛtāṃ haran kanyāṃ hy uttamaṃ hy anyathādhamam |
daṇḍaṃ dadyāt savarṇāsu prātilomye vadhaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
vivāhābhimukhīm alaṃkṛtāṃ savarṇāṃ kanyām apaharan uttamasāhasaṃ daṇḍanīyaḥ | tadabhimukhīm asavarṇāṃ haran prathamasāhasam | utkṛṣṭavarṇajāṃ kanyām apaharan kṣatriyādiḥ vadhyaḥ | daṇḍavidhānād apahartuḥ sakāśād ācchidyānyasmai deyeti vijñāneśaḥ | bhārucyādayas tu rājñā daṇḍanīya eva | kiṃ tu tasya varasaṃpattir asti cet, tasmai deyeti paiśācavivāhasya sadbhāvād ity āha | dhāreśvarādayas tu varasaṃpattipratipādanaṃ hy arthadaṇḍānantaram | savarṇā cet tasmai deyety āhuḥ |
atra viṣṇur viśeṣam āha “anulomāsu kāmato na doṣaḥ” iti | ayam arthaḥ – hīnavarṇāṃ sānurāgāṃ kanyāṃ yo ‘paharati tasya na doṣaḥ | doṣābhāvād eva daṇḍābhāva iti daṇḍāpūpikayā [472] gamyate | ayam evāsuravivāha ity āhur asahāyaprabhṛtayaḥ | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
sakāmāsv anulomāsu na doṣas tv anyathā damaḥ | iti |
kanyādūṣaṇe doṣam āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau: “anulomāsu dūṣaṇe tadaṅgacchedaḥ | uttamāsu vadhaḥ” iti | kanyāsv iti śeṣaḥ | tadaṅgachedanam iti yenāṅguliprakṣepeṇa yonikṣataṃ kurvan dūṣayati tadaṅgachedaḥ kāryaḥ | balātkāreṇa karādinā kanyāṃ dūṣyati tasya karachedaḥ kartavyaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
sakāmāṃ dūṣayānas tu nāṅgulichedam arhati |
dviśataṃ tu damaṃ dāpyaḥ prasaṅgavinivṛttaye || iti |
aṅguliśabdaḥ karasyāpy upalakṣakaḥ | prasaṅgavinivṛttaye iti atiprasaṅganivāraṇārtham ity arthaḥ | saṃbhoganivṛttyartham iti bhāruciḥ |
atrāpi viśeṣam āha gautamaḥ: “kanyaiva kanyāṃ dūṣyati tadaṅgachedo vā mauṇḍyaṃ vā” iti | atra viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
kanyaiva kanyāyāḥ kuryāt tasyās tu dviśato damaḥ |
yā tu kanyāṃ prakuryāt strī sā sadyo mauṇḍyam arhati || iti |
kanyāṃ kuryād iti yonikṣatavatīṃ kuryād ity arthaḥ | etac ca daṇḍavidhānaṃ savarṇāsavarṇamadhyahīnottamādikanyāsādhāraṇam iti bhāruciḥ | sānurāgād akāmāṃ gacchato hīnasya kṣatriyādeḥ vadhaḥ,
uttamāṃ sevamānas tu jaghanyo vadham arhati |
iti manusmaraṇāt | yadā savarṇāṃ sakāmām abhigacchati tadā gomithunaṃ śulkaṃ tatpitre dadyāt yadīcchati pitā | yadi necchati śulkaṃ tam eva daṇḍarūpeṇa rājñe dadyāt | savarṇām akāmāṃ tu gacchato [473] vadha eveti vijñāneśaḥ | atra viṣṇus tu viśeṣam āha: “kanyādūṣako mithyāvādī dviśataṃ tadardhaṃ vā” iti | daṇḍya iti śeṣaḥ | ayam arthaḥ – mithyābhiśaṃsanair dviśataṃ daṇḍyaḥ | nityadūṣaṇe śataṃ daṇḍya iti | apasmārarājayakṣmādidīrgharogakutsita-rogasaṃsṛṣṭamaithunatvādiduṣaṇāni | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
aparuddhāsu dāsīṣu bhujiṣyāsu tathaiva ca |
aniṣeddhā kṣamo yaḥ san sarve te kāryakāriṇaḥ |
gamyāsv api pumān dāpyaḥ pañcaśatyadhikaṃ damam ||
gacchann iti śeṣaḥ | puruṣāntaropabhogato niruddhā aparuddhāḥ | puruṣaniyataparigrahā bhujiṣyā | etayor viśeṣaṇaṃ gamyāsv apīti | sarvapuruṣasādhāraṇatayā gamyāsv ity anuvādaḥ | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
prasahya dāsyabhigame daṇḍo daśapaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
bahūnāṃ yady akāmā sā caturviṃśatitaḥ pṛthak ||
atra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
antyābhigamane tv aṅkyaḥ kudaṇḍena pravāsayet |
śūdras tadāntya eva syād antyasyābhigame vadhaḥ ||
asyārthaḥ – antyā caṇḍālī tadgamane traivarṇikāṇ prāyaścittān abhimukhān “sahasraṃ tv antyajastriyam” iti vacanāt paṇasahasraṃ daṇḍayitvā kudaṇḍena kutsitadaṇḍena bhagākāreṇāṅkayitvā svarāṣṭrān nirvāsayet | prāyaścittābhimukhasya tu daṇḍanam eva | . . . . . śūdraḥ punaś caṇḍālīm abhigamya caṇḍāla eva bhavati | antyajasya caṇḍālādes tūkṛṣṭajātistriyabhigamane ca sa eveti vacanārthaḥ | [474] atra viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
brahmahā ca surāpaś ca taskaro gurutalpagaḥ |
ete sarve pṛthag vadhyā mahāpātakino narāḥ ||
pratiṣiddhasurāyāḥ pātā surāpaḥ | taskaro brāhmaṇasuvarṇasteyī, brāhmaṇasuvarṇāpaharaṇaṃ mahāpātakam ity āpastambasmaraṇāt | etac ca brāhmaṇavyatiriktaviṣayam |
mahāpātakayukto ‘pi na vipro vadham arhati |
nirvāsanāṅkane mauṇḍyaṃ tac ca kuryān narādhipaḥ ||
daṇḍe kṛte ‘pi teṣāṃ doṣo ‘sty eva |
kṛtadaṇḍo ‘py asaṃbhāṣyo jñeya uttamasāhase |
iti uttamasāhasānukalpatvenātiduṣṭatayātrāpy asaṃbhāṣyatvaṃ veditavyam | ayam evārthaḥ prapañcito manunā |
asaṃbhojyā hy asaṃyājyā asaṃpāṭhyāvivāhinaḥ |
careyuḥ pṛthivīṃ sarvāṃ sarvakarmabahiṣkṛtāḥ ||
jñātisaṃbandhibhis tv ete tyaktavyāḥ kṛtalakṣaṇāḥ |
nirvāco nirṇamaskārāḥ tan manor anuśāsanam || iti |
yat tu bodhāyanoktam “kṣatriyādīnāṃ brāhmaṇavadhe vadhaḥ sarvasvaharaṇaṃ ca” iti, tat sāhasarūpaviṣayaṃ veditavyam | atra viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
taṭākachedakaṃ hanyād apsu śuddhavadhena vā |
sa yadi pratisaṃskuryād dadyāc cottamasāhasam ||
kātyāyanas tu viśeṣam āha |
hared bhindyād dahed vāpi devānāṃ pratimādikam |
tadgṛhaṃ cāpi yo bhindyāt prāpnuyāt pūrvasāhasam ||
[475]        pramāṇena tu kūṭena mudrayā vānukūlayā |
kāryaṃ tu sādhayed yo vai sa dāpyottamasāhasam ||
bṛhaspatis tu viśeṣam āha |
mantrauṣadhibalāt kiṃcit saṃśrāntiṃ janayanti yet |
mūlakarma ca kurvanti nirvāsyās te mahībhujā ||
mūlikādibhir vaśīkaraṇakarma | ata evāha manuḥ |
kūṭaśāsanakartṝṃś ca prakṛtīnāṃ ca dūṣakān |
strībālabrāhmaṇaghnāṃśca hanyāt saṃvananāṃs tathā ||
saṃvananopajīvinaḥ prakṛtidūṣakān nirvāsayed iti | prakṛtayas tu svāmyamātyadurgakośadaṇḍarāṣṭramitrāṇi pratipāditāny eva prakaraṇādau | saṃvananopajīvinaḥ vaśīkaraṇakarmopajīvinaḥ ||
iti śrīpratāparudramahādevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇde sāhasākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ |
[476]
atha vākpāruṣyākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
tallakṣaṇam āha nāradaḥ |
deśajātikulādīnām ākrośaṃ vyaṅgysaṃyutam |
yad vacaḥ pratikūlārthaṃ vākpāruṣyaṃ tad ucyate ||
pāruṣyaṃ rūkṣīkaraṇam | vācā manaso rūkṣīkaraṇaṃ vākpāruṣyaṃ | vyaṅgyasayutaṃ vyaṅgyārthayuktaṃ pratikūlārtham udvaigārtham | ayam arthaḥ – hiṃsakā prācyā iti deśākrośaḥ | viprās tv anācārā iti jātyākrośaḥ | maitrīśūnyā vaiśvāmitrā iti kulākrośaḥ | āsigrahaṇāt svarūpākṣepo ‘pi gṛhyate | yathārhaṃ vidyāśūnya iti vyaṅgyasayutaṃ bhaginyādigamanāropaḥ surāpo ‘sītyādimahāpātakābhiyogaś ca | tatra daṇḍavidhim āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
samajātiguṇānāṃ tu vākpāruṣye parasparam |
vinayo vihitaḥ śāstre paṇā ardhatrayodaśāḥ ||
jātimātre sāmye viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
samajātau tu sarveṣāṃ dvādaśaiva vyatikrame |
vyatikramo vāgvyatikramaḥ vākpāruṣyam iti yāvat | vāgvyatirikavyatikramasya paurvāparyādiviśeṣābhāve dvayoḥ samadoṣayoḥ dvādaśapaṇa eva daṇḍo bhavatīty arthaḥ | ata eva sumantuḥ |
pāruṣyadoṣād ubhayor yugapat saṃpravṛttayoḥ |
viśeṣaś cen na dṛśyeta vinayaś cet samastayoḥ ||
vinayaḥ – śikṣā damaḥ | viśeṣadarśane tu tadanusāreṇa viṣama eva [477] damaḥ syād ity abhiprāyaḥ | ata eva bṛhaspatiḥ |
samānayoḥ samo daṇḍo nyūne syād dviguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
uttamasyādhikaṃ proktaṃ vākpāruṣye parasparam || iti |
atra viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
pūrvam ākṣārayed yas tu niyataṃ syāt sa doṣabhāk |
paścād yasyodyasatkārī pūrve tu vinayo guruḥ ||
pūrvakṣāranimittako vinayaḥ paścātkṣāraṇanimittakavinayād abhyadhiko bhavatīty arthaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha viṣṇuḥ: “tulyakālaṃ rūkṣayoḥ sama eva daṇḍaḥ” iti | atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
satyāsatyānyathāstotraiḥ nyūnāṅgendriyarogiṇām |
kṣepaṃ karoto ced daṇḍyaḥ paṇān ardhatrayodaśān ||
nyūnāṅgāḥ kuṇyādayāḥ | nyūnendriyāḥ kāṇādayaḥ | rogiṇaḥ kuṣṭhādayaḥ |
daṇḍapraṇayanaṃ kāryaṃ varṇajātyuttarādhare |
daṇḍapraṇayanaṃ daṇḍavidhānam iti bhāruciḥ | daṇḍapraṇayanaṃ daṇḍasya prakarṣeṇa nayanam ūhanam iti vijñāneśaḥ | varṇāś ca jātayaś ca uttarāś cādharāś ca varṇajātyuttarādharāḥ |uttarādharvarṇe jātyapekṣayā daṇḍapraṇayanaṃ kāryam ity arthaḥ | yathā mūrdhāvasiktaṃ brāhmaṇādd dhīnaṃ kṣatriyād utkṛṣṭaṃ ca ākruśya brāhmaṇakṣatriyākṣepanimittāt pañcāśatpaṇātmakāt kiṃcid adhikaṃ pañcasaptatyātmakaṃ daṇḍam arhati | kṣatriyo ‘pi tam ākruśya brāhmaṇakṣepanimittāc ca taddaṇḍād ūnaṃ pañcasaptatim eva daṇdam | mūrdhāvasikto ‘pi tāv ubhāv ākruśya tam eva daṇḍam arhati | mūrdhāvasiktāmbaṣṭhādayo ‘pi parasparākṣepe brāhmaṇakṣatriyayoḥ [478] parasparākṣepanimittakād yathākrameṇa daṇḍau veditavyau | evam anyatrāpy ūhanīyaḥ | atra viṣṇuḥ: “prātilomyena pāruṣye dviguṇo damaḥ” iti | ayam arthaḥ – dvigunās triguṇā damāḥ brāhmaṇākṣepakāriṇoḥ kṣatriyavaiśyayoḥ pañcāśatpaṇāpekṣayā dviguṇāḥ śataṃ paṇāḥ triguṇāḥ sardhaśataṃ paṇā daṇḍo veditavya iti
prātilomyāparādheṣu dviguṇās triguṇā damāḥ |
varṇānām ānulomyena tasmād ardhārdhabhāginaḥ || iti |
śūdraviṣaye viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
śataṃ brāhmaṇam ākruśya kṣatriyo daṇḍam arhati |
vaiśyo ‘py ardhaśataṃ dve vā śūdras tu vadham arhati ||
vadho ‘tra jihvācheda iti vijñāneśaḥ | evaṃ viṭchūdrayor api kṣatriyād anantarayoḥ tulyanyāyatayā śatam ardhaśataṃ ca yathākrameṇa kṣatriyākrośe veditavyam | śūdrasya vaiśyākrośe śatam | evam itarad ūhyam | yad uktaṃ manunā |
pañcāśad brāhmaṇo daṇḍyaḥ kṣatriyasyābhiśaṃsane |
vaiśyasya tv ardhapañcāśac chūdre dvādaśako damaḥ || iti |
atra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
bāhugrīvānetrasakthivināśe vācike damaḥ |
śatyas tadardhakaḥ pādanāsākarṇakarādiṣu ||
śatyaḥ – śataparimito daṇḍaḥ | pādādibhaṅgaṃ kariṣyāmīti vācike vināse tadardhikaḥ pañcāśatpaṇiko daṇḍa ity arthaḥ | tīvrākrośe viśeṣam āha sa eva |
patanīyakṛte kṣepe daṇḍo madhyamasāhasaḥ |
upapātakayukte ca dāpyaḥ prathamasāhasam ||
[479]        upapātakayukte ca goghno ‘sīty evamādike |
traividyanṛpadevānāṃ kṣepa uttamasāhasaḥ ||
madhyamo jātipūgānāṃ prathamo grāmadeśayoḥ |
jātipūgāḥ jātisaṃghāḥ | atra pratiprasavam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
guṇahīnasya pāruṣye brāhmaṇo nāparādhnuyāt |
patitaṃ patitety uktvā coraṃ coreti vā punaḥ ||
vacanāt tulyadoṣaḥ syāt ............... | iti |
vacanāt tulyavacanād ity arthaḥ | yatra abhiyogādau pāṇītyādikaṃ sādhayet tatra vacanād doṣo na syād ity arthaḥ | evaṃ śrutābhijanadeśakarmaśarīrapitṛmātṛgutuvṛddhāsyādhikṣepadarśane ca tannibandhanoccāvacadaṇḍavidhāyakāḥ smṛtayo yady api bahvyaḥ samuccayakāraiḥ samuccitāḥ, asmābhis tu saṃprati tadanuṣṭhānābhāvāt granthavistarataḥ kim artham ity uparatam ||
iti śrīpratāparudradevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇde vākpāruṣyākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
[480]
atha daṇḍapāruṣyākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
yathāha nāradaḥ |
paragātreṣv abhidroho hastapādāyudhādibhiḥ |
bhasmādibhiś copaghāto daṇḍapāruṣyam ucyate || iti |
atra manuḥ |
eṣa daṇḍavidhiḥ prokto vākpāruṣyasya satvataḥ (tatvataḥ?) |
ata ūrdhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi daṇḍapāruṣyanirṇayam ||
yathāha pariśiṣṭakāraḥ |
duḥkhaṃ vraṇaṃ raktabhaṅgaṃ chedanaṃ bhedanaṃ tathā |
kuryād yat prāṇināṃ tad dhi daṇḍapāruṣyam ucyate ||
tasya traividhyam āha manuḥ |
asyāpi dṛṣṭaṃ traividhyaṃ hīnamadhyottamakramāt |
apagoraṇaniḥsaṅgapātakṣatajadarśanaiḥ ||
hīnamadhyottamānāṃ tu dravyāṇāṃ samatikramāt |
trīṇy eva sāhasāny āhuḥ tad akaṇṭakaśodhanam || iti |
niḥsaṅgapātanaṃ niḥśaṅkapraharaṇam | tasya daṇḍam āha manuḥ |
tvakchedakaḥ śataṃ daṇḍyo lohitasya tadardhakaḥ |
māṃsachettā tu ṣaṇṇiṣkān pravāsyas tv asthibhedakaḥ ||
māṃsachettā vraṇakarteti candrikākāraḥ | yamo ‘pi |
karṇoṣṭhaghrāṇapādākṣijihvāmukhakarasya ca |
chedane cottamo daṇḍaḥ bhedane madhyamo guruḥ || [481]
bṛhaspatir api |
bhasmādinā prakṣe(kṣi)paṇaṃ tāḍanaṃ ca karādinā |
prathamaṃ daṇḍapāruṣyaṃ damaḥ kāryo ‘tra māṣakaḥ ||
iṣtakāphalakādyaiś ca tāḍane tu dvimāṣakaḥ |
kāryaḥ kṛtānurūpais tu lagne ghāte damo budhaiḥ ||
eṣa daṇḍaḥ sameṣūktaḥ parastrīṣv adhikeṣu ca |
dviguṇas triguṇo jñeyaḥ prādhānyāpekṣayā budhaiḥ || iti |
tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
bhasmapaṅkajaḥ sparśe daṇḍo daśaguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ |
amedhyapārṣṇiniṣṭhyūtasparśane dviguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
sameṣv evaṃ parastrīṣu dviguṇas tūttameṣu ca ||
dviguṇadamaḥ viṃśatipaṇātmakaḥ | kātyāyanas tu viśeṣam āha |
chardimūtrapurīṣādyair āpādyaḥ sa caturguṇaḥ |
ṣaḍguṇaḥ kāyamadhye syān mūrdhni tv aṣṭaguṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
spaṣṭo ‘rthaḥ | atra kātyāyanas tu vākpāruṣyadaṇḍaprakāram asmin daṇḍapāruṣye ‘tidiśati |
vākpāruṣye yathā proktā prātilomyānulomataḥ |
tathaiva daṇḍe pāruṣye pātyā daṇḍā yathākramam ||
atra yājñavalkyaḥ |
viprapīḍākaraṃ chedyam aṅgam abrāhmaṇasya ca |
udgūrṇe prathamo daṇḍaḥ saṃsparśe tu tadardhakaḥ ||
yathāha bhṛguḥ |
yena kenacid aṅgena hiṃsyāc chreyāṃsam antyajaḥ |
chettavyaṃ tat tad evāsya tan manor anuśāsanam || [482]
dvijātimātrāparādhe śūdrasyāṅgachedanavidhānāt vaiśyasyāpi kṣatriyāpakāriṇaś ca ayam eva daṇḍaḥ tulyanyāyatvād iti vijñāneśaḥ | udgūrṇe vadhārtham udyate śastrādike | śūdrasya punar udgūrane ’pi hastādichedanam eva,
pāṇim udyamya daṇḍaṃ vā pāṇichedanam arhati |
iti manusmaraṇāt | udgūraṇārthaṃ śastrādisparśane tu prathamasāhasārdhaṃ sajātīyaviṣaye yājñavalkyaḥ |
udgūrṇe hastapāde tu pariviṃśatikau damau |
parasparaṃ tu sarveṣāṃ śastre madhyamasāhasam ||
kiṃ ca,
pādakeśāṅkuśakarolluñcaneṣu paṇān daśa |
pīḍākarṣāṃśukaāveṣṭapādādyane (-dhyāse) śataṃ damaḥ ||
eteṣāṃ samuccaye śataṃ daṇḍyāḥ | etad uktam – pādakeśavastrakarāṇām anyatamaṃ gṛhītvā yo jhaḍ ity ākarṣaty asau daśa paṇān daṇḍyaḥ | aṃśukenāveṣṭya gāḍham āpīḍyākṛṣya yaḥ pādena ghaṭṭayati taṃ daśa paṇān daṇḍayed iti brāhmaṇaviṣayam | tathā ca yamaḥ |
pādena praharan kopāt pādachedanam arhati |
avaniṣṭhīvato darpād dvāv oṣṭhau chedayen nṛpaḥ ||
avamūtrayato meḍhram avamehayato gudam |
keśeṣu gṛhṇato hastau chedayed avicārayan ||
keśeṣv ekena hastena gṛhṇato ‘py ubhayahastachedanavidhir dvivacanād gamyate |
śoṇitena vinā duḥkhaṃ kurvan kāṣṭhādibhir naraḥ |
dvāviṃśatipaṇān daṇḍyo dviguṇaṃ daṃśane ‘ntyajaḥ || iti |
vyaktārtham –
karavad dantabhaṅge ca chedane karṇanāsayoḥ |
[483]        madhye daṇḍo vraṇodbhede mṛtakalpahate tathā ||
kiṃ ca |
ceṣṭābhedanavāgrodhanetrādipratirodhane |
kandharābāhusakthnāṃ ca bhaṅge madhyamasāhasam ||
ekaṃ ghnatāṃ bahūnāṃ ca yathokto dviguṇo damaḥ ||
ayam arthaḥ – yadā punar bahavo militāḥ ekasya bhaṅgādikaṃ kurvanti tadā yasminn aparādhe yo yo daṇḍa uktaḥ tatrāsmāt dviguṇo daṇḍaḥ pratyekaṃ veditavya iti | tatra uśanā |
yatra nokto damaḥ sarvair ānantyāt tu mahātmabhiḥ |
tatra kāryaṃ pratijñāya kartavyaṃ daṇḍadhāraṇam ||
ānantyāt daṇḍārhavyaktīnām iti śeṣaḥ |
manuṣyāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ ca duḥkhāya prahṛte sati |
yathā yathā mahad duḥkhaṃ daṇḍaṃ kuryāt tathā tathā ||
abhighāte tathā chede bhede kuḍyāvapātane |
paṇān dāpyaḥ pañcadaśa viṃśatiṃ tadvyayaṃ tathā ||
mudgarādinā kuḍyābhighāt vidāraṇe dvidhībhāve ca yathākramaṃ pañcadaśapaṇo viṃśatipaṇaś ca daṇḍo veditavyaḥ | api ca –
duḥkhotpādi gṛhe dravyaṃ kṣipan prāṇaharaṃ tathā |
ṣoḍaśādyaḥ paṇān dāpyo dvitīyo madhayaṃ damam ||
ayam arthaḥ – paragṛhe duḥkhajanakaṃ kaṇṭakādi dravyaṃ kṣipan ṣoḍaśapaṇān prāṇaharaṃ bhujaṅgaviṣādikaṃ kṣipan madhyamasāhasam | kiṃ ca,
duḥkhe ca śoṇitotpāde śākhāṅgachedane tathā |
daṇḍaḥ kṣudrapaśūnāṃ ca dviguṇaprabhṛti kramāt || [484]
kṣudrāṇāṃ paśūnām ajādiharaṇaprāyāṇāṃ tāḍane duḥkhotpādane asṛksrāve śākhāṅgachedane, śākhāśabdena śṛṅgādikaṃ lakṣyate, aṅgāni prasiddhāni yathākramaṃ dviguṇaś caturguṇaṣaḍguṇa iti, aparādhagurutvāt |
prarohiśākhināṃ śākhāskandhasarvavidāraṇe |
upajīvyadrumāṇāṃ ca viṃśateḥ dviguṇo damaḥ ||
prarohiśākhino vaṭādayaḥ atra viṃśatipaṇaś catvāriṃśatpaṇo ‘śītipaṇa ity evaṃ trayo daṇḍā yathākramaṃ śākhāskandhasarvavidāraṇe bhavanti | aprarohiśākhinām upajīvyadrumāṇāṃ mātuluṅgādīnāṃ pūrvoktasthāne pūrvoktā eva daṇḍāḥ | anupajīvyaprarohiśākhiṣu vṛkṣeṣu daṇḍaḥ kalpyaḥ | kiṃ ca |
caityaśmaśānasīmāsu anyasthāne surālaye |
jātadrumāṇāṃ dviguṇo damo vṛkṣeṣu viśrute ||
spaṣṭārthaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
vanaspatīnāṃ sarveṣām upabhoge yathātatham |
tathā tathā damaḥ kāryo hiṃsāyām avicāraṇe || iti |
atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
dehendriyavināśe tu yathā daṇḍaṃ prakalpayet |
tathā tuṣṭikaraṃ deyaṃ samutthānaṃ ca paṇḍitaiḥ ||
samutthānavyayaṃ dāpyaḥ kalahāya kṛtaṃ ca yat ||
iti bṛhaspatismaraṇāt | atra pratiprasavam āha manuḥ |
chinne nasye yuge bhagne tiryakpratimukhāgate |
akṣabhaṅge ca yānasya cakrabhaṅge tathaiva ca ||
[485]        bhe(che)dane caiva yantrāṇāṃ yoktraraśmyos tathaiva ca |
ākrande cāpy avehīti na daṇḍo manur abravīt ||
śakaṭasthenāvehīty ākrandaḥ avehīti plutaṃ dūrād āhvānavācakatvāt | apehīti vā pāṭhaḥ | apasarety arthaḥ | avehīty ākranda ity anena anākrande doṣa ity avagamyate | tathāha sa eva |
yatrāpi vartate yugyaṃ vaiguṇyād vrajakasya (prājakasya) tu |
tatra svāmī bhaved daṇḍyo hiṃsāyāṃ dviśataṃ damam || iti |
vrajakaḥ gantā ||
iti śrīpratāparudramahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇde daṇḍapāruṣyākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ |
[486]
atha dyūtasamāhvayākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
pūrvaprakaraṇe cauryaprkaraṇam nirūpitam | dyūtasamāhvayayos tulyayogakṣematayā cauryānantaraṃ saṃgatiḥ | atra manuḥ |
prakāśam etat tāskaryaṃ yad devanasamāhvayau |
tayor nityapratīghāte nṛpatir yatnavān bhavet || iti |
tāskaryaṃ taskaratvam | brāhmaṇāditvāt ṣyañ | yatnavān bhūtvā tān nivārayet | tatsvarūpaṃ nāradenoktam |
akṣavardhraśalākādyair devanaṃ jihmakāritam |
paṇakṛīḍā vayobhiś ca padaṃ dyūtasamāhvayam ||
akṣāḥ pācakāḥ | vardhraḥ carmapratīkā, kācanirmitā pātrīti kecit | śalākā dantādimayī dīrghacaturasrā | vṛtteti kecit | kācapātryāṃ vṛtrair eva devanāt | śeṣaṃ sugamam | dyūtasamāhvayayor bhedaḥ prakaraṇādāv uktaḥ | yad vā saṃgatyantaraṃ pūrvaprakaraṇoktacauryaviṣaye rājñām anumatir nāsti ihānumatir astīti tayoḥ saṃgatiḥ | tathā hi |
dyūtaṃ niṣiddhaṃ manunā satyaśaucadhanāpaham |
abhyanujñātam anyais tu rājabhāgasamanvitam ||
sabhikādhiṣṭhitaṃ kāryaṃ taskarajñānahetunā || iti |
atha vā kitavo rājñe datvā bhāgaṃ yathoditam |
prakāśaṃ devanaṃ kuryād evaṃ doṣo na vidyate || iti
samāhavayaṃ prastutyāha bṛhaspatiḥ |
dvandvayuddhena yaḥ kaścid avasādam avāpnuyāt |
tat svāmine paṇo deyaḥ yas tatra parikalpitaḥ || [487]
ubhābhyām api rājñe tatpaṇacaturthāṃśo dātavya ity āha viṣṇuḥ: “dvandvayuddhe samāhvaye paṇacaturthāṃśo rājñe dātavyaḥ” iti | paṇacaturthāṃśa ity anena ubhābhyāṃ dātavyaḥ kara iti pratīyat ity āha bhāruciḥ | atra viśeṣam āha viṣnuḥ: “mallamahiṣavarjaṃ samāhūtaṃ jayine dadyāt parājitaṃ paṇaṃ cāpi dadyāt” iti | ayam arthaḥ – samāhūtaṃ samāhvaya iti iṣṭamallamahiṣān varjayitvā meṣakukkuṭātiriktādikaṃ mṛtaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā jayine dadyāt | yadi na dadāti rājñā dātavya iti | tatra dyūtasamādhikāriṇo vṛttim āha “glahavṛddhiṃ gṛhṇīyāt sabhikaḥ” iti | sabhikaḥ – sabhyaḥ dyūta ity anuvartate | vṛddhiparimāṇam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
glahe śatikavṛddhes tu sabhikaḥ pañcakaṃ śatam |
gṛhṇīyād dyūtakitavād itarād daśakaṃ śatam ||
parasparasaṃpratipattyā kitavaparikalpitaḥ paṇo glaha ity ucyate | tatra glahe tadāśrayā śatikā śataparimitā tadadhikapramāṇā veti vijñāneśaḥ | sāvṛddhir yasyāsau śatikavṛddhiḥ | tasmād dhūrtakitavāt pañcakaṃ śatam ātmavṛddhyarthaṃ sabhiko gṛhṇīyāt | itarasmāt jitadravyasya daśamaṃ bhāgaṃ gṛhṇīyād iti yāvat | tasya kartavyam āha viṣṇuḥ: “dyūtaṃ kārayet sabhiko deyaṃ ca dadyāt tatkṛtam” iti | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
sabhikaḥ kārayed dyūtaṃ deyaṃ dadyāc ca tatkṛtam | iti |
tatra viśeṣam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
sa samyak pālito dadyād rājñe bhāgaṃ yathākṛtam |
jitam udgrāhayej jetre dadyāt satyaṃ vacaḥ kṣamī || iti |
jitam utdgrāhayej jitasakāśād uddharet taj jetre dadyāt | dyūtakāriṇāṃ viśvāsasādhanaṃ sakṛt sakṛt satyavākyaṃ dadyāt | [488]
atra viśeṣam āha viṣṇuḥ: “jito yadi paṇaṃ sabhikāya na dadyāt rājñā dāpyaḥ adattaḥ rājabhāge pracchanne dyūte jitaṃ paṇaṃ na dāpayet” iti | spaṣṭārthaḥ | jayaparājayavipratipattau nirṇayahetum āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
draṣṭāro vyavahārāṇāṃ sākṣiṇaś ca ta eva hi |
dyūte vyavahārāṇāṃ draṣṭāras tata ta eva sabhyāḥ | ta eva kitavā rājñā niyoktavyā na tu sākṣiprkaraṇoktāḥ | na tatra strībālavṛddhakitavetyādiniṣedho ‘sti | kiṃ ca,
dyūtam ekamukhaṃ kāryaṃ taskarajñānakāraṇāt |
ayam arthaḥ – ekamukham ekapradhānakaṃ rājādhyakṣādyadhiṣṭhitaṃ kuryāt | prāyaśaś cauryārjitadhanā eva kitavyā bhavanti | dyūtadhamaṃ samāhvaye ‘tidiśati yājñavalkyaḥ |
eṣa eva vidhir jñeyaḥ prāṇidyūte samāhvaye |
glahe śtakavṛddheḥ ................ ||
ityādi | kapaṭadyūtam adhikṛtyāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
rājñā sacihnaṃ nirvāsyāḥ kūṭākṣopadhidevinaḥ | iti |
kūṭair akṣādibhiḥ | upādhir nāma mativañcanahetunā maṇimantrauṣadhādinā ye dīvyanti tān sapadyevāṅkayitvā rāṣṭrān nirvāsayet | eteṣāṃ corabhedhāt śvapadāṅkanam | nāradas tu viśeṣam āha |
kūṭākṣadevinaḥ prāptān rājā rāṣṭrād vivāsayet |
kaṇṭhe ‘kṣamālām āsjya sa hy eṣāṃ vinayaḥ smṛtaḥ || iti |
tatra viṣṇuḥ viśeṣam āha: “gauñjikacārmikādayaḥ pratārakā vivāsyāḥ” iti | gauñjikāś cārmikāḥ carmaṇā vyavaharanti | [489] atra bhāruciḥ: gauñjikacārmikagrahaṇenobhayoś cāturyeṇaikaviṣayatvena cauryam eveti ayaṃ gauñjikādivyatirekaiva kitavā | itaras tu vañcanīyakoṭir eveti gauñjikādayo na daṇḍyāḥ | api tu deśān nirvāsyāḥ | ataś ca dyūtaṃ niṣiddhaṃ manunetyādivacanajātaṃ kapaṭadyūtaviṣayam iti mantavyam ||
iti śrīpratāparudradevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇde dyūtasamāhvayākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ |
[490]
atha saptadaśavivādapadaśeṣatayā daṇḍavidhir ucyate
yājñavalkyaḥ |
saṃdighārthaṃ svatantro yaḥ sādhayed yaś ca niṣpatet |
na cāhūto vadet kiṃcid dhīno daṇḍyaś ca sa smṛtaḥ ||
apaṇaś ced vivādaḥ syāt tatra hīnaṃ tu dāpayet |
daṇḍaṃ ca svapaṇaṃ caiva dhanine dhanam eva ca ||
ādhyādīnāṃ vihartāraṃ dhanine dāpayed dhanam |
daṇḍaṃ ca tatsamaṃ rājñe śaktyapekṣam athāpi vā ||
praṇaṣṭādhigataṃ dravyaṃ nṛpeṇa dhanine dhanam |
vibhāvayen na cel liṅgais tatsamaṃ dhaṇḍam arhati ||
śaulkike sthānapāle vā niṣṭāpahṛtam āhṛtam |
arvāk saṃvatsarāt svāmī haret tat parsto nṛpaḥ ||
prasannaṃ sādhayen nārthaṃ na vāryo nṛpater bhavet |
sākṣyamāne nṛpaṃ gacched daṇḍyo ‘py anyasya tad dhanam ||
rājā tu svāminaṃ vipraṃ sāntvanena pradāpayet |
deśācāreṇa cānyāṃs tu dhṛṣṭān saṃpīḍya dāpayet ||
yājavalkyaḥ |
bhraṣṭaś cen mārgit datte dāpyo daṇḍaṃ ca tatsamam |
ājīvan svecchayā daṇḍyo dāpyas taṃ cāpi sodayam ||
manuḥ |
abhāvayan damaṃ dāpyo dūṣaṇaṃ sākṣiṇāṃ sphuṭam |
bhāvite sākṣiṇo varjyāḥ sākṣidharmanirākṛtāḥ ||
[491]        dṛśyate yasya saptāhād uktavākyasy sākṣiṇaḥ |
rogo ‘gnir jñātimaraṇam ṛṇaṃ dāpyo damaṃ ca saḥ ||
pṛthak pṛthag daṇḍanīyāḥ kūṛakṛt sākṣiṇas tathā |
vivādadviguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ vivāsyo brāhmaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ ||
anāgamaṃ tu yo bhuṅkte bahūny abdaśatāny api |
coradaṇḍena taṃ pāpaṃ daṇḍayet pṛthivīpatiḥ ||
yājñavalkyaḥ |
anṛte tu pṛthag daṇḍyā rājñā madhyamasāhasam |
śeṣāś ced anṛtaṃ brūyuḥ niyuktā bhūmikarmaṇi ||
pratyekaṃ tu jaghanyās te vineyāḥ pūrvasāhasam |
maunavṛddhādayas tv anye daṇḍagatyā pṛthak pṛthak ||
vineyāḥ prathamenaiva sāhasenānṛte sthitāḥ |
bahūnāṃ tu gṛhītānāṃ na sarve nirṇayaṃ yadi ||
kuryur bhayād vā lobhād vā daṇḍas tūttamasāhasam |
etat jñānaviṣayam |
kīrtite yadi bhedaḥ syād daṇḍyās tūttamasāhasam ||
evam ajñānādinānṛtavacane sākṣyādīn daṇḍayitvā punaś c sīmāvicāraḥ pravartanīyaḥ |
ajñānoktān daṇḍayitvā punaḥ sīmāṃ vicāreyat | iti |
maryādāyāḥ prabhede ca sīmātikramaṇe tathā |
kṣetrasya haraṇe daṇḍaḥ adhamottamamadhyamāḥ ||
nāradaḥ |
māṣaṃ gāṃ daṇḍayed daṇḍaṃ dvau māṣau mahiṣīṃ tathā |
[492]        tathājāvikavatsānāṃ daṇḍaḥ syād ardhamāṣakaḥ ||
ayam arthaḥ – yadi paśavaḥ parakṣetrasasyaṃ bhakayitvā tatraiva sthitā vasantaḥ śerate tadā yathoktadaṇḍād dviguṇo daṇḍyo veditavyaḥ |
samam eṣāṃ vivīte ‘pi kharoṣṭramahiṣīsamam |
vivīto nāmāvaruddhas tṛṇapracuraḥ |
yāvat sasyaṃ vināśyet tāvat syāṭ kṣetriṇaḥ phalam |
gopatvād yas tu gomī syāt pūrvoktaṃ daṇḍam arhati ||
gomī gosvāmī |
pathi grāme vivītānte kṣetre doṣo na vidyate |
akāmataḥ kāmacāre coravad daṇḍam arhati ||
atra mahokṣahastyaśvāndhabadhirakubjāgantukāḥ catuṣpathe na daṇḍyā iti | viṣṇuḥ: “yaḥ samartho visaṃvāde sarvasvaharaṇaṃ daṇḍas tasya purān nirvāsaḥ” iti | samarthaḥ – samayaṃ pālayituṃ śakto ‘mugdha iti yāvat | yājñavalkyaḥ |
abhakṣyeṇa dvijaṃ dūṣya daṇḍa uttamasāhasam |
madhyamaṃ kṣatriyaṃ vaiśyaṃ prathamaṃ śūdram ardhikam ||
kūṭasvarṇavyavahārī kumāṃsasya ca vikrayī |
tryaṅgahīnas tu kartavyo dāpyaś cottamasāhasam ||
ayam arthaḥ – rasavedhādhyāpāditapaṇotkarṣaiḥ kūṭaiḥ svarṇaiḥ vyavahāraśīlaḥ | kumāṃsasya kutsitamāṃsasya śvādisaṃbandhasya vikrayaśīlaḥ | caśabdāt kūṭarajatādivyavahārī ca sarve pratyekaṃ nāsākarṇakarais tribhir aṅgair hīnāḥ kāryāḥ| caśabdāt tryaṅgachedanasamuccitam uttamasāhasadaṇḍaṃ dāpya iti vijñāneśaḥ | [493] yat tu manunoktam,
sarvakaṇṭakapāpiṣṭhaṃ hemakāraṃ tu pārthivaḥ |
pravartamānam anyāye chedayel lavaśaḥ kṣuraiḥ ||
iti, tad devabrāhmaṇarājasvarṇaviṣayam iti vijñāneśaḥ |
jāraṃ corety abhivadan dāpyaḥ pañcaśataṃ damam |
upajīvyadhanaṃ muñcan tad evāṣṭaguṇīkṛtam || iti |
yājñavalkyaḥ |
manuṣyamāraṇe kṣipraṃ coravat kilbiṣī bhavet |
prāṇihṛ tsumahatsvārthaṃ gogajoṣṭrahayādiṣu ||
kṣudrakāṇāṃ paśūnāṃ tu hiṃsāyāṃ dviśato damaḥ |
pañcāśat tu bhaved daṇḍaḥ śubheṣu mṛgapakṣiṣu ||
gardabhājāvikānāṃ tu daṇḍaḥ syāt pañcamāṣakaḥ |
māṣakas tu bhaved daṇḍaḥ sasukaranipātitaiḥ || iti |
rājñaḥ kośāpahartṝṃś ca prakūleṣu ca sthitān |
ghātayed vividhair daṇḍair arīṇāṃ copakārakān || iti |
atra viśeṣam āha nāradaḥ |
āyudhāny āyudhīyānāṃ vāhādīn vāhyajīvinām |
veśyāstrīṇām alaṃkārān vāhyam ātodyajīvinām ||
yasya yasyopakaraṇaṃ yena jīvanti kārukāḥ |
sarvasvaharaṇe ‘py etan na rājā hartum arhati ||
mṛtāṅgalagnavikretuḥ guros tāḍayitus tathā |
rājayānāsanāroḍhur daṇḍa uttamasāhasam || [494]
smṛtyantaram api |
dvijātiliṅginaḥ śūdrān ghātayen manur abravīt |
haste ‘palapamānaṃ tu karaṇena vibhāvitam ||
dāpayed dhanikasyārthaṃ daṇḍaleśaṃ ca śaktitaḥ |
atra medhātithinā daśamāṃśadaṇḍadānāsamarthādhmarṇaviṣayam ity uktam |
śārīraś cārthadaṇḍaś ca daṇḍas tu dvividhaḥ smṛtaḥ |
śārīras tāḍanādis tu maraṇāntaḥ prakīrtitaḥ ||
kākiṇyādis tv arthadaṇḍaḥ sarvasvānta udāhṛtaḥ ||
iti |
śārīro daśadhā proktaḥ arthadaṇḍas tv anekadhā ||
atra manuḥ |
daśa sthānāni daṇḍasya manuḥ svāyaṃbhuvo ‘bravīt |
upastham udaraṃ jihvāṃ hastau pādau ca pañcamam |
cakṣur nāsā ca karṇau ca dhanaṃ dehas tathaiva ca || iti |
ayam arthaḥ – upasthaṃ strīpuruṣayor liṅgaṃ tannigraho ‘gamyāgamane cairye vidāraṇaṃ jaṭharasya – āhāranivṛttir iti kecit | vākpāruṣye jihvāchedanam | daṇḍapāruṣye hastayoḥ | tatraiva pādagrahaṇādau pādayoḥ | gopyanirīkṣaṇādau cakṣuṣoḥ | parastrīstanānuliptagandhāghrāṇādau nāsikāyāḥ | rājamantraśravaṇādau karṇayoḥ | dhanāpaharaṇe dhanāpaharaṇasya mahāpātakādau | ayaṃ ca vidhiḥ kṣatriyaviṭchūdrāṇāṃ yatra daṇḍāviśeṣo noktaḥ tatra jñātavyaḥ | brāhmaṇas tv akṣato vadhya ity uktaṃ prāk | dvividho daṇḍa ity upalakṣaṇārtham, vidhāntarasyāpi smṛtatvāt |
śiraso muṇḍanaṃ daṇḍaḥ tasya nirvāsanaṃ purāt |
lalāṭe gardabhasyāṅkaḥ prayāṇaṃ gardhabhena ca || [495] iti |
tasya brāhmaṇasyety arthaḥ,
mauṇḍyaṃ prāṇāntako daṇḍo brāhmaṇasya vidhīyate |
itareṣāṃ tu varṇānāṃ daṇḍaḥ prāṇāntako bhavet ||
iti manusmaraṇāt |
hṛtaṃ praṇaṣṭaṃ yo dravyaṃ parahastād avāpnuyāt |
anivedya nṛpe daṇḍyaḥ sa tu ṣaṇṇavatiṃ paṇān ||
deśaṃ kālaṃ ca bhāgaṃ c jñātva naṣṭe balābalam |
dravyāṇāṃ kuśalā brūyuḥ yat tad dāpyam asaṃśayam ||
śāṇakṣamādau dravye naṣṭe – hrāsam upagate dravyāṇāṃ kuśalāḥ dravyavṛddhikṣayodayābhijñāḥ deśaṃ kālam upabhogaṃ naṣṭadravyasya sārāsāratāṃ parīkṣya yat kalpayanti tad asaṃśayaṃ śilpino dātavyāḥ | ardhaprakṣepaṇād dviśataṃ bhāgaṃ śulkaṃ nṛpo haret |
vyāsiddhaṃ rājayogyaṃ ca vikrītaṃ rājagāmi tat |
vyāsiddham atra na vikreyam iti pratiṣiddham | rājñā rājayogyaṃ ca vikrītaṃ māṇikyādi pratiṣiddham iti | tathā ca nāradaḥ |
mithyā vadan parīmāṇaṃ śulkasthānād apāsaran |
dāpyas tv aṣṭaguṇaṃ yaś ca savyājakrayavikrayī ||
tarītesthajalaṃ śulkaṃ gṛhṇan dāpyaḥ paṇān daśa |
brāhmaṇaprātiveśānām etad evānimantraṇe ||
animantraṇe śrāddhādāv iti śeṣaḥ ||
iti śrīpratāparudradevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇde sarvaśeṣo daṇḍīvidhiḥ (?) |
[496]
atha prakīrṇakākhyasya padasya vidhir ucyate
pūrveṣu saptadaśasu prakaraṇeṣu vyavahārasya caturvyāpitvaṃ pratipāditam | caturvyāpitvaṃ nāma arthipratyarthisabhyarājarūpān avayavān caturo vyāpnotīti | atra prakīrṇakākhye rājaikaniyatatvam iti saṃgatiḥ |
eṣa vādikṛtaḥ prokto vyavahāraḥ samāsataḥ |
nṛpāśrayaṃ pravakṣyāmi vyavahāraṃ prakīrṇakam || iti |
eṣa iti saptadaśvivādapadātmakaḥ | rājāśrayatvaṃ nārada āha |
prakīrṇake punar jñeyā vyavahārā nṛpāśrayāḥ |
rājñām ājñāpratīdhātas tatkarmākaraṇāni ca ||
purapramāṇasaṃbhedāḥ prakṛtīnāṃ tathaiva ca |
pāṣaṇḍanaigamaśreṇīgaṇadharmaviparyayāḥ ||
pitāputravivādaś ca prāyaścittavyatikramāḥ |
pratigrahavilopaś ca lopa āśramiṇām api ||
dharmasaṃkaradoṣaś ca tadvṛttiniyamas tathā |
na dṛṣṭaṃ yac ca pūrveṣu sarvaṃ tat syāt prakīrṇakam || iti |
etad vacanaṃ nāradīyam | nāradoktāṣṭādaśavivādapadamadhyagatasaptadaśavivādeṣu yan noktaṃ tad eva prakīrṇakaṃ kathitam | asmadīyasmṛtinibandhas tu sarvasmṛtisamuccaya iti tadanusāreṇa pratipādye tat tad ākāṅkṣāvaśāt tatra tatra vivādapade rājaikaniyatā api vyavahārā nirṇītāḥ | atra vyavahrapade parkīrṇakākhye madīyagranthānusāreṇa [497] nāradīyānusāreṇa ca yat pūrvaṃ noktam tad eva kathyate | tathā hi – rājñām ājñāpratīghāta ity asyārthaḥ – rājñāsmau brāhmaṇāya kṣatriyāya vā etāvad dravyaṃ deyaṃ ity ājñāyāṃ dattāyāṃ yas tu na karoti tena dravyaṃ tasmai dāpayitvā sa taddviguṇaṃ daṇḍya iti | tad āha viṣṇuḥ: “ājñāpratighāte dviguṇo damaḥ” iti | ayam arthaḥ – “dviguṇa iti dvaiguṇyoktyaivājñaptaṃ dravyaṃ tasmai dāpayitavyam iti jñāyate” iti bhāruciḥ | tatkarmakaraṇānīty asyārthaḥ – tasya rājñaḥ karma rājyaṃ tasya karaṇaṃ mudrikām agṛhītvety śeṣaḥ, “anādiṣṭaḥ sann adhyakṣatāṃ vrajati tadanusāreṇa daṇḍyaḥ” iti viṣṇusmaraṇāt | purapramāṇasaṃbhedāḥ – puraṃ prasiddhaṃ pramāṇāni sādhanāni hastyaśvarathapadātiprabhṛtīni teṣāṃ saṃbhedaḥ śatrūṇām avedanaṃ marmodghāṭanam iti yāvat | prakṛtīnāṃ tv amātyānāṃ parasparapaiśunyakathanena bhedakaraṇam | tathā ca saṃvartaḥ |
amātyānāṃ ca paiśunye puramānaprabhedane |
madhyamaṃ cottamaṃ caiva daṇḍa eṣa kramoditaḥ || iti |
yathākramaṃ prakṛtīnāṃ paiśunye madhyamasāhasaṃ purapramāṇamarmakathane uttamasāhasaṃ daṇḍyaḥ | cakārāc chārīro daṇḍo yathāraha iti | atra paiśunaśabdaḥ bhāve ṣyañantaḥ | piṣunasya bhāvaḥ paiśunyam |
pāṣaṇḍanaigamaśreṇigaṇadharmaviparyayā iti | asyārthaḥ – pāṣanḍiṇāṃ dharmāḥ paṭṭaṇe asmin sthale pāṣaṇḍinaḥ sthāpayitavyāḥ naigamā api śreṇayo ‘pi gaṇā apītyādipratiniyatasthlāvasthānāni dharmāḥ teṣāṃ viparyayo na tu kuṅkumavasantāndolikādayas teṣāṃ samayānapāpakarmākhye pratipāditatvāt | teṣāṃ vivādānāṃ vādiprativādisadbhāve caturvyāpitvasadbhāvāt [498] rājaikaniyatatvābhāvāc ca | pitāputravivādaś ceti – ayam arthaḥ – yah kaścid bandikṛto vā deśāntaragato vā cirakālam api sthitvā samāgatyāsau mama pitāsau putra ityādi, pitṛputragrahaṇaṃ patnyādīnām upalakṣakam | asau mama patir iyaṃ patṇītyādi, atra sākṣiṇo na santi svayaṃ tu na jānanti divyādikaṃ nāvatarati śapathādibhḥ śodhayitum anucitam iti, ataś ca rājaikaniyatatvāt rājñaiva nirṇayaḥ kārya iti | pitāputrasaṃśaye nirṇayaprakāram āha viṣṇuḥ: “putrasaṃśaye mātā tam aṅkam āropayed vikṛtiś cen nirṇetavyaḥ’ iti | prakṛtiḥ kāmavikāraḥ | tad viṃśativarṣīyamātṛkapañcadaśvarṣīyaputraviṣayam | vṛddhamātṛviṣaye tadabhāvān nirṇayāntaram āhatuḥ śaṅkhalikhitau: “svapantaṃ putram āhūya jñātavyam” iti | ekenāpi prakāreṇa nirṇayābhāve abhiṣiktasya rājño hṛdayam eva pramāṇam ity āha viṣnuḥ: “atra rājño hṛdayam eva pramāṇam” iti | ata evāha kālidāsaḥ:
satāṃ hi saṃdehapadeṣu vastuṣu
pramāṇam antaḥkaraṇasya vṛttayaḥ | iti |
prāyaścittavyatikrama ity asyārthaḥ  prāyaścittakaraṇavyatikramah | tadvyatikrame prāyaścittaṃ kārayitavyam iti prāyaścittakaraṇasya rājaikaniyatatvāt rājñaiva prāyaścitaṃ kāryam iti | tathā ca devalaḥ |
kṛcchrāṇāṃ dāpako rājā nirdeṣṭā dharmapālakah | iti |
mahāpāpeṣu kṛcchrāṇāṃ prāyaścittānāṃ dāpako rājā bhavati | brahmahatyādiprāyaścitteṣu rājājñāṃ vinā dvijādyaiḥ prāyaścirraṃ na pravartayitavyam ity arṇavakāraḥ (?) | parakṛtopasargāt traividyavṛddhānām ākrośaṃ vedādhyayanaviṣayaṃ kṛtvā tatparipālanaṃ kartavyam, tasya rājaikaniyatatvāt | [499] lopa āśramiṇām iti asyārthaḥ – āśramiṇāṃ brahmacārigṛhivānaprasthayatīnāṃ tadviśeṣāṇāṃ kuṭīcakabahūdakahaṃsaparamahaṃsānāṃ ekatīrthyādīnāṃ lopaḥ taddharmalopaḥ sa ca nirvāhyaḥ | yatīnāṃ madhye yas tu bhraṣṭaḥ sa tu rājño dāsa iti pūrvam eva pratipāditatvāt tad etadviṣayaṃ na bhavatīy avagantavyam | sarvasaṃkaradoṣaḥ spaṣṭa eva | tadvṛttiniyamaḥ so ‘pi spaṣṭaḥ | na dṛṣṭaṃ yac ca pūrveṣu iti | ayam arthaḥ – yas tu grāme ‘bhiśastaḥ pratyarthī nāsti grāmīṇās tu abhiśāpa iti vadanti tatra rājñā nirṇayaḥ kārya ityādy ūhyam | ete rājadharmāḥ prakaraṇādāv eva vaktavyāḥ tathāpi vyavahārātmakatvāt vyavahārapadānantaram evoktam iti dhyeyam | atha vā saptadaśavivādapadaprakīrṇakapadayoḥ saṃgatyanantaram – pūrvaṃ parājitasyaiva daṇḍavidhānam uktam | atra jayino 'pi daṇḍavidhānam iti anayoḥ saṃgatiḥ | yathāha yājñavalkyaḥ |
durdṛṣṭāṃs tu punar dṛṣṭair vyavahārān nṛpeṇa tu |
sabhyāḥ sajayino daṇḍyā vivādadviguṇaṃ damam ||
aprāptajetṛdaṇḍavidhirūpatvād vacanasya sabhyadaṇḍaparatvaṃ sabhyānāṃ doṣasadbhāve daṇḍavidhānaṃ parkaraṇādau uktam iti jayino daṇḍavidhānam atra pratipādyate | atra yady api bhūtapūrvagatyā jayino jayitvaṃ na punarnyāyena jitatvād ity āśaṅkya pūrvatra saptadaśasu prakaraṇeṣu yan nirṇītaṃ tan na parāvartyam ity uktam,
sabhyadoṣāt tu yan naṣṭaṃ deyaṃ sabhyena tat tadā |
kāryaṃ tu kāriṇām eva niścitaṃ na vicārayet ||
iti smaraṇāt | atra tu sabhyadoṣasadbhāve punarnyāyaḥ kārya ity evaṃparaṃ vacanam ity uktaṃ vyākhyātṛbhiḥ | ataś ca saṃgatiś coktaiva asmin prakaraṇe punarnyāyaḥ kārya iti | ata eva yājñavalkyaḥ | [500]
yo manyetājito ‘smīti nyāyenāpi parājitaḥ |
tam āyāntaṃ punar jitvā dāpayed dviguṇaṃ damam ||
atra nāradaḥ |
strīṣu rātrau bahirgrāmād antarveśmany arātiṣu |
vyavahāraḥ kṛto ‘py eṣu punaḥ kartavyatām iyāt ||
rājña iti śeṣaḥ | rājaikaniyatatvād vyavahārasya | tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ |
balopadhivinirvṛttān vyavahārān nivartayet | iti |
ataḥ paścād vyavahārānantaraṃ pravartayed ity abhiprāya iti candrikākāraḥ | atrāpi viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
mattonmattātibhītaiś ca bālena sthavireṇa ca |
asaṃbandhakṛtaś caiva vyavahāro na sidhyati ||
asaṃbandho ‘rthipratyarthirahitaḥ | tathā ca manuḥ |
tarītaṃ (tīritaṃ?) cānuśiṣtaṃ ca yatra kvacana yad bhavet |
kṛtaṃ tad dharmato vidyān na tad bhūyo nivartayet ||
asat sad iti yaḥ pakṣaḥ sabhyair evāvadhāryate |
tarītaḥ so ‘nuśiṣṭas tu sākṣivākyāt praṇīyate ||
tat strīkṛtatvādihetvabhāvaviṣayam ity avagantavyam | punarnyāye nirṇayapūrvaṃ sabhyair anyāyena yad daṇḍarūpeṇa dravyam āhṛtaṃ tasya viniyogam āha yājñavalkyaḥ |
rājñā nyāyena yo daṇḍo gṛhīto varuṇāya tam |
nivedya datvā viprebhyaḥ svayam triṃśadguṇīkṛtam ||
anyāyena yo daṇḍaḥ pūrveṇa rājñā lobhādinā gṛhītaḥ tat triguṇīktaṃ [501] varuṇāyeti saṃkalpya brāhmaṇebhyaḥ svayaṃ dadyāt | yasmād anyāyenārjitāt sakāśād daṇḍarūpeṇa yāvad gṛhītaṃ tāvat tasmai pradeyam iti | atha vā saṃgatyantaram,
bhūtachalānurodhena dvigatiḥ samudāhṛtaḥ |
iti vyavahārasya chalānusaraṇaṃ tattvānusaraṇena saptadaśavivādapadāny anukrāntāni | chalānusareṇena prakīrṇakākhyaṃ vivādapadam anukrāntam iti | tathā ca bṛhaspatiḥ |
sākṣisabhyārthasannānāṃ dūṣaṇe darśanaṃ punaḥ |
svavācaiva jitānāṃ tu noktaḥ paunarbhavo vidhiḥ || iti |
atra vyavahārān nṛpaḥ paśyed iti vidhiḥ svāparādhena vyavahārasya samāptatvāt svavāganusaraṇarūpchalam evāvalambate na tu pramāṇāntaragaveṣaṇāṃ vilambāsahatvād vidheḥ | atra viśeṣam āha manuḥ |
amātyāḥ prāḍvivāko vā yaḥ kuryāt kāryam anyathā |
tat svayaṃ nṛpatiḥ kuryāt tān sahasraṃ ca daṇḍayet ||
ataḥ punarnyāyo rājaikaniyata iti svayaṃnṛpatipadābhyāṃ gamyate | atra pratiprasavam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
na kṣetragṛhadāsānāṃ dānādhamanavikrayāḥ |
asvatantrakṛtāḥ siddhiṃ prāpnuyur nānuvarṇitam ||
asvatantrakṛtaṃ parāvartyam eva nāsty atra punarnyāya iti bhāvaḥ | anuvarṇitāḥ svatantreṇādiṣṭāḥ | asvatantrān āha nāradaḥ |
asvatantrāḥ striyaḥ putrāḥ dāsādyāś ca parigrahāḥ | iti |
parigrahā upajīvinaḥ | atra viśeṣam āha kātyāyanaḥ |
asvatantrakṛtaṃ kāryaṃ tasya svāmī nivartayet |
[502]        na vārtā vivadetānyo (?) bhītonmattakṛtād ṛte ||
bhītonmattakṛtanivartane rājña eva adhikārāt | bhītonmattādisvāminā nivartakena saha nyasya vivādaḥ nātyantānucita ity abhiprāyaḥ | anuvarṇitasvarūpam āha bṛhaspatiḥ |
yaḥ svāminābhiyuktas tu dhanāyavyayapālane |
kusīdakṛṣivāṇijye nisṛṣṭārthas tu saḥ smṛtaḥ ||
pramāṇaṃ tatkṛtaṃ sarvaṃ lābhālābhavyayodayam |
svadeśe vā videśe vā svāmī tan na visaṃvadet || iti |
atra nāradaḥ |
kṭrāny apy apramānāni kāryāṇy āhur anāpadi | iti |
strīgrahaṇam asvatantropalakṣaṇārtham | evaṃ cāpadi tu asvatantrakṛtāṇy api pramāṇānītyārthikāryaḥ pratyetavyaḥ | atra apavādam āha sa eva |
viśeṣato gṛhakṣetre dānādhamanavikrayāḥ | iti |
gṛhakṣetrayoḥ dānādhamanavikrayās tu āpady apy asvatantrakṛtās tu na sidhyantīty arthaḥ | atra kātyāyanaḥ |
siddhis tu śāstratatvajñaiḥ cikitsā samudāhṛtā |
prāyaścittaṃ ca daṇḍaṃ ca tābhyāṃ sā dvividhā smṛtā || iti |
atra nāradaḥ |
evaṃ paśyan sadā rājā vyavahārān samāhitaḥ |
vitatyeha yaśo dīptaḥ indrasyaiti salokatām ||
bṛhaspatir api |
evaṃ sabhyair divā rājā kuryān nirṇayapālanam |
vitatyeha yaśo loke mahendrasacivo bhavet ||
[503]        sākṣilekhyānumānena prakuryāt kāryanirṇayam |
vitatyeha yaśo rājā bradhnasyāpnoti viṣṭapam ||
manur api |
evaṃ sarvān imān rājā vyavahārān sadā nayan |
vyapohya kilbiṣaṃ sarvaṃ brahmaloke mahīyate || iti |
kāmakrodhau tu saṃyamya yo ‘rthaṃ dharmeṇa paśyati |
prajās tam anuvartante samudram iv sindhavaḥ ||
prajārañjanaśīlasya nityaṃ vṛddhopajīvinaḥ |
āyuḥ prajā dhanaṃ kīrtiḥ tasya sidhyanty asaṃśayaḥ ||
amī nanu munīśvarā manuvasiṣṭhayogīśvarā
gatā vigatamatsarā bhavatu vā bhavānīpatiḥ
sa tuṣyatu parāṃ mama smṛtinibandhavāgvaikharīm ||
iti prakīrṇakākhyasya padasya vilāsaḥ
iti śrīvīragajapatigauḍeśvaranavakoṭikarṇāṭakaluburigeśvara-
jamunāpurādhīśvarahuśanasāhisuratrāṇaśaraṇarakṣaṇa-
śrīdurgāvaraputraparamapavitracaritrarājādhirājarājaparameśvara-
śrīpratāparudramahādevamahārājaviracite smṛtisaṃgrahe sarasvatīvilāse
vyavahārakāṇde aṣṭādaśavivādapadanirṇayo nāma pañcamollāsaḥ |
[1] In Mitākṣarā this deals with dūtas and not with cāras.
